Explanatory Remarks:

1. Editorial notes or additions are enclosed in ().

Text:

|| śrīḥ ||
śrīnārāyaṇabhaṭṭakṛtaṃ
nārāyaṇīyaṃ
deśamaṅgalavāryakṛtayā bhaktapriyākhyayā vyākhyayā saṃvalitam |
gajānanaṃ girāṃ devīṃ vyāsaṃ kaṃsahanaṃ gurūn |
bhūteśamīśamāśāsitārthadān praṇamāmyaham ||
śrīmadbhāgavatārthasaṅgahamayaṃ nārāyaṇīyāhvayaṃ
stotraṃ hṛdyamanardhamujjvalataraṃ dhvastāndhakārodayam |
yat kaṇṭheṣu satāmanuttamaguṇaṃ pratyagramudbhāsate
tasyeyaṃ kriyate yathāmati mayā vyākhyā hi bhaktapriyā ||
kīrtanaṃ bhagavatkīrtermatkṛtāvānuṣaṅgikam |
ityeva prayate nāsmavyākhyātṛtvaprasiddhaye ||
iha khalu samadhigatanikhilanigamārthasatattvatayā, śābdaparabrajhapārāvārapārīṇatayā, paramabhāgavatatayā ca sakalasahṛdayamahitayaśāḥ śrīnārāyaṇakaviḥ paramakāruṇikatayā bhaktānugrahāya śrībhāgavatārthānusāri nārāyaṇīyābhidhaṃ stotrarattaṃ cikīrṣuḥ prathamaṃ prathamakṣlokena prāripsitasya stotrasyāvighnena parisamāptipracayagamanābhyāṃ śrotṛjanasakalasamīhitasiddhaye ca stotrapratipādyajagatsargavisargādidaśalakṣaṇalīlānidānabhūtaparattattvānusmaraṇarūpaṃ maṅgalamācarati -- 
sāndrānandāvabodhātmakamanupamitaṃ kāladeśāvadhibhyāṃ
nirmuktaṃ nityamuktaṃ nigamaśatasahasreṇa nirmāsyamānam |
aspaṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭamātre punarurupuruṣārthātmakaṃ brahma tattvaṃ
tattāvad bhāti sākṣād gurupavanapure hanta bhāgyaṃ janānām || 1 ||
sāndreti | brahma gurupavanapure sākṣād bhātīti sambandhaḥ | brahma sarvāśrayaṃ sarvānusyūtaṃ śuddhacaitanyam | gurupavanapure gurupavanapuramiti prasiddhe kṣetre | sākṣād bhāti bhaktānugrahāyātisvacchataraśuddhasattvamayamūrti sad ācchāditanijasaccidānandarūpaṃ pratimāmiṣeṇa sakalajanacākṣuṣajñānagocarībhavatītyarthaḥ | nanu kiṃ sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ vā brahma sākṣād bhātītyucyata ityāśaṅkāyāmāha -- tattāvaditi | tāvacchabdo'vadhāraṇe | yad uktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaṃ niṣkala brahma , tadevetyarthaḥ | śuddhasattvasya ca nipkalabrahmānanyatvaṃ 'niṣkampa' ityatropapādayiṣyate | athavā tāvacchabdaḥ kramārthaḥ | tad brahma tāvat kramānurodhena sākṣāccākṣuṣatayā mānasatayā svaprakāśasaṃvidrūpeṇa ca bhātītyarthaḥ | ayaṃ cāśayaḥ -- śrīgurupavanapurādhīśaṃ cakṣuṣā sākṣātkṛtya śravaṇakīrtanādilakṣaṇayā bhaktyā bhajatāṃ jhaṭiti sañjātayā premalakṣaṇayā bhaktyā nikhilakalmaṣanāśe sati nirmalacetastayāvilambitameva sādhanacatuṣṭayasampatteradhikāritve jāte sakalalokaguroḥ śrīharereva prabhāveṇāhamakhaṇḍaṃ brahmāsmīti cittavṛttirudeti | sā ca brahmaviṣayā satī brahmagatājñānabādhanadvārā tatkāryabhūtaṃ śarīrādiprapañcaṃ, tadantarbhūtāmakhaṇḍākārāṃ cittavṛttimapi bādhate | tadā darpaṇādyabhāve bimbapratibimbaikyavat pratyagabhinnaṃ saccidānandaṃ brahma svaprakāśasaṃvidrūpeṇāvatiṣṭhata iti | evamanāyāsenaiva paramapuruṣārthalabdhiḥ sukṛtaparipākaikamūletyāha -- hanta bhāgyaṃ janānāmiti | janānāṃ bhāgyaṃ puruṣārthalabdhisāmagracānumīyata ityarthaḥ | janānāmityaviśeṣoktyā sarveṣāmapi bhagavadbhajanādhikāra iti dyotyate | hanteti harṣe | janānāṃ bhāgye sati harṣāvirbhāvo'sya śukanāradādibhagavadadhikāripuruṣāṇāmiva paramakāruṇikatayā parārthaghaṭanakutukitvaṃ dyotayati | nanu kimarthamidamucyate 'brahma bhāti, hanta bhāgyaṃ janānāmi'ti | na tāvat svapratipattyarthe, vākyaprayogavaiyarthyāt | na paraṃ bodhayituṃ, ‘bhajadhvaji'tyāditadupapādakavākyaśeṣāśravaṇāditi ced, maivam | yathā kenacillākṣaṇikena ‘nidhigarbheyaṃ bhūmiḥ, hanta bhāgyavanto yūyam' ityukte śrotārastadarthibhirnidhigrahaṇaṃ kartavyamityanenoktamityadhyāhṛtya vākyaśeṣaṃ kalpayanti, tadvadatrāpi vākyaśeṣo'dhyāhartavyaḥ | evañca kathañcid bahujanmārjitasukṛtaparipākavaśena labdhamanuṣyajanmāno yūyaṃ bhaktiyogapuraḥsaraṃ bhaktānugrahāyāṅgīkṛtasattvavigrahaṃ śrīguruvāyupuranāthaṃ paraṃ brahma saṃsevya paramapuruṣārthamanubhavatetyupadeśarahasyamevaitadityavagantavyam | nanu bhāgyamiti karmaphalamucyate | tajjanyatve paramapuruṣārthasyānityatvaprasaṅga iti ced, na, adhikāritvasampādanenopakṣīṇatvāt karmaphalānām | taduktam 'eṣāṃ nityādīnāṃ buddhiśuddhiḥ paraṃ prayojanam' iti | brahmaṇaḥ svarūpalakṣaṇamāhasāndrānandāvabodhātmakaṃ tattvamiti | ānandaḥ sukham, avabodho jñānaṃ, sāndrau ghanībhūtau ānandāvabodhāvātmā svarūpaṃ yasya tat tathoktam | ānandasya sāndratvaṃ nāma srakcandanāghupādhyanavacchinnatvam | avabodhasya ca ghaṭādiviṣayānavacchinnatvam | yaduktamabhiyuktaiḥ -- 
'yasminyasminnasti loke bodhastattadupekṣaṇe |
yad bodhamātraṃ tad brahmetyevaṃ vedāntaniścayād ||
iti | tattvaṃ paramārthaṃ sat, sarvasyāpyātmatvena prasiddhatvāt | kasyāpi hi na nāhamasmīti pratītirudeti | evañcāhaṃpratyaya evaṃ pratyagātmatvena brahmaṇaḥ siddhatvānna tatsattve vipratipattiriti bhāvaḥ | ābhyāṃ viśeṣaṇābhyāṃ saccidānandaṃ brahmeti svarūpalakṣaṇamuktaṃ, yathā prakṛṣṭaprakāśaścandramā iti | nanvevaṃlakṣaṇamapi brahma nānubhavapadavīmavatarati | ata īdṛśaṃ taditi sadṛṣṭāntaṃ pradarśyatāmityāśaṅkayāha -- anupamitamiti | sajātīyasya vijātīyasya vānyasyābhāvād brahmaṇaḥ kenāpyupamānamaśakyamiti bhāvaḥ | anena ca brahmaṇo'dvitīyatvamapyarthāduktam | nanu jīveśvarayorjīvānāṃ tadupādhīnāṃ ca bhede jāgrati kathamadvitīyatvaṃ brahmaṇa iti ced, maivaṃ, bhedaprapañcasya brahmaṇyanādyavidyākalpitatvena sragbhujaṅgavanmithyātvāt| tena tāvanna sadvitīyatvam | avidyā cendrajālavidyeva svāśrayamaindrajālikamavimohayantī brahmāśritā kācidaśeṣaprapañcanirmāṇaśaktiḥ | śaktiśaktimatorabheda iti brahmādvaitatvasiddhiḥ | nanu jīveśvarau bhinnau viruddhadharmatvād dahanatuhinavaditi bhedānumānamastīti ced, na, ekatvaśrutismṛtibādhitaviṣayatvādasyānumānasya | nanvīśvarasya jīvaikatve saṃsāritvaṃ syād, jīvānāṃ ceśvaratve saṃsārābhāvaprasaṅga iti ced, na, vidyāvidyayorbhedādubhayopapatteḥ | tathāhi vidyā nāma vikṣepaśaktipradhāneśvaropādhiḥ, yayeśvaraḥ sṛṣṭyādi karoti | avidyā nāmāvaraṇaśaktipradhānā jīvopādhiḥ, yayeśvaraḥ sannapi jīvastatkṛtopādhibhedena saṃsārī bhavati | ato jīveśvarayorekatve'pyavidyayā saṃsāro vidyayā ca tadabhāvo nānupapannaḥ | anāditvānnirguṇatvāñceśvarasya jarāmaraṇādisāṃsārikadharmāśca na bhavanti, yathā svapnādutthitasya svāmāḥ kartṛtvabhoktṛtvādaya ityalamatiprasaṅgena | nanu kartṛtvabhoktṛtvādirātmanaḥ svābhāviko dharma hāta na kadācidapi tena viyogaḥ syādityāśaṅkayāha -- kāladeśāvadhibhyāṃ nimuktamiti | kālato yo'vadhirdeśataśca, tābhyāṃ nirmuktaṃ virahitamityarthaḥ | tataśca vyomavadamūrtasya kūṭasthasya brahmaṇaḥ kriyādisaṃyogānupapatterna kartṛtvabhoktṛttre ityarthaḥ| nityatvaṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ kāryaprapañcasyotpatteḥ prāk paścādapi vidyamānatvam | vibhutvaṃ ca yathā mṛtsuvarṇāderghaṭakuṇḍalādiṣūpādānakāraṇatvenānugamaḥ, tadvat sarvopādānasya brahmaṇo'pi sarvatra śarīrādau kāraṇatvenānusyūtatvāt sarvavyāptatvam | ato'nena jagatkāraṇaṃ brahmeti ayāvallakṣyabhāvi taṭasthalakṣaṇamapyarthāduktaṃ veditavyam | evaṃ lakṣaṇadvayasiddhasya vastunaḥ pramāṇāpekṣāyāmāha  nigameti | nigamā upaniṣadaḥ, tāsāṃ śatasahasramiti bahutvābhiprāyam | bahubhirupaniṣadvākyairityarthaḥ | aspaṣṭaṃ yathā bhavati tathā nirbhāsyamānamiti, upakramopasaṃhārādibhirliṅgaistātparyeṇa lakṣaṇayā pratipādyamānamityarthaḥ | yadvā dṛṣṭamātre'spaṣṭamiti sambandhaḥ| vākyārthajñāne satyapi viparītānyathābhāvanādi dibyohavat sahasā na nivartate | ataḥ śravaṇamananādinā tannivṛttāveva brahmajñānamanubhavaparyavasāyyavatiṣṭhata iti bhāvaḥ | nanvevaṃbhūtabrahmadarśane sati kiṃ phalaṃ, tatrāha -- punarurupuruṣārthātmakamiti | punaḥ brahmajñānānantaramityarthaḥ | uruḥ puruṣārtho mokṣaḥ | tasya corutvaṃ duḥkhānuṣaṅgābhāvāt kāladeśānavacchedācca paripūrṇatvam, itarapuruṣārthānāṃ karmajanyatayā tadabhāvāt | puruṣārthātmakamiti | brahmajñānasamanantarameva brahma puruṣārtharūpeṇāvatiṣṭhata ityarthaḥ | evaṃ brahmabhūtasya na punarāvṛttirityabhiprāyeṇa brahma viśinaṣṭi -- nityamuktamiti | nityameva muktaṃ māyātatkāryoparāgarahitam | paramārthato'dvitīyasyāvidyātatkalpitaśarīrādisambandhasyāsambhāvanīyatvāditi bhāvaḥ | evañca muktāvasthāyā ajanyatvānna punarāvṛttiriti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ sāndrānandāvabodhātmakaṃ tattvamiti padadvayena saccidānandaṃ brahmeti svarūpalakṣaṇamuktam | anupamitapadena tasyādvitīyatvamuktam | kāladeśāvadhibhyāṃ nirmuktamiti brahmaṇo jīvaikye'pi kartṛtvādijīvadharmāsambhava uktaḥ | arthāt taṭasthalakṣaṇamapyuktam | nigamaśatasahasreṇa nirbhāsyamānamityupaniṣadāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ coktam | evambhūtaṃ brahma gurupavanapure sākṣād bhātītyatiprayāsasādhyasya brahmadarśanasya sakalajanasusampādyatvamuktam | hanta bhāgyaṃ janānāmiti brahmadarśanādhikārasya sukṛtaikamūlatvaṃ coktam | dṛṣṭamātre aspaṣṭamiti brahmānandānubhavasya śravaṇādisādhyatvamuktam | punarurupuruṣārthātmakamiti saṃsāraduḥkhocchedo brahmānandāvāptiśceti brahmadarśanasya phaladvitayaṃ darśitam | nityamuktamiti brahma prāptasya ‘anāvṛttiḥ śabdād' iti nyāyenāpunarāvṛttiśca darśitā evaṃ brahmaṇa upāsyatvokteḥ 'brahma bhāti, paraṃ dhīmahī' tyādipadānāṃ tātparyaikyādapyanena kṣlokena śrībhāgavataprathamakṣlokārtha eva pradarśitaḥ | atra ca stotre brahma pratipādyatvena viṣayaḥ, tatprāptiḥ prayojanaṃ, bhāgyavanto'dhikāriṇa ityapi sūcitam || 1 ||
evaṃ tāvanmaṅgalācaraṇapareṇa prathamakṣlokenārthāt pratipādite  api viṣayaprayojane śrotṛjanasukhapratibodhanāya mukhataḥ pratipādayati -- 
evaṃ durlabhyavastunyapi sulabhatayā hastalabdhe yadanyat
tanvā vācā dhiyā vā bhajati bata janaḥ kṣudrataiva sphuṭeyam |
ete tāvad vayaṃ tu sthirataramanasā viśvapīḍāpahatyai
niśśeṣātmānamenaṃ gurupavanapurādhīśamevāśrayāmaḥ || 2 ||
evamiti | atrāviśiṣṭena vastuśabdena paramārthasaṭbrahmocyate, mithyārthe viśiṣṭasyaiva prayogadarśanāt | tad durlabhyaṃ duḥkhena jñātuṃ prāptuṃ vā śakyaṃ, sādhanacatuṣṭayasampannairgurūpasattidvārā śravaṇamananādibhirevāvagantuṃ śakyatvāt tasmin | evam uktena prakāreṇa sulabhatayā anāyāsena | hastalabdhaptyoḥ saukarye dyotyate | durlabhyavastunyevaṃ sulabhatayā hastalabdhe satyapi anyad devatāntaraṃ viṣayādikaṃ vā tanvā vācā ghiyā vā bhajati jana iti yat, tad bata kaṣṭam | iyaṃ kṣudratā asārataiva, avivekitaiveti yāvat | sphuṭā sarvasammatetyarthaḥ | vivekino viśeṣamāha -- eta iti | vayamityātmani bahuvacanam | eta iti svasya bhagavadbhajanasaṃrambhaṃ darśayati | yadvā ete vayamiti śrotāro'pi gṛhyante | ete vayaṃ tāvat tu tathā kṣudratāṃ na kurmaḥ, kintu sthiratareṇa etadbhajanameva śreya iti niścitena manasā gurupavanapurādhīśaṃ paraṃ brahmaiva āśrayāmaḥ sevāmahe | atra ye sakāmāstān prati phalaṃ darśayati -- viśveti | viśvapīḍānāmādhyātmikādīnāṃ sarvapīḍānām, apahatyai nāśāya | athavā viśvapīḍāpahatirmokṣaḥ, tasyaiva sakalapīḍānivṛttirūpatvāt | anena ca ye'kāmanayainaṃ sevante, ta uttamādhikāriṇaḥ, ye tu phalakāmanayā sevante, te madhyamādhikāriṇaḥ, ye tu bhedadṛṣṭyānyad devatāntaraṃ sakāmāḥ sevante, te'dhamādhikāriṇa iti stotre'dhikāribhedo'pi pradarśitaḥ | viśvapīḍāpahatyai iti prayojanamuktam | niśśeṣātmānamiti bhagavadviśeṣaṇena viṣayo nirdiśyate | niśśeṣāṇāṃ carācarāṇām ātmā kāraṇam, athavā niśśeṣaścarācaraprapañca ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya sa tathā sarvātmā, yadvā niśśeṣāṇāmātmā antaryāmī, yadivā niśśeṣāṇāṃ brahmaviṣṇugirīśādīnām ātmā svarūpabhūtaḥ, taṃ sarvadevamayamityarthaḥ | atra kriyārūpā sādhanabhaktirapi viṣayatvena nirdiśyate -- tanvā vācā dhiyā veti | taduktaṃ -- 
"bhaktirhi dvividhā proktā sādhyasādhanabhedataḥ |
sādhyā hyahaitukī prītiḥ kriyā syāt sādhanātmikā ||
sā punarnavadhā proktā purāṇe sādhanātmikā | "
iti | atra vāceti kīrtanamucyate, tanvetyarcanavandananamaskārāḥ, dhiyeti śravaṇasmaraṇadāsyasakhyātmanivedanānīti | athavā durlabhyaṃ vastu manuṣyajanma tasmin | evaṃ brāhmaṇyavidyāpaṭukaraṇatvādyāśrayatayā hastalabdha iti kaiṅkaryādirāhityaṃ dyotayati | evaṃ satyapi a̱nyad anyaśarīreṣvapi sulabhaṃ viṣayādi jano bhajati
bata | vayaṃ tvityādi samānam | evañca stotre adhikāriviṣayaprayojanāni darśitāni | atra ca 'dharmaḥ projjhitakaitavo'tra'   'tanvā vācā dhiyā vā bhajati', 'vāstavaṃ vastu'   'durlabhyavastuni', ' kiṃ vā paraiḥ'   'anyat tanvā vācā dhiyā vā bhajati bata', ‘tāpatrayonmūlanaṃ' 'viśvapīḍāpahatyai', 'sadyo hṛdyavarudhyate' 'sthirataramanasā', ‘īśvaraḥ' ‘niśśeṣātmānami'tyādipadānāmarthasāmyādasya śrībhāgavatadvitīyakṣlokārthatā ca draṣṭavyā | ayaṃ cāparo'rthaḥ sahṛdayahṛdayamanurañjayati -- durlabhyaṃ vastu śrībhāgavataṃ purāṇam | tasminnevaṃ brahmanāradavyāsaparamparayā sulabhatayā hastalabdhe satyapi jano'nyat purāṇaṃ kāvyādi vā bhajatīti yat, seyaṃ kṣudratā sphuṭaiva | ete tāvad vayaṃ tu tanvā parimitayā śrībhāgavatārthasaṅkṣepaṇarūpayā vācā enaṃ śrībhāgavatapradhānapratipādyaṃ gurupavanapurādhīśamevāśrayāmaḥ gurupavanapurādhīśastotrarūpeṇa śrībhāgavatārthaṃ saṅkṣipāma iti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ bhāgavatārthaikadeśaṃ, netyāha -- niśśeṣātmānamiti | niśśeṣo daśalakṣaṇalīlālakṣaṇadvādaśaskandhārtharūpa ātmā yasya | avāntaraprayojanamāha -- viśveti | viśveṣāṃ strīśūdrādīnāmapi pīḍāpahatyai mokṣāya | stotrapaṭhane hi strīśūdrādīnāmapyadhikāraḥ | sthirataramanaseti manasaḥ sāroddhārasthirīkaraṇenāsya stotrasya sārasaṅgraharūpatvaṃ vyajyate | tanvā vāceti sādhikasahasrasaṅkhaparimitapadyātmakatvaṃ dhiyeti bhagavatsmaraṇavācinā tadanugrahalabghamādhuryādiguṇayuktatvaṃ ca vyajyate | evañcaivaṃvidhaṃ stotraṃ racayāma iti cikīrṣitapratijñāpi kṛtā | asmin pakṣe 'durlabhyavastuni' 'nigamakalpataroḥ phalaṃ bhāgavataṃ rasaṃ', 'sulabhatayā hastalabdhe' ‘śukamukhād bhuvi galitaṃ', 'jano'nyad bhajatīti yat sā kṣudrataiva' ‘pibata bhāgavatam', ‘ete vayaṃ'   'rasikā bhāvukā' ityādipadānāṃ bimbapratibimbavadarthasādṛśyapratīteḥ śrībhāgavatatṛtīyakṣlokārtho'pyanena parāmṛṣṭa iti draṣṭavyam | kṣlokadvayārthasyaikenopādānamapi asya saṅgraharūpatvaṃ dyotayati | idaṃ ca stotraṃ bhaktiyogaparatayā sarvairādareṇa paṭhanīyamityapyanenaiva sūcitaṃ, bhaktiyogaprādhānyasyāpyanenaiva sūcyamānatvāt | tathāhi -- durlabhyaṃ vastu bhaktiyogākhyaṃ, tasmin hastalabdhe satyapyanyad jñānaṃ karma vā bhajatīti yat, sā kṣudrataiva | anyadūhanīyam | evaṃ bhagavatkṛpāpātrībhūtaśrīnārāyaṇakavivākyasyānekārthagambhīramahimno mahimeti
diṅmātraṃ pradarśitāmiti | nanu bhaktiyogaparatayā bhaktairādaraṇayimidamityuktam | atha keyaṃ bhaktirnāma | ucyate | upāyapūrvakaṃ bhagavati manasaḥ sthirīkaraṇaṃ bhaktiḥ | sa ca bhaktiraso vihitāvihitakarmajñānamiśrādibhedena bahudhā | punarapi śṛṅgārādhupāyabhedānnavadheti boppadevaḥ | anye tvevaṃ bhaktāvapi vācyamiti vadanto bhakterarasatāmācakṣate | tathāhi -- bhaktirna rasaḥ, rasalakṣaṇābhāvād,
"vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca sāttvikairvyabhicāribhiḥ |
ānīyamānaḥ svādutvaṃ sthāyī bhāvo rasaḥ smṛtaḥ ||"
iti hi rasalakṣaṇam | yathā śṛṅgārādirase ratyādiḥ sthāyī bhāvaḥ | nāyikānāyakacandramandānilādayo vibhāvāḥ | kaṭākṣavīkṣaṇādayo'nubhāvāḥ | stambhasvedādayaḥ sāttvikāḥ | nirvedaglānyādayo vyabhicāriṇaḥ | evaṃvidhavibhāvādisaṃyogābhāvānna bhaktī rasatāmāpadyata iti, maivam, atrāpi rasasāmagrīsadbhāvāt | kenāpyupāyena śrīkṛṣṇe manoniveśaḥ sthāyī bhāvaḥ | 'tasmāt kenāpyupāyena manaḥ kṛṣṇe niveśayed' iti śrībhāgavatavacanāt | tatropāyo navadhā | taduktaṃ boppadevena ‘bhaktirasasyaiva hāsyaśṛṅgārakaruṇaraudrabhayānakabībhatsaśāntādbhutavīrarūpeṇānubhavād' iti, 'vyāsādibhiḥ kavibhirvarṇitasya viṣṇorviṣṇubhaktānāṃ vā caritrasya navarasātmakasya śravaṇādinā janitaścamatkāro bhaktirasa' iti ca | ata ete bhagavati prayujyamānā bhaktirasatāmāpadyante | govindo gopyaścālambanavibhāvāḥ | candramandānilacaritraśravaṇādaya uddīpanavibhāvāḥ | kaṭākṣabīkṣaṇastambhasvedādayo'nubhāvāḥ | nirvedaglāniśaṅkāghṛtyādayo yathāyogaṃ vyabhicāriṇaḥ | evaṃ sāmagrīsadbhāve'pi bhakterbahiṣkaraṇaṃ svayaṃ bhaktirasāsvādabahiraṅgatāmātraśaraṇam | naca vācyamasarvaviṣayatvād bhakte rasapadavyanāroha iti | tathāca śrotriyabrahmacāryādyaviṣayatayā śṛṅgārādīnāmapyarasatāpātāditi siddhaṃ bhakte rasatvam | evaṃvidhabhaktyanubhavād bhaktā api navavidhāḥ |
"gopyaḥ kāmād bhayāt kaṃso dveṣāccaidyādayo nṛpāḥ |
sambandhād vṛṣṇayaḥ snehād yūyaṃ bhaktyā vayaṃ vibho!||"
'nanu' ityārabhya 'sugamam' ityantaṃ tṛtīyakṣlokavyākhyānamadhye' uparisthamupādīyate' ityetadantaṃ vyatikṣiptaṃ mudritapustake |
iti bhāgavatokteḥ | evambhūtairbhaktairupāsyaṃ viṣṇurūpamapi pañcavidhaṃ puruṣabrahmagiriśaviṣṇunirguṇabhedāt | tatra śuddhasattvamayaṃ viṣṇurūpamevopāsyatamamiti boppadevaḥ | upāsanakramaṃ tu vakṣyāmaḥ | etat sarvaṃ dvitīya kṣlokasūcitamupari cānusandadhatāṃ sugamam || 2 ||
evaṃ viṣayaprayojanapradarśanena śrotūnabhimukhīkṛtya tepāmupāsanāsiddhaye bhagavadrūpaṃ varṇayan bhagavantaṃ stauti -- 
sattvaṃ yattat parābhyāmaparikalanato nirmalaṃ tena tāvad
bhūtairbhūtendriyaiste vapuriti bahuśaḥ kṣrūyate vyāsavākyam |
tat svacchatvād yadacchāditaparasukhacidgarbhanirbhāsarūpaṃ
tasmin dhanyā ramante śrutimatimadhure sugrahe vigrahe te || 3 ||
sattvamiti | tat prasiddham | parābhyāṃ rajastamobhyām | parikalanam itaretaramelanaṃ, tadabhāvo'parikalanaṃ, tena | nirmalaṃ śuddhaṃ sattvaṃ yat, tena tāvaditi tenaivopādānakāraṇena| bhūtairmahadādikrameṇotpannaiḥ | bhūtendriyaiḥ pañcabhūtairekādaśendriyaiḥ | etacca prāṇādīnāmapyupalakṣaṇam | he bhūmanniti siṃhāvalokitanyāyenoparisthamupādīyate | etaiḥ svecchayā vicitaṃ te tava vapurlīlāśarīramityasminnarthe pramāṇatayā vyāsasya ‘munīnāmapyahaṃ vyāsa' iti tavātmatvenābhimatasya vākyam | bahuśo vāraṃvāraṃ purāṇeṣu śrūyata ityaya | vākyaṃ ca -- 'jagṛhe pauruṣaṃ rūpam', 'viśuddhaṃ sattvamūrjitam', 'sattvaṃ viśuddhaṃ kṣemāya', 'nārāyaṇakalātmakam', 'śreyāṃsi tatra khalu sattvatanornṛṇāṃ syuḥ ityādi | tad bahuśaḥ śrutaṃ vapuḥ | svacchatvāt sphaṭikavadatinirmalatayā | acchāditā anupahitā yā parasukhacit cidānandaikarasaṃ brahma, sā pratibimbataḥ praviṣṭā garbhe yasya, tadacchāditaparasukhacidgarbham ata eva nirbhasarūpaṃ ca | vapurviśeṣaṇametat | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -- śuddhasattvamayasya vapuṣo'tisvacchatayā tatpraviṣṭayā parānandacidā tannirbhāsyāṃśasya jāḍyasya tiraskṛtatvāt parasukhacidekatāmāpannamiti | taduktamamṛtataraṅgiṇyāṃ -- tvadvapuratisvacchatayā svasmin pratiphalatā cidānandaikarasena brahmaṇā tiraskṛtajāḍyaṃ cidānandaikarasameva bhavatīti | svacchatvādacchāditaparasukhacidgarbhanirbhāsarūpaṃ tadvapuryaditi sambandhaḥ | tasminniti ‘sa vā ayaṃ brahma mahadvimṛgyam', 'kṛṣṇastu bhagavān svayam' ityādibhiḥ paracidabhedena pratipādite vapuṣītyarthaḥ | dhanyāḥ abhyupagatabhaktimārgatayetaramārgoktabahusādhanapariśramarahitāḥ  | ramante premalakṣaṇāṃ bhaktiṃ kurvantītyarthaḥ | atra hetumāha -- śrutimatimadhura iti | mādhuryayuktatvācchravaṇasmaraṇādiṣu sakalendriyāhlādaka ityarthaḥ | ata eva sugrahe sukhagrāhye vigrahe mūrttau || 3 ||
evaṃ bhagavataḥ śuddhasattvamayī mūrtimupapādya tasyā niṣkalabrahmābhedamupapādayati -- 
niṣkampe nityapūrṇa niravadhiparamānandapīyūṣarūpe
nirlīnānekamuktāvalisubhagatame nirmalabrahmasindhau |
kallolollāsatulyaṃ khalu vimalataraṃ sattvamāhustadātmā
kasmānno niṣkalastvaṃ sakala iti vacastvatkalāsveva bhūman! || 4 ||
niṣkampa iti | tatra brahmaṇaḥ sindhusāmyamāpādayati caturbhirviśeṣaṇaiḥ | niṣkampe nirvikāre, anyatra akṣobhye gambhīre ityarthaḥ | nityapūrṇe akhaṇḍe, anyatra varṣāsu prabhūtanānānadījalaughe sati grīṣme tasminnasati caikarūpe | niravadhiḥ kālato deśata upādhitaścāparicchinnaḥ paramānanda eva pīyūṣaṃ muktairāsvādanīyatvāt, tadrūpe | anyatra niravadhiḥ paramānandaḥ ratrakcandanavanitādisukhātiśāyi sukhaṃ yasmāt, tasya pīyūṣasya rūpaṃ prakāśa utpattiryasmāt, sa tathā | nilīṃnānāmapunarāvṛttiṃ gatānāmanekeṣāṃ muktānāṃ brahmasāyujyaṃ prāptānāmāvaliḥ samūhaḥ, tena subhagatame atimanohare | anyeṣāmapi mumukṣājanaka iti bhāvaḥ | anyatra muktāvalibhiḥ muktāratnanikaraiḥ subhagatame śobhite | nirmalabrahmāsindhāviti | brahmaṇo nirmalatvaṃ māyātatkāryoparāgarāhityam | sindhau tvanāvilatvam | evambhūte brahmasindhau yaḥ kallolollāsaḥ kallolānāṃ taraṅgāṇāmullāsastena tulyam | vimalataraṃ śuddhaṃ sattvamityarthaḥ | ayamabhiprāyaḥ -- 
yathā taraṅgāṇāṃ kallolādiśabdavācyatvavyatirekeṇa sindhormedaṃ , evaṃ śuddhasattvasyāpi brahmaṇo'bheda iti | atra pramāṇamāha -- āhuriti | ācāryā iti śeṣaḥ |
te hyāhuḥ -- athavā cidānandajalanighereko'bhinavastaraṅga eva sattvam ityādi | nanvevaṃ śuddhasattvasya brahmānanyatve bhagavanmūrtaḥ kimāyātaṃ, tatrāha -- tadātmeti | tat sattvamevātmā śrīmūrttiryasya saḥ | tvaṃ kasmānno niṣkalaḥ niṣkalaṃ brahmaiva tvamityuktiyuktimatītyarthaḥ | nanvevaṃ tarhi sakalamūrtiśabdo'nanyagaternirālambaḥ syādityāśaṅkayāha -- sakala iti | tvatkalāsu aṃśāvatāreveva sakalaśabdaḥ prayujyata iti śeṣaḥ | he bhūman ! paripūrṇa ! tvaṃ tu niṣkalabrahmaivetyarthaḥ || 4 ||
evaṃ bhagavanmūrteḥ svarūpotpattiṃ vimṛśya samprati tasyā utpattiṃ vimṛśati -- 
nirvyāpāro'pi niṣkāraṇamaja! bhajase yat kriyāmīkṣaṇākhyāṃ
tenaivodeti līnā prakṛtirasatikalpāpi kalpādikāle |
tasyāḥ saṃśuddhamaṃśa kamapi tamatirodhāyakaṃ sattvarūpaṃ
sa tvaṃ dhṛtvā dadhāsi svamahimavibhavākuṇṭha ! vaikuṇṭha ! rūpam || 5 ||
nirvyāpāra iti | he aja! viṣṇo ! tvaṃ nirvyāpāro niṣkriyaḥ sannapi īkṣaṇākhyāṃ māyāpreraṇātmikāṃ kriyāṃ bhajase aṅgīkaropi | yathā ayaskāntamaṇirniṣkriyo'pi sannidhimātreṇa lohadhātumākarṣati, tadvadityarthaḥ | niṣkāraṇaṃ nirgataṃ kāraṇaṃ prayojanaṃ yasmāditi kriyāviśeṣaṇam | tava māyāpreraṇe kāraṇamasmadādibhirna jñātavyamiti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ tvamīkṣaṇākhyāṃ kriyāṃ bhajasa iti yat, tenaiva kāraṇena līnā dviparārdhāvasāne tvayi layaṃ prāptā prakṛtirmāyā udeti prakāśate | atra līnodetiśabdau prakṛterutpattivināśābhāvaṃ sūcayata iti na tasyā anāditvahāniḥ | kadodetītyata āha -- kalpādikāla iti | kalpānāṃ brahmaṇo dinānāmādikāle ādyadinārambhakāle brahmapralayāvasāna ityarthaḥ | na tu kalpasyādikāle, tadā prakṛtilayodayābhāvāt | māyāṃ viśinaṣṭi -- asatikalpeti | prāyeṇāsatī sadasattvābhyāmanirvacanīyetyarthaḥ | he vaikuṇṭha ! vikuṇṭhanilaya ! saḥ māyāprerakastvaṃ tasyā māyāyāḥ taṃ pūrvoktaṃ sattvarūpaṃ kamapyaṃśaṃ dhṛtvā uddhṛtya rūpaṃ līlāvigrahaṃ dadhāsi bhaktānugrahāya gṛhṇāsi | nanu vigrahavāṃścat saṃsāraprasaṅga ityāśaṅkayāha -- atirodhāyakamiti | tatra hetumāha -- saṃśuddhamiti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -- śuddhasattvarūpasya māyāṃ -- 
śasyātisvacchatayā caitanyaparicchedakatvābhāvāt tanmaye līlāvigrahe'haṃmamābhimānāsambhavāt tannibandhanasya saṃsārasyāpi bhagavatyasambhava eveti | nanu śarīrasambandhe samāne'pyekaḥ saṃsaratyanyaḥ saṃsārayatīti kuta etad vaiṣamyamiti śaṅkāṃ pariharan bhagavantaṃ sambodhayati -- svamahimavibhavākuṇṭheti | svasya mahimā yogaiśvarye, sa eva vibhavaḥ śaktiḥ, tenākuṇṭhaḥ apratihatacicchaktirityarthaḥ | ayamāśayaḥ -- jīvo māyāparatantraḥ saṃsarati, īśvarastu svādhīnayā māyayāṅgīkṛtalīlāvigrahaḥ san sargādilīlāḥ karotīti mahadidaṃ vaiṣamyam || 5 ||
samprati bhagavanmūrteḥ saundaryaṃ varṇayati dvābhyāṃ -- 
tatte pratyagradhārādharalalitakalāyāvalīkelikāraṃ
lāvaṇyasyaikasāraṃ sukṛtijanadṛśāṃ pūrṇapuṇyāvatāram |
lakṣmīniśśaṅkalīlānilayanamamṛtasyandasandohamantaḥ
siñcat sañcintakānāṃ vapuranukalaye mārutāgāranātha ! || 6 ||
taditi| he mārutāgāranātha! te tat śuddhasattvamayaṃ vapuḥ anukalaye smarāmītyarthaḥ | pratyagreti sajalajaladharavat pratinavakalāyakusumavaccātimanoharaṃ, śyāmalamityarthaḥ | lāvaṇyasyaikasāramiti lāvaṇyadravyasya kevalasārāṃśenaiva viracitamityarthaḥ | sukṛtijanadṛśāṃ pūrṇapuṇyāvatāramiti | atra phalābhisandhirahitāni vihitasakalakarmāṇi sukṛtānyucyante, tadanuṣṭhātāraḥ sukṛtijanāḥ, teṣāṃ dṛśāṃ pūrṇasya niśśeṣasya puṇyasyāvatāraḥ (taṃ), sukṛtijanānāṃ cakṣurindriyairanubhavanīyaṃ puṇyapūrameva bhagavanmūrtyākāreṇa pariṇatamityarthaḥ | apica lakṣmyāḥ premapratipnattivisrambhādyekaviṣayatayā niśśaṅkalīlānilayanam | kiñca sañcintakānām upāsakānām antaḥ hṛdaye amṛtasyandasandohaṃ brahmānandāmṛtadravajharīṃ siñcad, upāsakānāṃ hṛdayaṃ brahmānandāmṛtadraveṇāplāvya sakalasantāpaṃ śamayatītyarthaḥ || 6 ||
kaṣṭā te sṛṣṭiceṣṭā bahutarabhavakhedāvahā jīvabhājāmityevaṃ pūrvamālocitamajita ! mayā naivamadyābhijāne |
no cejjīvāḥ kathaṃ vā madhurataramidaṃ tvadvapukṣcidrasārdre netraiḥ śrotraiśca pītvā paramarasasudhāmbhodhipūre rameran || 7 ||
kaṣṭeti | he ajita! māyāpāratantryarahita ! te tava sṛṣṭiceṣṭā kaṣṭā | kuta ityata āha -- jīvabhājāṃ bahutarabhavakhedāvahoti | atra jīvaśabdena liṅgaśarīramucyate, tad bhajantīti jīvabhājo jīvā eva, teṣām | bhavakhedā jananamaraṇaduḥkhāni, tānāvahatīti tathā | ityevaṃ mayā mūḍhamatinā pūrvamālocitaṃ cintitam | tattu tathā netyāha -- naivamadhābhijāna iti | idānīṃ tvadrūpāsvādanavyāpṛte manasi mahānupakāra evāsmāpakamiti niścinomītyarthaḥ | niścitamāha -- no cediti| yadi tvaṃ tvayyanuśayitān jīvān tadbhogyaṃ prapañcaṃ ca na sṛjasi, tarhi jīvāḥ kathaṃ kena prakāreṇa cidrasāīṃ cidānandāmṛtadraveṇāntarbahiśca vyāptaṃ tvadvapuḥ netraiḥ śrotraiśca pītvā taddarśanatadupāsanatatkathāśravaṇadvāreṇa hṛdaye kṛtvā paramarasasudhāmbhodhipūre 9paramānandāmṛtāmbhodhau rameran unmajjananimajjanābhyukṣaṇāsvādanādikriyāṃ kuryurityarthaḥ || 7 ||
evaṃ bhagavataḥ saundaryaguṇasya lokottaratvamuktvā phaladātṛtvasyāpi lokottaratvaṃ varṇayati -- 
namrāṇāṃ sannidhatte satatamapi purastairanabhyarthitānapyarthān kāmānajasraṃ vitarati paramānandasāndrāṃ gatiṃ ca |
itthaṃ niśśeṣalabhyo niravadhikaphalaḥ pārijāto hare ! tvaṃ kṣudraṃ taṃ śakravāṭīdrumamabhilapati vyarthamarthivajo'yam || 8 ||
namrāṇāmiti | loke hi pārijāto'bhīṣṭaphalada iti bhagavatastadatiśāyiguṇānāha -- he hare ! tvaṃ pārijātaḥ tvadātmakaḥ kalpavṛkṣaḥ namrāṇāṃ namaskāramātreṇāpi bhajatāṃ puraḥ purataḥ satatamapi sannidhatte varaditsayā prādurbhavati | anyastu  kalpavṛkṣaḥ svayaṃ na sannidhatte, na ca namaskāramātreṇa, na ca satataṃ, kintu khapustake tu 'kāruṇyād' ityādinavamakṣloko'ṣṭamatvena,'namrāṇām' ityādyaṣṭamakṣloko navamatvena ca vyākhyāyate |
svakarmabhirdevān prasādya svargaṃ gatānāṃ purata eva sannidhatte | kiñca, ajasram ihāmutra ca tairbhaktaiḥ anabhyarthitān kāmān abhīṣṭān arthān puruṣārthān vitarati
dadāti| anyastvanabhyarthitān na dadāti, na ca sarvān kāmān, na cājasraṃ, kintu amutraivābhyarthiteṣu kāṃścidevārthān dadāti | apica akāmanayā ye sevante, tebhyaḥ paramānandasāndrāṃ gatiṃ mokṣaṃ ca dadāti | anyastu sakāmebhyo'kāmebhyo vā mokṣaṃ na dadāti | evaṃ niśśeṣalabhyaḥ sakalajīvanivahaiḥ prāpyastvadātmakaḥ pārijātaḥ niravadhikaphalaḥ aparicchinnamokṣākhyaphalapradaḥ | anyastu paricchinnaphalapradaḥ | evaṃ tvadātmake pārijāte tiṣṭhati ayamarthivrajaḥ kāmisamūhaḥ vyarthaṃ vṛthaiva kṣudram asāraṃ taṃ mudhā pārijātaśabdavācyaṃ śakravāṭīdrumam indrodyānavṛkṣam abhilaṣati bahukleśena prāptumicchatītyarthaḥ || 8 ||
nanu brahmarudrādayo'pīśvaratvasāmyād bhaktābhīṣṭaphaladāḥ | ataste'pi sevyā ityāśaṅkaya bhagavatastebhyo viśeṣamāha -- 
kāruṇyāt kāmamanyaṃ dadati khalu pare svātmadastvaṃ viśeṣādaiśvaryādīśate'nye jagati parajane svātmano'pīśvarastvam |
tvayyuccairāramanti pratipadamadhure cetanāḥ sphītabhāgyāstvaṃ cātmārāma evetvatulaguṇagaṇādhāra! śaure ! namaste || 9 ||
kāruṇyāditi | pare brahmādayaḥ | kāruṇyād bhaktavatsalatayā | anyaṃ mokṣāvyatiriktaṃ kāmaṃ varaṃ dadati | tvaṃ viśeṣād viśiṣṭād brahmādyatiśayitāt kāruṇyāt svātmadaḥ svātmānaṃ svarūpaṃ mokṣamapi dadāsītyarthaḥ | kiñca, anye brahmādayaḥ aiśvaryād jagati asmin prapañce parajane svavyatirikteṣu sthiracareṣveva īśate nigrahītumanugrahītuṃ vā śaktā bhavanti | tvaṃ tu svātmanaḥ svarūpasya brahmaṇo'pīśvaraḥ | svātmānameva jīvarūpeṇa saṃsārārṇave nikṣipya nigrahītuṃ, tatsamakālameva svānandānubhavapade (saṃ)sthāpyānugrahītumapi śakto bhavasītyarthaḥ | nanu sevakānāṃ svātmadāne kiṃ phalamityāśaṅkaya bhagavataḥ svasthaiva phalarūpatāṃ darśayati -- tvayīti | tvāyi brahmaṇi| sphītabhāgyāḥ janmajanmāntarārjitapuṇyasañcayenādhigatabhaktijñānamārgāḥ cetanā jīvāḥ
uccairāramanti atiśayena modante | cātmārāma eva, castvarthaḥ, tvaṃ tu tvayyeva ramase, tava cānyeṣāṃ ca tvameva niratiśayasukhabhūta ityarthaḥ | iti ato hetoḥ atulānāṃ lokottarāṇāṃ guṇānāmādhāra ! āśrayabhūta ! he śaure ! śrīkṛṣṇa ! te tubhyaṃ namaḥ namaskaromītyarthaḥ || 9 ||
ṣaḍguṇāśrayatāyā apyasādhāraṇatāmāha -- 
aiśvaryaṃ śaṅkarādīśvarāviniyamanaṃ viśvatejoharāṇāṃ
tejassaṃhāri vīrya vimalamapi yaśo nispṛhaikṣcopagītam |
aṅgāsaṅgā sadā śrīrakhilavidasi na kkāpi te saṅgavārtā
tad vātāgāravāsin! murahara! bhagavacchabdamukhyāśrayo'si ||10||
aiśvaryamiti | he vātāgāravāsin ! murahara! te tavaiśvarya śaṅkarādīnāmīśvarāṇāṃ viniyamanaṃ tattadadhikārapravartakam | kiñca, tava vīryaṃ viśveṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ tejaḥ parākramaṃ harantīti viśvatejoharāḥ śrīśaṅkarādayaḥ, teṣāṃ tejaḥ prabhāvaṃ saṃhartuṃ śīlamasyeti tathā | vakṣyati ca 'muhustāvacchakram' ityādi | tava yaśo'pi vimalaṃ pāpaśodhakam | nispṛhairmuktairapyupagītaṃ, tvaccaritākṛṣṭacetastayā tairvarṇitamityarthaḥ | śrīśca sadā aṅgāsaṅgā tava vakṣaḥsthalamāśrityaivāste | akhilavidasīti | akhilaṃ svamātmānaṃ svamāyāṃ tatkāryaṃ ca vettītyakhilavidasi tvam | anye tvāṃ tattvato na jānantīti bhāvaḥ | kvāpi viṣaye | te saṅgasya vārtāpi kathāpi na śrūyata ityarthaḥ | tat tasmāt, yasmādaiśvaryayaśaḥ śrīvīryajñānāsaṅgatānāṃ bhagavacchabdavācyānāṃ tvayyatiśayena vṛttistasmādityarthaḥ | bhagavacchabdasya mukhyāśrayo vācyārtho bhavasi, anyeṣu bhagavacchabdasya lākṣaṇikī vṛttirityarthaḥ || 10 ||
iti bhagavanmahimānuvarṇanaṃ prathamaṃ daśakam
evaṃ bhagavadguṇaprastāvena jhaṭiti hṛdayadeśasamuditaparacidrasārdrabhagavadrūpārdrahṛdayatayā romāñcitaśarīrasya maulau baddhāñjalerbāṣpasaṃruddhavākprasarasya kavermukhataḥ sagadgadāni kānicit padāni nirgacchanti -- 
sūryaspardhikirīṭamūrdhvatilakaprodbhdāsiphālāntaraṃ
kāruṇyākulanetra mārdrahasitollāsaṃ sunāsāpuṭam |
gaṇḍodyanmakarābhakuṇḍalayugaṃ kaṇṭhojjvalatkaustubhaṃ
tvadrūpaṃ vanamālyahārapaṭalaśrīvatsadīpraṃ bhaje || 1 ||
sūryaspardhīti | ekīkṛtānekāditya bimbavadatyujjvalakana kakirīṭayuktam | ūrdhvatilaketi | ūrdhvāgravinyastakastūrītilakollasitaṃphāladeśam | kāruṇyākulanetraṃ bhaktajanaviṣayakaruṇā rasārdratayā muhurunmīlananimīlana vyāpṛtanayananalinayugalam | ārdrahasitollāsaṃ premārdratayātyullasitamandasmitayuktam | sunāsāpuṭaṃ suvarṇatilakusumavadatisundaraśrīnāsikodarayuktam | gaṇḍodyanmakarābhakuṇḍalayugaṃ gaṇḍamaṇḍalāvalambamānama kararekhāṅkitamaṇikuṇḍalayugmam | kaṇṭhojjvalatkaustubhaṃ śrīmatkaṇṭhadeśabhāsamānakaustubharatnam | vanamālyahārapaṭalaśrīvatsadīpraṃ vanamālāmuktāhāraśrīvatsaiḥ dīpraṃ dīpanaśīlam | tvadrūpaṃ bhaje samprati smarāmītyarthaḥ || 1 ||
keyūrāṅgadakaṅkaṇottamamahāratnāgulīyāṅkitaśrīmadbhāhucatuṣkasaṅgatagadāśaṅkhāripaṅkeruhām |
kāñcit kāñcanakāñcilāñchitalasatpītāmbarālambinīmālambe vimalāmbujayutipadāṃ mūrti tavārticchidam || 2 ||
keyūreti | aṃsāvalambikeyūrākhyābharaṇaviśeṣāṇāṃ tadadhastād dhṛtānāmaṅgadānāṃ prakoṣṭhasthānāṃ kaṅkaṇānāṃ śiñjadvalayānāṃ cottamaiḥ śreṣṭhaiḥ pratyuptāni mahānti
yasminnardhāryate, yaśca nirdhāryate, yaśca nirdhāraṇahetuḥ, etattritayasyāsānnidhānād 'na nirdhāraṇa' iti na niṣedha iti vyākhyāturāśayaḥ,
parārdhyāni ratnāni yeṣu, tairaṅgulīyai(ścā) ṅkitenālaṅkṛtena śrīmatā śobhamānenājānulambinā pīvareṇa bāhucatuṣkeṇa saṅgatāni gṛhatiāni gadāśaṅkhāripaṅkeruhāṇi yasyāṃ sā tathā, tām | kāñcanakāñci lāñchiteti | kāñcanakāñcyā uparivinyastayā lāñchitaṃ sañjātaparabhāgaṃ svataḥ samullasatpītāmbaramālambituṃ paridhātuṃ śīlamasyā iti tathā, tām | vimalāmbujadyutipadāṃ pratinavāruṇasarojavad virājamānapadayugalām | bhaktānāṃ hṛdi sanniviṣṭā satī teṣāmādhyātmikādyārti pīḍāṃ chinattītyārticchit, tām | kāñcid brahmādibhiradhyaśakyavarṇanasaubhāgyām | tava mūrtimālambe āśrayāmi, ūrdhvamukhīkṛtonnidrahṛdayakamalakarṇikāyāṃ nyasyopāsmaha ityarthaḥ || 2 ||
samprati bhagavanmūrteḥ saubhāgyaṃ varṇayati -- 
yat trailokyamahīyaso'pi mahitaṃ sammohanaṃ mohanāt
kāntaṃ kāntinidhānato'pi madhuraṃ mādhuryadhuryādapi |
saundaryottarato'pi sundarataraṃ tvadrūpamākṣcaryato’pyāścarya bhuvane na kasya kutukaṃ puṣṇāti viṣṇo ! vibho ! || 3 ||
yaditi | trailokye'smin jagati yad mahīyaḥ atiśayena mahat pūjitam utkṛṣṭaṃ vastu, tasmādapi mahitam atyutkṛṣṭaṃ yat | yacca trailokye mohanaṃ vastu, tasmādapyatimohanamāhlādakam | yacca kāntinidhānataḥ śobhāpātrībhūtād vastuno'pi kāntamatiśobhanaṃ sakalendriyākarṣaṇasiddhauṣadham | yacca mādhuryadhuryād mādhuryakāryasamarthād vastuno'pi madhuramatimādhuryayuktaṃ santāpaśāntikaram | yacca saundaryeṇottaramutkṛṣṭamatisundaraṃ ca yadvastu, tasmādapi sundarataram | yaccāścaryam adbhutarasālambanabhūtaṃ vastu, tasmādapyāścaryataraṃ ca | he vibho ! saṃsārārṇavottāraṇasamartha ! he viṣṇo! tādṛśaṃ tvadrūpaṃ bhuvane kasya kutukaṃ na puṣṇāti, baddhamuktamumukṣujanānāmapyatiśayena śravaṇasmaraṇādyautsukyaṃ dadātyevetyarthaḥ || 3 ||
tattādṛṅmadhurātmakaṃ tava vapuḥ sammāpya sampanmayī
sā devī paramotsukā cirataraṃ nāste svabhakteṣvapi |
tenāsyā bata kaṣṭamacyuta ! vibho ! tvadrūpamānojñakapremasthairyamayādacāpalabalāccāpalyavārtodabhūt || 4 ||
taditi | tadeva tādṛk tatsadṛśaṃ yasya tat tattādṛg, anupamamityarthaḥ | tādṛśamādhuryayukta ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya tat taba vapuḥ samprāpya vakṣasyadhivāsaṃ labdhvā sā devī mahālakṣmīḥ svabhakteṣvapi sampanmayī dhanadhānyādirūpiṇī bhūtvā cirataraṃ cirakālaṃ nāste cañcalaiva bhavati | atra hetumāha -- paramotsuketi, tvadvapuḥsaubhāgyātiśayenāpahṛtahṛdayatayetaratra gantumaśakyatvādityarthaḥ | tena yasmādevaṃ tasmād he acyuta! vibho ! asyāstava mahiṣyāḥ tvadrūpamānojñakapremasthairyamayāt tvadrūpasya mānojñake saubhāgye yat premṇaḥ sthairya tanmayāt tadrūpād acāpalasya cāpalyābhāvasya balādeva cāpalyavārtā capaleyamiti vārtā duṣkīrtirudabhūd udbhūtā| kaṣṭaṃ bateti khede | guṇa eva doṣaheturjāta iti khedahetuḥ | kiñca , tvatsaubhāgyātiśayenodbhūtā tvatpriyatamāyā duṣkīrtiḥ tvāmapi spṛśatīti mama kheda iti bhāvaḥ ||
nanu asyāḥ sevakajanasevākādācitkatayā cāpalyaṃ jātaṃ, kimatra mayā kartavyamityāśaṅkāṃ pariharati -- 
lakṣmīstāvakarāmaṇīyakahataivayaṃ pareṣvasthiretyasminnanyadapi pramāṇamadhunā vakṣyāmi lakṣmīpate ! |
ye tvaddhyānaguṇānukīrtanarasāsaktā hi bhaktā janāsteṣveṣā vasati sthiraiva dayitaprastāvadattādarā || 5||
lakṣmīriti | iyaṃ lakṣmīḥ tāvakaṃ  tvadīyaṃ yad rāmaṇīyakaṃ saubhāgyaṃ, tena hṛtaivāpahṛtahṛdayatayaiva pareṣu tvadanyeṣu svabhaktepvapyasthirā capaletyasminnarthe naitihyamātraṃ pramāṇaṃ, kintu anyat pramāṇamanumānamapyadhunā vakṣyāmi | pūrvaṃ lakṣmyā udbhūtayā cāpalyavārtayā tvatsaubhāgyāpahṛtatayeyaṃ parepvasthiretyayamartho'numitaḥ, tatra ca tvayā sevakajanasevākādācitkatvamupādhiḥ śaṅkitaḥ, ato'nyadanumānāntaraṃ vakṣyāmītyarthaḥ | prayogaśca -- lakṣmīstvatsaubhāgyāpahṛtatayā parepvasthirā, tvaddhyānaguṇānukīrtanarasāsakteṣu tvadbhakteṣu prastutatvaccaritadttādaratayā sthiravāsatvād, yā evaṃ, tā evaṃ, yathā tvaddhyānaguṇānukīrtanarasāsakteṣu tvadbhakteṣu dayitaprastāvadattādaratayā sthiravāsā gopyaḥ tvatsaubhāgyāpahṛtahṛdayatayā pareṣu svabhartṛṣvapyasthirā iti | kṣlokaśca vyākhyātaprāyaḥ || 5 ||
evaṃ trayodaśabhiḥ kṣlokairadhyāyatrayoktamupāsyaṃ bhagavadrūpaṃ varṇitam | samprati sevāsādhanasya bhagavadbhakterutpattiṃ darśayati -- 
evambhūtamanojñatānavasudhāniṣyandasandohanaṃ
tvadrūpaṃ paracidrasāyanamayaṃ cetoharaṃ śṛṇvatām |
sadyaḥ prerayate matiṃ madayate romāñcayatyaṅgakaṃ
vyāsiñcatyapi śītabāppavisarairānandamūrchodbhavaiḥ || 6 ||
evamiti| evambhūtā pūrvoktā yā manojñatā saubhāgyaṃ, saiva navasudhānipyandaḥ pratinavamadhudravaḥ, tasya sandohanaṃ varṣakaṃ paracidrasāyanamayaṃ parabrahmamayaṃ cetoharaṃ smaratāṃ punaḥpunaḥ smārakaṃ tvadrūpaṃ śṛṇvatāmiti | ayamabhiprāyaḥ -- yadṛcchayā tvatkathāyāṃ jātaśraddhasya puṃsaḥ kathāprasaṅge sati śrotradvārā tvadrūpaṃ hṛdayadeśamāgatyāsya sakalasantāpaṃ śamayatīti | tatprakāramāha -- sadya iti | sagho jhaṭiti matiṃ prerayate ākarṣati; viṣayebhyo mati balādākṛṣya svasmin sannidhāpayati | madayate ānandavivaśatāmāpādayati | kiñca, aṅgakam aṅgaṃ romāñcayati | apica, ānandamūrchodbhavaiḥ tvadrūpānubhavānandasya mūrchā vṛddhiḥ, tatsakāśādudbhavo yeṣāṃ taiḥ śītairbāṣpavisaraiḥ aśruprasarairyāsiñcati | anena ceśvare premaprakarṣaṇāhaitukena sakalendriyāṇāṃ vṛttiḥ premalakṣaṇā bhaktiḥ, sā ca tvatkathāśravaṇanāmakīrtanādilakṣaṇayā sādhanabhaktyotpadyata ityapyuktaṃ bhavati || 6||
anantaraṃ bhakterutkarṣaṃ varṇayati caturbhiḥ -- 
evambhūtatayā hi bhaktyabhihito yogaḥ sa yogadvayāta
karmajñānamayād bhṛśottamataro yogīśvarairgīyate |
saundaryaikarasātmake tvayi khalu premaprakarṣātmikā
bhaktirniḥśramameva viśvapuruṣairlabhyā ramāvallabhaḥ ! || 7 ||
evamiti | sa prahlādādibhirapyaṅgīkṛtaḥ bhaktirityabhihito yogo mokṣopāyaḥ evambhūtatayā uktaprakāratayeti | yaḥ kathāśravaṇādināyatnenotpadyate, utpanne sati ca hṛdayamānandayati, tādṛśatayetyarthaḥ | karmajñānamayāt karmamayād jñānamayācca yoga
dvayād bhṛśam atiśayena uttamataraḥ atyutkṛṣṭaḥ | atra pramāṇamāha -- yogīkṣvarairiti, vyāsanāradādibhiḥ purāṇeṣu gīyate varṇyata ityarthaḥ | kiñca, he ramāvallabha ! tvayi premṇo yaḥ prakarṣo'tiśayaḥ, tadātmikā tatsvarūpā bhaktiḥ niḥśramam anāyāsaṃ yathā bhavati tathā viśvapuruṣaiḥ sakalajanaiḥ labhyā khalu, nātra sandeha ityarthaḥ | tatra hetumāha -- saundaryaikarasātmaka iti | saundaryamekameva rasa ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya sa tathā, tasminnityarthaḥ || 7 ||
niṣkāmaṃ niyatasvadharmacaraṇaṃ yat karmayogābhidhaṃ
tad dūretyaphalaṃ yadaupaniṣadajñānopalabhyaṃ punaḥ |
tat tvavyaktatayā sudurgamataraṃ cittasya tasmād vibho !
tvatpremātmakabhaktireva satataṃ svādīyasī śreyasī || 8 ||
niṣkāmamiti | karmayoga ityabhidhā nāma yasya, tat karmayogābhidham | nirgataḥ kāmaḥ phalābhisandhiryasmāt tanniṣkāmaṃ, tādṛśam | niyatasya nityanaimittikādeḥ svadharmasya vihitasya caraṇam anuṣṭhānaṃ yat, tad dūretyaphalaṃ  dūretyaṃ dūrabhavaṃ phalaṃ yasya, kālāntare phalapradam | aupaniṣadam upaniṣatpratipādyaṃ brahma, tasya jñānenopalabhyaṃ mokṣākhyaṃ phalaṃ yat tat punaravya kttatayā karaṇāgocaratayā cittasya sudurgamataraṃ kathañcanāpi prāptumaśakyaṃ nirastasamastaiṣaṇānāṃ saṃnyastasamastakarmaṇāmeva tatrādhikārāt | he vibho ! karmajñānabhaktimārganiṣṭhānāmapyabhīṣṭaphalapradānasamartha ! tasmāt tvayi premātmikā bhaktireva satatam utpattau utpannāyāṃ phalaprāptau ca svādīyasī atiśayenāsvādanīyā śreyasī brahmādibhirapi praśasyatarā cetyarthaḥ || 8||
atyāyāsakarāṇi karmapaṭalānyācarya niryanmalā
bodhe bhaktipathe'thavāpyucitatāmāyānti kiṃ tāvatā |
kliṣṭvā tarkapathe paraṃ tava vapurbrahmākhyamanye punakṣcittārdratvamṛte vicintya bahubhiḥ sidhyanti janmāntaraiḥ || 9 ||
atīti | atyāyāsakarāṇi bahusādhanapariśramapūrvaka manuṣṭheyāni karmaṇāṃ paṭalāni nityanaimittikaprāyaścittopāsanā rūpa bahuvidhakarmasamūhān ācarya anuṣṭhāya
niryanmalā niryanti nirgacchanti rāgadveṣamohamadamātsaryarūpāṇi manomalāni yeṣāṃ ta,
tathā, tādṛśāḥ kecid bodhe jñānamārge bhaktimārge vāpyucitatāmāyānti adhikāraṃ prāpnuvanti | yadi samyagviraktāstarhi jñānayoge'dhikāriṇaḥ, yadi veṣadviraktāstarhi bhaktimārge'dhikāriṇo bhavanti | taduktaṃ ' na nirviṇṇo nātisakto bhaktiyogo'sya siddhida ' iti | tāvatā tāvanmātropayoginā karmaṇā bhaktimārgaprasthitānāmasmākaṃ kiṃ prayojanaṃ, na kimapītyarthaḥ | anye punaścittā rdratvaṃ rāgādirāhityam ṛte vinā viraktammanyāḥ kecit tarkapathe vedāntaśāstre paraṃ kliṣṭvā kleśaikabhājanāni bhūtvā he vibho! tava brahmākhyaṃ vapuḥ svarūpaṃ vicintya vedāntārthavicāropāyabhūtairvividhairnyāyaivicārya bahubhirupāsanāvidhibhiścopāsya bahubhirjanmāntaraiḥ sidhyanti phalaṃ sāghayanti || 9 ||
tarhi bhaktiyoge'pi kimuktadoṣāḥ santi, netyāha -- 
tvadbhaktistu kathārasāmṛtajharīnirmajjanena svayaṃ
sidhyantī vimalapravodhapadavīmakleśatastanvatī |
sadyaḥ siddhikarī jayatyayi vibho ! saivāstu me tvatpadapremaprauḍhirasārdratā drutataraṃ vātālayādhīśvara ! || 10 ||
tvaditi | tvadbhaktistu tathā na bhavatītyarthaḥ | kintu jayati karmajñānamayād yogadvayādutkṛṣṭā bhavati | utkarṣamevāha -- kathāraseti | tvatkathāyāṃ yaḥ śṛṅgārādirūpo rasaḥ, athavā kathāśraddhā, sa evāmṛtajharī madhudravaḥ tasmin nitarāṃ majjanena svayaṃ sidhyantīti | ayamarthaḥ -- tvatkathāḥ śṛṇvatāṃ punaḥpunaḥ śuśrūṣā jāyate | tataśca vividhaviṣayatṛṣṇāpaiti | tataścittaśuddhidvārā bhaktiḥ svayamevotpadyata iti | nanūtpannāpi bhaktirajñānakṛtaṃ bandhaṃ na nivartayati, jñānasyaivājñānanāśakatvādityāśaṅkayāha -- vimaleti| akleśataḥ kleśaṃ vinaiva vigataṃ naṣṭaṃ malaṃ śarīrādyahaṃmamābhimānarūpamajñānaṃ yasmāt prabodhāt sa vimalaprabodho brahmajñānaṃ, sa eva padavī mārga upāyo brahmaprāpteḥ, tāṃ tanvatī kurvatīti | ayamāśayaḥ -- bhaktterhi bandhamokṣākhyaphalajanane tvatsvarūpabrahmajñānamavāntaravyāpāramātramiti sā bhaktireva bhagavatyakleśenaiva
brahmajñānamutpādyājñānanivṛttidvārā mokṣamapi dadātīti | kiñca, sadyaḥ asmin janmanyeva siddhiṃ phalaṃ karoti, na jñānamārga iva kālāntare | ayi vibho ! vātālayādhīśvara ! me mama tvadekaśaraṇasya sā kleśarahitatayātiśubhā tvatpadapremaprauḍhirasārdratā tava pādāmbujayoḥ premṇaḥ prauḍhiratiśayo'napāyinī prītiḥ sa eva rasaḥ, āsvādanīyatvāt, tenārdratā vilīnahṛdayatā tādṛśī bhaktireva drutataramevāstu, mama sevāmāndye'pi madekaśaraṇo'yamiti dhiyā mahyaṃ tādṛśīṃ bhaktimevānugṛhāṇetyarthaḥ || 10 ||
iti bhagavadrūpavarṇanaṃ bhagavadbhaktyutpattyādivarṇanaṃ ca
dvitīyaṃ daśakam |
atha bhaktānāṃ svarūpaṃ varṇayati -- 
paṭhanto nāmāni pramadabharasindhau nipatitāḥ
smaranto rūpaṃ te varada ! kathayanto guṇakathāḥ |
caranto ye bhaktāstvayi khalu ramante paramamūnahaṃ dhanyān manye samadhigatasarvābhilaṣitān || 1 ||
paṭhanta iti | ye bhaktāḥ tvayi khalu tvayīśvara eva ramante, nāntarāntarā viṣayeṣvapi, tānamūn paraṃ kevalam ahaṃ dhanyān bhāgyavato manye | ramaṇaprakāramāha -- paṭhanta iti | te nāmāni paṭhantaḥ uccaiḥ kīrtayantaḥ, he varada ! bhaktābhīṣṭapra da! kīrtanadvārā hṛdi praviṣṭaṃ tava rūpaṃ smarantaḥ, ata eva pramadabharasindhau ānandātiśayarūpe samudre nipatitāḥ tādṛśānandānubhāvairharṣāśruromāñcādibhiścopalakṣitāḥ, guṇakathāḥ bhaktavātsalyādīn guṇān atyadbhutāni caritāni cānyonyaṃ kathayantaḥ, gṛhādyanapekṣayā carantaḥ | evambhūtānāṃ manaḥ punastvatpādāravindāccālayituṃ pārameṣṭhayādipadamapyakiñcitkaramityāha -- samadhigateti | samadhigaptāni samprāptāni sarvāṇyabhilaṣitānyabhīṣṭāni yeṣāṃ, te tathā | tvatpādāravindasya sakalapuruṣārtharūpatayā tat prāptāḥ punaraparaṃ na kāṅkṣantītyarthaḥ || 1 ||
nanu kimarthaṃ bhaktān stauṣi, tvamapi nāmāni paṭhan, guṇakathāśca kathayan, madrūpaṃ ca smaran teṣāmanyatamo bhavetyāśaṅkāyāmahaṃ tāvat tān bhaktānanukartumasamartha ityāha -- 
gadakliṣṭaṃ kaṣṭaṃ tava caraṇasevārasabhare'pyanāsaktaṃ cittaṃ bhavati vata viṣṇo ! kuru dayām |
bhavatpādāmbhojasmaraṇarasiko nāmanivahānahaṃ gāyaṃgāyaṃ kuhacana vivatsyāmi vijane || 2 ||
gadeti | bāhyairvātādibhirāntarai rāgādibhiśca gadairvyādhibhiḥ kliṣṭaṃ sañjātakleśaṃ madīyaṃ cittaṃ tava caraṇasevārasabhare bhavatpādāmbujopāsanarūpe sukhātiśaye'pyanāsaktaṃ samādhātumaśakyaṃ bhavati, kaṣṭaṃ vata khinno'smi | he viṣṇo ! mayyananyaśaraṇe dayāṃ kuru| ahaṃ tu bhavatpādāmbhojasmaraṇe rasikaḥ yadyahamarogo'bhaviṣyaṃ tarhyasmariṣyamiti sotkaṇṭhaḥ san nāmnāṃ nivahān samūhān gāyaṃgāyaṃ punaḥpunaḥ saṅkīrtayan kuhacana kutrāpi vijane pradeśe vivatsyāmi viśiṣṭaṃ vāsaṃ kariṣyāmi || 2 ||
kṛpā te jātā cet kimiva na hi labhyaṃ tanubhṛtāṃ
madīyakleśaughapraśamanadaśā nāma kiyatī |
na ke ke loke'sminnaniśamayi śokābhirahitā
bhavadbhaktā muktāḥ sukhagatimasaktā vidyate || 3 ||
kṛpeti | ayi viṣṇo! te kṛpā jātā cet, tanubhṛtāṃ strīśūdrādīnāṃ gajamṛgādiśarīrabhājāmapi kimiva hi na labhyaṃ, tiṣṭhantvitare puruṣārthāḥ, tvadaikyaṃ mokṣo'pi teṣāṃ sulabha eva | evaṃ sthite madīyāḥ kleśā vividhā ādhayo vyādhayaśca teṣāmoghasya samūhasya praśamanadaśā samūlonmūlanarūpāvasthā kiyatī atilaghīyasī nāma, yathā jvajvālāvalīḍhatūlarāśeḥ praśamanadaśā, tadvaditi bhāvaḥ | nanvetādṛkkṛpāpātrībhūtā durlabhā iti ced, netyāha -- na ke ka iti | ke ke janā aniśaṃ sarvadā śokābhirahitāḥ nivṛttaśarīrādyahaṃmamābhimānatayā tanmūlādhivyādhikleśavirahitāḥ bhavadbhaktāḥ bhavatīśvare labdhaprematayā nāmāni kīrtayantaścaritāni gāyantaśca muktā jīvanmuktā bhūtvāsmin loke'saktāḥ parānugrahaikaparāḥ sukhagatim abhīṣṭasañcāraṃ na
vidadhate kurvanti | athavā sukhagatiḥ brahmānandaprāptiḥ, tāṃ na vidadhate  | asmiṃlloke tvatkṛpayaiva tvadbhaktā bhūtvā sañcaranto janā bahavaḥ śrūyante | ayi viṣṇo!
mayyapi tādṛśīṃ kṛpāṃ kurviti bhāvaḥ || 3 ||
ke te janā ityata āha -- 
muniprauḍhā rūḍhā jagati khalu gūḍhātmagatayo
bhavatpādāmbhojasmaraṇavirujo nāradamukhāḥ |
carantīśa ! svairaṃ satataparinirbhātaparacitsadānandādvaitamasaraparimagnāḥ kimaparam || 4 ||
munīti | śrīnārado mukhamādiryeṣāṃ te muniprauḍhāḥ muniśreṣṭhāḥ jagati rūḍhāḥ prasiddhāḥ khalu | kintu gūḍhātmagatayaḥ gūḍho'smadādibhiraśakyadarśana ātmā gatiśca yeṣāṃ, te tathā| athavā ātmagatirmanogatiḥ devairapyaviditanigrahānugrahādyabhiprāyaviśeṣāḥ | athavā ātmano gatirjñānam | bhavatpādāmbhojasmaraṇena virujo vigatasakalapīḍāḥ, te khalu jagati svairaṃ caranti | kathamityata āha -- satateti | satataṃ sarvadaiva parinirbhātam anubhavapadavīmārūḍhaṃ paracitsadānandaṃ saccidānandaṃ paraṃ brahma, tadevopādhilayādadvaitaṃ, tasya prasare saṃplave parimagnāḥ tadekībhūtāḥ santaḥ sañcaranti | he īśa ! ataḥ paraṃ kiṃ teṣāṃ prārthanīyamavaśiṣṭam | tadvanmāmapyanugṛhṇīṣveti bhāvaḥ ||
nanu śrīnāradādayo matkṛpayā jñānaniṣṭhāḥ santo vidhūtakleśāḥ  svairaṃ carantīti jñānameva prārthyatām | kimajñānajanitakleśanirasane saṃśayitayā bhaktyetyāśaṅkāṃ pariharan bhakti prārthayate -- 
bhavadbhaktiḥ sphītā bhavatu mama saiva praśamayedaśeṣakleśaughaṃ na khalu hṛdi sandehakaṇikā |
na ced vyāsasyoktistava ca vacanaṃ naigamavaco bhavenmithyā rathyāpuruṣavacanaprāyamakhilam || 5 ||
bhavadbhaktiriti | mama sphītā bhavadbhaktirbhavatu | saiva mamāśeṣakleśaughaṃ praśamayedityatra pramāṇamāha -- na cediti | vyāsasyoktiḥ bhagavadbhaktimāhātmyapratipādakāni purāṇavacanāni, tava ca vacanam arjunamuddhavaṃ ca prati yaduktavānasi, naigamavacaḥ vedavākyāni, yadvā naigamā vedajñāsteṣāṃ vacanam | yadi bhaktiraśeṣakleśaughaṃ na
praśamayati, tarhetat sakalaṃ prāyeṇa rathyāpuruṣavacanavanmithyā apramāṇaṃ bhavediti vipakṣe bādhakastarkaḥ| avaidikāstvevaṃ pralapanti -- vedasmṛtipurāṇavacanāni pramāṇaṃ na bhavanti, vākyatvād, rathyāpuruṣavacanavaditi | nedamanumānaṃ śrutismṛtipurāṇavacanāprāmāṇyāpādakamityāha -- na khalviti | ayamabhiprāyaḥ -- yadyadvākyaṃ tattadapramāṇamiti idamanumānamapi vākyatvādevāpramāṇamiti tairmukto bāṇastāneva vidhyati | kiñca, ācāryairvedasya prāmāṇyaṃ tanmūlatayā smṛtipurāṇavacanānāmapi prāmāṇyaṃ prapañcitam | ataḥ śrutipurāṇavacanādīśvaravacanācca siddhā bhaktermokṣopāyatā || 5 ||
evañca bhaktirevoktamārgeṇa prārthanīyeti vadituṃ tasyā jñānādutkarṣamāha -- 
bhavadbhaktistāvat pramukhamadhurā tvadguṇarasāt
kimapyārūḍhā cedakhilaparitāpapraśamanī |
punakṣcānte svānte viṃmalaparivodhodayamilanmahānandādvaitaṃ diśati kimataḥ prārthyamaparam || 6 ||
bhavaditi | tāvacchabdaḥ kramārthaḥ | bhavadbhaktiḥ pramukhe ādau prathamasannipāte madhurā sukharūpā | kuta ityata āha -- tvadguṇarasāditi | tava guṇeṣu śravaṇasmaraṇādiṣu jātaśraddhatayāhādāt | jñānamārge tu naivaṃ, 'yattadagre viṣamive'ti bhagavadvacanāt | sā tvaguṇarasāt tvatkathāśravaṇaśraddhyaiva kimapi kiñcid ārūḍhā pravṛddhā ced yathābhakti tattvāvabodhasya viṣayavirakterapi sambhavādakhilaparitāpapraśamanī akhilasyādhyātmikādeḥ paritāpasya prakarṣeṇa samūlaṃ śānti kartrī | punarante pariṇāme ca svayaṃ paripoṣaṃ nītāyāṃ satyāṃ svānte hṛdi | vimalaparibrodheti| vigataṃ naṣṭaṃ malaṃ śarīrādyahaṃmamābhimānarūpamajñānaṃ yasmāt paribodhāt sa vimalaparibodho brahmaivedaṃ sarvamiti jñānaṃ, tasyodayena milat sahitaṃ mahānandādvaitaṃ mahad brahmaivānandarūpaṃ nirastasamastopādhikatayādvitīyaṃ diśati | bhaktirevānubhavaparyantaṃ brahmajñānaṃ dattvā
cidānandādvaitavākyarūpaṃ mokṣamapi dadātītyarthaḥ | ataḥ asmānmokṣādaparaṃ jīvaiḥ
kiṃ prārthyaṃ, na kimapītyarthaḥ | athavā ataḥ asyā ādimadhyāvasāne'pi sukharūpāyā bhakteḥ aparaṃ jñānādi ki prārthyaṃ, na prārthanīyamevetyarthaḥ || 6 ||
evaṃ premalakṣaṇāṃ bhaktiṃ stutvā svasya tasyāmanadhikāramanusandadhānastatsādhanarūpāṃ kriyātmikāṃ bhakti prārthayate -- 
vidhūya kleśāna me kuru caraṇayugmaṃ dhṛtarasaṃ
bhavatkṣetraprāptau karamapi ca te pūjanavidhau |
bhavanmṛrtyāloke nayanamatha te pādatulasīparighrāṇe ghrāṇaṃ śravaṇamapi te cārucarite || 7 ||
vidhyeti | kleśān rogān bāhyānābhyantarān vā vidhūya me caraṇayoryugmaṃ bhavatkṣetraprāptau dhṛtarasaṃ sañjātakautukaṃ kuru, anyathā kautuke satyapi tvatkṣetragamanapradakṣiṇāderaśakyatvāt | karaṃ karayugalamapi ca te pūjanasya vidhirvyāpāraḥ tasmin dhṛtarasaṃ kuru | nayanadvayamapi bhavataḥ pūrvoktāyā mūrterāloke darśane dhṛtarasaṃ kuru | atha te tava śrīpāde 'rcitāyāstulasyāḥ parighrāṇe ghrāṇaṃ ghrāṇendriyaṃ dhṛtarasaṃ kuru | śravaṇaṃ śrotrendriyamapi te cāruṇi manohare carite stutau dhṛtarasaṃ kuru || 7 ||
anantaraṃ smaraṇaṃ prārthayate -- 
prabhūtādhivyādhiprasamacalite māmakahṛdi
tvadīyaṃ tatra rūpaṃ paraparasacidrūpamudiyāt |
udañcadromāñco galitavahuharṣānivaho
yathā vismaryāsaṃ durupaśamapīḍāparibhavān || 8 ||
pr𳆫hūteti | prabhūtai rūḍhapadaiḥ ādhibhirāntarairvyādhibhirbāhyaiśca prasabhaṃ balāt calite śarīrādiyogakṣemaikavyāpṭate māmakahṭadi madīye hṛdaye tvadīyaṃ 'sattvaṃ yattad ityārabhya trayodaśabhiḥ kṣlokairvarṇitaṃ paramarasacidrūpaṃ paramarasaḥ paramānandaścāsau cid jñānaṃ ca, tanmayaṃ rūpamudiyāt | yathā taraṇimaṇḍalamudyattimiranikaraṃ dūrīkurvadevodeti, tadvanmamājñānavijṛmbhitādhivyādhijālaṃ samūlamunmūlayadeva mama hṛdi tvadrpamudetviti bhāvaḥ | evañcāhaṃ bhavati premātiśayalābhād udañcadromāñcālaṅṅkṛtaśarīraḥ galitavahuharṣāniyaho vāṣpajalāvasikkasarvāṅgaḥ san durupaśamāḥ śamayitumaśakyā yāḥ pīḍā ādhyātmikādyāḥ, tatkartṛkān paribhavān upadravān yathā samyag vismaryāsaṃ vismaraṇaṃ kuryām | athavā yathā vismaryāsaṃ tathā hṛdi tvadrpamudiyāditi sambandhaḥ || 8 ||
apica -- 
marudrehādhīśa ! tvayi khalu parāñco'pi sukhino
bhavatsnehī so'haṃ subahu paritapye ca kimidam |
akīrtiste mā bhūd varada ! gadabhāraṃ praśamayan
bhavadbhaktottasaṃ jhaṭiti kuru māṃ kaṃsadamana ! || 9 ||
maruditi | marudvehādhīśa ! he śrīguruvāyupuranātha ! tvayīśvare parāñco'pi vimukhā nāstikā api sukhino dṛśyante khalu, anye bhaktāḥ sukhina iti kimucyate | bhavatīśvare sneho'syāstīti bhavatsnehī so'haṃ yaḥ snehe satyapi kleśābhibhūtatayā smaraṇādiṣvasamarthaḥ, so'haṃ subahu adhikaṃ paritavye paritāpamanubhavāmi ca kimidamīśvare'pi vaiṣamyamiva | na ca te'pi janmāntare  bhavantaṃ sevitavantaḥ, ataḥ sukhino bhavanti, kutaścittu kāraṇāt samprati tvayi parāñco jātā iti śakyaṃ śaṅkituṃ, bhavatsevakānāmapracyutiprasiddhibhaṅgaprasaṅgāt | kiñca, ahaṃ duḥkhipvantarbhavāṇi, nātra khedaḥ | tava tvīśvarasyāpi jīveṣu vaiṣamyamastīti duṣpkīrtiḥ syāt | tataśca jīvānāṃ sevāmāndyamapi syādityeva mama kheda ityāha -- akīrtiriti | he varada ! kaṃsadamana! ityābhyāṃ sambodhanābhyāṃ duṣṭanigrahaśiṣṭābhīṣṭadānābhyāmeveśvarasya śarīropādānamiti satkīrtighotyate | tasyāṃ satyāṃ te tava kutaścidakīrtirduṣkīrtirapi mā bhūt | tatropāyamāha -- gadabhāramiti | gadā rogā eva bhāraḥ soḍhumaśakyatvāt taṃ praśamayan māṃ jhaṭiti akīrtivyāpteḥ pūrvamevātiśīghraṃ bhavadbhaktānāmuttaṃsamalaṅkārabhūtaṃ pradhānaṃ kuru | evaṃca tvaṃ cāhaṃ ca kṛtāryau sva ityarthaḥ || 9 ||
evaṃ stutimukhena prārthanāmukhenāṃ ca bhaktebhaktānāṃ ca svarūpaṃ varṇayan prakaraṇamupasaṃharati -- 
kimuktairbhūyobhistava hi karuṇā yāvadudiyādahaṃ tāvad deva ! prahitavividhārtapralapitaḥ |
puraḥ klṛpte pāde varada ! tava neṣyāmi divasān
yathāśakti vyaktaṃ natinutiniṣevā viracayan || 10 ||
kimiti | bhūyobhiruktairbahubhiḥ pralāpaiḥ kiṃ phalaṃ, tava karuṇābhāve na kimapītyarthaḥ| he deva! bhaktānāṃ hṛghudyudyotamāna ! yāvad yasmin kāle tava hi īśvarasyaiva karuṇā kṛpā udiyāt prādurbhavati, tāvat tāvatkālaparyantam ahaṃ prahitaṃ tyaktaṃ vividham uktaprakāreṇa bahuvidham ārtānāṃ pralapitaṃ nirarthakaṃ vacanaṃ yena sa prahitavividhārtapralapitaḥ tathābhūtaḥ san puraḥ purobhāge klṛpte saṅkalpite athavā pratimāsambandhini tava śrīpāde yathāśakti yāvadārogyaṃ vyaktaṃ niścinomīti yāvat | natirnamaskāraḥ nutiḥ stutiḥ niṣevā pūjā, tāḥ viracayan viśeṣeṇa bhaktiśraddhāvṛttisahitaṃ kurvan divasān nepyāmi, mama pralāpena kevalena tvatkṛpā nodayamāsādayati | kiñca, vṛthā kālakṣepaḥ syād, ato yathāśakti tvatsevāṃ kurvan tvatkṛpāṃ ca pratipālayan divasān nayāmītyarthaḥ | evaṃ pañcadaśabhiḥ kṣlokaiḥ parīkṣidākhyānasiddhaṃ bhakterbhaktānāṃ ca svarūpaṃ varṇitam || 10 ||
iti bhaktasvarūpavarṇanaṃ bhaktiprārthanā ca tṛtīyaṃ daśakam |
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyāyāṃ
prathamaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
triguṇaṃ dviguṇaṃ śuddhasattvaikaguṇanirguṇam |
dvitīyoktaṃ hare rūpamupāsyamupavarṇyate ||
atha dvitīyaskandhāśrayaṇena bhagavanmūrtibhedatadupāsanatatphalādi prapañcayiṣyan prathamaṃ svasya tasminnadhikārāyārogyaṃ prārthayate -- 
kalyatāṃ mama kuruṣva tāvatīṃ kalyate bhavadupāsanaṃ yayā |
spaṣṭamaṣṭavidhayogaṃcaryayā puṣṭayāśu tava tuṣṭimāpnuyām || 1 ||
kalyatāmiti | mama tāvatīṃ kalyatām ārogyaṃ kuruṣva, yayā yāvatyā kalyatayā bhavadupāsanaṃ kalyate anuṣṭhātuṃ śakyate | spaṣṭam idaṃ niścitam | yadyahamarogaḥ syāṃ, tarhi āśvaṣṭavidhasya yamaniyamādyaṣṭāṅgasya yogasya caryayā anuṣṭhānena puṣṭayā sampūrṇāṅgayā | samprati madakalyataiva yogāṅgavaikalye kāraṇamiti bhāvaḥ | tava tuṣṭiṃ prasādam āpnuyām labheya || 1 ||
idānīṃ yathāśakti karomītyāha -- 
brahmacaryadṛḍhatādibhiryamairāplavādiniyamaiśca pāvitāḥ |
kurmahe dṛḍhamamī sukhāsanaṃ paṅkajādyamapi vā bhavatparāḥ || 2 ||
brahmacaryeti | brahmacaryasya dṛḍhatā nāma strīṇāṃ darśanasparśanādivarjanam | ādiśabdenāhiṃsāsatyāsteyādi gṛhyate | āplavaḥ snānam | atrādiśabdena manaḥ -- śuddhijapatapohomādi gṛhyate | etairyamairniyamaiśca pāvitāḥ śuddhāḥ santaḥ bhavān paraḥ pradhāno yeṣāṃ te bhavatparāḥ amī vayaṃ paṅkajamādyaṃ prāthamikaṃ yasya tat paṅkajādyaṃ padmāsanaṃ svastikāsanamanyad vā sukhāsanaṃ yathāsukhamāsanaṃ dṛḍhaṃ kurmahe || 2 ||
āsanadāḍharye sati prāṇāyāmaṃ kurma ityāha -- 
tāramantaranucintya santataṃ prāṇavāyumabhiyamya nirmalāḥ |
indriyāṇi viṣayādathāpahṛtyāsmahe bhavadupāsanonmukhāḥ || 3 ||
tāramiti | tāraṃ praṇavam antaḥ hṛdi santataṃ tailadhārāvad ghaṇṭānādavaccāvicchinnamanucintya vijātīyapratyayānantaritasajātīyapratyayapravāhaṃ kṛtvā prāṇavāyumabhiyamya pūrakarecakakumbhakaiḥ vāyujayaṃ kṛtvā nirgatāni malāni rāgādīni yebhyaste nirmalāḥ | taduktaṃ ‘prāṇāyāmairdahed doṣān dhāraṇābhiśca kilbiṣān' iti | atha anantaram indriyāṇi viṣayādapahṭatyeti pratyāhāra uktaḥ | bhavadupāsane dhāraṇāghuttarabhūmikāyām unmukhā ārurukṣavaḥ santaḥ āsmahe pūrvabhūmikāmevābhyasyato bhavatkṛpāṃ pratipālayāma ityarthaḥ || 3 ||
kramaprāptāṃ dhāraṇāmāha -- 
asphuṭe vapuṣi te prayatnato dhārayema dhiṣaṇāṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
tena bhaktirasamantarārdratāmudhema bhavadaṅghricintakāḥ || 4||
asphuṭeti | asphuṭe aspaṣṭe āpātataḥ pratīte te tava vapuṣi prayatnato muhurmuhurdhiṣaṇāṃ buddhiṃ dhārayema | atra buddhernidrādoṣe sati taduddhodhane prayāsaḥ prayatnaḥ | tathānyaviṣayālambane sati tacchamanaṃ kartavyamiti muhurmuhurityuktam | evaṃ dhāraṇāṃ kalayāma iti yat tena dhāraṇākalanena bhaktireva rasaḥ taṃ tadanubhāvamantarārdratāṃ codvahema prāpnuyāma | tato vayaṃ bhavadaṅghricintakāḥ paramabhāgavatāśca bhavemeti || 4 ||
dhyānamāha -- 
visphuṭāvayavabhedasundaraṃ tvadvapuḥ suciraśīlanāvaśāt |
aśramaṃ manasi cintayāmahe dhyānayoganiratāstvadāśrayāḥ || 5 ||
visphuṭeti | bisphuṭā viśeṣeṇa spaṣṭāḥ śrīmatpādādikeśāntā avayavabhedā yasmiṃstad visphuṭāvayavabhedaṃ tādṛśaṃ sundaraṃ sphuṭapratīyamānasaundarye ca tvadvapuḥ suciraśīlanā dīrghakālaṃ dhāraṇādhyānayorabhyāsaḥ tadvaśād aśramam anāyāsena manasi cintayāmahe | ‘āhūta iva me śīghraṃ darśanaṃ yāti cetasi' iti yathā śrīnāradavacanaṃ, tathetyarthaḥ | evañca vayaṃ dhyānayoge
dhyānarūpe tvatprāptyupāye niratāstatparāstvadāśrayāstvadbhaktā bhavemeti śeṣaḥ || 5||
atha samādhimāśāste -- 
dhyāyatāṃ sakalamūrtimīdṛśīmunmipanmadhuratāhṛtātmanām |
sāndramodarasarūpamāntaraṃ brahma rūpamayi ! te'vabhāsate || 6 ||
dhyāyatāmiti | ayi bhagavan ! te tava īdṛśīṃ sakalamūrtiṃ dhyāyatām unmiṣantyā abhivyaktayā madhuratayā tava vapussaubhāgyena hṛta ātmā mano yeṣāṃ te unmiṣanmadhuratāhṛtātmānaḥ, teṣāṃ sāndramodarasarūpaṃ sāndrānandaikarasasvarūpaṃ te rūpaṃ svarūpabhūtaṃ brahma āntaram antarindriyagrāhyaṃ jñātṛjñānajñeyarūpeṇa bhedenābhedena cāvabhāsate| anena savikalpakasamādhiruktaḥ | tatra caivameva bhānaprakāraḥ | taduktamācāryeṇa 'tadā mṛṇmayagajādibhāne'pi mṛdbhānavad dvaitabhāne'pyadvitīyaṃ vastu bhāsata' iti || 6 ||
aṃtha nirvikalpakasamādheḥ svarūpaṃ darśayati -- 
tatsamāsvadanarūpiṇīṃ sthitiṃ tvatsamādhimayi viśvanāyaka ! |
āśritāḥ punarataḥ paricyutāvārabhemahi ca dhāraṇādikam || 7 ||
taditi | tasya tādṛśasya brahmaṇaḥ samāsvadanaṃ samyaganubhavaḥ, tadrūpiṇīṃ sthitiṃ jñātṛjñeyayorapi jñānarūpeṇāvasthānaṃ tvatsamādhiṃ tvadviṣayaṃ nirvikalpakasamādhimityarthaḥ | atra cāyameva bhānaprakāraḥ | taduktaṃ 'tadā jalākārākāritalavaṇānavabhāsena jalamātrāvabhāsavadadvitīyavastumātramavabhāsata' iti | he viśvanāyaka! jagannātha ! tādṛśaṃ tvatsamādhimāśritā vayaṃ punarato'smāt sthānāt paricyutau satyāṃ bhraṃśe sati dhāraṇādikaṃ punaścārabhemahīti | ayamāśayaḥ -- nirvikalpakasamādherlayavikṣepakaṣāyarasāsvādarūpāścatvāro vighnāḥ saṃbhavanti | tatra layo nidrā | vikṣepo'nyāvalambanam | kaṣāyo viṣayavāsanayā stabdhabhāvaḥ | rasāsvādaḥ savikalpakānandāsvādanam |
etairdoṣaiḥ paricyutau satyāṃ punarapi dhāraṇādikamārabdhavyam | yadā tu cittametairdoṣairvirahitatayācalaṃ brahmamātramavatiṣṭhate, tadā nirvikalpakasamādhirityucyate 'yathā
dīpo nivātastho neṅgate sopamā smṛtā' iti bhagavadvacanāditi || 7 ||
atha samādhivijaye jīvanmuktatāprāptimāha -- 
itthamabhyasananirbharollasattvatparātmamukhakalpitotsavāḥ |
muktabhaktakulamaulitāṃ gatāḥ sañcarema śukanāradādivat || 8 ||
itthamiti | ittham  uktaprakāreṇa dhāraṇādhyānasamādhīnāmabhyasanena nirbharam atiśayena ullasatā āvirbhūtena tvatparātmasukhena tvatsvarūpaparabrahmānubhavajanitenānandena kalpito nirvṛtta utsavo yeṣāṃ tathābhūtāḥ santo vayaṃ muktānāṃ bhaktānāṃ ca kulāni samūhāḥ, teṣāṃ maulitāṃ pradhānatāṃ gatāḥ prāptāḥ santa iti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -- yadyapi brahmaṇi sākṣātkṛte sati ajñānatatkāryapūrvasañcitakarmasaṃśayaviparyayādīnāṃ bāghitatvādakhilabandharahitā brahmaniṣṭhā bhavanti, tathāpi yāvat prārabdhakarma parisamāpyate, tāvat pūrvavāsanayā kriyamāṇāni karmāṇi bhujyamānāni ca | yathendrajālamidamiti jñānavantastadindrajālaṃ paśyanto'pi paramārthato na paśyanti, tadvadete'pi lokavyavahāraṃ brahmajñānabāghitatvādaparamārthatayā paśyantaḥ kañcit kālaṃ sañcaranti | seha jīvanmuktāvasthocyata iti tādṛśāvasthāpannāḥ śukanāradādayaḥ | he bhagavan ! vayamapi yadi bhavatkṛpābhājanaṃ bhavema, tarhi tādṛśāḥ santaḥ sañcaremetyarthaḥ || 8||
nanu brahmajñānānantaraṃ prārabdhakarmāvasāne muktirityuktam | tadanupapannaṃ, śrīnāradādīnāṃ prārabdhakarmanāśād dehapāte'satyapi dehāntaraprāptiśravaṇādityāśaṅkaya vijitasamādhīnāṃ tādṛśāṃ yatheṣṭāṃ gatimāha saptabhiḥ -- 
tvatsamādhivijaye tu yaḥ punarmaṅkṣu mokṣarasikaḥ krameṇa vā |
yogavaśyamanilaṃ ṣaḍāśrayairunnayatyaja! suṣumṇayā śanaiḥ || 9 ||
tvaditi | he aja! tvayīśvare samādhiścittaikāgracaṃ tvatsamādhiḥ, tasya vijayo nivātasthitadīpavadanāyāsena dīrghakālamavasthānaṃ, tasmin sati yastu punarmaṅkṣu jhaṭiti mokṣarasikaḥ sadyomuktyāgrahī bhavati | krameṇa veti yastu kramamuktyāgrahī vā bhavatītyarthaḥ | dvividho'pi saḥ yogavaśyaṃ prāṇāyāmavaśīkṛtam anilaṃ prāṇavāyuṃ ṣaḍāśrayaiḥ suṣumṇayā suṣumṇānāḍīdvāreṇa unnayatīti, svapārṇinā guḍhaṃ saṃpīḍya
mūlādhāranābhihaṭdurastālumūlabhūmadhyarūpaiḥ paḍbhirāśrayaiḥ sthānaiḥ śanaiḥ kramādadhiropayatītyarthaḥ ||9||
etadantamubhayorapi samānaṃ, punarnirāgrahasya viśeṣamāha -- 
liṅgadehamapi santyajannatho līyate tvayi pare nirāgrahaḥ |
ūrdhvalokakutukī tu sūrdhataḥ sārdhameva karaṇairnirīyate || 10 ||
liṅgeti  | nirāgrahaḥ sadyomuktyā grahī atho anilaṃ bhrūmadhyamadhiropya muhūtīrghaṃ sthitvānantaraṃ liṅgadehamapi santyajan sthūlaśarīraṃ sūkṣmaśarīraṃ ca samyagapunarāvṛttaye tyajan tvayi pare brahmātmake līyate mūrdhani vibhidya | yathā jyotijyotiṣi līyate, tathā tvatsāyujyaṃ prāpnotītyarthaḥ | anyasya viśeṣamāha -- ūrdhvalokakutukī tu brahmādilokānubhavakautukavāṃśced bhavati, tarhi mūrdhataḥ mūrdhani nirbhidya tadvāreṇa karaṇairindriyaiḥ sārdhameva pañcaprāṇamanobuddhidaśendriyasamudāyātmakaliṅgaśarīreṇa saha nirīyate nirgacchati | ayamāśayaḥ -- brahmarandhrāntaḥsthitāyāḥ suṣumṇānāḍyāḥ sūryaraśmīnāṃ ca nityasambandho'sti | ato jīvastadvārā brahmarandhrāntamāgatya niryadbhiḥ sūryaraśmibhiḥ saha bahirnirgacchatīti || 10 ||
anantaramagnyādyātivāhikadevatātikramaṇaprakāramāha -- 
agnivāsaravalarkṣapakṣagairuttarāyaṇajupā ca daivataiḥ |
prāpito ravipadaṃ bhavatparo modavāna dhruvapadāntamīyate || 11 ||
nirīyate 'īṅ gatau' divādiḥ |
agnivāsareti | valarkṣapakṣaḥ śuklapakṣa| agniṃ vāsaraṃ valarkṣapakṣaṃ ca gacchanti adhiṣṭhātṛtvena prāpnuvantīti tathā uttarāyaṇajuṣā uttarāyaṇaṣaṇmāsān juṣate
adhitiṣṭhatīti tathā | agnyādyabhimānibhirdaivatairityarthaḥ | etairdevataiḥ ravipadaṃ jyotiścakraṃ prāpito jīvaḥ bhavatparaḥ bhavānīśvara eva paraḥ sevyatayā pradhāno yasya sa tathā | modavān tattaddevatātulyabhogānubhavena santoṣātiśayavān bhūtvā dhruvapadāntaṃ dhruvalokāvadhi īyate prāpnoti | atra ceyaṃ prakriyā  -- agnyādidevatā hyenaṃ jīvamañjaligataputrikāvat kramād brahmalokaṃ prāpayanti | tatra prathamamagnidevatā svecchayā śarīramutsṛjya sūryaraśmidvārā nirgacchantaṃ jīvamagnilokamārgeṇa nītvā vāsarābhimānidevatāhastaṃ prāpayati | sā śuklapakṣābhimānidevatāhastaṃ prāpayati | sā cottarāyaṇaṣaṇmāsābhimānidevatāhastaṃ prāpayati | sā saṃvatsarābhimānidevatāhastaṃ prāpayati | sā punardevalokamārgeṇa nītvā vāyudevatāhastaṃ prāpayati | sā ca svalokamārgeṇādityadevatāhastaṃ nayati | sā ca svamaṇḍaladvārā gacchantaṃ jīvaṃ svalokamārgeṇa nītvā candradevatāhastaṃ nayati | sā svalokamārgeṇa vidyullokaṃ propayati | tataśca dhruvalokāntaṃ gacchatīti || 11 ||
āsthito'tha maharālaye yadā śeṣavaṛdahanoṣmaṇārdyate |
īyate bhavadupāśrayastadā vedhasaḥ padamataḥ puraiva vā || 12 ||
āsthita iti | atha dhruvapadaprāptyanantaraṃ maharālaye kalpāyuṣāṃ bhṛgvādīnāmālayabhūte maharloke āsthitaḥ sukhena vasan yadā yasmin kāle śeṣavakradahanoṣmaṇā anantasya mukhāt trailokyadāhāya nirgato'gnirūrdhvaśikho vardhate, tena ca trailokyaṃ dahyamānaṃ nirīkṣya tasyopmaṇā cārghate pīḍyate, tadā bhavadupāśrayo bhavantamīśvarameva śaraṇaṃ ga to vedhasaḥ padaṃ satyalokākhyam īyate prāpnoti | bhṛgvādibhiḥ saha janarlokaṃ tapo lokaṃ cātītya brahmasabhāṃ praviśatītyarthaḥ | ataḥ puraiva veti
śeṣavatrkadahanoṣmaprāpteḥ prāgeva vā yathecchaṃ tatra gantuṃ śakyameva | ayamatrābhiprāyaḥ -- yadyayaṃ tvaritaṃ tvatpadaṃ jigamipurbhavati, tarhi vighullokāvadhi gatamenaṃ brahmājñayā yaḥ kaścidameyavapurbrahmalokādāgatya vighullokamārgeṇa śakraprajāpatilokamārgeṇa ca nītvā virajānadīṃ tārayitvā brahmalokaṃ prāpayati | tadā cāyaṃ divyaśarīro bhavatīti || 12 ||
atha brahmalokaṃ prāptānāṃ gatidvaividhyamāha -- 
tatra vā tava pade'thavā vasan prākṛtapralaya eti muktatām |
svecchayā khalu purāpi mucyate saṃvibhidya jagadaṇḍamojasā || 13 ||
tatreti | evaṃ satyalokaṃ prāpto'yaṃ tatra brahmaloke vā athavā tava pade viṣṇuloke vā vasanniti | atrāyamāśayaḥ -- satyalokāntarbhāga eva brahmaviṣṇugiriśānāṃ lokā iti trayo lokāḥ santi | tatra brahmaviṣṇuśivānāmupāsakāstattallokaṃ prāpya tattatsamā nān bhogānanubhavanti ca | prākṛtapralaye sati muktatāmeti prākṛtānāṃ prakṛtikāryāṇāṃ mahadahaṅkāratanmātrapañcakādīnāṃ svasvakāraṇeṣu prakṛṣṭo layo hi prākṛtapralayaḥ, tasmin sati brahmaṇā saha mukto brahmamātramavatiṣṭhate | athavā svecchayeti  | idamatrākṛtaṃ -- yadyayaṃ bhujyamāneṣu brahmādibhogeṣu madhye viraktimāpadyate, tarhi mahāpralayāt prāgeva vimucyate mukto bhavati | kathamityatrāha -- jagadaṇḍaṃ brahmāṇḍam ojasā saṃvibhidya svasya yogabalenaiva brahmāṇḍakaṭāhaṃ nirbhidya tadvāretyarthaḥ || 13 ||
brahmāṇḍabhedanaprakāramāha -- 
tasya ca kṣitipayomahoniladyomahatprakṛtisaptakāvṛtīḥ |
tattadātmakatayā viśan sukhī yāti te padamanāvṛtaṃ vibho! || 14 ||
tasya ceti | tasya brahmāṇḍasya ca saptāvaraṇāni santi | tānyāha -- kṣitīti | kṣitipayomahoniladyāvaḥ pañcabhūtāni, mahat mahattattvaṃ, prakṛtirmāyā, āsāṃ
saptakameva āvṛtayaḥ āvaraṇāni | atra mahacchabdo'hantattvasyāpyupalakṣakaḥ | tena pañcabhūtāni mahadahaṅkārau ca saptāvaraṇāni | prakṛtyāvaraṇaṃ tvaṣṭamaṃ vyāpakameva | etā āvṛtīstattadātmakatayā tattadāvaraṇamevātmā mūrtiryasya sa tattadātmakaḥ, tattayā viśanniti tattadābaraṇeṣu tattadātmanā sūkṣmaśarīreṇa praviśannityarthaḥ | sukhī tatra tatra niratiśayaṃ sukhamanubhavan he vibho ! viśvavyāpin ! te anāvṛtaṃ padaṃ brahmapadaṃ yāti | evamāvaraṇānyatikramyānte prāṇendriyāṇi svasvakāraṇe vilāpya tata āvaraṇasyāpi vilayādanāvṛtaṃ brahmamātrameva bhavatītyarthaḥ || 14 ||
evaṃ muktaḥ punarna saṃsārītyāha -- 
arcirādigatimīdṛśīṃ vrajan vicyutiṃ na bhajate jagatpate ! |
sañcidātmaka ! bhavadguṇodayānuccarantamanileśa ! pāhi mām || 15 ||
acirādīti | īdṛśīmarcirādigatiṃ vrajan jīvaḥ vicyutiṃ punarāvṛttiṃ na bhajate | atra hetugarme sambodhanaṃ -- saccidātmaketi | he anileśa ! śrīguruvāyupuranātha ! jagatpate! bhavaguṇodayān tvaguṇotkarṣān uccarantaṃ yathāśakti stuvantaṃ māṃ pāhi ādhivyādhisaṅkaṭebhyo rakṣa || 15 ||
iti aṣṭāṅgayogavarṇanaṃ yogasiddhivarṇanaṃ ca
caturthe daśakaṃ sapañcakam |
evaṃ śuddhasattvamayamūryupāsanāṃ phalaparyantāmupapādya tasyāmanadhikāriṇāṃ viśvamūrtīśopāsanāṃ darśayiṣyannādau bhagavataḥ sthūlaśarīrasyotpattyupapattaye tasya prāgabhāvaṃ darśayati -- 
vyaktāvyaktamidaṃ na kiñcidabhavat prāk prākṛtaprakṣaye
māyāyāṃ guṇasāmyaruddhavikṛtau tvayyāgatāyāṃ layam |
no mṛtyuva tadāmṛtaṃ ca samabhūnnāho na rātreḥ sthitistatraikastvamaśiṣyathāḥ kila parānandaprakāśātmanā || 1 ||
vyakteti | prāk purā prākṛtaprakṣaye brahmapralaye vyaktāvyaktaṃ sthūlasūkṣmātmakaṃ kāryakāraṇātmakaṃ vā idaṃ śarīrādikaṃ kiñcinnābhavat | atra hetumāha -- māyāyāṃ tvayi layamāgatāyāmiti | māyālaye hetumāha -- guṇasāmyaruddhavikṛtāviti | guṇāḥ sattvarajastamāṃsi, teṣāṃ sāmyāvasthayā ruddhāḥ stambhitā vikṛtayaḥ kāryāṇi yasyāṃ tādṛśyāṃ, māyāyā brahmaṇi līnatvānmāyākārya kiñcidapi tadā nāsīdityarthaḥ | mṛtyuḥ saṃsāraḥ | amṛtaṃ mokṣaḥ | tadā bandho mokṣaśca na samabhūt | nāhro na rātreḥ sthitiḥ maryādāvibhāgo nāsīt | tatra tasmin kāle tvameko brahmaivāśiṣyathāḥ śiṣṭo'bhūḥ | tavāpi tadānīṃ na līlāvigrahaparigraha ityāha -- parānandaprakāśātmanā saccidānandasvarūpeṇeti | kilaśabdo'trāmyā avasthāyāḥ tadvāgrūpāmnāyāntaikavedyatāṃ darśayati || 1 ||
etadeva prapañcāyati
kālaḥ karma guṇākṣca jīvanivahā viśvaṃ ca kāryaṃ vibho !
cillīlāratimeyuṣi tvayi tadā nirlīnatāmāyayuḥ |
teṣāṃ naiva vadantyasattvamayi bhoḥ ! śaktyātmanā tiṣṭhatāṃ
no cet kiṃ gaganaprasūnasadṛśāṃ bhūyo bhavet sambhavaḥ || 2 ||
kāla iti | guṇaḥ sattvādiḥ, kālaḥ tatkṣobhakaḥ, karma jīvādṛṣṭaṃ, jīvānāṃ nivahāḥ samūhāśca, kiṃ bahunā viśvam akhilamapi kārya māyākāryaṃ he vibho! sargasthitilayanidānabhūta ! tvayi cidātmake svasvarūpānusandhānarūpā līlā cillīlā tasyāṃ ratim icchām eyupi prāptavanti, yoganidrāmāripsau satītyarthaḥ | tadaitāni nirlīnatām adarśanatām āyayuḥ prāptavanti | na cātyantamasattvaṃ jātamityāha -- teṣāmiti | teṣāṃ kālakarmādīnāṃ śaśaviṣāṇavadatyantāsattvaṃ naiva vadanti, śrutayastadraṣṭāra iti vā śeṣaḥ | tatropapattimāha -- śaktyātmaneti | ayi bhoḥ ! bhagavan ! tvayi śaktyātmanā kāraṇarūpeṇa tiṣṭhatāṃ sthitānāmityarthaḥ | nanu līnāścedasanta evetyāśaṅkaya vipakṣe bādhakamāha -- no cediti | yadyeṣāmasattvaṃ, tarhi gaganakusumasadṛśānāmeteṣāṃ bhūyaḥ pralayāvasāne sambhavo na bhavet | taccaniṣṭam | atasteṣāṃ kāraṇātmanā sattvamaṅgīkartavyamityarthaḥ || 2 ||
samprati bhagavato virāṇamūrterutpattiprakāraṃ darśayatyevamityaṣṭabhiḥ -- 
evañca dviparārdhakālavigatāvīkṣāṃ sisṛkṣātmikāṃ
bibhrāṇe tvayi cukṣubhe tribhuvanībhāvāya māyā svayam |
māyātaḥ khalu kālaśaktirakhilādṛṣṭaṃ svabhāvo'pi ca
prādurbhūya guṇān vikāsya vidadhustasyāḥ sahāyakriyām || 3 ||
evamiti | evamuktaprakāreṇa brahmamātrāvaśeṣeṇa dviparārdhakālasya vigatau avasāne sati | atra cakāro dviparārdhaparimitakāla eva brahmaṇaḥ paramāyuriti darśayati| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -- asmādṛśāṃ saṃvatsaro devānāmahorātraḥ "māsena syādahorātraḥ paitro varṣeṇa daivata" iti vacanāt sarve jñeyam | prākṛtapralayasyāpi brahmāyuḥ parimitadviparārdhakālatulyaparimāṇatvamanumeyamiti | tādṛśakālavigame sati tvayi brahmaṇi sisṛkṣātmikāmīkṣāṃ bibhrāgage iti | īkṣāṃ sisṛkṣācintanādiśabdavācyāṃ māyāpreraṇarūpāṃ kriyāmiva bibhrāṇe aṅgīkṛtavati sati māyā svayaṃ cukṣubhe calitā brahmaṇaḥ pṛthagiva prakāśaṃ prāptā sthitā | kimartha, tribhuvanībhāvāya, prāgatathāvidhāpi punastrailokyarūpeṇa vivartitumityarthaḥ | māyātaḥ evaṃ kṣubdhāyā māyāyāḥ sakāśāt | khalvityavadhāraṇe | kālaśaktiḥ īśvarasya kālākhyā śaktiḥ | akhilānāṃ prāktanajīvānāmityarthād, adṛṣṭaṃ sukṛtaduṣkṛtarūpaṃ svabhāvo'pi ca prādurbhūyāsyā guṇān rajastamaḥsattvākhyān vikāsya sāmyāvasthāmapāsya kāryonmukhān kṛtvā tasyā māyāyāḥ sahāyakriyāṃ tribhuvanībhāvāpattaye parikarma vidadhuścakruḥ || 3 ||
evaṃ māyāsṛṣṭimuktvā tatkāryasya mahataḥ sṛṣṭimabhidhātuṃ puruṣasvarūpamāha -- 
māyāsannihito'praviṣṭavapuṣā sākṣīti gīto bhavān
bhedaistāṃ pratibimvato viviśivān jīvo'pi naivāparaḥ |
kālādipratibodhitātha bhavatā sañcoditā ca svayaṃ
māyā sā khalu buddhitattvamasṛjad yo'sau mahānucyate || 4 ||
māyeti | māyāsannihito māyopādhikaḥ apraviṣṭena tadāśrayatvena bahiḥsthitenānupahitena vapuṣā svarūpeṇa copalakṣitaḥ, ekāṃśena māyāmavaṣṭabhya sthita ityarthaḥ | atra sannihitaśabdena paramārtharūpasya paramātmano'paramārthabhūtopādhisambandhasyāparamārthatā dyotyate | evambhūto bhavān sarvaprakāśakatvāt sākṣīti gītaḥ nigamāntaistadvidbhirveti śeṣaḥ| bhedairiti yāvadupādhibhedamātmānaṃ vibhajya tāṃ māyāṃ, tatkṛtānupādhīniti yāvat, pratibimbataḥ pratibimvarūpeṇa viviśivān praviṣṭaḥ śarīrādipvahamityabhimanyamāno bhavāneva jīvo'pi, nāparaḥ na tvatto'nyo jīvaḥ | evaṃ jīveśvarabhedena puruṣasya dvaividhyamuktvā tatsambandhe mahata utpattiṃ darśayati -- kālādīta | kālakarmasvabhāvaiḥ pratibodhitā saṃkṣobhitarajaādiguṇatayā kāryonmukhīkṛtā bhavatā ca sañcoditā āhitavīryā satī sā māyā khalu buddhitattvamasṛjat sṛṣṭavatī, yo'sau mahāniti mahattattvamityucyate | mahacchabdasāmānādhikaraṇyāya puṃstvanirdeśaḥ | tad buddhitattvamiti pūrveṇānvayaḥ || 4 ||
na nu kimidaṃ mahacchabdavācyaṃ, buddhitattvamiti cet, tadapi kiṃ, kīdṛśaṃ cāsya svarūpamityāśaṅkāyāmāha -- 
tatrāsau triguṇātmako'pi ca mahān savapradhānaḥ svayaṃ
jīve'smin khalu nirvikalpamahamityuddhodhaniṣpādakaḥ |
cakre'smin savikalpabodhakamahantattvaṃ mahān khalvasau
saṃpuṣṭaṃ triguṇaistamotibahulaṃ viṣṇo ! bhavatpreraṇāt || 2 ||
tatreti | tatra māyākāryeṣu asau mahān svayaṃ triguṇātmako'pi sattvapradhānaḥ prakāśabahulatvājjīvānāṃ samaṣṭireko mahān jīvaḥ | yathā vṛkṣāṇāṃ samaṣṭirvanam, evaṃ
samaṣṭyātmake'smin jīve khalu nirvikalpaṃ manuṣyatvādiviśeṣaṇarahitaṃ yathā bhavati tathā ahamityevaṃprakāreṇa sthitasyodvodhasya jñānasya niṣpādakaḥ kāraṇam | asau mahān mahattattvaṃ kartṛ he viṣṇo ! bhavatpreraṇāt khalu bhavān hi mahāntamadhiṣṭhāya taṃ preritavān, ataḥ khalvasau ahaṃtattvaṃ cakre | taccāsmin jīve savikalpabodhakam ahaṃ manuṣya ityādisavikalpakajñānaniṣpādakaṃ triguṇaiḥ saṃpuṣṭaṃ triguṇātmakaṃ, tathāpi tamotibahulam atitarāṃ tamaḥpradhānamityarthaḥ || 5 ||
athāhamastraividhyamāha -- 
so'haṃ ca triguṇakramāt trividhatāmāsādya vaikāriko
bhūyastaijasatāmasāviti bhavannādyena sattvātmanā |
devānindriyamānino'kṛta diśābātārkapāśyaśvino
vahīndrācyutamitrakān vidhuvidhiśrīrudraśārīrakān || 6 ||
sa iti | saḥ mahatkāryabhūtaḥ ahaṃ ca ahaṅkāraśca bhavan utpannaḥ san bhūyaḥ punaḥ triguṇakramāt trayāṇāṃ guṇānāṃ sattvaṃ rajastama ityanena krameṇa vaikārikastaijasastāmasa ityanena krameṇa trividhatāṃ triprakāratāmāsādya prāpya sattvātmanā sāttvikena ādyena vaikārikāhaṅkāreṇa indriyamānino devān indriyāṇāmadhiṣṭhātṛdevān akṛta kṛtavān | ke te ityata āha -- diśeti | digvātārkapracetośvinaḥ kramācchrotratvakcakṣurjihvāghrāṇānāṃ jñānendriyāṇāmadhiṣṭhānadevatāḥ | tatra cāyaṃ jīvaḥ karṇadvārādiṣu sthitānāṃ śrotrādīndriyāṇāṃ svasvādhiṣṭhānadevatābhiḥ preritaḥ śrotrādīndriyadvārā nirgacchantībhirantaḥkaraṇavṛttibhiḥ śabdādiviṣayānanubhavati | evaṃ vahīndropendramitraprajāpatisaṃjñāḥ kramād vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthākhyakarmendriyapañcakādhiṣṭhātryo devatāḥ | tairayaṃ prerito jīvo vacanādānagamanavisargānandānubhavarūpāṇi karmāṇi karoti | evaṃ candracaturmukhaśrīrudrakṣetrajā  manobuddhyahaṅkāracittākhyāntaḥkaraṇacatuṣṭayadevatāḥ | tairayaṃ saṅkalpaniścayaśarīrābhimānadhāraṇā adhigacchati || 6 ||
vahnīndropendramitraprajāpatibhiḥ |
punarapi baiṃkārikasṛṣṭimāi -- 
bhūman! mānasabuddhacahaṅkatimilaccittākhyavṛttyanvitaṃ taccāntaḥkaraṇaṃ vibho ! taba balāt sattvāṃśa evāsṛjat |
jātastaijasato daśendriyagaṇastattāmasāśāt punastanmātraṃ namaso marutpurapate ! śabdo'javi tvadvalāt || 7 ||
bhūmanniti | he bhūman ! vibho ! tava balāt tvatpreraṇāt sattvāṃśaḥ sāttvikāhaṅkāra eva mānasavudhdhahaṅkatibhiḥ milantyā sahitayā cittākhyavṛttyānvitaṃ sahitaṃ tat sattvapradhānamantaḥkaraṇaṃ cāsujat sṛṣṭavān | taijasataḥ rājasāhaṅkārād daśānāṃ jñānendriyāṇāṃ karmendriyāṇāṃ ca gaṇo jātaḥ | tasyāhaṅkārasya tāmasāṃśāt punaḥ he marutpurapate ! vātālayeśa ! tvadvalāt tvatpreraṇād nabhasa ākāśasya tanmātram apañcīkṛtatvāt sūkṣmoṃ'śaḥ śabdaḥ ajāni jātaḥ || 7 ||
punarapi tāmasāhaṅkārasṛṣṭiṃ prapañcayati -- 
śabdād vyoya tataḥ sasarjitha! sparśaṃ tato mārutaṃ
tasmād rūpamato maho'thaca rasaṃ toyaṃ ca gandhaṃ mahīm |
evaṃ mādhava ! pūrvapūrvakalādāghāgharmānvitaṃ
bhūtagrāmamimaṃ tvameva bhagavan ! prākāśayastāmasāt || 8 ||
śabdāditi | he vibho ! tvaṃ śabdād vyoma ākāśaṃ sasarjitha sṛṣṭavānasi | tato vyomnaḥ sparśa vāyuguṇaṃ, tataḥ sparśād vāyuṃ, tasmād mārulādrū rūpaṃ tejoguṇaṃ, ato'smād rūpād mahastejaḥ, athaca anantaram arthāt tejaso rasam abguṇaṃ, rasāt toyaṃ, toyād gandhaṃ pṛthivīguṇaṃ, gandhādū mahapṛthivīṃ ca sasarjitha | evaṃ pūrvapūrvaiḥ kalanād melanād uttaramuttaram ādyādyadharmeranvitamiti | atrāyamarthaḥ -- ākāśaṃ śabdaguṇakaṃ, vāyuḥ śabdasparśaguṇakaḥ, tejaḥ śabdasparśarūpaguṇakaṃ, jalaṃ śabdasparśarūparasaguṇakaṃ, pṛthivī śabdasparśarūparasagandhavatīti | he bhagavan ! bhajanīyaguṇa !he mādhava! śrīpate ! tvamevemaṃ bhūtagrāmaṃ saguṇamahābhūtapañcakaṃ tāmasāt tāmasāhaṅkārāt prākāśayaḥ prakāśitavānasi || 8 || sampratyetaistattvairbhagavato brahmāṇḍanirmāṇaprakāramāha -- 
ete bhūtagaṇāstathendriyagaṇā devākṣca jātāḥ pṛthaṅ
no śekurbhuvanāṇḍanirmitibidhā devairamībhistadā |
tvaṃ nānāvidhasūktibhirnutaguṇastattvānyamṛnyāviśaṃśreṣṭāśaktimudīrya tāni ghaṭayan hairaṇyamaṇḍaṃ vyathāḥ || 9 ||
eta iti | ete pūrvoktā bhūtagaṇāḥ śabdādisūkṣmabhūtānāṃ vyomādimahābhūtānāṃ ca gaṇā daśakaṃ, tathendriyagaṇā jñānendriyāṇi karmendriyāṇi antarindriyavṛttibhiścatasṛbhiḥ saha caturdaśa, tadadhiṣṭhāgyo devatāśca caturdaśa | caḥ samuccaye, yadvā apyarthe | ete jātā api bhuvanāṇḍanirmitividhau brahmāṇḍanirmāṇavyāpāre pṛthak pṛthagbhūtatvād no śekuḥ nāśaknuvan | yadā na śekuḥ, tadā amībhirdevaiścaturdaśabhiḥ nānāvidhābhiḥ sūktibhiḥ stotrairnuta guṇastvam | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -- māyāsṛṣṭyanantarameva tacchuddhasattvāṃśenāṅgīkṛtalīlāvigrahaṃ tvāmete devāḥ sūktibhiḥ stutavanta iti | tadarthanayā cāmūni tattvānyāviśan praviśan teṣāṃ tattvānāṃ ceṣṭāśaktimudīrya kriyāsāmarthyamutpādya tāni mitho ghaṭayan guṇapradhānabhāvena saṃyojayan hairaṇyaṃ prakāśabahulatayā suvarṇapiṇḍavadatyujjvalam aṇḍaṃ brahmāṇḍākhyaṃ svaśarīraṃ vyadhāḥ nirmitavānasītyarthaḥ ||9||
aṇḍaṃ tat khalu pūrvasṛṣṭasalile'tiṣṭhat sahasraṃ samā
nirbhindannakṛthākṣcaturdaśajagadrūpaṃ virāḍāhayam |
sāhasraiḥ karapādamūrdhaniyanivahairniśśeṣajīvātmako
nirmāto'si marutpurādhipa ! sa māṃ trāyasva sarvāmayāt || 10 ||
aṇḍamiti | tat khalu tadacetanamevāṇḍaṃ pūrvasṛṣṭasalile āvaraṇodake sahasraṃ samāḥ saṃvatsarān | atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā | atiṣṭhat sthitamabhūt | tataśca tvaṃ svāṃśena tat praviśya nirbhindan vividhaṃ vibhajan caturdaśajagadrūpaṃ vividhaṃ rājamānatvād virāṭsaṃjñakaṃ śarīramakṛthāḥ kṛtavānasi | tataḥ sāhasraiḥ sahasrasaṅkhayāparimitaiḥ karapādamūrdhādyavayavānāṃ nivahaiḥ samūhaiḥ niśśeṣajīvātmakaḥ niśśeṣāṇāṃ carācarāṇāṃ jīvaḥ samaṣṭyātmaka evātmā svarūpaṃ yasya sa niśśeṣajīvātmako hiraṇyagarbhaḥ,
tathāvidhastvaṃ nirbhāto'si | he marutpurādhipa ! sa tvaṃ sarvāmayāt sarvasmād bāhyādābhyantarācca rogād māṃ trāyasva rakṣatādityarthaḥ || 10 ||
iti virāṭpuruṣotpattiprakāravarṇanaṃ
pañcamaṃ daśakam |
evaṃ bhagavato birāḍāhvayasya sthūlaśarīrasyotpatti pradarśya sampratyupāsanārthe tadavayavabhedakalpanāprakāramāha -- 
evaṃ caturdaśajaganmayatāṃ gatasya
pātālamīśa ! taba pāitalaṃ vadanti |
pādordhvadeśamapi deva! rasātalaṃ te
gulphadvayaṃ khalu mahātaladbhutātman ! || 1 ||
evamiti | he īśa ! evam uktaprakāreṇa caturdaśa jaganti lokāḥ tanmayatāṃ tannirmitaśarīratāṃ gatasya tava pādatalaṃ pātālaṃ vadanti | upāsanāvido munaya iti śeṣaḥ | pādordhvadeśaṃ prapadayugalam | adbhutā ātmānaḥ śarīrāṇi yasya so'dbhutātmā || 1 ||
jaṅghe talātalamatho sutalaṃ ca jānū
kiñcorubhāgayugalaṃ vitalātale dve |
kṣoṇītalaṃ jaghanamambaragaṅga ! nābhirvakṣaśva śakranilayastava cakrapāṇe ! || 2 ||
jaṅghe iti | ūrubhāgayugalamiti ūrvoradhobhāgo vitalam ūrdhvabhāgo'talamityarthaḥ | kṣoṇītalaṃ javanaṃ kaṭītaṭam | jaghanaśabdanirdeśastu bhagavatkaṭitaṭasya saundaryavivakṣayā, ‘paścānnitambaḥ strīkaṭyāḥ klīve tu jaghanaṃ puraḥ' ityuktatvāt | aṅga ! he bhagavan ! śaṝnilayaḥ svargastava vakṣaśca he cakrapāṇe ! || 2 ||
grīvā mahastava mukhaṃ ca janastapastu
phālaṃ śirastava samastamayasya satyam |
evaṃ jaganmayatano ! jagadāśritairapyanyairnibaddhavapuṣe bhagavan ! namaste || 3 ||
grīveti | tava grīvā kaṇṭhadeśaḥ mahaḥ maharlokaḥ jano janalokaḥ tapastapolokaḥ phālaṃ lalāṭaṃ samastamayatya viśvātmanaḥ satyaṃ satyalokaḥ he jaganmayatano ! viśvamūrte! evam uktanakāreṇaiva jagadākṣritairanyairvakṣyamāṇaikṣca nibaddhaṃ
sampūrṇāvayavatvena sampāditaṃ vapuryasya sa nibaddhavapuḥ | he bhagavan ! tasmai te namo'stu || 3 ||
jagadāśritairanyairapi nivaddhavapuṣa ityuktam | tadeva prapañcayati -- 
tvadrajhrandharandhrapadamīkṣvara ! viśvakandacchandāṃli keśava! dhanāstava keśapāśāḥ |
ullāsicilliyugalaṃ druhiṇasya gehaṃ
pakṣmāṇi rātridivasau savitā ca netre || 4 ||
tvaditi | he īśvara ! viśvakanda ! jagatkāraṇabhūta ! tava brahmarandhaṃ dvādaśāntaṃ, tadeva padaṃ sthānaṃ chandāṃsi vedā eva | ghanā meghāḥ tava keśapāśāḥ | ullāsicilliyugalaṃ śobhanaśīlaṃ bhrūyugaṃ druhiṇasya brahmaṇo gehaṃ gṛham | pakṣmāṇi ūrdhvādhastanākṣiromāṇi kramād rātridivasau| netre nayanendriyayugaṃ savitā sūryaḥ | cakāro'nuktasamuccayārthaḥ | ataḥ sāṅkhyayogau makarakuṇḍale, ātapatraṃ vikuṇṭhanilaya ityādyanveṣṭavyam || 4 ||
niśśeṣaviśvaracanā ca kaṭākṣamokṣaḥ
karṇai diśo'kṣviyugala taba nāsike dve |
lobhatrape ca bhagavannagharoṣṭhau
tārāgaṇākṣca radanāḥ śamanakṣca daṃṣṭrā || 5 ||
niśśeṣeti | tava niśśeṣaviśvaracanā caturdaśalokanirmāṇaṃ kaṭākṣamokṣaḥ | tava karṇau diśaḥ | aśvinau tava nāsike dve | lobhastavādharoṣṭhaḥ | trapā tavottaroṣṭhaḥ | he bhagavan ! tārāgaṇā nakṣatrapatayastava radanāḥ dantāḥ | śamano yamastava daṃṣṭrā || 5 ||
māyā vilāsahasitaṃ śvasitaṃ samīro
jihvā jalaṃ vacanamīśa! śakuntapaṅktiḥ |
siddhādayaḥ svaragaṇā mukharandhramagnirdevā bhujāḥ stanayugaṃ tava dharmadevaḥ || 6 ||
māyeti | tava vilāsahasitaṃ līlāsmitaṃ māyā | samīro vāyuḥ śvasitaṃ śvāsaḥ | tava jihvā jalam | he īśa ! īśvara ! tava vacanaṃ śakuntapaṅktiḥ pakṣisamūhaḥ| siddhādayaḥ siddhavidyādharacāraṇādayaḥ tava svaragaṇāḥ svarāṇāṃ śrutibhedabhinnā gaṇāḥ ṣaḍjādayaḥ | mukharandhramāsyam agniḥ | devā indrādayastava bhujāḥ | dharmadevaḥ devatāmūrtirdharmastava stanayugam || 6 ||
pṛṣṭhaṃ tvadharma iha deva! manaḥ sudhāṃśuravyaktameva hṛdayāmbujamambujākṣa ! |
kukṣiḥ samudranivahā vasanaṃ tu sandhye
śephaḥ prajāpatirasau bṛṣaṇau ca mitraḥ || 7 ||
pṛṣṭhamiti | pṛṣṭhaṃ tvadharmaḥ | he deva! dyotanaśīla! iha asmin rūpe sudhāṃśuścandraḥ tava manaḥ | tava hṛdayāmvujamavyaktameva sattvādiguṇatrayasāmyaṃ pradhānameva | he ambujākṣa ! samudranivahāḥ samudrasamūhāstava kukṣiḥ udaram | prātaḥsāyaṃsandhye tu tava vasanaṃ vāsoyugalam | prajāpatirbrahmā tava śephaḥ meḍhram | asau mitraḥ tava vṛṣaṇau || 7||
śroṇīsthalaṃ mṛgagaṇāḥ padayornakhāste
hastyuṣṭrasaindhavamukhā gamanaṃ tu kālaḥ |
viprādivarṇabhavanaṃ vadanābjabāhucārūruyugmacaraṇaṃ karuṇāmbudhe ! te || 8 ||
śroṇīti | tava śroṇīsthalaṃ mṛgagaṇāḥ | te tava padayornakhāḥ hastyuṣṭrasaindhavamukhāḥ| saindhavo'śvaḥ | tava gamanaṃ tu kālaḥ | he karuṇāmbudhe ! evaṃvidhamūrtyupāsanāyāṃ mayi tava kṛpayā bhāvyamiti bhāvaḥ | vadanābjaṃ bāhuśca cāru sundaramūruyugmaṃ ca caraṇau ca, teṣāṃ dvandvaḥ | prāṇyaṅgatvādekavacanam | te tava badanābjādikaṃ viprādīnāṃ varṇānāṃ bhavanaṃ sthānam | viprāṇāṃ sthānaṃ tava mukhaṃ, kṣatriyāṇāṃ sthānaṃ tava bāhuḥ, vaiśyānāṃ sthānaṃ tavoruyugmam, śūdrāṇāṃ sthānaṃ tava caraṇau cetyarthaḥ || 8 ||
saṃsāracakramayi cakradhara ! kriyāste
vīrya mahāsuragaṇo'sthikulāni śailāḥ |
nāḍyaḥ saritsamudayāstaravakṣca roma
jīyādidaṃ puranirvacanīyamīśa ! || 9 ||
saṃsāreti | ayi cakradhara ! īśa! sarvaniyantaḥ ! saṃsāro jananamaraṇādiduḥkhaṃ, tadeva parivṛttisāmyāccakraṃ te kriyāḥ abhijñābhivadanopādānārthakriyārūpāḥ | mahatāmasurāṇāṃ gaṇaḥ samūhaḥ tava vīrya parākramaḥ | tavāsthinakhāni asthikulānīti vā pāṭhaḥ, śailāḥ parvatāḥ | nāḍyaḥ sirāḥ saritsamudayā nadītatayaḥ | roma tanūruhāṇi taravo vṛkṣāḥ | co'nuktasamuccaye | idam īdṛśaṃ vapuranirvacanīyam apariccheghaṃ jīyāt sarvotkarṣeṇa sahāviṣkurutādityarthaḥ || 9 ||
evaṃ viśvamūrtyupāsanāsiddhaye bhagavadrūpaṃ vibhṛśyāsyāmupāsanāyāmadhikāriṇa āha -- 
īdṛg jaganmayavapustava karmabhājāṃ
karmavasānasamaye smaraṇīyamāhuḥ |
tasyāntarātmavapuṣe vimalātmane te
vātālayādhipa ! namo'stu nirundhi rogān || 10||
