īdṛgiti | īdṛg uktaprakāraṃ tava jaganmayavapuḥ birāṅrūpaṃ karmabhājāṃ ka rmāvasānasamaye smaraṇīyamāhuḥ | karmāṇi śravaṇasmaraṇakīrtanādīni bhaktyaṅgāni bhajantīti karmabhājaḥ sādhanabhaktyadhikāriṇaḥ teṣāṃ karmaṇaḥ ṣoḍaśopacārāyāḥ pūjāyā avasānasamaye smaraṇīyaṃ smartuṃ śakyam | tadanyā niṣkalopāsanā śarīrābhimānibhiraśakyetyarthaḥ | taduktamarjunāya viśvarūpaṃ pradarśya 'mayyeca mana āṃdhatsva' iti, ‘avyaktā hi gatirduḥkhaṃ dehavadbhiravāpyate' iti ca | evaṃ triguṇātmakaviśvamūryupāsakaḥ kramācchuddhasattvamayamūrtyāpāsanāyāmadhikriyata iti darśayan bhagavantaṃ prārthayate -- tasyati | tasya tādṛśasya virājaḥ antarātmavapuṣe antaryāmirūpāya vimalātmane śuddhasattvamayamūrtaye te tubhyaṃ namo'stu | he vātālayādhipa ! evaṃ kramādupāsanāsāmarthyāya mama rogān nirundhi nāśayetyarthaḥ ||10||
iti virāḍdehasya jagadātmatvavarṇanaṃ
paṣṭaṃ daśakam |
evaṃ triguṇātmake bhagavadrupe sthiramanaso dviguṇātmakahiraṇyagarbhākhyabhagavadrūpaṃ darśayati -- 
evaṃ deva ! caturdaśātmakajagadūpeṇa jātaḥ punastasyordhva khalu satyalokanilaye jāto'si dhātā svayam |
yaṃ śaṃsanti hiraṇyagarbhamakhilatrailokyajīvātmakaṃ
yo'bhūt sphītarajovikāravikasannānāsisṛkṣārasaḥ || 1 ||
evamiti | atrāyamabhiprāyaḥ -- sthūlasūkṣmakrameṇa hi buddhayo'rthamavagāhanta iti prathamaṃ triguṇātmake bhagavadrūpe manaḥ praṇidhāya tatra sthiraṃ sad dviguṇātmake, tataḥ paraṃ sattvaikaguṇe, tataśca nirguṇe sūkṣme brahmaṇi praveśya niratiśayānandalābhena kṛtakṛtyatāmetīti | he deva! evaṃ caturdaśātmakajagadrūpeṇa jātastvaṃ svayaṃ khalu tvameva punaranantaraṃ tasya jagata ūrdhvam upari satyalokanilaye dhātā jāto'si | yaṃ tvāṃ hiraṇyagarbhaṃ śaṃsanti, śāstravida iti śeṣaḥ | yato'khilatrailokyasya virājo jīvaḥ pañcaprāṇamanovuddhidaśendriyātmakaliṅgaśarīrasamaṣṭayabhimānī, tadātmakaṃ tatsvarūpam | yaḥ sphītaiḥ pravṛddhaiḥ rajovikāraiḥ rajoguṇakāryaiḥ atra bhedabuddhirupakārabuddhī rāgādayaśca rajaḥkāryāṇi tairvikasan āvirbhavan nānā suranaratiryagādiśarīrāṇi tadbhogyāni ca sraṣṭumicchā sisṛkṣā tasyāṃ rasa āgraho yasya sa vikasannānāsisṛkṣārasaḥ atra brahmamūrtirviśvakarmaśāko darśitā
"haṃsārūḍhā caturvakrā taptakāñcanasannibhā |
śuklavastrā lambakarṇakūrcasaumyā caturbhujā ||
tundilā sottarīyopavītā yogapadāsanā |
jaṭāmakuṭaśobhāḍhya| piṅgalākṣī varapradā ||
kramaṇḍalākṣasūtraratrukeatruvapustakadhāriṇī |
prasannā brahmaṇo mūrtiścintanīyā surairapi" || 1 ||
so'yaṃ viśvavisargadattahṛdayaḥ saṃpakṣyamānaḥ svayaṃ
bodhaṃ khalvanavāpya viśvaviṣayaṃ cintākulastasthivān |
tāvat tvaṃ jagatāṃ pate ! tapa tapetyevaṃ hi vaihāyasīṃ
vāṇīmenamaśiśravaḥ śrutisukhāṃ kurvaṃstapaḥpreraṇām || 2 ||
sa iti | so'yaṃ viśveṣāṃ carācarāṇāṃ visarge guṇavaiṣamyād vividhe sarge sṛṣṭau dattahṛdayaḥ utsāhī san svayaṃ saṃpaśyamāno jijñāsamāno vicārayan viśvaviṣayaṃ bodhaṃ kimāśrayaṃ kiṃ vā kīdṛg vāhaṃ sṛjāmītyajānan cintākulastasthivān tasyau | he jagatāṃ pate ! lokotpattisthitilayakuśala ! tāvat tasminnava sare tvamenaṃ brahmāṇaṃ tapa tapetyevaṃ vāṇīṃ tapasi preraṇātmakaṃ vidhiṃ kurvan aśiśravaḥ śrāvitavānasi | kīdṛśīṃ vaihāyasīṃ vihāyasyabhivyaktām adarśitaśarīrāmityarthaḥ | athāpi śrutisukhāṃ śrotrānandadāyinīm || 2 ||
tadvacanānantaraṃ vidheḥ pravṛttimāha -- 
ko'sau māmavadat pumāniti jalāpūrṇe jaganmaṇḍale
dikṣūdrīkṣya kimapyanīkṣitavatā vākyārthamutpaśyatā |
divyaṃ varṣasahasramāttatapasā tena tvamārādhitastasmai darśitavānasi svanilayaṃ vaikuṇṭhamekādbhatam || 3
||
ko'sāviti | jaganmaṇḍale jalāpūrṇe sati ko'sau pumān māmavadat | jalamātraśeṣe jagati sati māṃ kaścidantarhito'yamīdṛśīṃ giramuktavān kila | sa pumān kaḥ na ca ko'pi nāstīti śakyaṃ vaktum | asau iti | vyaktasya vacasaḥ śravaṇāt ko'pi vaktāstītyanumayata eva | ataḥ sa draṣṭavya iti dikṣu catasṛṣūdvīkṣya tadvakṛdidṛkṣayā svasthānādutthāya parikrāman mukhamunnamayyāvalokya kimapyanīkṣitavatā na kevalaṃ vaktāraṃ pumāṃsa, jalāinyat kiñcidapi vastvadṛṣṭavatā tena punastadvākyārthe vicāryotpaśyatā jānatā tvaṃ tapaścara, tataste sṛṣṭisāmarthyaṃ bhaviṣyatīti māṃ pratīdagīśvarapreraṇāmiti niścinvānenetyarthaḥ| anantara ca
svādhiṣṇyamevādhiṣṭhāya divyaṃ varṣasahasramityatyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā | āttaṃ svīkṛtam anuṣṭhitamiti yāvat, tapo yena tādṛśena tena brahmaṇā ārādhitaḥ sevitastvaṃ tasmai brahmaṇe khanilayaṃ svadhiṣṇyabhūtaṃ vaikuṇṭhaṃ vaikuṇṭhalokaṃ darśitavānasi | kīdṛśam ekādbhutam ekaṃ mukhyamadbhutam āścaryam | yasmādadbhutāntaraṃ nāsti, tadekādbhutaṃ tādṛśamityarthaḥ|| 3||
ekādbhutatvaṃ pratipādayati māyeti tribhiḥ -- 
māyā yatra kadāpi no vikurute bhāte jagabhgho bahiḥ
śokakrodhavimohasādhvasamukhā bhāvāstu dūraṃ gatāḥ |
sāndrānandajharī ca yatra paramajyotiḥprakāśātmake
tat te dhāma vibhāvitaṃ vijayate vaikuṇṭharūpaṃ vibho ! || 4 ||
māyeti | yatra yasmin vaikuṇṭhanilaye māyā kadāpi brahmaṇa utpattau pralaye'pi no vikurute ātmanaḥ śarīrādeveṃśvarāt pṛthaktvena pradarśanarūpaṃ kārya na karotītyarthaḥ | tathāca sati kāmakrodhādisambhavābhāvaḥ kimu vaktavya ityāha -- śokakrodhavimohasādhvasamukhā bhāvāstu dūraṃ gatā iti | śokaḥ pīḍā, krodho'marṣaḥ, vimohaḥ śarīrādipvahaṃmamābhimānaḥ, sādhvasaṃ maraṇādibhayam etadādyā bhāvāḥ padārthāstu dūraṃ gatāḥ, yasmāditi śeṣaḥ | yatra ca sāndrasya ghanībhūtasyānandasya jharī pravāhaḥ, vartata ityarthāt | yatra sthitānāṃ sarvadā brahmānandānubhava eva bhavatītyarthaḥ| nanu satyaloke'pyasti leśataḥ śokaḥ | tadekadeśaśced vaikuṇṭhalokaḥ, kathaṃkāraṃ kleśaleśarāhityamityāśaṅkāyāmāha -- jagabhgho bahirbhāta iti | caturdaśabhyo lokebhyo bahirbhāte śobhita ityarthaḥ | ata eva paramajyotiḥprakāśātmake, nātrādityacandrādijyotiṣāṃ prakāśaḥ, kintu teṣāmapi prakāśakaṃ jyotiḥ paramajyotiḥ, ādityādiprakāśakatvapradaṃ jyotirbrahma, tadeva prakāśo yatra sa paramajyotiḥprakāśaḥ, tadātmake tādṛśasvarūpe | he vibho ! viśvavyāpin ! vibhāvitaṃ
tvayā brahmaṇe darśitaṃ tat tathāvidhaṃ vaikuṇṭhākhyaṃ vaikuṇṭhākhyaṃ te tava dhāma vijayate
sarvotkṛṣṭatvena vartata ityarthaḥ || 4 ||
yasmin lāna caturbhujā harimaṇiśyāmāvadātatviṣo
nānābhūpaṇaratnaṃdīpitadiśo rājadvimānālayāḥ |
bhaktiprāptatathāvidhonnatapadā dīvyanti divyā janāstat te dhāma nirastasarvaśamalaṃ vaikuṇṭharūpaṃ jayet || 5 ||
yasminniti | yasmin loke divyāḥ śuddhasattvamayamūrtayo bhaktā janā bhavatsārūpyaṃ prāptā dīvyanti dyotante | nanu kiṃ kurvanto vaikuṇṭhaṃ gamipyantītyata āha -- bhaktīti | phalābhisandhirahitayā premalakṣaṇayā bhaktyā prāptaṃ labdhaṃ tathāvidham unnataṃ ca padaṃ sthānaṃ yaiste , tādṛśāḥ santo dīvyantīti bhagavatsārūpyasya prāptiḥ karmaṇā na bhavatīti dyotayitum | bhagavatsārūpyamevāha caturbhujā ityādi | harimaṇirindranīlamaṇiḥ, tadvacchyāmā cāsāvavadātā svacchā ca tviṭ śobhā yeṣāṃ te | kiñca, nānāvidheṣu bhūṣaṇeṣu makuṭamakarakuṇḍalamañjīrādiṣu pratyuptānītyarthād yāni ratnāni taidīṃpitadiśo dyotitadigantarālāḥ | rājanti maṇikiṅkiṇījālamālāvitānādibhiḥ śobhamānāni vimānāni vyomayānāni ālayā yeṣāṃ te, tādṛśāśca santo dīvyanti | yatraivaṃ, tat tādṛśaṃ te tava nirastāni parākṛtāni sarvāṇi śamalānyajñānatatkāryāṇi yena tad vaikuṇṭharūpaṃ dhāma jayet sarvotkṛṣṭatayā prakāśatāmityarthaḥ || 5 ||
nānādivyavadhūjanairabhivṛtā vighullatātulyayā
viśvonmādanahudyagātralatayā vidyotitāśāntarā |
tvatpādāmbujasaurabhaikakutukālakṣmīḥ svayaṃ lakṣyate
yasmin vismayanīyadivyavibhavā tat te padaṃ dehi me || 6 ||
nāneti| yasmin tvatpādāmbujayoḥ saurabhe saurabhye ekakutukād avyabhicāriṇaḥ kautukādeva hetoḥ lakṣmīḥ viśvamātā mahālakṣmarbhigavatī svayaṃ svātmanā
lakṣyate | tatratyānāṃ nayanagocarībhavati | kīdṛśī, nānādivyavadhūjanaiḥ bahuvidhadivyāṅganāsahassrairabhivṛtā parivāritā | vighullatātulyayā sudhāliptavighutsamayā | viśvonmādanahṛdyā cāsau gātralatā ceti karmadhārayaḥ, tathā karaṇena vidyotitāśāntarā prakāśitadigantarālā satī | kiñca, vismayanīyā itaratrādṛṣṭaśrutapūrvāḥ divyāḥ sāttvikā vibhavā upakaraṇāni yasyāḥ sā tādṛśī ca satī lakṣyata iti | atha vismayanīyadivyavibhavamiti cet pāṭhaḥ, tadā te tat padamiti sambandhaḥ | tanme dehi tad vaikuṇṭhapadaṃ māmatyutsukaṃ prāpaya, kā te hāniriti śeṣaḥ || 6 ||
prasaṅge sati padyatrayeṇa vaikuṇṭhapadaṃ varṇayitvā svasya tatprāpyutkaṇṭhāmapi prakāśya prakṛte'vatarati -- 
tatraivaṃ pratidarśite nijapade ratnāsanādhyāsitaṃ
bhāsvatkoṭilasatkirīṭakaṭakāyākalpīkṛti |
śrīvatsāṅkitamāttakaustubhamaṇicchāyāruṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ
viśveṣāṃ tava rūpamaikṣata vidhistat te vibho ! bhātu me || 7 ||
tatreti | tatra tasmin evam uktaprakāreṇa pratidarśite prasādena prakāśite, tvayeti śeṣaḥ | nijapade ātmaloke vidhirbrahmā tava rūpamaikṣata dṛṣṭavān | kīdṛśaṃ tadityata āha -- ratnāsanāvyāsitaṃ maṇisiṃhāsanopaviṣṭaṃ| bhāsvaditi | koṭisaṅkhaparimitādityabimbasannipātaballasadbhiḥ śobhamānaiḥ kirīṭakaṭakādibhirākalpairbhūṣaṇairdīṃprā ākṛtiryasya tat, śrīvatsena bhṛgupadanyāsāṅkenāṅkitam, āttasya svīkṛtasya kaustubhamaṇeḥ kaustubhākhyaratnasyacchāyayā śobhayā sarvāṅgīṇayā aruṇaṃ pāṭalaṃ prāptaparabhāgamityarthaḥ, viśveṣāṃ carācarāṇāṃ kāraṇam upādānakāraṇabhūtaṃ yat tad rūpaṃ brahmā dṛṣṭavān | he vibho! viśvavyāpin ! tat tādṛśaṃ te tava rūpaṃ me bhātu mamāpi pratyakṣaṃ bhavatu | idamatrākūtaṃ -- svasthānasthitāya brahmaṇe yaḥ prasīdan ātmīyamadbhutaṃ baikuṇṭhalokaṃ, tatra ratnagṛhe maṇimayāsanopaviṣṭaṃ prakṛṣṭadivyākalpaprabhāpaṭalapāṭalaṃ svarūpaṃ ca yaḥ pradarśitavānāsi, tasya te matpurato'pyevaṃ prādurbhavituṃ prayāso nāstyeva | ato'haṃ prārthayetarāṃ tanme bhātviti || 7 ||
yadyapyetāvat puṇyaṃ mama nāsti, tathāpyetat prārthaya ityāha -- 
kālāmbhodakalāyakomalarucī cakreṇa cakraṃ diśāmāvṛṇvānamudāramandahasitasyandaprasannānanam |
rājatkambugadāripaṅkajadharaśrīmadbhajāmaṇḍalaṃ
ratraṣṭustuṣṭikaraṃ vapustava vibho ! madrogamudrāsayet || 8 ||
kāleti | kālāmbhodavat kalāyakusumavacca komalā yā rucyaḥ śobhāḥ, tāsāṃ cakreṇa maṇḍalena diśāṃ cakraṃ maṇḍalam āvṛṇvānam ācchādayad udārasya atimanoharasya mandahasitasya syandena nirantaraprasareṇa prasannamānanaṃ yatra, tat tathā virājamānaśaṅkhagadācakrāmbujadhāri śrīmat śobhānityayuktaṃ bhujāmaṇḍalaṃ yasmistat sraṣṭurbrahmaṇaḥ tuṣṭikaraṃ he vibho ! tava vapurmadrogaṃ mamāmayam udbāsayed uccāṭayed mama manasi tvadrupamāvirbhaved yadi, madrogāḥ pātakahetukāḥ palāyeranneva |
" smṛte manasi govinde dūrato yānti pātakāḥ |
udayācalamārūḍhe dinanāthe tamo yathā ||"
iti vacanāditi tadapyāśāmyata ityarthaḥ || 8 ||
prasaṅgavaśādautsukyenaivamāśāsya prastutamavismarannāha -- 
dṛṣṭvā sambhṛtasambhramaḥ kamalabhūstvatpādapāyoruhe
harṣāveśavaśaṃvado nipatitaḥ prītyā kṛtārthībhavan |
jānāsyeva manīṣitaṃ mama vibho ! jñānaṃ tadāpāḍhya
dvaitādvaitabhavatsvarūpaparamityācaṣṭa taṃ tvāṃ bhaje || 9 ||
dṛṣveti | kamalabhūḥ brahmā tava tad vapurddaṣṭvā sambhṛtasambhramaḥ jhaṭiti kartavyeṣūpacārapūjānatinutyādiṣvitikartavyatāmauḍhyamāpādayatā sambhrameṇa kañcit kālaṃ kiñcidapi kartumakṣamo'vasthitaḥ san harṣasya bhagavatsannidhānajanyasyāveśasya vaśaṃvadaḥ adhīnaśca san tvatpādapāthoruhe tava pādābje nipatitaḥ sāṣṭāṅgaṃ daṇḍavat patitaḥ| punaścotthāya prītyā kṛtārthībhavannityācaṣṭa uktavān | he vibho! tvaṃ mama manīṣitaṃ hṛdgataṃ jānāsyeva, sarvajanabuddhisākṣitvāt | tathāpi vakṣyāmītyabhiprāyeṇāha -- 
kṛdikārādaktina iti ṅīṣa |
dvaitādvaitaṃ yad bhavatsvarūpaṃ parāvare brahmaṇī, tatparaṃ tadviṣayaṃ tad yathārthaṃ jñānam āpādaya mayyanugraheṇotpādayeti yamācaṣṭa, taṃ tvāṃ bhaje || 9 ||
ātāmre caraṇe vinamramatha taṃ hastena haste spṛśan
bodhaste bhavitā na sargavidhibhirvandho'pi sañjāyate |
ityābhāṣya giraṃ pratoṣya nitarāṃ taccittagūḍhaḥ svayaṃ
sṛṣṭau taṃ samudairayaḥ sa bhagavannullāsayollāghatām || 10 ||
ātāmra iti | ( atha sa bhagavān ?) ātāmre aruṇasarojavadīṣattāmravarṇe caraṇe viśeṣaṇe bhaktiviśvāsabahumānādibhiḥ saha namra namanaśīlaṃ taṃ brahmāṇaṃ sakhā sakhāyamiva hastena haste spṛśan te bodho bhavitā yat prārthitaṃ tad jñānaṃ tavācirādeva bhaviṣyati, kiñca sargavidhibhiḥ sṛṣṭivyāpāraiḥ bandhaḥ śarīrabandho'pi na sañjāyata iti giramābhāṣya sānusaraṇamukttvā nitarām atiśayena pratopya santuṣṭaṃ kṛtvā svayaṃ taccittagūḍhaḥ tasya brahmaṇaḥ citta evāntarhitaḥ san taṃ brahmāṇaṃ sṛṣṭau samudairayaḥ brahmaṇo hṛdyantaryāmirūpeṇa sthitvā sṛṣṭau taṃ samyak preritavān yaḥ, sa tvaṃ bhagavan ! ullāghatāṃ mamārogyam ullāsaya samyak saṃpādayetyarthaḥ || 10 || 45 ||
triguṇaṃ dviguṇaṃ śuddhasattvaikaguṇanirguṇam |
dvitīye saphalaṃ rūpamupāsyamupavarṇitam ||
iti hiraṇyagarbhotpattitapaścaraṇavaikuṇṭhasvarūpabhagavatsvarūpasākṣātkāra -- bhagavadanugrahavarṇanaṃ
saptamaṃ daśakam |
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyākhyāyāṃ
dvitīyaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
uktarūpeśvaropāstivighnajālanivṛttaye |
viṣṇulīlānusandheyā tatra sargo nirūpyate ||
evaṃ mumukṣūṇāmupāsanāsiddhaye samarthā samādhisāmagrī darśitā | tatra yogāntarāyā vyādhistyānasaṃśayapramādālasyāviratibhrāntimanolabdhabhūmikatvānavasthitatvāni duḥkhadaurmanasyāṅgakampaśvāsapraśvāsāḥ tatsahabhuvaśca | tatra vyādhirdhyānākalyatā | aviratirviṣayecchā | alabdhabhūmikatvaṃ samādhibhūmeralābhaḥ | samādherutthānadaśāyāmeteṣāṃ śamanārthamiṣṭāvāptyarthaṃ ca bhagavadavatāralīlānusandhānaṃ kartavyam | taduktaṃ -- 
"janma karma ca me divyamevaṃ yo vetti tattvataḥ |
tyaktvā dehaṃ punarjanma naiti māmeti so'rjuna ! ||" 
iti | tatra līlā daśavidhā -- 
"atra sargo visargaśca sthānaṃ poṣaṇamūtayaḥ |
manvantareśānukathā nirodho muktirāśrayaḥ ||"
iti vacanāt | tatrāpi prathamaṃ bhagavataḥ sṛṣṭilīlāṃ tadupayoginastatprāsaṅgikāṃśca varāhakapilacatuḥsanaśrī rudranāradamanvādīnavatārān darśayitumārabhate -- 
evaṃ tāvat prākṛtaprakṣayānte brāhme kalpe hyādime labdhajanmā |
brahmā bhūyastvatta evāpya vedān sṛṣṭiṃ cakre pūrvakalpopamānām || 1 ||
evamiti | prākṛtaprakṣayo mahāpralayaḥ, tasyānte avasāne sati ādime brāhme kalpe śabdabrahmākhyasyedānīntanasya brahmaṇa ādidinārambhe tvatto viṣṇorlabdhajanmā jātaḥ san brahmā bhūyaḥ punaḥ tvatta eva vedān ṛgādīn āpya labdhvā pūrvakalpopamānāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ cakre, yathartuṣu ṛtuliṅgāni ekarūpāṇi tathā pratikalpaṃ sṛṣṭisāmyamiti mahābhāratokteruttarottareṣu kalpeṣu pūrvapūrvakalpasamānāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ bahuśo'sau kṛtavānityarthaḥ || 1 ||
so'yaṃ caturyugasahasramitānyahāni
tāvanmitākṣca rajanīrbahuśo nināya |
nidrātyasau tvayi nilīya samaṃ svasṛṣṭainaimittikapralayamāhurato'sya rātrim || 2 ||
sa iti | so'yaṃ brahmā caturyugāṇāṃ saharatrasaṅkhayayā mitāni paricchinnāni ahāni dināni kalpākhyāni tāvanmitāḥ tatsamapramāṇā rajanī rātrīśca bahuśo nināya bahuvāraṃ nītavān | asau khasṛṣṭaiścarācaraiḥ samaṃ saha tvayi śrīnārāyaṇe nilīya nidrātīti, ahaḥkṣaye hi brahmā svamṛṣṭaṃ carācaraṃ saṅgṛhṇannekārṇave'hīndratalpamadhiśayānena śrīnārāyaṇena sahaikībhūya nidrāṃ gacchati | ataḥ pralayasya brahmaṇaḥ svāpanimittatvādasya rātriṃ naimittikapralayamāhurvadantīti || 2 ||
asmādṛśāṃ punaraharmukhakṛtyatulyāṃ
sṛṣṭiṃ karotyanudinaṃ sa bhavatprasādāt |
prāg brāhmakalpajanuṣāṃ ca parāyuṣāṃ tu
suptaprabodhanasamāsti tadā visṛṣṭiḥ || 3 ||
asmādṛśāmiti | sa punarbrahmā asmādṛśāmaharmukhe yāni kṛtyāni śaucācamanaprāṇāyāmasandhyāvandanajapahomādīnyanudinamanuṣṭheyāni karmāṇi, tattulyāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ so'pi bhavatprasādādanudinaṃ karoti | atra ca prāk pūrva brāhme kalpe janurjanma yeṣāṃ te tathā, param aticirakālam āyuryeṣāṃ teṣāṃ mārkaṇḍeyādīnāmapi tadā naimittikapalyāvasāne suptaprabodhanasamā svāpaprabodhatulyā| nanu teṣāmapi brahmaṇa iva suptaprabodhau sta ityeva vācyam | kimiti kalpādau sṛṣṭirastītyucyate, maivaṃ, teṣāṃ brahmaṇi līnatvāt punaśca brahmaṇaiva sṛṣṭatvāñca | brahmaṇaḥ punaḥ brahmamṛṣṭayabhāvāt svāpaprabodhāveva | nanu tarhyasmādṛśāmiva sṛṣṭirevāstu, kimarthamucyate suptaprabodhanasameti, maivaṃ, prākkalpaviṣayānusmaraṇānapāyāt suptaprabodhena tulyatvam | asmādṛśāṃ tu tadabhāvāt sṛṣṭireveti bheda iti yuktamuktam || 3 ||
pañcāśadabdamadhunā svavayordharūpamekaṃ parārdhamativṛtya hi vartate'sau |
tatrāntyarātrijanitān kathayāmi bhūman !
paścād dināvataraṇe ca bhavadvilāsān || 4 ||
pañcāśaditi | asau brahmā adhunā pañcāśad abdāḥ saṃvatsarāḥ pramāṇaṃ yasya tat pañcāśadabdaṃ svastha vayaso dviparārdhalakṣaṇasyārdharūpaṃ pūrvārdhātmakamekaṃ parārdhamativṛtya atikramya vartate | hiśabdaḥ prasiddhau | ayaṃ vārāhaḥ kalpo dvitīyaparārdhasyādiriti prasiddha ityarthaḥ | tatra teṣu kalpeṣvādimo brāhmaḥ kathitaḥ | punaśca antyarātrijanitāniti samanantarātītaḥ pādmaḥ kalpaḥ, tasmādarvācīnā rātriḥ prathamaparārdhasyāntyarātriḥ, tasyāṃ janitān paścāt punaśca dināvataraṇe (pādma? vārāha) kalpārambhe ca he bhūman! kāladeśāparicchinna ! ye bhavadvilāsāḥ bhavataḥ sargapralayasambandhinyo līlāḥ, tān vilāsān kathayāmi stotumārabha ityarthaḥ ||
dināvasāne'tha sarojayoniḥ suṣuptikāmastvayi sannililye |
jaganti ca tvajjaṭharaṃ samīyustadedamekārṇavamāsa viśvam || 5 ||
dināvasāna iti | atha sarojayonirbrahmā dināvasāne prākkalpāvasāne suṣuptikāmaḥ tvayi śrīnārāyaṇe sannililye nilīnaḥ san nidrāṃ kṛtavān | jaganti lokāśca tadā tvajjaṭharaṃ tavodaradeśaṃ samīyuḥ brahmaṇā saha prāptāḥ | tadā pralaye idaṃ viśvam ekārṇavaṃ jalamātraśeṣam āsa abhūt || 5 ||
tavaiva veṣe phaṇirāji śeṣe jalaikaśeṣe bhuvane sma śeṣe |
ānandasāndrānubhavasvarūpaḥ svayoganidrāparimudritātmā || 6 ||
taveti | evaṃ bhuvane jalaikaśeṣe jalamātraśeṣe sati tvaṃ tava veṣe avasthābhedarūpa eva śeṣe anantākhye phaṇirāji nāgaśreṣṭhe śeṣe sma aśayiṣṭhāḥ | ānandaścāsau sāndraśca sa evāsāvanubhavaḥ samyagjñānaṃ tadrūpaḥ | atra sāndratvamānandānubhavetararūparāhityaṃ saccidānandasvarūpa ityarthaḥ | yogaḥ svasvarūpānusandhānaṃ sa eva nidreva nidrā yoganidrā svīyayā yoganidrayā parimudritaḥ samyag lāñchitaḥ ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya sa tathā || 6||
āsa pracakāśe | ‘asa gatidīghyādāneṣu' | bhvādiḥ | āsetyavyayaṃ vā tiṅpratirūpamastyarthakam |
kālākhyaśaktiṃ pralayāvasāne prabodhayetyādiśatā kilādau |
tvayā prasuptaṃ parisuptaśaktivrajena tatrākhilajīvadhāmnā || 7 ||
kāleti | tatrādau pralayārambhe parisuptaśaktitrajena parisupto nilīnaḥ śaktīnāṃ mūlaprakṛtimahadahaṅkārādīnāṃ vrajaḥ samūho yasmin, tathābhūtena akhilajīvadhāmnā saṃsāraduḥkhakhinnānāṃ jīvānāmaticirakālaṃ viśramasthānabhūtena kālākhyaśaktiṃ pralayāvasāne caturyugasahasralakṣaṇāyā rātreravasāne sati tvaṃ māṃ prabodhayetyādiśatā ājñāpayatā ca tvayā tathā kālākhyaśaktacā saha prasuptaṃ śaktiśaktimatorabhedamavalambya sthitam | yathā kaścid bahubhāryaḥ puruṣaḥ itarāsu prasuptāsu dayitayā saha kañcit kālaṃ vasan suprabodhāṃ tāṃ prabhāte tvaṃ māṃ prabodhayetyādiśya tayaiva saha gāḍhagāḍhamāliṅgaya susukhaṃ nidrāṃ gacchati, tadvaditi bhāvaḥ || 7 ||
caturyugāṇāṃ ca sahasramevaṃ tvayi prasupte punaradvitīye |
kālākhyaśaktiḥ prathamaprabuddhā prābodhayat tvāṃ kila viśvanātha ! || 8 ||
caturyugāṇāmiti | advitīye tvayi evaṃ caturyugāṇāṃ sahasraṃ yāvatpralayāvasānaṃ prasupte sati punaḥ kālākhyaśaktiḥ prathamaprabuddhā he viśvanātha ! tvāṃ prābodhayacca | kilaśabdo'tra kevalapurāṇaprasiddhiṃ dyotayati || 8 ||
vibudhya ca tvaṃ jalagarbhaśāyin ! vilokya lokānakhilān pralīnān |
teṣveva sūkṣmātmatayā nijāntaḥsthiteṣu viśveṣu dadātha dṛṣṭim || 9 ||
vibudhyeti | he jalagarbhaśāyin ! āvaraṇodakamadhyaśāyin ! tvaṃ prabudhya akhilān lokān tvāyi pralīnān vilokya | nanu pralīnānāṃ kuto vilokanasambhavastatrāha -- teṣviti | sūkṣmātmatayā kāraṇarūpeṇa nijāntaḥ sthiteṣu teṣveva viśveṣu lokeṣu dṛṣṭiṃ dadātha ca dattavān īkṣitavānityarthaḥ || 9 ||
nābhipadmadvārā prapañcasṛṣṭiprakāramāha -- 
tatastvadīyādayi ! nābhirandhrādudañcitaṃ kiñcana divyapadmam |
nilīnaniśśeṣapadārthamālāsaṅkṣeparūpaṃ mukulāyamānam || 10 ||
'tayādau' iti vyākhyātṛpāṭhaḥ syāt |
tata iti | ayi ! bhagavan ! tataḥ tava vilokanānantaraṃ tvadīyād nābhirandhrāt kiñcana divyaṃ prakāśabahulatvāt sattvamayaṃ padmam udañcitam udbhūtam | niśśeṣāṇāṃ jīvatadupabhogyānāṃ padārthānāṃ mālā samūhaḥ, tasya saṅkṣepaḥ sūkṣmarūpeṇāvasthānaṃ tadrūpaṃ niśśeṣaprapañcabījarūpamityarthaḥ | mukulāyamānaṃ mukulāvasthayā sthitam || 10 ||
tadetadambhoruhakuḍmalaṃ te kalevarāt toyapathe prarūḍham |
bahirnirītaṃ paritaḥ sphuradbhiḥ svadhāmabhirdhvāntamalaṃ nyakṛntat || 11 ||
tadetaditi | tadetadambhoruhasya kuḍmalaṃ mukulaṃ te tava śrīnārāyaṇasya kalebarāt śarīrāt toyapathe parito jalāvṛtatvājalapradeśe prarūḍham aṅkuritam | kramājjalamatikramya bahirnirītam | tataśca paritaḥ sphuradbhiḥ svadhāmabhiḥ nijadīptibhiḥ dhvāntamalaṃ tamorūpaṃ malaṃ, yadvā alam atiśayena dhvāntaṃ nyakṛntad unmūlitavadityarthaḥ || 11 ||
saṃphullapatre nitarāṃ vicitre tasmin bhavadvīryadhṛte saroje |
sa padmajanmā vidhirāvirāsīt svayaṃmabuddhākhilavedarāśiḥ || 12 ||
samphullapatra iti | samyak phullāni vikasitāni patrāṇi dalāni yasmistat tathā nitarām atiśayena vicitre asāmānye bhavata īśvarasya vīryeṇa yogacalena dhṛte pratiṣṭhite tasmin saroje sa tvameva vidhirāvirāsīd brahmaṇo rūpaṃ gṛhītvā prāviśadityarthaḥ | ata eva padmajanmeti nāmāpyāsīt | kiñca svayam upadeśanirapekṣaṃ prabuddhākhilavedarāśiḥ arthāvabodhaparyantamavagatanigamasamūhaḥ, svataḥ sarvajñaścābhūdityarthaḥ || 12 ||
asmin parātman ! nanu pādmakalpe tvamitthamutthāpita padmayoniḥ |
anantabhūmā mama rogarāśiṃ nirundhi vātālayavāsa! viṣṇo ! || 13 ||
asminniti | parātman ! he paramātman! jagatkāraṇabhūta ! asmin nanu pādmakalpe itthamuktaprakāreṇa utthāpitaḥ sṛṣṭaḥ padmayoniryena sa tathā anantabhūmā
aparicchinnamahimā tvaṃ mama rogarāśiṃ nirundhi mama duṣkṛtairitaḥparamapyāgacchantaṃ vyādhinivahaṃ nivārayetyarthaḥ || 13 ||
iti pralayajagatsṛṣṭiprakāravarṇanam aṣṭamaṃ daśakaṃ satrikam |
evamutpannasya brahmaṇaḥ pravṛttiṃ kathayati -- 
sthitaḥ sa kamalodbhavastava hi nābhipaṅkeruhe
kutaḥ svididamambudhāvuditamityanālokayan |
tadīkṣaṇakutūhalāt pratidiśaṃ vivṛttānanakṣcaturvadanatāmagād vikasadaṣṭadṛṣṭayambujām || 1 ||
sthita iti | sa hi kamalodbhavaḥ tava nābhipaṅkeruhe tatkarṇikāyāṃ sthitaḥ san idam ambujaṃ kutaḥ kasmādadhiṣṭhānāt | sviditi vitarke | ambudhau ekārṇave uditam utpannam ityetadanālokayan ajājan tadīkṣaṇe ambujasyotpattisthānarvākṣiṇe kutūhalāt kautukāt pratidiśaṃ catasṛṣu dikṣu vivṛttaṃ vivalitam ānanaṃ yatya sa tathā | caturvadanatāmagāditi | catasṛṣu dikṣu yugapadvīkṣaṇakautuke sati catvāri ca vadanānyabhūvannityarthaḥ | vikasanti unmīlitāni aṣṭau dṛṣṭyambujāni netrakamalāni yasyāstāṃ caturvadanatāmagāditi sambandhaḥ || 1 ||
mahārṇavavighūrṇitaṃ kamalameva tat kevalaṃ
vilokya tadupāśrayaṃ tava tanuṃ tu nālokayan |
ka eṣa kamalodare mahati nissahāyo hyahaṃ
kutaḥ svididamambujaṃ samajanīti cintāmagāt || 2 ||
mahārṇaveti | mahārṇave vicūrṇitaṃ kampitaṃ kevalaṃ tat kamalameva vilokya tadupāśrayaṃ tadadhiṣṭhānaṃ tatra tanuṃ tu nālokayan apaśyan iti itthaṃ cintāmagāt | cintāprakāramāha -- ka eṣa iti | mahati kamalodare nissahāya eṣo'iṃ hyeva kaḥ | kiñcedamambujaṃ kutaḥsvit samajanītyevaṃ cintāmagāt || 2 ||
punaścaivaṃ niścita vānityāha -- 
amuṣya hi saroruhaḥ kimapi kāraṇaṃ sambhaveditisma kṛtāniścayaḥ sa khalu nālarandhrādhvanā |
sayogavalavidyayā samavarūḍhavān prauḍhadhīstvadīyamatimohanaṃ na tu kalebaraṃ dṛṣṭavān || 3 ||
amuṣyeti | amuṇya madadhiṣṭhitasya saroruhaḥ sarojasyādhastāt kimapi kāraṇam adhiṣṭhānaṃ sambhavet | sambhāvanāyāṃ liṅ | itisma kṛtaniścayaḥ sa khalu brahmā nālarandhrādhvanā kamaladaṇḍasya randhraṃ suṣirameva adhvā mārgaḥ, tena samavarūḍhavān avarohaṇaṃ kṛtavān | kathamasyaitadupāyabodha ityata āha -- prauḍhadhīriti | prauḍhā ūhāpohanipuṇā dhīryasya sa tathā | nanu buddhisāmarthye satyapi susūkṣmarandhrapraveśaḥ kathaṃ sambhavedityata āha -- sayogabalavidyayeti | yogabalaṃ tapobalaṃ vidyā ātmajñānam | yogabalasahitayā vidyayeti | tapasā vidyayā ca prāptaiśvaryatayātisūkṣmaśarīro bhūtvā tatsaroruhanālarandhraṃ praviṣṭayānityarthaḥ | tadapi tvadīyamatimohanaṃ lokottarasaundaryayuktaṃ kalebaraṃ mūrti bahirmukhairvyāpārairduppāpaṃ na tu dṛṣṭavān naiva dadarśa || 3 ||
tataḥ sakalanālikāvivaramārgago mārgayan
prayasya śatavatsaraṃ kimapi naiva sandṛṣṭavān |
nivṛtya kamalodare sukhaniṣaṇṇa ekāgradhīḥ
samādhibalamādadhe bhavadanugrahaikāgrahī || 4 ||
tata iti | tataḥ anantaraṃ sakalāni nālikāvivarāṇi nālarandhrāṇyeva mārgāḥ, tān gacchatīti sakalanālikāvivaramārgagaḥ mārgayan anveṣaṇaṃ kurvan | ayamarthaḥ -- kamalanālasya bahurandhratvādekena randhreṇāvaruhyānviṣyādṛṣṭvā punaranyena punaścānyenaivamatandrito'nviṣṭavānityarthaḥ | evaṃ śatavatsaraṃ divyasaṃvatsarāṇāṃ śataṃ prayasya prayāsaṃ kṛtvā kamalasyāśrayatvena kimapi vastu naiva sandṛṣṭavān | nivṛtyeti īśvarasyānugrahaṃ vinā kimapi jñātuṃ kartuṃ vāśakyamiti niścitya bahirmukhāt prayāsānnivṛtya viramya kamalodare sukhaniṣaṇṇa ityaṣṭāṅgopalakṣaṇam | ekāgradhīriti dhāraṇocyate | samādhibalaṃ samādheścitaikāgryasya balam acāñcalyam ādadhe kṛtavān | bhavadanugraha ekasminnevāgraho'syāstīti bhavadanugrahaikāgrahī || 4 ||
evamantardṛṣṭyānveṣaṇe kṛte tu bhavantaṃ dṛṣṭavānityāha -- 
śatena parivatsarairdṛḍhasamādhibandhollasatprabodhaviśadīkṛtaḥ sa khalu padminīsambhavaḥ |
adṛṣṭacaramadbhutaṃ tava hi rūpamantardṛśā vyacaṣṭa parituṣṭadhīrbhujagabhogabhāgāśrayam || 5 ||
śateneti | śatena parivatsaraiḥ divyasaṃvatsaraiḥ śatena sa khalu padminīsambhavo brahmā dṛḍhena niścalena samādheścittaikāgryasya bandhena cittatadviṣayayorekībhāvalakṣaṇena grathanena ullasatā śobhamānena prabodhena dhyātṛdhyeyadhyānabhedabhānābhāvādekātmaviṣayeṇa jñānena aviśado viśadaḥ kṛta iti viśadīkṛtaḥ ajñānamālinyāstamayena vimalaḥ san tava hi rūpaṃ tava viṣṇoreva sākṣāt svarūpam antardṛśā bhagavatānugṛhītena divyena cakṣuṣā vyacaṣṭa dṛṣṭavān | kīdṛśam, adṛṣṭacaram itaḥ pūrva mahatā prayāsenāpi yanna dṛṣṭaṃ tad | adbhutam atyāścaryaṃ rūpaṃ bhujagabhogabhāgāśrayaṃ bhujagasya anantākhyabhujagasya bhogaḥ śarīraṃ tasya bhāgaḥ tatkalpitatalpadeśaḥ sa āśrayo yasya tat tathā | tat tava rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā parituṣṭadhīḥ santoṣātiśayena paravaśo'bhavadityarthaḥ || 5 ||
tadeva rūpaṃ varṇayati
kirīṭamakuṭollasat kaṭakahārakeyūrayug
maṇisphuritamekhalaṃ suparivītapītāmbaram |
kalāyakusumaprabhaṃ galatalollasatkaustubhaṃ
vapustadayi ! bhāvaye kamalajanmane darśitam || 6 ||
kirīṭeti | mūrdhni puraḥ pṛṣṭhataścālaṅkṛtābhyāṃ kirīṭamakuṭābhyām ullasat śobhamānam | kaṭakaiḥ prakoṣṭhasthavalayaiḥ, hāraiḥ muktāhāraiḥ, aṃsadeśālaṅkṛtaiḥ keyūraiśca yujyata iti kaṭakahārakeyūrayuk | tat | maṇibhiḥ parārdhyanānāratnanikaraiḥ sphuritā ujjvalā mekhalā yasmiṃstad maṇisphuritamekhalam | suparivītam atimohanaveṣaviśiṣṭatayā parivītaṃ parihitaṃ pītāmbaraṃ yasmiṃstat tathā | ayi ! bhagavan! tvayā kamalajanmane yad darśitaṃ nayanagocarīkṛtaṃ, tad vapuḥ sampratyahaṃ bhāvaye kevalaṃ smarāmītyarthaḥ || 6 ||
evaṃ bhagavadrūpadarśanānantaraṃ vidheḥ pravṛttiṃ varṇayan bhagavantaṃ prārthayate -- 
śrutiprakaradarśitapracuravaibhava ! śrīpate !
hare ! jaya jaya prabho ! padamupaiṣi diṣṭyā dṛśoḥ |
kuruṣva ghiyamāśu me bhuvananirmitī karmaṭhāmiti druhiṇavarṇitasvaguṇabaṃhimā pāhi mām || 7 ||
śrutīti | śrutiprakaraiḥ upaniṣadvākyanikaraiḥ darśitaṃ lakṣaṇayā pratipāditaṃ pracuravaibhavam atiśayena mahattvaṃ yasya | athavā vaibhavaṃ jagatsṛṣṭyādisāmarthya śrutibhirjagatkāraṇatvena pratipāditamityarthaḥ | śrīpate! jagadanugrāhaka ! hare! jagatsaṃhartaḥ! tvaṃ jaya utkarṣamāviṣkuru | harṣāveśasambhramād dviruktiḥ | he vibho ! tvaṃ mama dṛśoḥ padaṃ gocaram upaiṣi prāpto'si diṣṭyā aho mama sukhaṃ jātam | mamāśu bhuvananirmitau jagatsṛṣṭau karmaṭhāṃ samarthāṃ dhiyaṃ kuruṣva jagannirmāṇetikartavyatāviṣayaṃ jñānamanugṛhāṇetyarthaḥ | iti druhiṇena brahmaṇā varṇitaḥ saṃstutaḥ svaguṇānām aiśvaryādīnāṃ baṃhimā bāhulyaṃ yasya sa tvaṃ tvadguṇāneva varṇayantaṃ pāhi druhiṇasyevātmānaṃ puraḥ pradarśya kṛtārthīkurvityarthaḥ || 7 ||
atha stuvato'sya bhagavadanugrahaprakāramāha -- 
labhasva bhuvanatrayīracanadakṣatāmakṣatāṃ
gṛhāṇa madanugrahaṃ kuru tapaśca bhūyo vidhe ! |
bhavatvakhilasādhanī mayi ca bhaktiratyutkaṭetyudīrya giramādadhā muditacetasaṃ vedhasam || 8 ||
labhasveti | ayi vidhe! tvam akṣatām anapāyinīṃ bhuvanatrayīracanadakṣatāṃ trailokyanirmāṇasāmarthyaṃ labhasva prāpnuhi | madanugrahaṃ gṛhāṇa, tava jagannirmāṇavidhau yadyadaṃnugṛhṇāmi, tattat parigṛhṇīṣvetyarthaḥ | bhūyastapaḥ kuru | kiñca tava mayyakhilasādhanī yadyadicchasi, tasya tasyānāyāsena niṣpādayitrī atyutkaṭā prakṛṣṭapremalakṣaṇā bhaktiśca bhavatviti giramudīryokttavā vedhasaṃ brahmāṇaṃ muditacetasaṃ santuṣṭamānasam ādadhāḥ kṛtavānasi || 8 ||
evamanugṛhya bhagavati tirohite vedhasaḥ pravṛttimāha -- 
śataṃ kṛtatapāstataḥ sa khalu divyasaṃvatsarānavāpya ca tapovalaṃ matibalaṃ ca pūrvādhikam |
udīkṣya kila kampitaṃ payasi paṅkajaṃ vāyunā
bhavadvalavijṛmbhitaḥ pavanapāthasī pītavān || 9 ||
śatamiti | saḥ brahmā khalu śataṃ divyasaṃvatsarān kṛtaṃ cīrṇe tapo yena sa kṛtatapāḥ tataḥ pūrvādhikaṃ tapobalaṃ matibalaṃ vijñānabalaṃ cāvāpya sa punaḥ payasyekārṇave vāyunā tātkālikenātyutkaṭena vātena kampitaṃ svādhiṣṭhānaṃ paṅkajamudvīkṣya bhavadvalena bhavāneva balaṃ bhavadvalaṃ tena, ‘sa vai balaṃ balināṃ cāpareṣām' iti prahlādavacanāt | vijṛmbhito vardhitaḥ pavanapāthasī vāyuṃ jalaṃ ca pītavān nyapibat kila || 9 ||
tavaiva kṛpayā punaḥ sarasijena tenaiva sa
prakalpya bhuvanatrayīṃ pravavṛte prajānirmitau |
tathāvidhakṛpābharo gurumarutpurādhīśvara !
tvamāśu paripāhi māṃ gurudayokṣitairīkṣitaiḥ || 10 ||
taveti | sa punastava kṛpayaiva tenaiva ativipulena sarasijena bhuvanatrayīṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svariti trīn lokān | prakalpyeti | atrātalavitalasutalatalātalamahātalarasātalapātālānāṃ saptānāṃ bhūrloke'ntarbhāvād maharjanastapaḥsatyamityeteṣāṃ svarloke cāntarbhāvāt trilokapakṣaḥ kathyate | tataśca tat kamalaṃ dvisaptadhā vibhajya caturdaśa lokānakalpayadityarthaḥ | anantaraṃ ca prajānāṃ śubhāśubhādṛṣṭatāratamyena tattallokayogyānāṃ jīvānāṃ nirmitau śarīraviracanāyāṃ pravavṛte pravṛttavān | he gurumarutpurādhīśvara! tathāvidhaḥ sarvajanaviṣayaḥ kṛpābharaḥ karuṇātiśayo yasya sa tvaṃ gurudayokṣitaiḥ gurvyā ghanībhūtayā dayayā ukṣitaḥ ārdrīkṛtaiḥ īkṣitaiḥ kaṭākṣaiḥ āśu drutataraṃ māṃ pāhīti || 10 ||
iti jagatsṛṣṭiprakāravarṇanaṃ navamaṃ daśakam |
atha sṛṣṭibhedānāha vaikuṇṭheti daśabhiḥ -- 
vaikuṇṭha ! vardhitabalo'tha bhavatprasādādambhojayonirasṛjat kila jīvadehān |
sthāstūni bhūruhamayāni tathā tirakṣcāṃ
jātīrmanuṣyanivahānapi devabhedān || 1 ||
vaikuṇṭheti| he vaikuṇṭha ! viṣṇo ! bhavatprasādād vardhitabalo vidyākarmabhyāṃ prāptaiśvaryaḥ sannambhojayoniḥ atha anantaraṃ jīvānāṃ dehānasṛjat kila | tatra dehāḥ sthāvarajaṅgamabhedād dvividhāḥ | tadāha -- sthāsnūnīti | bhūruhamayāṇi vṛkṣaprāyāṇi oṣadhilatāvīrudrapāṇi sthāvarāṇītyarthaḥ | tiraścāṃ gavādīnāṃ jātīḥ, tatra gavādayo dviśaphāḥ, aśvādaya ekaśaphāḥ, śvādayaḥ pañcanakhāḥ, kākakaṅkagṛdhrādayaśca, etān | devān manuṣyāṃścāsṛjadityarthaḥ || 1 ||
atha sraṣṭurabuddhikṛtaṃ tāmasasargamāha -- 
mithyāgrahāsmimatirāgavikopabhītirajñānavṛttimiti pañcavidhāṃ sa sṛṣṭvā |
uddāmatāmasapadārthavidhānadūnastene tvadīyacaraṇasmaraṇaṃ viśuddhayai || 2 ||
mithyeti | ātmano'nyatvena prapañcasyāstitvapratītirmithyāgrahaḥ, śarīrādābahaṃmamābhimāno'smimatiḥ, upabhogyabuddhyā dhanādiṣvicchā rāgaḥ, dhanādyapahartari krodho vikopaḥ, upabhogyapadārthānāṃ vyayanāśāghutprekṣayā janitaṃ bhayaṃ bhītiḥ ityevaṃ pañcavidhāṃ pañcaprakārām ajñānavṛttim ātmājñānakāryaṃ mithyāgrahādipañcājñānakāryāṇi sṛṣṭvetyarthaḥ | anantaram uddāmnāṃ prabhūtānāṃ tāmasapadārthānāṃ kevalatamoguṇakāryāṇāṃ padārthānāṃ vidhānena sṛṣṭyā dūnaḥ kṣīṇamānasaḥ san sa viśuddhyai ajñānanivṛttaye tvadīyacaraṇasmaraṇaṃ tene kṛtavān || 2 ||
tāvat sasarja manasā sanakaṃ sanandaṃ
bhūyaḥ sanātanamuniṃ ca sanatkumāram |
te sṛṣṭikarmāṇa tu tena niyujyamānāstvatpādabhaktirasikā jagṛhurna vāṇīm || 3 ||
tāvādati | yāvadasau tvadīyacaraṇasmaraṇena śuddhamanā jātaḥ, tāvat tasmin kāle sanakasanandasanātanasanatkumārān manasā sasarja | te tu sanakādayaḥ tena brajhaṇā sṛṣṭikarmaṇi niyujyamānāḥ preritāḥ tasya vāṇīṃ niyogarūpaṃ vākyaṃ na jagṛhuḥ nāṅgīkṛtavantaḥ | tatra hetuḥ -- tvatpādabhaktirasikā iti | tvatpādāmbujayoḥ yā premalakṣaṇā bhaktiḥ, tasyāṃ rasikā icchāvantaḥ śarīrādibandhapradaṃ sargakarma naicchannityarthaḥ || 3 ||
tāvat prakopamuditaṃ pratirundhato'sya
bhramadhyato'jani mūḍo bhavadekadeśaḥ |
nāmāni me kuru padāni ca hā viriśce -- 
tyādau ruroda kila tena sa rudranāmā || 4 ||
tāvaditi | yadaivaṃ putraiḥ svānuśāsanaṃ pratyākhyātaṃ, tadetyarthaḥ | uditam utpannaṃ prakopaṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ kopaṃ pratirundhato ghiyā niyacchato'pyasya brahmaṇaḥ bhrūmadhyato bhruvormadhyād mṛḍo'jani taṃ kopameva śarīratvena parigṛhya śrīśaṅkarastatrāvatīrṇa ityarthaḥ | sa ca bhavadekadeśaḥ viṣṇorevāṃśaḥ, "paraḥ puruṣa eka ihāsya dhatte sthityādaye hariviriñcahareti saṃjñāḥ |" iti vacanāt | sa cādau jātamātra eva hā viriñca ! me nāmāni padāni sthānāni ca kurviti ruroda kila, tena rodanena sa ru dra iti nāma prāptavān || 4 ||
ekādaśāhvayatayā ca vibhinnarūpaṃ
rudraṃ vidhāya dayitā vanitāśca dattvā |
tāvantyadatta ca padāni bhavatpraṇunnaḥ
prāha prajāviracanāya ca sādaraṃ tam || 5 ||
ekādaśāhvayatayeti |
"manurmanyurmahādevo mahāñchiva ṛtadhvajaḥ |
ururetā bhavaḥ kālo vāmadevo dhṛtavrataḥ ||"
ityekādaśanāmabhedāt cakārāt sthānabhedāñca vibhinnaṃ bhedabuddhigocaraṃ rudraṃ vidhāya tebhyo dayitā vanitāśceti "dhī dhīrvṛttiraśanomā ca niyutsarpiriḍāmbikā | irāvatī sudhā dīkṣā" ityuktāḥ striyaśca dattvā tābatti ekādaśaiva padāni sthānāni ca adatta | tāni ca "hṛdindriyāṇyasurvyoma batpuragnirjalaṃ mahī | sūryaścandraratapaścaiva" ityuktāni sthānāni ca adatta dattavān | bhavatpraṇunnaḥ hṛdi sthiteneśvareṇa preritaḥ vidhiḥ taṃ rudraṃ nāmādibhedādanekatvapratibhāne'pi paramārthata ekarūpaṃ prajāviracanāya bhāryāsu putrānutpādayituṃ sādaraṃ saṃmānasahitaṃ prāha nyayuṅktta || 5 ||
rudrābhisṛṣṭabhayadākṛtirudrasaṅghasaṃpūryamāṇabhuvanatrayabhītacetāḥ |
mā mā prajāḥ sṛja tapaścara maṅgalāyetyācaṣṭa taṃ kamalabhūrbhavadīritātmā || 6 ||
rudreti | rudrābhisṛṣṭaḥ rudreṇa svāsu strītpāditaḥ, bhayadā vṛṣabhārūḍhā bhasmāṅgajñāgā dhṛtakhaṭvāṅgaparaśuhariṇakapālacarmavasanā phaṇipatibhūṣaṇā vahninayanā ākṛtirthasya tādṛśena rudrasaṅghena saṃpūryamāṇaṃ yad bhuvanatrayaṃ tasmād bhītacetāḥ tatkṣaṇaṃ sṛtena bhavateritaḥ prerita ātmā mano yasya sa kamalabhūḥ brahmā taṃ rudramācaṣṭa ākhyātavān | mā mehaśīḥ prajāḥ sṛja kintu maṅgalāya sarvalokasukhāya tapaścareti || 6||
tasyātha sargarasikasya marīciratristatrāṅgirāḥ kratumuniḥ pulahaḥ pulastyaḥ |
aṅgādajāyata bhṛguśca vasiṣṭhadakṣau
śrīnāradakṣca bhagavān bhavadaṅghridāsaḥ || 7 ||
tasyeti | atha tapaścaraṇāya svapitaramanujñāpya rudrasya tapovana praveśānantaraṃ
sargarasikasya kathaṃ srakṣyāmītyevaṃ cintayatastasyāṅgād marīcyādayo daśa putrā ajāyanta | tatra marīcirbrahmaṇo manaso'jāyata, atrirnetrād, aṅgirā mukhataḥ, ṛtumuniḥ karāt, pulaho nābheḥ, pulastyaḥ karṇataḥ, bhṛgustvacaḥ, vasiṣṭhaḥ prāṇādū, dakṣo'ṅguṣṭhād, utsaṅgānnāradaścājāyata | bhavadaṅghridāsaḥ bhavataḥ śrīpādābjayordāso'hamityabhimanyamāna ityarthaḥ || 7 ||
dharmādikānabhisṛjannatha kardamaṃ ca
vāṇīṃ vidhāya vidhiraṅgajasakulo'bhūt |
tvadbodhitaiḥ sanakadakṣamukhaistanūjairuddhodhitaśca virarāma tamo vimuñcan || 8 ||
dharmādikāniti | vidherdakṣiṇāt stanād dharmaḥ śrīnārāyaṇaśca jātaḥ, adharmo mṛtyuścaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ, hṛdayāt kāmaḥ, bhruvoḥ krodhaḥ, adharāllobha ityādyanveṣaṇīyam | atha kardamaṃ cābhisṛjan chāyāyāḥ kardamo jāta ityarthaḥ | atha vidhirvāṇīṃ sarasvatīṃ vidhāya aṅgajasaṅkulaḥ kāmaparavaśo'bhūt | tataḥ sarvajanabuddhisākṣiṇā tvayā bodhitaiḥ sanakādibhirdakṣādibhikṣca tanūjaiḥ putrairudvodhitaḥ pratibodhitaḥ tamo'jñānaṃ vimuñcan virarāma nivṛtto'bhūt || 8||
vedān purāṇanivahānapi sarvavidyāḥ
kurvan nijānanagaṇāccaturānano'sau |
putreṣu teṣu vinidhāya sa sargavṛddhimaprāpnuvaṃstava padāmbujamāśrito'bhūt || 9 ||
vedāniti | asau caturānano brahmā nijānanagaṇāt caturmukhebhyaḥ vedān ṛgādīṃścaturaḥ purāṇanivahān saṅkṣeparūpān sarvavidyāḥ śikṣādyaṅgāni ṣaḍ mīmāṃsāyupāṅgāni catvāri, āyurvedāghupavedāścatvāra iti caturdaśa vidyāḥ | tatra sarvavi
dyā iti purāṇasyāpi grahaṇe purāṇanivahāniti tasya pṛthagupādānaṃ prādhānyakhyāpanārtham | taduktaṃ -- 
"yaścaturvedavid vipraḥ purāṇaṃ vetti nārthataḥ |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā bhayamāpnoti vedo māṃ pratariṣyati ||"
iti | kiñcāsya pañcamavedatvāt sarvamukhebhyaḥ sṛṣṭatvācca prādhānyam | tā vidyāsteṣu marīcyādiṣu putreṣu vinidhāya saṅkramayya sa brahmā sargavṛddhim aprāpnuvan trilokyāṃ prajāvistārārthaṃ nityavyāpto'pi tadalābhāt tatra daivavirodhamāśaṅkaya tannivṛttaye tava padāmbujamāśrito'bhūt cintayāmāsetyarthaḥ || 9 ||
jānannupāyamatha dehamajo vibhajya
strīpuṃsabhāvamabhajanmanutadvadhūbhyām |
tābhyāṃ ca mānuṣakulāni vivardhayaṃstvaṃ
govinda ! mārutapurādhipa ! rundhi rogān || 10 ||
jānanniti | bhavatpadāmbujadhyānānubhāvena sargavṛddhābupāyajño'jaḥ brahmā svadehaṃ vibhajya dvidhā kṛtvā manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvaḥ tadvadhūḥ śatarūpā tābhyāṃ strīpuṃsabhāvramabhajat svaśarīrārdhena śatarūpām anyārdhena manuṃ ca sasarjetyarthaḥ | tābhyāṃ manuśatarūpābhyāṃ brahmaṇa ādeśena maithunadharmeṇa prajāsarge pravṛttābhyāṃ bhuvi manuṣyakulāni vivardhayan he govinda ! mārutapurādhipa ! tvaṃ mama rogān rundhi vārayetyarthaḥ || 10 ||
iti sṛṣṭibhedavarṇanaṃ daśamaṃ daśakam |
krameṇa sarge parivardhamāne kadāpi divyāḥ sanakādayaste |
bhavādvilokāya vikuṇṭhalokaṃ prapedire mārutamandireśa ! || 1 ||
krameṇeti | divyā aprākṛtāḥ sāttvikā ityarthaḥ | prapedire prāpuḥ || 1 ||
vikuṇṭhanilayaṃ varṇayati
manojñanaiḥśreyasakānanādyairanekavāpīmaṇimandiraiśca |
anopamaṃ taṃ bhavato niketaṃ munīśvarāḥ prāpuratītakakṣyāḥ || 2 ||
manojñeti | manojñaiḥ sarvartuśobhādibhiratimanoharaiḥ naiḥśreyasakānanādyaiḥ niḥśreyasaṃ kaivalyameva mūrtimattvena sthitamiti naiḥśreyasaṃ nāma kānanamudyānaṃ tadādyaiḥ anekaiḥ vāpyo dīrghikāḥ maṇimandirāṇi ratnamayāni bhavanāni taiḥ anopamam anupamaṃ taṃ sarvalokābhivandyaṃ bhavato niketaṃ vikuṇṭhanilayaṃ munīśvarāḥ sanakādayaḥ atītakakṣyāḥ parikhāprākārādivibhaktāḥ ṣaṭ kakṣyā atītya bhavananikaṭasthāṃ saptamīṃ kakṣyāṃ prāpurityarthaḥ || 2 ||
bhavaddidṛkṣūn bhavanaṃ vivikṣūn dvāḥsthau jayastān vijayo'pyarundhām |
teṣāṃ ca citte padamāpa kopaḥ sarvaṃ bhavatpreraṇayaiva bhūman ! || 3 ||
bhavaditi | bhavaddidṛkṣūn samādhigamyaṃ bhavantaṃ nayanagocarīkartukāmān tadartha bhavanaṃ bhavanmaṇimandiraṃ vivikṣūna praveṣṭumārabdhān tān sanakādīn jayo vijayaśca dvāḥsthau bhavatsārūpyaṃ prāptau dvārapālau arundhāṃ vetreṇa pratyaṣedhatāṃ, tatasteṣāṃ citte kopaḥ padamavakāśamāpa ca | sarve sanakādīnāṃ vaikuṇṭhaprāptiḥ, jayavijayayostatpratikūlācaraṇaṃ, munīnāṃ kopaścaitat sarve he bhūman ! bhavato varāhanarasiṃhaśrīrāmakṛṣṇādyavatāraiḥ krīḍiṣyato bhavata īśvarasya preraṇayaivetyarthaḥ || 3 ||
evaṃ bhagavaddarśanabhaṅge sati sañjāteṣatkopā munayo dvāḥsthau śaptavanta ityāha -- 
baikuṇṭhalokānucitapraceṣṭauṃ kaṣṭau yuvāṃ daityagatiṃ bhajetam |
iti praśaptau bhavadāśrayau tau harismṛtinoṃ'stviti nematustān || 4 ||
vaikuṇṭhaloketi | rajastamaḥkṣobharahite vaikuṇṭhaloke anucitā itaḥ prāka kasyāpyanutpannā prārabdhā sadatikramarūpā ceṣṭā vyāpāro yayostau kaṣṭau atikrūrau yuvāṃ daityagatim āsurīṃ yoniṃ bhajetaṃ prāpnutam iti munibhiḥ praśaptau tau bhavadāśrayau bhavatkiṅkarau jayavijayau naḥ asmākaṃ harismṛtirastu yuṣmadatikrameṇa yāṃ yāṃ yoniṃ vrajāmastatra tatrāpi bhagavatsmṛtirastviti nematuḥ bhuvi patitvā teṣāṃ pādān saṅgṛhyaivaṃ prārthayantau namaścakraturityarthaḥ || 4 ||
tadetadājñāya bhavānavāptaḥ sahaiva lakṣmyā bahirambujākṣa ! |
khageśvarāṃsārpitacārubāhurānandayaṃstānabhirāmamūrtyā || 5||
tadetaditi | tasminnavasare ambujākṣa ! bhavān etat svīyānāṃ sadatikramādikamājñāya vibudhya lakṣmyā sahaiva bahiḥ maṇibhavanasya bāhyāṅkaṇe avāptaḥ niṣkrāntaḥ khageśvarasya śrīgaruḍasyāṃse samarpito vinyastaḥ cārubāhuḥ ājānulambī pīvaro bhujo yasya kiñcid vāmapārśvalambitamūrtitayā śrīgaruḍasya vāmāṃsadeśadattakarāmbuja ityarthaḥ | amirāmayā atimanoharayā mūrtyā tān munīndrān ānandayan brahmānandaparitṛptānāmapi śarīramanaḥkṣobhaṃ janayannityarthaḥ || 5 ||
prasādya gīrbhiḥ stuvato munīndrānananyanāthāvatha pārṣadau tau |
saṃrambhayogena bhavaistribhirmāmupetamityāttakṛpaṃ myagādīḥ || 6 ||
prasādyeti | tataśca tvaṃ stuvataḥ tvaddarśanānantaraṃ mūrdhnā praṇamya stuvato munīndrān mādhvībhirgīrbhiḥ prasādya atha anantaram ananyanāthau ātmaikaśaraṇau tau saṅkaṭasthau svapārṣadau jayavijayau āttakṛpam āttā aṅgīkṛtā kṛpā yasminniti kriyāviśeṣaṇam | ityevaṃ nyagādīḥ uktavān | kathamityāha -- tribhirhiraṇyakaśipurāvaṇaśiśupālādirūpairbhavairjanmabhiḥ tatra tatra saṃrambhayogena saṃrambhakartṛkeṇa mayi samādhinairantaryeṇa yuvāṃ māmupetaṃ matsamīpamāgacchatamiti nyagādīrityarthaḥ || 6 ||
tvadīyabhṛtyau kila kāśyapāt tau surārivīrāvuditau ditau dvau |
sandhyāsamutpādanakaṣṭaceṣṭau yamau ca lokasya yamāvivānyau || 7 ||
tvadīyeti | tau tvadīyabhṛtyau bhavatpārṣadau kāśyapānmunerditau tadbhāryāyāṃ dvau surārivīrau hiraṇyakaśipuhiraṇyākṣa nāmadheyāvasuraśreṣṭhau uditau utpannau kila | sandhyāsamutpādanakaṣṭaceṣṭāviti ditiḥ kila sapatlīnāṃ putrasamṛddhyā santaptahṛdayā satī kāśyapaṃ svabhartāraṃ sandhyāyāmagnimiṣṭvāgnyagāra āsīnaṃ kāmāturā mayi putra mutpādayeti prārthitavatī | tena ca sandhyeyaṃ kṣaṇaṃ pratipālayetyuktāpi manmathonma 'svānām' iti tu sarvatra paṭhyate |
thitacittā balādṛṣervāso jagrāha | sa ca tayā sahopaviveśeti | ataḥ sandhyāyāṃ samutpādanena kaṣṭaceṣṭau krūrakarmāṇau yamau yugapadekagarbhadhṛtau yamau lokasya janasyānyau yamau krūrakarmaṇā vaivasvatād yamādanyāvantakau (iva) babhūvaturityarthaḥ || 7 ||
hiraṇyapūrvaḥ kaśipuḥ kilaikaḥ paro hiraṇyākṣa iti pratītaḥ |
ubhau bhavannāthamaśeṣalokaṃ ruṣā nyarundhāṃ nijavāsanāndhau || 8 ||
hiraṇyapūrva iti | tayoreko jyeṣṭho hiraṇyapūrvaḥ kaśipuḥ hiraṇyakaśipuriti pratītaḥ prasiddhaḥ | paro'nujo hiraṇyākṣa iti prasiddhaḥ | tāvubhau bhavān nātho rakṣitā yasya sa bhavannāthaḥ, tamaśeṣalokaṃ sarvalokavāsino janān ruṣā nyarundhāṃ pīḍayāmāsatuḥ | nijavāsanayā āsureṇa svabhāvena andhau bibhraṃśitaparamārthajñānāvityarthaḥ || 8 ||
tayorhiraṇyākṣamahāsurendro raṇāya dhāvannanavāptavairī |
bhavatpriyāṃ kṣmāṃ salile nimajjya cacāra garvād vinadan gadāvān || 9||
tayoriti | tayormadhye hiraṇyākṣamahāsurendraḥ trilokyāṃ raṇāya dhāvannanavāptavairī devānapi pratiyogitayālabhamānaḥ bhavatpriyāṃ kṣmāṃ bhūmiṃ salile pralayārṇave nimajjya bhūmiṃ karṇābharaṇīkṛtya jale nimagnaḥ san gadāvān aṃsanyastamahāgadaḥ garvād vinadaṃścacāra || 9 ||
tato jaleśāt sadṛśaṃ bhavantaṃ niśamya babhrāma gaveṣayaṃstvām |
bhakttaikadṛśyaḥ sa kṛpānidhe! tvaṃ nirundhi rogān marudālayeśa ! || 10 ||
tata iti | tato jānudaghnajale vārinidhau krīḍiṣyan vibhāvarīṃ varuṇapurīṃ gatvā taṃ saṃyugāyāhvayat | tato jaleśād varuṇamukhād bhavantaṃ sadṛśaṃ niśamya raṇamārgeṣu bhavataḥ sadṛśaḥ purāṇapuruṣaḥ śrīnārāyaṇa eka eveti śrutvāyaṃ tvāṃ gaveṣayan mṛgayan babhrāma| sa tvaṃ bhaktaikadṛśya iti | kevalaṃ bhaktaireva prahlādanāradādibhiḥ dṛśyaḥ draṣṭuṃ śakyaḥ | nanu bhaktaikadṛśyaśced bhagavān, upari "bhavantaṃ saṃprāpad " ityuktiranupapanneti ced, maivaṃ, hiraṇyākṣo hi raudrarasopāyena bhagavati manoniveśanād bhaktaḥ| ‘tasmāt kenāpyupāyena manaḥ kṛṣṇe niveśayed' iti vacanāt | ato'sya bhagavaddarśanaṃ sambhavati | viśeṣato maraṇadaśāyāmityapi sūcitam || 10 ||
iti sanakādivaikuṇṭhapraveśajayavijayaśāpahiraṇyakaśipuhiraṇyākṣotpattivarṇanam
ekādaśaṃ daśakam |
atha varāhāvatārahiraṇyākṣavadhādyavatārayituṃ manoḥ pravṛttimāha -- 
svāyambhuvo manuratho janasargaśīlo dṛṣṭvā mahīmasamaye salile nimagnām |
sraṣṭāramāpa śaraṇaṃ bhavadaṅghrisevātuṣṭāśayaṃ munijanaiḥ saha satyaloke || 1 ||
svāyambhuva iti | atho prajāsarge brahmaṇo nideśānantaraṃ janasargaḥ prajotpādanaṃ śīlaṃ yasya sa janasargaśīlaḥ asamaye anāsanne'pi pralayakāle sraṣṭāraṃ svajanakaṃ brahmāṇaṃ bhavadaṅghrisevayā tvatpādāmbujopāsanayā tuṣṭāśayaṃ santuṣṭamanasaṃ munijanaiḥ saha satyaloke sthitam | munijanaiḥ saha śaraṇamāpeti vā || 1 ||
kaṣṭaṃ prajāḥ sṛjati mayyavanī nimaśnā
sthānaṃ sarojabhava! kalpaya tat prajānām |
ityevameṣa kathito manunā svayambhūrambhoruhākṣa! tava pādayugaṃ vyacintīt || 2 ||
kaṣṭamiti | he sarojabhava ! mayi prajāḥ sṛjati sati avaniḥ pralayāmbhasi nimanābhūt kaṣṭam anāśrayatvānnāha bhavannideśaṃ kartuṃṃ samarthaḥ | tat tasmāt prajānāṃ mama ca sthānaṃ kalpaya ityevaṃ manunā kathito vijñāpitaḥ eṣa svayambhūḥ he ambhoruhākṣa! tava pādayugaṃ vyacintīt cintayāmāsa || 2 ||
cintayaṃstatkṣaṇameva hṛdi darśanaṃ gataṃ bhavantaṃ khindānamānasaścaivamāha -- 
hā hā vibho ! jalamahaṃ nyapivaṃ purastādadyāpi majjati mahī kimahaṃ karomi |
itthaṃ tvadaṅghriyugalaṃ śaraṇaṃ yato'sya
nāsāpuṭāt samabhavaḥ śiśukolarūpī || 3 ||
hāheti | he vibho ! hāhā kaṣṭaṃ jātam | ahaṃ purastād janasargāt prāgeva pṛthivīniveśanāya jalaṃ nyapibaṃ pītavānasmi | adyāpi tucchīkṛte jale mahī majjati | ahaṃ bhavatā sarge yojitaḥ sannapi kiṃ karomi | asmin karmaṇyasmākaṃ tvameva śaraṇam | itthaṃ tvadaṅghriyugalaṃ śaraṇaṃ yataḥ prāpnuvataḥ asya brahmaṇaḥ nāsāpuṭād nāsikodarataḥ śiśukolarūpī varāhatokarūpaṃ bibhrat samabhavaḥ avatīrṇo'bhūrityarthaḥ || 3 ||
aṅguṣṭhamātrava purutpatitaḥ purastād
bhūyo'tha kumbhisadṛśaḥ samajṛmbhathāstvam |
abhre tathāvidhamudīkṣya bhavantamuccairvismeratāṃ vidhiragāt saha mṛnubhiḥ svaiḥ || 4 ||
aṅguṣṭeti | tvaṃ purastāt pūrvaṃ nāsāpuṭād aṅguṣṭhamātravapuḥ aṅguṣṭhamātraparimitaśarīraḥ san utpatitaḥ | atha anantaraṃ bhūyaḥ punaśca kumbhisadṛśo gajamātraḥ samajṛmbhathāḥ vavṛdhiṣe | tathāvidhaṃ kṣaṇena vardhiṣṇum abhre meghamārge uccaiḥ unnatamudīkṣya mukhānyunnamayya dṛṣṭvā vidhiḥ brahmā vismeratāṃ vismitatvam agāt prāpa | saha sūnubhiḥ svairiti | sūnavo marīcyādayaḥ, te'pi vismitā babhūvurityarthaḥ ||
ko'sāvacintyamahimā kiṭirutthito me
ghoṇāpuṭāt kimu bhavedajitasya māyā |
itthaṃ vicintayati dhātari śailamātraḥ
sadyo bhavan kila jagarjitha ghoraghoram || 5 ||
ko'sāviti | acintyamahimā aparicchedyaśarīramahattvaḥ aprameyaprabhāvo vā kiṭiḥ sūkaro me ghoṇāpuṭād nāsāpuṭād utthita utpannaḥ | ajitasya viṣṇormāyā kimu bhavet sa eva māyayā sūkaramūrtiravatīrṇaḥ kimu bhavet | dhātari itthaṃ
vicintayati sati sadyaḥ śailamātraḥ śailadvayasavapurbhavan tvaṃ ghoraghoram atiśayena
bhayaṅkaraṃ yathā bhavati tathā jagarjitha kila || 5 ||
taṃ te ninādamupakarṇya janastapaḥsthāḥ
satyasthitāśca munayo nunuvurbhavantam |
tatstotraharṣulamanāḥ pariṇadya bhūyastoyāśayaṃ vipulamūrtiravātarastvam || 6 ||
tamiti | te tava taṃ ninādaṃ garjitaṃ janastapaḥsthāḥ janastapolokasthā apītyarthaḥ | satyasthitāḥ satyalokasthā nunuvuḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ | tatstotreṇa tena stotreṇa harṣulamanāḥ prasannacetāḥ san vipulamūrtiḥ pūrvasmādativistṛtakāyaḥ san tvaṃ bhūyaḥ punarapi teṣāṃ prasādāya pariṇadya garjan toyāśayaṃ pralayābdhimavātaraḥ avagāhase sma ||
ūrdhvaprasāriparidhūmravidhūtaromā
protkṣiptavāladhiravāṅmukhaghoraghoṇaḥ |
tūrṇamadīrṇajaladaḥ parighūrṇadakṣṇā
stotana munīñchiśirayannavateritha tvam || 7 ||
ūrdhveti | ūrdhvaṃ prasaraṇaśīlāni paridhūmrāṇi kṛṣṇalohitāni vidhūtāni kampitāni romāṇi yasya, sa tathā | utkṣiptavāladhiḥ unnītapucchaḥ avāṅmukhā adhaḥkṛtā ghorā bhayaṅkarākārā ghoṇā nāsikā yasya, sa tathā | tūrṇam atilaghu pradīrṇāḥ śakalīkṛtā jaladā yena | parighūrṇatā akṣṇā kampitapiṅgalitocchranākṣidvayena prakaṭitānukampena stotṝn munīn śiśirayan ānandayan tvamavateritha avajagāhiṣe || 7 ||
antarjalaṃ tadanu saṅkulanakracakraṃ
bhrāmyattimiṅgilakulaṃ kaluṣormimālam |
āviśya bhīṣaṇaraveṇa rasātalasthānākampayan vasumatīmagaveṣayastvam || 8 ||
antarjalamiti | tadanu jalapraveśānantaraṃ tvaṃ saṅkulanakracakraṃ bhrāmyagrāhasamūhaṃ bhrāmyattimiṅgilakulaṃ bhrāmyanta itastataḥ sañcarantaḥ timijhilā anekayojanāyāmavistārā mahāmatsyāḥ, teṣāṃ kulaṃ samūho yasmin | kṣubdhajalatvāt kaluṣā
āvilā ūrmimālāḥ taraṅgasamūhā yasmistad antarjalaṃ jalamadhyam āviśya bhīṣaṇaraveṇa rasātalasthān janān ā samantāt kampayan vasumatīṃ nijapriyāṃ bhūmim agaveṣayaḥ anveṣaṇamakarorityarthaḥ || 8 ||
dṛṣṭvātha daityahatakena rasātalānte
saṃveśitāṃ jhaṭiti kūṭakiṭirvibho ! tvam |
āpātukānavigaṇayya surārikheṭān
daṃṣṭrākureṇa vasudhāmadadhāḥ salīlam || 9 ||
dṛṣṭveti | atha anantaraṃ daityahatakena asurādhamena hiraṇyākṣeṇa rasātalānte saṃveśitāṃ vinyastāṃ vasudhāṃ bhūmiṃ he vibho ! kūṭakiṭirmāyāvarāhavapustvam āpātukān āgacchataḥ surārikheṭān asurāpaśadān avigaṇayya tṛṇīkṛtya daṃṣṭrāṅkureṇa daśanāgreṇa salīlaṃ yathā bhavati tathā adadhāḥ uddhṛtavānasi || 9 ||
abhyuddharannatha dharāṃ daśanāgralagnamustāṅkurāṅkita ivādhikapīvarātmā |
uddhṛtaghorasalilājjaladherudañcan
krīḍāvarāhavapurīśvara ! pāhi rogāt || 10 ||
abhyuddharanniti | atha dharāmabhyuddharan daśanāgralagnamustāṅkurāṅkita iva yathānyo varāhaḥ palvale mustākṣatiṃ kurvan daśanāgre mustāṅkureṇāṅkito bhavati, tadvad darśanīyo bhavan adhikapīvarātmā atipīnakāyaḥ udbhūtaṃ saṃkṣubdhaṃ ghoraṃ bhayaṅkaraṃ salilaṃ yasmin, tasmājaladherudañcannunmajjan he krīḍāvarāhavapuḥ ! krīḍārthaṃ ghṛtavarāhatano ! he īśvara ! tvaṃ rogāt pāhi || 10 ||
iti varāhāvatārabhūmyuddharaṇavarṇanaṃ dvādaśaṃ daśakam |
anantaraṃ nāradasya hiraṇyākṣavavasāhāyyaṃ darśayati -- 
hiraṇyākṣaṃ tāvad varada ! bhavadanveṣaṇaparaṃ
carantaṃ sāṃbateṃ payasi nijapaṅghāparimite |
bhavadbhakto gatvā kapaṭapaṭudhīrnāradamuniḥ
śanairūce nandan danujamapi nindaṃstava balam || 1 ||
hiraṇyākṣamiti | tāvat tava dharoddharaṇasamakālameva saṃvartaḥ pralayaḥ, tatsambandhini payasi kapaṭapaṭudhīḥ jagadanugrahāyāṅgīkṛtakāpaṭyā paṭṭī tannipuṇā dhīryasya sa tathā danujaṃ hiraṇyākṣaṃ nandan praśaṃsan tava balamapi nindan śanaiḥ sānunayamūce uktavān || 1 ||
śrīnāradoktimeva darśayati -- 
sa māyāvī viṣṇuharati bhavadīyāṃ vasumatī
prabho ! kaṣṭaṃ kaṣṭaṃ kimidamiti tenābhigaditaḥ |
nadana kkāsau kkāsāviti sa muninā darśitapatho
bhavantaṃ saṃprāpad dharaṇidharamuyantamudakāt || 2 ||
sa iti | sa viṣṇuḥ māyāvī bhavadbhiyā varāhavyājenātmānamācchādya bhavadīyāṃ bhavatā brahmaṇo'pahṛtya rasātale niveśitāṃ, vasumatīmiti | anenopekṣānaucityaṃ dyotayati | prabho! tannivāraṇasamartha ! kaṣṭaṃ kaṣṭaṃ kimidam aho mandā api prabhūn dhikkrurvanti, prabhavaścopekṣāparāḥ ko'yaṃ krama iti tenāmigaditaḥ protsāhitaḥ sa nadan siṃhanādaṃ kurvan kkāsau kvāsāviti sāminiveśaṃ pṛṣṭena muninā ita ita iti darśitaḥ panthā yasya saḥ dharaṇidharam udakādudyantaṃ bhavantaṃ saṃprāpat saṃprāptavān || 2 ||
darśanasamanantarameva sa bhagavantamadhikṣiptavānityāha -- 
aho āraṇyo'yaṃ mṛga iti hasantaṃ bahutarairduruktairvidhyantaṃ ditisutamavajñāya bhagavan ! |
mahīṃ dṛṣṭvā daṃṣṭrāśirasi cakitāṃ svena mahasā
payodhāvādhāya prasabhamudayuṅkthā mṛdhavidhau || 3 ||
aho iti | aho kaṣṭametadanena muninā mama pratiyodghṛtvena pradarśito'yamāraṇyaḥ araṇyanivāsī mṛgaḥ sūkara iti hasantam evamādivacanaiḥ parihasantaṃ punarapi bahutarairduruktaiḥ re surādhama ! māyāmātraprabhāva ! anayā gadayā śīrṇaśiraskaṃ
tvāṃ nihanmi, ityādibhirdurvāgiṣubhirvidhyantaṃ tāḍayantaṃ ditisutaṃ diteḥ priyaṃ putraṃ na tu kāśyapasya he bhagavan ! tvamapyavajñāyādhikṣipya daṃṣṭrāśirasi cakitāṃ duḥsthāṃ mahīṃ dṛṣṭvā matpriyasyānena saṅgare sati nimajjeyamahamiti vicintya bhītāṃ svena ananyasādhāraṇena mahasā yogaiśvaryeṇa payodhau agādhasya sindhujalasyopari ādhāya vinyasyāvanimajjanābhāvāya carācarasantānāya ca tasyāṃ svavīryamādhāyetyarthaḥ | prasabhaṃ balātkāreṇa mṛdhavidhau yuddhakarmāṇi udayuṅkthāḥ sannāhaṃ cakṛṣe || 3 ||
gadāpāṇau daitye svamapi hi gṛhītonnatagado
niyuddhena krīḍan ghaṭaghaṭaravodghaṣṭaviyatā |
raṇālokautsukyānmilati surasaṅghe drutamamuṃ
nirundhyāḥ sandhyātaḥ prathamamiti dhātrā jagadiṣe || 4 ||
gadāpāṇāviti | daitye gaḍhāpāṇau yuyutsati sati, hi hetau, yasmādevaṃ tasmāt tvamapi gṛhītā smṛtimātra eva karāmbujaṃ prāptā unnatā mahatī gadā kaumodakī yena tathābhūtaḥ san niyuddhena dvandvayuddhena krīḍan śanaiśśanaiḥ | kīdṛśena niyuddhena, ghaṭaghaṭaravairitaretaragadābhighātasamutthairudghaṣṭamuccaiḥ śabdāyamānaṃ viyad aṇḍakaṭāhakuharaṃ yasmistad ghaṭaghaṭaravodghuṣṭaviyat, tena | raṇālokautsukyād bhagavataḥ śrīvarāhamūrteḥ parākramadarśana kautukātiśayena surāṇāṃ saṅghe milati sasammardaṃ sthitavati sati sandhyātaḥ prathamaṃ pūrva drutataraṃ yāvat sandhyāṃ svakālaṃ prāpyāsau na vardheta tāvad drutataramamuṃ daityaṃ nirundhyā nigṛhāṇa iti dhātrā jagadiṣe brahmā bhavantaṃ vijñāpitavānityarthaḥ || 4 ||
gadonmarde tasmiṃstava khalu gadāyāṃ ditibhuvo
gadāghātād bhūmau jhaṭiti patitāyāmahaha bhoḥ ! |
mṛdusmerāsyastvaṃ danujakulanirmūlana caṇaṃ
mahācakraṃ smṛtvā karabhuvi dadhāno ruruciṣe || 5 ||
gadonmarda iti | tasmin gadonmarde gadāyuddhe tava khalu gadāyāṃ tava
hastasthitāyāmeva ditibhuvo hiraṇyākṣasya gadāghātād gadāprahāreṇa jhaṭiti bhūmau patitāyām | ahaheti khede, athavādbhute | mayi saṃrambhayogenāpi samādhinairantarye kurvato maddāsasya sakṛdapi samīhitaṃ sādhayāmīti bhagavatā svayameva pātitā gadā, aho bhavato bhaktavātsalyamiti bhāvaḥ | he vibho ! ata eva tvaṃ mṛdusmerāsyaḥ na gadāpātāt khinno'bhūḥ, kintu mṛdumandasmitavikasitamukhāmbujaḥ san danujakulānāmasurasamūhānāṃ nirmūlanena sudarśanamiti prasiddhaṃ mahācakraṃ smṛtvā karabhuvi smṛtamātra eva samprāptaṃ dadhāno ruruciṣe śatroḥ kaṇṭhadeśaṃ lakṣīkṛtyonnamitadakṣiṇakareṇa sudarśanaṃ gṛhṇan tvam | jājvalyamānakālacakratejasā vaiṣṇavena tejasā ca sañjātotkarṣaṃ tava rūpaṃ sakalajanacakṣurindriyagocaratāmagamadityarthaḥ || 5 ||
tataḥ śūlaṃ kālapratimaruṣi daitye visṛjati
tvayicchindatyenat karakalita cakramaharaṇāt |
samāruṣṭo puṣṭyā sa khalu vitudaṃstvāṃ samatanod
galanmāye māyāstvayi kila jaganmohanakarīḥ || 6 ||
tata iti | yasmāt tava karakamalād gadāpāte'pyavyākulahṛdayatā vaiṣṇavatejaḥprakāśaśca tata ityarthaḥ | kālapratimaruṣi jagatsaṃhārakārikālāgnirudrasadṛśakope daitye kartari śūlaṃ visṛjati prayoktumudyacchati satyeva tvayi enat triśūlaṃ kare kalitasya cakrasya sudarśanasya praharaṇāt chindati dvidhākurvati sati sa khaluṃ daityaḥ samāruṣṭaḥ pūrvasmādapyatikupito muṣṭyā tvāṃ vitudan muṣṭīkṛtābhyāmubhābhyāṃ karābhyāmurasi dṛḍhaṃ praharan tvayi jaganmohanakarīḥ tvaditarajanasammohasampādayitrīḥ māyāḥ samatanot prāyuṅktta | galanmāya iti evaṃvidhā māyā galantyo dūrasthā bhavanti yasmāt sa tathā, athaveśvarasya svarūpabhūtā vigalantī pṛthagibātmānaṃ prakāśayantī jagatsṛṣṭyādikāriṇī yasmāt tādṛśe tvayītyarthaḥ || 6 ||
galanmāyatvameva darśayati -- 
bhavacakrajyotiṣkaṇalavanipātena vidhute
tato māyācakre vitataghanaropāndhamanasam |
gariṣṭhābhirmuṣṭimahatibhirabhighnantamasuraṃ
karāgreṇa svena śravaṇapadamūle niravadhīḥ || 7 ||
bhavaditi | bhavato viṣṇoḥ sudarśanākhyasya cakrasya jyotiṣastejasaḥ kaṇaḥ sphuliṅgastasya lavo leśastasya nipātena prasaraṇena māyānāmāsurīṇāṃ cakre samūhe vidhute calite | yathā raveḥ prakāśaleśa nipātena naiśaṃ tamaḥ, tadvadadarśanaṃ gate satītyarthaḥ | tataḥ svārambhavaiphalyānantaraṃ vitato vyāpto ghano nibiḍo yo roṣastenāndhaṃ mano yasya sa vitataghanaroṣāndhamanāḥ tam | karakalitaṃ sudarśanamananyasādhāraṇaṃ prabhāvaṃ ca dṛṣṭvāpi prāgjanmaśāpaprabhāvena svasvāminamajānantamiti bhāvaḥ | gariṣṭhābhirgurutarābhirmuṣṭiprahṛtibhirabhighnantaṃ vijigīṣayā punaḥ punaḥ prayuñjānaṃ " karāgreṇa svena śravaṇapadamūla " iti pāṭhaḥ samīcīnaḥ | "kareṇa karṇamūle'han", "nihataḥ kareṇa" iti ca bhāgavatokteḥ | anantarakṣloke ca "tava karasarojapramathita " iti || 7 ||
mahākāyaḥ so'yaṃ tava karasarojapramathito
galadrakto vaktrādapatadṛṣibhiḥ kṣlāghitahatiḥ |
tadā tvāmuddāmapramadabharavidyotihṛdayā
munīndrāḥ sāndrābhiḥ stutibhiranuvannadhvaratanam || 8 ||
mahākāya iti | 'bhagavatā svakarāgreṇa karṇakapolasandhau tāḍito'sya mukhaṃ paśyannevāsau tanuṃ jahau, aho bhāgyaṃ ko nvimāṃ saṃsthitiṃ labheta' ityṛṣibhiḥ kṣlāghitā saśiraḥ kampaṃ stutā hatirnigraho yasya sa tathā | uddāmapramadabhareṇāntadurbaheṇa santoṣātiśayena vidyoti śobhanaśīlaṃ hṛdayaṃ yeṣāṃ te munīndrā janastapaḥ -- satyalokanivāsinaḥ sāndrābhiḥ sambhūya kṛtatvād badhirīkṛtadaśadiśābhiḥ stutibhiḥ stotrairadhvaratanuṃ yajñāṅgaṃ śrīvarāhamūrtimanuvan tuṣṭuvuḥ || 8 ||
adhvaratanutvenaiva stauti
tvaci cchando romasvapi kuśagaṇakṣcakṣuṣi ghṛtaṃ
caturhotāro'ṅdhrau srugapi badane codara iḍā |
grahā jihvāyāṃ te parapuruṣa ! karṇe ca camasā
vibho ! somo vīrya varada ! galadeśe'pyupasedaḥ || 9 ||
tvacīti | chando gāyadhyādi tvaci gāyatryādicchandāṃsyeva tava tvāgityarthaḥ | adhvaryubrahmahotrudgātāraścatvāra ṛtvigyajamānā eva caturhotāraḥ aṅghrāvaṅghriṣu | srug juhūḥ badane tuṇḍe | iḍā puroḍāśadravyanidhānapātraviśeṣaḥ udare | grahāḥ somasaṅgrahaṇapātraviśeṣāste jihvayām | camasāḥ bhakṣaṇasādhanadravyanidhānapātrabhedāḥ karṇe karṇarandhrayoḥ | somaḥ somavallīraso vīrye retaḥ | upasadaḥ pravayārnantaraṃ kriyamāṇā iṣṭayaḥ, tāste galadaśe || 9 ||
munīndrairityādistavanamukharairmoditamanā
mahīyasyā mūrtyā vimalatarakīrtyā ca vilasan |
svadhiṣṇyaṃ saṃprāptaḥ sukharasavihārī madhuripo !
nirundhyā rogaṃ me sakalamapi vātālayapate !|| 10 ||
munīti | munīndrairityādītyādiśabdena prāyaṇīyodayanīye iṣṭī te daṃṣṭre ityādi gṛhyate| stavanamukharaiḥ stutiśabdajalpākaiḥ moditaṃ kṛtaprasādaṃ mano yasya sa tathā | mahīyasyā atiśayena pūjanīyayā mūrtyā svavigraheṇa vimalatarayā jagatpavitrarūpayā kīrtyā ca vilasan śobhamānaḥ sukharasavihārī svairaṃ krīḍamānaḥ svadhiṣṇyaṃ vaikuṇṭhaṃ saṃprāptaḥ || 10 ||
iti hiraṇyākṣayuddhatadvadhayajñavarāhastutivarṇanaṃ
trayodaśaṃ daśakam |
evaṃ varāhāvatāreṇa kṛtapratiṣṭhāyāṃ bhuvi tadāśrayatvena svāyaṃbhuvasya manoḥ prajāpālanaprakāramāha -- 
samanusmṛtatāvakāṅghriyugmaḥ sa manuḥ paṅkajasambhavāṅgajanmāṃ |
nijamantaramantarāyahīnaṃ caritaṃ te kathayan sukhaṃ nināya || 1 ||
samanusmṛteti | samyag yamaniyamādiyuktamanusmṛtaṃ layavikṣepādirāhityena cintitaṃ tāvakāṅghriyugmaṃ tvatpādapadmayugalaṃ yena | paṅkajasambhavasyāṅgajanmā putraḥ sa
prasiddhaḥ svāyambhuvo manuḥ nijamantaraṃ caturyugāṇāṃ sādhikaikasaptatiparimitaṃ kālam antarāyāḥ śārīramānasadaiauvakabhautikāḥ kleśāḥ prajāpālanoparodhāḥ, tadgrahitam | etacca bhavadaṅṅghrismaraṇataccaritakathanānubhāvāt | ata eva sukhaṃ svairaṃ yathā bhavati tathā nināya gamayāmāsa || 1 ||
atha kāpilaprasaṅgāya kardamakathā prastūyate -- 
samaye khalu tatra kardamākhyo ḍhahiṇacchāyabhavastadīyavācā |
dhṛtasargaraso nisargaramyaṃ bhagavaṃstvāmayutaṃ samāḥ siṣeve || 2 ||
samaya iti | tatra samaye tasmin kāle kardama ityākhyā yasya sa prajāpatiḥ druhiṇacchāyabhava iti, "chāyāyāḥ kardamo jajñe" ityukteḥ| tadīyavācā prajāḥ sṛjeti brahmaṇa ādeśād dhṛtasargarasaḥ prajāsṛṣṭikāmaḥ san | nisargaramyaṃ svata eva sañcintakahṛdayarañjakam ayutaṃ samā daśasahasrasaṃvatsaraṃ siṣeve tapaścakāra || 2 ||
garuḍopari kālameghakamraṃ vilasatkelisarojapāṇipadmam |
hasitollasitānanaṃ vibhe! tvaṃ vapurāviṣkuruṣe sma kardamāya || 3 ||
garuḍeti | tvaṃ tasmai garuḍopari garuḍāṃsadeśavinyastacaraṇāmbujaṃ vapurāviṣkuruṣe sma darśayāmāsitha || 3 ||
stuvate pulakādvatāya tasmai manuputrīṃ dayitāṃ navāpi putrīḥ |
kapilaṃ ca sutaṃ svameva paścāt svagatiṃ cāpyanugṛhya nirgato'bhūḥ || 4 ||
stuvata iti | tataśca bhuvi patitvā praṇamyotthā ya stuvate pramoda bhāreṇa pulakāvṛtāya tasmai kardamaprajāpataye manuputrīṃ devahūtiṃ dayitāṃ bhārthī tasyāṃ nava putrīrapi svaṃ svāṃśabhūtaṃ kapilaṃ sutaṃ ca paścādante svagatiṃ mokṣaṃ cānugṛhya nirgato'bhūḥ prasthitavānāsi svāyaṃbhuvo manurbrahmāvarta nāma deśamadhivasati, sa rathena svamahiṣyā śatarūpayā sahetaḥ
paraśvastvāṃ diddakṣurāgatya tubhyaṃ svātmajāṃ dāsyati, tvaṃ ca tasyāṃ nava putrīrjanayiṣyasi, ahaṃ ca svāṃśena tava putratvenāvatariṣyāmītyanugṛhya bha vān jagāmetyarthaḥ ||4||
sa manuḥ śatarūpayā mahiṣyā guṇavatyā sutayā ca devahūtyā |
bhavadīritanāradopadiṣṭaḥ samagāt kardamamāgatipratīkṣam || 5 ||
sa iti | bhavadīritanāradopadiṣṭaḥ bhavadīritena bhavatā preritena nāradenopadiṣṭaḥ| nāradena hi devahūtyai kardamavayaḥ śīlarūpaguṇavarṇanapūrvakaṃ kardamāya svātmajāṃ prayaccheti manuruktaḥ | āgatipratīkṣamiti, apināma manuridānīṃ svāṃ dayitāṃ duhitaramādāyāgacchatīti mārge dattadṛṣṭimityarthaḥ ||5||
manunopahṛtāṃ ca devahūtiṃ taruṇīratnamavāpya kardamo'sau |
bhavadarcananirvṛto'pi tasyāṃ dṛḍhaśuśrūṣaṇayā dadhau prasādam || 6||
manuneti | upahṛtāṃ sapāribarhaṃ satkṛtya dattām | bhavadarcananirvṛto'pi nivṛttidharmanirato'pi dṛḍhayā avyabhicāriṇyā amāyayā śuśrūṣaṇayā prasādaṃ dadhau ||6||
sa punastvadupāsanaprabhāvād dayitākāmakṛte kṛte vimāne |
vanitākulasaṅkule navātmā vyaharad devapatheṣu devahūtyā || 7 ||
sa iti | dayitākāmakṛte priyayābhyarthitastadicchānusaraṇārthaṃ kṛte svayogānubhāvena racite vanitākulasaṅkule paricārakavidyādharastrīsahasraparivṛte navo nūtno mohanaveṣojjvalaḥ ṣoḍaśavarṣīya ātmā deho yasya sa navātmā | athavā nava ātmānaḥ śarīrāṇi yasya, suratotsukāṃ priyāmātmānaṃ navadhā vibhajya ramayan devapatheṣu nandanapuṣpabhadracaitrarathādidevodyāneṣu vyaharat krīḍāṃ cakre || 7 ||
śatavarṣamatha vyatītya so'yaṃ nava kanyāḥ samavāpya dhanyarūpāḥ |
vanayānasamudyato'pi kāntāhitakṛt tvajjananotsuko nyavātsīt || 8 ||
śatavarṣamiti | vanayānasamudyataḥ tapase vanaṃ jigamiṣurapi kāntāhitad duhitṛvarapradānaputrajananādi kurvan, tatrāpi tvajjananotsukaḥ || 8 ||
nijabhartṛgirā bhavanniṣevāniratāyāmatha deva ! devahūtyām |
kapilastvamajāyathā janānāṃ prathayiṣyan paramātmatattva vidyām || 9 ||
nijeti | paramātmanastattvānāṃ caturviṃśateśca vidyāṃ jñānaṃ janānāṃ prathayiṣyan ācāryaparamparayāgamarūpeṇa prakāśayitumudyataḥ || 9 ||
vanameyuṣi kardame prasanne matasarvasvamupādiśañjananyai |
kapilātmaka ! vāyumandireśa! tvaritaṃ tvaṃ paripāhi māṃ gadaughāt || 10 ||
vanamiti | vanameyuṣi svaduhitṛrmarīcyādibhyo brahmaputrebhyo dattvā vanaṃ gatavati sati jananyai matasarvasvaṃ sarvasvīyāvayavayuktaṃ svasiddhāntamupādiśan grāhayan | tvaritamatilaghu || 10 ||
iti kapilopākhyānaṃ caturdaśaṃ daśakam |
matiriha guṇasaktā bandhakṛt teṣvasaktā
'tvamṛtakṛduparundhe bhaktiyogastu saktim |
mahadanugamalabhyā bhaktirevātra sādhyā
'kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ || 1 ||
matiriti | iha triṣu lokeṣu ye guṇāḥ srakcandanavanitāmṛtādiviṣayāsteṣu saktā matiḥ puruṣasya bandhakṛt śarīrabandhaṃ karoti | teṣu guṇeṣvasaktā tu tathā bandhāya na bhavati | apicāmṛtaṃ mokṣaṃ karoti | tarhi guṇasaṅgaḥ kena tyajyetāta āha -- uparundha iti | bhaktiyogastu saktimuparundhe pratibadhnāti | atha bhaktiḥ kena sādhyata ityata āha -- mahaditi | mahatāṃ satāmanugamanena śuśrūṣayā labhyā bhaktiḥ | saivātra loke mumukṣubhiḥ sādhyeti kapilatanuḥ kapilavāsudevatvenāvatīrṇastvaṃ devahūtyai svamātre nyagādīḥ upadiṣṭavān || 1 ||
prakṛtimahadahaṅkārākṣca mātrāśca bhūtā'nyapi hṛdapi daśākṣī puruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśaḥ |
iti viditavibhāgo mucyate'sau prakṛtyā
'kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ || 2 ||
prakṛtīti| prakṛtirmūlaprakṛtiḥ sarvakāraṇatvād, yā pradhānamavyaktamavidyā māyetyādiśabdairabhidhīyate, mahad mahattattvaṃ mūlaprakṛtikāryam, ahaṅkārā mahata utpannāḥ sāttvikarājasatāmasāḥ, mātrāstāmasāhaṅkārakāryāṇyapañcīkṛtapañcabhūtāni śabdasparśarūparasagandharūpāṇi, bhūtāni pañca mahābhūtāni pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśarūpāṇi, hṛd manaḥ, daśānāmakṣāṇāmindriyāṇāṃ ghrāṇarasanacakṣuḥśrotratvagvākpāṇipādapāyūpasthānāṃ samāddāro daśākṣī | evaṃ caturviṃśatitattvāni | pūruṣaḥ puruṣo jīveśvarabhedād dvidhā iti vidito vibhāgo yena sa tathā | asau jīvaḥ | prakṛtyā māyayā, tatkāryaśarīrādibandhairvā || 2 ||
nanu bandhasya vāstavatve jīvasya tasmād mokṣo na syādityāśaṅkaya tasyāvidyāvilasitatvena jñānanivartyatvamāha
prakṛtigataguṇaughairnājyate puruṣo'yaṃ
yadi tu sajati tasyāṃ tadguṇāstaṃ bhajeran |
madanubhajanatattvālocanaiḥ sāpyapeyāt
kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ || 3 ||
prakṛtīti | prakṛtiriti prakṛtikāryacaturviṃśatitattvamayāni jarāyujāṇḍajasvedajodbhijjāni caturvidhāni śarīrāṇyucyante, tadgataguṇaughāḥ kartṛtvabhoktṛtvādayaḥ, tairnājyate noparajyate| śarīrastho'pyayaṃ jīvaḥ paramārthataḥ śararidharmaiḥ kartṛtvabhoktṛtvajanmamaraṇādibhirna lipyata ityarthaḥ | ayaṃ puruṣo yadi tasyāṃ prakṛtyāṃ śarīrādau sajati ahaṃ mametyabhimanyate, tarhi tadguṇāḥ prakṛtiguṇāstaṃ bhajeran jananamaraṇādisaṃsāraduḥkhamanubhāvayantītyarthaḥ | atra yadiśabdaḥ prakṛtisaṃbandhasyāparamārthatvaṃ dyotayati| madanubhajanamīśvarapraṇidhānaṃ, tattvālocanaṃ brahmajñānaṃ, taiḥ | tatra śravaṇamanananididhyāsanarūpaprakārabhedād bahuvacanam | sā prakṛtirapeyāt | taduktaṃ "tīvreṇātmasamāghinā | prakṛtiḥ puruṣasyeha dahyamānā tvaharniśam | tirobhavitrī" iti || 3 ||
īśvarabhajanasyāṣṭāṅgatvamāha -- 
vimalamatirupāttairāsanādyairmadaṅgaṃ
garuḍasamadhirūḍhaṃ divyaṃbhūṣāyudhāṅkam |
rucitulitatamālaṃ śīlayetānuvelaṃ
kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ || 4 ||
vimaleti | vimalā yamaniyamairviśuddhā matiryasya sa tathā | upāttaiḥ svīkṛtairvijitairityarthaḥ | āsanādyaiḥ prāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasamādhibhiḥ mameśvarasyāṅgaṃ rūpaṃ divyānyaprākṛtāni bhūṣāyudhānyevāṅko lāñchanaṃ yasya | anuvelaṃ sarvadā śīlayetābhyasyet || 4 ||
saṃprati jñānayogād bhaktimārgasya sugamatvamāha -- 
mama guṇagaṇalīlākarṇanaiḥ kīrtanādyairmayi surasaridoghaprakhyacittānuvṛttiḥ |
bhavati paramabhaktiḥ sā hi mṛtyorvijetrī
kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ || 5 ||
mameti | tatra bhaktirdvedhā sādhanasādhyabhedāt | mameśvarasya guṇagaṇā bhaktavātsalyādayaḥ, līlāḥ caritāni, tadākarṇanaiḥ | kīrtanamādyaṃ yeṣāṃ smaraṇanamaskārārcanabandanadāsyasakhyātmanivedanānāṃ te tathā taiḥ | navalakṣaṇayā sādhanabhaktyetyarthaḥ | paramabhaktiḥ sādhyabhaktirbhavatītyuktam | tasyāḥ svarūpamāha -- mathīti | surasaridoghaprakhyā gaṅgāpravāhatulyā cittānuvṛttiḥ premātmikā manovṛttiḥ, sādhyabhaktirityarthaḥ| sā bhaktiḥ| hiravadhāraṇe | mṛtyoḥ saṃsārasya vijetrī viśeṣeṇānāyāsenaiva saṃsārasya samūlonmūlanītyarthaḥ || 5 ||
atha bhaktidāryārthe kevalapāpakarmaṇā narakapātaprakāramāha -- 
ahaha bahulahiṃsāsañcitārthaiḥ kuḍumbaṃ
pratidinamanupuṣṇan strījito bālalālī |
viśati hi gṛhasakto yātanāṃ mayyabhaktaḥ
kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ || 6||
ahaheti | ahaheti khede | bahulayā prabhūtathā hiṃsayā upadraveṇa parārthaspṛhayā itastataśrauryādinā sañcitairārjitairarthairdhanadhānyādibhiḥ kuṭumbaṃ putrabhāryādīn | yātanāṃ rausvādinaskaṃ viśati| kevalapāpakarmiṇo narakapraveśaprakāraścoktaḥ "yamadūtairyātanāśarīraṃ nītvā pāśairgale baddhvākṛṣya tribhirmuhūrtairnavanavatisahasrayojanadūre yamasadanaṃ prāpito'ṣṭāviṃśatikoṭinarakabhedān pāpatāratamyanānubhūyānantaraṃ tṛṇādiṣu viṃśatilakṣaṃ janmānyavāpya jalajantuṣu navalakṣaṃ kṛmijātiṣvekādaśalakṣaṃ pakṣijātiṣu daśalakṣaṃ catuṣpājjātiṣu saptakoṭijanmāni cāvāpyānantaraṃ manuṣyajātiṣu caturlakṣaṃ brāhmaṇajātiṣu hīnamadhyottamatayā śataṃ ca janmānyavāpya tatra punarapi pāpakṛñcennarakeṣu patati" iti || 6 ||
samprati samābhyāṃ puṇyapāpābhyāṃ punaḥ punarmartyalokaprāptimāha -- 
yuvatijaṭharakhinno jātabodho'pyakāṇḍe
prasavagalitabodhaḥ pīḍayollaṅghaya bālyam |
punarapi vata muhyatyeva tāruṇyakāle
kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ || 7 ||
yuvatīti | māturgarbhe vimūtragarte khinnaḥ kaṭumlakṣāroṣṇairmātṛbhuktānnapānādibhiḥ kṛmibhiścopadrutaḥ akāṇḍe duḥkhapratīkārakaraṇāsambhavakāle jātabodhaḥ garbhasthaḥ saptame māsi daivāllabdhasmṛtirbhavati | tathāpi jīvaḥ kiṃ nāma karoti | prasave sūtimārutanodanena yoniyantranippīḍanena ca galito'pagato janmaśatasañcitakarmaviṣayo bodho yasya | punaśca pīḍayeṣṭāniṣṭaprāptiparihārāsāmarthyasambhūtena duḥkhena saṃha bālyamullaṅghaya punarapi tāruṇyakāle bhagavadbhajanasāmarthya satyapi muhyatyeva | kāmakrodhaparādhīnaḥ san kāmyaṃ niṣiddhaṃvā karma kurvāṇastamo viśatītyarthaḥ || 7 ||
kevalena puṇyena svargādigatiṃ darśayati -- 
pitṛsuragaṇayājī dhārmiko yo gṛhasthaḥ
sa ca nipatati kāle dakṣiṇādhvopagāmī |
māya nihitamakāmaṃ karma tūdakpathārthaṃ
kapilatanuriti tvaṃ devahūtyai nyagādīḥ || 8 ||
pitriti | bhagavaddharmavimukhatayā pitṝn suragaṇāṃśca sarvakāmyādiphalakāmanayā yaṣṭuṃ śīlamasyeti tathā | dhārmikaḥ niṣiddhakarmaparityāgena kevalavihitakarmānuṣṭhātā | co'pyarthe | so'pi kāle puṇyakṣaye sati nipatati punarāvartate | dakṣiṇādhvopagāmī dhūmādimārgagamanaśīlaḥ | dhūmādimārgagamanāgamanaprakāraścetthaṃ -- kevalakānyakarmakṛjjīvaḥ prārabdhāvasāne cakṣurādīndriyadvārā liṅgaśarīreṇa nirgacchati | taṃ ca dhūmābhimāninī devatā rājyabhimānidevatāhaste prayacchati, rātrikālābhimāninī devatā kṛṣṇapakṣābhimānidevatāyai, sā ca dakṣiṇāyanaṣaṇmāsābhimāninyai | sā ca pitṛlokamārgeṇākāśe candramasaṃ sannidhāpayati | tatra yāvatsukṛtāvasānaṃ svargasukhamanubhūya bhūmau patiṣyan prathamamākāśamārge patitvā tato vāyudhūmābhravṛṣṭikrameṇauṣadhīṣu patati | oṣadhībhyaḥ annamannādreto bhūtvā strīyoniṃ gataḥ śarīrī bhavatīti | yadi puṇyaśeṣastarhi brāhmaṇoṃdiyonau, pāpaśeṣastarhi caṇḍālādiyonau ca jāyate | tarhi kiṃ kurvanuttarāyaṇamārgeṇa yātītyata āha -- mayīti | mayīśvare nihitaṃ samarpitam akāmaṃ kāmyaniṣiddhavyatiriktaṃ karma nityanaimittikaprāyaścittopāsanarūpam | turevārthe | udakpathārthamārkcirādimāṃrgaprāptiphalakamiti yāvat || 8 ||
atha bhagavatprayāṇaṃ devahūtermokṣaprāptiṃ caikena kṣlokenāha -- 
iti suviditavedyāṃ deva! he devahūtiṃ
kṛtanutimanugṛhya tvaṃ gato yogisaṅgaiḥ |
vimalamatirathāsau bhaktiyogena muktā
tvamapi janahitārtha vartase prāgudīcyām || 9 ||
itīti | suviditaṃ samyagavaghṛtaṃ vedyaṃ vastu brahma tatprāptisādhanaṃ ca yayā tām | atha tvadgamanānantaram | asau devahūtiḥ | tvamapi kapilo'pi janahitārthe trailokyarakṣāyai prāgudīcyāṃ diśi vartase || 9 ||
atha kāpilamupasaṃharan bhaktiṃ prārthayate -- 
parama! kimu bahūktyā tvatpadāmbhojabhaktiṃ
sakalabhayavinetrīṃ sarvakāmopanetrīm |
vadāsa khalu dṛḍhaṃ tvaṃ tad vidhūyāmayān me
gurupavanapureśa! tvayyupādhatsva bhaktim || 10 ||
parameti | sakalabhayānāṃ saṃsāraduḥkhasyāpi vinetrīṃ vinayanamupaśamaṃ kartrī samūlonmūlanīmityarthaḥ | sarvakāmānabhīṣṭārthānupanayati prāpayatīti sarvakāmopanetrī, tām|
dṛḍhaṃ niścayena | tat tasmād, yasmād bhayanivṛttaye'bhīṣṭaprāptyai ca tvadbhaktireva prārthanīyati nissaṃśayaṃ tvayoktaṃ, tasmādityarthaḥ | upādhatsva saṅkrāmaya | bhaktiṃ premalakṣaṇām || 10 || 83 ||
iti kapilopākhyānaṃ pañcadaśaṃ daśakam |
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyākhyāyāṃ
tṛtīyaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
mahadādeḥ prajeśānāṃ brahmāṇḍasya ca sambhavaḥ |
sargalīloditā viṣṇorvisargo'tha nirūpyate ||
atha visargopayoginaranārāyaṇāvatāracaritaprastāvāya dakṣapariṇayanādyāha -- 
dakṣo viriñcatanayo'tha manostanūjāṃ
labdhvā prasūtimiha ṣoḍaśa cāpa kanyāḥ |
dharme trayodaśa dadau pitṛṣu svadhāṃ ca
svāhāṃ havirbhuji satīṃ giriśe tvadaṃśe || 1 ||
dakṣa iti | kathāntarārambhārtho'thaśabdaḥ | manoḥ svāyambhuvasya | iha prasūtisaṃjñāyāṃ svabhāryāyām | dharme dharmadevāya trayodaśa mainyādyāḥ putrīḥ | pitṛṣu pitṛbhyo yuktebhyaḥ svadhām | tvadaṃśe tvatsvarūpe || 1 ||
mūrtirhi dharmagṛhiṇī suṣuve bhavantaṃ
nārāyaṇaṃ narasakhaṃ mahitānubhāvam |
yajjanmani pramuditāḥ kṛtatūryaghoṣāḥ
puṣpotkarān pravavṛṣurtunuvuḥ suraughāḥ || 2 ||
mūrtiriti | mūrtiḥ dharmagṛhiṇīṣvavarajā bhavantaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ narasakhamiti viṣṇoraṃśabhūtau naranārāyaṇau suṣuve janayāmāsa || 2 ||
daityaṃ sahasrakavacaṃ kavacaiḥ parītaṃ
sāhasravatsaratapassamarābhilavyaiḥ |
paryāyanirmitatapassamarau bhavantau
śiṣṭaikakaṅkaṭamamuṃ nyahatāṃ salīlam || 3 ||
daityamiti | kavacaiḥ parītaṃ sahajasahasrakavacasannaddhaśarīram | sāhasreti | sahasrasaṃvatsaraṃ cīrṇana tapasā tāvatkālaṃ kṛtena samareṇa cacchedyarityarthaḥ paryāyanirmitau parasparaparihāreṇa kṛtau tapassamarau yābhyāṃ tau tathā | ekasmistapasyati satyanyo yudhyati, tathānyasminnapītyarthaḥ | śiṣṭaikakaṅkaṭam avaśiṣṭaikakavacaṃ nyahutāmavadhiṣṭām || 3 ||
anvācarannupadiśannapi mokṣadharmaṃ
tvaṃ bhrātṛmān badarikāśramamadhyavātsīḥ |
śakro'tha te śamatapobalanissahātmā
divyāṅganāparivṛtaṃ prajighāya māram || 4 ||
anvācaranniti | lokasaṅgrahaṇārthaṃ svayamanutiṣṭhan, upadiśan tadarthibhyo nāradādibhya iti śeṣaḥ | mokṣadharmaṃ nivṛttilakṣaṇaṃ dharmam | tvaṃ śrīnārāyaṇaḥ bhrātṛmān nareṇa bhrātrā sahitaḥ | śamenendriyanigraheṇa tapobalena ca nissaha īrṣyāyukta ātmā mano yasya sa tathā | prajighāya preṣayāmāsa | ayaṃ tapasā svargaṃ jighṛkṣatīti śaṅkayā taponāśāya kāmaṃ saparivāraṃ preṣayāmāsetyarthaḥ || 4 ||
atha śaṛprahitasya kāmasya pravṛttimāha -- 
kāmo vasantamalayānilabandhuśālī
kāntākaṭākṣaviśikhairvikasadvilāsaiḥ |
vidhyanmuhurmuhurakampamudīkṣya ca tvāṃ
bhītastvayātha jagade mṛduhāsabhājā || 5 ||
kāma iti | kāntā divyāṅganāstāsāṃ kaṭākṣā eva viśikhāḥ taiḥ vikasanto'bhivyaktā vilāsā bhrūcalanādayaḥ patrasthānīyā yeṣu taiḥ muhurmuhurvidhyan akampaṃ samādheracalacittaṃ tvāṃ bhītaḥ san udīkṣya dūrādevonnamitavadanaḥ sannavalokyātha mṛduhāsabhājā mandasmitasahitena tvayā jagade || 5 ||
bhītyālamaṅgajavasantamurāṅganā ! vo
manyānasaṃ tviha juṣadhvamiti bruvāṇaḥ |
tvaṃ vismayena paritaḥ stutratāmathaiṣāṃ
prādarśayaḥ svaparicārakakātarākṣīḥ || 6 ||
bhītyeti | he aṅgajavasantasurāṅganāḥ ! vo yuṣmākaṃ bhītyā alaṃ bhayaṃ
mā kuruta | ihāsmatsakāśametya manmānasaṃ tu juṣadhvaṃ maccittamanuvartadhvam | athavā mama mānasaṃ manasā saṅkalpitaṃ satkāraṃ juṣadhvaṃ prītyā nirbhayāḥ parigṛhṇīdhvam | yadvā mama manasā sṛjyamānaṃ vaḥ svargadurlabhaṃ kiñcidvastu straritnākhyaṃ mayā dīyamānaṃ juṣadhvamādadīdhvam | manmānanamiti pāṭhe matkartṛkāṃ pūjāmityarthaḥ | iti bruvāṇaṃ tvāṃ paritaḥ kiñcit samīpaṃ gatvā parivārya stuvatāmeṣām atha vacanānantaraṃ svaparicārikā ātmaśuśrūṣāṃ kurvatīḥ kātarākṣīḥ sundarīḥ prādarśayaḥ yogabalena sṛṣṭvā pradarśitavān asi || 6 ||
sammohanāya militā madanādayaste
tvaddāsikāparimalaiḥ kila mohamāpuḥ |
dattāṃ tvayā ca jagṛhustrapayaiva sarvasvarvāsigarvaśamanīṃ punarurvaśīṃ tām || 7 ||
sammohanāyeti | tvāṃ mohayituṃ sahitāḥ saṃhatyāgatā madanādayo madanamalayamārutavasantasurāṅganāḥ | urvaśīṃ tvatsṛṣṭāsvanyatamām || 7 ||
dṛṣṭvorvaśīṃ tava kathāṃ ca niśamya śakraḥ
paryākulo'jani bhavanmahimāvamarśāt |
evaṃ praśāntaramaṇīyataro'vatārastvatto'dhiko varada ! kṛṣṇatanustvameva || 8 ||
dṛṣveti | praśāntaḥ saumyaḥ | tvattaḥ śrīnārāyaṇāvatārād adhikaḥ praśāntatvena vicitrakarmatayā ramaṇīyataratvena cotkṛṣṭaḥ | kṛṣṇatanuḥ śrīkṛṣṇātmā || 8 ||
evaṃ dakṣaputrīṣu dharmapatnyā mūrteḥ kathāmuktvā bhavapatnyāḥ satyāḥ kathāmāha -- 
dakṣastu dhāturatilālanayā rajondho
nātyādṛtastvayi ca kaṣṭamaśāntirāsīt |
yena vyarundha sa bhavattanumeva śarve
yajñe ca vairapiśune svasutāṃ vyamānīt || 9 ||
vimānitavān | 'māna pūjāyām' ityata ādhṛṣīyād ṇijabhāṣe luṅi rūpam | dakṣa iti | dhāturbrahmaṇa atilālanayā prajāpatīnāmādhipatye saṃsthāpyābhiṣekādirūpayā rajasā tatkāryeṇa kāmakrodhalobhādinā andho vivekaśūnyaḥ ata eva brahmaṇāpi nātyādṛtaḥ upekṣitaḥ aśāntiravidyamānā śāntiḥ kṣamā yasya sa aśāntirakṣama āsīt | yenāśāntitvena sa dakṣo bhavattanuṃ tvadaṃśabhūtameva śarvaṃ vyarundha dveṣṭi sma, yajñe dakṣādhvare vairapiśune dakṣaśarvavairadyotake svasutāṃ śarvapatnīṃ satīṃ vyamānīd avamānitavān || 9 ||
kruddheśamarditamakhaḥ sa tu kṛttaśīrṣo
devaprasāditaharādatha labdhajīvaḥ |
tvatpūritakratuvaraḥ punarāpa śānti
sa tvaṃ praśāntikara ! pāhi marutpureśa ! || 10 ||
kruddheśeti | svapriyāvamānena kruddheneśena marditaścūrṇīkṛto makhastadupakaraṇayajñapātrayūpaśālādiryasya saḥ svayaṃ ca dakṣaḥ kṛttaśīrṣo'bhavat | atha devaiḥ prasāditāddharād labdhajīva ajamukhaścābhūt | tatastvayā pūritaḥ samyak samāpitaḥ ṛtuvaro yasya sa dakṣaḥ punaḥ śānti svāṃ prakṛtimāpa | he praśāntikara ! evaṃprabhāvaḥ sa tvaṃ pāhi || 10 ||
iti naranārāyaṇacaritavarṇanaṃ dakṣayāgavarṇanaṃ ca
ṣoḍaśaṃ daśakam |
evaṃ manuputrīṇāṃ paramparāṃ varṇayitvā tatputraparamparāṃ varṇayiṣyan prathamaṃ dhruvacaritārthamuttānapādacaritamāha
uttānapādanṛpatermanunandanasya
jāyā babhūva surucirnitarāmabhīṣṭā |
anyā sunītiriti bharturanādṛtā sā
tvāmeva nityamagatiḥ śaraṇaṃ gatābhūt || 1 ||
uttāneti | uttānapādākhyasya nṛpatermanoḥ svāyambhuvasya nandanaḥ priyavratāvarajaḥ, tasya dve jāye suruciḥ sunītiśca | tayoḥ surucirnāma jāyā nitarāmabhīṣṭā babhūva | sunītirityanyā jāyā sā bharturanādṛtā nātyabhīṣṭā ata evāgatiraśaraṇā tvāmīśvarameva nityaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatābhūt nijadaurbhāgya hetuduritaśamanāyeśvaramupāsāṃcakra ityarthaḥ || 1 ||
aṅke pituḥ suruciputrakamuttamaṃ taṃ
dṛṣṭvā dhruvaḥ kila sunītisuto'dhirokṣyan |
ācikṣipe kila śiśuḥ sutarāṃ surucyā
dussantyajā khalu bhavadvimukhairasūyā || 2 ||
aṅka iti | kadācit sunītisuto dhruvaḥ pituḥ siṃhāsanasthasyāṅke uttamāsvyaṃ taṃ suruciputratvenātilālitaṃ dṛṣṭvā svayamadhirokṣyan āroḍhumārabdhaḥ san sutarāṃ śiśurapi surucyā ācikṣipe durvāgmiradhikṣipto'bhūt | bhavadvimukhairasūyā dussantyajā tyaktumaśakyā khallu | anena surucyā īśvaravimukhatayā putranāśādi, sunīteśceśvarānugraheṇa putreṇa saha dhruvapadaprāptyādi vyajyate || 2 ||
tvanmohite pitari paśyati dāravaśye
dūraṃ duruktinihataḥ sa gato nijāmbām |
sāpi svakarmagatisantaraṇāya puṃsāṃ
tvatpādameva śaraṇaṃ śiśave śaśaṃsa || 3 ||
tvaditi | tvanmohite īśvaraśaktyā bhavitavyatayā mūḍhacitte | dāravaśya ityanena svāṅkamārurukṣantaṃ svaputraṃ paśyannapi surucibhayena nāropayaditi vyajyate | dūramatiśayena duruktibhirdurvāgmirnihato viddhaḥ sa dhruvaḥ nijāmbāṃ svamātaraṃ sunītimeva gataḥ prāptavān | sāpi svasya karmagaterduṣkarmaphalasya daurbhāgyasya santaraṇāya nivṛttaye | yadvā puṃsāṃ sarvaśarīriṇāmapi svakarmagateḥ saṃsārārṇavasya santaraṇamanāyāsenātikramaḥ tasmai tvatpādameva śaraṇaṃ nānyaditi śaśaṃsa | śiśava ityanena putramaṅkamāropya mā rodīrityādyanunayapūrvakamuktavatīti vyajyate || 3 ||
ākarṇya so'pi bhavadarcananiścitātmā
mānī niretya nagarāt kila pañcavarṣaḥ |
sandṛṣṭanāradaniveditamantramārgastvāmārarādha tapasā madhukānanānte || 4 ||
ākaryeti | ākarṇya māturvacanamiti śeṣaḥ | sa dhruvaḥ bhavadarcane tapaścaraṇe niścita ātmā mano yasya saḥ mānī | pañcavarṣa iti | pañcavā api jigamiṣuṃ dṛṣṭvā viṣaṇṇāṃ skhamātaraṃ kathañcidanujñāpyānyānanavalokayannekākī svayaṃ nagarānnirjagāmetyarthaḥ | sa pathi dṛṣṭena nāradena nivedita upadiṣṭo mantramārgo yasya saḥ tvām ārarādha prasādayāmāsa | madhukānanānte madhuvanamiti prasiddhe deśe || 4 ||
tāte viṣaṇṇahṛdaye nagarīṃ gatena
śrīnāradena parisāntvita cittavṛttau |
bālastvadarpitamanāḥ kramavardhitena
ninye kaṭhoratapasā kila pañca māsān || 5||
tāta iti | tāte pitaryuttānapāde viṣaṇṇahṛdaye ātmadoṣeṇa bālakasya nagarānnirgamanamupaśrutya tasyāpāyaśaṅkayā sotkaṇṭhahṛdaye dhruvasya tapaścaraṇetikartavyatopadeśānantaramevāsya pitṛdarśanāya nagarīṃ gatena śrīnāradena parisāntvitā tasya bhāviśreyaḥprāptikathanādinā mandīkṛtaśokāvegā cittavṛttirmano yasya sa tathā tasmin evaṃsthite sati bālo dhruvastvadarpitamanāḥ tvayīśvare samāhitacittaḥ san kramabarghitena phalaparṇajalādikrameṇa śanaiḥ parihṛtenāhāreṇābhivyaktakramotkarṣeṇa kaṭhoraṇogreṇa tapasā pañca māsān ninye || 5 ||
tataśca jātaśaṅkhairdevarairthitaḥ śrīharistasya purataḥ prādurbabhūvetyāha -- 
tāvat tapobalanirucchvasite digante
devārthitastvamudayatkaruṇārdracetāḥ |
tvadrūpacidrasanilīnamateḥ purastādāvirbabhūvitha vibho ! garuḍādhirūḍhaḥ || 6 ||
tāvāditi | tvadrūpacidrase tvadākāre caturbhujatvādiviśiṣṭatayā ghanībhūya
sthite cidrase parabrahmaṇi nilīnā tadākārākāritā matiryasya tasyetyarthaḥ | yadvā tādṛśyā matestasya purastādāvirbabhūvitheti | anena ca tvadrūpaṃ hṛdi sahasā tirohitamupalakṣyonmīlitanayanastadavasthaṃ bāhiḥsthitaṃ bhagavadrūpamasau dadarśeti vyajyate || 6 ||
tvadarśanapramadabhārataraṅgitaṃ taṃ
dṛgbhyāṃ nimagnamiva rūparasāyane te |
tuṣṭūmāṇamavagamya kapoladeśe
saṃspṛṣṭavānasi dareṇa tathādareṇa || 7 ||
tvaditi | tvaddarśanapramadabhāre taraṅgitam itikartavyatāmūḍhatayā kiñcidiva sambhrāntaṃ punaśca te rūparasāyane rūpāmṛte dṛgbhyāṃ nimagnamavagāḍhanayanayugmamivasthitaṃ taṃ tuṣṭūṣamāṇaṃ stotumicchantaṃ tadasamarthaṃ cāvagamya tatsāmarthyasampādanāya dareṇa śabdabrahmātmakena pāñcajanyākhyena śaṅkhena kapoladeśe saṃspṛṣṭavānasi | ādareṇa lālanapūrvakam || 7 ||
tāvad vibodhavimalaṃ praṇuvantamenamābhāṣathāstvamavagamya tadīyabhāvam |
rājyaṃ ciraṃ samanubhūya bhajasva bhūyaḥ
sarvottaraṃ dhruva ! padaṃ vinivṛttihīnam || 8 ||
tāvaditi | dareṇa kapole spṛṣṭamātra eva vibodhena jñānavijñānābhyāṃ vagataṃ malamajñānaṃ yasmāt sa vimalaḥ, tam | yadvā vimalaṃ yathā bhavati tatheti stavanakriyāviśeṣaṇam | anena ca stuternirguṇabrahmaniṣṭhatvaṃ vyajyate | tadīyaṃ dhruvasambandhinaṃ bhāvamabhiprāyamavagamya ābhāṣathā uktavānasi | sarvottaraṃ saptarṣilokādapyupariṣṭāt sthitaṃ padaṃ sthānaṃ he dhruva ! vinivṛttihīnaṃ punarāvṛttirahitam || 8 ||
dhruve rājyabhāraṃ nyasya pitā vanaṃ gatavānityāha -- 
ityucuṣi tvayi gate nṛpanandano'sāvānanditākhilajano nagarīmupetaḥ |
reme ciraṃ bhavadanugrahapūrṇakāmastāte gate ca vanamāhatarājyabhāraḥ || 9 ||
punaruttamacaritamāha -- 
yakṣeṇa deva ! nihate punaruttame'smin
yakṣaiḥ sa yuddhanirato virato manuktyā |
śāntyā prasannahṛdayād dhanadādupetāt
tvadbhaktimeva sudṛḍhāmavṛṇonmahātmā || 10 ||
yakṣeṇeti | uttame mṛgayāyāṃ kenacid yakṣeṇa yuddhe nihate'nantaraṃ sa dhruvo bhrātṛvadhāmarṣeṇa yakṣairyuddhaniratastān nihatavān, manoḥ pitāmahasyoktyā virataścābhūt | aya dhruvasya śāntyā yuddhanivṛttyā dhruvasamīpamāgatād dhanadād rājarājād varaṃ vṛṇīṣvetyukto dhruvastvadbhaktimevāvṛṇot | atra heturmahātmeti || 10 ||
atha dhruvasya svādaprāptimāha -- 
ante bhavatpuruṣanītavimānayāto
mātrā samaṃ dhruvapade mudito'yamāste |
evaṃ svabhṛtyajanapālanaloladhīstvaṃ
vātālayādhipa ! nirundhi mamāmayaughān || 11 ||
anta iti | ante ṣaḍviṃśadvarṣasahasraṃ rājyamanubhūya tadante bhavatpuruṣābhyāṃ sunandanandābhyāṃ nītena vimānena yāto gataḥ san mātrā sunītyā samaṃ saha muditaḥ santuṣṭa ayamāste ityanenādhunāpi tasya vidyamānatvaṃ nakṣatrarūpeṇa dṛśyamānatvaṃ ca sūcitam || 11 ||
iti dhruvacaritavarṇanaṃ saptadaśaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
atha pṛthucaritakathanāya venakathāṃ prastauti -- 
jātasya dhruvakula eva tuṅgakīrteraṅgasya vyajani sutaḥ sa venanāmā |
yaddoṣavyathitamatiḥ sa rājavaryastvatpāde vihitamanā vanaṃ gato'bhūt || 1 ||
jātasyeti | dhruvakula eva jātasyāṅganāmno rājñaḥ sunīthāyāṃ venanāmā suto'jani, yasya venasya doṣeṇādhārmikatvena vyathito nirviṇṇaḥ so'ṅganāmā rājavaryo vanaṃ gataḥ | niśīthe janairalakṣito gata iti draṣṭavyam || 1 ||
