venasyādhārmikatvaṃ prapañcayati -- 
pāpo'pi kṣititalapālanāya venaḥ
paurādyairupanihitaḥ kaṭhoravīryaḥ |
sarvebhyo nijavalameva sampaśaṃsan
bhūcakre tava yajanānyayaṃ nyarautsīt || 2 ||
pāpa iti | upanihitaḥ siṃhāsane saṃsthāpyābhiṣiktaḥ || 2 ||
atha sajjananindayeśvaranindayā cāsau muniśāpāgninirdagdho'bhūdityāha -- 
samprāpte hitakathanāya tāpasaughe
matto'nyo bhuvanapatirna kaścaneti |
tvannindāvacanaparo munīśvaraistaiḥ
śāpāgnau śalabhadaśāmanāyi venaḥ || 3 ||
atha pṛthvavatāramāha -- 
tannāśāt khalajanabhīrukairmunīndraistanmātrā ciraparirakṣite tadaṅge |
tyaktādhe parimathitādathorudaṇḍād dordaṇḍe parimathite tvamāvirāsīḥ || 4 ||
tannāśāditi | tasya venasya nāśādarājake khalajanebhyo dasyubhyo bhīrukaiḥḥ | tanmātrā sunīthayā yogavalena rakṣite tadaṅge venaśarīre parimathitādūrudaṇḍāt tyaktāgha iti | ayamarthaḥ -- ṛṣibhirmathitādūrorniṣādo nāma kaścit puruṣo jātaḥ | sa ca venakalmaṣāṃśatvāt kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ | tadvaṃśyā naiṣādā girikānenacarāścābhūvanniti | atha dordaṇḍe parimathite tasmāt tvaṃ pṛthuvapuḥ san āvirāsīḥ avatīrṇavān || 4 ||
vikhyātaḥ pṛthuriti tāpasopadiṣṭaiḥ
sutādyaiḥ pariṇutabhāvibhūrivīryaḥ |
venārtyā kavalitasampadaṃ dharitrīmākrāntāṃ nijadhanuṣā samāmakārṣīḥ || 5 ||
vikhyāta iti | pṛthuriti vikhyātastvaṃ tāpasopadiṣṭaiḥ "ayaṃ sākṣāddhareraṃśaḥ" ityāditāpasopadeśena tatprabhāvajñaiḥ sūtamāgadhavandibhiḥ pariṇutāni stutāni bhāvibhūrivīryāṇi mahīdohanaikonaśatavājimedhādīni yasya sa tathā | venārtyā venakṛtayā ārtyā pīḍayā kabalitā grastā sampadoṣadhiryayā tāṃ kabalitasampadam ata evākrāntāṃ kruddhena tvayā āttaśarāsanena dhāvayatā natonnatāṃ kṛtāṃ nijadhanuṣkoṭyā samāmakārṣīḥ, girikūṭāni cūrṇayan puragrāmādivibhāgena prāyaḥ samatalāṃ kṛtavāniti bhāvaḥ || 5 ||
atha prajāḥ pṛthuśāsanād gorūpiṇīṃ mahīṃ svasvābhimatānnamayaṃ kṣīraṃ duduhurityāha -- 
bhūyastāṃ nijakulamukhyavatsayukttairdevādyaiḥ samucitacārubhājaneṣu |
annādīnyabhilaṣitāni yāni tāni
svacchandaṃ surabhitanūmadūduhastvam || 6 ||
bhūya iti | tāṃ mahīṃ nijakulamukhyavatsayuktairiti | ayamarthaḥ -- pṛthuḥ svakulamukhyaṃ manuṃ vatsaṃ kṛtvā pāṇipātre oṣadhīraghukṣat | ṛṣayo bṛhaspatiṃ vatsaṃ kṛtvendriyeṣu pātreṣu mahīṃ chandomayaṃ kṣīraṃ duduhuḥ | devā indraṃ vatsaṃ kṛtvā svarṇapātre somaṃ duduhuḥ | asurāṇāṃ prahlādo vatsaḥ ayaḥ pātraṃ surā payaḥ, gandharvā madhu| pitaraḥ kavyam | evaṃ draṣṭavyam | surabhitanūṃ gorūpiṇīmadūduhaḥ, tvacchāsanād duduhuriti tātparyam || 6 ||
ātmānaṃ yajati makhaistvayi tridhāmannārabdhe śatatamavājimedhayāge |
spardhāluḥ śatamakha etya nīcaveṣo
hṛtvāśvaṃ tava tanayāt parājito'bhūt || 7 ||
ātmānamiti | he tridhāman ! viṣṇo ! tvayi pṛthau makhairaśvameghairātmānaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ viṣṇuṃ yajati sati śatatame śatasaṃkhyāyā antime vājimedhayāge ārabdhe śatamakhaḥ iha jagatyahameka eva śatakraturityabhimānīndraḥ nīcaveṣaḥ pāṣaṇḍaveṣaḥ sannetyākṣvaṃ hṛtvā tava tanayāt pṛthuputrādanenaiva karmaṇā vijitāśvasaṃjñāt || 7 ||
devendraṃ muhuriti vājinaṃ harantaṃ vahnau taṃ munivaramaṇḍale juhūpau | rundhāne kamalabhave kratoḥ samāptau sākṣāt tvaṃ madhuripumaikṣathāḥ svayaṃ svam || 8 ||
"devendramiti | anena prakāreṇa nagnaraktapaṭajaṭābhasmādipāṣaṇḍaveṣeṇa muhuḥ punaḥ punaḥ vājinamaśvaṃ harantaṃ muṣṇantaṃ taṃ devendraṃ munivarāṇāṃ maṇḍale samūhe vahnau dakṣiṇāgnau juhūṣau vadhārthamābhicāravidhānena hotumicchati sati kamalabhave brahmaṇi rundhāne 'tavaikonaśatakratutvenālaṃ, śatakratuśca prīto bhavatu, he pṛtho! tava kratubhiralam' ityāgatya pratiṣedhaṃ kurvati sati kratoḥ samāptau jātāyāṃ tvaṃ pṛthuḥ madhuripuṃ viṣṇuṃ sākṣāccākṣuṣatayā aikṣathā dṛṣṭavān | svayaṃ svamityanena pṛthoravatāratvamuktam || 8 ||
taddattaṃ varamupalabhya bhaktimekāṃ
gaṅgānte vihitapadaḥ kadāpi deva ! |
satrasthaṃ muninivahaṃ hitāni śaṃsannaikṣiṣṭhāḥ sanakamukhān munīn purastāt || 9 ||
taddattamiti | tena viṣṇunā dattaṃ varamekāmanapāyinīṃ bhaktimupalabhyānantaraṃ gaṅgānte gaṅgāyamunayormadhye vihitaṃ padaṃ sthānaṃ yasya sa tathā | tatra vasan kadāpi satrasthaṃ satrayāge prāptaṃ munīnāṃ devarṣibrahmarṣirājarṣīṇāṃ nivahaṃ samūhaṃ hitāni pravṛttinivṛttilakṣaṇaṃ dharmadvayaṃ śaṃsan upadiśan tadante sanakamukhān sanakasanandasanātanasanatkumārān aikṣiṣṭhā dṛṣṭavān || 9 ||
vijñānaṃ sanakamukhoditaṃ dadhānaḥ
svātmānaṃ svayamagamo banāntasevī |
tattādṛkpṛthuvapurīśa ! satvaraṃ me
rogaughaṃ praśamaya vātagehavāsin ! || 10 ||
vijñānamiti | sanakamukhoditaṃ vijñānaṃ brahmajñānaṃ dadhānaḥ samyagavadhārya svayaṃ jagadanugrahāyāṅgīkṛtasattvavigrahastvaṃ svātmānaṃ svasvarūpaṃ parabrahmāgamaḥ ahaṃ brahmetyanubhūtavān | paścācca vanāntasevī putre rājyaṃ nyasya vanaṃ gatastapaścaran svayaṃ svātmānamagamaḥ prāptavān | parabrahmamātramupatiṣṭhase sma iti bhāvaḥ || 10 ||
iti pṛthucaritavarṇanam aṣṭādaśaṃ daśakam |
atha prācīnabarhiṣaścaritamārabhate -- 
pṛthostu naptā pṛthudharmakarmaṭhaḥ prācīnavarhiryuvatau śatadbhutau |
pracetaso nāma sucetasaḥ sutānajījanat tvatkaruṇākurāniva || 1 ||
pṛthoriti | naptā pautrasya putraḥ | pṛthoḥ putro vijitāśvaḥ, tatputro havirdhānaḥ, tatputro barhiṣmadayamucyate | sa ca pṛthurvistṛto dharmo yasya, yadvā pṛthoriva dharmo yasya saḥ pṛthudharmā karmaṭhaḥ karmaśūraḥ, karmakāṇḍe niṣṇāta ityarthaḥ | ata eva
prācīnāgnaiḥ kuśairāstṛtamidaṃ bhūmaṇḍalamiti prācīnabarhiriti nāmāsyābhṛt | sa ca
śatadrutau bhāryāyāṃ pracetaso nāmeti pracetasa iti tulyanāmnaḥ sutānajījanat | te ca sarve sucetasaḥ | ata eva tvatkaruṇāyāḥ prācīnabarhirviṣayāyā aṅkarāniveti || 1 ||
pituḥ sisṛkṣāniratasya śāsanād bhavattapasyāniratā daśāpi te |
payonidhiṃ paścimametya tattaṭe sarovaraṃ sandadṛśurmanoharam || 2 ||
pituriti | sisṛkṣāniratasya prajāsargakāmasya prajāḥ sṛjateti śāsanāt te pracetaso daśāpi bhavattapasyāyāṃ niratāḥ || 2 ||
atha bhagavān rudrastatrāgatya teṣāṃ bhagavatstotramupadideśetyāha -- 
tadā bhavattīrthamidaṃ samāgato bhavo bhavatsevakadarśanādṛtaḥ |
prakāśamāsādya puraḥ pracetasāmupādiśad bhaktatamastava stavam || 3 ||
stavaṃ japantastamamī jalāntare bhavantamāseviṣatāyutaṃ samāḥ |
bhavatsukhāsvādarasādamīṣviyān vabhūva kālo dhruvavanna śīghratā || 4 ||
stavamiti | bhavāneva sukhaṃ bhavatsukhaṃ brahmānandaḥ tasyāsvādane rasa āgrahaḥ ||
tapobhireṣāmatimātravardhibhiḥ sa yajñahiṃsānirato'pi pāvitaḥ |
pitāpi teṣāṃ gṛhayātanāradapradarśitātmā bhavadātmatāṃ yayau || 5 ||
tapobhiriti | sa yajñahiṃsānirato venaḥ pṛthorjanakaḥ pāvitaḥ duritānevṛttidvārā narakāduttāritaḥ | teṣāṃ pitā prācīnabarhirapi gṛhayātena nāradena pradarśita
upadiṣṭa ātmā śrīhariryasya, 'āptatvācca pramātṛtvādātmā hi paramo hariri'tyuktatvāt | bhavadātmatāṃ sāyujyam || 5 ||
atha bhaktavātsalyenaiva teṣāṃ tvaṃ prādurabhūrityāha -- 
kṛpābalenaiva tataḥ pracetasāṃ prakāśamāgāḥ pataṃgendravāhanaḥ |
virājicakrādivarāyudhāṃśubhirbhujābhiraṣṭābhirudañcitadyutiḥ || 6 ||
pracetasāṃ tāvadayācatāmapi tvameva kāruṇyabharād varānadāḥ |
bhavadvicintāpi śivāya dehināṃ bhavatvasau rudranutiśca kāmadā || 7 ||
pracetasāmiti | varānadā ityuktaṃ, tadevāha bhavadvicinteti | bhavatāṃ pracetasāṃ vicintānusmaraṇaṃ śivāya maṅgalāya, rudranutiḥ rudreṇa gītā mama stutiśca || 7 ||
avāpya kāntāṃ tanayāṃ mahīruhāṃ tayā ramadhvaṃ daśalakṣavatsarīm |
suto'stu dakṣo nanu tatkṣaṇācca māṃ prayāsyatheti nyagado sudaiva tān || 8 ||
avāpyeti | kiñca mahīruhāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ tanayāṃ putrīṃ, pramlocānāmno'psarasaḥ putrī tatparityaktā vṛkṣaiḥ putrītvena parigṛhīteti draṣṭavyam | tāṃ kāntāmavāpya yuṣmābhirdaśabhiḥ sahaivodvāhya tayā daśalakṣavatsarāṇāṃ samāhāro daśalakṣavatsarī tāvatkālaṃ ramadhvaṃ bhogānanubhavata | tasyāṃ dakṣo nāma sutaścāstu | tatkṣaṇād daśalakṣavatsarānantarameva māṃ prayāsyatha muktā bhaviṣyatha iti mudaiva tatprārthanāṃ vinā tvaṃ tān nyagada uktavān || 8 ||
tataśca te bhūtalarodhinastarūn krudhā dahanto druhiṇena vāritāḥ |
drumaikṣca dattāṃ tanayāmavāpya tāṃ tvaduktakālaṃ sukhino'bhiremire || 9 ||
tataśceti | tato bhagavadgamanānantaraṃ salilādutthitāste bhūtalaṃ roddhuṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāmiti bhūtalarodhinastān tarūn kruṣā koṣodbhatābhyāmanilānalābhyāṃ dahanto druhiṇena brahmaṇā vāritāḥ | tataśca bhītairbrahmaṇopadiṣṭairdumairdattāṃ tāṃ kamalalocanām || 9 ||
avāpya dakṣaṃ ca sutaṃ kṛtādhvarāḥ pracetaso nāradalabdhayā dhiyā |
avāpurānandapadaṃ tathāvidhastvamīśa ! vātālayanātha! pāhi mām || 10 ||
avāpyeti | nāradāllabdhayā ghiyā jñānenānandarūpaṃ padaṃ, padyate prāpyata iti padaṃ brahma avāpuḥ | tathāvidhastādṛśabhaktavātsalyayuktaḥ || 10 || 41 ||
iti dakṣotpattiprasaṅgavarṇanaṃ dakṣotpattivarṇanaṃ ca ekonaviṃśaṃ daśakam |
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyākhyāyāṃ
caturthaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
lokānāṃ prāk svasṛṣṭānāṃ maryādāpālanātmikā |
līlā sthānasamākhyātā hareḥ prastūyate'dhunā ||
idānīṃ pañcamaskandhāśrayaṇena bhagavato maryādāpālanātmikāṃ līlāmabhidadhānaḥ prathamamṛṣabhadevāvatārakathanāya tatpūrvaparamparāmāha  -- 
priyavratasya priyaputrabhūtādāgnīdhrarājādudito hi nābhiḥ |
tvāṃ dṛṣṭvāniṣṭadamiṣṭimadhye tavaiva tuṣṭayai kṛtayajñakarmā || 1 ||
priyavratasyeti | svāyambhuvasya manorjyeṣṭhaputraḥ priyavrataḥ | tasya barhiṣmatyāṃ daśa putrā abhavan | teṣu jyeṣṭha āgnīdhro nāma rājā svasamānaśīlatayā pituḥ priyaḥ | tasmāt pūrvacittināmāpsarasi nābhirnāma rājā udita utpannaḥ | iṣṭimadhye yajñamadhye || 1 ||
abhiṣṭutastatra munīśvaraistvaṃ rājñā svatulyaṃ sutamarthyamānaḥ |
svayaṃ janiṣye'hamiti bruvāṇastirodadhā varhiṣi viśvamūrte ! || 2 ||
abhiṣṭuta iti | svatulyamaśviratulyam | barhiṣi agnau tirodhāḥ || 2 ||
nābhipriyāyāmatha merudevyāṃ tvamaṃśato'bhūrṛṣabhābhidhānaḥ |
alokasāmānyaguṇaprabhāvaprabhāvitāśeṣajanapramodaḥ || 3 ||
nābhīti | merudevyāṃ merusutāyām | alokasāmānyairamānuṣairguṇaiḥ prabhāvaiśca prabhāvita utpāditaḥ aśeṣajanapramodo yena || 3 ||
tvayi trilokībhṛti rājyabhāraṃ nidhāya nābhiḥ saha merudevyā |
tapovanaṃ prāpya bhavanniṣevī gataḥ kilānandapadaṃ padaṃ te || 4 ||
tvayīti | ānandapadaṃ niratiśayasukhasthānaṃ te padaṃ vaikuṇṭham || 4 ||
indrastvadutkarṣakṛtādamarṣād vavarṣa nāsminmajanābhavarṣe |
yadā tadā tvaṃ nijayogaśaktyā svavarṣamenad vyadadhāḥ suvarṣam || 5 ||
jitendradattāṃ kamanīṃ jayantīmathodvahanātmaratāśayo'pi |
ajījanat tatra śataṃ tanūjān yeṣāṃ kṣitīśo bharato'grajanmā || 6 ||
jiteti | yogaśaktyā jitenendreṇa dattām | ātmarataḥ ātmanaḥ śrīharerupāsana evotsuka āśayo'ntaḥkaraṇaṃ yasya || 6 ||
navābhavan yogivarā navānye tvapālayan bhāratavarṣakhaṇḍān |
saikā tvaśītistava śeṣaputrāstapobalād bhūsurabhūyamīyuḥ || 7 ||
naveti | bhāratavarṣakhaṇḍān kuśāvartelāvartabrahmāvartamalayaketubhadrasenavidarbhakīkaṭādīn | saikā aśītirekasahitā aśītiḥ | bhūsurabhūyaṃ brāhmaṇatvam || 7 ||
uktvā sutebhyo'tha sunīndramadhye viraktibhaktyanvitamuktimārgam |
svayaṃ gataḥ pāramahaṃsyavṛttimadhā jaḍonmattapiśācacaryām || 8 ||
uktveti | svayamṛṣabhadevaḥ | sutebhyo bharatādibhyaḥ | paramahaṃsānāṃ vṛttiṃ gato jaḍonmattapiśācavaccaraṇamadhāḥ akaroḥ || 8 ||
parātmabhūto'pi paropadeśaṃ kurvan bhavān sarvanirasyamānaḥ |
vikārahīno vicacāra kṛtsnāṃ mahīmahīnātmarasābhilīnaḥ || 9 ||
pareti | bhavān parātmabhūto brahmabhūtaḥ, tathāpi parebhya upadeśaṃ kurvan brahmopadeśaṃ vā | svasya pāramahaṃsyavṛttyaiva paropadeśo na mukhata ityāha -- sarveti | sarvanirasyamāno'vadhūtaḥ| vikārahīnaḥ śarīrābhimānanivṛtteḥ kartṛtvabhoktṛtvasukhaduḥkhā dirahitaḥ | tatra hetuḥ ahīnātmarase paramānandānubhave abhilīnaḥ tadaikyaṃ prāpto jīvanmukta iti yāvat || 9||
śayuvataṃ gomṛgakākacaryā ciraṃ carantrāpya paraṃ svarūpam |
davāhatāṅgaḥ kuṭakācale tvaṃ tāpān mamāpākuru vātanātha ! || 10 ||
śayviti | śayuvratamājagaraṃ vrataṃ gopṛtkagakākaryāṃ ca ciraṃ caran śayuvadanudyamaḥ san gomṛgakākavacchuddhyaśuddhividhiniṣedhādernivṛttaḥ, kevalaṃ dehasvabhāvāt sañcarannityarthaḥ | paraṃ svarūpamāpya kuṭakācale dravāhatāṅgo davāgnidagdhaśarīraḥ papātetyarthaḥ || 10 ||
iti ṛṣabhayogicaritavarṇanaṃ viṃśaṃdaśakam |
atha lokarakṣāyai tattadbhūkhaṇḍeṣu tattadrūpeṇa sannidadhānaṃ bhagavantaṃ stauti -- 
madhyodbhave bhuva ilāvṛtanāmni varpe
gaurīpradhānavanitājanamātrabhāji |
śarveṇa mantranutibhiḥ samupāsyamānaṃ
saṅkarṣaṇātmakamadhīśvara ! saṃśraye tvām || 1 ||
madhyodbhava iti | bhuvo madhyaṃ jambūdvīpaḥ, tanmadhyabhave varṣe khaṇḍe | gaurīśāpena purupāgamyatayātra śarva eka eva puruṣaḥ | ato gaurīpradhānaṃ vanitājanamātrameva bhajatīti tathā | śarveṇa mantrairnutibhiśca || 1 ||
bhadrāśvanāmaka ilāvṛtapūrvavarṣe
bhadraśravobhirṛṣibhiḥ pariṇūyamānam |
kalpāntagūḍhanigamoddharaṇapravīṇaṃ
dhyāyāmi deva! hṛyaśīrṣatanuṃ bhavantam || 2 ||
vyāyāmi dakṣiṇagate harivarṣavarṣe
prahlādamukhyapurupaiḥ paripevyamāṇam |
uttuṅgaśāntadhavalākṛtimekaśuddhajñānapradaṃ narahariṃ bhagavan ! bhavantam || 3 ||
dhyāyāmīti | ekaṃ śuddhaṃ nirupādhikaṃ jñānaṃ dadātīti tathā || 3 ||
varṣe pratīci lalitātmani ketumāle
līlāviśeṣalalitasmitaśobhanāṅgam |
lakṣmyā prajāpatisutaiśca niṣevyamāṇaṃ
tasyāḥ priyāya dhṛtakāmatanuṃ bhaje tvām || 4 ||
varṣa iti | vṛtakāmatanum aṅgīkṛtakāmadevasvarūpam || 4 ||
ramye'pyudīci khalu ramyakanāmni varṣe
tadvarṣanāthamanuvaryasaparyamāṇam |
'sapara pūjāyāmi'ti kaṇḍvādaḥ karmaṇi śānaci saparyamāṇaśabdaḥ |
bhaktaikavatsalamamatsarahṛtsu bhāntaṃ
matsyākṛtiṃ bhuvananātha ! bhaje bhavantam || 5 ||
ramya iti | ramyakavarṣanāthena vaivasvatena manuvaryeṇa saparyamāṇamārādhyamānam || 5 ||
varṣe hiraṇmayasamāhṛyamauttarāha -- 
māsīnamadridhṛtikarmaṭhakāmaṭhāṅgam |
saṃsevate pitṛgaṇapravaro'ryamāyaṃ
taṃ tvāṃ bhajāmi bhagavan ! paracinmayātman ! || 6||
varṣamiti | auttarāddam uttaradigbhavam | adridhṛtikarmaṭham amṛtamathane mandarādridhāraṇauyikaṃ kāmaṭhaṃ kaurmamaṅgaṃ yasya | paracinmayātman ! viśuddhajñānasvarūpa ! || 6 ||
kiñcottareṣu kuruṣu priyayā dharaṇyā
saṃsevito mahitamantranutimabhedaiḥ |
daṃṣṭrāgravṛghṛṣṭadhanapṛṣṭhagariṣṭhavarṣmā
tvaṃ pāhi vijñanutayajñavarāhamūrte ! || 7 ||
kiñceti | daṃṣṭrāgreṇa ghṛṣṭaṃ ghanapṛṣṭhaṃ yena tādṛśaṃ gariṣṭhamatyunnataṃ ca varṣma svarūpaṃ yasya | vijñairjñānimirnutā stutā yajñavarāharūpā mūrtiryasya || 7 ||
yāmyāṃ diśaṃ bhajati kimpuruṣākhyavarṣe
saṃsevito hanumatā dṛḍhabhaktibhājā |
sītābhirāmaparamādbhutarūpaśālī
rāmātmakaḥ parilasan paripāhi viṣṇo ! || 8 ||
yāmyāmiti | yāmyāṃ diśaṃ bhajati ilāvṛtasamanantara deśavartini || 8 ||
śrīnāradena saha bhāratakhaṇḍa mukhyaistvaṃ sāṅkhyayoganutibhiḥ samupāsyamānaḥ |
ākalpakālamiha sādhujanābhirakṣī
nārāyaṇo narasakhaḥ paripāhi bhūman ! || 9 ||
śrīti | sāṅkhayaṃ prakṛtipuruṣavivekajñānaṃ, yogo'ṣṭāṅgaḥ, tābhyāṃ nutibhiśca || 9 ||
imaṃ jambūdvīpaṃ paritaḥ kramāt plakṣaśālmalikuśa krauñcaśākapuṣkaranāmānaḥ ṣaḍ dvīpāḥ | tatra kramādarkenduvahnayambuvāyubrahmarūpeṇa tatratyā bhagavantaṃ bhajantītyāha -- 
plākṣe'rkarūpamayi śālmala indurūpaṃ
dvīpe bhajanti kuśanāmani vahnirūpam |
krauñce'mburūpamatha vāyumayaṃ ca śāke
tvāṃ brahmarūpamayi puṣkaranāmni lokāḥ || 10 ||
atha jyotiranīkātmakasya bhagavata upāsanāprakāramāha -- 
sarvairdhruvādibhiruḍuprakarairgrahaikṣca
pucchādikeṣvavayaveṣvabhikalpyamānaiḥ |
tvaṃ śiṃśumāravapuṣā mahatāmupāsyaḥ
sandhyāsu rundhi narakaṃ mama sindhuśāyin ! || 11 ||
sarvairiti | uḍḍuprakarairakṣvinyādibhiḥ grahaiḥ sūryādibhiryathākramaṃ pucchādyavaya veṣūpāsakairabhikalpyamānairupalakṣitastvaṃ śiṃśumāravapuṣā avākśirasaḥ kuṇḍalībhūtaśarīrasya śiṃśumārasya vapuriva vapuryasya, tadrūpeṇa mahatāṃ mahadbhiḥ sandhyāsu trikālamupāsyaḥ || 11 ||
atha prapañcasyoddhārakaṃ śeṣamūrti stauti -- 
pātālamūlabhuvi śeṣatanuṃ bhavanta lolaikakuṇḍalavirājisahasraśīrṣam |
nīlāmbaraṃ dhṛtahalaṃ bhujagāṅganābhirjuṣṭaṃ bhaje hara gadān gurugehanātha ! || 12 || 22 ||
iti jambūdvīpādiṣu bhagavadupāsanāprakāravarṇanam ekaviṃśaṃ daśakaṃ sadvikam |
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyākhyāyāṃ
pañcamaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
kṛte'pi pāpe bhaktānāṃ yātanā naiva nārakī |
iti ṣaṣṭagato viṣṇoḥ stūyate'nugraho'dhunā ||
athājāmilopākhyānamāramate ajāmiletyekādaśabhiḥ -- 
ajāmilo nāma mahīsuraḥ purā caran vibho ! dharmapathān gṛhāśramī |
gurorgirā kānanametya dṛṣṭavān sudhṛṣṭaśīlāṃ kulaṭāṃ madākulām || 1 ||
ajāmila iti | purājāmilo nāma ajāmimakulīnāṃ striyaṃ lātyādatta ityajāmila iti prasiddhaḥ kaścid mahīsuro gṛddāśramī svāśramocitān dharmapathān pravṛttinivṛttinārgān caran anutiṣṭhan gurorgirā piturnideśana phalapuppasamitkuśānāhatu kānanameya subhṛṣṭamatiśayena dhṛṣṭaṃ mithyāvilāsādibhiḥ paramatibañcanasamarthaṃ śīlaṃ yasyāḥ, madākulāṃ madhumadakhelagamanāṃ kāñcit kulaṭāṃ puṃścalīṃ dṛṣṭavān ||1||
svataḥ praśānto'pi tadāhṛtāśayaḥ svadharmasutsṛjya tayā samāraman |
adharmakārī daśamī bhavan punardadhau bhavannāmayute sute ratim || 2 ||
svata iti | tataśca svataḥ praśānto'pyasau tayāhṛta āśayo yasya sa tathā svadharmaṃ sahadharmacāriṇīṃ cotsṛjya tathā saha samāraman tasyāḥ kuḍumbabharaṇāyādharmakārī babhūva | punaśca daśamī bhavan jaraṭhaḥ san bhavannāmayute nārāyaṇāhvaye sute bālake daśamaputre ratimatisnehaṃ dadhau || 2 ||
sa mṛtyukāle yamarājakiṅkarān bhayaṅkarāṃstrīnabhilakṣayan bhiyā |
purā manāk tvatsmṛtivāsanāvalājjuhāva nārāyaṇanāmakaṃ sutam || 3 ||
sa iti | mṛtyukāle maraṇasamaye manāgū īṣat svasmṛterbhagavadupāsanāyā yā vāsanā saṃskārastadvalād nārāyaṇanāmakaṃ sutaṃ juhāva nārāyaṇa ! ityājuhāva || 3 ||
durāśayasyāpi tadātvanirgatatvadīyanāmākṣaramātravaibhavāt |
puro'bhipeturbhavadīya pārṣadācaturbhujāḥ pītapaṭā manoharāḥ || 4 ||
durāśayasyeti | kuḍumbāsaktacetaso'pyasya tadātve maraṇadaśāyāṃ nirgatasya
tvadīyanāmākṣaramātrasyetyanena bhaktiśraddhāvṛttivarṇavyaktaṃtādyabhāvo vyajyate, tathāpi nārāyaṇetyakṣaracatuṣṭayasya prabhāvād bhavadīyapārṣadā viṣṇudūtā asya puro'bhipetuḥ prādurbhūtāḥ || 4 ||
amuṃ ca sampāśya vikarṣato bhaṭān vimuñcatetyārurudhurbalāḍhamī |
nivāritāste ca bhavajjanaistadā tadīyapāpaṃ nikhilaṃ nyavedayan || 5 ||
amumiti | amumajāmilaṃ sampāśya pāśena gale baddhvā vikarṣato bhaṭān yamabhaṭān amī viṣṇudūtā balād vimuñcatetya nena vacananenaivārurudhuḥ | te yamabhaṭāstadīyamajāmilasyedaṃ pāpaṃ nikhilaṃ nyavedayan || 5 ||
bhavantu pāpāni kathaṃ tu niṣkṛte kṛte'pi bho daṇḍanamasti paṇḍitāḥ ! |
na niṣkṛtiḥ kiṃ viditā bhavādṛśāmiti prabho ! tvatpuruṣā vabhāṣire ||6||
bhavantviti | anantaraṃ ca tvatpuruṣā viṣṇudūtā yamabhaṭānidamuttaraṃ babhāṣire bhoḥ paṇḍitāḥ ! satyaṃ bhavadbhiruktāni pāpānyasya bhavantu, kintu niṣkṛte prāyaścitte kṛte'pi kathaṃ daṇḍanamasti, anena ca harināmagrahaṇarūpaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kṛtameva, bhavādṛśāṃ yupmadvidhairdharmarājakiṅkarairniṣkṛtiḥ kiṃ na viditeti || 6 ||
nanu mahatāmapi pāpānāṃ nāmagrahaṇamātraṃ prāyaścittamityayuktaṃ, śrautasmārtādiprāyaścittāna|matikleśamātrarūpāṇāṃ vaiyarthyaprasaṅgādityāśaṅkayāha -- 
śrutismṛtibhyāṃ vihitā vratādayaḥ punanti pāpaṃ na lunanti vāsanām |
anantasevā tu nikṛntati dvayīmiti prabho ! tvatpuruṣā babhāṣire || 7 ||
śrutīti | vratāni cāndrāyaṇādīni, ādiśabdena tīrthasnānādi | etā niṣkṛtayaḥ pāpaṃ punanti śodhayanti kevalaṃ, na vāsanāṃ sajātīyakarmotpādakaṃ saṃskāramapi lunanti | tataśca punarapi pāpaṃ kurvanti | evaṃ cāndrāyaṇādīnāṃ pāpātyantikanivartakatvābhāvānna mukhyaprāyaścittatvam | anantasya hareḥ sevā smaraṇakīrtanādi tu dvayīṃ pāpaṃ tadvāsanāṃ ca nikṛntati unmūlayati || 7 ||
ayaṃ ca maraṇabhayākulaḥ san harināmoccāritavān, ata itaḥ paraṃ na bhavaddaṇḍanamarhatītyāha -- 
anena bho ! janmasahasrakoṭibhiḥ kṛteṣu pāpeṣvapi niṣkṛtiḥ kṛtā |
yadagrahīnnāma bhayākulo hareriti prabho! tvatpuruṣā vabhāṣire || 8 ||
nanu nāsau mriyamāṇo harināmoccāritavān, kintu putraṃ tannāmānamājuhāvaiva | ato na pāpakṣaya ityāśaṅkayāha -- 
nṛṇāmabuddhayāpi mukundakīrtanaṃ dahatyaghaughān mahimāsya tādṛśaḥ |
yathāgniredhāṃsi yathauṣadhaṃ gadāniti prabho! tvatpuruṣā vabhāṣire || 9 ||
nṛṇāmiti | idaṃ bhagavannāma kīrtitaṃ sadaśeṣāghaughaharaṃ ceti dhiyā nāmoñcāraṇīyam | ato'nyathābuddhayāpi mukundakīrtanamaghaughān dahati, dahanakaṇastūlapiṇḍasañcayamivonmūlayati | kathamasya tādṛśaṃ sāmarthyamiti nānuyojanīyam | nāmno mahimnaḥ śrūyamāṇatvād dṛṣṭatvācetyāha -- mahimeti | asya nāmnastādṛśo'paricchedyo mahimā vidyate | teṣāṃ sandehahānāya sadṛṣṭāntamāha -- yatheti | bālādinā abuddhyā nikṣipto'gniḥ abuddhayopayuktamauṣadhaṃ ca yathā || 9 ||
itīritairyāmyabhaṭairapāsṛte bhavadbhaṭānāṃ ca gaṇe tirohite |
bhavatsmṛti kañcana kālamācaran bhavatpadaṃ prāpi bhavadbhaṭairasau || 10 ||
itīti | itīritaiḥ pratibodhitairyāmyabhaṭairapāsṛte'pasaraṇe kṛte sati bhavadbhaṭānāṃ gaṇe samūhe tirohite antarhite ca sati asāvajāmilo gaṅgādvāre kasmiṃścid devasadane sthitvā kañcana kālaṃ bhavatsmṛtimācaran samādhijaye sati prādurbhūtaistaireva bhavatpārṣadairlabdhasārūpyo vimānena bhavatpadaṃ vaikuṇṭhaṃ prāpi anīyata || 10 ||
svakiṅkarāvedanaśaṅkito yamastvadaṅghribhakteṣu na gamyatāmiti |
svakīyabhṛtyāna śiśikṣaccakaiḥsa deva! vātālayanātha! pāhi mām || 11 ||
sveti | yamakiṅkarāstu svāmine sarve nivedayāmāsuḥ | yamastu svakiṅkarāṇāmāvedanena śaṅkitaḥ svabhṛtyāparādhaḥ svasminnapi syāditi bhītaḥ san tvadaṅghribhakteṣu bhavadbhirna gamyatāṃ bhagavadbhaktāḥ pariharaṇīyā yuṣmābhirityuccakairatinirbandhena svabhṛtyānaśiśikṣat śikṣitavān | tādṛśabhaktavātsalyayuktastvaṃ pāhi || 11 ||
ityajāmilopākhyānaṃ dvāviṃśaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
evamajāmiladṛṣṭāntena nṛṇāmanugrahamuktvā svargiṇāṃ bhagavadanugrahaṃ darśayituṃ dakṣakathāmārabhate -- 
prācetasastu bhagavannaparo'pi dakṣastvatsevanaṃ vyadhita sargavivṛddhikāmaḥ |
āvirbabhūvitha tadā lasadaṣṭavāhustasmai varaṃ dadiya tāṃ ca vadhūmasiknīm || 1 ||
prācetasa iti | prācīnabarhiṣaḥ putrā daśa pracetasaḥ, teṣāṃ putraḥ prācetaso dakṣaḥ | aparo brahmaputrād dakṣādanyaḥ | sa manasaivemāḥ prajāḥ sṛṣṭvā punastadvivṛddhikāmastvatsevanaṃ vyadhita kṛtavān | tvaṃ ca varaṃ tvattaḥ prabhṛti vyavāyadharmeṇa prajāsargo bhaviṣyatītyevaṃ rūpaṃ, tāṃ pañcajanaduhitaram asiknīṃ vadhūṃ bhāthīṃ ca daditha dattavān || 1 ||
tasyātmajāstvayutamīśa ! punaḥ sahasraṃ
śrīnāradasya vacasā tava mārgamāpuḥ
naikatravāsamṛṣaye mumuce sa śāpaṃ
bhaktottamastvṛṣiranugrahameva mene || 2 ||
tasyeti | tasya dakṣasya haryaśvasaṃjñā ayutamātmajā āsan | punaḥ śabalāśvasaṃjñāḥ sahasraṃ ca sutā āsan | te sarve'pi piturādeśāt sargavṛddhaye tapaḥ kurvantaḥ śrīnāradasya vacasā nivṛttimārgopadeśarūpeṇa tava mārga mokṣamāpuḥ prāptavantaḥ | sa dakṣaḥ kruddho munaye śrīnāradāya naikatravāsaṃ sadāsañcāritvaṃ śāpaṃ mumuce | ṛṣistu bhaktottamatvena tamanugrahameva mene || 2 ||
ṣaṣṭyā tato duhitṛbhiḥ sṛjataḥ kulaughān
dauhitrasunuratha tasya sa viśvarūpaḥ |
tvatstotravamiṃtamajāpayadindramājī
deva! tvadīyamahimā khalu sarvajaitraḥ || 3 ||
ṣaṣṭyeti | tataḥ ṣaṣṭyā duhitṛbhiraditidityādyābhiḥ kulaughān sthiracarabhedasamūhān sṛjato'sya dakṣasya dauhitro diteḥ putrastvaṣṭa | tatsūnustvāṣṭro viśvarūpaḥ | saḥ tvatstotreṇa nārāyaṇātmakena varmaṇā varmitaṃ kṛtarakṣam indram ājau devāsurayuddhe ajāpayad jayamakarot || 3 ||
atha vṛtravadhaprastāvāya prāgjanmakathāmāha -- 
prāk śūrasenaviṣaye kila citraketuḥ
putrāgrahī nṛpatiraṅgirasaḥ prabhāvāt |
labdhvaikaputramatha tatra hate sapatnīsaṅgairamuhyadavaśastava māyayāsau || 4 ||
prāgiti | aṅgirasaḥ prabhāvāt tatkṛtayajñocchiṣṭaprāśanena jyeṣṭhabhāryāyāmekaṃ putraṃ labdhvā tatra tasmin putre sapatnīsaṅghairhate || 4 ||
taṃ nāradastu samamaṅgirasā dayāluḥ
samprāpya tāvadupadartha sutasya jīvam |
kasyāsmi putra iti tasya girā vimohaṃ
tyaktvā tvadarcanavidhau nṛpatiṃ nyayuṅkta || 5 ||
tamiti | aṅgirasā samaṃ saha sutasya mṛtasya jīvaṃ yogabalenopadarśya darśayitvā ahaṃ kasya putro'smīti tasya girā nṛpatervimohaṃ tyaktvā || 5 ||
stotraṃ ca mantramapi nāradato'tha labdhvā
toṣāya śeṣavapuṣo nanu te tapasyan |
jayantaṃ prayojitavān |
vidyādharādhipatitāṃ sa hi saptarātre
labdhvāpyakuṇṭhamatirancabhajad bhavantam || 6 ||
stotramiti | śeṣavapuṣo'nantamūrteḥ te tava harestoṣāya prasādāya tapasyan sa citraketuḥ saptarātre saptarātrānte vidyādharādhipatitāṃ labdhvā bhavantaṃ śeṣamūrtim || 6 ||
tvaṃ cānantarūpeṇa prādurbhūyātmatattvamupadiśya tirohitavānityāha -- 
tasmai mṛṇāladhavalena sahasraśīrṇā
rūpeṇa baddhanutisiddhagaṇāvṛtena |
prādurbhavannacirato nutibhiḥ prasanno
dattvātmatattvamanugṛhya tirodadhātha || 7 ||
tvadbhaktamauliratha so'pi ca lakṣalakṣaṃ
varṣāṇi harṣulamanā bhuvaneṣu kāmam |
saṅgāpayan guṇagaṇaṃ tava sundarībhiḥ
saṅgātirekarahito lalitaṃ cacāra || 8 ||
tvāditi | (te?) tava guṇagaṇaṃ haricaritaṃ vidyādharasundarībhiḥ saṅgāpayan ||
atyantasaṅgavilayāya bhavatpraṇunno
nūnaṃ sa rūpyagirimāpya mahatsamāje |
niśśaṅkamaṅkakṛtavallabhamaṅgajāriṃ
taṃ śaṅkaraṃ parihasannumayābhiśepe || 9 ||
atyanteti | sa ca śrīśaṅkaraṃ parihasan umayābhiśepe āsurīṃ yoniṃ yāhīti śapto'bhūt | svataḥ saṅgātirekarahitasyātyantasaṅgavilayāya bhavateśvareṇa praṇunnaḥ prerito'sau nūnamiti tarkayāmi, anyathā tasya devahelanāyogāt || 9 ||
nissambhramastvayamayācitaśāpamokṣo
vṛtrāsuratvamupagamya surendrayodhī |
bhaktyātmatattvakathanaiḥ samare vicitraṃ
śatrorapi bhramamapāsya gataḥ padaṃ te || 10 ||
niḥsambhrama iti | tataścāsau niḥsambhramaḥ śāpabhayarahitaḥ samara ātmatattvakathanaiḥ śatrorindrasyāpi bhramamajñānamapāsya tadvatreṇa hataḥ te padaṃ vaikuṇṭhaṃ gataḥ | citramāścaryametat | atra guruhelanenāsurebhyaḥ parājitairdevaiḥ paurohityāya vṛto viśvarūpaḥ | sa tu ditisutāpatyatayā mātṛpakṣapātena rahasi daityebhyo havirbhāgaṃ dattavān | indrantu tadālaśyātya śirasyavṛścat | tataśca tvaṣṭuhataputrasyābhicārakuṇḍādutthito vṛtraḥ samarabhuvi indreṇa hata iti kathānusandheyā || 10 ||
atha diterghorasaṅkalpādindrasya marudvadhācca bhagavadanugrahācchreyaḥ prāptimāha -- 
tvatsevanena ditirindravadhodyatāpi
tān pratyutendrasuhṛdo maruto'bhilebhe |
duṣṭāśaye'pi śubhadaiva bhavanniṣevā
tattādṛśastvamava māṃ pavanālayeśa ! || 11 ||
tvaditi | ditirindrabadhodyatā kāśyapaṃ prasādya indrahaṇaṃ putraṃ vṛtavatī | tasmād gabheṃ cādhatta | tathāpi tvatsevanena kāśyapopadiṣṭapuṃsavanavratānuṣṭhānenendrahaṇaṃ putraṃ lebhe | apitu pratyuta viparītaṃ jātam | indreṇākāle svapantyā diterudaraṃ praviśya ye sapta saptadhā vṛṇāstānindrasya suhṛdo maruta ekonapañcāśatputrānabhilebhe| evaṃ ca duṣṭāśaye'pi bhavanniṣevā śubhadaiva, yathā diterindrasya ca | tattādṛśo'nanyasadṛśaḥ || 11 || 22 ||
iti citraketūpākhyānaṃ marudutpattivarṇanaṃ ca trayoviṃśaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyākhyāyāṃ
ṣaṣṭhaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
prākkarmavāsanābhedā ūtayaḥ saptamoditāḥ |
prahlādapitṛdṛṣṭāntairlakṣyante'tha harerguṇāḥ ||
atha prahlādacaritavarṇanāya tadutpatteḥ pūrvaṃ tatpiturhiraṇyakaśiporbhagavadvidveṣādikaṃ prastauti -- 
hiraṇyākṣe potripravaravapuṣā deva ! bhavatā
hate śokakrodhaglapitadhṛtiretasya sahajaḥ |
hiraṇyaprārambhaḥ kaśipuramarārātisadasi
pratijñāmātene taba kila vadhārtha muraripo ! || 1 ||
vidhātāraṃ ghoraṃ sa khalu tapasitvā nacirataḥ
puraḥ sākṣātkurvan suranaramṛgādyaira nidhanam |
varaṃ labdhvā hapto jagadiha bhavannāyakamidaṃ
parikṣundannindrādaharata divaṃ tvāmagaṇayan || 2 ||
vidhātāramiti | parikṣundan cūrṇāṃkurvan || 2 ||
nihantuṃ tvāṃ bhūyastava padmavātasya ca riporvahirdṛṣṭerantadedhitha hṛdaye sūkṣmavapuṣā |
nadannuccaistatrāpyakhilabhuvanānte ca mṛgayan
bhiyā yātaṃ matvā sa khalu jitakāśī nivavṛte || 3 ||
nihantumiti | tava padaṃ vaikuṇṭhamavāptasya prāptasya riporhiraṇyakaśiporbahiīṣṭermāṃsacakṣuṣaḥ sakāśāt tvaṃ sūkṣmavapuṣā jñānadṛṣṭirahitasyāsya hṛdaye antardadhitha antarhitavān | tatastvāṃ bhītaṃ matvoccairnadan siṃhanādaṃ kurvan tatra vaikuṇṭhaloke akhilabhuvanānte caturdaśasu lokeṣu jitakāśī jitaṃmanyo nivavṛte nivṛtto'bhūt || tato'sya prahlādaḥ samajani suto garbhavasatau munervīṇāpāṇeradhigatabhavadbhaktimahimā |
sa vai jātyā daityaḥ śiśurapi sametya tvayi ratiṃ
gatastvadbhaktānāṃ varada! paramodāharaṇatām || 4 ||
tata iti | hiraṇyakaśipau tapase mandarādriṃ gatavati devā asurān nirjiyāntarvanlī prahlādamātaraṃ kayādhuṃ netumārabdhā nāradavacanād visṛjya pratinivṛttāḥ | devarṣistu tasyai mahādamuddiśya bhaktijñānamārgānupadideśa | ato'sau śiśurapi tvayīśvare ratiṃ premalakṣaṇāṃ bhaktim || 4 ||
surārīṇāṃ hāsyaṃ tava caraṇadāsyaṃ nijasute
sa dṛṣṭvā duṣṭātmā gurubhiraśiśikṣacciramamum |
guruproktaṃ cāsāvidamidamabhadrāya dṛḍhāmityapākurvan sarve tava caraṇabhaktyaiva vavṛdhe || 5 ||
surārīṇāmiti | gurubhiḥ śukraputrābhyāṃ śaṇḍāmarkābhyām | guruproktaṃ trivargasyopāyaṃ vārtādaṇḍanītyādi bhedadṛṣṭyāśritatvādidamidamabhadrāya śarīrabandhena saṃsārāya bhavati dṛḍhaṃ niścitamiti tvadbhakteranyat sarvamapākurvan tava caraṇabhaktyā kevalayā vavṛdhe | asya śarīravṛddhyanusāreṇa bhaktirapi vavṛdha ityarthaḥ || 5 ||
adhīteṣu śreṣṭhaṃ kimiti paripṛṣṭe'tha tanaye
bhavadbhakti varyāmabhigadati paryākuladhṛtiḥ |
gurubhyo roṣitvā sahajamatirasyetyabhividan
vadhopāyānasmin vyatanuta bhavatpādaśaraṇe || 6 ||
adhīteṣviti | atha kadācit pitrā tvayādhīteṣu madhye kiṃ śreṣṭhamiti tanaye prahlāde pṛṣṭhe sati bhavadbhakti varyā śreṣṭhāmabhigadati ca sati paryākulā calitā dhṛtiryasmāt sa tathā asya matiḥ sahajeti gurumukhādabhividan asmin prahlāde vadhopāyān vyatanuta || 6 ||
vaghopāyānāha -- 
sa śulairāviddhaḥ subahu mathito diggajagaṇairmahāsarpairdaṣṭo'pyanaśanagarāhāravidhutaḥ |
girīndrāvakṣipto'pyahaha paramātmannayi vibho !
tvayi nyastātmatvāt kimapi na nipīḍāmabhajata || 7 ||
sa iti | paramātmāne tvāya nyasta ātmā mano yasya | athavātmā jīvaḥ, brahmabhūtatvādityarthaḥ || 7 ||
tataḥ śaṅkāviṣṭaḥ sa punaratiduṣṭo'sya janako
gurūktyā tadgehe kila varuṇapāśaistamaruṇat |
gurodhāsānnidhye sa punaranugān daityatanayān
bhavadbhaktestattvaṃ paramamapi vijñānamaśiṣat || 8 ||
tata iti | tadgehe gurugṛhe sa grahlādo'nugān sakhīn bhavadbhaktestattvaṃ svarūpaṃ kāraṇaṃ phalaṃ ca paramaṃ vijñānaṃ brahmajñānamapyaśiṣad upadideśa || 8 ||
pitā śṛṇvan vālaprakaramakhilaṃ tvatstutiparaṃ
ruṣāndhaḥ prāhainaṃ kulahataka ! kaste balamiti |
valaṃ me vaikuṇṭhastava ca jagatāṃ cāpi sa valaṃ
sa eva trailokyaṃ sakalamiti dhīro'yamagaḍhīt || 9||
piteti | kulahataka ! anurādhama ! mamājñālaṅghane kaste balamiti pitā prāha papraccha | vaikuṇṭho viṣṇurme balaṃ, na kevalaṃ mama, tavāpi tribhuvanavijaye sa viṣṇureva balaṃ, jagatāṃ jagadvāsināmapi | kiñca sakalaṃ trailokyaṃ sa viṣṇureva, na tataḥ pṛthak kiñcidapyastītyayaṃ prahlādo'gadīd vyaktamuktavān | dhīro nirbhayo vidvān vā || 9 ||
tadanantaraṃ hiraṇyakaśipoḥ pravṛttimāha -- 
are ! kkāsau kkāsau sakalajagadātmā haririti
prabhinte sma stambhaṃ calitakaravālo ditisutaḥ |
ataḥ paścād viṣṇo! na hi vaditumīśo'smi sahasā
kṛpātman ! viśvātman ! pavanapuravāsin! mṛḍaya mām || 10 ||
are iti | sakalaṃ jagad ātmā svarūpaṃ yasyāsau hariḥ kveti durghaṭatvena
kopāveśena cāsakṛduccaranneva stambhaṃ prabhinte sma tāḍitavān | ataḥ paścāt stambhabhañjanānantaraṃ tatra kiṃ jātamiti na hi sahasā vaktumīśo'smi | kṛpāyukta ātmā yasya, he kṛpātman! bhaktavatsala ! viśvātman ! spaṣṭaviśvātmatattva ! he viṣṇo ! jagadvyāpanaśīla! he pavanapuravāsin ! māṃ mṛḍaya sukhaya || 10 ||
iti prahlādacarite nṛsiṃhāvirbhāvaprasaṅgavarṇanaṃ caturviṃśaṃ daśakam |
stambhe ghaṭṭayato hiraṇyakaśipoḥ karṇī samācūrṇayannāghūrṇajjagadaṇḍakuṇḍakuharo ghorastavābhūd ravaḥ |
śrutvā yaṃ kila daityarājahṛdaye pūrvaṃ kadāpyaśrutaṃ
kampaḥ kakṣcana sampapāta calito'pyambhojabhūrviṣṭapāt || 1 ||
stambha iti | evaṃ stambhabhañjanānantaraṃ prādurbhavatastava ghoro ravaḥ siṃhanādo'bhūt | ghoratvamevāha -- ghaṭṭayato nighnataḥ | ādhūrṇajjagadaṇḍakuṇḍakuharaḥ sambhrāntabrahmāṇḍakaṭāhāntargatacarācaraḥ | pūrve kadāpyaśrutaṃ yaṃ śrutvā daityasya hiraṇyakaśiporhṛdaye kaścanānirdeśyarūpaḥ kampaḥ sampapāta | apica ambhojabhūrapi viṣṭapāt satyalokāccalitaḥ kimidamakāṇḍe kalpāpāya iti sambhrānto'bhūt || 1 ||
daitye dikṣu visṛṣṭacakṣuṣi mahāsaṃrambhiṇi stambhataḥ
sambhūtaṃ na mṛgātmakaṃ na manujākāraṃ vapuste vibho ! |
kiṃ kiṃ bhīṣaṇametadadbhutamiti vyudbhrāntacitte'sure
visphūrjaddhavalograromavikasadvarṣmā samājṛmbhathāḥ || 2 ||
daitya iti | visṛṣṭacakṣuṣi · sarvataḥ sañcāritanayane sati na mṛgātmakaṃ na manujākāraṃ narasiṃhākāraṃ vapuḥ tad dṛṣṭvā kiṃ kimetad bhīṣaṇamadbhutaṃ ceti vyudbhrāntacitte'sure hiraṇyakaśipau visphūrjadbhirvikasvarairdhavalomaromabhiḥ vikasat prakāśamānaṃ ca varṣma svarūpaṃ yasya sa tvaṃ samājṛmbhathāḥ sphuṭanirgatonnatarūpo'bhūḥ || 2 ||
tadrūpameva varṇayati -- 
taptasvarṇasavarṇaghūrṇadatirūkṣākṣaṃ saṭākesaraprotkampapranikumvitāmbaramaho jīyāt tavedaṃ vapuḥ |
vyāttavyāptamahādarīsakhamukhaṃ khaḍgogravalganmahājihānirgamadṛśyamānasumahādaṃṣṭrāyugoḍḍāmaram || 3 ||
tapteti | taptasvarṇasavarṇe vilīnāvasthitapuraṭasadṛśe ghūrṇatī bhramite atirūkṣe akṣiṇī yasmin, saṭākesaraprotkampapranikumvitāmbaraṃ pracalitaskandharomāñcalācchāditagaganatalaṃ, vyāttaṃ vivṛtaṃ vyāptamahādarīsakhaṃ vistṛtamahāguhāsadṛśaṃ mukhaṃ yasmin, khaṅgavadugrāyā valgantyāścalitāyā mahājihvāyā bahirnirgame dṛśyamānena sumahatā daṃṣṭrāyugenoḍḍāmaramatibhīṣaṇaṃ tadidaṃ te vapurjīyāt sarvotkṛṣṭatayā prakāśatām || 3 ||
utsarpadvalibhaṅgabhīṣaṇahanu hrasvasthavīyastaragrīvaṃ pīvaradośśatodgatanakhakrūrāṃśudūrolvaṇam |
vyomolāṅghanāghanopamaghanapradhvānanirdhāvitaspardhāluprakaraṃ namāmi bhavatastannārasiṃhaṃ vapuḥ || 4 ||
utsarpaditi | bhavatastannārasiṃhaṃ vapurnamāmi | kīdṛśam utsarpadbhiraṭṭahāsavijṛmmaṇādiṣūparyupari gacchadbhirvalibhaṅgairbhīṣaṇau hanupradeśau yasmin, hrasvā sthavīyastarā atiśayena sthūlā grīvā yasmin, pīvarāṇāṃ doṣṇāṃ śatādugatānāṃ nakhānāṃ krūrairaṃśubhirdūrolbaṇamatiśayena bhayaṅkaraṃ, sajalajaladharadhvānavadatibhīṣaṇasiṃhanādairvidrāvitavairinikaram || 4 ||
nūnaṃ viṣṇurayaṃ nihanmyamumiti bhrāmyadgadābhīṣaṇaṃ
daityendraṃ samupādravantamadhṛthā dorbhyāṃ pṛthubhyāmamum |
vīro nirgalito'tha khaḍgaphalake gṛhṇan vicitraśramān
vyāvṛṇvan punarāpapāta bhuvanagrāsodyataṃ tvāmaho || 5 ||
nūnamiti| kapaṭaveṣabhṛd viṣṇurevāyaṃ nūnaṃ tarkayāmi, amuṃ bhrātṛhaṇaṃ nihanmīti niścitya | aghṛthāstvamagrahīḥ | karābhyāṃ nirgalito vīro vīrammanyo'thānantaraṃ vicitraśramān vyāyāmamārgeṣu dakṣiṇasavyasañcaraṇabhedeṣu śikṣāviśeṣān vyāvṛṇvan prakāśayan bhuvanagrāsodyataṃ brahmāṇḍakavalīkaraṇasamarthaṃ tvāmāpapātāsasāda || 5 ||
bhrāmyantaṃ ditijādhamaṃ punarapi modgṛhya dorbhyā javāda
dvāre'thoruyuge nipātya nakharān vyutkhāya vakṣobhuvi |
nirbhindannadhigarbhanirbharagaladraktāmbu baddhotsavaṃ
pāyaṃ pāyamudairayo bahujagatsaṃhārisiṃhāravān || 6 ||
bhrāmyantamiti| antarbahiśca hantumaśakyatvād dvāre | jale sthale cāśakyatvādūruyuge ditijādhamaṃ nipātyāyudhairhantumaśakyatvānnakharān vakṣobhuvi vyutkhāya nirbhindan vidārayan adhigarbha vakṣontarbhāge nirbharagaladvaktāmbu sirāmukhataḥ santataniḥṣyandamānaṃ rudhirāmbu | baddhotsavaṃ yathā bhavati tathā | bahūn jagatsaṃhāriṇo brahmāṇḍakaṭāhabhedakān siṃhāravānudairayaḥ prāyuṅkthāḥ || 6 ||
tyaktvā taṃ hatamāśu raktalaharīsiktonnamavarṣmaṇi
pratyutpatya samastadaityapaṭalīṃ cākhādyamāne tvayi |
bhrāmyadbhūmi vikampitāmbudhikulaṃ vyālolaśailotkaraṃ
protsarpatkhacaraṃ carācaramaho duḥsthāmavasthāṃ dadhau || 7 ||
tyaktveti | raktalaharīsiktonnamadvamaṇi rudhirasrāvāktonnatakāye tvayi hataṃ taṃ tyaktvāśu pratyutpatya tadanugasamastadaityasamūhāṃścākhādyamāne bhakṣayati ca sati carācaraṃ duḥsthāmanavasthitasvasthānaniveśāmavasthāṃ dadhau | tadevāha -- bhrāmyantī bhūmiryasmin | vikampitamambudhikulaṃ yasmin | vyālolaśailotkaraṃ kandukāyamānakulācalanikaram | protsarpatkhacaraṃ kesarotkṣiptajyotirgaṇam || 7||
tāvanmāṃsavapākarālavapuṣaṃ ghorāntramālādharaṃ
tvāṃ madhyesabhamiddharopamuṣitaṃ durvāragurvāravam |
abhyetuṃ na śaśāka ko'pi bhuvane dūre sthitā bhīravaḥ
sarve śarvaviriñcavāsavamukhāḥ pratyekamastoṣata || 8 ||
tāvaditi | tāvat tasmin kāla eva madhyesabhamamupitaṃ tvāmabhyetuṃ ko'pi na śaśāka | ko'pi brahmāpīti vā | tatra hetavaḥ māṃsavapābhyāṃ karālaṃ vapuryasya tam | tataśca sarve pratyekamastoṣata tuṣṭuvuḥ || 8 ||
bhūyo'pyakṣataroṣadhābhni bhavati brahmājñayā vālake
prahlāde padayornamatyapabhaye kāruṇyabhārākulaḥ |
śāntastvaṃ karamasya mūrdhni samadhāḥ stotrairathodgāyatastasyākāmadhiyo'pi tenitha varaṃ lokāya cānugraham || 9 ||
bhūya iti | bhūyo'pi brahmādyaiḥ saṃstuto'pyakṣataropadhāmni anyūnakopabhāji bhavati sati prahlāde tava padayornamati sati tadvātsalyena śāntastyaktaroṣastvamasya mūrdhni karaṃ samadhāḥ | akāmadhiyo'pyasya daityādhipatyaṃ lokānugraharūpaṃ varaṃ tenitha tadaudāsīnye'pi svayaṃ cakartha || 9 ||
nanu kimevaṃ krūrastrabhāvo hariḥ, na, yathā nado raudrarasamabhinayati tadvadityāha -- 
evaṃ nāṭitaraudraceṣṭita! vibho! śrītāpanīyābhidhakṣrutyantasphuṭagītasarvamahimannatyantaśuddhākṛte! |
tattāddaṅnikhilottaraṃ punaraho kastvāṃ paro laṅghayet
prahlādapriya! he marutpurapate! sarvāmayāt pāhi mām ||10||
evamiti | atra pramāṇamā -- śrītāpanīyeti | śrītāpanīyopaniṣatpratipāditaivaṃvidhasakalamahattva ! atyantaśuddhā saccida nandarūpā ākṛtiḥ svarūpaṃ yasya | tattādṛśamanupamaṃ nikhilottaraṃ sarvotkṛṣṭaṃ tvāṃ kaḥ paro laṅghayet tvatsvarūpabhūtatvatpārṣadādanyaḥ ko vā tvadapriyamācaredityarthaḥ | he prahlādapriya! bhaktavatsala! || 10 ||20||
iti prahlādacaritavarṇanaṃ pañcaviṃśaṃ daśakam |
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyākhyāyāṃ
saptamaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
manvantarākhyaḥ saddharmo manvādipratibodhitaḥ |
lakṣyate'ṣṭamanirdiṣṭo harilīlopabṛṃhitaḥ ||
athāpadi śrīharau manoniveśanaṃ dharmasārāṃśabhūtamiti gajendradṛṣṭāntena darśayipyan asya prāgjanmakathāmāha dvābhyām -- 
indraghumnaḥ pāṇḍacakhaṇḍādhirājastvadbhaktātmā candanādrau kadācit |
vatsevāyāṃ magnadhīrāluloke naivāgastyaṃ prāptamātithyakāmam || 1 ||
indreti | pāṇḍyakhaṇḍo bhāratakhaṇḍadakṣiṇabhāgaḥ || 1 ||
kumbhodbhūtaḥ sambhṛtakrodhabhāraḥ stabdhātmā tvaṃ hastibhūyaṃ bhajeti |
śaptvāthainaṃ pratyagāt so'pi lebhe hastīndratvaṃ vatsmṛtivyaktidhanyam || 2||
kumbhodbhūta iti | hastibhūyaṃ hastitvam | pratyagāt pratinivṛttaḥ | tvatsmṛteḥ prāgjanmābhyastāyā vyaktiḥ prakāśastena dhanyaṃ puruṣārthapradam || 2 ||
dugdhāmbhoghermadhyabhāji trikūṭe krīḍañchaile yūthapo'yaṃ vaśābhiḥ |
sarvān jantūnatyavartiṣṭa śaktayā tvadbhaktānāṃ kutra notkarṣalābhaḥ || 3 ||
dugdheti | tvadbhaktānāṃ kutra keṣu yoniṣu notkarṣalābhaḥ, yad gajatve'pi sarvajantūnatyavartiṣṭa atiśayitavān || 3 ||
svena sthemnā divyadehavaśaktayā so'yaṃ khedānaprajānan kadācit |
śailaprānte gharmatāntaḥ sarasyāṃ yūthaiḥ sārdhaṃ tvatpraṇunno'bhireme || 4 ||
sveneti | svenānanyasādhāraṇena sthemnā balena balādhiketarasattvasamudbhūtān khedān pīḍāḥ| gharmatāntaḥ grīṣmakālodbhūtenoṣmaṇā klāntaḥ | śailaprānte parvatanitambavartini ṛtumannāmnyudyāne sarasyāṃ tvatpraṇunno nijapadaṃ prāpayiṣyateśvareṇa preritaḥ || 4 ||
huhūstāvad devalasyāpi śāpād grāhībhūtastajjale vartamānaḥ |
jagrāhainaṃ hastinaṃ pādadeśe śāntyarthaṃ hi śrāntido'si svakānām || 5 ||
hūhūriti | tāvat tasmin kāla eva hūhūrnāma gandharvo'pi devalasyarṣeḥ śāpād grāhībhūto grāhatāṃ prāptaḥ | nanu gajendraśced bhaktaḥ kathaṃ grāhādabhibhava iti nāśaṅkanīyamityāha -- śāntyarthamiti | hi yasmācchāntyarthaṃ viṣayaviraktyarthaṃ svakānāṃ bhaktānāṃ śrāntido'si pīḍākaro bhavasi || 5 ||
tvatsevāyā vaibhavād durnirodhaṃ yudhyantaṃ taṃ vatsarāṇāṃ sahasram |
prāpte kāle tvatpadaikāgrayasiddhayai natrkākrāntaṃ hastivīraṃ vyadhāstvam || 6 ||
tvaditi | prāpte kāle prārabdhe'vasite tvatpadayoraikāgryaṃ samādhistasya sidhghai prāptaye | athavā prāgjanmābhyastasya tvatpadaikāgryasya siddhiḥ phalaṃ mokṣastaprāptaye || 6 ||
ārtivyaktaprāktanajñānabhaktiḥ śuṇḍotkṣipteḥ puṇḍarīkaiḥ samarcan |
pūrvābhyastaṃ nirviśeṣātmaniṣṭhaṃ stotraśreṣṭhaṃ so'nvagādīt parātman ! || 7 ||
ārtīti | ārtyā nakrākrāntijanyathā pīḍayā vyakte prāktane janmāntarābhyaste jñānabhaktī yasya | nirviśeṣātmaniṣṭhaṃ nirguṇabrahmaviṣayam || 7 ||
śrutvā stotraṃ nirguṇasthaṃ samastaṃ brahmeśādyairnāhamityaprayāte !
sarvātmā tvaṃ bhūrikāruṇyavegāt tārkṣyārūḍhaḥ prekṣito'bhūḥ purastāt || 8 ||
śrutveti | brahmeśādyairbrahmāhamīśo'hamityabhimānibhirnāhamanena stutaḥ, yato brahmaniṣṭhaṃ stotramasyetyaprayāte sati tvaṃ purastāt prekṣitaḥ prādurbhūto'bhūḥ | nanu viṣṇurapi viṣṇurahamityabhimānyeva, netyāha -- sarvātmeti | brahmādīnāmapyātmāntaryāmī tvam || 8 ||
atha gajendramokṣamāha -- 
hastīndraṃ taṃ hastapadmena dhṛtvā cakreṇa tvaṃ nakravaryaṃ vyadārīḥ |
gandharve'smin muktaśāpe sa hastī tvatsārūpyaṃ prāpya dedīpyate sma || 9 ||
bhagavatavānugṛhītāṃ phalaśrutimāha
etad vṛttaṃ tvāṃ ca māṃ va prage yo gāyet so'yaṃ bhūyase śreyase syāt |
ityuktvainaṃ tena sārdhaṃ gatastvaṃ dhiṣṇyaṃ viṣṇo! pāhi vātālayeśa ! ||10||
etaditi | prage prātaḥ | vṛttaṃ gajendramokṣaṇarūpaṃ haricaritam | bhūyase śreyase syād mokṣaprāptiyogyo bhavedityuktvānugṛhya | dhiṣṇyaṃ vaikuṇṭham || 10 ||
iti gajendramokṣavarṇanaṃ paḍaviśaṃ daśakam |
atha samudradṛṣṭāntena sampadi satyāṃ tāmarthiṣu svātmanā saha samarpayedityayaṃ dharmaḥ prakāśyate | sa hi surāsurebhbo'rthibhya ātmamathanena sakalasampatsamarpaṇaṃ kṛtavāniti jaladhimathanatadupayogibhagavadavatāracaritakathanāya prathamaṃ devānāṃ śāpanimittaiścaryabhraṃśamāha dvābhyāṃ -- 
durvāsāḥ suravanitāgtadivyamālyaṃ śakrāya svayamupadāya tatra bhūyaḥ |
nāgendrapratimṛdite śaśāpa śakraṃ kā kṣāntistvaditaradevatāṃśajānām || 1 ||
durvāsā iti | suravanitāyā hastāllabdhaṃ śakrāyairāvatamāruhya gacchate | upadāya dattvā | tatra divyamālye | śakraṃ niḥśrīkā (devā) bhavantviti śaśāpa | tvaditaradevatāḥ śrīśaṅkarādayaḥ, durvāsā hi śaṅkarāṃśajaḥ || 1 ||
śāpena prathitajare'tha nirjarendre deveṣvapyasurajiteṣu niṣprabheṣu |
śarvāghāḥ kamalajametya sarvadevā nirvāṇaprabhava ! samaṃ bhavantamāpuḥ || 2 ||
śāpeneti | he nirvāṇaprabhava ! mokṣaprāptisthānabhūta ! || 2 ||
brahmādyairnutamahimā ciraṃ tadānīṃ prāduḥṣan varada ! puraḥ pareṇa dhāmnā |
he devā! ditijakulairvidhāya sandhiṃ pīyūpaṃ parimathateti paryaśāstvam || 3||
'niḥśrīko bhava tvamiti' iti vā pāutham | 'mathe viloḍane' bhauvādikaḥ |
sandhānaṃ kṛtavati dānavaiḥ suraudhe manthānaṃ nayati madena mandarādrim |
bhraṣṭe'smin vadaramivodvahan khagendre sadyastvaṃ vinihitavān payaḥpayodhau || 4 ||
sandhānamiti | suradānavaudhe manthānaṃ mathanasādhanam | asmin mandarādrau śrāntaśakravairocanādihastebhyo bhraṣṭe nipatite sati tvaṃ khagendre garuḍe ekena hastenoddhṛtyāropyodvahan || 4 ||
ādhāya drutamatha vāsukiṃ vastrāṃ pāthoṣau vinihitasarvavījajāle |
prārabdhe mathanavidhau murāsuraistairvyājāt tvaṃ bhujagamukhe'karoḥ surārīn || 5 ||
ādhāyeti | atha vāsukiṃ vastrāṃ netram | vinihitāni nikṣiptāni sarvabījajālāni vīruttṛṇauṣadhisamūhā yasmin | vyājāditi | hariḥ suraiḥ sārdhamahipucchamamaṅgalamityuccairvadan pūrvaṃ pūrvakāyaṃ jagṛhe | tato'surā vayaṃ jalāghyāḥ pucchaṃ na gṛhṇīma iti tūpṇīṃbhūtān murārīn atisammānanayā bhujagamukhe nirgamiṣyadviṣānyulbaṇe || 5 ||
kṣubdhādrau kṣubhitajalodare tadānīṃ dugdhāndhau gurutarabhārato nimagne |
deveṣu vyathitatameṣu tatpriyaiṣī prāṇaiṣīḥ kamaṭhatanuṃ kaṭhorapṛṣṭhām || 6 ||
kṣubdheti | kṣubdhe bhramite'drau mandarācale dugdhābdhau nimagne sati tvaṃ kamaṭhatanuṃ kacchaparūpaṃ prāṇaiṣīḥ praṇītavān || 6 ||
vajrātisthiratarakarpareṇa viṣṇo ! vistārāt parigatalakṣayojanena |
ambhodheḥ kuharagatena varṣmaṇā tvaṃ nirmagnaṃ kṣitidharanāthamunninetha || 7 ||
vajreti | vajrādatisthiratarapṛṣṭhadeśayuktena varpmaṇā mūrtyā unninetha unmagnamakaroḥ || 7 ||
unmagne jhaṭiti tadā dharādharendre nirmedhurhaḍhamiha sammadena sarve |
āviśya dvitayagaṇe'pi sarparāje vaivaśyaṃ pariśamayannavīvṛdhastān || 8 ||
unmana iti | dvitayagaṇe suragaṇe'suragaṇe ca tadanyatamarūpeṇāviśya sarparāje'pi sūkṣmarūpeṇāviśya teṣāṃ vaivaśyaṃ pīḍāṃ pariśamayan avīvṛdhastān balavīryasampannānakaroḥ || 8 ||
uddāmabhramaṇajavonnamadgirīndranyastaikasthiratarahastapaṅkajaṃ tvām |
abhrānte vidhigiriśādayaḥ pramodādudbhrāntā nunuvurupāttapuṣpavarṣāḥ || 9 ||
uddāmeti | uddāmnā sātiśayena bhramaṇajavenonnamati utpatati girīndre nyasto nikṣipta ekaḥ sthiratarahastapaṅkajo yena taṃ tvām | abhrānte meghamārga udbhrāntāḥ pramodavivaśā nunuvustuṣṭuvuḥ || 9 ||
daityaughe bhujagamukhānilena tapte tenaiva tridaśakule'pi kiñcidārte |
kāruṇyāt tava kila deva ! vārivāhāḥ prāvarṣannamaragaṇān na daityasaṅghān ||
daityaugha iti | vārivāhāḥ meghāḥ || 10 ||
udbhrāmyadbahutiminakracakravāle tatrābdhau ciramathite'pi nirvikāre |
ekastvaṃ karayuṃgakṛṣṭasarparājaḥ saṃrājan pavanapureśa ! pāhi rogāt || 11 ||
udbhrāmyaditi | udbrāmyatāmutpatatāṃ, timirmahāmatsyaḥ, bahutiminakrāṇāṃ cakravālaṃ samūho yasmin | tatra tasmin || 11 ||
ityamṛtamathane devāsurāṇāmaśaktau bhagavatkṛtamathanavarṇa
saptaviṃśaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
garalaṃ taralānalaṃ purastājjaladherudvijagāla kālakūṭam |
amarastutivādamodanighno giriśastannipapau bhavatpriyārtham || 1 ||
'garalamiti | udvijagāla udgalitamabhūt tadāmarāṇāṃ giriśaviṣayo yaḥ stutivādastannighnastadadhīnaḥ san tad garalaṃ nipapau pītavān || 1 ||
vimathatsu surāsureṣu jātā surabhistāmṛṣiṣu nyadhāstridhāman ! |
iyaratnamabhūdathebharatnaṃ ghutaruścāpsarasaḥ sureṣu tāni || 2 ||
vimathatsviti | hayaratnamuccaiḥśravāḥ | ibharatnamairāvataḥ | ghutaruḥ kalpavṛkṣaḥ| tāni sureṣu ca nyadhāḥ dattavān || 2 ||
jagadīśa ! bhavatparā tadānīṃ kamanīyā kamalā babhūva devī |
amalāmavalokya yāṃ vilolaḥ sakalo'pi spṛhayāmbabhūva lokaḥ || 3 ||
jagaditi | bhavān paraḥ pradhāno yasyāḥ sā bhavatparā || 3 ||
tvayi dattahṛde tadaiva devyai tridaśendro maṇipīṭhikāṃ vyatārīt |
sakalopahṛtābhiṣecanīyairṛṣayastāṃ śrutigīrbhirabhyaṣiñcan || 4 ||
tvayīti | tvayi dattaṃ samarpitaṃ hṛd yayā sā tathā tasyai dattahṛde | yadaivamavagatā, tadaiva sakalaiḥ saridbhūmyādibhirupahṛtairabhiṣecanīyairabhiṣekasādhanairoṣadhīkumbhapavitrādibhirṛṣayastāṃ mahālakṣmī śrutigīrbhirmantrairabhyaṣiñcan || 4 || |
abhiṣekajalānupātimugdhatvadapāṅgairavabhūṣitāṅgavallīm
maṇikuṇḍalapītacelahārapramukhaistāmamarādayo'nvabhūṣan || 5 ||
abhiṣeketi | abhiṣekajalapātasamanantaraṃ patituṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāṃ tairmugdhaistvadapāṅgairevāvabhūṣitā aṅgavallī yasyāstām | amarādayo maṇikuṇḍalapītacelahārapramukhairanvabhūṣan alañcakruḥ || 5 || varaṇasrajamāttabhṛṅganādāṃ dadhatī sā kucakumbhamandayānā | padaśiñjitamañjunapurā tvāṃ kalitavrīlavilāsamāsasāda || 6 ||
varaṇeti | varaṇasrajaṃ svayaṃvaramālām || 6 ||
giriśaḍahiṇādisarvadevān guṇabhājo'pyavimuktadoṣaleśān |
avamṛśya sadaiva sarvaramthe nihitā tvayyanayāpi divyamālā || 7 ||
giriśeti | avimuktadoṣaleśān kāmakrodhādiṣaḍdoṣayuktān avamṛśya vicārya sadaiva sarvairguṇairdoṣarahitai ramye tvayi tavāṃsadeśe'nayā śriyā divyamālā nihitā || 7 ||
'bhūṣa alaṅkāre' bhauvādikaḥ |
urasā tarasā mamānithaināṃ bhuvanānāṃ jananīmananyabhāvām |
tvadurovilasattadīkṣaṇaśrīpariṣṭayā paripuṣṭamāsa viśvam || 8 ||
uraseti | tvaṃ ca tarasaināmurasā mamānitha svorasi nivāsamakaroḥ | nānyasmin bhāvo'nurāgo yasyāḥ sānanyabhāvā | tvadurasi vilasantyāstasyā īkṣaṇaśriyaḥ sakaruṇakaṭākṣaśobhāyāḥ parivṛṣṭayā viśvaṃ paripuṣṭaṃ sakalasampatsamṛddham āsa abhūt || 8 ||
atha vāruṇī devyudabhūdityāha -- 
atimohanavibhramā tadānīṃ madayantī khalu vāruṇī nirāgāt |
tamasaḥ padavīmadāstvamenāmatisammānanayā mahāsurebhyaḥ || 9 ||
atīti | tamaso'jñānasya padavīṃ hetubhūtāṃ vāruṇīm || 9 ||
atha dhanvantaryavatāramāha -- 
taruṇāmbudasundarastadā tvaṃ nanu dhanvantarirutthito'mburāśeḥ |
amṛtaṃ kalaśe vahana karābhyāmakhilārti hara mārutālayeśa ! || 10 ||
ityamṛtamathanavarṇanam aṣṭāviṃśaṃ daśakam |
udgacchatastava karādamṛtaṃ haratsu
daityeṣu tānaśaraṇānanunīya devān |
sadyastirodhitha deva ! bhavatprabhāvādudyatsayūthyakalahā ditijā vabhūvuḥ || 1 ||
udgacchata iti | udgacchataḥ prādurbhavataḥ | aśaraṇānananyaśaraṇān | bhavataḥ prabhāvaḥ śaktirmāyā tasmādudyan sayūthyakalaho'nyonyavivādo yeṣāṃ te || 1 ||
śyāmāṃ rucāpi vayasāpi tanuṃ tadānīṃ
prāpto'si tuṅgakucamaṇḍalabhaṅgurāṃ tvam |
pīyūṣakumbhakalahaṃ parimucya sarve
tṛṣṇākulāḥ pratiyayustvadurojakumbhe || 2 ||
śyāmāmiti | vayasāpi śyāmāṃ yauvanamadhyasthām | bhaṅgurāṃ namrām | tvadurojakumbhe pīyūṣādapyatimadhure || 2 ||
kā tvaṃ mṛgākṣi! vibhajasva sudhāmimāmityārūḍharāgavivaśānabhiyācato'mṛn |
viśvasyate mayi kathaṃ kulaṭāsmi daityā !
ityālapannapi suviśvasitānatānīḥ || 3 ||
keti | he daityāḥ ! ahaṃ kulaṭā puṃścalyasmi, bhavadbhiḥ kathaṃ mayi viśvasyate ityālapannapi tadānīntanasapremammitakaṭākṣādibhiḥ suviśvasitānatānīḥ || 3 ||
modāt sudhākalaśameṣu dadatsu sā tvaṃ
duśceṣṭitaṃ mama sahadhvamiti bruvāṇā |
paṅktiprabhedaviniveśitadevadaityā
līlāvilāsagatibhiḥ samadāḥ sudhāṃ tām || 4||
modāditi | yatkiñcid mayā kriyamāṇaṃ duśceṣṭitaṃ sahadhvam | paṅktiprabhedena dvidhā viniveśitā devadaityā yayā sā tathā daityān vañcayituṃ tadviṣayayā laliyā vilāsena gatyātisamīpasañcaraṇena ca samadāḥ samyak surebhya evādadāḥ | tāṃ jarāmaraṇahāriṇīṃ sudhāmityarthaḥ || 4 ||
asmāsviyaṃ praṇayinītyasureṣu teṣu
joṣaṃ sthiteṣvatha samāpya sudhāṃ sureṣu |
tvaṃ bhaktalokavaśago nijarūpametya
svarbhānumardhaparipītasudhaṃ vyalāvīḥ || 5 ||
asmāsviti | asureṣu tu asmāsviyaṃ praṇayinī, aho bhāgyam, eṣāsmān na vañcayediti | joṣaṃ tūṣṇīm | svarbhānuṃ devaliṅgacchannaṃ cakreṇa vyalāvīḥ śiro'cchinaḥ || 5 ||
tvattaḥ sudhāharaṇayogyaphalaṃ pareṣa
dattvā gate layi suraiḥ khalu te vyagṛhṇan |
ghore'tha mūrchati raṇe balidaityamāyāvyāmohite suragaṇe tvamihāvirāsīḥ || 6 ||
vatta iti | tvattaḥ sudhāharaṇasya yogyaṃ phalaṃ vyarthapariśramaṃ pareṣu daityeṣu |
te daityāḥ surairvyagṛhṇan kalahaṃ cakruḥ || 6 ||
tvaṃ kālanemimatha mālimukhāñjaghantha
śakro jaghāna balijambhavalān sapākān |
śuṣkārdraduṣkaravadhe namucau ca lūne
phenena nāradagirā nyaruṇo raṇaṃ tam || 7 ||
tvamiti | śuṣkeṇārdreṇa ca duṣkaravadho namucistasmin ubhayātmakena phenena lūne chinnaśiraske brahmaṇā preṣitasya nāradasya girā tvaṃ raṇaṃ nyaruṇaḥ || 7 ||
yopāvapurdanujamohanamāhitaṃ te
śrutvā vilokanakutūhalavān maheśaḥ |
bhūtaiḥ samaṃ girijayā ca gataḥ padaṃ te
stutvābravīdabhimataṃ tvamatho tirodhāḥ || 8 ||
yoṣeti| te yoṣāvapuḥ strīrūpamāhitaṃ kṛtaṃ śrutvā te padaṃ vaikuṇṭham | abhimataṃ yoṣidrupadarśanam | atho prārthanānantaram || 8 ||
ārāmasīmani ca kandukaghātalīlālolāyamānanayanāṃ kamanīṃ manojñām |
tvāmeṣa vīkṣya vigaladvasanāṃ manobhūvegādanaṅgaripuraṅga! samāliliṅga || 9 ||
ārāmeti | vigaladvasanāṃ mārutahṛtadukūlām | yadyapyanaṅgaripurayaṃ, tathāpi manobhūvegāt tvāṃ samāliliṅga || 9 ||
bhūyo'pi vidrutatīmupadhāvya devo
vīryapramokṣavikasatparamārthabodhaḥ |
tvanmānitastava mahattvamuvāca devyai
tattādṛśastvamava vātaniketanātha! || 10 ||
bhūya iti | vidrutavatīṃ prādravantīmupadhāvya vīryasya retasaḥ pramokṣeṇa | tvayā sammānitaḥ tava mahattvaṃ devyai uvāca || 10 ||
iti devānāmamṛtopalavdhiprakāravarṇanaṃ mohinīdarśanena śivasya dhairyacyutivarṇanaṃ ca
ekonatriṃśaṃ daśakam |
pratijñānirvahaṇādau dharme mahābalirdṛṣṭānta iti tatkathāṃ tadupayogibhagavadavatāracaritaṃ ca prakāśayiṣyan prathamaṃ balivijayamāha -- 
śakreṇa saṃyati hato'pi valirmahātmā
śukreṇa jīvitatanuḥ kratuvardhitoṣmā |
vikrāntimān bhayanilīnasurāṃ trilokīṃ
cakre vaśe sa tava cakramukhādabhītaḥ || 1 ||
śakreṇeti | śakreṇa hato'pi śukreṇa mṛtasañjīvinyākhyayā vidyayā jīvitatanuḥ | viśvajidākhyena kratunā vardhita ūṣmā śaktiryasya | bhayena yuddhe nilīnāḥ surā yasyām | sa baliścakramukhāt sudarśanādabhītaḥ, prahlādavaṃśyatvāt || 1||
putrārtidarśanavaśādaditirviṣaṇṇā
taṃ kāśyapaṃ nijapatiṃ śaraṇaṃ prapannā |
tvatpūjanaṃ taduditaṃ hi payovratākhyaṃ
sā dvādaśāhamacarat tvayi bhaktipūrṇā || 2 ||
putreti | putrasyārtiḥ indrasya svargāpaharaṇajā pīḍā | taduditaṃ kāśyapoditam || 2 ||
tasyāvadhau tvayi nilīnamateramuṣyāḥ
śyāmaścaturbhujavapuḥ svayamāvirāsīḥ |
namrāṃ ca tāmiha bhavattanayo bhaveyaṃ
gopyaṃ madīkṣaṇamiti pralapannayāsīḥ || 3 ||
tasyeti | tasya payovratasyāvadhāvante | pralapan tvaṃ bhartaryevaṃrūpaṃ māṃ bhāvayantī bhartāramupatiṣṭhetyādi kathayan ayāsīḥ tirobhūḥ || 3 ||
tvaṃ kāśyape tapasi sannidadhat tadānīṃ
prāpto'si garbhamaditeḥ praṇuto vidhātrā |
prāmṛta ca prakaṭavaiṣṇavadivyarūpaṃ
sā dvādaśīśravaṇapuṇyadine bhavantam || 4 ||
tvamiti | kāśyape kāśyapasambandhini tapasi tapaḥsambhṛte vīrye sannidhat praviṣṭaḥ | prakaṭavaiṣṇavadivyarūpaṃ śaṅkhacakragadāpadmakirīṭamakuṭamakarakuṇḍalaśrīvatsakaustubhavanamālāpītavasanakāñcīnūpurāghupalakṣitam || 4 ||
puṇyāśramaṃ tamabhivarṣati puṣpavarṣerharpākule surakule kṛtasūryaghoṣa |
baddhavāñjaliṃ jaya jayeti nutaḥ pitṛbhyāṃ tvaṃ tatkṣaṇe paṭutamaṃ vaṭurūpamādhāḥ || 5 ||
puṇyeti | tatkṣaṇa eva vaṭurūpam || 5 ||
tāvat prajāpatiplukhairupanīya mauñjīdaṇḍājinākṣavalayādibhirarcyamānaḥ |
dedīpyamānava purīśa! kṛtāgnikāryastvaṃ prāsthithā baligṛhaṃ prakṛtāśvamedham || 6 ||
tāvaditi | prajāpatimukhairṛṣibhirupanīya upanayanākhyaṃ karma kṛtvā tairmaujñjyādibhirarcyamāna iti | savitā tasya gāyatrīmupadideśa | bṛhaspatiryajñopavītaṃ, pitā mauñjīṃ, bhūmiḥ kṛṣṇājinaṃ, somo daṇḍam, aditiḥ kaupīnaṃ, dyaurachatraṃ, brahmā kamaṇḍaluṃ, saptarṣayo darbhān, sarasvatyakṣamālāṃ ca dadau | evaṃ dedīpyamānamatiśayena śobhamānaṃ vapuryasya, kṛtāgnikāryo'nuṣṭhitasamidādhānakarmā prakṛtāśvamedhaṃ prārabdhāśvamedhayāgaṃ baligṛhaṃ baleryajñaśālāṃ prāsthithāḥ prasthitavān || 6 ||
gāṃtreṇa bhāvimahimocitagauravaṃ prāg
vyāvṛṇvateva dharaṇīṃ calayannayāsīḥ |
chatraṃ paroṣmatiraṇārthamivādadhāno
daṇḍaṃ ca dānavajaneṣviva sannidhātum || 7 ||
gātreṇeti | bhāvimahimā viśvarūpaṃ, taducitaṃ gauravaṃ prāṃgeva dharaṇīcalanena vyāvṛṇvatā prakāśayatā | pareṣāṃ daityānāmūṣmaṇaḥ parākramasya tiraṇārthaṃ vāraṇārtham | sannidhātuṃ daṇḍayitum || 7 ||
tāṃ narmadottarataṭe hayamedhaśālāmāsedupi tvayi rucā tava ruddhanetraiḥ
bhāsvān kimeṣa dahano nu sanatkumāro
yogī nu ko'yamiti śukramukhaiḥ śaśaṅke || 8 ||
tāmiti | āseduṣi prāptavati || 8 ||
ānītamāśu bhṛgubhirmahasābhibhūtaistvāṃ ramyarūpamasuraḥ pulakāvṛtāṅgaḥ |
bhaktyā sametya sukṛtī pariṣicya pādau
tattoyamanvadhṛta mūrdhani tīrthatīrtham || 9 ||
ānītamiti | tvanmahasābhibhūtai rpraṣṭatejaskairbhṛgubhiḥ śukreṇānītaṃ pratigṛhya balinikaṭaṃ nītam | asuro baliḥ | tīrthatīrthaṃ sarvaśodhakam || 9 ||
prahlādavaṃśajatayā kratubhirdvijeṣu
viśvāsato nu tadidaṃ ditijo'pi lebhe |
yat te padāmbu giriśasya śirobhilālyaṃ
sa tvaṃ vibho ! gurupurālaya ! pālayethāḥ || 10 ||
prahlādeti | bhaktottaṃsaprahlādavaṃśajatayā nu, kratubhirnu, dvijeṣu viśvāsataḥ satsaṅgānnu, samastairetairnu (na) niścitaṃ, balirditijo'pi tadidaṃ pādodakasya mūrdhni dhāraṇaṃ lebhe | tādṛṅmahimedamityāha -- yaditi || 10 ||
iti vāmanacarite mahābalerātithyavarṇanaṃ triṃśaṃ daśakam |
prītyā daityastava tanumahaḥprekṣaṇāt sarvathāpi
tvāmārādhyannajita ! racayannañjaliṃ sañjagāda |
mattaḥ kiṃ te samabhilaṣitaṃ viprasūno ! vada tvaṃ
vittaṃ bhaktaṃ bhavanamavanīṃ vāpi sarvaṃ pradāsye || 1 ||
prītyeti | tanormahasaśca prekṣaṇāt sarvathā svāgatavacanādibhiḥ | bhaktaṃ bhṛṣṭānnam || 1 ||
tāmakṣīṇāṃ baligiramupākarṇya kāruṇyapūrṇo’pyasyotsekaṃ śamayitumanā daityavaṃśa praśaṃsan |
bhūmiṃ pādatrayaparimitāṃ prārthayāmāsitha tvaṃ
sarvaṃ dehīti tu nigadite kasya hāsyaṃ na vā syāt || 2 ||
tāmiti | akṣīṇāṃ jagadīśvaratvakhyāpayitrīṃ, he viśveśvara ! sarve sarvasvaṃ me dehīti nigadite yācite tadvacaḥ kasya vā na hāsyaṃ syāt | ataḥ padatrayameva yācitavānasi || 2 ||
viśveśaṃ māṃ tripadamiha kiṃ yācase bāliśastvaṃ
sarvā bhūmiṃ vṛṇu kimamunetyālapat tvāṃ sa dṛpyan |
yasmād darpāt tripadaparipūrtyakṣamaḥ kṣepavādāna
bandhaṃ cāsāvagamadatadarho'pi gāḍhopaśāntyai || 3 ||
viśveśamiti | sa dṛpyan sarvo bhūmiṃ vṛṇvityālapat | darpasya phalamāha -- yasmādviti | atadarho bandhanakṣepavādānarhaḥ | tarhi kathaṃ bhagavān babandha | anuprahāyetyāha -- gāḍheti | samyag viṣayaviraktimāpādya svasmin manassamādhānāyetyarthaḥ || 3 || bhagavānuttaramāha -- 
pādatrayyā yadi na mudito viṣṭapairnāpi tuṣyedityukte'smin varada ! bhavate dātukāme'tha toyam |
daityācāryastava khalu parīkṣārthinaḥ preraṇāt taṃ
mā mā deyaṃ harirayamiti vyaktamevābabhāṣe || 4||
pādeti | yadi na muditaḥ so'santuṣṭo viṣṭapaistribhirlokairapi na tuṣyet | toyaṃ toyapūrvakaṃ mahīṃ dātukāme daityācāryaḥ śukro mā mā deyamiti bhaviṣyatripadakramaṇādikathanapūrvakamābabhāṣe nyaṣedhat | nanu kathaṃ kāvyasya magavatpratikūlācaraṇamityāśaṅkayāha -- tava preraṇāditi | tadapi kimarthamityata āha -- parīkṣārthina iti | balerdharmasthairyaparīkṣārthī tvaṃ tadantaryāmitvenaṃ preritavānityarthaḥ || 4 ||
yācatyevaṃ yadi sa bhagavān pūrṇakāmo'smi so'haṃ
dāsyāmyeva sthiramiti vadan kāvyaśapto'pi daityaḥ |
vindhyāvalyā nijadayitayā dattapādyāya tubhyaṃ
citraṃ citraṃ sakalamapi sa prārpayat toyapūrvam || 5 ||
yācatīti | śukreṇa bhraṣṭaiśvaryo bhaveti śapto'pi sakalaṃ harirayaṃ trailokyamācchidya śakrāya dāsyatīti guruvacanādviditatattvastrailokyaṃ sakalaṃ prārpayat || 5 ||
nissandehaṃ ditikulapatau tvayyaśeṣārpaṇaṃ tad
vyātanvāne mumucurṛṣayaḥ sāmarāḥ puṣpavarṣam |
divyaṃ rūpaṃ tava ca tadidaṃ paśyatāṃ viśvabhājāmuccairuccairavṛdhavadhīkṛtya viśvāṇḍabhāṇḍam || 6 ||
niḥsandehamiti | paśyatāṃ viśvabhājāṃ sarveṣu paśyatsu | tadidaṃ vāmanarūpam | viśvāṇḍabhāṇḍaṃ brahmāṇḍakaṭāhamavadhīkṛtya vavṛdhe || 6 ||
tvatpādāgraṃ nijapadagataṃ puṇḍarīkodbhavo'sau
kuṇḍītoyairasicadapunād yajjalaṃ viśvalokān |
harṣotkarṣāt suvahu nanṛte khecarairutsave'smin
bherīṃ nighnan bhuvanamacarajjāmbavān bhaktiśālī || 7 ||
tvaditi | nijapadagataṃ satyalokāgatam || 7 ||
tāvad daityāstvanumatimṛte bharturārabdhayuddhā
devopetairbhavadanucaraiḥ saṅgatā bhaṅgamāpan |
kālātmāyaṃ vasati purato yadvaśāt prāg jitāḥ smaḥ
kiṃ vo yuddhairiti baligirā te'tha pātālamāpuḥ || 8 ||
pāśairbaddhaṃ patagapatinā daityamuccairavādīstārtīyīkaṃ diśa mama padaṃ kiṃ na viśveśvaro'si |
pādaṃ mūrdhni praṇaya bhagavannityakampaṃ vadantaṃ
prahlādastaṃ svayamupagato mānayannastavīt tvām || 9 ||
pāśairiti | tārtīyīkaṃ tṛtīyam || 9 ||
darpocchintyai vihitamakhilaṃ daitya ! siddho'si puṇyairlokaste'stu tridivavijayī vāsavatvaṃ ca paścāt |
matsāyujyaṃ bhaja ca punarityanvagṛhṇā baliṃ taṃ
vipraiḥ santānitamakhavaraḥ pāhi vātālayeśa ! || 10 ||
darṣeti | vipraiḥ santānitaḥ kāritaprāyaścitto makhavaro rājasūyākhyo yena || 10 ||
iti vāmanacaritavarṇanam ekatriṃśaṃ daśakam |
atha brahmaṇe satyavratāya ca vedatadarthapratipādanāya mātsyaṃ vapurghṛtavato bhagavataścaritamupakramate -- 
purā hayagrīvamahāsureṇa ṣaṣṭhāntarāntodyadakāṇḍakalpe |
nidronmukhabrahmamukhāddhṛteṣu vedeṣvadhitsaḥ kila matsyarūpam || 1 ||
pureti | ṣaṣṭhāntarasya cākṣuṣamanvantarasyānte'vasāne udyatyudbhūte ata evākāṇḍakalpe'vāntarapralaye | aghitsaḥ vidhātumaicchaḥ || 1 ||
satyavratasya dramilādhibharturnadījale tarpayatastadānīm |
karāñjalau sa jvalitākṛtistvamadṛkṣyathāḥ kaścana vālamīnaḥ || 2 ||
satyeti | nadyā kṛtamālāyā jale || 2 ||
kṣiptaṃ jale tvāṃ cakitaṃ vilokya ninthe'mbupātreṇa muniḥ svageham |
svalpairahobhiḥ kalaśīṃ ca kūpaṃ vāpīṃ sarakṣcānaśiṣe vibho ! tvam || 3 ||
kṣiptamiti | ānaśiṣe vyāpto'bhūḥ || 3 ||
yogaprabhāvād bhavadājñayaiva nītastatastvaṃ muninā payodhim |
pṛṣṭo'munā kalpadikṣumenaṃ saptāhamāssveti vadannayāsīḥ || 4 ||
yogeti | muninā rājarṣiṇā | yogaprabhāvāt tapaśśaktyā | bhavadājñayā mahāmatsyaṃ māmapīdānīmudadhijale prakṣipetyevaṃrūpayā | kalpadidṛkṣuṃ pralayārṇavadarśanotsukam || 4 ||
prāpte tvadukte'hani vāridhārāpariplute bhūmitale munīndraḥ |
saptarṣibhiḥ sārdhamapāravāriṇyudghūrṇamānaḥ śaraṇaṃ yayau tvām || 5 ||
prāpta iti | apāravāriṇi pralayābdhau | udghūrṇamānaḥ sambhrāntaḥ || 5 ||
dharāṃ tvadādeśakarīmavāptāṃ naurūpiṇīmāruruhustadā te |
tatkampakampreṣu ca teṣu bhūyastvamambudherāvirabhūrmahīyān || 6 ||
dharāmiti | tatastvadādeśakarīṃ tvatpreritāṃ naurūpiṇīṃ bhūmim | teṣu satyavratasaptarṣiṣu || 6 ||
jhaṣākṛti yojanalakṣadīrghā dadhānamuccaistaratejasaṃ tvām |
nirīkṣya tuṣṭā munayastvaduktyā tvattuṅgaśṛṅge taraṇiṃ babandhuḥ || 7 ||
jhaṣākṛtimiti | taraṇiṃ nāvam || 7 ||
ākṛṣṭanauko munimaṇḍalāya pradarśayan viśvajagadvibhāgān |
saṃstūyamāno nṛvareṇa tena jñānaṃ paraṃ copadiśannacārīḥ || 8 ||
ākṛṣṭeti | munimaṇḍalāya satyavratasaptarṣibhyaḥ | jñānaṃ paraṃ ca śāstrajñānaṃ brahmajñānaṃ ca || 8 ||
kalpāvadhau sapta munīn purovat prasthāpya satyavratabhūmipaṃ tam |
vaivasvatākhyaṃ manumādadhānaḥ krodhāddhayagrīvamabhidruto'bhūḥ || 9 ||
kalpeti | vaivasvatākhyamidānīntanam || 9 ||
svatuṅgaśṛṅgakṣatavakṣasaṃ taṃ nipātya daityaṃ nigamān gṛhatviā |
viriñcaye prītahṭade dadānaḥ prabhañjanāgārapate ! prapāyāḥ || 10 ||
sveti | viriñcaye brahmaṇe || 10 || 71 ||
iti matsyāvatāravarṇanaṃ dvātriṃśaṃ daśakam |
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyākhyāyām
aṣṭagaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
varṇite rāmacarite kimanyaiścaritairiti |
tatpradhāneśānukathā lakṣyate navamoditā ||
athāmbarīṣacaritamupakramate -- 
vaivasvatākhyamanuputranabhāgajātanābhāganāmakanarendrasuto'mbarīṣaḥ |
saptārṇavāvṛtamahīdayito'pi reme
tvatsaṅgiṣu tvayi ca magnamanāḥ sadaiva || 1 ||
vaivasvateti | brahmagaḥ putro marīciḥ, tatputraḥ kāśyapaḥ, tatputro vivasvān, tatputraḥ śrāddhadevo'yaṃ vaivasvatākhyamanuḥ, tatputro nabhāgaḥ, tatputro nābhāgaḥ, tasya putro'mbarīṣaḥ, sa saptārṇavāvṛtāyāḥ saptadvīpavatyā dayitaḥ sārvabhaumo'pi tvayi īśvare tvatsaṅgiṣu tvadbhakteṣu ca reme || 1 ||
tvatprītaye sakalameva vitanvato'sya
bhaktyaiva deva ! nacirādabhṛthāḥ prasādam |
yenāsya yācanamṛte'pyabhirakṣaṇārthaṃ
cakraṃ bhavān pravitatāra sahasradhāram || 2 ||
tvaditi | sakalaṃ laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ ca karma tvatprītaye vitanvataḥ kurvato'sya bhaktyaiva tvaṃ nacirādalpena kālena prasādamabhṛthāḥ, yena prasādenāsyābhirakṣaṇārtha śatrunivāraṇārthaṃ bhavān cakraṃ sudarśanaṃ pravitatāra dattavān || 2 ||
sa dvādaśīvratamatho bhavadarcanārthe
varṣe dadhau madhuvane yamunopakaṇṭhe |
patnyā samaṃ sumanasā mahatīṃ vitanvan
pūjāṃ dvijeṣu visṛjan paśuṣaṣṭikoṭim || 3 ||
sa iti | atho anantaraṃ so'mbarīṣo bhavadarcanārthaṃ varṣaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ dvādaśīvrataṃ
dadhau | yamunopakaṇṭhe yamunāyā upakaṇṭhe tīre sumanasā bhaktiyuktayā mahatīṃ sarvopahāramahābhiṣekayuktāṃ pūjāṃ vitanvan | visṛjan dadat || 3 ||
tatrātha pāraṇadine bhavadarcanānte
durvāsasāsya muninā bhavanaṃ prapede |
bhoktuṃ vṛtaśca sa nṛpeṇa parārtiśīlo
mandaṃ jagāma yamunāṃ niyamān vidhāsyan || 4 ||
tatreti | bhavadarcanasyānte'vasāne | pāraṇadina ityavasānadine viprān bhojayitvā svayaṃ bhoktukāme satītyarthaḥ | śrīdurvāsasā munināsya bhavanaṃ prapede prāpadyata | parārtiśīlaḥ parapīḍānirataḥ | ataḥ pāraṇakālātikrama eva niyamān vidhāyāgamiṣyāmīti mandaṃ jagāma || 4 ||
rājñastatsaṅkaṭamāha
rājñeti pāraṇamuhūrtasya samāptikhedād
vāraiva pāraṇamakāri bhavatpareṇa |
prāpto munistadatha divyadṛśā vijānan
kṣipyan krudhoddhṛtajaṭo vitatāna kṛtyām || 5 ||
rājñeti | pāraṇamuhūrtasya samāptirbhaviṣyati pratipālane, apratipālane ca munikopaḥ syāditi khedād vārā jalenaiva pāraṇamakāri | tat pāraṇaṃ vijānan kṣipyan bahvākṣepavādān kurvan, krudhodghṛtajaṭaḥ jaṭayā kṛtyāṃ vitatāna || 5 ||
kṛtyāṃ ca tāmasidharāṃ bhuvanaṃ dahantīmagre'bhivīkṣya nṛpatirna padāccakampe |
tvadbhaktavādhamabhivīkṣya sudarśanaṃ te
kṛtyānalaṃ śalabhayanmunimanvadhāvīt || 6 ||
kṛtyāmiti | te sudarśanaṃ tvadbhaktasya bādhaṃ pīḍām | kṛtyānalaṃ śalabhayad analapatitaśalabhamiva kurvad munimanvadhāvīd dhāvato muneḥ pṛṣṭhato dhāvanamakarot || 6 ||
dhāvannaśeṣabhuvaneṣu bhiyā sa paśyan
viśvatra cakramapi te gatavān viriñcam |
kaḥ kālacakramatilaṅghayatatyapāstaḥ
śarve yayau sa ca bhavantamavandataiva || 7 ||
dhāvanniti | sa viśvatra sarvatrāpi te cakraṃ paśyan viriñcaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatavān | kaḥ kālacakramatilaṅghayatīti tadvacanādapāstaḥ pratinivṛttaḥ śarve śivaṃ prāptavān | yasya cakramidaṃ, tameva hariṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajetyabhiprāyeṇa sa bhavantamavandataiva || 7 ||
bhūyo bhavannilayametya muniṃ namantaṃ
proce bhavānahamṛṣe ! nanu bhaktadāsaḥ |
jñānaṃ tapaśca vinayānvitameva mānyaṃ
yāhyambarīṣapadameva bhajeti bhūman ! || 8 ||
bhūya iti | bhavannilayaṃ vaikuṇṭham | ahaṃ bhaktānāṃ dāsa iva, tadadhīnatvāt | nanvahamapi jñānataponiṣṭhatvāt tvadbhakta eva | satyam | jñānaṃ tapaśca yadi vinayenopaśamena yuktaṃ bhavati, tarheva mānyaṃ niḥśreyasāya bhavati | durvinītasyaitad dvayamapi na sukhāya, yathā bhavataḥ | tadambarīṣasya padaṃ pādāveva śaraṇaṃ bhaja vraja || 8 ||
bhūtāvat sametya muninā sa gṛhītapādo
rājāpasṛtya bhavadatramasāva (nauṣī?nāvī)t |
cakre gate muniradādakhilāśiṣo'smai
tvadbhaktimāgasi kṛte'pi kṛpāṃ ca śaṃsan || 9||
tāvaditi | so'mbarīṣaḥ pādasparśalajjayā apasṛtya bhavadastraṃ sudarśanamanauṣīt tuṣṭāva | cakre gate śānte sati āgasi kṛtyotpādanāparādhe kṛte'pi svaviṣayā mambarīṣasya kṛpāṃ śaṃsan stuvan asmā āśiṣo varān || 9 ||
bhūrājā pratīkṣya munimekasamāmanāśvān
sambhojya sādhu tamṛṣiṃ visṛjan prasannam |
bhuktvā svayaṃ tvayi tato'pi dṛḍhaṃ rato'bhūt
sāyujyamāpa ca sa māṃ pavaneśa! pāyāḥ || 10 ||
rājeti | rājā ekasamāmekasaṃvatsaramanāśvān anaśnannabbhakṣo bhūtvā muniṃ pratīkṣya pratipālya sādhu sambhojya taccheṣaṃ svayaṃ bhuktvā visṛjannanujñāya tato'pi pūrvasmādapi tvayi rato bhakto'bhūt | ante sāyujyaṃ mokṣaṃ cāpa || 10 ||
ityambarīṣacaritavarṇanaṃ trayastriśaṃ daśakam |
atha śrīrāmacaritaṃ prastauti -- 
gīrvāṇairarthyamāno daśamukhanidhanaṃ kosaleṣvṛśyaśṛṅge
putrīyāmiṣṭimiṣṭvā daduṣi daśarathakṣmābhṛte pāyasāgrayam |
tadbhuktyā tatpurandhrīṣvapi tisṛṣu samaṃ jātagarbhāsu jāto
rāmastvaṃ lakṣmaṇena svayamatha bharatenāpi śatrughnanāmnā || 1 ||
tvaṃ śrīharireva rāmo jāta ityuktam | tadupapādayati -- 
kodaṇḍī kauśikasya ṛtuvaramavituṃ lakṣmaṇenānuyāto
yāto'bhūstātavācā munikathitamanudvandvaśāntādhvakhedaḥ |
nṛṇāṃ trāṇāya vāṇairmunivacanavalāt tāṭakāṃ pāṭayitvā
labdhvāsmādatrajālaṃ munivanamagamo deva! siddhāśramākhyam || 2 ||
kodaṇḍīti | bālya eva duṣṭanigrahaśiṣṭaparipālanasajjanabahumānādiḥ kevalamanuṣyasambandhī na bhavatīti bhāvaḥ || 2 ||
apica, munipatnyāḥ śāpamokṣo maheśvaracāpakhaṇḍanādi cāsya viṣṇvaṃśatvaṃ prakaṭayatītyāha
mārīcaṃ drāvayitvā makhaśirasi śarairanyarakṣāṃsi nighnan
kalyāṃ kurvannahalyāṃ pathi padarajasā prāpya vaidehageham |
bhindānaścāndracūḍaṃ dhanuravanisutāmindirāmeva labdhvā
rājyaṃ prātiṣṭhathāstvaṃ tribhirapi ca samaṃ bhrātṛvīraiḥ sadāraiḥ || 3 ||
bhārgavarāmajayaḥ tena svatejasaḥ śrīrāme samarpaṇamapi rāmāvatārasyetarāvatārād
viśiṣṭatvamavagamayatītyāha -- 
ārundhāne ruṣāndhe bhṛgukulatilake saṃkramayya svatejo
yāte yāto'syayodhyāṃ sukhamiha nivasan kāntayā kāntamūrte ! |
śatrughnenaikadātho gatavati bharate mātulasyādhivāsaṃ
tātārabdho'bhiṣekastava kila vihataḥ kekayādhīśaputryā || 4 ||
evamavatāramupapādyāsya lokasaṃgrahaṇārthamaṅgīkṛtaṃ caritaṃ stauti
tātoktyā yātukāmo vanamanujavadhūsaṃyutakṣcāpadhāraḥ
paurānādhya mārge guhanilayagatastvaṃ jaṭācīradhārī |
nāvā santīrya gaṅgāmadhipadavi punastaṃ bharadvājamārānnatvā tadvākyahetoratisukhamavasaścitrakūṭe girīndre || 5 ||
tātoktyeti | tātoktyaiva rājyaśriyamapahāya jaṭācīradhārī tvaṃ citrakūṭe sukhamavasaḥ| evaṃ guruvacanaparairbhavitavyamiti lokān grāhayitumetaditi bhāvaḥ || 5||
śrutvā putrārtikhinnaṃ khalu bharatamukhāt svargayātaṃ svatātaṃ
tapto dattvāmbu tasmai nidadhiya bharate pādukāṃ medinīṃ ca |
atriṃ natvātha gatvā vanamativipulāṃ daṇḍakāṃ caṇḍakāyaṃ
hatvā daityaṃ virādhaṃ sugatimakalayakṣcāru bhoḥ ! śārabhaṅgīm || 6 ||
śrutveti | śārabhaṅgīṃ śarabhaṅgasambandhinīṃ sugatiṃ mokṣam || 6 ||
natvāgastyaṃ samastāśaranikarasapatrākṛtiṃ tāpasebhyaḥ
pratyaśrauṣīḥ priyaiṣī tadanu ca muninā vaiṣṇave divyacāpe |
brahmāstre cāpi datte pathi pitṛsuhṛdaṃ vīkṣya bhūyo jaṭāyuṃ
modāi godātaṭānte pariramasi purā pañcavaṭyāṃ vadhūṭyā || 7 ||
natveti | samastāśaranikarasapatrākṛtiṃ sarvarākṣasavadhaṃ pratyaśrauṣīḥ pratijñātavān | godā godāvarī | pariramasi purā svairaṃ nyavasaḥ || 7 ||
prāptāyāḥ śūrpaṇakhyā madanacaladhṛterarthanairnissahātmā
tāṃ saumitrau visṛjya pravalatamaruṣā tena nirlūnanāsām |
dṛṣṭaivanāṃ ruṣṭacittāṃ kharamabhipatitaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ ca trimūrdhaṃ
vyāhiṃsīrāśarānapya yutasamadhikāṃstatkṣaṇādakṣatoṣmā || 8 ||
sodaryāproktavārtāvivaśadaśamukhādiṣṭamārīcamāyāsāraṅgaṃ sārasākṣyā spṛhitamanugataḥ prāvadhī rvāṇaghātam
tanmāyākrandaniryāpitabhavadanujāṃ rāvaṇastāmahārṣīt
tenārto'pi tvamantaḥ kimapi mudamadhāstadvadhopāyalābhāt || 9||
sodaryeti | tena sītāviraheṇa bahirārta iti sidhyati, antaḥ kimapi mudamadhā ityukteḥ | anena ca strīvacanaṃ pramāṇīkurvatāmevaṃ duḥkhamiti grāhayitumetadityapi sūcitam || 9||
bhūyastanvīṃ vicinvannahṛta daśamukhastvadvadhūṃ madvadhenetyuktvā yāte jaṭāyau divamatha suhṛdaḥ prātanoḥ pretakāryam |
gṛhṇānaṃ taṃ kaṃbandhaṃ jaghanitha śabarīṃ prekṣya pampātaṭe tvaṃ
sammāpto vātasūnuṃ bhṛśamuditamanāḥ pāhi vātālayeśa ! || 10 ||
iti śrīrāmacarite pampāsarasi hanūmatsamāgamavarṇanaṃ
catutriṃśaṃ daśakam |
nītaḥ sugrīvamaitrīṃ tadanu hanumatā dundubheḥ kāyamuccaiḥ
kṣiptvāṅguṣṭhena bhūyo lulavitha yugapat patriṇā sapta sālān |
hatvā sugrīvaghātodyatamatulabalaṃ vālinaṃ vyājavṛtyā
varṣāvelāmanaiṣīrvirahataralitastvaṃ mataṅgagāśramānte || 1 ||
sugrīveṇānujoktyā sabhayamabhiyatā vyūhitāṃ vāhinīṃ tāmṛkṣāṇāṃ vīkṣya dikṣu drutamatha dayitāmārgaṇāyāvanamrām 'karaṇe hanaḥ' iti ṇamul |
sandeśaṃ cāṅgulīyaṃ pavanasutakare mādiśo modaśālī
mārge mārge mamārge kapibhirapi tadā tvatpriyā saprayāsaiḥ || 2 ||
sugrīveṇeti | anujasya lakṣmaṇasyoktyā sugrīveṇa sahābhiyatā bhigacchatā | ṛkṣāṇāṃ vāhinīm | mamārge anviṣṭā || 2 ||
tvadvārtākarṇanodyadrurudurujavasampātisampātivākyaprottīrṇārṇoghirantarnagari janakajāṃ vīkṣya dattvāṅgulīyam |
prakṣudyodyānamakṣakṣapaṇacaṇaraṇaḥ soḍhavandho daśāsyaṃ
dṛṣṭvā ṣṭaṣṭvā ca laṅkāṃ jhaṭiti sa hanumāna mauliratnaṃ dadau te || 3 ||
tvaditi | rāmāyaṇākarṇanena sañjātapakṣasya urujavasampātina uparyuḍḍīya sītāṃ dṛṣṭavataḥ sampātervākyena | prakṣudya cūrṇāṃkṛtya | akṣakṣapaṇenākṣakumāravadhena prasiddho raṇo yasya | soḍhaḥ śāpasmaraṇānmarṣito bandho yena || 3 ||
tvaṃ sugrīvāṅgadādiprabalakapicamṛcakravikrāntabhūmīcakro'bhikramya pārejaladhi niśicarendrānujā śrīyamāṇaḥ | tatproktāṃ śatruvārtā rahasi niśamayan prārthanāpārthyaropaprāstāgneyāstratejastrasadudadhigirā labdhavān madhyamārgam || 4 ||
tvamiti | pārejaladhiṃ jaladheḥ pāre | prārthanāyā āpārthye vaiphalyam | prāstasya prakṣiptasya || 4 ||
kīśairāśāntaropāhṭatagirinikaraiḥ setumādhāpya yāto
yātūnyāmargha daṃṣṭrānakhaśikhariśilāsālaśastraiḥ svasainyaiḥ |
vyākurvan sānujastvaṃ samarabhuvi paraṃ vikramaṃ śakrajetrā
vegānāgākhavaddhaḥ patagapatigarunmārutairmocito'bhūḥ || 5 ||
kīśairiti | vyākurvan prakāśayan || 5 ||
saumitristvatra śaktipravṛtigaladasurvātajānītaśailaghrāṇāt prāṇānupeto vyakṛṇuta kusṛti jalāghinaṃ meghanādam |
māyākṣobheṣu vaibhīṣaṇavacanahṛtastambhanaḥ kumbhakarṇaṃ
samprāptaṃ kampitorvītalamakhilacamūbhakṣiṇaṃ vyakṣiṇostvam || 6 ||
gṛhṇan janbhārisampreṣitarathakavacau rāvaṇenābhiyudhyanbrahmāstreṇāsya bhindan galatatimavalāmagniśuddhāṃ pragṛhṇan |
deva ! śreṇīvarojjīvitasamaramṛtairakṣatairṛkṣasaṅghairlaṅkābhartrā ca sākaṃ nijanagaramagāḥ sapriyaḥ puṣpakeṇa || 7 ||
gṛhṇanniti | śreṇīvarairhanumadādibhirujjīvitāḥ samaramṛtā yeṣu ṛkṣasaṅgheṣu | devaśreṇīnāṃ varairujjīvitā iti vā || 7 ||
prīto divyābhiṣekairayutasamadhikān vatsarān paryaraṃsīrmaithilyāṃ pāpavācā śiva śiva kila tāṃ garbhiṇīmabhyahāsīḥ |
śatrughnenārdayitvā lavaṇaniśicaraṃ prārdayaḥ śūdrapāśaṃ
tāvad vālmīkigehe kṛtavasatirupāsūta sītā sutau te || 8 ||
prīta iti | ardayitvā ghātayitvā svayameva śūdrapāśaṃ kutsitaṃ śūdramuniṃ prārdayaḥ || 8 ||
vālmīkestvatsutodgāpitamadhurakṛterājñayā yajñabāṭe
sītāṃ tvayyāptukāme kṣitimaviśadasau tvaṃ ca kālārthito'bhūḥ |
hetoḥ saumitrighātī svayamatha sarayūmagnaniśśeṣabhṛtyaiḥ
sākaṃ nākaṃ prayāto nijapadamagamo deva! vaikuṇṭhamādyam || 9 ||
vālmīkeriti | tvatsutābhyāṃ kuśalavābhyāmudgāpitā madhurā kṛtī rāmāyaṇaṃ kāvyaṃ yena sa tathā | kālārthito yamadharmarājena svargārohaṇāya yācito'bhūḥ | hetoḥ saumitrighātīti | ayamarthaḥ -- rahassaṃvādinorāvayordraṣṭāraṃ tya(kṣye?kṣyāmi) iti dharmarājasya purataḥ kṛtapratijño rāmastadantarā prāptaṃ saumitriṃ pratijñābhaṅgabhayāt tatyājeti | atha svayaṃ niśśeṣairdevāṃśaiḥ pauraiśca sākaṃ nākaṃ svarga tataśca nijapadaṃ vaikuṇṭham | ādyaṃ brahmāṇḍotpatteḥ prāgapi vidyamānam || 9 ||
yudhyannityanudāttettvalakṣaṇasyātmanepadasyānityatvāt | nanvīśvarasyāpi yadi śarīrabandhastarhi saṃsārityaprasaṅga iti cad netyāha -- 
so'yaṃ martyāvatārastava khalu niyataṃ martyaśikṣārthamevaṃ
vikṣleṣārtirnirāgastyajanamapi bhavet kāmadharmātisaktyā |
no cet svātmānubhūteḥ kkanu tava manaso vikriyā cakrapāṇe !
sa tvaṃ sattvaikamūrte! pavanapurapate ! vyādhunu vyādhitāpāna || 10 ||
sa iti | tava svādhīnamāyasya saḥ brahmādibhirapyavijñātavibhavaḥ ayaṃ pradarśito martyāvatāraḥ khalu līlāvigrahaparigraha eva, netaravanmāyāparatantratayā saṃsāra iti bhāvaḥ | tadapi martyāśekṣārtham | kintarhyanena śikṣitaṃ bhavati, tatrāhaevamiti | kāmātisaktyā vikṣleṣārtiḥ priyāvirahaduḥkhaṃ dharmātisaktyā nirāgastyajanamapi bhavati, yathā sītāyāḥ | ataḥ kāmādāvatisaktirmā bhūditi | tadupapādayati -- no cediti | svātmā svasvarūpamevānubhūtiḥ prakāśo yasya tasya svaprakāśasaṃvidrūpasya parabrahmaṇastava manaso vikriyā rāgadveṣamohādayaḥ kvanu, na bhavatyevetyarthaḥ | kālātmakaṃ cakraṃ pāṇau svādhīnatayā sthitaṃ yasya tasya tatra saṃsārāpādane kālo na prabhuḥ| kintu sattvaikamūrtte! iti | bhaktānugrahāya śuddhasattvamayamūrtyaṅgīkāra evāyamavatāra iti bhāvaḥ | vyādhunu apākuru || 10 ||
iti śrīrāmacaritavarṇanaṃ pañcatriṃśaṃ daśakam |
atha dattātreyāvatāramāha -- 
atreḥ putratayā purā tvamanasūyāyāṃ hi dattābhidho
jātaḥ śiṣyanibandhatandritamanāḥ svasthaśvaran kāntayā |
dṛṣṭo bhaktatamena hehayamahīpālena tasmai varānaṣṭaiśvaryamukhān pradāya daditha svenaiva cānte vadham || 1 ||
atreriti | datto mayāhamiti bhagavatoktatvād datta ityabhidhā yasya sa tvaṃ samādhinairantaryavighātakatayā śiṣyanibandhatandritamanāstannirākaraṇāya kāntayā saha madhumadavivaśabhāvaṃ pradarśya svastha ātmārāmaścaran hehayamahīpālena śrīkārtavīryārjunena || 1 || atha śrīparaśurāmāvatāramāha -- 
satyaṃ kartumathārjunasya ca varaṃ tacchaktimātrānataṃ
brahmadveṣi tadākhilaṃ nṛpakulaṃ hantuṃ ca bhūmerbharam |
sañjāto jamadagnito bhṛgukule tvaṃ reṇukāyāṃ hare !
rāmo nāma tadātmajeṣvavarajaḥ pitroradhāḥ sampadam || 2 ||
satyamiti | arjunasya varamante svenaiva vadham | tacchaktirarjunasya śaktistanmātreṇānatam īpacchamitavrahmopadravam || 2 ||
labdhāmnāyagaṇaścaturdaśavayā gandharvarāje manāgāsaktāṃ kila mātaraṃ prati pituḥ krodhākulasyājñayā |
tātājñātigasodaraiḥ samamimāṃ chitvātha śāntāt pitusteṣāṃ jīvanayogamāpitha varaṃ mātā ca te'dād varam || 3 ||
labdheti | caturdaśavayā eva labdha arthajñānaparyantamadhīta āmnāyagaṇo yena gandharvarāje citrarathe manāgīṣadāsaktāmata eva jalānayane savilambāṃ mātaraṃ reṇukāṃ prati imāṃ jahīti tātasya jamadagnerājñāmatigacchantyatikrāmantīti tathā taiḥ sodarairjyeṣṭhaiḥ samaṃ sahemāṃ reṇukāṃ mātaraṃ chitvā nigṛhya punaḥ śāntāt prasannāt pituḥ sakāśāt teṣāṃ māturbhrātṝṇāṃ ca jīvanayogaṃ varamāpitha prāptavān || 3 ||
pitrā mātṛmude stavātaviyaddhenornijādāśramāt
prasthāyātha bhṛgorgirā himagirāvārādhya gaurīpatim |
labdhvā tatparaśuṃ taduktadanujacchedī mahāstrādikaṃ
prāpto mitramathākṛtatraṇamuniṃ prāpyāgamaḥ svāśramam || 4 ||
pitreti| athānantaraṃ pitrā jamadagninā mātū reṇukāyā muṃde prītyai stavenāhṛtānītā viyaddhenuḥ surabhiryasmin tasmādāśramād bhṛgoḥ pitāmahasya girā prasthāya tasya gaurīpateḥ paraśuṃ labdhvā tena gaurīpatinoktam | tasmādeva mahāstrādikaṃ prāptaḥ siṃhamukhād rakṣitamakṛtatraṇamuniṃ mitraṃ prāpya svāśramamagamaḥ || 4 ||
ākheṭopagato'rjunaḥ suragavīsalprāptasampadgaṇaistvapitrā paripūjitaḥ puragato durmantrivācā punaḥ |
gāṃ kretuṃ sacivaṃ nyayuṅkta kudhiyā tenāpi rundhanmuniprāṇakṣepasaropagohatacamṛcakreṇa vatso hṛtaḥ || 5 ||
ākheṭeti | ākheṭopagato mṛgayāprasaṅgenāśramaṃ gataḥ | suragavīsamprāptaiḥ surabhisakāśāllabdhaiḥ sampadgaṇaidivyairbhogyaiḥ | puraṃ māhimmatī gataḥ | punardurmantrivācā gāṃ tāṃ surabhi kretuṃ svīkartum | kudhiyā (tena sacivenaḥ |) gavāharaṇaṃ runvato munerjamadagneḥ prāṇakṣepo vadhastena saroṣayā gavā hataṃ camūcakraṃ yasya tena sacivena surabhevatso hṛto māhiṣmatī nītaḥ || 5 ||
śukrojjīvitatātavākyacalitakrodho'tha saṃkhyā samaṃ
vibhrad dhyātamahodaropanihitaṃ cāpaṃ kuṭhāraṃ śarān |
ārūḍhaḥ sahavāhayantṛkarathaṃ māhiṣpratīmāviśan
vāgbhirvatsamadāśuṣi kṣitipatau sammāstuthāḥ saṅgaram || 6||
śukreti | sakhyā akṛtavraṇena dhyātena mahodareṇa giriśapārṣadenopanihitamānītaṃ cāpādicatuṣṭayaṃ bibhradārūḍhaśca san kṣitipatāvarjune | samprāstuthā āravdhavān || 6 ||
atha sainyanāśe satyarjunaḥ svayameva śrīparaśurāmabhāpatadityāha -- 
putrāṇāmayutena saptadaśabhiśrākṣauhiṇībhirmahāsenānībhiranekamitranivahairvyājṛmbhitāyodhanaḥ |
saghastvatkakuṭhāravāṇavidalanniśśeṣa sainyotkaro
bhītipradrutanaṣṭaśiṣṭatanayastvāmāpataddhehayaḥ || 7 ||
līlāvāritanarmadājalavalallaṅkeśagarvāpahaśrīmadbhāhusahasramuktabahuśastrāstraṃ nirundhannamum |
cakre tvayyatha vaiṣṇave'pi viphale buddhvā hariṃ tvāṃ mudā
dhyāyantaṃ chitasarvadoṣamavadhīḥ so'gāt paraṃ te padam || 8 ||
līleti | līlayā bāhusahasreṇa vārite narmadājale valataḥ plavamānasya laṅkeśasya garvamapahantīti tathā, yena kārāgṛhaṃ prāpito rāvaṇastena śrīmadvāhusahasreṇa muktāni bahūni śastrāṇi astrāṇi ca yena tamamuṃ kārtavīrya nirundhan nivārayan atha tvayi tatprayukte vaiṣṇave cakre'pi viphale jāte tvāṃ hariṃ buddhvā śrīhariṃ dhyāyantamata eva chitasarvadoṣaṃ naṣṭakāmakrodhādidoṣaṃ vṛkṇabāhusahasraṃ kṛtvāvadhīrnihatavān || 8 ||
bhūyo'marṣitahehayātmajagaṇaistāte hate reṇukāmāghnānāṃ hṛdayaṃ nirīkṣya bahuśo ghorāṃ pratijñāṃ vahan |
dhyānānītarathāyudhastvamakṛthā vipradruhaḥ kṣatriyān
dikcatreṣu kuṭhārayan viśikhayan niḥkṣatriyāṃ medinīm || 9 ||
bhūya iti | amarṣitairnijapitṛvadhamasahamānairnaṣṭaśiṣṭhairhehayātmajagaṇaistvadvihīnamāśramaṃ prāptaistava tāte jamadagnau hate hṛdayamānānāṃ sorastāḍanaṃ vilapantīm | pūrvavad dhyānānītarathāyudho dikcaktreṣu ye vipradruhaḥ kṣatriyāstān kuṭhārayan kuṭhāreṇa nighnan viśikhayan viśikhairnighnan || 9 ||
tātojjīvanakṛnnṛpālakakulaṃ triḥsaptakṛtvo jayan
samtarpyātha samantapañcakamahāraktahadaughe pitṝn |
yajñe kṣmāmapi kāśyapādiṣu diśan sālvena yudhyan punaḥ
kṛṣṇo'muṃ nihaniṣyatīti śamito yuddhāt kumārairbhavān || 10 ||
tāteti | pituḥ śiraḥ kāyena sandhāya makhaiḥ pitaraṃ jīvayan kāśyapādiṣvṛtvikṣu diśan dadat punaḥ sālvena yudhyan kumāraiḥ sanakādibhistadvākyena śrīkṛṣṇo'muṃ nihaniṣyatīti jñātvā bhavān yuddhācchamito'bhūt || 10 ||
‘prātipadikād dhātvarthe bahulamiṣṭhavacca' iti ṇic | evaṃ viśikhayanniti | atha mahendrabhūbhṛti tapasyan munibhirabhyarthitaḥ keralabhūmiṃ samudramagnāmuddhṛtavānityāha -- 
nyasyāstrāṇi mahendrabhūbhṛti tapastanvan punarmajjitāṃ
gokarṇāvadhi sāgareṇa dharaṇī ddaṣṭvārthitastāpasaiḥ |
dhyāteṣvāsadhṛtānalāstracakitaṃ sindhuṃ sruvakṣepaṇādutsāryoddhṛtakeralo bhṛgupate! vāteśa! saṃrakṣa mām || 11 || 41 ||
iti dattātreyaparaśurāmayoravatāravarṇanaṃ paraśurāmacaritavarṇanaṃ ca ṣaṭtriśaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
kṣlokārthacintāsandehapraśnākhyānopapādanaiḥ |
haricintāvinābhūtaiḥ kṣaṇaṃ saphalayāmahe ||
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyākhyāyāṃ
navamaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
kṛṣṇalīlā nirodhākhyā bhūbhāraharaṇātmikā |
stūyate nātisaṃkṣiptā kṣlokaiḥ śrotramanoharaiḥ ||
sāndrānandatano ! hare ! nanu purā daivāsure saṅgare
tvatkṛttā api karmaśeṣavaṃśato ye te na yātā gatim |
teṣāṃ bhūtalajanmanāṃ dvitibhuvāṃ bhāreṇa durārditā
bhūmiḥ māpa viriñcamāśritapadaṃ devaiḥ puraivāgataiḥ || 1 ||
sāndrānandeti | ādau maṅgalārthamupāttamante'pi 'paramānandasandohalakṣmīma' iti prayokṣyan madhye'pi maṅgalārtha parabrahmavācakamānandapadaṃ prayuṅkte -- sāndrānandeti | he paramānandasvarūpa ! daivāsure devāsurasambandhini | tvatkṛttā apīti | tvayā nigṛhītānāṃ sadya eva muktiḥ dvitrairjanmabhirvā syādeva | tatra ye te prasiddhāḥ kālanemyādayo daityāḥ, te karmaśeṣavaśato manuṣyatvāpādakapuṇyapāpaśeṣaparatantratayā bhagavatā nigṛhītā api gatiṃ mokṣaṃ na yātāḥ na prāptāḥ | ata eva kaṃsādirūpeṇa bhūtale janma yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ bhāreṇa bhareṇa dūrārditā atiśayena pīḍitā purā bhuvaḥ prāpteḥ pūrvamevāgataiḥ prāptairdevaiḥ saha bhūmirviriñcaṃ prāpa | āśritapadaṃ satyalokastham, athavopāsitabhagavatpādapadmaṃ, yadvā devairāśritaṃ pādapadmaṃ yasya sa tatheti || 1 ||
hā hā durjanabhūribhāramathitāṃ pāthonidhau pātukāmetāṃ pālaya hanta me vivaśatāṃ saṃpṛccha devānimān |
ityādipracurapralāpavivaśāmālokya dhātā mahīṃ
devānāṃ vadanāni vīkṣya parito dadhyau bhavantaṃ hare ! || 2 ||
hā heti | hā hā kaṣṭaṃ durjanā eva bhūribhārastena mathitāṃ pari kliṣṭām | pāthonidhāvāvaraṇodake pātukāṃ magnaprāyām | vadanāni satyamevāsyā vaca iti vacanagarbhāṇi mukhāni parito vilokya || 2 ||
ūce cāmbujabhūramanayi surāḥ satyaṃ dharitryā vaco
nanvasyā bhavatāṃ ca rakṣaṇavidhau dakṣo hi lakṣmīpatiḥ |
sarve śarvapurassarā vayamito gatvā payovāridhi
natvā taṃ stumahe javāditi yayuḥ sākaṃ tavāketanam || 3 ||
ūca iti | dharitryā vacaḥ satyaṃ, mayā divyena cakṣuṣā dṛṣṭam | lakṣmīpatiriti | bhuvo rakṣaṇābhāve'sau kevalaṃ lakṣmīpatireva syāditi bhāvaḥ | tavāketanaṃ kṣīrābdhim || 3 ||
te mugdhānilaśālidugdhajaladhestīraṃ gatāḥ saṅgatā
yāvat tvatpadacintanaikamanasastāvat sa pāthojabhūḥ |
tvadvācaṃ hṛdaye niśamya sakalānānandayannūcivānākhyātaḥ paramātmanā svayamahaṃ vākyaṃ tadākarṇyatām || 4||
ta iti | te yāvat tīraṃ gatāḥ, tāvat pāthojabhūstava śeṣaśāyino vācaṃ hṛdaye hṛdayākāśe prakāśitāṃ niśamyāvagamya paramātmanā viṣṇunā svayaṃ svenaivāhamākhyātaḥ, tat tasya vākyamākarṇyatāmityūcivān || 4 ||
jāne dīnadaśāmahaṃ diviṣadāṃ bhūmeśca bhīmairnṛpaistatkṣepāya bhavāmi yādavakule so'haṃ samagrātmanā |
devā vṛṣṇikule bhavantu kalayā devāṅganākṣcāvanau
matsevārthamiti tvadīyavacanaṃ pāthojabhūrūcivān || 5||
