jāna iti | bhīmairnṛpairjarāsandhādibhirhetubhūtaiḥ | dīnadaśāṃ parādhīnavṛttitām | teṣāṃ kṣepāya nigrahāya saḥ saccidānandasvarūpo'haṃ samagrātmanā, nāṃśena | devāḥ kalayā aṃśena vṛṣṇikule gopālāḥ devāṅganā gopyaśca bhavantu || 5 ||
śrutvā karṇarasāyanaṃ tava vacaḥ sarveṣu nirvāpitasvānteṣvīśa ! gateṣu tāvakakṛpāpīyūṣatṛptātmasu |
vikhyāte mathurāpure kila bhavatsānnidhyapuṇyottare
dhanyāṃ devakanandanāmudavahad rājā sa śūrātmajaḥ || 6||
śrutveti | nirvāpitāni sukhitāni svāntāni yeṣām | bhavatsānnidhyena yat puṇyamabhyudayaniḥśreyasakaratvaṃ tenottara utkṛṣṭe | udavahad upayeme | śūrātmajo vasudevaḥ || 6 ||
udvāhāvasitau tadīyasahajaḥ kaṃso'tha sammānayannetau sūtatayā gataḥ pathi rathe vyomotthayā tvadgirā |
asyāstvāmatiduṣṭamaṣṭamasuto hanteti interitaḥ
santrāsāt sa tu hantumantikagatāṃ tanvīṃ kṛpāṇīmadhāt || 7 ||
udvāheti | etau devakīvasudevau sūtatayā sammānayan | tvadbhireti ‘daivamapyanṛtaṃ vakti' iti punaḥ kaṃsavacanāt | hantā haniṣyati | hanta aho kṛtārthā devā bhūścetīritaḥ | tanvīṃ devakīm| adhād ādade ||
gṛhṇānacikureṣu tāṃ khalamatiḥ śaurekṣciraṃ sāntvanairno muñcan punarātmajārpaṇagirā prīto'tha yāto gṛhān |
ādyaṃ tvatsahajaṃ tathārpitamapi snehena nāhannasau
duṣṭānāmapi deva ! puṣṭakaruṇā dṛṣṭā hi dhīrekadā || 8 ||
gṛhṇāna iti | ciraṃ dīrghakālaṃ prayuktaiḥ sāntvavacanaiḥ | ātmajārpaṇagirā asyāḥ putrāṃstubhyaṃ dāsyāmīti pratijñayā || 8 ||
tāvat tvanmanasaiva nāradamuniḥ proce sa bhojeśvaraṃ
yūyaṃ nanvasurāḥ surākṣca yadavo jānāsi kiṃ na prabho! |
māyāvī sa harirbhavadūdhakṛte bhāvī suraprārthanādityākarṇya yadūnadūdhunadasau śaurekṣca sūnūnahan || 9 ||
tāvaditi | yāvat kaṃso gṛhaṃ gatavān, tāvat tvanmanasā tvayā prerita eva, anyathā śrīnāradasya kaṃsaṃ prati dvaidhībhāvaprakāśanāyogāt | yūyamasurāḥ kālanemyādayaḥ| bhavadvadhakṛte nṛpībhūtānāṃ bhavatāṃ vadhārthe harirasyā aṣṭamaputro bhāvī | adūdhunat sthānāccyāvayāmāsa || 9 ||
prāpte saptamagarbhatāmahipatau tvatpreraṇānmāyayā
nīte mādhava ! rohiṇīṃ tvamapi bhoḥ ! saccitsukhaikātmakaḥ |
devakyā jaṭharaṃ viveśitha vibho ! saṃstūyamānaḥ suraiḥ
sa tvaṃ kṛṣṇa! vidhūya rogapaṭalīṃ bhaktiṃ parāṃ dehi me || 10 ||
prāpta iti | devakyāḥ saptamagarbhatāṃ prāpte'hipatāvanante māyayā rohiṇīṃ nīte | sacciditi saccidānandaikarasaḥ | he kṛṣṇa ! saḥ devakījaṭharasthaḥ || 10 ||
iti kṛṣṇāvatāraprasaṅgavarṇanaṃ saptatriṃśaṃ daśakam |
ānandarūpa! bhagavannayi! te'vatāre
prāpte pradīptabhavadaṅga nirīyamāṇaiḥ |
kāntitrajairiva ghanāghanamaṇḍalairdhāmāvṛṇvatī viruruce kila varṣavelā || 1 ||
ānandeti | pradīptāt prakāśojjvalād bhavadaṅgānnirīyamāṇairbahirnirgacchadbhiḥ || 1 ||
āśāsu śītalatarāsu payodatoyeṃrāśāsitāptivivaśeṣu ca sajjaneṣu |
naiśākarodayavidhau niśi madhyamāyāṃ
lkeśāpahastrijagatāṃ tvamihāvirāsīḥ || 2 ||
āśāsviti | āśāsitasyābhīṣṭārthasyāptyā lābhena vivaśeṣu prītiveṃgenāviditetikartavyatākeṣu | madhyamāyāṃ niśi ardharātre || 2 ||
vālyaspṛśāpi vapuṣā dadhuṣā vibhūtīrughatkirīṭakaṭakāṅgadahārabhāsā |
śaṅkhārivārijagadāparibhāsitena
meghāsitena parilesitha sūtigehe || 3 ||
bālyeti | bālyaṃ spṛśati tena sambadhyata iti bālyaspṛk | vibhūtīrīśva'īṅ gatau' divādiḥ |
ratvadyotakāni dadhuṣā dhṛtavatā | tadevāha -- udyaditi | udyantyaḥ kirīṭādīnāṃ
bhāso yasminniti vapurviśeṣaṇam | parilesitha śuśubhiṣe || 3 ||
vakṣaḥsthalīsukhanilīnavilāsilakṣmīmandākṣalakṣitakaṭākṣavimokṣabhedaiḥ |
tanmandirasya khalakaṃsakṛtāmalakṣmīmunmārjayanniva virejitha vāsudeva ! || 4 ||
vakṣaḥsthalīti | vakṣaḥsthalīsukhanilīnāyā nijakamiturmūrtyantaraparigraheṇa vilāsinyā navīkṛtapremaprakarṣadyotakaceṣṭāviśeṣayuktāyā lakṣmyā mandākṣeṇa lākṣatairupalakṣitairlajjāmanthairarityarthaḥ || 4 ||
śauristu dhīramunimaṇḍalacetaso'pi dūrasthitaṃ vapurudīkṣya nijekṣaṇābhyām |
ānandabāṣpapulakodgamagadgadārdrastuṣṭāva dṛṣṭimakarandarasaṃ bhavantam || 5 ||
śauririti | dhīrasya sādhanacatuṣṭayasampannasya munimaṇḍalasya cetasaḥ samādheḥ sakāśādapi dūre sthitam ānandabāppeṇa pulakodgamena gadgadena cārdro militaḥ san||
deva! prasīda parapūruṣa ! tāpavallīnirlūnidātra ! samanetra ! kalāvilāsin! |
khedānapākuru kṛpāgurubhiḥ kaṭākṣairityādi tena muditena ciraṃ nuto'bhūḥ || 6 ||
deveti | parapūruṣa ! paramātman ! tāpavallyā nilūrnau mūlacchede dātra ! atitīkṣṇaśastrabhūta ! samanetra ! sarvaniyantaḥ ! kalayā svāṃśabhūtayā māyayā jagatsṛṣṭyādikrīḍāśīla !, athavā tāpavallyā nirlanau dātrasamo netrakalāyāḥ kaṭākṣasya vilāso'syāstīti tatheti || 6 ||
mātrā ca netrasalilāstutagātravallayā
stotrairabhiṣṭutaguṇaḥ karuṇālayastvam |
prācīnajanmayugalaṃ pratibodhya tābhyāṃ
mātugiṃrā dadhitha mānuṣavālaveṣam || 7 ||
mātreti | prācīnajanmayugalaṃ prāg yuvayoḥ pṛśnisutapasoḥ putratayā pṛśnigarbhākhyayāhaṃ jātaḥ, punaścāditikāśyapayorvāmanākhyatheti pratibodhya || 7 ||
tvatpreristatadanu nandatanūjayā te
vyatyāsamāracayituṃ sa hi śūrasunuḥ |
tvāṃ hastayoradhita cittavidhāryamāryairambhoruhasthakalahaṃsakiśoraramyam || 8 ||
tvāditi | kaṃsabhrāntyarthaṃ māṃ yaśodāśayane nyasya tatsutāṃ manmātuḥ śayane kurviti tvayā preritaḥ saḥ āryairmunibhiścittena vidhāryaṃ dhyeyam || 8 ||
jātā tadā paśupasadmani yoganidrā
nidrāvimudritamathākṛta pauralokam |
tvatpreraṇāt kimiha citramacetanairyad
dvāraiḥ svayaṃ vyaghaṭi saṅghaṭitaiḥ sugāḍham || 9 ||
jāteti | tadā tvatprasthānārambhe sati yoganidrā māyā bhagavatī paśupasya nandagopasya sadmani yaśodāyāṃ jātāvatīrṇā sā tvatpreraṇāt pauralokaṃ mathurāpuravāsinaṃ janaṃ nidrāvimudritaṃ niśceṣṭamakṛta | kimiva citramiti | aghaṭamānaghaṭanāpaṭīyasyāstvatpreraṇād dvāravighaṭanakaraṇādi na citramityarthaḥ || 9 ||
śeṣeṇa bhūriphaṇavāritavāriṇātha
svairaṃ pradarśitapatho māṇadīpitena |
tvāṃ dhārayan sa khalu dhanyatamaḥ pratasthe
so'yaṃ tvamīśa! mama nāśaya rogavegān || 10 ||
śeṣeṇeti | bhūribhiḥ phaṇairvāritaṃ vāri varṣabindavo yena tena śeṣeṇānantena phaṇāmaṇidīpitenāta eva svairaṃ pradarśitaḥ panthā mārgo yasya || 10 ||
iti kṛṣṇāvatāravarṇanaṃ kṛṣṇasya gokulanayanavarṇanaṃ ca
aṣṭātriṃśaṃ daśakam |
bhavantamayamudvahan yadukuloho nissaran
dadarśa gaganoccalajjalabharāṃ kalindātmajām |
aho salilasañcayaḥ sa punaraindrajālodito
jalaugha iva tatkṣaṇāt prapadameyatāmāyayauṃ || 1 ||
bhavantamiti | nissaran gacchan| gaganoccalajjalabharāṃ vīcījālaigaganatalabilaṅghijalaughapūrṇām || 1 ||
prasuptapaśupālikāṃ nibhṛtamārūdadvālikāmapātakavāṭikāṃ paśupavāṭikāmāviśan |
bhavantamayamarpayan prasavatalpake tatpadād
vahan kapaṭakanyakāṃ svapuramāgato vegataḥ || 2 ||
prasupteti | prasuptāḥ paśupālikā yasyāṃ nibhṛtaṃ yaśodāsūtigehajñāpanārthaṃ mandaṃ mandamārudatī bālikā yasyām, apāvṛtā vighaṭitāḥ kavāṭikā yasyāṃ, tāṃ paśupavāṭikāṃ nandagopagṛhamāviśannayaṃ vasudevo yaśodāyāḥ prasavatalpake bhavantamarpayan tatpadāt tasmāt sthānāt || 2 ||
tatastvadanujāravakṣapitanidravegadravadbhaṭotkaraniveditamasabavārtayaivārtimān |
vimuktacikurotkarastvaritamāpatana bhojarāḍatuṣṭa iva dṛṣṭavān bhaginikākare kanyakām || 3 ||
tata iti | tvadanujāyā yaśodākanyāyā ruditaraveṇa kṣapitanidravegaiḥ prabuddhairdravadbhirdhāvadbhirbhaṭotkarai rakṣipuruṣairniveditayā, devakyā aṣṭamaḥ putro jāta iti prasavavārtayaivārtimān maraṇabhayayuktaḥ kathamiyaṃ kanyakā bhavediti bahuvidhacintākulatayātuṣṭa iva || 3 ||
dhruvaṃ kapaṭaśālino madhuharasya māyā bhavedasāviti kiśorikāṃ bhaginikākarāliṅitām |
dvipo nalinikāntarādiva mṛṇālikāmākṣipannayaṃ tvadanujāmajāmupalapaṭṭake piṣṭavān || 4 ||
dhruvamiti | madhuharasya madhusūdanasya | ajāṃ māyām || 4 ||
tato bhavadupāsako jhaṭiti mṛtyupāśādiva
pramucya tarasaiva sā samadhirūḍharūpāntarā |
adhastalamajagmuṣī vikasadaṣṭavāhusphuranmahāyudhamaho gatā kila vihāyasā dighute || 5 ||
tata iti | tataḥ pramucya kaṃsakarād vigalitā vikasantyaṣṭabāhusphuranmahāyudhāni yasminniti kriyāviśeṣaṇam | vihāyasā mārgeṇa gatā dighute śuśubhe || 5 ||
nṛśaṃsatara! kaṃsa ! te kimu mayā viniṣpiṣṭayā
babhūva bhavadantakaḥ kvacana cintyatāṃ te hitam |
iti tvadanujā vibho ! khalamudīrya taṃ jagmuṣī
marudgaṇapaṇāyitā bhuvi ca mandirāṇyeyuṣī || 6 ||
nṛśaṃseti | nṛśaṃsatara! atiduṣkarmakārin ! ! taṃ khalaṃ kaṃsamudīryoktvā marukṣapitanidrairvaigena dravadbhiśceti tu yuktam |
dgaṇaiḥ paṇāyitā stutā jagmuṣī gatavatī bhuvi mandirāṇyeyuṣī nṛṇāmanugrahāya
tattatsthānābhimānitayā sthitavatī || 6 ||
prage punaragātmajāvacanamīritā bhūbhujā
pralambabakapūtanāpramukhadānavā māninaḥ |
bhavannidhanakāmyayā jagati vabhramunirbhayāḥ
kumārakavimārakāḥ kimiva duṣkaraṃ niṣkṛpaiḥ || 7 ||
praga iti | punaḥ parechuḥ prage'harmukhe bhūbhujā kaṃsena pralambādyā agātmajāyā devyā vacanamīritā uktāḥ || 7 ||
tataḥ paśupamandire tvayi mukunda ! nandapriyāprasūtiśayaneśaye ruvati kiñcidañcatpade |
vibudhya vanitājanaistanayasambhave ghoṣite
mudā kimu vadāmyaho sakalamākulaṃ gokulam || 8 ||
tata iti | tataḥ paśupamandire nandagehe prasūtiśayane śeta iti prasūtiśayaneśayastasmin kiñcidañcatpade calitālpakamṛdulāṅghripallave tvayi ruvati rudati sati || 8 ||
aho khalu yaśodayā navakalāyacetoharaṃ
bhavantamalamantike prathamamāpibantyā dṛśā |
punaḥ stanabharaṃ nijaṃ sapadi pāyayantyā mudā
manoharatanuspṛśā jagati puṇyavanto jitāḥ || 9 ||
aho iti| yaśodayā bhavadarśanasparśanastanadānādinā puṇyavanto munayo'pi jitā adhaḥkṛtāḥ khalu aho || 9 ||
bhavatkuśalakāmyayā sa khalu nandagopastadā
pramodabharasaṃkulo dvijakulāya kiṃ nādadāt |
tathaiva paśupālakāḥ kimu na maṅgalaṃ tenire
jagatritayamaṅgala ! tvamiha pāhi māmāmayāt || 10 ||
bhavaditi | bhavataḥ kuśalakāmyayābhyudayecchayā || 10 ||
iti yogamāyānayanādivarṇanam ekonacatvāriṃśaṃ daśakam |
tadanu nandamamandaśubhāspadaṃ nṛpapurīṃ karadānakṛte gatam |
samavalokya jagāda bhavatpitā viditakaṃsasahāyajanodyamaḥ || 1 ||
tadanviti | amandaśubhāspadaṃ lokottaraguṇākaraṃ nandaṃ karadānakṛte yadurājāya vārṣikaṣaṣṭhāṃśadānārthaṃ gataṃ mathurāpurīstho bhavatpitā vasudevo viditaḥ kaṃsasahāyajanānāṃ pūtanātṝṇāvartādīnāmudyamaḥ sakalabālahananotsāho yena || 1 ||
ayi sakhe! tava vālakajanma māṃ sukhayate'dya nijātmajajanmavat |
iti bhavatpitṛtāṃ vrajanāyake samadhirogya śaśaṃsa tamādarāt || 2 ||
ayīti | vasudevo nandaṃ prati tvadnehastho bālo matputra iti novāca, kintu vrajanāyake nande bhavatpitṛtāmāropya tamādarācchaśaṃsa | svaputravat sāvadhānaṃ rakṣitumetaditi jñātavyam || 2 ||
iha ca santyanimittaśatāni te kaṭakasīmni tato laghu gamyatām |
iti ca tadvacasā vrajanāyako bhavadapāyabhiyā drutamāyayau || 3 ||
iheti | animittaśatāni durnimittāni | kaṭakasīni vraje || 3 ||
nandasya bajāgamanāt pūrvaṃ lalitaveṣāyāḥ pūtanāyā brajapraveśamāha -- 
avasare khalu tatra ca kācana vrajapade madhurākṛtiraṅganā |
taralaṣadpadalālitakuntalā kapaṭapotaka! te nikaṭaṃ gatā || 4 ||
sapadi sā hṛtavālakacetanā niśicarānvayajā kila pūtanā |
vrajavadhaṣviha keyamiti kṣaṇaṃ vimṛśatīṣu bhavantamupādade || 5 ||
sapadīti | hṛtavālakacetanā bālaghātinītyarthaḥ || 5 ||
lalitabhāvavilāsahṛtātmabhiryuvatibhiḥ pratiroddhumapāritā |
stanamasau bhavanāntaniṣeduṣī pradaduṣī bhavate kapaṭātmane || 6 ||
laliteti | yuvatibhirgopībhiḥ pratiroddhuṃ laṅghayitumapāritāśakyā satī bhavanānte niṣeduṣī upaviṣṭā pradaduṣī dattavatī | kapaṭa ātmā mano mūrtirvā yasya || 6 ||
samadhiruhya tadaṅkamaśaṅkitastvamatha bālakalopanaroṣitaḥ |
mahadivāmraphalaṃ kucamaṇḍalaṃ praticucūṣitha durviṣadūṣitam || 7 ||
samadhiruhyeti | bālakānāṃ lopanena māraṇena roṣitaḥ praticucūṣitha āmlād vakre niveśyauṣṭhābhyāmavapīḍya bahuśo niṣṭhīvanaṃ kṛtavān || 7 ||
asubhireva samaṃ dhayati tvayi stanamasau stanitopamanisvanā |
nirapatad bhayadāyi nijaṃ vapuḥ pratigatā pravisārya bhujāvubhau || 8 ||
asubhiriti | asubhiḥ prāṇaiḥ samaṃ saha dhayati pibati satyasau pūtanā stanitopamo meghadhvanisadṛśo nisvana ākranditaṃ yasyāḥ sā tathā || 8 ||
bhayadaghoṣaṇabhīṣaṇavigrahaśravaṇadarśanamohitavallave |
vrajapade taduraḥsthalakhelanaṃ nanu bhavantamagṛhṇata gopikāḥ || 9 ||
bhayadeti | bhayadadghoṣaṇasya bhīṣaṇavigrahasya ca śravaṇena darśanena ca mohitā vallavā gopā yatra || 9 ||
bhruvanamaṅgala ! nāmabhireva te yuvatibhirbahudhā kṛtarakṣaṇaḥ |
tvamayi vātaniketananātha! māmagadayan kuru tāvakasevakam || 10 ||
bhuvaneti | agadayan nīrogaṃ kurvan || 10 ||
iti vārṣikakaradānāya nandasya mathurāpraveśavarṇanaṃ pūtanāmokṣavarṇanaṃ ca
catvāriṃśaṃ daśakam |
vrajeśvaraḥ śaurivaco niśamya samāvrajannadhvani bhītacetāḥ |
niṣpiṣṭaniśśeṣataruṃ nirīkṣya kañcit padārtha śaraṇaṃ gatastvām || 1 ||
vrajeti | śaurivaca 'iha ca santyanimittaśatāni' iti | samāvrajan svagṛhamāgacchan nippiṣṭāḥ patanajavena patitāścūrṇīkṛtāśca niśśeṣataravo gavyūtyantarasthadrumā yena taṃ pūtanādehaṃ kañcit padārthamatimahattvādadṛṣṭapūrvatayā ca kimidamiti jñātumaśakyam etādṛśasaṅkaṭe  bhraman bālakaḥ kveti paravaśastvadrakṣaṇāya tvāmeva śaraṇaṃ gataḥ rakṣitṛtvenāśritavān || 1 ||
niśamya gopīvacanādudantaṃ sarve'pi gopā bhayavismayāndhāḥ |
tvatpātitaṃ ghorapiśācadehaṃ dehurvidūre'tha kuṭhārakṛttam || 2 ||
niśamyeti | udantaṃ pūtanāvṛttāntam | ghorarūpadarśanād bhayaṃ, tvayā pātitamiti vismayaḥ | atha vidūre kuṭhāraiḥ khaṇḍaśaḥ kṛtvā bahubhirdūraṃ nītvā dehurbhasmīcakruḥ || 2 ||
tvatpītatastanataccharīrāta samuccalacuccataro hi dhūmaḥ |
śaṅkāmadhādāgaravaḥ kimeṣa kiṃ cāndano gauggulavo'thaveti || 3 ||
tvaditi | tvatpītatvāt pūtaṃ rajastamovilaye śuddhasattvamayaḥ stano yasmistasmāt taccharīrāditi, tatstanādhiṣṭhānatayā bhagavatastadaṅkārohaṇāñca śuddhasattvamayatāmāpannāt pūtanāśavaśarīrādityarthaḥ | ata eva sattvakāryatvād dhūmasyāgaravādiśaṅkā || 3 ||
madaṅgasaṅgasya phalaṃ na dūre kṣaṇena tāvad bhavatāmapi syāt |
ityullṛpan vallavatalajebhyastvaṃ pūtanāmātanuthāḥ sugandhim || 4 ||
maditi | mama brahmamayasthāṅkāropaṇalālanādau yo'ṅgasaṅgastasya phalaṃ muktirna dūre janmāntare, yathā māṃ hantumicchantyā asyāḥ piśācyāḥ | bhavatāṃ madyogakṣemaikacittānāṃ kṣaṇena | vallavatallajebhyo bhāgyavattayā praśastebhyo gopālakebhyaḥ ityullapan iti samyag bodhanārthameva tvaṃ pūtanāṃ sugandhimātanuthāḥ || 4 ||
citraṃ piśācyā na hataḥ kumāraścitraṃ puraivākathi śauriṇedam |
iti praśaṃsan kila gopaloko bhavanmukhālokarase nyamāṅkṣīt || 5 ||
citramiti | puraiva 'iha ca santyanimittaśatānī' tyādinedaṃ bhayaṃ śauriṇā vasudevenākathi kathitamiti śauriṃ praśaṃsan bhavanmukhāloke rasa ānandastasmin nyamāṅkṣīd nimagno'bhūt || 5 ||
dine dine'tha prativṛddhalakṣmīrakṣīṇamaṅgalyaśato vrajo'yam |
bhavannivāsādayi vāsudeva! pramodasāndraḥ parito vireje || 6 ||
dine dina iti | vrajo vrajajanaḥ paritaḥ pramodasāndra iti sthiracarāṇāmapyānandātiśaya uktaḥ || 6 ||
gṛheṣu te komalarūpahāsamithaḥkathāsaṅkulitāḥ kamanyaḥ |
vṛtteṣu kṛtyeṣu bhavannirīkṣāsamāgatāḥ pratyahamatyanandan || 7 ||
gṛheṣviti | komalayo rūpahāsayormithaḥkathayā saṅkulitāḥ savyāpārāḥ | vṛtteṣvavasiteṣu || 7 ||
aho kumāro mayi dattadṛṣṭiḥ smitaṃ kṛtaṃ māṃ prati vatsakena |
ehyehi māmityupasārya pāṇiṃ tvayīśa! kiṃ kiṃ na kṛtaṃ vadhūbhiḥ || 8 ||
aho iti | he īśa ! viṣṇo ! tvayi gopabālarūpiṇi vadhūbhiraṅkāropaṇākṣleṣacumbanalālanādi kiṃ kiṃ na kṛtam, aho bhāgyavatyantā iti bhāvaḥ || 8 ||
bhavadvapuḥ sparśanakautukena karāta karaṃ gopavadhūjanena |
nītastvamātāmrasarojamālāvyālambilolamvatulāmalāsīḥ || 9 ||
bhavaditi | vadhūjanapāṇiparamparāyā aruṇasarojamālāyamānatayā tava ca taddhyālambipratinavamadhulubdhalolambadeśīyatayā tattulāmalāsīḥ kalitavānasītyarthaḥ ||
nipāyayantī stanamaṅkagaṃ tvāṃ vilokayantī vadanaṃ hasantī |
daśāṃ yaśodā katamāṃ na bheje sa tādṛśaḥ pāhi hare ! gānmām || 10 ||
nipāyayantīti | yaśodā katamāṃ kīdṛśīṃ daśāṃ vismayautsukyāpāyaśaṅkādaivaprārthanavaivaśyādirūpāmavasthāṃ na bheje prāptavatī || 10 ||
iti pūtanāśarīradāhavarṇanaṃ bālakalālanena yaśodādīnāṃ niratiśayānandavarṇanaṃ ca
ekacatvāriṃśaṃ daśakam |
kadāpi janmarkṣadine taba prabho! nimaṃntritajñātivadhūmahīsurā |
mahānasastvāṃ savidhe nidhāya sā mahānasāḍhau vavṛte vrajeśvarī || 1 ||
kadāpīti | nimantritā āmantritā jñātayastadvadhvo mahīsurāśca yayā | mahato'nasaḥ śakaṭasya | mahānasādau pākagehādau vavṛte pravṛttābhūt || 1 ||
tato bhavattrāṇaniyuktabālakaprabhītisaṅkrandanasaṅkulāravaiḥ |
vimiśramaśrāvi bhavatsamīpataḥ parisphuṭaddārucaṭaccaṭāravaḥ || 2 ||
alāsīriti 'lā ādāne' ityato luṅ |
tata iti | bhavataḥ  tava bālakasya trāṇe rakṣaṇe yaśodayā niyuktānāṃ bālakānāṃ śakaṭapatanacaṭacaṭāśabdotthayā prabhītyā saṃkrandanasyākrandanasya bahubhiḥ kṛtatvāt saṅkulāravaiḥ pravṛddhairāravairvimiśraṃ yathā bhavati tathā parisphuṭatāṃ vighaṭitasandhibandhānāṃ dārūṇāmanovayavānāmāravo'śrāvi yaśodādibhiḥ śrūyate sma || 2 ||
tatastadākarṇanasaṃbhramaśramaprakampivakṣojabharā vajāṅganāḥ |
bhavantamantardadṛśuḥ samantato viniṣpataddāruṇadārumadhyagam || 3 ||
tata iti | tatastadākarṇanena prāgvat kimidamiti sambhrameṇa svasvagehānnandagehaṃ prati dhāvanapariśrameṇa ca prakampinau vakṣojabharau yāsāṃ tāḥ antarbhavanānte samantataḥ parito vinippatatāṃ dāruṇānāṃ gurutarāṇāṃ dārūṇāmakṣakūbarādyanovayavānāṃ madhyagaṃ dadṛśuḥ || 3 ||
śiśoraho kiṃ kimabhūditi drutaṃ pradhāvya nandaḥ paśupāśca bhūsurāḥ |
bhavantamālokya yaśodayā dhṛtaṃ samāśvasannajalārdralocanāḥ || 4 ||
śiśoriti | nandādayaḥ pradhāvya vegādāgatya yaśodayā dhṛtamuddhṛtamavalokya samāśvasan āśvāsaṃ kṛtavantaḥ || 4 ||
kasko nu kautaskuta eṣa vismayo viśaṅkaṭaṃ yacchakaṭaṃ vipāṭitam |
na kāraṇaṃ kiñcidiheti te sthitāḥ svanāsikādattakarāstvadīkṣakāḥ ||5||
kaska iti | kasko nu kautaskutaḥ kaḥ kaḥ kīdṛśaḥ kutaḥ kutaḥ kasmāt kāraṇādāgataḥ | sambhramād dviruktiḥ | viśaṅkaṭaṃ vistṛtaṃ vipāṭitaṃ kenāpi bhinnāvayavaṃ kṛtam | iha na kiñcit kāraṇaṃ, yato'smaraṇīyaivaṃvidhasāmarthyo bālakaḥ svanāsikādattakarā iti vismayānubhāvaḥ || 5 ||
kumārakasyāsya payodharārthinaḥ prarodane lolapadāmbujāhatam |
mayā mayā dṛṣṭamano viparyagāditīśa ! te pālakabālakā jaguḥ || 6 ||
kumārakasyeti | kumārakasya padāmbujābhyāmāhatamano viparyagād vyatyastāvayavamabhūdū, mayā dṛṣṭaṃ mayāpi dṛṣṭamiti bālakā jaguḥ || 6 ||
bhiyā tadā kiñcidajānatāmidaṃ kumārakāṇāmatidurghaṭaṃ vacaḥ |
bhavatprabhāvāvidurairitīritaṃ manāgivāśaṅkayata dṛṣṭapūtanaiḥ || 7||
bhiyeti | idaṃ vaco'tidurghaṭamatyantānupapannam | atra hetuḥ -- kumārakāṇāṃ bālānāṃ viśeṣatastadā bhiyā kiñcidapyajānatāmiti | bhavataḥ prabhāvaḥ śaktistadaviduraistadanabhijñaiḥ kaiściditīritamuktam | dṛṣṭapūtanairnandopanandādibhirmanāgīṣat tvatprabhāvādidamitthamityāśaṅkayataiva || 7 ||
pravālatāmraṃ kimidaṃ padaṃ kṣataṃ sarojaramyau nu karau virojitau |
iti prasarpatkaruṇātaraṅgitāstvadaṅgamāpaspṛśuraṅganājanāḥ || 8 ||
pravāleti | padaṃ kṣataṃ vraṇitaṃ kiṃ, karau virojitau bhagnau nviti vadantyaḥ prasarpantyā karuṇayā tatkāryasantatāśrudhārāpanayanapravṛttyā taraṅgitāścalitā aṅganā gopyastvadaṅgaṃ mandaṃ mandamāpaspṛśuḥ || 8 ||
aye sutaṃ dehi jagatpateḥ kṛpātaraṅgapātāt paripātamadya me |
iti sma sagṛhya pitā tvadaṅgakaṃ muhurmuhuḥ kṣliṣyati jātakaṇṭakaḥ || 9 ||
aye iti | apāyaniścaye satyatathādarśanādaye ityatarkitopanate | jagatpaterviṣṇoḥ kṛpātaraṅgapātāt karuṇālavaprāpteradya paripātaṃ rakṣitaṃ me sutaṃ dehīti pitā nandaḥ kṣliṣyati sma || 9 ||
anonilīnaḥ kila hantumāgataḥ surārirevaṃ bhavatā vihiṃsitaḥ |
rajo'pi no dṛṣṭamamuṣya tat kathaṃ sa śuddhasattve tvayi līnavān dhruvam ||
anonilīna iti | anonilīnastadrūpaḥ | nanu vihiṃsita ityuktaṃ, tat
katham amuṣya śakaṭāsurasya rajo'pi rajomātratayāpi śarīraṃ no dṛṣṭamityāśaṅkaya ājñātamityāha -- sa iti | sa daityaḥ saśarīra eva tvayi nilanivān dhruvaṃ niścinomi | rajo'pi no dṛṣṭamatastamasaḥ kā kathā | rajastamovilaye ca tvayi līnavān ityuktiryuktimatītyarthaḥ || 10 ||
prapūjitaistatra tato dvijātibhirviśeṣato lambhitamaṅgalāśiṣaḥ |
vrajaṃ nijairvālyarasairvimohayan marutpurādhīśa ! rujāṃ jahīhi me || 11 ||
prapūjitairiti | bhaviṣyadapāyaśaṅkayā viśeṣato lambhitāḥ prāpitā maṅgalarūpā āśiṣo'nugrahā yasya sa tvaṃ me rujāṃ rogaṃ jahīhi tyaja || 11 ||
iti śakaṭāsuravadhavarṇanaṃ dvicatvāriṃśaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
tvāmekadā gurumarutpuranātha! voḍhuṃ
gāḍhādhirūḍhagarimāṇamapārayantī |
mātā nidhāya śayane kimidaṃ bateti
dhyāyantyaceṣṭata gṛheṣu niviṣṭaśaṅkā || 1 ||
tvāmiti | tvāṃ gāḍhādhirūḍhagarimāṇamatiśayena prāptagurutvaṃ voḍhum aṅkenoddhartumapārayantyaśaknuvatī mātā yaśodā śayane nidhāya kimidaṃ bateti niviṣṭā śaṅkā yasyāṃ sā tvāṃ dhyāyantī gṛheṣvaceṣṭata || 1 ||
tāvad vidūramupakarṇitaghoraghoṣavyājṛmbhipāṃsupaṭalīparipūritāśaḥ |
vātyāvapuḥ sa kila daityavarastṛṇāvartākhyo jahāra janamānasahāriṇaṃ tvām || 2 ||
" ‘ac' iti yogavibhāgād acpratyayaḥ samāsāntaḥ padmanābhavata | āśiṣaśabda eva vā svārthāṇantaḥ samāse ghaṭitaḥ prajñāderākṛtigaṇatvāt |
tāvaditi | vidūraṃ dūrādevopakarṇitena ghoraghoṣeṇa vyājṛmbhinyā pravṛddhayā pāṃsupaṭalyā ca paripūritā daśāśā yena | vātyā vātasamūhastadvapustṛṇāvartākhyo janānāṃ mānasahārī tvaṃ, taṃ tvāmeva jahāra || 2 ||
uddāmapāṃsutimirāhatadṛṣṭipāte
draṣṭuṃ kimapyakuśale paśupālaloke |
hā bālakasya kimiti tvadupāntamāptā
mātā bhavantamavilokya bhṛśaṃ ruroda || 3 ||
uddāmeti | uddāmābhyāṃ pāṃsutimirābhyāmāhato dṛṣṭipāto yasya | ataḥ kimapi draṣṭumakuśale'samarthe bālakasya kiṃ jātamiti || 3 ||
tāvat sa dānavavaro'pi ca dīnamūrtirbhāvakabhāraparidhāraṇalūnavegaḥ |
saṅkocamāpa tadnu kṣatapāṃsughoṣe
ghoṣe vyatāyata bhavajjananīninādaḥ || 4 ||
tāvaditi | dīnamūrtiḥ kṣīṇaśarīraḥ | bhāvatko bhavadīyo bhāro gurutvaṃ tasya paridhāraṇena lūnavego naṣṭagamanavegaḥ saṅkocaṃ niśceṣṭatāmāpa | tadanu anantaraṃ kṣatā naṣṭāḥ pāṃsavo ghoṣaśca yasmin tasmin ghoṣe vraje vyatāyata vyāpto'bhūt || 4 ||
rodopakarṇanavaśādupagamya gehūṃ
krandatsu nandamukhagopakuleṣu dīnaḥ |
tvāṃ dānavastvakhilamuktikaraṃ mumukṣustvayyapramuñcati papāta viyapradeśāt || 5 ||
rodeti| yaśodāyā rodopakarṇanavaśāt tasyā gehamupagamya | kaṇṭhe bāhubhyāṃ baddhaṃ tvāṃ mumukṣuḥ | dīnaḥ kṣīṇajīvitaḥ || 5 ||
rodākulāstadanu gopagaṇā bahiṣṭapāṣāṇapṛṣṭhabhuvi dehamatisthaviṣṭham |
praikṣanta hanta nipatantamamuṣya vakṣasyakṣīṇameva ca bhavantamalaṃ isantam || 6 ||
rodeti | tadanu nipatantamatisthaviṣṭhaṃ sthūlataraṃ tṛṇāvartasya deham amuṣya vakṣasi nipatantaṃ bhavantaṃ ca praikṣanta, yato'laṃ hasantam ato'kṣīṇameva ca praikṣantetyarthaḥ || 6 ||
grāvaprapātaparipiṣṭagariṣṭhadehabhraṣṭāsuduṣṭadanujopari dhṛṣṭahāsam |
āghnānamambujakareṇa bhavantametya
gopā dadhurgirivarādiva nīlaratnam || 7 ||
grāveti | grāvaprapātena pāṣāṇapṛṣṭhanipatanena paripiṣṭaścūrṇāṃkṛto gariṣṭho yo dehastasmād bhraṣṭā asavo yasya tasya duṣṭadanujasyopari ambujakareṇāghnānaṃ mayā hato'yamiti diviṣṭhān grāhayituṃ nighnantamiva calitakarāmbujam ata eva dhṛṣṭahāsaṃ kapaṭahāsayuktaṃ gopā dadhuruddhṛtavantaḥ || 7 ||
ekaikamāśu parigṛhya nikāmanandannandādigopaparirabdhavicumbitāṅgam |
ādātukāmapariśaṅgitagopanārīhastāmbujaprapatitaṃ praṇumo bhavantam || 8 ||
ekaikamiti | ekaikaṃ mṛdukaracaraṇādikaṃ parigṛhya nikāmamatiśayena nandadbhirmodamānairnandādibhiḥ parivārya yugapat parirabdhāni vicumbitāni cāṅgāni yasya | ādātukāmeti māmādātukāmeyamiti pariśaṅkitānāṃ gopanārīṇāṃ hastāmbujeṣu prapatitaṃ hastaprasāraṇāt pūrvameva nipatya tā ākulīkurvantam || 8 ||
bhūyo'pi kinnu kṛṇumaḥ praṇatārtihārī
govinda eva paripālayatāt sutaṃ naḥ |
kurma ityarthaḥ | ‘kṛvi hiṃsākaraṇayośca' |
ityādi mātarapitṛpramukhaistadānīṃ
samprārthitastvadavanāya vibho ! tvameva || 9 ||
bhūya iti | bhūyo'pyevaṃvidhasaṅkaṭeṣu govindo viṣṇureva naḥ sutaṃ paripālayatāt || 9 ||
vātātmakaṃ danujamevamayi pradhūnvan
vātodbhavān mama gaḍhān kimu no dhunoṣi |
kiṃ vā karomi punarapyanilālayeśa !
niśśeṣarogaśamanaṃ muhurarthaye tvām || 10 ||
vāteti | vātātmakaṃ tṛṇāvarte pradhūnvan nigṛhṇan he anilālayeśa ! tava vātālayasthatve vātanigrāhakatve ca satyapi mama vātodbhavān gadān kimu no dhunoṣi matprārabdhakarmadoṣāditi cet kiṃ vā karomi | api vā punaḥ prārabdhāvasāne sthūlasūkṣmadehadvayapātena niḥśeṣāṇāṃ bāhyānāṃ vātādīnāmāntarāṇāṃ rāgādīnāmapi rogāṇāṃ śamanaṃ mokṣamapyarthaye prārthayāmi || 10 ||
iti tṛṇāvartavadhavarṇanaṃ trayaścatvāriṃśaṃ daśakam |
atha kṣlokadaśakena gargasya nāmakaraṇajātakarmākhyāne darśayati -- 
gūḍhaṃ vasudevagirā kartuṃ te niṣkriyasya saṃskārān |
hṛdratahorātattvo gargamunistvadgṛ hān vibho ! gatavān || 1 ||
gūḍhamiti | paramārthato niṣkriyasyāpi manuṣyadṛṣṭyā kartavyān saṃskārān nāmakaraṇādīn hṛdgatahorātattvaḥ samyag viditajyotiśśāstrayāthārthyaḥ || 1 ||
nando'tha nanditātmā bṛndiṣṭhaṃ mānayannamuṃ yaminām |
mandāsmitārdramṛce tvatsaṃskārān vidhātumutsukadhīḥ || 2 ||
nanda iti | nanditātmā santuṣṭaḥ yamināṃ munīnāṃ bṛndiṣṭhaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ mānayan yathāvidhi pūjayan || 2 ||
yaduvaṃśācāryatvāt sunibhṛtamidamārya ! kāryamiti kathayan |
gargo nirgatapulakakṣcakre tava sāgrajasya nāmāni || 3 ||
yadviti | mama yaduvaṃśācāryatvānmayā saṃskṛte tava putre kaṃsasya yadukumāraśaṅkā syāt | tatparihārāya sunibhṛtamatigūḍhamidaṃ kāryam | tvatsparśānnirgatapulakaḥ || 3 ||
kathamasya nāma kurve sahasranāmno 
hyanantanāmno vā |
iti nūnaṃ gargamuniścakre tava nāma nāma rahasi vibho ! || 4 ||
kathamiti | anantanāmnaḥ kathaṃ nāma kurve | kathaṃ kānicinnāmāni kṛtavāniti pṛṣṭaḥ kiṃ brūyāmiti viṣaṇṇo nāma munistava nāma rahasi cakre || 4 ||
kṛṣṇanāma nirvakti -- 
kṛṣidhātuṇakārābhyāṃ sattānandātmatāṃ kilābhilapat |
jagadadhakārṣitvaṃ vā kathayadṛṣiḥ kṛṣṇanāma te vyatanot || 5 ||
kṛṣīti | kṛṣidhātuḥ sattāvācakaḥ, ṇakāra ānandavācakaḥ, tayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyena kṛṣṇanāma sattānandātmatāmabhilapat parabrahmavācakaṃ bhavati | kilaśabdo'tra purāṇaprasiddhiṃ dyotayati | taduktaṃ -- 
"kṛṣirbhūvācakaḥ śabdo ṇaśca nirvṛtivācakaḥ |
tayoraikyaṃ paraṃ brahma kṛṣṇa ityabhidhīyate || "
iti | athavā jagatāmaghaṃ karṣatīti kṛṣṇa iti nirvacanamityāha -- jagaditi | kathayaditi nāmaviśeṣaṇam || 5 ||
anyāṃkṣca nāmabhedān vyākurvannagraje ca rāmādīn |
atimānuṣānubhāvaṃ nyagadat tvāmaprakāśayan pitre || 6 ||
anyāniti | anyāṃśca vāsudevādīn vyākurvannanvarthatayā kathayan prāk ` kadācid vasudevājñāto'yaṃ tava pūtraḥ | tasmād vāsudeva iti cāsya nāmasvu | kiñca mānuṣānatikramya vartata ityatimānuṣānubhāvaṃ tvāṃ vipṇumaprakāśayan jātakadvaṣṭayā pitre nandāya nyagadaduktavān || 6 ||
tadāha -- 
strihyati yastava putre muhyati sa na māyikaiḥ punaḥ śokaiḥ |
druhyati yaḥ sa tu naśyedityavadat te mahattvamṛṣivaryaḥ || 7 ||
strihyatīti | yastava putre strihyati yaḥ kṛṣṇabhakto bhavati, sa māyikaiḥ ` śokairna mudyati na jananamaraṇādiduḥkhamanubhavati | tava putrāya druhyati yo yaḥ kṛṣṇād vimukho bhavati, sa tu naśyet saṃsārī syāditi gṛḍhārtharūpaṃ te mahattvamṛṣivaryo'vadadityarthaḥ || 7 ||
jeṣyati bahutaradaityān neṣyati nijavandhulokamamalapadam |
śroṣyasi suvimalakī rtīrasyeti bhavadvibhūtimṛṣirūce || 8 ||
jeṣyatīti | jamalapadaṃ niṣkalabrahna neṣyati prāpayiṣyati | asya tava putrasya suvimalāḥ pāpaharāḥ kīrtīrayaṃ lokaḥ kṣroṣyatīti bhavato vibhūtimaikṣvaryam || 8 ||
amunaiva sarvadurgaṃ taritāstha kṛtāsthamatra tiṣṭhadhvam |
harirevetyanabhilapannityādi tvāmavarṇayat sa muniḥ || 9 ||
amuneti | yūyamamunā putreṇaiva sarvadurgaṃ saṅkaṭajātaṃ taritāstha tariṣyatha | kṛtāsthaṃ kṛtādaraṃ yathā bhavati tathātrāsmin putre tiṣṭhadhvaṃ vikṣvastā bhavata gargamu| harirayamityevānabhilapan tvāmuddiśya ityādi kṣrīkṛṣṇāvatārakaryamakhilaṃ sa gargamuniravarṇayat || 9 ||
garge'tha nirgate'smin nanditanandādinandhamānastvam |
madgadamudgatakaruṇo nirgamaya śrīmarutpurādhīśa! || 10 ||
garga iti | nanditairmuditairnandādibhirnandyamāno lālyamāno mama gadaṃ rogaṃ nirgamaya nirākuru || 10 ||
iti kṛṣṇasya jātakarmanāmakaraṇavarṇanaṃ
catuścatvāriṃśaṃ daśakam |
ayi sabala! murāre! pāṇijānupracāraiḥ
kimapi bhavanabhāgān bhūṣayantau bhavantau |
calitacaraṇakañje mañjumañjīraśiñjāśravaṇakutukabhājī ceratukṣcāru vegāt || 1 ||
ayīti | pāṇijānupracāraiḥ pāṇidvayena jānudvathena ca kṛtaiścaṅgamaṇairbhavanabhāgānalindādipradeśān, ekatra ciramanavasthānāt kimapi kañcit kālaṃ bhūṣayantau calite caraṇakañje pādāmbuje mañjumañjīraśiñjāśravaṇakutukabhājau yataḥ, ato vegāñveratuḥ ||
mṛdu mṛdu vihasantāvunmiṣaddantavantau
vadanapatitakeśau dṛśyapādābjadeśau |
bhujagalitakarāntavyālagatkaṅkaṇāṅko
matimaharatamuccaiḥ paśyatāṃ viśvanṛṇām || 2 ||
mṛdviti | antarāntarā yatkiñcidavalokya mṛdu mṛdu vihasantāvata evonmiṣaṃddantavantau dṛśyamānakatipayadantamukulau | aṅghridvayākarṣaṇe dṛśyau dhvajavajrāghaṅkitatvena darśanīyau pādābjadeśau yayoḥ | bhujagaliteti bhujoparideśād galitatvānmaṇibandhalagnakaṅkaṇaiḥ sañjātaparabhāgau yuvāṃ viśveṣāṃ nṛṇāṃ matimuccairatiśayenāharataṃ jahathuḥ || 2 ||
anusarati janaudhe kautukavyākulākṣe
kimapi kṛtaninādaṃ vyāhasantau dravantau |
valitavadanapadmaṃ pṛṣṭhato dattadṛṣṭī
kimiva na vidadhāthe kautukaṃ vāsudeva ! || 3 ||
anviti | janaughe'nusarati sati dravantau vegena carantau tato dūrasthe janaughe valitavadanapadmaṃ tiryakkṛtamukhapadmaṃ yathā tathā pṛṣṭhato dattadṛṣṭī, kimiveti atiśayena kautukaṃ vidadhāthe ajanayatamityarthaḥ || 3 ||
drutagatiṣu patantāvṛtthitau liptapaṅkau
divi munibhirapaṅkaiḥ sasmitaṃ vandyamānau |
drutamatha jananībhyāṃ sānukampaṃ gṛhītau
muhurapi parirabdhau drāg yuvāṃ cumbitau ca || 4 ||
druteti | drutagatiṣu vegena sañcaraṇeṣu vrajakardame patantau divi draṣṭumāgatairmunibhirasmānapaṅkīkartuṃ samarthāvetau svayaṃ liptapaṅkau adhaḥpatanabhayena yāvāśrayāmastau svayaṃ patantāviti sasmitam, aho bhaktapāratantryamīśvarayoriti vandyamānau || 4 ||
snutakucabharamaṅke dhārayantī bhavantaṃ
taralamati yaśodā stanyadā dhanyadhanyā |
kapaṭapaśupa! madhye mugdhahāsāṅkuraṃ te
daśanamukulahṛdyaṃ vīkṣya vakraṃ jaharṣa || 5 ||
snuteti | snutaṃ prasṛtastanyaṃ kucayugaṃ yasmin, taralā kṛpākulā matiryasmin iti ca kriyāviśeṣaṇam | tathā bhavantamaṅke dhārayantī yā stanyaṃ dadāti, sā yaśodā dhanyadhanyā | he kapaṭapaśupa! stanapānamadhye te daśanamukulairhṛdyaṃ mugdho hāsāṅkuro mandasmitaṃ yasmin tad vakraṃ vīkṣya jaharṣa santuṣṭābhūt || 1 ||
tadanu caraṇacārī dārakaiḥ sākamārānnilayatatiṣu khelan bālacāpalyaśālī |
bhavanaśukabiḍālān vatsakāṃkṣcānudhāvan
kathamapi kṛtahāsairgopakairvārito'bhūḥ || 6 ||
tadanviti | ārāt samīpasthāsu nilayatatiṣu bhavanāntareṣu khelan gacchan || 6 ||
gopīnāṃ bhavadanusaraṇaikacittatayā gṛhakṛtyādiṣvapi vismṛtirjātatyāha -- 
haladharasahitastvaṃ yatra yatropayāto
vivaśapatitanetrāstatra tatraiva gopyaḥ |
vigalitagṛhakṛtyā vismṛtāpatyabhṛtyā
murahara ! muhuratyantākulā nityamāsan || 7 ||
gopīnāṃ navanītādipaṇena tvaṃ taddarśanārthaṃ nartanādyapi kṛtavānasītyāha -- 
pratinavanavanītaṃ gopikādattamicchan
kalapadamupagāyan komalaṃ kkāpi nṛtyan |
sadayayuvatilokairarpitaṃ sarpiraśnan
kkacana navavipakkaṃ dugdhamapyāpibastvam || 8 ||
mama khalu baligehe yācanaṃ jātamāstāmiha punarabalānāmagrato naiva kurve |
iti vihitamatiḥ kiṃ deva! santyajya yācyāṃ
dadhighṛtamaharastvaṃ cāruṇā coraṇena || 9 ||
mameti | mama baligehe prāg yācanaṃ jātam | tattu balerbalitvādeva nāsmākaṃ dainyāpādakamityāstāṃ khalvetat | iha tadviparītānāmabalānāmagratastanna kurve iti vihitamatirniścitacittaścāruṇā vicitropāyaracitena coraṇena steyakarmaṇā || 9 ||
tava dadhighṛtamoṣe ghoṣayoṣājanānāmabhajata hṛdi roṣo nāvakāśaṃ na śokaḥ |
hṛdayamapi muṣitvā harṣasindhau nyadhāstvaṃ
sa mama śamaya rogān vātagehādhinātha! || 10 ||
taveti | dadhighṛtayormope moṣaṇe ghoṣayopājanānāṃ gopastrīṇāṃ moṣake tvayi roṣo dravyanāśe śokaścāvakāśamāspadaṃ nābhajata | kasmādityatrāha -- hṛdayamiti  | muṣitvāpahṛtya | nanvapahṛtaṃ hṛdayaṃ yatra tatra roṣaśokau prāpnuyātāmeva | tatrāha -- harṣasindhāviti | yathā jalasthe kāṣṭhe jvalanapraveśo'śakyaḥ, tadvaddharṣasindhusthe hṛdaye tadviruddhayo roṣaśokayorapyanavakāśa iti bhāvaḥ || 10 ||
śākhāgre'tha vidhuṃ vilokya phalamityambāṃ ca tātaṃ muhuḥ
samprārthyātha tadā tadīyavacasā protkṣiptavāhau tvayi |
citraṃ deva! śaśī sa te karamagāt kiṃ brūmahe sampatajjyotirmaṇḍalapūritākhilavapuḥ prāgā virāḍrūpatām || 11 ||
kiṃ kiṃ batedamiti saṃbhramabhājamenaṃ
brahmārṇave kṣaṇamamuṃ parimajjya tātam |
māyāṃ punastanayamohamayīṃ vitanvannānandacinmaya! jaganmaya! pāhi rogāt || 12 ||
iti kṛṣṇasya vālakrīḍāvarṇanaṃ pañcacatvāriṃśaṃ daśakaṃ sadvikam |
ayi deva! purā kila tvayi svayamuttānaśaye stanandhaye |
parijṛmbhaṇato vyapāvṛte vadane viśvamacaṣṭa vallavī || 1 ||
ayīti | ayi deva! krīḍāpara ! purātiśaiśave svayaṃ sākṣādīśvare tvayi uttānaśaya ūrdhvamukhaśāyini | stanaṃ dhayati pibatīti stanandhayaḥ | parijṛmbhaṇato mukhavijṛmbhaṇadaśāyāṃ vyapāvṛte vidārite vadane āsye viśvamacaṣṭa dṛṣṭavatī | vallavī yaśodā || 1 ||
punarapyatha bālakaiḥ samaṃ tvayi līlānirate jagatpate! |
phalasañcayavañcanakrudhā tava mṛdbhojanamūcurarbhakāḥ || 2 ||
punariti | punarapi kiñcidvayotikramaṇe sati tvayi bālakaiḥ samaṃ saha || 2 ||
ayi te pralayāvadhau vibho ! kṣititoyādisamastabhakṣiṇaḥ |
mṛdupāśanato rujā bhavediti bhītā jananī cukopa sā || 3 ||
ayīti | pralaya evāvadhiḥ pralayāvadhiḥ | pratyahamiva pratipralayaṃ kṣityādisamastaṃ prapañcaṃ bhakṣayituṃ śīlamasyeti tathā tasya te mṛdupāśanataḥ kṣitilavamātrabhakṣaṇena rujā rogo bhavet || 3 ||
ayi durvinayātmaka ! tvayā kimu mṛtsā vata vatsa ! bhakṣitā |
iti mātṛgiraṃ ciraṃ vibho ! vitathāṃ tvaṃ pratijajñiṣe hasan || 4 ||
ayīti | vitathāmayarthāṃ pratijajñiṣe pratijñātavānasi || 4 ||
ayi te sakalairviniścite vimatiśced vadanaṃ vidāryatām | iti mātṛvibhatsiṃto mukhaṃ vikasatpadmanibhaṃ vyadārayaḥ || 5 ||
ayīti | sakalaistvatsakhibhistavāgrajena ca viniścite mṛddhojane tavaikasya vimatirvivādo'sti cet sandehahānāya vadanamāsyaṃ vidāryatāmiti mātrā vibharsito'dhikṣiptaḥ vikasatpadmanibhaṃ yathā bhavati tathā vyadārayaḥ || 5 ||
ayi mṛllavadarśanotsukāṃ jananīṃ tāṃ bahu tarpayanniva |
pṛthivīṃ nikhilāṃ na kevalaṃ bhuvanānyapyakhilānyadīdṛśaḥ || 6 ||
ayīti | bahu atiśayena tarpayan prīṇayan adīdṛśaḥ pradarśayāmāsitha ||
kuhacid vanamambudhiḥ kkacit kkacidabhraṃ kuhacid rasātalam |
manujā danujāḥ kkacit surā dadṛśe kiṃ na tadā tvadānane || 7 ||
kuhaciditi | tavāntarbhāge kuhacit kutracit || 7 ||
kalaśāmbudhiśāyinaṃ punaḥ paravaikuṇṭhapadādhivāsinam |
svapuraśca nijārbhakātmakaṃ katidhā tvāṃ na dadarśa sā mukhe || 8 ||
kalaśeti | kalaśāmbudhiśāyinaṃ kṣīrābdhau śeṣaparyaṅkaśāyinaṃ, punastataḥ parabaikuṇṭhapadādhivāsinaṃ, svastha puraḥ purato nijārbhakātmakaṃ yaśodātanayarūpaṃ tvāṃ sā katidhā na dadarśa || 8 ||
nanu yaśodā svasutasyāsye  svasutaṃ dadarśenyuktaṃ, tasyāpyāsye kiṃ bhuvanāni vidyante na veti śaṅkāyāmāh -- 
vikasadbhuvane mukhodare nanu bhūyo'pi tathāvidhānanaḥ |
anayā sphuṭamīkṣito bhavānanavasthāṃ jagatāṃ vatātanot || 9 ||
vikasaditi | vikasanti bhuvanāni yasmiṃstad vikasadbhuvanaṃ prakāśitabrahmāṇḍaṃ tasmin mukhodara āsye tathāvidhaṃ vikasadbhuvanamānanaṃ yasya sa bhavānanayā sphuṭaṃ spaṣṭamīkṣitaḥ jagatāṃ prapañcānāmanavasthāmanavasānatām | ayamarthaḥ yaśodā svasutasyāsye jagat svasutaṃ ca dadarśa | tasyāpi svasutasyāsye jagat svasutaṃ ca dadarśa, punastasyāpyevaṃ, punaśca tasyāpi evaṃ darpaṇasthapratibimbadarpaṇānavasthābad bhavān jagatāmanavasthāmātanoditi || 9 ||
dhṛtatattvadhiyaṃ tadā kṣaṇaṃ jananīṃ tāṃ praṇayena mohayan |
stanamamba! diśetyupāsajan bhagavannadbhutavāla! pāhi mām || 10 ||
dhṛteti | tasyāntarbhāge'nekabrahmāṇḍasya tadvahiṣṭhavaikuṇṭhalokasyāpi darśanāt tadvahiṣṭhāyāḥ prakṛteḥ paraṃ sañcidānandādvayaṃ brahmevedaṃ yogaiśvaryeṇa matputrabhāvenātmānaṃ pradarśya mohayatīti kṣaṇaṃ dhṛtā tattvadhīḥ samyagjñānaṃ yayā tāmupāsajannaṅkamārurukṣan anekabrahmāṇḍadarpaṇasthānīyatvādadbhutabāla! || 10 ||
iti viśvarūpapradarśanavarṇanaṃ ṣaṭcatvāriṃśaṃ daśakam |
ekadā dadhivimāthakāriṇīṃ mātaraṃ samupasedivān bhavān |
stanyalolupatayā nivārayannaṅkametya papivān payodharau || 10 ||
ekadeti | dadhno vimātho vimathanaṃ tat karotīti tathā | samupasedivān samīpaṃ prāptavān | aṅkametyāruhya nivārayan dadhivimathanamiti śeṣaḥ || 1 ||
ardhapītakucakuḍmale tvayi snigdhahāsamadhurānanāmbuje |
dugdhamīśa! dahane parisrutaṃ dhartumāśu jananī jagāma te || 2 ||
ardheti | tvayyardhapītakucakuḍmale'tṛpte tadantarā mātrāṅgulībhyāṃ kapolatalacālanāt snigdhahāsamadhurānanāmbuje ca sati || 2 ||
sāmipītarasabhaṅgasaṅgatakrodhabhāraparibhūtacetasā |
manthadaṇḍamupagṛhya pāṭitaṃ hanta deva! dadhibhājanaṃ tvayā || 3 ||
sāmīti | sāmipītasyārdhapītasya rasasya stanyasya bhaṅgena vicchedena saṅgataḥ prāpto yaḥ krodhabhārastena paribhūtamāveśitaṃ ceto yasya sa tathā || 3 ||
uccalad dhvanitamuccakaistadā saṃniśamya jananī samādrutā |
tvadyaśovisaravad dadarśa sā sadya eva dadhi vistṛtaṃ kṣitau || 4 ||
uccaladiti | uccakaiḥ pūrṇakumbhapāṭanena sātiśayamuccaladudgataṃ dhvanitaṃ śabdaṃ niśamya samādrutā vegenāgatā | yaśaso visaro vyāptiḥ | sadyaḥ samādrutā dadhyeva dadarśetyanena tava navanītamoṣaṇārthamanyato gamanaṃ vyajyate || 4 ||
vedamārgaparimārgitaṃ ruṣā tvāmavīkṣya parimārgayantyasau |
sandadarśa sukṛtinyulūkhale dīyamānanavanītamotave || 5||
vedeti | asau yaśodā munibhirvedamārgaiḥ kāṇḍatrayātmakaiḥ parimārgitaṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ taṃ tvāṃ ruṣā na bhaktyā parimārgayantyanveṣaṇaṃ kurvantī samyak cākṣuṣatayā dadarśa | ataḥ sukṛtinī navanītabhājanādhaḥsthitolūkhale'grapādena sthitvautave mārjārāya dīyamānaṃ navanītaṃ yena tam || 5 ||
tvāṃ pragṛhya bata bhītibhāvanābhāsurānanasarojamāśu sā |
roṣarūṣitamukhī sakhīpuro bandhanāya raśanāmupādade || 6 ||
tvāmiti | nāṭyavad bhītibhāvanayā bhāsuramānanasarojaṃ yasya taṃ tvām | roṣeṇa rūṣitaṃ bhrūbhaṅgadaśanacchadadaṃśananetraraktimādibhiścikṣairaṅkitaṃ mukhaṃ yasyāḥ | sakhīnāṃ puraḥ purobhāge raśanāṃ pāśam || 6 ||
bandhumicchati yameva sajjanastaṃ bhavantamāyi bandhumicchatī |
sā niyujya raśanāguṇān bahūn vghaṅgulonamakhilaṃ kilaikṣata || 7 ||
bandhumiti | ayi bhagavan ! yaṃ sajjano mokṣārthī bandhuṃ śaraṇamicchati, taṃ bhavantaṃ sā yaśodā pāśena bandhumicchatī raśanāguṇān bahūn niyujya tvadaṅge nikṣipya vghaṅgulonatayā punaḥ punarnikṣipyākhilamapi raśanāguṇaṃ yagulonamaikṣata dṛṣṭavatī kila || 7 ||
vismitotsmitasakhījanekṣitāṃ svinnasannavapuṣaṃ nirīkṣya tām |
nityamuktavapurapyaho hare ! vandhameva kṛpayānvamanyathāḥ || 8 ||
vismiteti | vdyagulonatādarśanena vismitairata evotsmitaiḥ sakhījanairīkṣitāṃ svinnaṃ svedārdraṃ sannaṃ klāntaṃ ca vapuryasyāstām | nityamuktaṃ parabrahmākhyaṃ vapuryasya sa tvaṃ raśanāguṇairbandhaṃ bandhanamevānvamanyathā anujñātavān || 8 ||
sthīyatāṃ ciramulūkhale khaletyāgatā bhavanameva sā yadā |
prāgulūkhalavilāntare tadā sarpirarpitamannavāsthithāḥ || 9 ||
sthīyatāmiti | sā tvāṃ baddhvā he khala! tvayā ciramulūkhale sthīyatāmityuktvā bhavanamevāgatā yadā, tadā prāgulūkhalabilāntare'rpitaṃ sarpirnavanītamadannavāsthithāḥ sthitavānasi || 9 ||
yadyapāśasugamo bhavān vibho! saṃyataḥ kimu sapāśayānayā |
evamādi divijairabhiṣṭuto vātanātha! paripāhi māṃ gadāt || 10 ||
yadīti | apāśānāmapagataviṣayaśraddhānāṃ sugamaḥ sukhena prāptuṃ śakyaḥ | evaṃ sati sapāśayā pāśasahitayānayā yaśodayā kimu kathaṃ saṃyato baddhaḥ || 10 ||
iti kṛṣṇasyolūkhalabandhanavarṇanaṃ saptacatvāriṃśaṃ daśakam |
mudā muraustvamudārasammadairudīrya dāmodara ityabhiṣṭutaḥ |
mṛdūdaraḥ svairamulūkhale lagannadūrato dvau kakubhāvudaikṣathāḥ || 1 ||
mudeti | prāgevodārasammadaiḥ pravṛddhasantoṣaiḥ suraughairdābhrodare baddhatvāddhetordāmodara ityudīrya sambodhya mudābhiṣṭutaḥ mṛdu bandhanāyogyamudaraṃ yasya sa tvaṃ svairaṃ yathāsukhaṃ lagan lagnaḥ san kakumau vṛkṣāvudaikṣathāḥ dṛṣṭavānasi || 1 ||
kuberasūnurnalakuvarābhidhaḥ paro maṇigrīva iti prathāṃ gataḥ |
maheśasevādhigataśriyonmadau ciraṃ kila tvadvimukhāvakhelatām || 2 ||
kubereti | paro'nyaḥ suto maṇigrīva iti prathāṃ prasiddhiṃ gataḥ | maheśasya śivasya sevayādhigatayā prāptayā śriyonmadau pravṛddhamadau tvadvimukhau viṣṇuvimukhāvakhelatāṃ ceratuḥ kila || 2 ||
surāpagāyāṃ kila tau madotkaṭau surāpagāyadvahuyauvatāvṛtau |
vivāsasau keliparau sa nārado bhavatpadaikapravaṇo niraikṣata || 3 ||
sureti | surāpagāyāṃ gaṅgāyāṃ surāpena gāyatā bahunā yauvatena yuvatisamūhenāvṛtau parivāritau bhavatpada ekasmin pravaṇo dattahṛdayo niraikṣata dṛṣṭavān || 3 ||
bhiyā priyālokamupāttavāsasaṃ puro nirīkṣyāpi madāndhacetasau |
imau bhavadbhaktyupaśāntisiddhaye munirjagau śāntimṛte kutaḥ sukham || 4 ||
bhiyeti | bhiyā śāpabhayenopāttavāsasaṃ dhṛtasvasvavasanaṃ priyālokaṃ nijastrīsamūham | bhavadbhaktirviṣṇubhaktiḥ, upaśāntirīdṛśakarmaṇo manaso nigrahaṇaṃ, tayoḥ siddhaye munirnāradaḥ jagau śaptavān | nanu śāntyā kiṃ phalaṃ tatrāha -- śāntiṃmiti | ṛte vinā sukhaṃ mokṣaḥ || 4 ||
yuvāmavāptau tkakubhātmatāṃ ciraṃ hariṃ nirīkṣyātha padaṃ svamāpnutam |
itīritau tau bhavadīkṣaṇaspṛhāṃ gatau vrajānte kakubhau babhūvatuḥ || 5 ||
yuvāmiti | kakubhātmattāmarjunataruyugmarūpatām | atha hariṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ nirīkṣya svaṃ padaṃ kuberāntikamāpnutaṃ prāpnutam || 5||
atandramindradruyugaṃ tathāvidhaṃ sameyuṣā mantharagāminā tvayā |
tirāyitolūkhalarodhanirdhutau cirāya jīrṇau paripātitau tarū || 6 ||
atandramiti | tathāvidhaṃ yamalamindradruyugamarjunataruyugmamatandraṃ sotsāhaṃ yathā bhavati tathā ulṛkhalākarṣaṇānmantharagāminā mandagāminā sameyuṣā prāptavatā tirāyitasya tiryaggatasyolūkhalasya rodhena nirdhutau nirbhinnamūlau cirāya jīrṇo purāṇau paripātitau sarvataḥ prasṛtaśākhopaśākhatayā pātitau || 6 ||
abhāji śākhidvitayaṃ yadā tvayā tadeva tadgarbhatalānnireyuṣā |
mahātviṣā yakṣayugena tatkṣaṇādabhāji govinda ! bhavānapi stavaiḥ || 7 ||
abhājīti | tvayā śākhidvitayaṃ yadābhāji bhagnaṃ, tadā tadgarbhatalācchākhidvitayāntarbhāgānnireyuṣā nirgatena mahatī tdiḍ yasya tena yakṣayugena stavaiḥ stotrairbhavānapyabhāji sevitaḥ | atra bhagnaṃ śākhidvitayaṃ puruṣarūpaṃ gṛhītvā pratyupacakāreti pratīyate || 7 ||
ihānyabhakto'pi sameṣyati kramād bhavantametau khalu rudrasevakau |
muniprasādād bhavadaṅghrimāgatau gatau vṛṇānau khalu bhaktimuttamām ||8 ||
iheti | iha jagatyanyeṣāṃ triguṇadviguṇamūrtīnāṃ brahmagiriśādīnāṃ bhaktaḥ sevako'pi kramādadhikārakrameṇa bhavantaṃ śuddhasattvamūrti samepyati bhajanādhikārī bhaviṣyati khalu niścitam | kuta ityata āha -- etāviti | uttamāṃ premalakṣaṇāṃ bhaktiṃ vṛṇānau prārthayantau || 8 ||
tatastarūddāraṇadāruṇāravaprakampisampātini gopamaṇḍale |
vilajjitatvajjananīmukhekṣiṇā vyamokṣi nandena bhavān vimokṣadaḥ ||9||
tata iti | tarvoruddāraṇaṃ bhañjanaṃ tajjanitena dāruṇenāraveṇa prakampisampātini kkedaṃ kimidamiti sasambhramamāgamanaśīle sati bandhanānuśayena vilajjitāyā stvajjananyāḥ ‘kiṃ kṛtaṃ yaśode ! tvayā, aho avimṛśyakāritā bhavatyā' ityabhiprāyagarbhe mukhekṣiṇā nandena bhavān vyamokṣi pāśabandhanānmocitaḥ || 9 ||
mahīruhormadhyagato vatārbhako hareḥ prabhāvādaparikṣato'dhunā |
iti bravāṇairgamito gṛhaṃ bhavān marutpurādhīśvara! pāhi māṃ gaḍhāt || 10 ||
mahīti | mahīruhorvṛkṣayoḥ | prabhāvaḥ śaktiḥ | aparikṣato'vikalāṅgaḥ | bruvāṇarnandādibhiriti śeṣaḥ || 10 ||
iti yamalārjunabhañjanavarṇanam aṣṭacatvāriṃśaṃ daśakam |
bhavatprabhāvāvidurā hi gopāstaruprapātādikamatra goṣṭhe |
ahetumutpātagaṇaṃ viśaṅkaya prayātumanyatra mano vitenuḥ || 1 ||
bhavaditi | bhavatprabhāvāvidurā aviditabhavatsvarūpaiśvaryāḥ kevalaṃ martyabuddhayaḥ taruprapātādikamahetutvādutpātagaṇaṃ durnimittaughaṃ viśaṅkaca || 1 ||
tatropanandābhidhagopavaryo jagau bhavatpreraṇayaiva nūnam |
itaḥ pratīcyāṃ vipinaṃ manojñaṃ vṛndāvanaṃ nāma virājatīti || 2 ||
tatreti | tatra teṣu gopeṣūpanandābhidho gopavarya iti jagāvuvāca | tattu bhavatpreraṇayaiva nūnaṃ niścinomi || 2 ||
bṛhadvanaṃ tat khalu nandamukhyā vidhāya gauṣṭhīnamatha kṣaṇena |
tvadanvitatvajjananīniviṣṭagariṣṭhayānānugatā viceluḥ || 3 ||
bṛhaditi | nandamukhyā nandādayo gopāḥ | yatrastho bhagavānetāvantaṃ kālaṃ krīḍitavān, tat khalu bṛhadvanaṃ nāma deśaṃ gauṣṭhīnaṃ bhūtapūrvagosthānaṃ vidhāya kṛtvā bṛhadvanākhyaṃ pūrvagoṣṭhaṃ vihāyetyarthaḥ | tvadanviteti | rāmakṛṣṇānvitayaśodārohiṇīniviṣṭamaṃta eva gariṣṭhaṃ mahanīyaṃ yānaṃ śakaṭamanugatā gopā vicelurjagmuḥ || 3 ||
anomanojñadhvanidhenupālīkhurapraṇādāntarato vadhūbhiḥ |
bhavadvinodālapitākṣarāṇi prapīya nājñāyata mārgadairghyam || 4 ||
ana iti | anasaḥ śakaṭasya manojño yo dhvaniḥ, yaśca dhenupālyāḥ paśusamūhasya khurapraṇādaḥ, tayorantarato madhye śrūyamāṇāni bhavadvinodālapiteṣu kalabhāṣaṇeṣvamṛtamayāni akṣarāṇi prapīya śrotrāñjalibhirāpīya mārgadairghya nājñāyata na viditam || 4 ||
nirīkṣya bṛndāvanamīśa! nandatprasūnakundapramukhadrumaugham |
amodathāḥ śādvalasāndralakṣmyā harinmaṇīkuṭṭimapuṣṭaśobham || 5 ||
nirīkṣyeti | nandanti samṛddhāni prasūnāni yeṣu tathāvidhāḥ kundapramukhā drumaughā yasmin | śādvalasāndralakṣmyā haritatṛṇanīrandhritasthalaśobhayā harinmaṇīkuṭṭimavadindranīlamaṇikuṭṭimavat puṣṭaśobhaṃ saṃpūrṇaśobhāyuktam | amodathāḥ santuṣṭo'bhūḥ || 5 ||
navākanirvyūḍhanivāsabhedeṣvaśeṣagopeṣu sukhāsiteṣu |
vanaśriyaṃ gopakiśorapālīvimiśritaḥ ‘paryagalokathāstvam || 6 ||
navāketi | navākanirvyūḍheṣu nūtanatayā raciteṣu nivāsabhedeṣu viśiṣṭeṣu bhavaneṣu | paryagalokathāḥ parito dṛṣṭavānasi || 6 ||
arālamārgāgatanirmalāpāṃ marālakūjākṛtanarmalāpām |
nirantarasmerasarojavakrāṃ kalindakanyāṃ samalokayastvam || 7 ||
arāleti | marālakūjābhiḥ kalahaṃsakūjitaiḥ kṛtanarmalāpāmuccāritasarasabacanām | arālena kuṭilena mārgeṇāgatā nirmalā āpo yasyām | nirantarāṇi nīrandhritāni smerāṇi sarojānyeva vakraṃ yasyāḥ | kalindakanyām ata evopabhogakṣamām || 7 ||
mayūrakekāśatalobhanīyaṃ mayūkhamālāśabalaṃ maṇīnām |
viriñcalokaspṛśamuccayṛṅgairgiriṃ ca govardhanamaikṣathāstvam || 8 ||
mayūreti | mayūrāṇāṃ kekāśataiḥ kalakalaninādairlobhanīyaṃ manoharaṃ maṇīnāṃ
anvagādivad añcūttarapadaṃ paryaṃgiti| parita iti tadarthaḥ |
ratnānāṃ mayūkhamālābhiḥ prabhāpaṭalaiḥ śabalaṃ vicitravarṇamuccairunnataiḥ śṛṅgaiḥ viriñcalokaṃ satyalokaṃ spṛśatīti tathā || 8 ||
samaṃ tato gopakumārakaistvaṃ samantato yatra vanāntamāgāḥ |
tatastatastāṃ kuṭilāmapaśyaḥ kalindajāṃ rāgavatīmivaikām || 9 ||
samamiti | tato gopakumārakaiḥ samaṃ saha vanāntaṃ samantataḥ sarvatra yatra yatrāgāḥ gatavānasi tatastataḥ tatra tatra kalindajāmekāmapaśyaḥ | tat kathaṃ, kuṭilāṃ vakramārgeṇa tatra tatrāgatāmatastvadanusaraṇāt tvayi rāgavatīmiva || 9 ||
tathāvidhe'smin vipine paśavye samutsuko vatsagaṇapracāre |
caran sarāmo'tha kumārakaistvaṃ samīragehādhipa! pāhi rogāt || 10 ||
tatheti | paśavye paśubhyo hite || 10 ||
iti vṛndāvanagamanavarṇanam ūnapañcāśaṃ daśakam |
taralamadhukṛdvūnde bṛndāvane'tha manohare
paśupaśiśubhiḥ sākaṃ vatsānupālanalolupaḥ |
haladharasakho deva! śrīman ! viceritha dhārayan
gavalamuralīvetraṃ netrābhirāmatanudyutiḥ || 1 ||
taraleti | taralamadhukṛvṛnde calitabhṛṅgasamūhe | gavalaṃ śṛṅgaṃ muralīṃ veṇuṃ vetraṃ yāṣṭaṃ ca dhārayan viceritha sañcaraṇaṃ kṛtavān || 1 ||
vihitajagatīrakṣaṃ lakṣmīkarāmbujalālitaṃ
dadati caraṇadvandvaṃ bṛndāvane tvayi pāvane |
kimiva na babhau sampatsampūritaṃ taruvallarīsaliladharaṇīgotrakṣetrādikaṃ kamalāpate! || 2 ||
vihiteti | vihitā jagatyā bhūmyā rakṣā yena | lakṣmyāḥ karāmbujābhyāṃ
lālitaṃ saṃvāhitam | tarvādikaṃ kimiva sampatsampūritaṃ na babhau śuśubhe taravaḥ puṣpādibhiḥ sampadbhiḥ, vallaryaśca, salilaṃ padmādibhiḥ, dharaṇī sasyādibhiḥ, gotraḥ parvato ratnādibhiḥ, kṣetramākaro hemādibhiśca sampūrṇa śuśubha ityarthaḥ || 2 ||
vilasadulape kāntārānte samīraṇaśītale
vipulayamunātīre govardhanācalamūrdhasu |
lalitamuralīnādaḥ sañcārayan khalu vātsakaṃ
kkacana divase daityaṃ vatsākṛtiṃ tvamudaikṣathāḥ || 3 ||
vilasaditi | vilasadulape śobhamānatṛṇaviśeṣayukte kāntārānte vanapradeśe, yamunātaraṅgasaṅginā samīraṇena śītale vipule pulinamayatayā vistṛte yamunātīre ca vātsakaṃ vatsasamūham | udaikṣathā dṛṣṭavān || 3 ||
rabhasavilasatpucchaṃ vicchāyato'sya vilokayan
kimapi valitaskandhaṃ randhrapratīkṣamudīkṣitam |
tamatha caraṇe bibhrad vibhrāmayana muhuruccakaiḥ
kuhacana mahāvṛkṣe cikṣepitha kṣatajīvitam || 4 ||
rabhaseti | rabhasavilasatpucchaṃ vegāditastataḥ sañcāritapucchaṃ yathā bhavati tathā vicchāyato gacchato'sya batsāsurasya kimapi valitaskandhamīṣattiryakkṛgrīvaṃ, randhrapratīkṣamavasarapratipālanarūpaṃ ca yadudīkṣitamavalokanaṃ tad vilokayan atha māṃ hantukāmo'yamiti niścitya taṃ caraṇe biśrad gṛhītvoccakairvegānmuhurvibhrāmayan vitramaṇenaiva kṣatajīvitaṃ kuhacana kasmiṃkṣciccikṣepitha prakṣiptavān || 4 ||
nipatati mahādaitye jātyā durātmani tatkṣaṇaṃ
nipatanajavakṣuṇṇakṣoṇīruhakṣatakānane |
divi parimiladvṛndā bṛndārakāḥ kusumotkaraiḥ
śirasi bhavato harṣād varṣanti nāma tadā hare ! || 5 ||
nipatatīti | jātyā janmataḥ prabhṛtyāsurabhāvena durātmani duṣṭe nipatana
jabena kṣuṇṇaiścūrṇīkṛtaiḥ kṣoṇīruhavṛkṣaiḥ kṣataṃ naṣṭaṃ kānanaṃ yena | parimilanti samūhībhūtāni vṛndāni gaṇā yeṣu te bṛndārakā devāḥ kusumotkaraiḥ suratarukusumanikaraiḥ || 5 ||
surabhilatamā mūrdhanyūrdhvaṃ kutaḥ kusumāvalī
nipatati tavetyukto bālaiḥ sahelamudairayaḥ |
jhaṭiti danujakṣepeṇordhvaṃ gatastarumaṇḍalāt
kusumanikaraḥ so'yaṃ nūnaṃ sameti śanairiti || 6 ||
surabhileti | surabhilatamātiśayena surabhilā saurabhyayuktā kusumāvalī kutastava mūrdhani nipatati ūrdhvamuparibhāge vṛkṣābhāvāt kuta etaditi bālairuktaḥ pṛṣṭastvaṃ sahelaṃ salīlamudairaya uktavān, danujakṣepeṇa vatsāsuraśavaśarīraprakṣepeṇa tadvegotthaḥ kusumanikarastarumaṇḍalād vṛkṣanikarādūrdhvaṃ gataḥ so'yaṃ śanaiḥ sameti mūrdhni patati nūnaṃ tarkayāmi || 6 ||
kkacana divase bhūyo bhūyastare paruṣātape
tapanatanayāpāthaḥ pātuṃ gatā bhavadādayaḥ |
calitagarutaṃ prekṣāmāsurbakaṃ khalu vismṛtaṃ
kṣitidharagarucchede kailāsaśailamivāparam || 7 ||
kkacaneti | bhūyaḥ punaḥ kvacana divase bhūyastare bhūyiṣṭhe paruṣātape grīṣmakāle tapanatanayāyāṃ yamunāyāṃ pātho jalaṃ pātuṃ bhavadādayo gopabālakā bakaṃ bakāsuraṃ prekṣāmāsuḥ dṛṣṭavantaḥ | calitagarutaṃ calitapakṣam | indrakartṛke kṣitidharagarucchede vismṛtamacchinnapakṣamaparaṃ dvitīyam || 7 ||
pibati salilaṃ gopavrāte bhavantamabhidrutaḥ
sa kila nigilannagniprakhyaṃ punardrutamudraman |
dalayitumagāt troṭyāḥ koṭyā tadā tu bhavān vibho !
khalajanabhidācuñcuścañcū pragṛhya dadāra tam || 8 ||
pibatīti | gopānāṃ vrāte samūhe | abhidruto'bhidhāvan bhavantaṃ nigilan grasan agniprakhyamagnisadṛśamata eva drutamudvaman punarvamanānantaraṃ troṭyāḥ cañcoḥ koṭyāgreṇa dalayituṃ vidārayitumagāt | khalajanabhidācuñcurduṣṭanigraheṇa prasiddho bhavān adharottare cañcū pragṛhya taṃ dadāra nigṛhītavān || 8 ||
sapadi sahajāṃ sandraṣṭuṃ vā mṛtāṃ khalu pūtanāmanujamaghamapyagre gatvā pratīkṣitumeva vā |
śamananilayaṃ yāte tasmin bake sumanogaṇe
kirati sumanobṛndaṃ vṛndāvanāda gṛhamaiyathāḥ || 9 ||
sapadīti | agre pūrvaṃ gatvāghamaghāsuraṃ pratīkṣituṃ pratipālayitum | tasminnaghapūtanājyeṣṭhe bakāsure śamananilayaṃ yamasadanaṃ yāte sati sumanasāṃ devānāṃ gaṇe sumanasāṃ puṣpāṇāṃ bṛndaṃ samūhaṃ kirati varṣati satyaiyathā agacchaḥ || 9 ||
lalitamuralīnādaṃ dūrānniśamya vadhūjanaistvaritamupagamyārādārūḍhamoda mudīkṣitaḥ |
janitajananīnandānandaḥ samīraṇamandiraprathitavasate! śaure! dūrīkuruṣva mamāmayān || 10 ||
laliteti | lalitamatisundaraṃ muralīnādaṃ veṇunādaṃ niśamya dūrādudīkṣita unnamitavadanaṃ vīkṣitaḥ punastvaritamupagamya prasthāya tatsamīpaṃ gatvārāt samīpe'ṅgapratyaṅgadarśanādārūḍhamodamudīkṣitaḥ janita utpādito jananīnandayorānando yena || 10 ||
iti vatsāsuravadhavarṇanaṃ bakāsuravabhavarṇanaṃ ca
pañcāśaṃ daśakam |
kadācana vrajaśiśubhiḥ samaṃ bhavān
vanāśane vihitamatiḥ pragetarām |
samāvṛto bahutaravatsamaṇḍalaiḥ
satemanairniragamadīśa! jemanaiḥ || 1 ||
kadācaneti | pragetarāmuṣasyāsanne satemanaiḥ savyañjanairjemanairodanaiḥ || 1 ||
viniryatastava caraṇāmbujadvayādudaścitaṃ tribhuvanapāvanaṃ rajaḥ |
maharṣayaḥ pulakadharaiḥ kalebarairudhire dhṛtabhavaḍhīkṣaṇotsavāḥ || 2 ||
viniryata iti | viniryato vinirgacchataḥ | udañcitaṃ patitaṃ tribhuvanapāvanaṃ jagatāṃ pāpaśodhakam | pulakadharairiti bhaktacanubhāvaḥ | udūhire dhṛtavantaḥ||2||
pracārayatyaviralaśādvale tale
paśūn vibho! bhavāta samaṃ kumārakaiḥ |
aghāsuro nyaruṇadaghāya vartanīṃ
bhayānakaḥ sapadi śayānakākṛtiḥ || 3 ||
pracārayatīti | aviralaśādvale tṛṇabhūyiṣṭhe tale pradeśe | aghāya bhavato bhavannāthānāṃ ca grasanarūpamaghamaparādhaṃ kartuṃ, paramārthatastu bhavabhayapradamadhaṃ duritaṃ tyaktuṃ vartanīṃ mārga nyaruṇad rurodha | śayānakākṛtirajagararūpaḥ || 3 ||
mahācalapratimatanorguhānibhaprasāritaprathitamukhasya kānane |
mukhodaraṃ viharaṇakautukād gatāḥ
kumārakāḥ kimapi vidūrage tvayi || 4 ||
mahācaleti | kumārakā vivekarahitāstvayi kimapi kiñcid vidūrage dūrasthe sati kānane biharaṇakautukāt tasya mukhodaramāsyaṃ gatāḥ praviṣṭāḥ | kathametad, mahācalapratimatanoḥ parvataguhānibhaṃ prasāritamāstṛtaṃ prathitaṃ vistṛtaṃ mukhamāsyaṃ yasya, ataḥ ajagaratanormahācalabhrāntyā tanmukhasyācalaguhābhrāntyā cetyarthaḥ || 4 ||
pramādataḥ praviśati pannagodaraṃ
kathattanau paśupakule savātsake |
vidannidaṃ tvamapi viveśiya prabho !
suhṛjjanaṃ viśaraṇamāśu rakṣitum || 5 ||
pramādata iti | kkathattanau dahyamānaśarīre savātsake tatsasamūhasahite | tvamapīdamaghāsuraceṣṭitaṃ vidan jānan || 5 ||
galodare vipulitavarṣmaṇā tvayā
mahorage luṭhati niruddhamārute |
drutaṃ bhavān vidalitakaṇṭhamaṇḍalo
vimocayan paśupapaśūn viniryayau || 6 ||
galodara iti | vipulitavarṣmaṇā vardhitaśarīreṇa tvayā niruddho mārutaḥ prāṇavāyuryasya | ata eva prāṇanirodhālluṭhati itastato veṣṭamāne sati vidalitaṃ vidāritaṃ kaṇṭhamaṇḍalaṃ yena sa bhavāṃstena prāṇanirgamamārgeṇa paśupān paśūṃśca vimocayan bahirnirgamayan svayamapi viniryayau || 6 ||
kṣaṇaṃ divi tvadupagamārthamāsthitaṃ
mahāsuraprabhavamaho maho mahat |
vinirgate tvayi tu nilīnamañjasā
nabhaḥsthale nanṛturatho jaguḥ surāḥ || 7 ||
kṣaṇamiti | tvadupagamārthaṃ tvānnargamanārthaṃ tvayi praveṣṭuṃ vā kṣaṇaṃ divyākāśa āsthitaṃ mahāsuraprabhavamaghāsuraśarīrānnirgataṃ mahad vipulaṃ mahaḥ sūkṣmaśarīrasyāpi tyāgena paricchedakābhāvādatyujjvalaṃ tejaḥpujaṃ tvāye jyotiṣi jyotiriva nilīnam | aho dviṣannapyaghāsuro bhavantamantaḥkṛtvā mṛto'tastatkṣaṇaṃ tatphalaṃ sāyujyākhyaṃ mokṣaṃ sarvalokasamakṣaṃ prāptavān | aho bhaktavātsalyaṃ bhavata iti bhāvaḥ || 7 ||
savismayaiḥ kamalabhavādibhiḥ surairanudrutastadanu gataḥ kumārakaiḥ |
dine punastaruṇadaśāmupeyuṣi
svakairbhavānatanuta bhojanotsavam || 8 ||
savismayairiti | anudbhuto vyomamārgeṇānugataḥ dine taruṇadaśāmupeyuṣi madhyāhne prāpte sati bhojanotsavaṃ banāśanakautukam || 8 ||
viṣāṇikāmapi muralīṃ nitambake
niveśayan kabaladharaḥ karāmbuje |
prahāsayan kalavacanaiḥ kumārakān
bubhojitha tridaśagaṇairmudā nutaḥ || 9 ||
viṣāṇikāmiti | viṣāṇikāṃ śṛṅga, muralī veṇuṃ, nitambake kaṭipradeśe pītapaṭāntare || 9 ||
sukhāśanaṃ tviha tava gopamaṇḍale
makhāśanāt pririyamiva devamaṇḍale |
iti stutastridaśavarairjagatmabho !
marutpurīnilaya ! gadāt prapāhi mām || 10 ||
sukhāśanamiti | gopamaṇḍale sukhāśanaṃ devamaṇḍale makhāśanād yajñabhāgabhojanāt priyamiva tridaśavarairbrahmādibhiriti stutaḥ || 10 ||
ityadhāsuravadhavarṇanam ekapañcāśaṃ daśakam |
anyāvatāranikareṣvanirīkṣitaṃ te
bhramātirekamabhivīkṣya tadāghamokṣe |
brahmā parīkṣitumanāḥ sa parokṣabhāvaṃ
ninye'tha vatsakagaṇān pravitasya māyām || 1 ||
anyeti | anyāvatāranikareṣu nṛsiṃhādiṣvapyanirīkṣitamadṛṣṭaṃ bhūmātirekamaiśvaryātiśayam | parīkṣitumanā ito'pyaiśvaryātiśayaṃ draṣṭukāmaḥ pravitatya prayujya | parokṣabhāvamantardhānam || 1 ||
vatsānavīkṣya vivaśe paśupotkare tānānetukāma iva dhātṛmatānuvartī |
tvaṃ sāmibhuktakavalo gatavāṃstadānīṃ
bhuktāṃstirodhita sarojabhavaḥ kumārān || 2 ||
vatsāniti | dhāturmataṃ bhūmātirekadarśanaṃ tadanuvartī taddarśanārthītyarthaḥ | sāmibhuktakabalo'rdhabhuktapāṇikabalaḥ ahameva vatsānāneṇyāmīti bhuñjānān bālakānākhyāya tānānetukāma iva, nānetukāmaḥ, kintu dhātṛmatānuvartanārthaṃ gatavāṃstvam | tadānīṃ śrīkṛṣṇe dūrasthe sati sarojabhavo brahmā bhuktān bhuñjānān tirodhita tiraskṛtavān || 2 ||
evaṃ vatsakavatsapeṣu brahmaṇā tiraskṛteṣvekākī bhagavān sāyametairvinā mama brajapraveśe sati gogopīnāṃ svātmajādarśanajanitaḥ khedaḥ syād etān brahmaṇo'pahūtya gamane brahmaṇo'pi kheda iti vicintya tadubhayābhāvāya svamāyayā svenaiva sarvaṃ sampāditavānityāha -- 
vatsāyitastadanu gopagaṇāyitastvaṃ
śikyādibhāṇḍamuralīgavalādirūpaḥ |
prāgvad vihṛtya vipineṣu cirāya sāyaṃ
tvaṃ māyayātha bahudhā vrajamāyayātha || 3 ||
vatsāyita iti | tadanu brahmaṇā vatsavatsapasamūhe tiraskṛte sati tvaṃ māyayā bahudhā sarvasvarūpo bhūtaḥ vatsāyito vatsā ivācaran gopagaṇāyitaḥ śikyādirūpaśca cirāya ciraṃ vipineṣu prāgvad vihṛtyātha sāyaṃ sandhyāyāṃ vrajamāyayāthāgatavān || 3 ||
tvāmeva śikyagavalādimayaṃ dadhāno
bhūyastvameva paśuvatsakavālarūpaḥ |
gorūpiṇībhirapi gopavadhūmapībhirāsādito'si jananībhiratipraharṣāt || 4 ||
tvāmiti | bhūyaḥ punaḥ paśuvatsakabālarūpastvaṃ śikyagavalādimayaṃ tvāmeva dadhānaḥ | gorūpiṇībhiriti | gorūpiṇībhirjananībhiḥ paśuvatsarūpastvamatipraharṣādatiśayna vātsalyādāsa ditaḥ svaṃ svaṃ vatsa matvā gāvastvāṃ sahuṅkāramaudhasaṃ payo'pāyayan | tathā gopavadhūmayībhirjananībhirgopabālakarūpastvamāsādito'si tā api svaṃ svaṃ sutaṃ matvā tvāmaṅkāropaṇākṣleṣastanadānādi cakrurityarthaḥ || 4 ||
nanu gogopīnāṃ vatsavatsapavālepvevamatipraharṣe ko heturityāśaṅkaya sarvo'pyā tmāni snihyati | anye hi tadarthaṃ priyāḥ | ātmā ca viṣṇuḥ kṛṣṇaḥ | atastadrūpeṣu putreṣu premadhirabhūditi pariharannāha -- 
jīvaṃ hi kañcidabhimānavaśāt svakīyaṃ
matvā tanūja iti rāgabharaṃ vahantyaḥ |
ātmānameva tu bhavantamavāpya sūnuṃ
prītiṃ yayurna viyanītākṣca gāvaḥ || 5 ||
jīvamiti | kañcit svaprārabdhakarmaphalabhogāyodyataṃ jīvamātmatvenābhimataṃ sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ vā śarīramabhimānavaśānmamatvāropavaśena tanūjo mama putra iti svakīyaṃ matvā rāgabharaṃ vahantyo bhavanti tanmātaraḥ | hi yasmāditthaṃbhāve'pyetādṛśo rāgaḥ, viśeṣe sati kimucyata ityāha -- ātmānamiti | ātmānamahampratyaye nedamaṃśarūpaṃ bhavantaṃ śrīkṛṣṇameva sūnumavāpya vanitā gopyo gāvaśca na kiyatīṃ prītiṃ yayuḥ atimahatīṃ prītimavāptā evetyarthaḥ || 5 ||
evaṃ pratikṣaṇavijṛmbhitaharpabhāraniśśeṣagopagaṇalālitabhūrimṛrtim |
tvāmagrajo'pi bubudhe kila vatsarānte
brahmātmanorapi mahāna yuvayorviśeṣaḥ || 6 ||
evamiti | pratikṣaṇaṃ vijṛmbhito vardhito harṣabhāraḥ santopātiśayo yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ niśśeṣāṇāṃ gopānāṃ gaṇena lālitabhūrimūrti tattadgo gopyātmajarūpaṃ tvāmagrajaḥ śrībalabhadro'pi (tvadbhūrimūrtitvaṃ?) vatsarānte saṃvatsarāvasāne bubudhe | brahmātmanorbramasvarūpayorapi yuvayostvaṃ niṣkalaṃ brahma agrajaḥ sakalamiti mahān viśeṣaḥ || 6 ||
varṣāvadhau navapurātanavatsapālān
dṛṣṭvā vivekamasṛṇe druhiṇe vimṛḍhe |
prādīdṛśaḥ pratinavān makuṭāṅgadādibhūṣāṃkṣcaturbhujayujaḥ sajalāmbudābhān || 7 ||
varṣāvadhāviti | varṣāvadhau saṃvatsarāvasāne navān śrīkṛṣṇamayān purāta nān svena tiraskṛtān vatsān vatsapālāṃśca dṛṣṭvā druhiṇe brahmaṇi vimūḍhe ete satyā anye māyāmayā iti samyagjñānarahite vivekamasṛṇe vicārayitumapyasamarthe sati pratinavān māyāmayān makuṭāṅgadādiviśiṣṭāna prādīdṛśaḥ pradarśayānāsitha || 7 ||
pratyekameva kamalāparilālitāṅgān
bhogīndrabhogaśayanān nayanābhirāmān |
līlānimīlitadṛśaḥ sanakādiyogivyāsevitān kamalabhūrbhavato dadarśa || 8 ||
pratyekamiti | līlānimīlitadṛśo'ṅgīkṛtayoganidrān bhavato viṣṇūn dadarśa || 8 ||
nārāyaṇākṛtimasaṅkhayatamāṃ nirīkṣya
sarvatra sevakamapi svamavekṣya dhātā |
māyānimagnahṛdayo vimumoha yāvadeko babhūvitha tadā kabalārdhapāṇiḥ || 9 ||
nārāyaṇeti | sevakaṃ svamātmānam | māyāyāṃ nimagnaṃ mūḍhaṃ hṛdayaṃ yasya | yāvad bimumohācetanaprāyo'bhūt , tadā tvaṃ bahurūpaṃ saṃhṛtyaikaḥ kabalārdhapāṇiḥ śrīkapṇarūpeṇa vatsānveṣaṇaparo babhūvitha || 9 ||
nakṣyanmade tadanu viśvapatiṃ muhustvāṃ
natvā ca nūtavati dhātari dhāma yāte |
potaiḥ samaṃ pramuditaiḥ praviśantriketaṃ
vātālayādhipa! vibho! paripāhi rogāt || 10 ||
naśyaditi | nūtavati stutavati sati dhāma satyalokaṃ yāte gate ca sati potairvatsapālaiḥ samaṃ saha niketaṃ gṛhaṃ praviśan || 10 ||
iti brahmakṛtavatsāpahāravarṇanaṃ bhagavanmāyayā brahmaṇo mohavarṇanaṃ ca
dvipaścāśaṃ daśakam |
atītya vālyaṃ jagatāṃ pate ! tvamupetya paugaṇḍavayo manojñam |
upekṣya vatsāvanamutsavena prāvartathā gogaṇapālanāyām || 1 ||
atītyeti | bālyaṃ pañca varṣāṇi | paugaṇḍavayo dvitīyapañcakaṃ manojñaṃ bālyādatimohanaṃ tadupetya prāpya vatsānāmavanaṃ rakṣaṇamupekṣyotsavena kautukena gogaṇapālanāyāṃ prāvartathāḥ pravṛtto'bhūḥ || 1 ||
upakramasyānuguṇaiva seyaṃ marutpurādhīśa! tava pravṛttiḥ |
gotrāparitrāṇakṛte'vatīrṇastadeva devārabhathāstadā yat || 2 ||
upakramasyeti | upakramasyārambhasyānuguṇā yogyā | gotrāparitrāṇakṛte bhūme rakṣaṇārtham | tadeva Sgotrāparitrāṇaṃ gorakṣaṇam || 2 ||
kadāpi rāmeṇa samaṃ vanānte vanaśriyaṃ vīkṣya caran sukhena |
śrīdāmanāmnaḥ svasakhasya vācā modādagā dhenukakānanaṃ tvam || 3 ||
kadeti | dhenukakānanaṃ dhenukāsuranivāsasthānaṃ tālavanam || 3 ||
uttālatālīnivahe tvaduktayā valena dhṛte'tha balena dorbhyām |
mṛduḥ kharacābhyapatat purastāt phalotkaro dhenukadānavo'pi || 4 ||
uttāleti | uttāle'timahati tālīnivahe tālavṛkṣasamūhe balena śaktyā dorbhyāṃ dhūte cālite purastāt prathamaṃ mṛdurapariṇataḥ kharaḥ pariṇataḥ phalotkaraḥ phalasamūho'bhyapatat sambhūyāpatat | anantaraṃ dhenukadānavo'pi tālaphalapatanaravajanitakrodhena khararūpyabhyapatadabhyadhāvat || 4 ||
samuyato ghainukapālane'haṃ vadhaṃ kathaṃ ghainukamadya kurve |
itīva matvā dhruvamagrajena suraugharoddhāramajīghatastvam || 5||
samudyata iti | ghainukasya dhenusamūhasya pālane samudyata utsāhī dhainukaṃ vadhaṃ dhenukāsuravadhamiti prakṛto'rthaḥ | kathaṃ kurve na karomi dhruvaṃ niścitamiti matvā niścityevāgrajena tamajīghato ghātitavānasi || 5 ||
tadīyabhṛtyānapi jambukatvenopāgatānagrajasaṃyutastvam |
jambūphalānīva tadā nirāsthastāleṣu khelan bhagavan ! nirāsthaḥ || 6 ||
tadīyeti | jambukatvenopāgatān jambukāḥ kroṣṭāro bhūtvāgatān | nirāstha āsthārahitaḥ san nirāstho nirasanaṃ kṛtavān nigṛhītavān || 6 ||
" 'initrakaṭyacaśca' iti trapratyayaḥ |
vinighnati tvayyatha jambukaudhaṃ sanāmakatvādvaruṇastadānīm |
bhayākulo jambukanāmadheyaṃ śrutiprasiddhaṃ vyaghiteti manye || 7 ||
vinighnatīti | sanāmakatvāt svasyāpi jambukanāmakatvāt || 7 ||
tavāvatārasya phalaṃ murāre! sañjātamadyeti surairnutastvam |
satyaṃ phalaṃ jātamiheti hāsī bālaḥ samaṃ tālaphalānyabhuṅkthāḥ || 8||
taveti | avatārasya phalaṃ dhenukanigrahaḥ | tadadya sañjātamiti surairnṛtastvaṃ tava stutiśravaṇāt satyaṃ yathārthavādino devā iha phalaṃ tālaphalajātaṃ jātam || 8 ||
madhudravascunti bṛhanti tāni phalāni medobharabhṛnti bhuktvā |
tṛptaiśca ddaptairbhavanaṃ phalauṃcaṃ vahadbhirāgāḥ khalu vālakaistvam || 9 ||
madhudraveti | madhudravaḥ sravatyebhya iti tathā medobharabhṛnti antaḥsāravanti tālaphalāni bhuktvā tṛptairata eva ddaptairahaṅkṛtairbandhubhyo dātuṃ phalaughaṃ skandhopaneyaṃ bhavanaṃ vahadbhi prāpayadbhiḥ || 9 ||
hato hato dhenuka ityupetya phalānyadadbhirmadhurāṇi lokaiḥ |
jayeti jīveti nuto vibho! tvaṃ marutpurādhīśvara! pāhi rogāt || 10 ||
hata iti | harṣātiśayena hato hata iti dviruktiḥ || 10 ||
iti dhenukāsuravadhavarṇanaṃ tripañcāśaṃ daśakam |
atha daśakatrayeṇa kāliyamardanarūpaṃ bhagavaccaritaṃ kathayan stauti -- 
tvatsevotkaḥ saubharirnāma pūrve kālinyantardvādaśābdaṃ tapasyan |
mīnavrāte snehavān bhogalole tārkṣye sākṣādaikṣatāgre kadācit || 1 ||
tvaditi | tvatsevāyāmukka utsukaḥ saubharirnāma munirbhogalole itaretarasnehasukhapare | sākṣāt pratyakṣata eaikṣata dṛṣṭavān || 1 ||
tvadvāhaṃ taṃ sakṣudhaṃ tṛkṣamanuṃ mīnaṃ kañcijjakṣataṃ lakṣayan saḥ |
taptakṣcitte śaptavānatra cet tvaṃ jantūn bhoktā jīvitaṃ cāpi moktā ||2||
tvādati | tvadvāhaṃ tava vāhanabhūtaṃ tṛkṣasūnuṃ tārkṣyaṃ jakṣataṃ bhakṣayantaṃ lakṣayan paśyan citte taptaḥ santāpavān atra kālindīhrade tvaṃ jantūn matsyādīn bhoktā bhakṣayiṣyasi ced jīvitaṃ moktā mariṣyasi || 2 ||
tasmin kāle kāliyaḥ kṣveladarpāt sarpārāteḥ kalpitaṃ bhāgamaśnan |
tena krodhāt tvatpadāmbhojabhājā pakṣakṣitastaddurāpaṃ payo'gāt || 3 ||
tasminniti | kṣveladarpād viṣavīryamadāt sarpairātmarakṣaṇārthe kalpitaṃ bhāgaṃ māsikaṃ balimaśnan tvatpadāmbhojaṃ bhajatīti tathā | ataḥ pakṣeṇa savyena kṣipto vivaśīkṛitaḥ taddurāpaṃ sarpārātidurāpaṃ payaḥ kālindīhradamagāt || 3 ||
ghore tasmin sūrajānīravāse tīre vṛkṣā vikṣatāḥ kṣvelavegāt |
pakṣivrātāḥ peturabhre patantaḥ kāruṇyārdraṃ tvanmanastena jātam || 4 ||
ghora iti | ghore krūrakarmāṇi tasmin kāliye sūrajānīravāse kālindīhṛdanivāsini sati vikṣatā viṣāgnijvālāvalīḍhatayā naṣṭāḥ | abhra uparibhāge | tena, yato na kevalaṃ tajjalopabhogābhāvaḥ pratyuta sarvalokavināśaśca, tenetyarthaḥ || 4 ||
kāle tasminnekadā sīrapāṇiṃ muktvā yāte yāmunaṃ kānanāntam |
tvayyuddāmagrīṣmabhīṣmoṣmataptā gogopālā vyāpiban kṣvelatoyam ||5||
kāla iti | sīrapāṇiṃ muktvā rāmaṃ vinā yāmunaṃ yamunāsambandhinaṃ kānanāntaṃ vanapradeśaṃ yāte tvayi | uddāmeti | pravṛddhagrīṣmakālotthena bhīṣmeṇoṣmaṇā taptāḥ kṣvelatoyaṃ viṣajalaṃ vyāpican yathāsukhaṃ papuḥ || 5 ||
naśyajjīvān vicyutān kṣmātale tān viśvān paśyannacyuta! tvaṃ dayārdraḥ|
prāpyopāntaṃ jīvayāmāsitha drāk pīyūṣāmbhovarṣibhiḥ śrīkaṭākṣaiḥ || 6 ||
naśyaditi | naśyajjīvān mriyamāṇān vicyutān patitān viśvān sarvān || 6 ||
kiṃ kiṃ jāto harṣavarṣātirekaḥ sarvāṅgeṣvityutthitā gopasaṅghāḥ |
dṛṣṭvāgre tvāṃ tvatkṛtaṃ tad vidantastvāmāliṅgan dṛṣṭanānāprabhāvāḥ || 7 ||
kimiti | gopasaṅghāstataḥ kiṃ kimidamaho sarvāṅgeṣu harṣavarṣātirekaḥ paramānandapīyūṣāplavo jāta iti svapnādivotthitāḥ prāgapyaghapūtanāmokṣādiṣu dṛṣṭanānāprabhāvatayā idaṃ viṣajalādū rakṣaṇaṃ tvatkṛtaṃ vidantastvāmāliṅgan ākṣleṣaṃ kṛtavantaḥ || 7 ||
gāvaścaivaṃ labdhajīvāḥ kṣaṇena sphītānandāstvāṃ ca dṛṣṭvā purastāt |
drāgāvavruḥ sarvato harṣabāṣpaṃ vyāmuñcantyo mandamudyanninādāḥ || 8 ||
gāva iti | gāvaścaivamutthitā dvāg jhaṭityāvavruḥ parivāya sthitāḥ || 8 || romāñco'yaṃ sarvato naḥ śarīre bhūyasyantaḥ kācidānandamūrchā | āśraryo'yaṃ kṣvelavego mukundetyukto gopairnandito vandito'bhūḥ || 9 ||
romāñca iti | he mukunda ! ayaṃ kṣvelavego viṣavega āścaryaḥ sarpadaṣṭānāmadṛṣṭapūrvaḥ | no'smākamantarhṛdi kācidaparicchedyānandasya niratiśayasukhasya mūrchā vṛddhiḥ bhūyasyatinibiḍā | kathaṃ jñāyate, śarīre sarvataḥ sarvatra romāñco'yaṃ paśyatu bhavāniti bhāvaḥ || 9||
evaṃ bhaktān muktajīvānapi tvaṃ mugdhāpāṅgairastarogāṃstanoṣi |
tādṛgbhūtasphītakāruṇyabhūmā rogāt pāyā vāyugehādhivāsa ! || 10 ||
evamiti | muktajīvān mṛtānapyastarogān labdhajīvāṃstyaktaśokāṃśca tanoṣi || 10 ||
iti kāliyamardane viṣajalena bhṛtānāṃ gogopānāmujjīvanavarṇanaṃ
catuṣpañcāśaṃ daśakam |
atha vāriṇi ghorataraṃ phaṇinaṃ prativārayituṃ kṛtadhīrbhagavan ! |
drutamāritha tīraganīpatarūṃ viṣamārutaśoṣitaparṇacayam || 1 ||
atheti | prativārayituṃ kṛtadhīrniścitabuddhirnīpataruṃ kadambavṛkṣamāritha prāptavān || 1 ||
adhiruhya padāmburuheṇa ca taṃ navapallavatulyamanojñarucā |
hṛdavāriṇi dūrataraṃ nyapataḥ parighūrṇitaghorataraṅgagaṇe || 2 ||
adhiruhyeti | navapallavatulyamanojñarucetyanena nīpatarurbhagavatsparśāt tyaktaviṣadoṣaḥ sañjātanavapallavaśca jāta ityadhaḥsthitānāṃ pratītirghotyate || 2 ||
bhuvanatrayabhārabhṛto bhavato gurubhāravikampivijṛmbhijalā |
parimajjayati sma dhanuḥśatakaṃ taṭinī jhaṭiti sphuṭaghoṣavatī || 3 ||
bhuvaneti | bhuvanatrayameva bhārastamudare bibhartī ti tathā, tādṛśasya bhavato gurubhāreṇa vikampi kampanaśīlaṃ vijṛmbhi vardhanaśīlaṃ ca jalaṃ yasyāṃ tathābhūtā taṭinī nadī jhaṭiti dhanuḥśatapramāṇaṃ parimajjayati sma plāvayāmāsa || 3||
atha dikṣu vidikṣu parikṣubhitabhramitodaravārininādabharaiḥ |
udakādudagāduragādhipatistvadupāntamaśāntaruṣāndhamanāḥ || 4 ||
atheti | dikṣvaṣṭasu vidikṣvavāntareṣu ca parikṣubhitaṃ paricalitaṃ bhramitaṃ sañjātāvartamudaramantarbhāgo yasya tādṛśaṃ yad vāri tasya ninādabharairhetubhūtairaśāntaruṣāndhaṃ mano yasya tādṛśaḥ san tvadupāntaṃ tvatsamīpamudagād unmajjya prāptavān || 4 ||
phaṇaśṛṅgasahasraviniḥsṛmarajvaladagnikaṇograviṣāmbudharam |
purataḥ phaṇinaṃ samalokayathā bahugṛṅgiṇamañjanaśailamiva || 5 ||
phaṇeti | phaṇaśṛṅgasahasrād viniḥsṛmaraṃ kṣaraṇaśīlaṃ jvaladagnikaṇamata evograṃ krūraṃ viṣāmbu viṣadravaṃ dharata iti tathā || 5 ||
jvaladakṣiparikṣaradugravipaśvasanoṣmabharaḥ sa mahābhujagaḥ |
paridaśya bhavantamanantavalaṃ samaveṣṭayadasphuṭaceṣṭamaho || 6 ||
jvaladiti | jvaladakṣi yathā bhavati tathā parikṣarannugraviṣaśvasanasyoṣmabharo yasya saḥ paridṛśya marmasu daṣṭvā samaveṣṭayat tadāsphuṭaceṣṭamadṛśyavyāpāram ||
avilokya bhavantamathākulite taṭagāmini bālakadhenugaṇe |
vrajagehatale'pyanimittaśataṃ samudīkṣya gatā yamunāṃ paśupāḥ || 7 ||
avilokyeti | animittaśataṃ durnimittaugham | paśupā nandādayaḥ || 7 ||
akhileṣu vibho ! bhavadīyadaśāmavalokya jihāsuṣu jīvabharam |
phaṇibandhanamāśu vimucya javādudagamyata hāsajuṣā bhavatā || 8 ||
akhileṣviti | jīvabharaṃ bhavadvirahe voḍhumaśakyān prāṇān jihāsuṣu vihātumicchatsu tebhya ātmakuśalajñāpanārthaṃ hāsajuṣā bhavatodagamyatotthitam ||8||
adhiruhya tataḥ phaṇirājaphaṇān nanṛte bhavatā mṛdupādarucā |
kalaśiñjitanūpuramañjumilatkarakaṅkaṇasaṃkulasaṃkkaṇitam || 9 ||
adhiruhyeti | tataḥ phaṇirājasva kāliyasya phaṇānadhiruhya bhavatā nanṛte nṛttamakāri | mṛdvī phaṇāmaṇirañjitatayā snigdhā pādarug yasya | kalaśiñjitau nūpurau yasmin tat kalaśiñjitanū puraṃ mañju manojñaṃ ca tālavyāpṛtatayā milatoḥ karayoḥ kaṅkaṇasaṃkulasya saṃkkaṇitaṃ yasminniti nartanakriyāviśeṣaṇam || 9 ||
jahaṣuḥ paśupāstutuṣurmunayo vaṭṭaṣuḥ kusumāni surendragaṇāḥ |
tvayi nṛtyati mārutagehapate ! paripāhi sa māṃ tvamadāntagadāt || 10 ||
jahapuriti | jahaṣurhaṣṭāḥ | tutuṣustuṣṭāḥ | adāntādavirataṃ vardhamānād gadādū vyādheḥ paripāhi || 10 ||
iti kāliyamardane kāliyaśirasi bhagavannartanavarṇanaṃ
pañcapañcāśaṃ daśakam |
rucirakampitakuṇḍalamaṇḍalaḥ suciramīśa ! nanartitha pannage |
amaratāḍitadundubhisundaraṃ viyati gāyati daivatayauvate || 1 ||
rucireti | daivatayauvate devastrīsamūhe'maratāḍitadundubhinundaraṃ yathā tathā viyati divi vimāneṣu gāyati sati pannage tatphaṇāsu suciraṃ nanartitha || 1 ||
namati yadyadamuṣya śiro hare! parivihāya tadunnatamunnatam |
parimathan padapaṅkaruhā ciraṃ vyaharathāḥ karatālamanoharam || 2 ||
namatīti | amuṣya phaṇino yadyacchiro bhagavatpadaparimathitatayā namati, tattat parivihāyonnatamunnataṃ tacchiraḥ padapaṅkaruhā parimathan vyaharathāḥ krīḍitavān || 2 ||
tvadavabhagnavibhugaphaṇāgaṇe galitaśoṇitaśoṇitapāthasi |
phaṇipatāvavasīdati sannatāstadavalāstava mādhava! pādayoḥ || 3 ||
tvaditi | tvayāvabhagno mardito vibhugnaḥ kuṭilo'vāṅmukhaḥ phaṇāgaṇo yasya | galitena vāntena śoṇitena raktena śoṇitamaruṇīkṛtaṃ pātho jalaṃ yena | avasīdati bhagnagātre sati tadabalā nāgapatnyaḥ || 3 ||
ayi puraiva cirāya pariśrutatvadanubhāvavilīnahṭado hi tāḥ |
munibhirapyanavāpyapathaiḥ stavairnunuvarīśa ! bhavantamayantritam || 4 ||
ayīti | purā pūrvameva cirāya ciraṃ pariśrutena tavānubhāvena māhātmyena vilīnahṛdaḥ kṣlathaddhṛdayāḥ ata eva munibhiraṇyanavāpyaḥ panthāḥ sakalaniṣkalarūpārthayojanarūpo mārgo yeṣu tādṛśaiḥ stavairayantritamanargalaṃ nunuvustuṣṭuvuḥ || 4 ||
phaṇivadhgaṇabhaktivilokanapravikasatkaruṇākulacetasā |
phaṇipatirbhavatācyuta ! jīvitastvayi samarpitamūrtiravānamat || 5 ||
phaṇīti | jīvito maraṇānnivartitastvayi samarpitamūrtiḥ sāṣṭāṅgapātamavānamad namaścakāra || 5 ||
ramaṇakaṃ vraja vāridhimadhyagaṃ phaṇiripurna karoti virodhitām |
iti bhavadvacanānyatimānayan phaṇipatirniragāduragaiḥ samam || 6||
ramaṇakamiti | ramaṇakaṃ ramaṇakākhyaṃ nāgānāṃ nivāsabhūtaṃ dvīpam | phaṇiripurgaruḍaḥ sāparādhe'pi tvayi virodhitāṃ na karoti || 6 ||
phaṇivadhūjanadattamaṇivrajajvalitahāraḍhukūlavibhūṣitaḥ |
taṭagataiḥ pramadāśruvimiśritaiḥ samagathāḥ svajanairdivasāvadhau || 7 ||
phaṇīti | pramadākṣruvimiśritaiḥ pramadābhirabhi kṣca, pramadāśrubhirharṣā śrubhirvā vimiśritaiḥ svajanaiḥ samagathāḥ saṅgato'bhūḥ || 7 ||
niśi punastamasā vrajamandiraṃ vrajitumakṣama eva janotkare |
svapati tatra bhavaccaraṇāśraye davakṛśānurarundha samantataḥ || 8 ||
niśīti | punaranantaraṃ niśi rātrau tamasā vrajamandirameva vrajituṃ gantumakṣame tatra kālindītaṭa eva svapati satyarundhāvṛṇot || 8 ||
prabudhitānatha pālaya pālayetyudayadārtaravān paśupālakān |
avitumāśu papātha mahānalaṃ kimiha citramayaṃ khalu te mukham || 9 ||
prabudhitāniti | atha prabudhitān kṛśānūṣmaṇā prabuddhānavituṃ trātuṃ papātha pītavān | iha mahānalapāne kiṃ citraṃ, yato'yaṃ kṛśānuste mukham || 9 ||
śikhini varṇata eva hi pītatā parilasatyadhunā kriyayāpyasau |
iti nutaḥ paśupairmuditairvibho! hara hare! duritaiḥ saha me gadān || 10 ||
śikhinīti | śikhinyagnau varṇato rūpabhedenaiva pītatā | adhunā kriyayā
pānakriyākarmatayāpyasau pītatā | he hare ! duritairgadakāraṇabhūtairduṣkarmaphalaiḥ saha gadān kāryabhūtān hara nāśaya || 10 ||
iti kāliyamardane kāliyasya bhagavadanugrahavarṇanaṃ bhagavato davānalapānavarṇanaṃ ca
ṣaṭpañcāśaṃ daśakam |
rāmasakhaḥ kvāpi dine kāmada ! bhagavan ! gato bhavān vipinam |
sunubhirapi gopānāṃ dhenubhirabhisaṃvṛto lasadveṣaḥ || 1 ||
rāmeti | rāmaḥ sakhā yasya sa bhavān lasadveṣaḥ śobhamānālaṅkṛtiviśeṣaḥ || 1 ||
sandarśayan balāya svairaṃ vṛndāvanaśriyaṃ vimalām |
kāṇḍīraiḥ saha bālairbhāṇḍīrakamāgamo vaṭaṃ krīḍan || 2 ||
sandarśayanniti | kāṇḍīraiyaṣṭīkairudatayaṣṭibhirityarthaḥ | bhāṇḍīrakaṃ nāma baṭamāgataḥ || 2 ||
tāvat tāvakanidhanaspṛhayālurgopamūrtiradayāluḥ |
daityaḥ pralambanāmā pralambabāhuṃ bhavantamāpede || 3 ||
tāvaditi | tāvakanidhanaspṛhayālustvannigrahecchāvān gopamūrtirgopaveṣaḥ adayāluḥ niṣkṛpaḥ pralambanāmā daityaḥ pralambabāhuṃ tādṛśavāhudvayayuktamāpede prāpa ||
jānannapyavijānanniva tena samaṃ nibaddhasauhārdaḥ |
vaṭanikaṭe paṭupaśupavyāvaddhaṃ dvandvayuddhamārabdhāḥ || 4||
jānanniti | jānannapi vairotpādanāya nibaddhaṃ sauhārdaṃ suhṛdbhāvo yena | vaṭasya nikaṭe samīpe paṭubhirdvandvayuddhakrīḍākovidaiḥ paśupaiḥ śrīdāmādibhirvyābaddhamitaretarasambaddhaṃ dvandvayuddhamārabdhāḥ prārabdhavān || 4 ||
rāmasya sakhā | byākhyānarītyā tu rāmasakheti pāṭhaḥ syāt |
gopān vibhajya tanvan saṅgaṃ valabhadrakaṃ bhavatkamapi |
tvadvalabhīruṃ daityaṃ tvaddhalagatamanvamanyathā bhagavan ! || 5 ||
gopāniti | gopān yogipratiyogibhedena vibhajya balabhadrakaṃ saṅgha bhavatakaṃ saṅgha ca tanvan | tvadvalabhīrumiti | pralambaścintitavān asmin dvandvayuddhe parājitā jetāraṃ vaheyuriti nyāyaḥ | diṣṭyā mithyāparājito'hamanayoranyataraṃ vahan nepyāmi | tatra rāmasaṅghasthaḥ kṛṣṇaṃ vahan kadācit tṛṇāvarta iva vipannaḥ syāmiti tvadvalādū bhīruṃ tvadvalagataṃ tvatsaṅghasthaṃ tvamanvamanyathā anujñātavān || 5 ||
kalpitavijetṛvahane samare parayūthagaṃ svadayitataram |
śrīdāmānamadhatthāḥ parājito bhaktadāsatāṃ prathayan || 6||
kalpiteti | kalpitaṃ vijetṝṇāṃ vahanaṃ yasmin | bhaktānāṃ macchira ākramya sthātumapi śakyamiti bhaktadāsatāṃ prathayan prakāśayan śrīdāmānamadhatthā ūḍhavān || 6 ||
evaṃ bahuṣu vibhūman! vāleṣu vahatsu vāhyamāneṣu |
rāmavijitaḥ pralambo jahāra taṃ dūrato bhavadbhītyā || 7 ||
evamiti | bhavadbhītyā rāmam | dūrata iti | vahannavarohaṇasthānāvadhi śanairgatvā tato jahāra kṣipraṃ nītavān || 7 ||
tvad dūraṃ gamayantaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā halini vihitagarimabhare |
daityaḥ svarūpamāgād yadrūpāt sa hi balo'pi cakito'bhūt || 8 ||
tvaditi | tvat tvatsakāśād dūraṃ dūradeśaṃ gamayantaṃ nayantaṃ taṃ daityaṃ saśaṅkaṃ dṛṣṭvā halini rāme vihitagarimabhara utpāditātmagaurave sati vahanākṣamatayā daityaḥ svarūpamatibhīṣaṇamāgāt | yasmādrūpād rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa hi balo'pi śeṣamūrtirapi mānuṣabhāvena cakita īṣat trasto'bhūt || 8 ||
balabhadranetṛkamityarthaḥ | evaṃ bhavatkam | ubhayatra ‘sa eṣāṃ grāmaṇīḥ' iti kan |
uccatayā daityatanostvanmukhamālokya dūrato rāmaḥ |
vigatabhayo dṛḍhamuṣṭayā bhṛśaduṣṭaṃ sapadi piṣṭavānenam || 9||
uccatayeti | uccatayonnatatayā tatskandhastho rāmo dūrato'pi tvanmukhamālokya bhṛśamatiśayena duṣṭaṃ dṛḍhayā mthirayā muṣṭayā sapadi jhaṭiti piṣṭavān nigṛhītavān || 9 ||
hatvā dānavavīraṃ prāptaṃ balamāliliṅgitha premṇā |
tāvanmilatoyuvayoḥ śirasi kṛtā puṣpavṛṣṭiramaragaṇaiḥ || 10 ||
hatveti | milatoḥ prakṛtilaye sakalaniṣkalaikyavadekībhūtayoḥ || 10 ||
ālambo bhuvanānāṃ prālambaṃ nidhanamevamāracayan |
kālaṃ vihāya sadyo lolambaruce ! hare ! hareḥ kleśān || 11 ||
ālamba iti | bhuvanānāmālamba āśrayaḥ prālambaṃ pralambasyedaṃ nidhanaṃ nigrahamevamuktaprakāreṇāracayan kurvan kālaṃ kālavilambaṃ vihāya tyaktvā harernāśaya ||
iti pralambāsuravadhavarṇanaṃ saptapañcāśaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
tvayi viharaṇalole vālajālaiḥ pralambapramathanasavilambe dhenavaḥ svairacārāḥ |
tṛṇakutukaniviṣṭā dūradūraṃ carantyaḥ
kimapi vipinamaiṣīkākhyamīṣāṃbabhūvuḥ || 1 ||
tvayīti | tvayi pralambamathanena savilambe sati iṣīkatṛṇabhūyiṣṭhatvādaiṣīkākhyaṃ vanamīṣāṃbabhūvurjagmuḥ || 1 ||
anadhigatanidāghakrauryabṛndāvanāntād
bahiridamupayātāḥ kānanaṃ dhenavastāḥ |
tava virahaviṣaṇṇā ūṣmalagrīṣmatāpaprasaravisaradambhasyākulāḥ stambhamāpuḥ || 2 ||
anadhigateti | anadhigatamaprāptaṃ nidāghakrauryaṃ grīṣmakālabhava ūṣmā yasmiṃstasmād bṛndāvanāntād bahirdeśamidamaiṣīkākhyaṃ kānanamupayātāḥ tava viraheṇa viṣaṇṇāśca ūṣmalo yo grīṣmastattāpaprasarairvisaradambhasi naṣṭajala iti bahirdeśaviśeṣaṇam | athavoṣmalagrīṣmatāpaprasareṇa visarantyā vyāptayāmbhasyayā pipāsayākulāḥ stambhaṃ niśceṣṭatāmāpuḥ prāpuḥ || 2 ||
tadanu saha sahāyairdūramanviṣya śaure !
galitasaraṇimuñjāraṇyasañjātakhedam |
paśukulamabhivīkṣya kṣipramānetumārāt
tvayi gatavati hī hī sarvato'gnirjajṛmbhe || 3 ||
tadanviti | galitasaraṇau bhraṣṭamārge muñjākhyatṛṇapradhāne'raṇye | tvayyārāt samīpaṃ gatavati hī hī kaṣṭaṃ kaṣṭaṃ davāgniḥ sarvato gopa gavāṃ parito jajambhe prajvalito'bhūt || 3 ||
sakalahariti dīpte ghorabhāṅkārabhīme
śikhini vihatamārgā ardhadagdhā ivārtāḥ |
ahaha bhuvanavandho ! pāhi pāhīti sarve
śaraṇamupagatāstvāṃ tāpahartāramekam || 4 ||
sakaleti | sakalaharityaṣṭasu dikṣu | vihatamārgā bahirgantumapārayantastvāṃ śaraṇamupagatāḥ || 4||
alamalamatibhītyā sarvato mīlayadhvaṃ
dṛśamiti tava vācā mīlitākṣeṣu teṣu |
kkanu davadahano'sau kutra muñjāṭavī sā
sapadi vavṛtire te hanta bhāṇḍīradeśe || 5 ||
alamiti | sarvataḥ sarve sapadi punarnayanonmīlanānantarameva bhāṇḍīradeśe
vavṛtire sthitā āsan | bhagavatā yogaiśvaryeṇa pūrvasthānaṃ nītāsta iti jñātavyam || 5 ||
jaya jaya tava māyā keyamīśeti teṣāṃ
nutibhiruditahāso baddhanānāvilāsaḥ |
punarapi vipinānte prācaraḥ pāṭalādiprasavanikaramātragrāhyadharmānubhāve || 6 ||
jayeti | he īśa! jaya jaya lokottaro bhūyāḥ | keyaṃ tava māyā, yata aiṣīkasthā vayaṃ mīlitākṣāḥ santo bhāṇḍīravaṭamūlasthāḥ smaḥ | pāṭaleti | pāṭalaśirīṣāditarukusumamātreṇa grāhyo gharmānubhāva ūṣmā yasmiṃstasmin prācaraḥ sañcaraṇaṃ cakṛṣe || 6 ||
tvayi vimukhamivoccaistāpabhāraṃ vahantaṃ
tava bhajanavadantaḥ paṅkamucchoṣayantam |
taba bhrujavadudaścadbhūritejaḥpravāhaṃ
tapasamayamanaiṣīryāmuneṣu sthaleṣu || 7 ||
tvayīti | tvaṃ yāmuneṣu sthaleṣu yamunātīreṣu krīḍan tapasamayaṃ grīṣmakālamanaiṣīḥ | tapasamayaṃ viśinaṣṭi -- tvayīti | tvayīśvare vimukhaṃ sevārahitamiva tāpabhāraṃ vahantaṃ prāpayantam | asevakapakṣe tāpabhāraṃ cittasantāpaṃ vahantaṃ dadhatam | tava bhajanavadantarnadyādeḥ manasi vā paṅkaṃ kardamaṃ duritaṃ vā | tapasamayapakṣe teja ātapaḥ anyatra parākramaḥ || 7 ||
tadanu jaladajālaistvadvapustulyabhābhirvikasadamalavighatpītavāsovilāsaiḥ |
sakalabhuvanabhājāṃ harṣadāṃ varṣavelāṃ
kṣitidharakuhareṣu svairavāsī vyanaiṣīḥ || 8||
tadanviti | tvadvapustulyā bhāḥ śyāmalā dīptiryeṣām | vikasantyaḥ śobhamānā amalā vighuta eva pītaṃ vāsastadvilāsāḥ śobhā yeṣām | tvadvapurapi pītavāso
vilāsayuktam| santāpaśāntyā sasyasampattyā vā harṣadām| kṣitidharakuhareṣu govardhanaguhāsu svairavāsī krīḍan vyanaiṣīḥ || 8 ||
kuharatalaniviṣṭaṃ tvāṃ gariṣṭhaṃ girīndraḥ
śikhikulanavakekākākubhiḥ stotrakārī |
sphuṭakuṭajakadambastomapuṣpāñjaliṃ ca
pravidadhadanubheje deva ! govardhano'sau || 9 ||
kuhareti | kuharatalaniviṣṭamātmaguhāśritaṃ gariṣṭhaṃ mahānubhāvaṃ tvāmīśvaraṃ girandrio govardhanaḥ śikhikulānāṃ navā varṣārambhodgatāḥ kekāstadravā eva kākavo bhaktyatiśaya janitoktiviśeṣāstābhiḥ stotrakārī sphuṭānāṃ vikasitānāṃ kuṭajānāṃ girimallikākusumānāṃ kadambakusumānāṃ (ca) stomena samūhena puṣpāñjaliṃ pravidadhat kurvannanubheje upāsāñcake ||| 9 ||
atha śaradamupetāṃ tāṃ bhavadbhaktacetovimalasalilapūrāṃ mānayan kānaneṣu |
tṛṇamamalavanānte cāru sañcārayan gāḥ
pavanapurapate! tvaṃ dehi me dehasaukhyam || 10 ||
atheti| bhavadbhaktacetobad vimalaḥ salilapūro yasyāṃ tāmabhisarantīṃ nāyikāmivopetāṃ śaradaṃ mānayan | tṛṇaṃ sañcārayan bhakṣayan | me dehasākhyamārogyam, athavā dehasaukhyaṃ videhakaivalyam || 10 ||
iti gogopānāṃ dāvāgnimokṣavarṇanam ṛtuvarṇanaṃ ca
aṣṭapañcāśaṃ daśakam |
tvadvapurnavakalāyakomalaṃ premadohanamaśeṣamohanam |
brahma tattva para cinmudātmakaṃ vīkṣya sammumuhuranbahaṃ striyaḥ || 1 ||
tvāditi | tvadvapurbrahma, tacca tattvaparacinmudātmakaṃ saccidānandātmakam | ataḥ premadohanaṃ rāgavardhanam | striyo gopyaḥ sammumuhuḥ || 1 ||
manmathonmathitamānasāḥ kramāt tvadvilokanaratāstatastataḥ |
gopikāstava na sehire hare ! kānanopagatimapyaharmukhe || 2 ||
manmatheti | gopikāstatastataḥ punaḥ punaḥ kramād prati sakramotkarṣam | aharmukhe'pi na sarvadā, kānanopagatimapi na vidūragamanaṃ, na sehire na marṣitavatyaḥ || 2 ||
nirgate bhavati dattadṛṣṭayastvadgatena manasā mṛgekṣaṇāḥ |
veṇunādamupakarṇya dūratastvadvilāsakathayāmiremire || 3
||
nirgata iti | nirgate prasthite bhavati tvāyi dattā nikṣiptā dṛṣṭayo yāsām ata eva tvadgatena manasā tyaktagṛhakṛtyā dūrato'pi veṇunādamukarṇya śrutvā tvadvilāsasya kathayānyonyakathanenābhiremire kālaṃ ninyuḥ || 3 ||
kānanāntamitavān bhavānapi snigdhapādapatale manorame|
vyatyayākalitapādamāsthitaḥ pratyapūrayata veṇunālikām || 4 ||
kānanāntamiti | vyatyayena vyatyāsenākalito dhṛtau pādau yasmiṃstathā āsthito veṇunālikāṃ pratyapūrayata || 4 ||
māravāṇadhutakhecarīkulaṃ nirvikārapaśupakṣimaṇḍalam |
drāvaṇaṃ ca dṛṣadāmapi prabho! tāvakaṃ vyajaniṃ veṇukūjitam || 5 ||
