māreti | dhutaṃ janitakaṃmpasvedādikaṃ khecarīkulaṃ yena | dṛṣadāmupalānāmapi drāvaṇaṃ dravaṇasādhanaṃ vyajani jātam || 5 ||
veṇurandhrataralāṅgulīdalaṃ tālasañcalitapādapallavam |
tat sthitaṃ taba parokṣamapyaho saṃvicintya mumuhurvrajāṅganāḥ || 6||
veṇviti | vrajāṅganā gopyastad vyatyastapādaṃ tava sthitamavasthānaṃ parokṣamapratyakṣamapi saṃvicintya mumuhuḥ || 6 ||
nirviśaṅkabhavadaṅgadarśinīḥ khecarīḥ khagamṛgān paśūnapi |
tvatpadamaṇayi kānanaṃ ca tā dhanyadhanyamiti nanvamānayan || 7 ||
nirviśaṅketi | nirviśaṅkabhavadanaṅgadarśinīstiraskṛtaśarīratayā sannihitatayā nimeṣarahitatayā ca nityaṃ bhavadaṅgapratyaṅgadarśanaśīlāḥ | tvatpadapraṇāyi bhavatpadāmbujasaṃsargi kānanamapi dhanyadhanyam atiśayena bhāgyavaditi tā vrajasthāḥ paśupāṅganā amānayan || 7||
āpiveyamadharāmṛtaṃ kadā veṇubhuktarasaśeṣamekadā |
dūrato bata kṛtaṃ durāśayetyākulā muhurimāḥ samāsuhan || 8 ||
āpibeyamiti | veṇunā bhukto rasaviśeṣo yasmin tadadharāmṛtam | tad dūrato'mmākaṃ na sambhavati | tatra durāśayā kṛtamalam | samāmuhan sammumuhuḥ || 8 ||
pratyahaṃ ca pu naritthamaṅganāścittayonijanitādanugrahāt |
baddharāgavivaśāstvayi prabho ! nityamāpuriha kṛtyamūḍhatām || 9 ||
pratyahamiti | anugrahād na tūpadravāt | kutaḥ, yatastvayi baddharāgavivaśāḥ śṛṅgārākhyapremalakṣaṇabhaktivivaśā ih kṛtyamūḍhatām idaṃ mayaiva kartavyamityavadhāraṇavidhuratāmāpuḥ || 9||
rāgastāvajjāyate hi svabhāvānmokṣopāyo yatnataḥ syānna vā syāt |
tāsāṃ tvekaṃ tad dvayaṃ labdhamāsīd bhāgyaṃ bhāgyaṃ pāhi māṃ māruteśa ! ||10||
rāga iti | rāgastāvat strīṇāṃ puṃsāṃ vā mithaḥ svabhāvādeva jāyate | mokṣopāyastvīśvare prema keṣāñcideva prayatnataḥ syādvā na vā | tāsāṃ vrajāṅganānāṃ taddūyaṃ kāmasukhadaṃ kāntaprema mokṣasukhadamīśvaraprema caikaṃ labdhamāsīd ekasya kṛṣṇasyaiva kāntatvādīśvaratvācca | aho bhāgyaṃ bhāgyam || 10 ||
iti veṇugānavarṇanam ekonaṣaṣṭitamaṃ daśakam |
madanāturacetaso'nvahaṃ bhavadaṅaghridvayadāsyakāmyayā |
yamunātaṭasīmni saikatī taralākṣyo girijāṃ samārcican || 1 ||
madaneti | saikatīṃ sikatāmayīṃ pratimāṃ samārcican pūjayāmāsuḥ || 1 ||
tava nāmakathāratāḥ samaṃ sudṛśaḥ mātarupāgatā nadīm |
upahāraśatairapūjayan dayito nandasuto bhavediti || 2 ||
taveti | tava nāmni kathāyāṃ ca ratā nāma kīrtayantyaḥ kathāḥ kathayantyaśca samaṃ sambhūyopahāraśatairjalagandha puṣpadhūpādibhirnandasuto dayito bhavediti prārthayantya iti śeṣaḥ || 2 ||
iti māsamupāhitavratāstaralākṣīrabhivīkṣya tā bhavān |
karuṇāmṛdulo nadītaṭaṃ samayāsīt tadanugrahecchayā || 3 ||
itīti | upāhitavratā dhṛtavratāḥ || 3 ||
niyamāvasitau nijāmbaraṃ taṭasīmanyavamucya tāstadā |
yamunājalakhelanākulāḥ puratastvāmavalokya lajitāḥ || 4 ||
niyameti | niyamāvasitau vratāvasāne nijāmbaramavamucya nagnā yamunāyāṃ jalakhelana udakavādyādāvākulāścalitāḥ || 4 ||
trapayā namitānanāsvatho vanitāsvambarajālamantike |
nihitaṃ parigṛhya bhūruho viṭapaṃ taṃ tarasādhirūḍhavān || 5||
trapayeti | trapayā dayitasannidhau vivastratājanitalajjayā | bhūruho vṛkṣasya || 5 ||
iha tāvadupetya nīyatāṃ vasanaṃ vaḥ sudṛśo ! yathāyatham |
iti narmamṛdusmite tvayi bruvati vyāmumuhe vadhūjanaiḥ || 6 ||
iheti | he sudṛśaḥ ! ihopetya vo vasanaṃ yathāyathaṃ svaṃ svaṃ nīyatām | narmaṇā mṛdu smitaṃ yasmistasmistvayi bruvati sati vadhūjanairvyāmumuhe tat kartuṃ mā maivamiti vaditumapyasamarthā babhūvurityarthaḥ || 6 ||
ayi jīva ciraṃ kiśora ! nastava dāsīravaśīkaroṣi kim |
pradiśāmvaramambujekṣaṇetyuditastvaṃ smitameva dattavān || 7 ||
ayīti | ayi kiśora kumāraka! tvaṃ ciraṃ jīva, tava dāsīḥ proktakāriṇīrnaḥ kiṃ kimarthamavaśīkaroṣi paravaśāḥ karoṣi, ambaraṃ pradiśa dehītyudito yācitastvaṃ smitameva dattavān nāmbaramapi | ayi kiśora! aprāptavivāha ! tava dāsīḥ premārhāḥ kimavaśīkaroṣi vaśīkuru parigṛhāṇa, ambaraṃ pradiśa, naḥ patistvaṃ, he ambujekṣaṇa! tvatkaṭākṣavirahe vayaṃ parādhīnajīvitā iti sābhiprāyamivoditatvāt tvaṃ tadanumodanarūpaṃ mandasmitaṃ dattavān || 7 ||
adhiruhya taṭaṃ kṛtāñjalīḥ pariśuddhāḥ svagatīrnirīkṣya tāḥ |
vasanānyakhilānyanugrahaṃ punarevaṃ giramapyadā mudā || 8 ||
adhiruhyeti | pariśuddhāḥ tvannamanarūpaprāyaścittād vivastrālavanarūpavratabhaṅgadoṣānnivṛttāḥ svagatīrātmaśaragāḥ | anugraharūpāmevaṃ vakṣyamāṇarūpāṃ giramayadāḥ dattavān || 8 ||
viditaṃ nanu vo manīṣitaṃ vaditārastviha yogyamuttaram |
yamunāpuline sacandrikāḥ kṣaṇadā ityavalāstvamūcivān || 9 ||
viditamiti | manīṣitaṃ saṅkalpaḥ | iha vo manīṣite yogyamuttaraṃ tathāstviti idānīṃ nāhaṃ vadāmi | kintu yamunāpuline sacandrikāḥ kṣaṇadā bhavatībhyo yogyamuttaraṃ kevalaṃ kriyayaiva vaditāro vadiṣyantīti || 9 ||
upakarṇya bhavanmukhacyutaṃ madhuniṣyandi vaco mṛgīdṛśaḥ |
praṇayādayi vīkṣya vīkṣya te vadanābjaṃ śanakairgṛhaṃ gatāḥ || 10 ||
upakarṇye̍ti | upakarṇya śrotrāñjalibhiḥ pītvā || 10 ||
iti nanvanugṛhya vallarvīpinānteopu pureva sañcaran |
karuṇāśiśiro hare ! hara tvarayā meṃ sakalāmayāvalim || 11 ||
itīti | karuṇayā bhaktavātsalyena śiśira ārdrahṛdayaḥ || 11 ||
iti gopīvastrāpahāravarṇanaṃ paṣṭitamaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
tataśca vṛndāvanato'tidūrato vanaṃ gatastvaṃ khalu gopagokulaiḥ |
hṛdantare bhaktataradvijāṅganākadamvakānugrahaṇāgrahaṃ vahan || 1 ||
tata iti | vṛndāvanato bṛndāvanādatiṃdūra to'tidūre || 1 ||
tato nirīkṣyāśaraṇe vanāntare kiśoralokaṃ kṣudhitaṃ tṛṣākulam |
adūrato yajñaparān dvijān prati vyasarjayo dīdiviyācanāya tān ||
tata iti | aśaraṇe gṛharahite kiśoralokaṃ bālajanaṃ tṛṣā pipāsayā kṣudhā cākulam | adūratastatpradeśāt samīpe yajñaparān karmapradhānatayā bhaktijñānavimukhān dīdiviyācanāyānnayācanāya tān gopān vyasarjayaḥ preṣayāmāsitha || 2 ||
gateṣvatho teṣvabhidhāya te'bhidhāṃ kumārakeṣvodanayāciṣu prabho ! |
śrutisthirā apyabhininyuraśrutiṃ na kiścidūcuśva mahīsurottamāḥ || 3 ||
gateṣviti | te'bhidhāṃ kṛṣṇo va odanaṃ yācata iti tava nāmābhidhāya | śrutau sthirā niścita buddhayo'pyaśrutiṃ śravaṇābhāvamabhininyurabhinītavantaḥ | mahīsurottamā brāhmaṇaśreṣṭhammanyāḥ kiñcid dāsyāmi neti vā nocurapi || 3 ||
anādarāt khinnadhiyo hi bālakāḥ samāyayuryuktamidaṃ hi yajvasu |
cirādabhaktāḥ khalu te mahīsurāḥ kathaṃ hi bhaktaṃ tvayi taiḥ samaryate ||4||
anādarāditi | yajvasuṃ yāyajūkeṣvidamarthinirākaraṇaṃ yuktam | bhaktamannam || 4 ||
nivedayadhvaṃ gṛhiṇījanāya māṃ diśeyurannaṃ karuṇākulā imāḥ |
iti smitārdaṃ bhavatoretā gatāste dārakā dārajanaṃ yayācire || 5 ||
nivedayadhvamiti | imā yajvapatnyaḥ annaṃ diśeyurdadhuritīritā uktāste dārakāḥ kumārā dārajanaṃ gṛhiṇījanaṃ yayācire yācitavantaḥ || 5 ||
gṛhītanāmni tvayi sambhramākulāścaturvidha bhojyarasaṃ pragṛhya tāḥ |
ciraṃ dhṛtatvatpravilokanāgrahāḥ svakairniruddhā api tūrṇamāyayuḥ || 6 ||
gṛhīteti | gṛhītanāmni kṛṣṇo'smanmukhena bhavatīrodanaṃ yācata iti tvannāmaśravaṇamātra eva tā dārajanāścaturvidhaṃ bhojyakhādyapeyalehyarūpaṃ bhojyarasaṃ rasavad bhojyam | svakairbhartrādimirniruddhā api tūrṇamāyuḥ || 6 ||
vilolapiñchaṃ cikure kapolayoḥ samullasatkuṇḍalamārdramīkṣite |
nighāya bāhuṃ suhṛdaṃsasīmani sthitaṃ bhavantaṃ samalokayanta tāḥ || 7||
viloleti | īkṣite kaṭākṣe ārdaṃ vātsalyātiśayajanitānandajalārdram || 7 ||
tadā ca kācit tvadupāgamoghatā gṛhītahastā dayitena yajvanā |
tadaiva sañcintya bhavantamañjasā viveśa kaivalyamaho kṛtinyasau || 8 ||
tadeti | tadā itarāsa prasthitāsu kācid yajvapatnī tvadupagame udyatā yajvanā gṛhītahastā pāpe! kimidaṃ pratikūlācaraṇamiti pratiruddhā bhavantaṃ sañcintya niratiśayabhavatsaṅkalpasukhānubhavena bhavadvirahajanitasantāpaduḥkhānubhavena ca kṣīṇasukṛtaduṣkṛtā añjasānāyāsena kaivalyaṃ mokṣaṃ viveśa | kṛtinī bhāgyavatī || 8 ||
ādāya bhojyānyanugṛhya tāḥ punastvadaṅgasa isvṛhayojjhatīrgṛham |
vilokya yajñāya visarjayannimāñcakartha bhartanapi tāsvagarhaṇān || 9 ||
ādāyeti | tvadaṅgasaṅgaspṛhayā tvayā saha riraṃsayā gṛhaṃ sānubandhamujjhatīstyajatīḥ vilokyānugṛhya tāsvagarhaṇān bahumānayuktāṃścakartha || 9 ||
nirūpya ḍhoṣaṃ nijamaṅganājane vilokya bhaktiṃ ca punarvicāribhiḥ |
prabuddhatattvaistvamabhiṣṭuto dvijairmarutpurādhīśa! nirundhi me gadān || 10 ||
nirūpyeti | nijaṃ doṣamaṅganājane bhaktiṃ ca nirūpya prabuddhaṃ tattvaṃ yaistairdvijairabhiṣṭutaḥ || 10 ||
iti yajvapatnyuddharaṇavarṇanamekaṣaṣṭitamaṃ daśakam |
kadācid gopālān vihitamakhasambhāravibhavān
nirīkṣya tvaṃ śaure ! maghavamadamuddhvaṃsitumanāḥ |
vijānannapyetān vinayamṛdu nandādipaśupānapṛcchaḥ ko vāyaṃ janaka ! bhavatāmudyama iti || 1 ||
kadāciditi | vihitā upārjitā makhasambhāravibhavā indrayāgasādhanarūpapadārthā yaistānetān nandādipaśupān vinayena mṛdu anuddhataṃ yathā bhavati tathā apṛcchaḥ || 1 ||
vabhāṣe nandastvāṃ suta ! nanu vidheyo maghavato
makho varṣe varṣe mukhayati sa varṣeṇa pṛthivīm |
nṛṇāṃ varṣāyattaṃ nikhilamupajīvyaṃ mahitale
viśeṣādasmākaṃ tṛṇasalilajīvā hi paśavaḥ || 2 ||
babhāṣa iti | upajīvyaṃ sasyādi | asmākaṃ paśuvṛttīnāṃ viśeṣād, yatastṛṇasalilajīvanāḥ paśavaḥ || 2 ||
iti śrutvā vācaṃ piturayi bhavānāha sarasaṃ
dhigetanno satyaṃ maghavajanitā vṛṣṭiriti yat |
adṛṣṭaṃ jīvānāṃ sṛjati khalu dṛṣṭiṃ samucitāṃ
mahāraṇye vṛkṣāḥ kimiva balimindrāya dadate || 3 ||
itīti | dhig aho andhaparamparā mahatāṃ mano'pi spṛśati, yato maghavajanitā vṛṣṭirityetanno satyam | yad yasmājjīvānāmadṛṣṭaṃ karmaphalaṃ khalu vṛṣṭiṃ sṛjati, na maghavā | so'pyadṛṣṭenaivaindraṃ padamāptaḥ | atra hetumāha -- mahāraṇya iti | akṛtendrayāgānāṃ vṛkṣāṇāmapi vṛṣṭikṛtayogakṣemadarśanādityarthaḥ || 3 ||
idaṃ tāvat satyaṃ yadiha paśavo naḥ kuladhanaṃ
tadājīvyāyāsau baliracalabhartre samucitaḥ |
surebhyo'pyutkṛṣṭā nanu dharaṇidevāḥ kṣititale
tataste'pyārādhyā iti jagaditha tvaṃ nijajanān || 4 ||
idamiti | yadihoktaṃ paśavo naḥ kurudhanamiti, tadidaṃ tāvat satyam | atastadājīvyāya tṛṇasalilasampattaye'sau prārabdho baliracalabhartre govardhanāya, yato'calo nastṛṇaṃ jalaṃ ca dadāti, ataḥ samucitaḥ | phiñca dharaṇidevā brāhmaṇāḥ, ataste'pyārādhyāḥ pūjanīyā iti jagaditha || 4 ||
bhavadrācaṃ śrutvā bahumatiyutāste'pi paśupā
dvijendrānarcanto valimadaduruccaiḥ kṣitibhṛte |
vyadhuḥ prādakṣiṇyaṃ subhṛśamanamannādarayutāstvamādaḥ śailātmā valimakhilamābhīrapurataḥ || 5 ||
bhavaditi | uccaiḥ pūrvasmād viśiṣṭabahusādhanopabṛṃhitaṃ bali pūjāṃ adaduḥ | vyadhurvidadhire | tvaṃ śailātmā śailavadatimahad vapurgṛhītvā ābhīrāṇāṃ gopānāṃ purataḥ śailo'smi prasanno'smīti bruvan balimādaḥ abhuṅkthāḥ || 5 ||
avocaścaivaṃ tān kimiha vitathaṃ meṃ nigaditaṃ
girīndro nanveṣa svabalimupabhuṅkte svavapuṣā |
ayaṃ gotro gotradviṣi ca kupite rakṣitumalaṃ
samastānityuktā jahapurakhilā gokulajuṣaḥ || 6 ||
avoca iti | rūpāntareṇa gopasamūhasthaśca tvaṃ tān gopānevaṃ vakṣyamāṇapra
kāramavocaḥ | iha me nigaditaṃ kiṃ vitaśramasatyam | ayaṃ mūrtimattvena dṛśyamāno gotro govardhanākhyo gotradviṣi indre cāpi kupite rakṣitumalaṃ śaktaḥ || 6 ||
pariprītā yātāḥ khalu bhavadupetā brajajuṣo
vrajaṃ yāvat tāvannijamakhavibhaṅgaṃ niśamayan |
bhavantaṃ jānannapyadhikarajasākrāntahṛdayo
na sehe devendrastvaduparacitātmonnatirapi || 7 ||
pariprītā iti | nijamakhavibhaṅgaṃ niśamayan śṛṇvan bhavantaṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ viṣṇuṃ jānannapi adhikarajasā tatkāryarāgādibhirātrkāntaṃ hṛdayaṃ yasya saḥ nijamakhavibhaṅgaṃ na sehe | tvaduparacitā tvatkṛtā ātmonnatiraindraṃ padaṃ yasya || 7 ||
manuṣyatvaṃ yāto madhubhidapi deveṣvavinayaṃ
vidhatte cennaṣṭastridaśasadasāṃ ko'pi mahimā |
tataśca dhvaṃsiṣye paśupahatakasya śriyamiti
pravṛttastvāṃ jetuṃ sa kila maghavā durmadanidhiḥ || 8 ||
manuṣyatvamiti | madhubhid viṣṇurapi manuṣyatvaṃ yāto nikṛṣṭo jātaḥ | ayaṃ madhubhicced deveṣvavinayaṃ na vidhatte, yadi ced vidhatte tarhi na madhubhidayamiti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ cet tridaśasadasāṃ devasabhānāṃ ko'pyanirdeśyo mahimā naṣṭa eva | paśupahatakasya gopālādhamasya kṛṣṇasya || 8 ||
tvadāvāsaṃ hantuṃ pralayajaladānambarabhuvi
prahiṇvan vibhrāṇaḥ kuliśamayamabhrebhagamanaḥ |
pratasthe'nyairantardahana marudādyairvihasito
bhavanmāyā naiva tribhuvanapate ! mohayati kam || 9 ||
tvaditi | prahiṇvan muñcan athrebhagamana airāvatamārūḍhaḥ anyairdahanamarudādyairagnivāyvādibhirantarhadi vihasitaḥ parihasitaḥ | bhavanmāyā kaṃ naiva mohayati || 9 ||
surendraḥ kuddhakṣced dvijakaruṇayā śailakṛpayāpyanātaṅko'smākaṃ niyata iti viśvāsya paśupān |
aho kiṃ nāyāto giribhiditi sañcintya nivasan
marudgehādhīśa ! praṇuda muravairin ! mama gadān || 10 ||
surendra iti | ātaṅkāt saṅkaṭādanyo'nātaṅkaḥ sukhaṃ niyato niścitaḥ | giribhidindraḥ kupitaḥ kiṃ nāyāta iti sañcintya nivasan | praṇuda tyaja || 10 ||
iti indrayāgavighātavarṇanaṃ dviṣaṣṭitamaṃ daśakam |
dahaśire kila tatkṣaṇamakṣatastanitajṛmbhitakampitadiktaṭāḥ |
suṣamayā bhavadaṅgatulāṃ gatā brajapadopari vāridharāstvayā || 1 ||
dadṛśira iti | akṣatenāvicchinnena stanitena jṛmbhitena meghavyāptyā ca kampitā diktaṭāḥ tatsthāścarācarā yaiḥ | suṣamayā śobhyā bhavadaṅgasya tulāmupamāṃ gatā vāridharāstvayā vrajapadasya tvadgṛhasyopari dadṛśire adṛśyanta || 1 ||
vipulakarakamiśraistoyadhārānipātairdiśi diśi paśupānāṃ maṇḍale daṇḍyamāne |
kupitaharikṛtānnaḥ pāhi pāhīti teṣāṃ
vacanamajita ! śṛṇvan mā bibhītetyabhāṇīḥ || 2 ||
vipuleti | vipulakarakamiśraiḥ sthūlajalaśarkarāmiśraiḥ | kupitena hariṇendreṇa kṛtād varṣāditi śeṣaḥ | mā vibhīta bhayaṃ mā kurutetyabhāṇīravādīḥ || 2 ||
kula iha khalu gotro daivataṃ gotraśatrovihatimiha sa rundhyāt ko nu vaḥ saṃśayo'smin |
iti sahasitavādī deva! govardhanādri
tvaritamudamumūlo mūlato bāla! dorbhyām || 3 ||
kula iti | iha kule gotraḥ parvato daivatam | sa govardhano gotraśatrorindrājjātāṃ vihatiṃ nāśaṃ rundhyāt | asmin madukte vaḥ kaḥ saṃśayaḥ, yato gotrāṇād gotro govardhana iti cāsya saṃjñā | udamumūla unmūlitavānasi || 3 ||
tadanu girivarasya proddhṛtasyāsya tāvat
sikatilamṛdudeśe dūrato vāritāpe |
parikaraparimiśrān dhenugopānadhastādupanidadhadadhatthā hastapadmena śailam || 4 ||
tadanviti | sikatile vālukāprāye mṛduni snigdhe deśe vāritā bahiṣkṛtā āpo yasmāt tatra girivarasyādhastāt parikaraiḥ śikyabhāṇḍādibhiḥ parimiśrān upanidadhat praveśayan adhatthā dhṛtavān || 4 ||
bhavati vidhṛtaśaile bālikābhirvayasyairapi vihitavilāsa kelilāpādilole |
savidhamilitadhenūrekahastena kaṇḍūyati sati paśupālāstoṣamaipanta sarve || 5 ||
bhavatīti | kelilāpādau krīḍāsallām̐pādau lole sakutuke toṣaṃ santoṣamaiṣanta | bālikābālanarmālāpaikahastadhenukaṇḍūyanādibhirapagatācalapatanabhayāḥ santoṣaṃ prāptā ityarthaḥ || 5 ||
atimahān girireṣa tu vāmake karasaroruhi taṃ dharate ciram |
kimidamadbhutamadribalaṃ nviti tvadavalokibhisakathi gopakaiḥ || 6 ||
atīti | karasaroruhi karābje dharate dadhāti, kimidamadrerbalaṃ pakṣarahitasyāpyuḍḍayanaśaktirnu ityākathi kathyate sma || 6 ||
ahaha dhāṣṭaryamamuṣya vaṭorgiriṃ vyathitabāhurasābavaropayet |
iti haristvayi vaddhavigarhaṇo divasasaptakamugramavarṣayat || 7 ||
ahaheti | vyathitaḥ prāptasādo bāhuryasya | avaropayed adhaḥ sthāpayet | baddhavigarhaṇaḥ sañjātanindaḥ hariḥ indraḥ divasasaptakamugramavarṣayat || 7 ||
acalati tvayi deva ! padāt padaṃ galitasarvajale ca dhanotkare |
apahṛte marutā marutāṃ patistvadabhiśaṅkitadhīḥ samupādravat || 8 ||
acalatīti | tvayi padāt svasthānāt padapramāṇaṃ deśamapi acalāti agacchati sati galitasarvajale nissāre ata eva marutā vāyunā apahṛte tvatsakāśādū abhiśaṅkitadhīrbhītaḥ | athavā tvadabhiśaṅkitā viṣṇuḥ svayamayamiti saśaṅkā dharyisya | samupādravat samprāptavān || 8 ||
śamamupeyuṣi varṣabhare tadā paśupadhenukule ca vinirgate |
bhuvi vibho ! samupāhitabhūdharaḥ pramuditaiḥ paśupaiḥ parirebhiṣe || 9 ||
śamamiti | upeyuṣi prāptavati | bhuvi samupāhitabhūdharaḥ yathāsthānaṃ nikṣiptagovardhanaḥ tvaṃ parirebhiṣe ākṣliṣṭo'bhūḥ || 9 ||
dharaṇimeva purā dhṛtavānasi kṣitidharoddharaṇe tava kaḥ śramaḥ
iti nutastridaśaiḥ kamalāpate! gurupurālaya ! pālaya māṃ gadāt || 10 ||
dharaṇimiti | purā varāhāvatāre || 10 ||
iti govardhanoddharaṇavarṇanaṃ triṣaṣṭitamaṃ daśakam |
ālokya śailoddharaṇādirūpaṃ prabhāvamuccaistava gopalokāḥ |
viśveśvaraṃ tvāmabhimatya viśve nandaṃ bhavajjātakamanvapṛcchan || 1 ||
ālokyeti | viśve sarve gopālakāstavoccaiḥ utkṛṣṭaṃ prabhāvamālokya tvāṃ viśveśvaramavabudhya nandaṃ prati bhavato jātakamanvapṛcchan || 1 ||
gargodito nirgadito nijāya vargāya tātena tava prabhāvaḥ |
pūrvādhikastvayyanurāga epāmaidhiṣṭa tāvad bahumānabhāraḥ || 2 ||
gargodita iti | tātena nandena gargoditaḥ 'jepyati bahutaradaityān' ityevaṃrūpaḥ tava prabhāvo nijāya vargāya gopasamūhāya nirgadita upadiṣṭaḥ ||
tato'vamānoditatattvavodhaḥ surādhirājaḥ saha divyagavyā |
upetya tuṣṭāva sa naṣṭagarvaḥ spṛṣṭvā padāvjaṃ maṇimaulinā te || 3 ||
tata iti | tataḥ avamānena parājayena uditatattvabodhaḥ madarthamavatīrṇo matsvāmyayamiti | divyagavī surabhiḥ, tathā sah || 3 ||
snehastutaistvāṃ surabhiḥ payobhirgovindanāmāṅkitamabhyapiñcat |
airāvatopāhṛtadivyagaṅgāpāthobhirindro'pi ca jātaharṣaḥ || 4 ||
sneheti | snehena snutaiḥ kṣaritaiḥ surabhirabhyaṣiñcad ata eva govindanāmāṅkitam indro'pi divyagaṅgāyāḥ pāthobhiḥ jalaiḥ || 4 ||
jagattrayeśe tvayi gokuleśatayābhiṣikte sati gopavāṭaḥ |
nāke'pi vaikuṇṭhapade'pyalabhyāṃ śriyaṃ prapede bhavataḥ prabhāvāt || 5 ||
jagaditi | tvayi gokuleśatayābhiṣikte sati gopavāṭo vrajaḥ nāke svarge ||
kadācidantaryamunaṃ prabhāte snāyan pitā vāruṇapūrūpeṇa |
nītastamānetumagāḥ purīṃ tvaṃ tāṃ vāruṇīṃ kāraṇamartyarūpaḥ || 6 ||
kadāciditi | antaryamunaṃ yamunāyām | vāruṇapūruṣeṇa varuṇadūtena | kāraṇamartyarūpaḥ bhūbhāraharaṇāyopāttamanuṣyadeho vāruṇīṃ purīmagāḥ || 6 ||
sasambhramaṃ tena jalādhipena prapūjitastvaṃ pratigṛhya tātam |
upāgatastatkṣaṇamātmagehaṃ pitāvadat taccaritaṃ nijebhyaḥ || 7 ||
sasambhramamiti | jalādhipena varuṇena sasambhramaṃ tvadarśanajātasambhramaṃ prapūjitaḥ | tātaṃ nandam | taccaritaṃ nandaharaṇarūpam || 7 ||
hariṃ viniścitya bhavantametān bhavatpadālokanabaddhatṛṣṇān |
nirīkṣya viṣṇo ! paramaṃ padaṃ tad durāpamanyaistvamadīdṛśastān || 8 ||
'harimiti | evamādiprabhāvairbhavantaṃ hariṃ viniścitya bhavataḥ padaṃ paraṃ brahma, tadālokane baddhatṛṣṇān paramaṃ padaṃ brahma adīdṛśaḥ pradarśayāmāsitha || 8 ||
sphuratparānandarasapravāhapūrṇakaivalyamahāpayodhau |
ciraṃ nimagnāḥ khalu gopasaṅghāstvayaiva bhūman ! punaruddhṛtāste || 9 ||
sphuraditi | paramānandāmṛtapravāhapūrṇe kaivalyākhye mahārṇave || 9 ||
tvaṃ śrīkṛṣṇaḥ sākṣāt paramātmaiva, bhaktebhyaḥ karāmalakavat svasvarūpabrahmapradarśakatvādityāha -- 
karabadaravadevaṃ deva! kutrāvatāre
nijapadamanavāpyaṃ darśitaṃ bhaktibhājām |
tadiha paśuparūpī tvaṃ hi sākṣāt parātman !
pavanapuranivāsin! pāhi māmāmayebhyaḥ || 10 ||
iti govindapaṭṭābhiṣekavarṇanaṃ varuṇalokāda nandānayanavarṇanaṃ ca
catuṣpaṣṭitamaṃ daśakam |
gopījanāya kathitaṃ niyamāvasāne
mārotsavaṃ tvamatha sādhayituṃ pravṛttaḥ |
sāndreṇa cāndramahasā śiśirīkṛtāśe
prāpūrayo muralikāṃ yamunāvanānte || 1 ||
gopīti | niyamāvasāne girijārcanavratasamāptau kathitaṃ pratijñātaṃ mārotsavaṃ rāsakrīḍādi | yamunāvanānte sthitvā muralikāṃ veṇuṃ prāpūrayaḥ udairayaḥ ||
sampūrchanābhiruditasvaramaṇḍalābhiḥ
sammūrchayantamakhilaṃ bhuvanāntarālam |
tvadveṇunādamupakarṇya vibho ! taruṇyastattādṛśaṃ kamapi cittavimohamāpuḥ || 2 ||
sammūrchanābhiriti | uditāni svaramaṇḍalāni prayogabhedāḥ yāsu tābhiḥ sammūrchanābhiḥ ārohāvarohaṇarūpaiḥ saptabhiḥ svarai 'kramayuktāḥ svarāḥ sapta mūrchanāḥ parikīrtitāḥ' ityukteḥ | sammūrchayantaṃ mohayantam | tattādṛśam anupamam || 2 ||
tā gehakṛtyaniratāstanayaprasaktāḥ
kāntopasevanaparāśca saroruhākṣyaḥ |
sarvaṃ visṛjya muralīravamohitāste
kāntāradeśamayi kāntatano ! sametāḥ || 3 ||
tā iti | kāntopasevanaparāḥ bhartṛśuśrūṣāparāḥ || 3 ||
kākṣcinijāṅgaparibhūṣaṇamādadhānā
veṇupraṇādamupakarṇya kṛtārdhabhūṣāḥ |
tvāmāgatā nanu tathaiva vibhūṣitābhyastā eva saṃrurucire tava locanāya || 4 ||
kāciditi | nijāṅgaparibhūṣaṇaṃ sarvāvayavabhūṣaṇam ādadhānāḥ kurvatyaḥ veṇupraṇādamupakarṇya tvāmāgatāḥ prāptāḥ kāścit | kṛtārdhabhūṣāḥ veṇupraṇādamupakarṇya tathaiva kṛtārdhabhūṣā evāgatāḥ | tatra vibhūṣitābhyaḥ sarvāvayavabhūṣaṇasavilambābhyaḥ tāḥ kṛtārdhabhūṣā eva etā matpremātiśayena vismṛtabhūṣaṇavaikalyā aho iti nanu tava locanāya saṃrurucire tava nayanaruciviṣayā babhūvurityarthaḥ || 4 ||
hāraṃ nitambabhuvi kācana dhārayantī
kāñcīṃ ca kaṇṭhabhuvi deva! samāgatā tvām |
hāritvamātmajaghanasya mukunda! tubhyaṃ
vyaktaṃ babhāṣa iva mugdhamukhī viśeṣāt || 5 ||
hāramiti | hāro'styasminniti hārī, tattvaṃ hāritvaṃ manoharatvaṃ ca ātmanaḥ svasya jaghanasya viśeṣānmanoharatvaṃ mugdhamukhī tubhyaṃ bhavate babhāṣa iva || 5 ||
kācit kuce punarasajjitakañculīkā
vyāmohataḥ paravadhūbhiralakṣyamāṇā |
tvāmāyayau nirupamapraṇayātibhārarājyābhiṣekavidhaye kalaśīdhareva || 6 ||
kāciditi | asajjitakañculīkā asaṃyojitamātrikā | tasyāḥ paravadhūnāṃ ca vyāmohataḥ | nirupameti | praṇayātiśayarūpe rājye'vasthāpyābhiṣekāya kucabhararūpakalaśīghareva || 6 ||
kākṣcid gṛhāt kila niretumapārayantyastvāmeva deva ! hṛdaye sudṛḍhaṃ vibhāvya |
dehaṃ vidhūya paracitsukharūpamekaṃ
tvāmāviśan paramimā nanu dhanyadhanyāḥ || 7 ||
kākṣciditi | kāścid bandhubhirniṣidhyamānatayā gṛhād niretuṃ nirgantum apārayantyaḥ aśaktāḥ tvāṃ sudṛḍhaṃ vibhāvya samādhāya dehaṃ sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ ca vidhūya tyaktvā | paraciditi | saccidānandādvayaṃ paraṃ brahmāviśan | dhanyadhanyāḥ atiśayena bhāgyavatyaḥ || 7||
jārātmanā na paramātmatayā smarantyo
nāryo gatāḥ paramahaṃsagatiṃ kṣaṇena |
tat tvāṃ prakāśaparamātmatanuṃ kathañciccitte vahannamṛtamaśramamakṣnuvīya || 8 ||
jārātmaneti | jārātmanā jārabuddhyā na paramātmatayā kintu kevalamanuṣyabuddhacā tvāṃ smarantyo nāryo gopyaḥ paramahaṃsānām ātmānātmavivekināṃ gatiṃ mokṣaṃ gatāḥ prāptā iti yat tat tasmāt prakāśaparamātmatanuṃ prakaṭīkṛtabrahmasvarūpaṃ tvāṃ kathāñcit kāmād dveṣād bhayādvā citte vahan cintayan amṛtaṃ mokṣam aśnuvīya prāpnuyām || 8 ||
abhyāgatābhirabhito vrajasundarībhirmugdhasmitārdravadanaḥ karuṇāvalokī |
nissīmakāntijaladhistvamavekṣyamāṇo
viśvaikahṛdya ! hara me parameśa ! rogān || 9 ||
abhyāgatābhiriti | he viśvaikahṛdya ! abhito'bhyāgatābhiḥ parivārya sthitābhiḥ avekṣyamāṇaḥ kaṭākṣapātrībhūtaḥ || 9 ||
iti rāsakrīḍāyāṃ gopīnāṃ bhagavatsamīpagamanavarṇanaṃ
pañcaṣaṣṭitamaṃ daśakamekonam |
upayātānāṃ sudṛśāṃ kusumāyudhavāṇapātavivaśānām |
abhivāñchitaṃ vidhātuṃ kṛtamatirapi tā jagātha vāmamiva || 1 ||
upayātānāmiti | upayātānāṃ samīpaṃ prāptānāṃ sudṛśāṃ sundarīṇām abhivāñchitaṃ mārotsavaṃ vidhātuṃ kartuṃ kṛtamatirniścitabuddhirapi prathamaṃ tāḥ gopīḥ vāmaṃ pratikūlamiva jagātha uktavān || 1 ||
gaganagataṃ muninivahaṃ śrāvayituṃ jagitha kulavadhūdharmam |
dharmya khalu te vacanaṃ karma tu no nirmalasya viśvāsyam || 2 ||
gaganeti | jagitha uktavān | nanvanupanītasya kulavadhūbhiḥ kāmotsavo'dharmastatkartrā prokta kulavadhūdharme kathaṃ munīnāṃ viśvāsa ityāśaṅkāyāmāha -- dharmyaṃ khalviti | te īśvarasya vacanaṃ dharmyaṃ dharmādanapetam | karma tu no viśvāsyam | kvācidadharmarūpamapi bhavati | tena tava na doṣaḥ, yato nirmalaḥ, nirguṇatvāt karmānadhikārī tvam || 2 ||
ākarṇya te pratīpāṃ vāṇīmeṇīdṛśaḥ paraṃ dīnāḥ |
mā mā karuṇāsindho! parityajetyaticiraṃ vilepustāḥ || 3 ||
ākarṇyeti | te tava pratīpāṃ 'bhavatīnāṃ niśi bhartṝn virayyeha matsamīpāgamanam anucitam | atastvaritamātmagṛhaṃ yāte tyādirūpatayā pratikūlām | dīnāḥ kṣīṇamānasā mā māsmān parityajeti vilepuḥ rodanapūrvakamuktavatyaḥ || 3 ||
tāsāṃ ruditairlapitaiḥ karuṇākulamānaso murāre! tvam |
tābhiḥ samaṃ pravṛtto yamunāpulineṣu kāmamabhirantum || 4 ||
tāsāmiti | lapitairvacanaiśca | tābhiḥ samaṃ saha kāmaṃ yatheccham abhirantuṃ krīḍituṃ pravṛttaḥ ārabdhaḥ || 4 ||
candrakarasyandalasatsundarayamunātaṭāntavīthīṣu |
gopījanottarīyairāpāditasaṃstaro nyaṣīdastvam || 5 ||
candreti | candrakarasyāmṛtamayasya syando dravaḥ tena lasantīṣu sundarīṣu yamunātīrasthalīṣu gopījanānāmuttarīyairāpādita utpāditaḥ saṃstaro viṣṭaro yasya sa tvaṃ nyaṣīda upaviṣṭo'bhūḥ || 5 ||
sumadhuranarmālapanaiḥ karasaṃgrahaṇaikṣca cumvanollāsaiḥ |
gāḍhāliṅganasaṅgaistvamaṅganālokamākulīcakṛpe || 6 ||
sumadhureti | sumadhuraiḥ śrotrānandaiḥ narmālapanaiḥ krīḍāvacanaiḥ | gāḍhāliṅganaiḥ kṣīranīravadaṅgapratyaṅgasaṃkṣlaṣaṇaiḥ ākulīkṛṣe drāvayāmāsitha || 6 ||
vāsoharaṇadine yad vāsoharaṇaṃ pratiśrutaṃ tāsām |
tadapi vibho! rasavivaśasvāntānāṃ kāntasubhruvāmadadhāḥ || 7 ||
vāsa iti | vāsoharaṇadine girijārcanasamāptidine pratiśrutaṃ pratijñātaṃ vāsoharaṇaṃ tāsāṃ nitambapradeśād vasanākṣepam adadhāḥ kṛtavān || 7 ||
kandalitadharmaleśaṃ kundamṛdusmeravaṛpāthojam |
nandasuta ! tvāṃ trijagatsundaramupagṛhya nanditā vālāḥ || 8 ||
kandaliteti | kandalitā aṅkuritāḥ gharmaleśāḥ svedabindavo yasmin | kundakusumavad mṛdusmeram alpasmitaṃ vaṛpāthojaṃ vadanapadmaṃ yasya | nanditā hṛṣṭāḥ || 8 ||
viraheṣvaṅgāramayaḥ śṛṅgāramayaśca saṅgame'pi tvam |
nitarāmaṅgāramayastatra punaḥ saṅgame'pi citramidam || 9 ||
viraheṣviti| śṛṅgāramayaḥ śṛṅgārarasocitabhāṣāveṣavyāpārastvaṃ viraheṣu strīṇāmaṅgāramayo dahanarūpaśca santāpakaratvāt | saṅgame'pi tvaṃ nitarāmaṅgāramaya itīdaṃ citram | he aṅga! tvaṃ nitarāmaramaya ityevārthaḥ || 9 ||
tāsu rādhāyāṃ bhagavataḥ premātiśayāt saviśeṣaṃ tāmabhiramayantaṃ bhagavantaṃ prārthayate -- 
rādhāturūpayodharasādhuparīrambhalolupātmānam |
ārādhaye bhavantaṃ pavanapurādhīśa ! śamaya sakalagadān || 10 ||
iti rāsakrīḍāyāṃ gopīnāṃ bhagavatkṛtadharmopadeśavarṇanaṃ yamunāpuline
bhagavatā saha vihāravarṇanaṃ ca
ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamaṃ daśakam |
sphuratparānandarasātmakena tvayā samāsāditabhogalīlāḥ |
asīmamānandabharaṃ prapannā mahāntamāpurmadamambujākṣyaḥ || 1 ||
sphuraditi | sphuratparānandarasa ātmā mūrtiryasya tena tvayā samāsāditā prāptā bhogalīlā ratikrīḍā yābhiḥ | asīmam anavadhim ānandarasaṃ ratisukhāparaparyāyaṃ brahmānandaṃ prapannā mahāntaṃ madamāpuḥ || 1 ||
tatprakāramāha -- 
nilīyate'sau mayi mayyamāyaṃ ramāpatirviśvamanobhirāmaḥ |
itisma sarvāḥ kalitābhimānā nirīkṣya govinda ! tirohito'bhūḥ || 2 ||
nilīyata iti | asau kṛṣṇo mayyamāyaṃ nirvyājaṃ nilīyate sakto bhavati | anyāsu saujanyādyarthaṃ māyayā praṇayavāniva | anyā mayi nilīyate | iti kalito dhṛto'bhimāno yāsāṃ tā nirīkṣya tirohito'ntarhito'bhūḥ || 2 ||
rādhābhidhāṃ tāvadajātagarvāmatipriyāṃ gopavadhūṃ murāre ! |
bhavānupādāya gato vidūraṃ tayā saha svairavihārakārī || 3 ||
rādheti | ajātagarvatayātipriyām || 3 ||
tirohite'tha tvāya jātatāpāḥ samaṃ sametāḥ kamalāyatākṣyaḥ |
bane bane tvāṃ parimārgayantyo viṣādamāpurbhagavannapāram || 4 ||
tirohita iti | samaṃ jātatāpāstulyasantāpāḥ sametāḥ sahitāḥ parimārgayantyo'nveṣamāṇāḥ bhavantaṃ nāpuḥ kintu viṣādamāpuḥ || 4 ||
tā viṣaṇṇatayācetanānapi bhavantaṃ papracchurityāha -- 
hā cūta ! hā campaka! karṇikāra! hā mallike! mālati! bālavallyaḥ! |
kiṃ vīkṣito no hṛdayaikacora ityādi tāstvatpravaṇā vilepuḥ || 5 ||
hā cūteti | he bālavallyaḥ! yuṣmābhirno'smākaṃ hṛdayaikacoraḥ kiṃ vīkṣitaḥ | tvatpravaṇāḥ tvadekacittāḥ || 5 ||
nirīkṣito'yaṃ sakhi ! paṅkajākṣaḥ puro mametyākulamālapantī |
tvāṃ bhāvanācakṣuṣi vīkṣya kācit tāpaṃ sakhīnāṃ dviguṇīcakāra || 6 ||
nirīkṣita iti | he sākhi! ayaṃ paṅkajākṣaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ mama puro nirīkṣitaḥ pratyakṣīkṛtaḥ| bhāvanācakṣuṣi saṅkalpātmake cakṣuṣi pratyakṣamiva tvāṃ vīkṣya sakhīnāṃ tāpam āśājananena dviguṇīcakāra || 6 ||
tvadātmikāstā yamunātaṭānte tavānucakruḥ kila ceṣṭitāni |
vicitya bhūyo'pi tathaiva mānāt tvayā viyuktāṃ dadṛśukṣca rādhām || 7 ||
tvaditi | tvadātmikāḥ bhāvanābalena tvanmayatāṃ gatāḥ tava ceṣṭitāni pūtanāvadhārjunabhañjanādīni anucakruḥ anukṛtavatyaḥ | bhūyo'pi tvāṃ vicitya anvipya yathānyāsāṃ tathaiva mānād ahamasyātipriyetyabhimānāt tvayā viyuktāṃ rādhāṃ dadṛśuḥ || 7 ||
tataḥ samaṃ tā vipine samantāt tamovatārāvadhi mārgayantyaḥ |
punarvimiśrā yamunātaṭānte bhṛśaṃ vilepukṣca jagurguṇāṃste || 8 ||
tata iti | tataḥ samaṃ tayā saha tā gopyaḥ vane samantāt paryantataḥ tamovatārāvadhi candrāstamayaparyantaṃ mārgayantyaḥ anveṣamāṇāḥ vimiśrāḥ sahitāḥ vilepuḥ saprārthanaṃ susvaraṃ ruruduḥ || 8 ||
tathāvyathāsaṃkulamānasānāṃ vrajāṅganānāṃ karuṇaikasindho ! |
jagatrayīmohanamohanātmā tvaṃ prādurāsīrayi mandahāsī || 9 ||
tatheti | tathābhūtayā anirdeśyayā vyathayā pīḍayā saṃkulam ākulaṃ mānasaṃ yāsām | jagatrayīmohanaḥ kāmaḥ tasyāpi mohana ātmā mūrtiryasya sa tvaṃ mandahāsī san prādurāsīḥ || 9 ||
sandigdhasandarśanamātmakāntaṃ tvāṃ vīkṣya tanvyaḥ sahasā tadānīm |
kiṃ kiṃ na cakruḥ pramadātibhārāt sa tvaṃ gadāt pālaya māruteśa! ||10||
sandigdheti | itaḥ paramapi nāma taṃ drakṣyāmi na veti sandigdhaṃ sandarśanaṃ yasya | pramadātibhārād harṣāveśena || 10 ||
iti rāsakrīḍāyāṃ gopīnāṃ madāpanodāya bhagavatastirodhānavarṇanaṃ
bhagavadanveṣaṇavarṇanaṃ bhagavadāvirbhāvavarṇanaṃ ca
saptaṣaṣṭitamaṃ daśakam |
tava vilokanāda gopikājanāḥ pramadasaṃkulāḥ paṅkajekṣaṇa ! |
amṛtadhārayā saṃplutā iva stimitatāṃ dadhustvatpurogatāḥ || 1 ||
taveti | prabhadasaṃkulāḥ pramodākulāḥ saṃplutāḥ abhiṣiktāḥ stimitatāṃ niścalatāṃ dadhuḥ || 1 ||
tadanu kācana tvatkarāmbujaṃ sapadi gṛhṇatī nirviśaṅkitam |
dhanapayodhare sannidhāya sā pulakasaṃvṛtā tasthuṣī ciram || 2 ||
tadanviti | sapadi darśanasamanantarameva itarāsu sthitāsu nirviśaṅkitaṃ ghane nibiḍe payodhare tvatkarāmbujasparśajanitānandātiśayānubhāvena pulakena saṃvṛtā ciraṃ tasthuṣī sthitavatī || 2 ||
tava vibho ! parā komalaṃ bhujaṃ nijagalāntare paryaveṣṭayat |
galasamudataṃ prāṇamārutaṃ pratinirundhatīvātiharpulā || 3 ||
taveti | aparā tava bhujaṃ gṛhītvā nijagalāntare paryaveṣṭayad babandha tvadvirahavyathayā nirgamiṣyantaṃ prāṇamārutaṃ pratinirundhatīva || 3 ||
apagatratā kāpi kāminī tava mukhambujāt pūgacarvitam |
pratigṛhayya tad vaṝpaṅkaje nidadhatī gatā pūrṇakāmatām || 4 ||
apagateti | pratigṛhayya gṛhītvā tat pūgacarvita svavatrkapaṅkaje nidadhatī āsvādayantī pūrṇakāmatāṃ sañjātajīvanaphalatāṃ gatā prāptā || 4 ||
vikaruṇo bane saṃvihāya māmapagato'si kā tvāmiha spṛśet |
iti saroṣayā tāvadekayā sajalalocanaṃ vīkṣito bhavān || 5 ||
vikaruṇa iti | vane māṃ saṃvihāya apagato dūraṃ gato'si | ato vikaruṇo niṣkṛpastvamidānīṃ kimarthamāgato'si | iha asmāsu kā tvāṃ spṛśediti sajalalocanaṃ yathā bhavati tathā vīkṣitaḥ || 5 ||
iti mudākulairvallavījanaiḥ samupāgato yāmune taṭe |
mṛdukucāmbaraiḥ kalpitāsane ghusugabhāsure paryaśobhayāḥ || 6 ||
itīti | mṛdubhiḥ kucāmbaraiḥ uttarīyai kalpite āsane ghusṛṇabhāsure kuṅkumaśobhite || 6 ||
tataśca sahopaviṣṭāstā bhavantamāhurityāha -- 
katividhā kṛpā ke'pi sarvato dhṛtadayodayāḥ kecidāśrite|
katicidīdṛśā mādṛśeṣvapītyabhihito bhavān ballavījanaiḥ || 7 ||
kaṃtividheti | iha jagati katividhā kṛpā | ke'pi sarvataḥ sarvajaneṣu ghṛtadayodayāḥ kṛpāvantaḥ | kecidāśrite jana eva dhṛtadayodayāḥ | katicit kecid mādṛśeṣvāśriteṣvapi īdṛśāḥ bhavāniva niṣkaruṇāḥ | tatra tattvaṃ brūhīti śeṣaḥ || 7 ||
bhavānuttaramāha -- 
ayi kumārikā ! naiva śaṅkayatāṃ kaṭhinatā mayi premakātare |
mayi tu cetaso vo'nuvṛttaye kṛtamidaṃ mayetyūcivān bhavān || 8 ||
anīti | he kumārikāḥ ! abalāḥ ! premakātare praṇayāpāyabhīrau mayi kaṭhinatā niṣkaruṇatā naiva śaṅkayatām | vo yuṣmākaṃ mayi cetaso'nuvṛttaye manassamādhānāya mayedaṃ tirodhānaṃ kṛtam || 8 ||
ayi niśamyatāṃ jīvallabhāḥ priyatamo jano nedṛśo mama |
tadiha ramyatāṃ ramyayāminīṣvanuparodhamityālapo vibho ! || 9 ||
ayīti | ayi jīvavallabhāḥ! prāṇanāthāḥ ! niśamyatāṃ tattvam | mamedṛśaḥ priyatamo nāsti yathā bhavatyaḥ | tat tasmād iha yamunātīreṣu sacandratayā ramyāsu yāminīṣu anuparodhaṃ niśśaṅkaṃ mayā saha ramyatāmityālapaḥ ūciṣe || 9 ||
iti girādhikaṃ modamedurairbrajavadhūjanaiḥ sākamāraman |
kalitakautuko rāsakhelane gurupurīpate! pāhi māṃ gadāt || 10 ||
itīti | modena meduraiḥ vyāptaiḥ | āraman krīḍan | rāsakhelane rāsakrīḍāyāṃ kalitakautukaḥ dhṛtāgrahaḥ || 10 ||
iti rāsakrīḍāyāṃ gopīnāṃ bhagavatsamāgamādānandapāravaśyavarṇanaṃ
praṇayakopavarṇanaṃ bhagavatkṛtasāntvanavarṇanaṃ ca
aṣṭaṣaṣṭitamaṃ daśakam |
atha rāsakrīḍāṃ varṇayati
keśapāśadhṛtapiñchikāvitati sañcalanmakarakuṇḍalaṃ
hārajālavanamālikālalitamaṅgarāgaghanasaurabham |
pītaceladhṛtakāñcikāñcita mudañcadaṃśumaṇinūpuraṃ
rāsakeliparibhūṣitaṃ tava hi rūpamīśa ! kalayāmahe || 1 ||
keśeti | keśapāśadhṛtapiñchikāvitati mayūrapiñchaviracitaḥ paritaḥ paridṛśyamānacandrakaḥ śirolaṅkāraviśeṣo yasya | gaṇḍamaṇḍale sañcalad makarakuṇḍalaṃ yasya | hārajālairvanamālikayā ca lalitaṃ sundaram | samanuliptairaṅgarāgaiḥ kuṅkumādibhiḥ ghanaṃ nibiḍaṃ saurabhaṃ saurabhyaṃ yasya | pītacelopari ghṛtayā kāñcikayā maṇimekhalayā añcitaṃ śobhitam | udañcadaṃśu udgatamayūkhaṃ maṇimayaṃ nūpuraṃ vasya | evaṃ rāsakelyai paribhūṣitaṃ viracitanaipathyaṃ tava rūpa kalayāmahe upāsmahe || 1 ||
tāvadeva kṛtamaṇḍane kalitakañculīkakucamaṇḍale
gaṇḍalolamaṇikuṇḍale yuvatimaṇḍale'tha parimaṇḍale |
antarā sakalasundarīyugalamindirāramaṇa ! sañcaran
mañjulāṃ tadanu rāsakelimayi kañjanābha! samupādadhāḥ || 2 ||
tābaditi | yāvad dhṛtarāsakrīḍocitaveṣo bhagavān, tāvadeva kṛtaṃ maṇḍanaṃ yena | kalitā dhṛtā kañculī gātrikā yatra tat kalitakañculīkaṃ, tat kucamaṇḍalaṃ yasya | atha bhūṣaṇānantaraṃ parimaṇḍale nikhātaśaṅkuni samataladeśe maṇḍalākārega sthite sakalasundarīyugalamantarā dvayordvayormadhye sañcaran tatkucakaṇṭhakapolādāvabhinaye ca vyagrabhujayugalatayā samyak caran | mañjulāṃ manoharām || 2 ||
vāsudeva! tava bhāsamānamiha rāsakelirasasaurabhaṃ
dūrato'pi khalu nāradāgaditamākalavya kutukākulā |
veṣabhūṣaṇavilāsapeśalavilāsinīśatasamāvṛtā
nākato yugapadāgatā viyati vegato'tha suramaṇḍalī || 3 ||
vāsudeveti | rāsakelireva rasaḥ sakalendriyāhādakatvāt tasya saurabhaṃ mānojñakaṃ śrīnāradenāgaditaṃ niveditam ākalavya śrutvā | veṣabhūṣagavilāseṣu peśalaiḥ vidagdhaiḥ vilāsinīśataiḥ svastrīsahasraiḥ | nākataḥ svargād vegato yugapad vimānairviyatyāgatā suramaṇḍalī devasamūhaḥ || 3 ||
veṇunādakṛtatānadānakalagānarāgagatiyojanālobhanīyamṛdupādapātakṛtatālamelanamanoharam |
idaṃ parimaṇḍalaviśeṣaṇaṃ syāt |
pāṇisaṃkkaṇitakaṅkaṇaṃ ca muhuraṃsalamvitakarāmbujaṃ
śroṇibimbacaladambaraṃ bhajata rāsakelirasaḍambaram || 4 ||
veṇviti | veṇumādena kṛtena tānadānena gānanidānabhūtasvaramadarśanena kalena kaṇṭhotthitatayātimadhureṇa gānena rāgagatiyojanayā rāgāvaśeṣamārgasaṃyojanena ca lobhanīyo yaḥ pādapātaḥ, tatkṛtena tālamelanena manoharaṃ rāsakelirasasya ḍambaraṃ paripoṣam || 4 ||
śraddhayā viracitānugāna kṛtatāratāramadhurasvare
nartane'tha lalitāṅgahāralulitāṅgahāramaṇibhūṣaṇe |
sammadena kṛtapuṣpavarṣamalamunmipad diviṣadāṃ kulaṃ
cinmaye tvayi nilīyamānagniva saṃmumoha savadhūkulam || 5 ||
śraddhayeti | śraddhayā kautukena virāvatenānugānena paronnīyanena kṛtastāratāraḥ kramādatyucco madhuratvaro yasmin, lalitairaṅgahāraiḥ aṅgavikṣepaiḥ lulitāni svasthānāt cyutāni aṅgasthahāramaṇibhūṣaṇāni yasmin, tasmin nartane sati sammadena svātiśāyiprayogātiśayadarśanajanitaharṣāveśena kṛtaḥ puṣpavarṣo yena | alam atiśayena unmiṣad darśanotsukaṃ sabadhūkulaṃ sastrīsamūhaṃ diviṣadāṃ kulaṃ devasamūhaḥ saṃmumoha | nilīyamānamiva adṛṣṭadraṣṭṛdṛśyadarśanabhedamityarthaḥ || 5||
svinnasannatanuvallarī tadanu kāpi nāma paśupāṅganā
kāntamaṃsamavalambate sma taba tāntibhāramukulekṣaṇā |
kācidācalitakuntalā navapaṭīrasāranavasaurabhaṃ
vañcanena taba sañcucumba bhujamañcitorupulakāṅkuram || 6 ||
svinneti | svinnā svedārdrā sannā prāptasādā tanuvallarī yasyāḥ sā tathā tāntibhāreṇa klamātiśayena mukulitanayanā taba kāntaṃ sundaram asaṃ bhujamūlam avalambate sma āśritavatī | kācid aparā ācalitakuntalā vikṣlathatkabarībharā navapaṭīrasāreṇa māleyena navam ananubhūtacaraṃ saurabhaṃ yasmin | yadvā navapaṭīrasāravannavasaurabhaṃ taba bhujaṃ vañcanena anyāpadeśena sañcucumba | tajjanitam
urupulakāṅkaraṃ yasmin || 6 ||
kāpi gaṇḍabhuvi sanbhidhāya nijagaṇḍamākulitakuṇḍalaṃ
puṇyapūranidhirambavāpa tava pūgacarvitarasāmṛtam |
indirāvihṛtimandiraṃ bhuvanasundaraṃ hi naṭanāntare
tvāmavāpya dadhuraṅganāḥ kimu na sampradonmadadaśāntaram || 7 ||
kāpīti | tava gaṇḍabhuvi nijagaṇḍam ācalitakuṇḍalaṃ yathā bhavati tathā sannidhāya pūgacarvitarasāmṛtam amṛtamayaṃ vīṭikārasamanvabāpa | sammadena pramodātiśayena unmadadaśāntaraṃ pūrvāparānusandhānabaidhuryalakṣaṇamavasthāntaram || 7 ||
gānamīśa! virataṃ krameṇa kila vādyamelanamupārataṃ
brahmasammadarasākulāḥ sadasi kevalaṃ nanṛturaṅganāḥ |
nāvidannapi ca nīvikāṃ kimapi kuntalīmapica kañculīṃ
jyotiṣāmapi kadambakaṃ divi vilambitaṃ kimaparaṃ bruve || 8 ||
gānamiti | aṅganāḥ brahmasagmadarasena brahmānandānubhava rasena ākulāḥ paravaśāḥ kevalaṃ sadasi nanṛtuḥ | nartanasya kaivalyamevāha -- gānamiti | jyotiṣāṃ grahanakṣatrādīnāṃ kadambakamapi divi vilambitamityanena sūryodayavilambāt triyāmāyāmo dyotyate | kimaparaṃ bruve gānavāghādau prasādaḥ nībīkuntalagātrikāviślathanānavagatirityādi bhuvisthānāṃ pāravaśyaṃ kimarthamucyata ityarthaḥ || 8 ||
modasīmni bhuvanaṃ vilāpya vihṭatiṃ samāpya ca tato vibho !
kelisamprṛditanirmalāṅganadharmaleśasubhagātmanām |
manmathāsahanacetasāṃ paśupayoṣitāṃ sukṛtacoditastāvadākalitamūrtirādhitha māravīraparamotsavān || 9||
'modeti | modasīmni brahmānande bhuvanaṃ draṣṭṭalokaṃ vilāpya majjayitvā
kelisammṛditatayā nirmalāni lalitānyaṅgāni yāsāṃ navadharmaleśaiḥ subhaga ātmā śarīraṃ yāsāṃ, manmathenāsahanaṃ suratalīlākālakṣepāsahiṣṇu ceto yāsāṃ, tāsāṃ sukṛtena coditaḥ preritaḥ yāvatyo gopyastāvatya ākalitā dhṛtā mūrtayo yena || 9 ||
kelibhedaparilolitābhiratilālitābhiravalālibhiḥ
svairamīśa! nanu sūrajāpayasi cāru nāma vihṛtiṃ vyadhāḥ |
kānane'pi ca visāriśītalakiśoramārutamanohare
mūnasaurabhamaye vilesitha vilāsinīśatavimohanam || 10 ||
kelīti| kelibhedaiḥ āliṅganacumbanaprahara ṇadantavraṇanakhakṣatacūṣaṇasītkārahikkāśvāsādiprayogarūpaiḥ parilolitābhiḥ ākulīkriyamāṇābhiḥ | sūrajāpayasi yamunājale vihṛtiṃ jalakrīḍāṃ kānane'pi ca vihṛtiṃ vyathāḥ kṛtavān | visāriṇā vījanaśīlena śītalena kiśoramārutena mandavāyunā manohare sūnānāṃ puṣpāṇāṃ saurabhamaye | vilāsinīśatavimohanaṃ yathā tathā vilesitha aśobhathāḥ || 10 ||
kāminīriti hi yāminīṣu khalu kāmanīyakanidhe ! bhavān
pūrṇasammadarasārṇavaṃ kamapi yomigamyamanubhāvayan |
brahmaśaṅkaramukhānapīha paśupāṅganāsu bahumānayan
bhaktalokagamanīyarūpa! kamanīya! kṛṣṇa! paripāhi mām || 11||
kāminīriti | he kāmanīyakanidhe! saubhāgyārṇava! bhavān yāminīṣu kāminīḥ yogibhirgamyaṃ prāpyaṃ pūrṇasammadarasārṇavaṃ brahmānandam anubhāvayan āsvādayan brahmādīnapi paśupāṅganāsu bahumānayan bahumānayuktān kurvan bhaktalokairgamanīyaṃ prāpyaṃ rūpaṃ yasya | kamanīya! kānta! he kṛṣṇa! paripāhi || 11 ||
iti rāsakrīḍāyāṃ kamanīyarūpadhāriṇā bhagavatā saha gopīnāṃ nṛttagītādivilāsavarṇanaṃ
ekonasaptatitamaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
iti tvayi rasākulaṃ ramitavallabhe vallavāḥ
kadāpi punaramvikākamituramvikākānane |
sametya bhavatā samaṃ niśi niṣevya divyotsavaṃ
sukhaṃ suṣupuragrasīda brajapamugranāgastadā || 1 ||
itīti | iti uktaprakāreṇa rasena kautukena ākulaṃ yathā bhavati tathā ramitā vallabhā yena tasmiṃstvayi sati punaḥ kadācid ballavā gopāḥ ambikākamituḥ ambikāvanākhye śivakṣetre bhavatā samaṃ sametya niśi divyotsavaṃ niṣevya sukhaṃ suṣupuḥ | tadā ugraḥ krūro nāgo'jagaro brajapaṃ nandam agrasīt || 1 ||
samunmukhamatholmukairabhihate'pi tasmin valādamuñcati bhavatpade nyapati pāhi pāhīti taiḥ |
tadā khalu padā bhavān samupagamya pasparśa taṃ
vabhau sa ca nijāṃ tanuṃ samupasādya vaidyādharīm || 2 ||
samunmukhamiti | samunmukhaṃ grasanavyagram | ulmukaiḥ alātaiḥ | taiḥ gopaiḥ bhavatpade nyapati | vaidyādharī vidyādharasambandhinam | samupasādya prāpya babhau śuśubhe || 2 ||
sudarśanadhara! prabho! nanu sudarśanākhyo'smyahaṃ
munīn kkacidapāhasaṃ ta iha māṃ vyadhurvāhasam |
bhavatpadasamarpaṇādamalatāṃ gato'smītyasau
stuvan nijapadaṃ yayau vrajapadaṃ ca gopā mudā || 3 ||
sudarśaneti | kacit kadācid munīn aṅgirasaḥ apāhasam apahasitavānasmi | te munayaḥ iha asmin pradeśe māṃ vāhasam ajagaraṃ vyadhuḥ yata ātmanaḥ surūpatayā hapto'smān virūpatayāpahasitavān, atastvamajagaro bhaviteti śaptavantaḥ | idānīmamalatāṃ gataḥ śāpānmukta ityuktvā stuvan nijapadaṃ vidyādharalokam || 3 ||
parasmaipadamātmanepadānityatvāt |
kadāpi khalu sīriṇā viharati tvayi strījanairjahāra dhanadānugaḥ sa kila śaṅkhacūḍo'valāḥ |
atidrutamanudrutastamatha muktanārījana
rurojitha śiromaṇiṃ halabhṛte ca tasyādadāḥ || 4 ||
kadāpīti | sīriṇā valabhadreṇa saha viharati krīḍati sati dhanadānugo vaiśravaṇabhṛtyaḥ avalāḥ gopīḥ jahāra | atidrutaṃ śīghram anudrutaḥ anugataḥ bhayānmukto nārījano yena taṃ śaṅkhacūḍaṃ rurojitha nigṛhītavān tasya śiromaṇiṃ halabhṛte'grajāyādadāśca || 4 ||
dineṣu ca suhṛjjanaiḥ saha vaneṣu līlāparaṃ
manobhavamanoharaṃ rasitaveṇunādāmṛtam |
bhavantamamarīddaśāmamṛtapāraṇādāyinaṃ
vicintya kimu nālapana virahatāpitā gopikāḥ || 5 ||
dineṣviti | evaṃ dineṣu pratidinaṃ ca suhṛjjanaiḥ gopabālakaiḥ saha | manobhavādapi manoharam | rasitam āsvāditaṃ veṇunāda evāmṛtaṃ yena | amarīdṛśāṃ svargastrīnayanānām amṛtapāraṇādāyinaṃ nijarūpāmṛtapānamāpādayantam | viraheṇa kṣaṇaṃ jātena tāpitāḥ santaptahṛdayāḥ kinu nālapan, tattadavasthāṃ vicintya vilepurityarthaḥ || 5 ||
bhojarājabhṛtakastvatha kaścit kaṣṭaduṣṭapathadṛṣṭirariṣṭaḥ |
niṣṭhurākṛtirapaṣṭhuninādastiṣṭhate sma bhavate vṛṣarūpī || 6 ||
bhojeti | bhojarājasya kaṃsasya bhṛtakaḥ bhṛtyaḥ | kaṣṭā hiṃsābahulā duṣṭapathe paropadrave dṛṣṭiranveṣaṇaṃ yasya | niṣṭhurā krūrā ākṛtiryasya | vṛṣarūpī apaṣṭuninādaḥ krūrasvanaḥ bhavate tiṣṭhate sma bhavadarśanapathe sthitavān || 6 ||
śākaro'tha jagatīdhṛtihārī mūrtimeṣa bṛhatīṃ pradadhānaḥ |
paṅktimāśu parighūrṇya paśūnāṃ chandasāṃ nidhimavāpa bhavantam || 7 ||
śākara iti | śākkaro vṛṣabhaḥ jagatyā bhūmyā dharti sthairya harttuṃ śīlamasyeti tathā | paśūnāṃ paṅktiṃ parighūrṇya vidrāvya chandasāṃ nidhiṃ vedagarbham | eṣo'riṣṭaḥ śakkarījagatīdhṛtibṛhatīpaṅktticchindomātra muktaḥ | bhavāṃ ntucchandonidhiriti mahān viśeṣa iti ca pratīyate || 7 ||
tuṅgaśṛṅgamukhamāśvabhiyantaṃ saṅgṛhayya rabhasādabhiyaṃ tam |
bhadrarūpamapi daityamabhadraṃ maīyannanadayaḥ suralokam || 8 ||
tuṅgeti | tuṅge śṛṅgamukhe śṛṅgāgre yasya, tuṅgaśṛṅgaṃ mukhaṃ yasyeti vā | abhiyantam abhigacchantam abhiyaṃ bhayarahitaṃ bhadrarūpaṃ vṛṣarūpam abhadraṃ duṣṭaṃ saṅgṛhayya gṛhītvā mardayan nigṛhṇan | amadayaḥ prasādayāmāsitha || 8 ||
citramadya bhagavan! vṛṣaghātāt susthirājani vṛpasthitirurvyām |
vardhate ca vṛṣacetasi bhūyānmoda ityabhinuto'si suraistvam || 9 ||
citramiti | bṛṣaghātādū ariṣṭavadhād urvyāṃ vṛṣasya dharmasya sthitiḥ  susthirājani| vṛṣṇa indrasya cetasi, vṛṣasya dharmasya cetasi, dharmabuddhau sajjane vā | vṛṣaghātād vṛṣasya sthitiḥ vṛṣacetasi modaśceti citram || 9 ||
aukṣakāṇi! paridhāvata dūraṃ vīkṣyatāmayamihokṣavibhedī |
itthamāttahasitaiḥ saha gopairgehagastvamava vātapureśa! || 10 ||
aukṣakāṇīti | he aukṣakāṇi! ukṣasamūhāḥ ! vṛṣabhasamūhāḥ ! yūyaṃ dūraṃ0 paridhāvata | ihāyamukṣavibhedī vṛṣahantā ariṣṭahantā vā | itthamāttahasitaiḥ vyāhṛtahāsyavacanaiḥ || 10 ||
iti sudarśanākhyavidyādharasya śāpamokṣavarṇanaṃ śaṅkhacūḍavadhavarṇanaṃ vṛṣabhāsuravadhavarṇanaṃ ca saptatitamaṃ daśakam |
yatneṣu sarveṣvapi nāvakeśī keśī sa bhojeśituriṣṭavandhuḥ |
tvaṃ sindhujāvāpya itīva matvā saṃprāptavān sindhujavājirūpaḥ || 1 ||
yatreṣviti | yatneṣu nāvakeśī avandhyaḥ sa keśī  tvaṃ sindhujayā lakṣmyā avāpya iti sindhujāvāpyaścedahamapi sindujo bhavāmīti matveva || 1 ||
gandharvatāmeṣa gato'pi rūkṣairnādaiḥ samudvejitasarvalokaḥ |
bhavadvilokāvadhi gopavāṭīṃ pramargha pāpaḥ punarāpatat tvām || 2 ||
gandharvatāmiti | gandharvatām aśvatām gato'pi rūkṣairnādaiḥ | gandharvo hi mṛdunādaḥ || 2 ||
tārkṣyarpitāṅghrestava tārkṣya eṣa cikṣepa vakṣobhuvi nāma pādam|
bhṛgoḥ padāghātakathāṃ niśamya svenāpi śakyaṃ taditīva mohāt || 3 ||
tārkṣyeti | eṣa tārkṣyo danujaḥ | bhṛgurhi viṣṇuṃ vakṣasi padāhan | tatkathāṃ niśamya tat padāhananaṃ svenātmanāpi śakyamiti mohādiva || 3 ||
pravañcayannasya khurāñcalaṃ drāgamuṃ ca cikṣepitha dūradūram |
saṃmūrchito'pi hyatipūrchitena krādhoṣmaṇā khāditumādrutastvām || 4 ||
pravañcayanniti | khugañcalaṃ khuraprahāraṃ pravañcayan chalayan drāg jhaṭiti pādayorgṛhītvāmuṃ dūradūraṃ cikṣepitha visasarjitha | atimūrchitena pravṛddhena| ādrutaḥ abhipatitaḥ || 4 ||
tvaṃ vāhadaṇḍe kṛtadhīkṣca vāhādaṇḍaṃ nyadhāstasya mukhe tadānīm |
taddhṛddhiruddha śvasano gatāsuḥ saptībhavannapyayamaikyamāgāt || 5 ||
tvamini | vāhadaṇḍe aśvavavadhe | bāhādaṇḍaṃ bhujadaṇḍam | taddvṛddhyā bāhādaṇḍavṛddhayā | saptabhivan aśvībhannapi bhagavatyaikyaṃ sāyujyam | vāhadaṇḍe kṛtadhīrapi bāhādaṇḍamiti virodhaśca dyotyate || 5 ||
ālambhamātreṇa paśoḥ surāṇāṃ prasādake nūnna ivāśvamedhe |
kṛte tvayā harṣavaśāt surendrāstvāṃ tuṭuyuḥ keśavanāmadheyan || 6 ||
ālambheti | aśvamedhayāge hi paśumālabhya tena yajane kṛta eva devānāṃ prasādaḥ syāt | iha tvālambhamātreṇa aśvahiṃsāmātreṇa surāṇāṃ prasāho jātaḥ | ato nūtne apūrve aśvamedhayāge aśvavadhe vā kṛte tataḥ keśavanāmadheyaṃ tvāṃ tuṣṭuvuḥ || 6 ||
kaṃsāya te śaurisutatvapruktvā taṃ taddhotkaṃ pratirudhya vācā |
prāptena keśikṣapaṇāvasāne śrīnāradena tvamabhiṣṭuto'bhūḥ || 7 ||
kaṃsāyeti | nāyaṃ kṛṣṇo nandasutaḥ, kintu devakyā aṣṭamamuta iti te śaurisutatvamuktvā tatantadvadhotkaṃ vasudevavadhoghuktaṃ kaṃsaṃ vācā pratirudhya keśikṣapaṇāvasāne prāptena || 7 ||
kadāpi gopaiḥ saha kānanānte nilāyanakrīḍanalolunaṃ tvām |
mayātmajaḥ prāpa durantamāyo vyomābhidho vyomacaroparodhī || 8 ||
kadāpīti | nilāyanakrīḍane tirodhānanirīkṣaṇatparśa narūpe saśraddham | vyomacaroparodhī devaśatruḥ || 8 ||
sa corapālāyitavallaveṣu corāyito gopaśiśun paśūṃdha |
guhāsu kṛtvā pidadhe śilābhistvayā ca buddhvā parimardito'bhūt ||9||
sa iti | kecid vallavāścorāyitāḥ | kecit pālāyitāḥ | mayaputrastu gopaveṣadhārī corāyitaḥ paśūn gopaśiśuṃśca parvataguhāsu kṛtvā nikṣipya piddhe babandha | tvayā parimardito nigṛhītaḥ || 9 ||
evaṃvidhaiścādrutakelibhedairānandamūrchāmatulāṃ vrajasya |
pade pade nūtanayannasīmaṃ parātmarūpin! pavaneśa! pāyāḥ || 10 ||
evaṃvidhairiti | ānandasya mūrchī vṛddhiṃ pade pade anukṣaṇam asīmaṃ niravadhi | atra hetuḥ parātmarūpīti || 10 ||
iti keśivadhavarṇanaṃ vyomāsuravadhavarṇanaṃ ca
ekasaptatitamaṃ daśakam |
atha kaṃsavadhamāha daśakacatuṣṭayena -- 
kaṃso'tha nāradagirā vrajavāsinaṃ tvāmākarṇya dīrṇahṛdayaḥ sa hi gāndineyam |
āhūya kārmukamakhacchalato bhavantamānetumenamahinodahināthaśāyin ! || 1 ||
kaṃsa iti | viṣṇustvadvadhārthaṃ devakyāṃ jāto nandavraje vasatīti nāradasya girā dīrṇahṛdayaścakitamatiḥ gāndineyam akrūraṃ kārmukamakhacchalato dhanuryāganirīkṣaṇārthamiti vyājena ahinot preṣayāmāsa || 1 ||
akrūra eṣa bhavadaṅghriparaścirāya
tvadarśanākṣamamanāḥ kṣitipālabhītyā |
tasyājñayaiva punarīkṣitumudyatastvāmānandabhāramatibhūritaraṃ babhāra || 2 ||
akrūra iti | bhavadaṅghriparaḥ bhavadbhaktaḥ kṣitipālabhītyā kaṃsabhayena ahaṃ kṛṣṇadarśanākṣama iti mano yasya sa tvaddarśanākṣamamanāḥ || 2 ||
so'yaṃ rathena sukṛtī bhavato nivāsaṃ
gacchan manorathagaṇāṃstvayi dhāryamāṇān |
āsvādayan muhurapāyabhayena daivaṃ
saṃprārthayan pathi na kiñcidapi vyajānāt || 3 ||
sa iti | tvayi tvadvitraye manorathagaṇān darśanasparśanādirūpān manasā ghāryamāṇān āsvādayan apāyabhayena svasya kaṃsabhṛtyatādihetukena magavadarśanābhā
vabhayena mamādya śrīkṛṣṇadarśanaṃ bhūyāditi daivaṃ prārthayan | evaṃ pathi tvāmeva cintayan nānyat kiñcidapi vyajānāt || 3 ||
tadevāha -- 
drakṣyāmi vedaśatagītagatiṃ pumāṃsaṃ
spakṣyāmi kiṃsvidapināma pariṣvajeya |
kiṃ vakṣyate sa khalu māṃ ka nu vīkṣitaḥ syāditthaṃ nināya sa bhavanmayameva mārgam || 4 ||
drakṣyāmīti | vedaśataiḥ upaniṣadbhiḥ gītā pratipāditā gatiḥ svarūpaṃ yasya taṃ pumāṃsaṃ puruṣaṃ kiṃsvid drakṣyāmi sprakṣyāmi sparśanasukhamanubhavāmi | apināmaivaṃ bhūyāt | sa khalu bhagavān māṃ kṛtāñjaliṃ 'he adūreti kiṃ vakṣyate | kva nu kasmin pradeśe mayā sa vīkṣitaḥ syāt | itthaṃ bhavatsmaraṇanairantaryeṇa tadālambanatayā bhavanmayaṃ mārga nināya atikrāntavān || 4 ||
bhūyaḥ kramādabhiviśan bhavadaghripūtaṃ
bṛndāvanaṃ haraviriñcasurābhivandyam |
ānandamana iva lagna iva pramohe
kiṃ kiṃ daśāntaramavāpa na paṅkajākṣa ! || 5 ||
bhūya iti | pramohe lagno bahirvikārābhāvānmūḍha iva kiṃ kiṃ daśāntaraṃ nāvāpa || 5 ||
paśyannavandata bhavadvivṛtisthalāni
pāṃsuṣvaveṣṭata bhavaccaraṇāṅkiteṣu |
kiṃ brūmahe bahujanā hi tadāpi jātā
evaṃ tu bhaktitaralā viralāḥ parātman ! || 6 ||
paśyanniti | bhavadvihṛtisthalāni krīḍā sthānāni avandata | bhavaccaraṇāṅkiteṣu pāṃsuṣvaveṣṭata śrīkṛṣṇapādapāṃsava iti sarvāṅgepvakarot | kiṃ brūmahe | tadā bhavataḥ kāle bahujanā jātāḥ, tathāpyakrūravadevaṃ bhaktyā taralāḥ paravaśāḥ viralā nyūnāḥ || 6 ||
sāyaṃ sa gopabhavanāni bhavaccaritragītāmṛtaprasṛtakarṇarasāyanāni |
paśyan pramodasariteva kilohyamāno
gacchan bhavadbhavana nnidhimanvayāsīt || 7 ||
sāyamiti | sāyaṃ sandhyākāle bhavaccaritragītamevāmṛtaṃ, tatprasṛtamamṛtaniṣyandaḥ, tadeva karṇarasāyanaṃ śrotrānandakaraṃ yeṣu tāni gopabhavanāni paśyan pramodarūpayā saritā nadyā udyamāna iva || 7 ||
tāvad dadarśa paśudohavilokalolaṃ bhaktottamāgatimiva pratipālayantam |
bhūman! bhavantamayamagrajavantamantarbrahmānubhūtirasasindhumivodvamantam || 8 ||
tāvaditi | paśudohavilokane lolaṃ saśraddhaṃ bhaktottamasyākrūrasya āgatiṃ pratipālayantamivāyamakrūro bhavanta dadarśa brahmānubhūtirbrahmajñānaṃ, tajjanyo rasasindhuḥ sukhārṇava ānandaḥ | pathi śrīkṛṣṇākārākāritāyāścittavṛtteḥ antaḥsthatvād bhagavaddarśanasamanantaraṃ tatyā indriyadvārā bahirnirgamanāccāntaḥsthaṃ śrīkṛṣṇadarśanasukhaṃ bahirudvamantamivetyarthaḥ || 8 ||
sāyantanāplavaviśeṣaviviktagātrau
dvau pītanīlarucirāmbaralobhanīyau |
nātiprapañcadhṛtabhūṣaṇacāruveṣau
mandasmitārdravadanau sa yuvāṃ dadarśa || 9 ||
bahirbhavanānukūlavyāpāravṛtterudvamateriha bahirbhāvamātre vṛttirnivṛttapreṣaṇapakṣāśrayaṇena | evañcākrūrahṛdayād bahirbhavantaṃ brahmānubhūtirasasthindhumiva sthitamiti bhagavadviśeṣaṇamupapannam | asyā evāpapattaye vyākhyāne 'pathi śrīkṛṣṇa'tyāghuktiḥ | sāyantaneti | sāyantanaḥ sandhyāsamayasthaḥ āplavaḥ ākaṇṭhaṃ snānaṃ tena viśeṣeṇa viviktagātrau svacchadehau nātiprapañcam anatibahulaṃ yathā tathā dhṛtaiḥ bhūṣaṇaiḥ cārurmanoharo veṣo'laṅkiyāviśeṣo yayoḥ || 9||
dūrād rathāt samavaruhya namantamanamutthāpya bhaktakulamaulimathopagūhan |
harṣānmitākṣaragirā kuśalānuyogī
pāṇiṃ pragṛhya sabalo'tha gṛhaṃ ninetha || 10 ||
dūrāditi | harṣādupagūhan mitākṣarayā girā kuśalānuyogī kuśalaṃ pṛcchan gṛhaṃ ninetha praveśayāmāsitha || 10 ||
nandena sākamamitādaramarcayitvā
taṃ yādavaṃ taduditāṃ niśamayyaḥ vārtām |
gopeṣu bhūpatinideśakathāṃ nivedya
nānākathābhiriha tena niśāmanaiṣīḥ || 11 ||
nandeneti | bhūpatinideśakathāṃ kaṃsājñāvacanaṃ nivedya upāyanasaṃbhārasaṃbhṛtaśakaṭasaṃyojanādyādiśya tenākrūreṇa saha nānākathābhiḥ niśāmanaiṣīḥ || 11 ||
tasyāṃ niśāyāṃ śrīkṛṣṇe gopīnāmanyāsaktiśaṅkābhūdityāha -- 
candrāgṛhe kimuta candrabhagāgṛhe nu rādhāgṛhe nu bhavane kimu maitravinde |
dhūrtī vilambata iti pramadābhiruccairāśaṅkito niśi marutpuranātha! pāyāḥ || 12 ||
iti akrūrasya gokulayātrābṛndāvanaprāptibhagavatsamāgamabhagavatkṛtasatkārādīnāṃ varṇanaṃ dvisaptatitamaṃ daśakaṃ sadvikam |
niśamayya tavātha yānavārto bhṛśamārtāḥ paśupālavālikāstāḥ |
kimidaṃ kimidaṃ kathaṃ nvitīmāḥ samavetāḥ paridevitānyakurvan || 1 ||
niśamayyeti | yānavārto śvaḥ prabhāte śrīkṛṣṇaṃ netumāgato'krūra ityevarūpām | kimidam amṛtāsvāde viṣaganamivātikaṣṭamavasthāntaramāpatitam | kathaṃ nvidam asmadbhāgyadoṣād nandasūnorniṣkaruṇatayā vā ityevamādīni paridevitāni bilapanānyakurvan || 1 ||
paridevitānyevāha -- 
karuṇānidhireṣa nandasūnuḥ kathamasmān visṛjedananyanāthāḥ |
bata naḥ kimu daivamevamāsīditi tāstvadvatamānasā vilepuḥ || 2 ||
karuṇeti | no daivaṃ bhāgyam evaṃ niratiśayasukhapradaṃ sahasā naṣṭaṃ cāsīt kimu bata khidyāmahe || 2 ||
caramaprahare pratiṣṭhamānaḥ saha pitrā nijamitramaṇḍalaiśca |
paritāpabharaṃ nitambinīnāṃ śamayiṣyan vyamucaḥ sakhāyamekam || 3 ||
carameti | caramaprahare antyayāme pratiṣṭhamāno nirgacchan ekaṃ sakhāyamāptaṃ dūtatayā vyamucaḥ preṣitavān || 3 ||
acirādupayāmi sannidhiṃ vo bhavitā sādhu mayaiva saṅgamaśrīḥ |
amṛtāmbunidhau nimajjayiṣye drutamityāśvasitā vadhūrakārṣīḥ || 4 ||
acirāditi | vo yupmākaṃ mayā saṅgamaśrīḥ saṃbhogasaukhyam acirād bhavitaiva | kiñca amṛtāmbunidhau sukhārṇave, athavā amṛtaṃ mokṣastadrūpe'mbunidhau | ante mokṣamapi vo vitaripyāmītyarthaḥ || 4 ||
saviṣādabharaṃ sayācñamuccairatidūraṃ vanitābhirīkṣyamāṇaḥ |
mṛdu taddiśi pātayannapāṅgān sabalo'krūrarathena nirgato'bhūḥ || 5 ||
saviṣādeti | vanitābhiḥ atidūraṃ saviṣādabharaṃ yathā bhavati tathā tvadāgamanavilambe'smatprāṇā yāsyanti, ataratvaritamāgantavyamiti sayācñaṃ ca yathā bhavati tathā īkṣyamāṇastvaṃ taddiśi apāṅgān kaṭākṣān mṛdu mukulīkṛtābhyāṃ nayanābhyāṃ pātayan nirgato'bhūḥ || 5 ||
anasā bahulena vallavānāṃ manasā cānugato'tha vallabhānām |
vanamārtamṛgaṃ viṣaṇṇavṛkṣaṃ samatīto yamunātaṭīmayāsīḥ || 6 ||
anaseti | ballavānāṃ gopānām anasā śakaṭena | vallabhānāṃ gopīnāṃ manasānugataḥ ārtāḥ pīḍitā mṛgāḥ yasmin viṣaṇṇā vṛkṣā yasmin tad vanaṃ samatītaḥ atikrāntaḥ || 6 ||
niyamāya nimajjya vāriṇi tvāmabhivīkṣyātha rathe'pi gāndineyaḥ |
vivaśo'jani kinnvidaṃ vibhoste nanu citraṃ tvavalokanaṃ samantāt ||
niyamāyati | niyamāya sandhyāvandanādi kartuṃ vāriṇi yanunājale nimajjya tatra tvāmabhivīkṣya atha unmajjanānantaraṃ rathe'pi tvāmabhitrīkṣā gāndineyaḥ akrūraḥ vivaśo'jani | nanu vibhoste samantādavalokanaṃ kinnu citraṃ, vibhutvād vyāptatvādeva na citramityarthaḥ || 7 ||
punareṣa nimajjya puṇyaśālī puruṣaṃ tvāṃ paramaṃ bhujaṅgabhoge |
arikambugadāmbujaiḥ sphurantaṃ surasiddhoghaparītamāluloke || 8 ||
punariti | eṣo'krūraḥ pugyaśālī sukṛtī, anyathā śeṣaśāyinī bhavato darśanābhāvāt, punarnimajjya tvāṃ paramaṃ puruṣaṃ viṣṇum āluloke dadarśa || 8 ||
sa tadā paramātmasaukhyasindhau vinimayaḥ praṇuvan prakārabhedaiḥ |
avilokya punaśca harṣasindhoranuvṛttyā pulakāvṛtoyau tvām || 9 ||
sa iti | paramātmasaukhyasindhau brahmāna dārṇave | prakārabhedaiḥ saguṇārguṇabhedaiḥ praṇuvan stuvan avilokya tadrūpaṃ tirohitaṃ vīkṣya punaścānavalokane satyāpe harṣabhindhonuvṛtyā paramānandānubhavānivṛtteḥ pulakāvṛtasttvāṃ yayau unmajjya tvatsamīmā || 9 ||
kimu śītalimā mahān jale yat pulako'sāviti coditena tena |
atiharṣaniruttareṇa sārdhaṃ rathavāsī pavaneśa! pāhi māṃ tvam || 10 ||
kinviti | atiharṣaniruttareṇa bhagavataḥ svarūpadarśanenānandabāppaniruddhakapṭhatathā adattottareṇa || 10 ||
iti bhagavato mathurāprasthānavarṇanaṃ yamunājale akrūrastha
bhagavatsvarūpasākṣātkārādivarṇanaṃ ca
trisaptatitamaṃ daśakam
sampāpto mathurāṃ dinārdhavigame tatrāntarasmin vasannārāme vihitāśanaḥ sakhijanairyātaḥ purīmīkṣitum |
prāpo rājapathaṃ ciraśrutidhṛtavyālokakautūhalastrīpuṃsodya dagaṇya puṇya nigalairākṛṣyamāṇo nu kim || 1 ||
samprāpta iti | dinārdhavigame madhyāhne tatra mathurāyām antarasminnārāme kāṅkodyāne vasan rājapathaṃ rājamārgaṃ prāpaḥ prāptavān | ciraśrutyā dhṛtaṃ vyālokakautūhalaṃ yeṣāṃ tebhyaḥ strīpuṃsebhyaḥ udyanti phalonmukhāni agaṇyāni aparimeyāni puṇyānyeva nigalāḥ śṛṅkhalāḥ tairākṛṣyamāṇaḥ kinnu || 1 ||
tatra kathamapi tvatsādṛśyānumeyasukṛtā mathurāstriyastvadarśanasukhamanvabhūvannityāha -- 
tvatpādaghutivat sarāgasubhagāstvanmūrtivad yoṣitaḥ
samprāptā vilasatpayodhararuco lolā bhavadadṛṣṭivat |
hāriṇyastvadurassthalīvadayi te mandasmitamauḍhivannairmalyollasitāḥ kacaugharucivad rājatkalāpāśritāḥ || 2 ||
tvatpādeti | tvatvādaghutiḥ sarāgā raktavarṇā subhaṇā ca | tanmadhurāpurastriyo'pi sarāgāḥ tvahuṇaśravaṇāt tvayi ciraṃ dhṛtapraṇapāḥ sunagāśca | atattvaddarśanārthaṃ samprāptāḥ | tvanmūrtiḥ vilatatpayo ghararuk sajaṭajāladhāśtābalā | tadvat tā api vilasatpayodhararucaḥ āgamanasambhramāt calitakucāḥ | itastataḥ samprāptayoṣiddarśanavyāpṛtatayā lolā cañcalā bhavadṛṣṭiḥ | tadvat tā ani lolāḥ bhavadarśanakautukayuktāḥ | hārāḥ santyasyāmiti hāriṇī urasthalī | manohāriṇya va tāḥ | āya bhagavan ! te tava mandatmitaprauḍhiḥ mandasmitasya praudiḥ udāratā sā nairmalyollasitā | tā āpe nai lyollasitāḥ svacchaśarīrāḥ śuddha manasaśca | tvakacau gharuciḥ rājatkalāpāśritā barhālaṅkṛtā | anyatra kalānā bhūṣaṇāni | sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā ityarthaḥ || 2 ||
tāsāmākalayannapāṅgavalanairmodaṃ praharṣādbhutabyāloleṣu janeṣu tatra rajakaṃ kañcit paṭīṃ prārthayan |
kaste dāsyati rājakīyavasanaṃ yāhīti tenoditaḥ
sadyastasya kareṇa śīrṣamahathāḥ so'pyāpa puṇyāṃ gatim || 3 ||
tāsāmiti | apāṅgavalanaiḥ kaṭākṣavīkṣaṇaiḥ tāsāṃ modamākalayan prāpayan janeṣu praharṣādadrutaḥcca vyālolevū | parṭī vātāṃti| pugṇāṃ gatiṃ mokṣam || 3 ||
bhūyo vāyakamekamāyatamatiṃ toṣeṇa veṣocitaṃ
dāśvāṃsaṃ svapadaṃ ninetha sukṛtaṃ ko veda jīvātmanāt |
mālābhiḥ stavakaiḥ svairapi punarmūlākṛtā mānito
bhakti tena vṛtāṃ dideśitha parāṃ lakṣmī ca lakṣmīpate! || 4 ||
bhūya iti | vāyakaṃ tantuvāyam ekaṃ bhagavatprasādapātram āyatamati vipulamanasaṃ veṣocitaṃ vicitravarṇadukūlādikaṃ dākṣvāṃnaṃ datavadaṃ svapadaṃ sārūpyaṃ ninetha anayaḥ | sukṛtaṃ ko veda, kevalaṃ phalānuneyamiti bhāvaḥ | stabakaiḥ puṣpaiḥ stavaiḥ stotraiśca mālāṃ karotīti mālākṛt tena mānitaḥ pūjitaḥ | parāṃ premalakṣaṇāṃ bhaktiṃ dideśitha dattavān || 4 ||
kubjāmanjavilocanāṃ pathi punarddaṣṭvāṅgarāge tayā
ddatte sādhu kilāṅgarāgamadadāstaspā mahāntaṃ hṛdi |
cittasthāmṛtāmatha prathayituṃ gātre'pi tasyāḥ sphuṭaṃ
gṛhṇan mañju kareṇa tāmudanayastāvajjagatsundarom || 5||
kubjāmiti | kubjāṃ trivaṛām abjavilocanām itarāvayavasaundaryātiśayayuktam | tayāṅgarāge anulepane datte sati tasyā hṛdi mahāntam aṅge bhavanmūrtau rāgagapurāgaṃ bhavatyaparaparyāya madadāḥ | aṅgeti pṛthakpadaṃ vā | cittasthāṃ parokṣām ṛtunāṃ gātre'pi prathāṃyatum aparokṣīkartuṃ mphuṭaṃ nūnaṃ kareṇa mañju anutpāditavedanaṃ yathā bhavati tathā gṛhṇan udanayaḥ ṛjvīcakartha || 5 ||
tāvanniścitavaibhavāstava vibho! nātyantapāpā janā
yatkiñcid dadate sma śaktyanuguṇaṃ tāmbūlamālyādikam |
gṛhṇānaḥ kusumādi kiñcana tadā māga nibaddhāñjalirnātiṣṭhaṃ bata hā yato'dya vipulāmārtiṃ vrajāmi prabho! || 6 ||
tāvaditi | yābadaṅgarāgasamarpaṇena trivakrāmapyanugṛhītavān, tāvat taddṛṣṭāntena tava niścitaṃ vaibhava varapradānaśaktiryaiḥ | ye na cātyantapāpāḥ, te janāḥ śaktyanuguṇaṃ yatkiñcit tāmbūlaptālyādikaṃ tubhyaṃ dadate sma dattavantaḥ | te ca kṛtārthāḥ | tadāhamapi kiñcana kusumādi gṛhṇāno mārge nibaddhāñjalirnātiṣṭham | hā bata kaṣṭaṃ, yatastadakaraṇādadya vipulāṃ mahatīm ārti rogādijanitāṃ pīḍāṃ vrajāmi || 6 ||
eṣyāmīti vimuktayāpi bhagavannālepadātryā tayā
dūrāt kātarayā nirīkṣitagatistvaṃ prāviśo gopuram |
āghoṣānumivatvadāgaptamahārṣollaladdevakīvakṣojapragalatpayorasamiṣāt tvatkīrtirantargatā || 7||
eṣyāmīti | madgṛhānāgaccheti prārthitena tvayā kṛtakāryo'hameṣyāmyāgacchāmīti vimuktayā preṣitayāpi tayā ālepadātryā trivakrayā kātarayā virahebhītayā
dūrād nirīkṣitā gatiryasya sa tvaṃ gopuraṃ prāviśaḥ praviṣṭavān | āghoṣeṇa tvaddarśanādipravṛttapaurajanajanitenānumitaḥ kalpito yastvadāvamaḥ tajjanitena mahatā harṣeṇa ullalataḥ kṣubhitād devakīvakṣojād yaḥ payorasaḥ prajagāla, taghghājena tvatkīrtistvatpraveśāt pūrvamantargatā nagaraṃ praviṣṭā || 7 ||
āviṣṭo nagarī mahotsavavartī kodaṇḍaśālāṃ vrajan
mādhuryeṇa nu tejasā nu puruṣairdūreṇa dattāntaraḥ |
sragbhibhūṣitamarcitaṃ varadhanurmā meti vādāt puraḥ
prāgṛhṇāḥ samaropayaḥ kila samākrākṣīrabhāṅkṣīrapi || 8 ||
āviṣṭa iti | mādhuryeṇa vā śrīmūrtervā madhuratayā tejasā adhṛpyatayā vā pratiṣeddhumaśakyatayā rakṣirupairdūreṇa dattāntaraḥ parihṛtamārgaḥ san mā mā dhanuḥ spṛśeti vādād rakṣipurutravacanāt puraḥ pūrvameva dhanuḥ prāgṛhṇāḥ agrahīḥ | punarapi mā mā samāropayeti vādāt puraḥ samaropayaḥ | evaṃ mā mā ākarṣayeti vādāt  puraḥ samākrākṣīḥ | mā mā bhāṅgīriti vādāt puraḥ abhāṅkṣīrapi || 8 ||
śvaḥ kaṃsakṣapaṇotsavasya purataḥ prārambhatūryopamakṣcāpadhvaṃsamahādhvanistava vibho! devānaromāñcayat |
kaṃsasyāpi ca vepathustaduditaḥ kodaṇḍakhaṇḍadvayīcaṇḍābhyāhatarakṣipūruṣaravairutkulito'bhūt tvayā || 9 ||
śva iti | kaṃsakṣapaṇaṃ kaṃsavadhanāmotsavo bhāvī | tasya purataḥ pūrve maṅgalārthaḥ prārambhatūryaravaḥ kartavyaḥ | tatpaśantava cāpadhvaṃsamahādhvanirdevānaromāñcayat kaṃsamapi vadhiṣyatīti romāñcitaśarīrānakṛta | kaṃsasyāpi taduditaḥ cāpadhvaṃ samahādhvaniśravaṇājjāto vepathuḥ kampaḥ tasyaiva kodaṇḍasya khaṇḍadvayyā caṇḍābhyāhatānāṃ sudṛḍha prahṛtānāṃ rakṣipūruṣāṇāṃ ravaiḥ dhiga dhig hā hatāḥ sma iti vilapanarūpaiḥ tvayā utkūlito vardhito'bhūt || 9 ||
'vadhiḥ prakṛtyantaraṃ vyañjanānto'sti' iti 'janivadhyāśca' iti sūtre vṛttikāraḥ |
śiṣṭairduṣṭajanaikṣca dṛṣṭamahimā prītyā ca bhītyā tataḥ
saṃpaśyan purasampadaṃ pravivaran sāyaṃ gato vāṭikām |
śrīdāmnā saha rādhikāvirahajaṃ khedaṃ vadan prasvapannānandabhavatārakāryaghaṭanād vāteśa! saṃrakṣa mām ||10 ||
śiṣṭairiti | yiṣṭhaiḥ sajjanaiḥ prītyā haṭo mahimā adbhutaparākramo yasya | duṣṭajanaiḥ bhītyā dṛṣṭamahimā san sāyaṃ sandhyāyāṃ vāṭikāṃ śakaṭasthānaṃ gataḥ śrīdāmnāptena gopena | avatārakāryaghaṭanāt kaṃse hate tacchṛśurasya māgadhasya paribhavāt tasya tadvandhūnāṃ śiśupāladīnāṃ ca vadho bhāvītyādicintayā bhūbhāraharaṇanirvāhadarśanādānandan || 10 ||
iti bhagavato mathurāpurīpraveśarajakanigrahavāyakamālākārakubjānugrahadhanurbhaṅgādivarṇanaṃ catussaptatitamaṃ daśakam |
prātaḥ santrastabhoja kṣitipativacasā prastute mallatūrye
saṅge rājñāṃ ca mañcānabhiyayuṣi gate nandagope'pi harmyam |
kaṃse saudhādhirūḍhe tvamapi sahabalaḥ sānugakṣcāruveṣo
radvāraṃ gato'bhūḥ kupitakuvalayāpīḍanāgāvalīḍham || 1 ||
prātariti | aparepuḥ prātaḥ santrastanya devakyā aṣṭama putratayā atimānuṣeṇa parākramega cāyaṃ māṃ haniṣyatīti bhītasya (kaṃsanya) vacasā ājñayā mallatūrye mallakrīḍānusārivādyaprayoge prastute ārabdhe sati | abhiyayuṣi ārūḍhe ca sati | mahāmātrapraṇodāt kupitena kuvalayāpīḍākhyena nāgena avalīḍhaṃ vighnitapraveśaṃ mallaraṅgadvāraṃ gato'bhūḥ || 1 ||
pāpiṣṭhāpehi mārgād drutamiti vacasā niṣṭhurakruddhabuddherambaṣṭhasya praṇodādadhikajavajuṣā hastinā gṛhyamāṇaḥ |
kelīmukto'tha gopīkucakalaśaciraspardhinaṃ kumbhamasya
vyāhatyālīyathāstvaṃ caraṇabhuvi punarniṃrgato valguhāsī || 2 ||
pāpiṣṭheti | he pāpiṣṭha! atiśayena duṣṭa! mārgād drutataram apehi apasara iti tvadvacasā niṣṭhurā svataḥ krūrā kruddhā ca buddhiya tasyāmbaṣṭhasya mahāmātrasya praṇodāt codanayā | kelīmuktaḥ krīḍayā hattikarād vigalitaḥ atha anantaraṃ gopīkucakalaśāciraspardhinam, ata eva tatsāmyāpādaka puṇyena bhagavatkarasparśalābhaḥ, asya kumbhaṃ mastakaṃ vyāhatya hastena tāḍayitvā caraṇabhuvi caraṇacatuṣṭayamadhye līlayālīyathāḥ || 2 ||
hastaprāpyo'pyagamyo jhaṭiti munijanasyeva dhāvan gajendraṃ
krīḍannāpatya bhūmau punarabhipatatastasya dantaṃ sajīvam |
mūlādunmūlya tanmūlagamahitamahāmauktikānyātmamitre
prādāstvaṃ hāramebhirlalitaviracitaṃ rādhikāyai diśeti || 3 ||
hasteti | hastena śuṇḍayā prāpyaḥ spṛśyamāno'pyagamyo'mpṛśyamāno yathā bhavati tathā jhaṭiti dhāvan krīḍan bhūmau svayamāpatya daṇḍavat patitvā punaḥ paścādabhipatato dantābhyāṃ bhūmau ghnatastasya sajīvaṃ jīvena saha dantaṃ mūlādakhaṇḍamunmūlya tanmūlagāni dantamūlasthāni mahitāni parārdhyāni mahānti sthūlāni maukti kānyātmamitre śrīdāmni prādāḥ emirlāletavi citaṃ manoharatayā racitaṃ hāraṃ mālāṃ rādhikāyai matpreyasyai diśa pradehīti vacanapūrvakam || 3 ||
gṛhṇānaṃ dantamaṃse yutamatha halinā raṅgamaṅgāviśantaṃ
tvāṃ maṅgalyāṅbhīrabhasahṛtamanolocanā vīkṣya lokāḥ |
haṃho dhanyo nu nando nahi nahi paśupālāṅganā no yaśodā
no no dhanyekṣaṇāḥ smatrijagati vayameveti sarve śarāṃsuḥ || 4 ||
gṛhṇānamiti | aṅga! he śrīkṛṣṇa ! itara ntamaṃse gṛhṇānena halinā yutaṃ
mallakrīḍāraṅgatāviśantam | maṅgalyarūpayāṅgabhaṅgayā rabhasahṛtāni balātkāreṇa hṛtāni
manolocanāni yepām | haṃho he lokāḥ! yatputro'yaṃ, sa nandastrijagati dhanyaḥ nu niścitam | athavā nahi nahi, yāḥ enaṃ stanāntare kṛtvā āliṅganādisukhamanvabhūvan, tāḥ paśupāṅganāḥ nandādapi dhanyatamā iti kecit | no no gopyaḥ, yāsya mātṛbhāvamanvabhūt, sā yaśodā dhanyā | no no yaśodā, vayameva dhanyekṣaṇāḥ yāni śrīkṛṣṇākhye parabrahmaṇi nimagnāni, tānyasmākamīkṣaṇāntheva bhāgyavantīti śaśaṃsuḥ stutavantaḥ || 4 ||
nanu sarveṣāmevaṃ mathi snehabahumānādeḥ kiṃ kāraṇamityāśaṅkāyāmāha -- 
pūrṇa brahmaiva sākṣānniravadhiparamānandasāndraprakāśaṃ
gopeṣu tvaṃ vpalāsīrna khalu bahujanaistāvadāvedito'bhūḥ |
dṛṣṭvāya tvāṃ tadedaṃprathamamupagate puṇyakāle janaughāḥ
pūrṇānandā vipāpāḥ sarasamabhijagustvatkṛtāni smṛtāni || 5 ||
pūrṇamiti | pūrṇaṃ sarvā_gatam | atastavaiva sarvaśarīreṣu jīvarūpeṇāvasthānād ātmani ca sarveṣāṃ niratiśayasnehabahumānādisambhavāt tvayīdamupapadyata iti bhāvaḥ | nanvekasyānekatrāvatthānaṃ kathamityatrāha -- brahmeti | ākāśavad bṛhattvādevetyarthaḥ | nanu brahmaṇaḥ kathaṃ cākṣuṣataḥ tatrāha -- sākṣādū gopeṣu vyalāsīriti | śrīkṛṇātmanāvatīrṇatvādityarthaḥ | nanu śarīraparigrahaśced mamāpi saṃsārapraṅga ityaṃtrāha -- niravadhiparamānandasāndraprakāśamiti | niravadhi nityaṃ paramānandarūpaṃ sāndraprakāśaṃ vijñānadhanam | tatra nityatvāt jananamaraṇāsambhavaḥ, paramānandarūpatvād duḥkhābhāvaḥ, vijñānaghanatvādajñānābhāvaśceti saṃsāranidānābhāvānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi kaṃsādayo māṃ brahmatayā na jānantītyata āha -- na khalviti | yaiḥkai|ścajjanmajanmāntarājitapuṇyaparipākavadbhireva tvaṃ brahmetyāvedito'bhūrityarthaḥ | nanu tarhi pāviṣṭhānāṃ kathaṃ madarśanaṃ saṃbobhavītīti cet, satyaṃ, puṇyapāpayoḥ puṇyādhikye sati talaripākatraśena tvaddarśanasukhaṃ tāvat sambhavati | tatastvaddarśanasmaraṇacaritravarṇanādinā vigatapāpāḥ santaḥ kramādete'pi tvattattvajñāne'dhikriyanta evetyabhiprāyeṇāha -- dṛṣṭvātha tvāmiti | janaughāḥ ānino'jñāninaśca tvāṃ puṇyakāle puṇyaparipāka upagate idaṃprathamaṃ dṛṣṭvā vigatapāpāḥ pūrṇānandāśca santaḥ tvaddarśanena smṛtāni tvaccaritāni sarasaṃ sānandaṃ yathā bhavati tathā abhijaguḥ praśaśaṃsurityarthaḥ || 5 ||
cāṇūro mallavīrastadanu nṛpagirā muṣṭiko muṣṭiśālī
tvāṃ rāmaṃ cābhiṣede jhaṭajhaṭiti mitho muṣṭipātātirūkṣam |
utpātāpātanākarṣaṇavividharaṇānyāsatāṃ tatra citraṃ
mṛtyoḥ prāgeva mallaprabhuragamadayaṃ bhūriśo bandhamokṣān || 6||
cāṇūra iti | jhaṭajhaṭiti saśabdaṃ mitho muṣṭipātairatirūkṣaṃ yathā bhavati tathā | utpāta unnayanam, āpātanaṃ bhuvyavapātanam, mitho baddhahastapādayoritaretarata ākarṣaṇam, evaṃvidhāni vividhāni raṇāni tāvadāsatām | mṛtyormaraṇāt prāgevānyonyahastapādādikṛtān bandhān mokṣāṃścāyamagamat, kimucyate mṛto bandhānmokṣamagamaditīti bhāvaḥ || 6 ||
hā dhik kaṣṭaṃ kumārau sulalitavapuṣau mallavīrau kaṭhorau
na drakṣyāmo vrajāmastvaritamiti jane bhāṣamāṇe tadānīm |
cāṇūraṃ taṃ karodbhrāmaṇavigaladasuṃ pothayāmāsithorvyāṃ
piṣṭo'bhūnmuṣṭiko'pi drutamatha halinā naṣṭaśiṣṭairdadhāve || 7 ||
hā dhigiti | kareṇa yad uddhāmaṇaṃ tenaiva vigaladasum urvyāṃ pothayāmāsitha prakṣiptavān | naṣṭaśiṣṭaiḥ prāṇaparīpsayā dadhāve dhāvanamakāri || 7 ||
kaṃsaḥ saṃvārya tūrya khalamatiravidan kāryamāryān pitṛṃstānāhantuṃ vyāptamūrtestava ca samaśiṣad dūramutsāraṇāya |
ruṣṭo duṣṭoktibhistvaṃ garuḍa iva giriṃ mañcamañcanudañcatkhaḍavyāvalgadussaṃgrahamapi ca haṭhāt prāgrahīraugrasenim || 8 ||
kaṃsa iti | kaṃsaḥ kāryam evaṃ sthite yat kartavyaṃ tvatpādapatanaṃ, tad bhavitavyatābalāvū avidan ajānan āryān sajjanān | āhantuṃ nigrahāya | pitṛn
vasudevanandograsenādīn | vyāptasyotsāraṇamaśakyaṃ tadapi samaśiṣat ājñāpayāmāsa | duṣṭasya kaṃsasyoktibhiḥ ruṣṭaḥ kupitastvam añcan utpatan udañcataḥ unnatmitasya khaḍasya vyāvalgena calanena dussaṅgrahaṃ grahītumaśakyamapi haṭhād balātkāreṇaiva augraseniṃ kaṃsaṃ prāgrahīḥ || 8 ||
sadyo niṣpiṣṭasandhiṃ bhuvi narapatimāpātya tasyopariṣṭāt
tvayyāpātyetadaiva tvadupari patitā nākināṃ puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ |
kiṃ kiṃ brūmastadānīṃ satatamapi bhiyā tvadgatātmā sa bheje
sāyujyaṃ tvadvadhotthā parama ! paramiyaṃ vāsanā kālanemeḥ || 9 ||
sadya iti | āpātya pātayitvā tasyopariṣṭād upari āpātye āpatati tvayi tava kaṃsoparipatanaṃ nākināṃ puṣpavṛṣṭipatanamapi yugapajjātamityarthaḥ | kiṃ kiṃ brūmaḥ | tvatsmaraṇaṃ tvatsāyujyaṃ kuruta ityatropapattīrbahu na kathayāmaḥ, yatastadānīṃ bhiyā satataṃ tvadgatātmā sa kaṃsaḥ sāyujyaṃ bheje | yad bhītyāpi viṣṇusmaraṇaṃ, seyaṃ paraṃ kevalaṃ kālanemestvadvadhotthā vāsanā | kaṃsaḥ prāk kālanemirnāmāsuraḥ | sa ca viṣṇunā hataḥ || 9 ||
tadbhrātṛnaṣṭa piṣṭvā drutamatha pitarau sanmanugrasenaṃ
kṛtvā rājānamuccairyadukulamakhilaṃ modayan kāmadānaiḥ |
bhaktānāmuttamaṃ coddhavamamaragurorāptanītiṃ sakhāyaṃ
labdhvā tuṣṭo nagaryo pavanapurapate ! rundhi me sarvarogān ||10||
taditi | tadbhrātṝn kaṃsānujān kaṅkanyagrodhakādīn | pitarau devakīvasudevau | uccaiḥ atiśayena modayan | amaraguroḥ bṛhaspateḥ āptā gṛhītā nītirnayaśāstraṃ yena tamuddhavaṃ sakhāyaṃ kāryasacivatayā narmasacivatayā ca labdhvā tuṣṭaḥ kṛtakṛtya iva nagaryāṃṃ vasanniti śeṣaḥ || 10 ||
iti kuvalayāpīḍavadhavarṇanaṃ bhagavato mallaraṅgapraveśavarṇanaṃ mallayuddhavarṇanaṃ
mallavadhavarṇanaṃ kaṃsavadhādivarṇanaṃ ca
pañcasaptatitamaṃ daśakam |
āpatatītyāpātyaḥ ‘bhavyageya'  iti nipātanāt kartari ṇyat |
athopanayanapūrvakaṃ vidyāṃ gṛhītvā mṛtaputraṃ dakṣiṇīkṛtya samāvṛtta ityāha -- 
gatvā sāndīpanimatha catuṣṣaṣṭimātrairahobhiḥ
sarvajñastvaṃ saha musalinā sarvavidyāṃ gṛhītvā |
putraṃ naṣṭaṃ yamanilayanādāhṛtaṃ dakṣiṇārthaṃ
dattvā tasmai nijapuramagā nādayan pāñcajanyam || 1 ||
gatveti | svataḥ sarvajñastvaṃ, tathāpi lokasaṅgrahārthaṃ sāndīpani nāma muniṃ gatvopanayanaṃ prāpya sarvavidyāḥ sāṅgopāṅgopavedarūpāḥ gṛhītvā naṣṭaṃ mṛtaṃ guroḥ putraṃ tasmai gurave dakṣiṇārthaṃ dattvā pāñcajanyaṃ śaṅkhaveṣadharapañcajananāmāsuravadhāt tadaṅgaprabhavaṃ śaṅkham || 1 ||
śrīkṛṣṇena bṛndāvanānmathurāprasthāne gopīnāṃ paritāpa śamanārthaṃ yaduktam ‘acirādupayāmi sannidhiṃ va' iti, tat satyaṃ kartumātmanirviśeṣamuddhavaṃ mathurāsthaḥ svayaṃ preṣayāmāsetyāha -- 
smṛtvā smṛtvā paśupasudṛśaḥ premabhārapraṇunnāḥ
kāruṇyena tvamapi vivaśaḥ māhiṇoruddhavaṃ tam |
kiñcāmuṣmai paramasuhṛde bhaktavaryāya tāsāṃ
bhaktyudrekaṃ sakalabhuvane durlabhaṃ darśayiṣyan || 2 ||
smṛtveti | paśupasudṛśo gopyaḥ tvāṃ smṛtvā smṛtvā punaḥ punaḥ smaraṇāt premabhāreṇa anurāgātiśayena praṇunnāḥ paravaśā jātāḥ | tvamapi kāruṇyena bhaktavātsalyena vivaśaḥ paravaśaḥ| kiñceti, uddhavapreṣaṇe hetvantaramapyastītyarthaḥ | tadevāhaamuṣmā iti | tāsāṃ bhakyudrekam uddhavāt bhaktyatiśayam amuṣmai uddhavāya pradarśayiṣyan pradarśanārthamityarthaḥ | paramasuhṛde bhaktavaryāyeti bhaktavaryābhimānāpagama eva tvatparamasuhṛttvamucitamiti tavābhiprāya ityabhiprāyaḥ || 2 ||
tvanmāhātmyaprathimapiśunaṃ gokulaṃ prāpya sāyaṃ
tvadvārtābhirbahu sa ramayāmāsa nandaṃ yaśodām |
prātarddṣṭvā maṇimayarathaṃ śaṅkitāḥ paṅkajākṣyaḥ
śrutvā prāptaṃ bhavadanucaraṃ tyaktakāryāḥ samīyuḥ || 3 ||
tvaditi | tvanmāhātmyasya prathimā vistāraḥ tatpiśunaṃ tatsūcakaṃ gokulaṃ tatra nandagehaṃ prāpya sāyaṃ sandhyāyām | ataḥ khakhagehasthābhirgopībhiruddhavāgamanaṃ na viditam | ataḥ kevalaṃ nandaṃ yaśodāmeva bahu atiśayena ramayāmāsa | prātastvadāgamaṃ śaṅkitāḥ || 3 ||
dṛṣṭvā cainaṃ tvaduṣamalasadveṣabhūṣābhirāmaṃ
smṛtvā smṛkhā tava vilasitānyuccakaistāni tāni |
ruddhālāpāḥ kathamapi punargadgadāṃ vācamūcuḥ
saujanyādīn nijaparabhidāmapyalaṃ vismarantyaḥ || 4 ||
dṛṣṭyeti | tvaduṣamābhyāṃ tvadveṣabhūṣaṇasadṛśābhyāṃ lasadbhyāṃ veṣabhūṣābhyām ābharaṇatadvinyāsaviśeṣābhyāmabhirāmam | ataḥ sadṛśadarśanāduccakairatiśayena tāni tāni vilasitāni krīḍāḥ smṛtvā bāṣpakaṇṭhatayā ruddha ālāpo yāsām | tvadgatamānasatayā nijaparabhidāṃ khaparabhedamapi || 4 ||
śrīman! kiṃ tvaṃ pitṛjanakṛte preṣito nirdayena
kāsau kānto nagarasudṛśāṃ hā hare! nāya! pāyāḥ
|
ākṣleṣāṇāmamṛtavapuṣo hanta te cumbanānāmunmādānāṃ kuhakavacasāṃ vismaret kānta ! kā vā || 5||
śrīmanniti | nirdayena kṛṣṇena pitṛjanakṛte nandrayaśodayoḥ priyārthaṃ kiṃ preṣito'si | asau nagarasudṛśāṃ kāntaḥ vidagdhajanavidheya idānīṃ va iti praṇayavacanasamanusmṛtabhagavadguṇakṛtapāravaśyā ūcuḥ hā iti | hare! bhaktajanasantāpaśamana! hā nātha! asmatprāṇasaṃrakṣaka! nijarūpāmṛtapānena jīvaya | amṛtamayaṃ vapuryasya tasya tava ākṣloṣāṇāṃ cumbanānāṃ hanta unmādānām ātmānaṃ vismṛtyaiva kṛtānāṃ tattadvilasitānāṃ kuhakavacasāṃ kapaṭavacanānām | athavā he kuhaka! kapaṭa! tavonmādānām ananusaṃhitapūrvāparāṇāṃ vacasāṃ kā vā vismaret || 5 ||
rāsakrīḍālulitalalitaṃ viślathatkeśapāśaṃ
mandodbhinnaśramajalakaṇaṃ lobhanīyaṃ tvadaṅgam |
kāruṇyābdhe! sakṛdapi samāliṅgituṃ darśayeti
premonmādād bhuvanamadana! tvatpriyāstvāṃ vilepuḥ ||6||
rāseti | rāsakrīḍāyāṃ lulitaṃ marditaṃ lalitaṃ sundaraṃ ca tvadaṅgaṃ he kāruṇyābdhe ! bhaktavatsala ! sakṛd ekavāramapi samāliṅgitumapi | bhuvanamadana ! sarveṣāṃ sarvadāpi sarvakāmonmādaprada ! || 6 ||
evampāyairvivaśavacanairākulā gopikāstāstvatsandeśaiḥ prakṛtimanayat so'tha vijñānagarbhaiḥ |
bhūyastābhirmuditamatibhistvanmayībhirvadhūbhistattadvārtāsarasamanayat kānicid vāsarāṇi || 7 ||
evamiti | athoddhavo vijñānagarbhaistvatsandeśaistāḥ prakṛtiṃ kṛtārthatāmanayat | tvanmayībhiḥ tvaccintayātmānaṃ vismarantībhiḥ tattadvārtābhiḥ sarasaṃ yathā bhavati tathā kānicid vāsarāṇyanayat || 7 ||
tvatprodgānaiḥ sahitamaniśaṃ sarvato gehakṛtyaṃ
tvadvārtaiva prasarati mithaḥ saiva cotsvāpalāpāḥ |
ceṣṭāḥ prāyastvadanukṛtayastvanmayaṃ sarvamevaṃ
dṛṣṭvā tatra vyamuhadadhikaṃ vismayāduddhavo'yam || 8 ||
tvaditi | utkhāpalāpāḥ svapnavacanāni saiva tvadvārtaiva | ceṣṭāḥ vyāpārāḥ prāyastvadanukaraṇarūpāḥ | evam uktaprakāreṇa tāsāṃ sarve tvanmayaṃ dṛṣṭvā ayamuddhavo vismayādadhikaṃ vyamuhad vimumoha pratipattimūḍho'bhūdityarthaḥ || 8 ||
evamuddhavaḥ strīṇāmapīśvare svātiśayapremalakṣaṇabhaktidarśanāt tyakttabhakttaṃmanyabhābastāḥ pratyekamāśvāsayāmāsa | tatra pradhānabhūtāyāṃ rādhāyā māśvāsanaprakāramāha -- 
rādhāyā me priyatamamidaṃ matpriyaivaṃ bravīti
tvaṃ kiṃ maunaṃ kalayasi sakhe! māninī matpriyeva |
ityādyeva pravadati sakhi! tvatpriyo nirjane māmitthaṃvādairaramayadayaṃ tvatpriyāmutpalākṣīm || 9 ||
rādhāyā iti | he sakhi! rādhe! tvatpriyaḥ śrīkṛṣṇaḥ nirjane māmityādi tvatsambandhivacanameva pravadati | kathaṃ, he sakhe! uddhava! me rādhāyā idaṃ priyatamaṃ, matpriyā sai vaṃ bravīti, kiṃ tvaṃ māninī mapriyeva maunaṃ kalayasi dhatsa iti | janasaṃsadi tu tasya gambhīracetastayā sannapi tvadvirahakhedo jñātumaśakyaḥ | ayam uddhava itthaṃvādaistvatpremasūcakairvacanaireva tvapriyāṃ rādhāmaramayat || 9 ||
sandeśamāha -- 
eṣyāmi drāganupagamanaṃ kevalaṃ kāryabhārād
vikṣleṣe'pi smaraṇadṛḍhatāsambhavānmāstu khedaḥ |
brahmānande milati nacirāt saṅgamo vā viyogastulyo vaḥ syāditi tava girā so'karonnirvyathāstāḥ ||10||
eṣyāmīti | ahaṃ drāg jhaṭiti eṣyāmi | anupagamanaṃ bhavatsannidhāvanāgamanaṃ kevalaṃ mama kāryabhārādeva, na premamānyāt | kiñca bhavatīnāṃ madviśleṣo mahate phalāya, matsmaraṇasya dṛḍhatāsambhavāt | tasmād viśleṣaviṣayaḥ khedo māstu | smaraṇadṛḍhatāyāḥ samādhyaparaparyāyāyāḥ phalamāha -- brahmānanda iti | brahmarūpasyamama smaraṇadṛḍhatāyāṃ satyāṃ nacirāda brahmānande milati sati vo bhavatīnāṃ matsaṅgamo madviyogaśca tulyaḥ syāt | virahe'pi vaḥ sarvātmanā na madvirahaḥ, bhavatīnāmapyantarantaryāmirūpeṇa mamaivāvasthānāditi bhāvaḥ | iti yā tava gīḥ sandeśaḥ, tayā sa uddhavastā nirvyathā akarot || 10 ||
evaṃ bhaktiḥ sakalabhuvane nekṣitā na śrutā vā
kiṃ śāstraughaiḥ kimiha tapasā gopikābhyo namo'stu |
ityānandākulamupagataṃ gokulāduddhavaṃ taṃ
dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭo gurupurapate! pāhi māmāmayaughāt || 11||
evamiti | evaṃ gopikānāmiva | śāstraughaistapasā vā kiṃ phalam | tadrahitānāmapi bhaktidarśanānna śāstrādiphalaṃ bhaktirityarthaḥ | tasmād gopikābhyo namo'stu | iti ‘evam evaṃbhaktirityādinamo'stvi'tyantamantra purassaraṃ punaḥ punastā namaskṛtya ānandākulaṃ gokulādupagataṃ tvatsannidhiṃ prāptaṃ taṃ tyaktabhaktābhimānamuddhavaṃ dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭaḥ idānīmasya matsakhitvādhikāro jāta iti santuṣṭaḥ || 12 ||
iti uddhavadūtyavarṇanaṃ ṣaṭsaptatitamaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
sairandhrayāstadanu ciraṃ smarāturāyā
yāto'bhūḥ salalitamuddhavena sārdham |
āvāsaṃ khadupagamotsavaṃ sadaiva
dhyāyantyāḥ pratidinavāsasajjikāyāḥ || 1 ||
sairandhrayā iti | adyaiṣyati kānta iti pratidinaṃ taducitaveṣabhūṣaṇavibhavasaṃbharaṇapūrvakaṃ vāsagṛhe pratipālanāt vāsakasajjikāyāḥ sairandhrayā anulepadācyā āvāsaṃ salalitaṃ lalitaveṣabhūṣaṇasahāyasampatsahitaṃ yathā bhavati tathā yāto'bhūḥ | tvadupagamotsavaṃ tvatsaṅgamakautukam || 1 ||
upagate tvayi pūrṇamanorathāṃ
pramadasambhramakamprapayodharām |
vividhamānanamādadhatīṃ mudā
rahasi tāṃ ramayāñcakṛṣe sukham || 2 ||
upagata iti | pramadasambhrameṇa harṣātiśaya janitasaujanyavyāpāratvarayā kamprau kampanaśīlau payodharau yasyāḥ | vividhamānanaṃ saṃmānanam ādadhatīṃ to sukhaṃ ramayāñcakṛṣe || 2 ||
pṛṣṭā varaṃ punarasāvavṛṇod varākī
bhūyastvayā suratameva niśāntareṣu |
sāyujyamastviti vaded budha eva kāmaṃ
sāmīpyamastvaniśamityapi nābravīt kim || 3 ||
pṛṣṭeti | yadicchasi, tad vṛṇviti tvayā punaḥ prasthāne varaṃ pṛṣṭā asau sairandhrī bhūyastvayā suratamevāvṛṇod, na mokṣam tvatsāyujyasāmīpyādirūpam | tatra budhaḥ sāyujyākhyamokṣarasābhijña eva sāyujyamastviti vadet prārthayet | eṣā tu varākī nirviṣayamokṣarasānabhijñetyetat kāmaṃ bhavatu | aniśaṃ sāmīpyamastvityapi kiṃ nābravīt | tvatsāmīpyasukhaṃ tu tayā kañcit kālamanubhūtameva | tasmāt sarvamīśvarecchāparatantramiti veditavyam || 3 ||
tato bhavān deva! niśāsu kāsucinmṛgīdṛśaṃ tāṃ nibhṛtaṃ vinodayan |
adādupakṣloka iti śrutaṃ sutaṃ
sa nāradāt sātvatatantravid babhau || 4 ||
tata iti | upaśloka iti śrutaṃ viśrutaṃ sutam adādū utpādayāmāsa | saḥ nāradāt sāttvatatantraṃ pāñcarātrādyāgamaṃ vettīti tathā || 4 ||
akrūramandiramito'tha baloddhavābhyāmabhyarcito bahu nuto muditena tena |
enaṃ visṛjya vipināgatapāṇḍaveyavṛttaṃ viveditha tathā dhṛtarāṣṭraceṣṭām || 5 ||
akrūreti | enam akrūraṃ visṛjya vipinād āgatānāṃ pāṇḍaveyānāṃ vṛtta tathā dhṛtarāṣṭraceṣṭāṃ ca viveditha || 5 ||
vighātājjāmātuḥ paramasuhṛdo bhojanṛpaterjarāsandhe rundhatyanavadhiruṣāndhe'tha mathurām|
rathādyairdyaulabdhaiḥ katipayabalastvaṃ valayutastrayoviṃśatyakṣauhiṇi tadupanītaṃ samahathāḥ || 6 ||
vighātāditi | jarāsandhasya jāmātā kaṃsaḥ, tatputryorastiprāstyoḥ patitvāt, tasya bhojanṛpatervadhād anavadhiruṣāndhe jarāsandhe mathurāṃ rundhati sati dyoḥ svargāllabdhairmātalinānītai rathādyaiḥ saparikaraiḥ katipayabalaḥ parimitasenāsahitaḥ tadupanītaṃ jarāsandhāhṛtaṃ trayoviṃśatyakṣauhiṇi balaṃ samahathāḥ sañjahartha || 6 ||
baddhaṃ balādatha valena balottaraṃ tvaṃ
bhūyo balodyamarasena mumocithainam |
niśśeṣadigjayasamāhṛtaviśvasainyāt
ko'nyastato hi valapauruṣavāṃstadānīm || 7 ||
baddhamiti | atha balena balāḍū baddhaṃ balena uttaram utkṛṣṭaṃ bhūyo balodyamarasena jarāsandhaḥ parājito bhūyo'pi bhūribalamupaneṣyatīti kautukena enaṃ jarāsandhaṃ mumocitha | kīdṛśo jarāsandha ityapekṣāyāmāha -- niśśeṣadigjaye samāhṛtāni viśveṣāṃ sarvanṛpāṇāṃ sainyāni yena tasmājjarāsandhādanyaḥ kaḥ || 7||
bhagnaḥ sa lagnahṛdayo'pi nṛpaiḥ praṇunno
yuddhaṃ tvayā vyadhita ṣoḍaśakṛtva evam |
akṣauhiṇīḥ śiva śivāsya jaghantha viṣṇo!
sambhūya saikanavatitriśataṃ tadānīm || 8 ||
bhagna iti | sa  jarāsandhastvatsakāśāt saptadaśakṛtvaḥ parājitaḥ || 8 ||
aṣṭādaśe'sya samare samupeyuṣi tvaṃ
dṛṣṭvā puro'tha yavanaṃ yavanatrikoṭhyā |
tvaṣṭrā vidhāpya puramāśu payodhimadhye
tatrātha yogavalataḥ svajanānanaiṣīḥ || 9 ||
aṣṭādaśa iti | asyāṣṭādaśe samare samupeyuṣi āgāmini sati tvaṃ yavanānāṃ mlecchānāṃ trikoṭyā yutaṃ yavanaṃ purato dṛṣṭvā tvaṣṭrā viśvakarmaṇā payodhimadhye āśu dvāravatyākhyaṃ puraṃ vidhāpya kārayitvā yogabalataḥ yogaiśvaryeṇa tatra vajanānanaiṣīḥ || 9 ||
padbhyāṃ tvaṃ padmamālī cakita iva purānnirgato dhāvamāno
mleccheśenānuyāto vadhasukṛtavihīnena śaile nyaleṣīḥ |
suptenāṅghracāhatena drutamatha mucukundena bhasmīkṛte'smin
bhūpāyāsmai guhānte sulalitavapuṣā tasthiṣe bhaktibhāje ||10||
padbhyāmiti | tvayā vadhaḥ sukṛtaikalabhyaḥ tadvihīnena | śaile tadguhāyāṃ devadattayā nidrayā ciraṃ suptena yavanena kṛṣṇo'yamiti bhrāntyā acāhatena mucukundenāsmin yavane bhasmīkṛte tasmai mucukundāya tasthiṣe ātmānaṃ prakāśya sthitavānasi || 10 ||
aikṣvāko'haṃ virakto'smyakhilanṛpasukhe tvatprasādaikakāṅkṣī
hā deveti stuvantaṃ varavitatiṣu taṃ nispṛhaṃ vīkṣya hṛṣyan |
muktestulyāṃ ca bhaktiṃ dhutasakalamalaṃ mokṣamapyāśu dattvā
kārye hiṃsāviśuddhayai tapa iti ca tadā prāttha lokapratītyai || 11||
aikṣvāka iti | aikṣvākaḥ ikṣvākuvaṃśajaḥ akhilanṛpasukhe rājyabhoge viraktaḥ | āśu samanantarabrāhmaṇajanmānte dhutasakalamalam ajñānakalpitasaṃsāranivṛttirūpaṃ mokṣaṃ tāvatkālaparyantaṃ muktestulyāṃ niratiśayasukharūpāṃ premalakṣaṇāṃ bhaktimapi dattvāṃ kṣātradharmeṇa kṛtāyā hiṃsāyā viśughghai tapaḥ kāryamiti ca tadā prāttha abravīḥ | yadyapi tvaddarśanādeva kṛtakṛtyo mucukundaḥ, tathāpi lokapratītyai, kṣatradharma lokān grāhayitumidamātthetyarthaḥ || 11 ||
ārthikārthakathanamidam |
tadanu mathurāṃ gatvā hatvā camūṃ yavanāhutāṃ
magadhapatinā mārge sainyaiḥ pureva nivāritaḥ |
caramavijayaṃ dapīyāsmai pradāya palāyito
jaladhinagarī yāto bātālayeśvara! pāhi mām || 12 ||
tadanviti | dvārakāṃ vrajan mārge magadhapatinā jarāsandhena purā yathā, tathā nivāritaḥ caramavijayam aṣṭādaśasaṅgrāmajayaṃ darpāya ahaṅkāramutpādayituṃ pradāya palāyito dhāvan pravarṣaṇākhyaṃ girimāruhya tasmādutpatya bhuvi patitvā jarāsandhenālakṣito jaladhinagarī dvārakāṃ yātaḥ || 12 ||
iti sairandhrayāmupakṣlokotpattivarṇanaṃ jarāsandhādiyuddhavarṇanaṃ
mucukundānugrahavarṇanaṃ ca
saptasaptatitamaṃ daśakaṃ sadvikam |
tridivavardhakivardhitakauśalaṃ tridaśadattasamastavibhūtimat |
jaladhimadhyagataṃ tvamabhūṣayo navapuraṃ vapurañcitarociṣā || 1 ||
tridiveti | tridivavardhakinā viśvakarmaṇā vardhitāni sarvaprayatnena kṛtāni kauśalāni yatra | tridaśairlokapālaiḥ dattāḥ pratyarpitāḥ samastā vibhūtayaḥ sudharmāpārijātādyāḥ santyasminniti tathā | navapuraṃ dvārakāṃ vapurañcitena rociṣā dehakāntyā tvamabhūṣayaḥ || 1 ||
daduṣi revatabhūbhṛti revatīṃ halabhṛte tanayāṃ vidhiśāsanāt |
mahitamutsavaghoṣamapūpuṣaḥ samuditairmuditaiḥ saha yādavaiḥ || 2 ||
