daduṣīti | revato nāma rājā revatyākhyāmātmatanayāmādāya satyalokaṃ gatvā brahmāṇaṃ pṛṣṭavān ‘enāṃ kasmai pradāsyāmī'ti |'tvatsamānakālikā rājānaḥ sampratyadarśanaṃ gatāḥ | kintu tvayi bhūmigate sati rāmakṛṣṇāvavatīrṇo bhaviṣyataḥ | tayo rāmāyaināṃ dehī'ti vidheḥ śāsanāt | samuditaiḥ sahitaiḥ || 2 ||
atha vidarbhasutāṃ khalu rukmiṇīṃ praṇayinīṃ tvayi deva! sahodaraḥ |
svayamāditsata cedimahībhuje svatamasā tamasādhumupāśrayan || 3 ||
atheti | sahodaro rukmī | svayaṃ, nānye pitrādayaḥ, cedimahībhuje śiśupālāya aditsata dātumaicchat | svatamasā svājñānena tamasādhuṃ cedinṛpam upāśrayan || 3 ||
ciradhṛtapraṇayā tvayi bālikā sapadi kāṅkṣitabhaṅgasamākulā |
tava nivedayituṃ dvijamādiśat svakadanaṃ kadanaṅgavinirmitam || 4 ||
cireti | kadanaṅgena vinirmitaṃ svasya kaḍhanaṃ pīḍām || 4 ||
dvijasuto'pi ca tūrṇamupāyayau tava puraṃ hi durāśadurāsadam |
mudamavāpa ca sādarapūjitaḥ sa bhavatā bhavatāpahṛtā svayam || 5 ||
dvijeti | durāśadurāsadaṃ hi duṣṭāśayaireva duṣpraveśam | bhavatāpaṃ saṃsāraduḥkhaṃ haratīti bhavatāpahṛt || 5 ||
sa ca bhavantamavocata kuṇḍine nṛpasutā khalu rājati rukmiṇī |
tvayi samutsukayā nijadhīratārahitayā hi tayā prahito'smyaham || 6||
sa ceti | kuṇḍine vidarbhapure | tayā prahito'smi || 6 ||
rukmiṇyāḥ sandeśamāha dvābhyāṃ -- 
tava hṛtāsmi puraiva guṇairahaṃ harati māṃ kila cedinṛpo'dhunā |
ayi kṛpālaya! pālaya māmiti prajagade jagadekapate! tayā || 7 ||
taveti | guṇaiḥ pāśasthānīyaistava bhaktavātsalyādiguṇaiḥ ahaṃ purā svayaṃvaraprasaṅgāt prāṃgeva | adhunā svayaṃvare cedinṛpaḥ śiśupālaḥ māṃ tvaddhṛtamanasaṃ harati hartumutsahate kila | ayi kṛpālaya! bhaktavatsala ! māṃ pālaya śarīramapi hṛtvā manasā saṃyojayeti tathā prajagade || 7 ||
aśaraṇāṃ yadi māṃ tvamupekṣase sapadi jīvitameva jahāmyaham |
iti girā sutanoratanoda bhṛśaṃ suhṛdayaṃ hṛdayaṃ tava kātaram || 8 ||
aśaraṇāmiti | ayaṃ dvijaḥ iti sutano rukmiṇyā girā tava hṛdayaṃ bhṛśaṃ kātaraṃ priyatamāprāṇāpāyamīru atanot || 8 ||
akathayastvamathainamaye sakhe! tadadhikā mama manmathavedanā |
nṛpasamakṣamupetya harāmyahaṃ tadayi tāṃ dayitāmasitekṣaṇām || 9 ||
akathaya iti | tadadhikā rukmiṇyā adhikā | tat tasmād ayi sakhe ! nṛpasamakṣaṃ paśyatāṃ nṛpāṇāṃ tāṃ dayitāṃ harāmi || 9 ||
pramuditena ca tena samaṃ tadā rathagato laghu kuṇḍinameyivān |
gurumarutpuranāyaka! me bhavān vitanutāṃ tanutāṃ nikhilāpadām ||10||
pramuditeneti | tvadvacanaśravaṇena pramuditena tena dvijena samaṃ saha rathe gataḥ laghu śīghram eyivān gatavān bhavān | tanutāṃ nyūnatāṃ vitanutāṃ kurutām ||10||
iti dvārakāvāsavarṇanaṃ rukmiṇīpariṇaye bhagavataḥ kuṇḍinapuraprāptivarṇanaṃ ca
aṣṭasaptatitamaṃ daśakam |
balasametabalānugato bhavān puramagāhata bhīṣmakamānitaḥ |
dvijasutaṃ tvadupāgamavādinaṃ dhṛtarasā tarasā praṇanāma sā || 1 ||
baleti| kalahaśaṅkinā balasametena senāsahitena śrībalabhadreṇānugataḥ bhīṣmakeṇa rukmiṇyā janakena mānitaḥ pūjitaḥ | agāhata praviveśa| tvadupāgamavādinaṃ prāptastvaddayita iti nivedayantam | dhṛtarasā sañjātakautukā tarasā praṇanāma, nānyat kiñcid dattavatī | sā śriyoṃ'śabhūtā namaskāreṇa svātmānaṃ samagrāṃ śriyamarpitavatī || 1 ||
bhuvanakāntamavekṣya bhavadvapurnṛpasutasya niśamya ca ceṣṭitam |
vipulakhedajuṣāṃ puravāsināṃ saruditairuditairagamanniśā || 2 ||
bhuvaneti | nṛpasutasya rukmiṇaḥ ceṣṭitaṃ śiśupālāya kanyāṃ sādhayitumudyamaṃ rukmiṇyāḥ sadṛśaṃ bhavadvapuścāvekṣya saruditairuditaiḥ pralāpaiḥ || 2 ||
tadanu vanditumindumukhī śivāṃ vihitamaṅgalabhūṣaṇabhāsurā |
niragamad bhavadarpitajīvitā svapurataḥ purataḥ subhaṭāvṛtā || 3 ||
tadanviti | svapurataḥ svasya purāt | purataḥ purobhāge || 3 ||
kulavadhūbhirupetya kumārikā girisutāṃ paripūjya ca sādaram |
muhurayācata tatpadapaṅkaje nipatitā patitāṃ tava kevalam || 4 ||
kuleti | kulavadhūbhiḥ maṅgalastrībhiḥ sahopetya tatpadapaṅkaje girisutāpadapaṅkaje nipatitā kevalaṃ  tava patitāṃ śrīkṛṣṇo me patirbhūyāditi muhurayācata || 4 ||
samavalokya kutūhalasaṅkule nṛpakule nibhṛtaṃ tvayi ca sthite |
nṛpasutā niragād girijālayāta suruciraṃ rucirañjitadiṅmukhā || 5 ||
samiti| tvayi ca nibhṛtaṃ sthite tāṃ hartuṃ randhraṃ pratīkṣya gūḍhaṃ sthite sati | rucyā rañjitāni diṅmukhāni yayā || 5 ||
bhuvanamohanarūparūcā tadā vivaśitākhilarājakadambayā |
tvamapi deva! kaṭākṣavimokṣaṇaiḥ pramadayā madayāñcakṛṣe manāk || 6 ||
bhuvaneti | vivaśitaḥ paravaśīkṛtaḥ akhilarājakadambo yayā tayā pramadayā rukmiṇyā tvamapi manāga īṣad madayāñcakṛṣe sañjātamadaḥ kṛtaḥ || 6 ||
kka nu gamiṣyasi candramukhīti tāṃ sarasametya kareṇa haran kṣaṇāt |
samadhiropya rathaṃ tvamapāhṭathā bhuvi tato vitato ninado dviṣām || 7 ||
kva nu gamiṣyasīti | apāhṛthāḥ apahṛtavān || 7 ||
kka nu gataḥ paśupāla iti krudhā kṛtaraṇā yadubhiśca jitā nṛpāḥ |
na tu bhavānudacālyata tairaho piśunakaiḥ śunakairiva kesarī || 8 ||
kka nu gata iti | kṛto raṇo yaiḥ te nṛpāḥ yadubhirjitāḥ parājitāḥ | taiḥ piśunakaiḥ durjanaiḥ bhavān na tūdacālyata, tān bhavān nājīgaṇadityarthaḥ | śunakaiḥ śvabhiḥ kesarīva || 8||
tadanu rukmiṇamāgatamāhave vadhamupekṣya nivadhya virūpayan |
hṛtamadaṃ parimucya baloktibhiḥ puramayā ramayā saha kāntayā || 9 ||
tadanviti | rukmiṇaṃ virūpayan vapanādinā virūpaṃ kurvan baloktibhiḥ agrajavacanaiḥ parimucya vigalitabandhanaṃ kṛtvā ramayā kāntayā saha puramayāḥ prāptavān || 9 ||
navasamāgamalajjitamānasāṃ praṇayakautukajṛmbhitamanmathām |
aramayaḥ khalu nātha! yathāsukhaṃ rahasi tāṃ hasitāṃśulasanmukhīm ||10||
navasamāgameti | hasitāṃśubhirlasanmukhīṃ tāṃ rahasyaramayaḥ || 10 ||
vividhanarmabhirevamaharniśaṃ pramadamākalayan punarekaḍhā |
ṛjumateḥ kila vakragirā bhavān varatanoratanodatilolatām || 11 ||
vividhanarmabhiriti | pramadaṃ harṣamākalayan janayan | vaṛgirā vakroktayā ṛjumateḥ rukmiṇyāḥ atilolatāṃ paravaśatāmatanot || 11 ||
tadadhikairatha lālanakauśalaiḥ praṇayinīmadhikaṃ ramayannimām |
ayi mukunda ! bhavaccaritāni naḥ pragadatāṃ gadatāntimapākuru || 12 ||
tadadhikairiti | pragadatāṃ stuvatāṃ gadatānti rogapīḍām apākuru ||12||
iti rukmiṇīpariṇayavarṇanam ekonāśītitamaṃ daśakaṃ sadvikam |
satrājitastvamatha lubdhavadarkalabdhaṃ
divyaṃ syamantakamaṇiṃ bhagavannayācīḥ |
tatkāraṇaṃ bahuvidhaṃ mama bhāti nūnaṃ
tasyātmajāṃ tvayi ratāṃ chalato vivodum || 1 ||
satrājita iti | tasya syamantakayācanasya kāraṇaṃ jāmbavadanugrahatatsutāvāptisatyabhāmādātṛpratigrahītṛsatrājicchatadhanvavadhākrūraprītyāpādanādirūpaṃ bahuvidhaṃ mama bhāti pratibhāti | nūnam evaṃ tu niścinomi | tasya satrājita ātmajāṃ tvayi ratāṃ satyabhāmāṃ chalato vyājena vivoḍhuṃ vivāhārtham | khāparādhabhīruṇānicchatāpi khasutāṃ dāpayitumityarthaḥ || 1 ||
adattaṃ taṃ tubhyaṃ maṇivaramanenālpamanasā
prasenastadbhrātā galabhuvi vahan prāpa mṛgayām |
ahannenaṃ siṃho maṇimahasi māṃsabhramavaśāt
kapīndrastaṃ hatvā maṇimapi ca bālāya dadivān || 2 ||
adattamiti | kapīndro jāmbavān || 2 ||
śaśaṃsuḥ satrājidgiramanu janāstvāṃ maṇiharaṃ
janānāṃ pīyūṣaṃ bhavati guṇināṃ doṣakaṇikā |
tataḥ sarvajño'pi svajanasahito mārgaṇaparaḥ
prasenaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā harimapi gato'bhūḥ kapiguhām || 3 ||
śaśaṃsuriti | kṛṣṇo me bhrātaraṃ hatvā maṇi hṛtavāniti satrājidgiramanu tacchrutvā janāḥ prāṅmaṇiyācanāt tava maṇijighṛkṣāmapyutpaśyantastvāṃ maṇiharaṃ śaśaṃsuḥ | guṇināṃ doṣakaṇikā doṣaleśo'pi janānāṃ pīyūṣavadākhādanīyā bhavati hi | hariṃ siṃhamapi dṛṣṭvā kaperjāmbavato guhām || 3 ||
bhavantamavitarkayannativayāḥ svayaṃ jāmbavān
mukundaśaraṇaṃ hi māṃ ka iha rodbhumityālapan |
vibho! raghupate! hare! jaya jayetyalaṃ muṣṭibhikṣcaraṃstava samarcanaṃ vyadhita bhaktacūḍāmaṇiḥ || 4 ||
bhavantamiti | ativayāḥ vṛddhaḥ ata eva bhavantaṃ śrīkṛṣṇātmanāvatīrṇam avitarkayan anirūpayan mukundo viṣṇuḥ śaraṇaṃ yasya taṃ māṃ roddhum iha jagati kaḥ śaktaḥ vibho! hare! raghupate! jaya jaya svotkarṣamāviṣkurvityālapana mantramiva punaḥ punarjapan caran pradakṣiṇaṃ kurvanniva dvandvayuddhamārge sañcaran muṣṭibhiḥ kusumairiva tava svasvāminaḥ samarcanaṃ samyak saṃrambhātmakabhaktipurassaraṃ pūjāṃ vyadhita prasādayāmāsa, yato'yaṃ bhaktacūḍāmāṇiḥ tvatprītyai kṛtasakalakarmā || 4 ||
buddhātha tena dattāṃ navaramaṇīṃ varamaṇīṃ ca parigṛhṇan |
anugṛhṇannamumāgāḥ sapadi ca satrājite maṇiṃ mādāḥ || 5 ||
buddhveti | atha buddhvā tvanmuṣṭipātena sañjātavyathatayā tvāṃ purāṇapuruṣaṃ jñātvā svāparādhamārjanāya tena jāmbavatā dattāṃ navām aprauḍhāṃ ramaṇīṃ jāmbavatīṃ varamaṇīṃ syamantakaṃ ca parigṛhṇan anuṃ bhaktam anugṛhṇan pāṇipadmena pratyaṅgamabhimṛśyāpanītākhilaparitāpaṃ kurvan dvārakāmāgāḥ | sapadi āgamanānantarameva satrājite maṇiṃ tatprāptyākhyānapūrvakaṃ prādāḥ dattavān || 5||
tadanu sa khalu vrīḍālolo vilolavilocanāṃ
duhitaramaho dhīmān bhāmāṃ giraiva parārpitām |
adita maṇinā tubhyaṃ labhyaṃ sametya bhavānapi
pramuditamanāstasyaivādānmaṇi gahanāśayaḥ || 6 ||
tadanviti | sa khalu satrājit satyabhāmākhyaṃ strīratnaṃ ratnamapi śrīkṛṣṇāya dattvā ātmāparādhaṃ pramājmīti śubhā dhīrasyāstīti dhīmān giraiva, na kriyayā parāya śatadhanvane arpitāṃ pratiśrutām maṇinā saha tubhyam adita dattavān | bhavānapi maṇerlabhyaṃ svarṇabhārāṣṭakaṃ sametya dātavyamiti samayapūrvakaṃ prāpya tasya satrājita eva maṇimadāt | kimarthaṃ gahanāśayaḥ bhūyo'pyasya maṇihetukāmāpadaṃ vidhātuṃ vānyat kiñcidveti devairapyavidita āśayo'bhiprāyo yasya || 6 ||
vrīḍākulāṃ ramayati tvayi satyabhāmāṃ
kaunteyadāhakathayātha kurūn prayāte |
hī gāndineyakṛtavarmagirā nipātya
satrājitaṃ śatadhanurmaṇimājahāra || 7 ||
vrīḍākulāmiti | jatugehe kaunteyā dagdhā iti kathā lokavādaḥ, tayā tvayi kurūn hastinapuraṃ prayāte sati hī kaṣṭaṃ gāndineyasya akrūramya kṛtavarmaṇaśca pratiśrutasyādātāraṃ jahīti girā śatadhanvā satrājitaṃ nipātya hatvā maṇimājahāra || 7 ||
śokāt kurūnupagatāmavalokya kāntāṃ
hatvā drutaṃ śatadhanuṃ samaharṣayastām |
ratne saśaṅka iva maithilagehametya
rāmo gadāṃ samaśiśikṣata dhārtarāṣṭram || 8 ||
śokāditi | tāṃ satyabhāmāṃ pitṛhanturvadhena samaharṣayaḥ prasāditavān | ratne maṇau saśaṅka iva maṇihetoḥ śatadhanvānaṃ nihatavāniti khyāpayituṃ tadvāsasi māṇimanviṣṭavānityarthaḥ|‘tvaṃ purīṃ gatvā maṇimanvicche'ti kṛṣṇamuktvā rāmo maithilasya gehaṃmetya dhārtarāṣṭraṃ suyodhanaṃ gadāṃ samaśiśikṣata || 8 ||
nanvakrūro bhaktaḥ kathaṃ satyabhāpāyāḥ pitaraṃ hantuṃ śatadhanvānaṃ niyuktavānityāśaṅkayāsya bhaktammanyatāgarvāpanayanāya kubuddhistvayaivotpāditetyāha akrūra iti dvābhyām -- 
akrūra eṣa bhagavan ! bhavadicchayaiva
satrājitaḥ kucaritasya yuyoja hiṃsām |
akrūrato maṇimanāhṛtavān punastvaṃ
tasyaiva bhūtimupadhātumiti bruvanti || 9 ||
akrūra iti | akrūrataḥ śatadhanvanā śrīkṛṣṇabhītyā palāyane sati nyastaṃ maṇiṃ tvamanahṛtavān | tasyākrūrasya bhūtim aiśvaryam upadhātum utpādāyitumiti bruvanti | abhijñā iti śeṣaḥ || 9 ||
bhaktastvayi sthirataraḥ sa hi gāndineyastasyaiva kāpathamatiḥ kathamīśa! jātā |
vijñānavān praśamavānahamityudīrṇa
garva dhruvaṃ śamayituṃ bhavatā kṛtaiva || 10 ||
bhakta iti | kāpathe kutsite mārge hiṃsādau | udīrṇam utpannam || 10 ||
yātaṃ bhayena kṛtavarmayutaṃ punastamāhūya tadvinihitaṃ ca māṇiṃ prakāśya |
tatraiva suvratadhare vinidhāya tuṣyan
bhāmākucāntaraśayaḥ pavaneśa! pāyāḥ || 11 ||
yātamiti | satrāji ddhanturvadhāt tatprayojakayora|vayorapi vadhaḥ kṛṣṇena bhāvīti bhayena yātaṃ videśagataṃ kṛtavarmasahitaṃ tam akrūraṃ punardūtamukhena āhūya anīya śatadhanvanā tasminnakrūre vinihitaṃ prakāśya agrajādibhyaḥ pradarśya tatra akrūra eva vinidhāya tupyan kṛtakṛtyaḥ san || 11 ||
iti syamantakopākhyānamaśītitamaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
snigdhāṃ mugdhāṃ satatamapi tāṃ lālayan satyabhāmāṃ
yāto bhūyaḥ saha khalu tayā yājñasenīvivāham |
pārthaprītyai punarapi manāgāsthito hastipuryāṃ
śakraprasthaṃ puramapi vibho! saṃvidhāyāgato'bhūḥ || 1 ||
snigdhāmiti | snigdhāṃ praṇayinīṃ mugdhāṃ mohanarūpām | saṃvidhāya viśvakarmaṇā kārayitvā || 1 ||
bhadrāṃ bhadrāṃ bhavadavarajāṃ kauraveṇārthyamānāṃ
tvadvācā tāmahṛta kuhanāmaskarī śakrasūnuḥ |
tatra kruddhaṃ balamanunayan pratyagāstena sārdhaṃ
śakraprasthaṃ priyasakhamude satyabhāmāsahāyaḥ || 2 ||
bhadrāmiti | bhadrāṃ subhadrāṃ bhavato'varajāṃ kanīyasīm | kuhanāmaskarī kapaṭayatiḥ | tatra subhadrāharaṇe | tena arjunena sārdham | pratyagāḥ gatavān || 2 ||
tatra krīḍannapi ca yamunākūladṛṣṭāṃ gṛhītvā
tāṃ kālindī nagaramagamaḥ khāṇḍavaprīṇitāgniḥ |
bhrātṛtrastāṃ praṇayavivaśāṃ deva! paitṛṣvaseyīṃ
rājñāṃ madhye sapādi jaddiṣe mitravindāmavantīm || 3 ||
tatreti | tatra śakraprasthe krīḍan arjunena saha mṛgayāṃ gacchan tāṃ sūryaputrīṃ kālindīm | tadaiva khāṇḍavena prīṇitastarpito'gniryena | bhrātṛbhyāṃ vindānuvindābhyāṃ tadvaraṇapratiṣedhāt trastāṃ rājñāṃ madhye svayaṃvare ja ( hṛ? hi) ṣe hṛtavān || 3 ||
satyāṃ gatvā punarudavaho nagnajinnandanāṃ tāṃ
baddhvā saptāpi ca vṛṣavarān saptamṛrtirnimeṣāt |
bhadrāṃ nāma madaduratha te deva! santardanādyāstatsoda ryā varada! bhavataḥ sāpi paitṛṣvaseyī || 4 ||
satyāmiti | punarnagnajinnṛpate rājadhānīṃ gatvā ātmānaṃ saptadhā kṛtvā sapta vṛṣavarān baddhvā tannandanāṃ satyām udavahaḥ | tatsodaryāṃ santardanādisodarīṃ śrutakīrteḥ sutāṃ bhadrāṃ te tatsodarāḥ te tubhyaṃ pradaduḥ | paitṛṣvaseyī vapudevasodaryāḥ putrī || 4 ||
pārthādyairapyakṛtalavanaṃ toyamātrābhilakṣyaṃ
lakṣaṃ chitvā śapharamavṛthā lakṣa ṇāṃ madrakanyām -- 
aṣṭāvevaṃ tava samabhavan vallabhāstatra madhye
śuśrotha tvaṃ surapatigirā bhaumadukṣceṣṭitāni || 5 ||
hakārarephasaṃyoge pākṣikaṃ laghutvaṃ hrasvasya 'prahe vā' iti |
pārthādyairiti | pārthāghaiḥ bhīmaduryodhanamāgadhādibhiraṇyakṛtalavanam aśakyacchedanaṃ toyamātre kevalaṃ  jale pratibimvarūpeṇābhilakṣyaṃ śapharaṃ matsyātmakaṃ lakṣam | avṛthāḥ vṛtavān || 5 ||
smṛtāyātaṃ pakṣipravaramadhirūḍhastvamagamo
vahannaṅke bhāmāmupavanamivārātinagaram |
vibhindan durgāṇi truṭitapṛtanāśoṇitarasaiḥ
puraṃ tāvat prāgjyotiṣamakuruthāḥ śoṇitapuram || 6 ||
smṛtāyātamiti | smṛtāyātaṃ smaraṇamātreṇa prāptam| arāterbhaumasya nagaram | durgāṇi giriśastrajalāgnyanilādimayāni vibhindan truṭitānāṃ śastrakṛttānāṃ pṛtanānāṃ śoṇitarasaiḥ rudhiradravaiḥ prāgjyotiṣākhyaṃ puraṃ śoṇitaṃ raktavarṇamakuruthāḥ || 6 ||
murastvāṃ pañcāsyo jaladhivanamadhyādudapatat
sa cakre cakreṇa pradalitaśirā maṅkṣu bhavatā |
caturdantairdantāvalapatibhirindhānasamaraṃ
rathāṅgenacchitvā narakamakarostīrṇanarakam || 7 ||
mura iti | muro nāma daityaḥ jaladhivanamadhyāt samudrajalāntarbhāgāt | saḥ muraḥ bhavatā cakreṇa pradalitaśirāścakre kṛtaḥ | indhāne pravṛddhe samare narakaṃ bhaumaṃ tīrṇanarakaṃ saṃsārottīrṇam || 7 ||
stuto bhūmyā rājyaṃ sapadi bhagadatte'sya tanaye
gajaṃ caikaṃ dattvā prajighayitha nāgān nijapurīm |
khalenābaddhānāṃ svagatamanasāṃ ṣoḍaśa punaḥ
sahasrāṇi strīṇāmapi ca dhanarāśiṃ ca vipulam || 8 ||
stuta iti | khalena bhaumena āvaddhānāṃ strīṇāṃ ṣoḍaśasahastrāṇi vipulaṃ dhanarāśiṃ caturdantān nāgānapi ca nijapurīṃ prajighayitha prāhiṇoḥ || 8 ||
bhaumāpāhṛtakuṇḍalaṃ tadaditerdātuṃ prayāto divaṃ
śakrādyairmahitaḥ samaṃ dayitayā ghustrīṣu dattahiyā |
hṛtvā kalpataru ruṣābhipatitaṃ jitvendramabhyāgamastattu śrīmadadoṣa īdṛśa iti vyākhyātumevākṛthāḥ || 9||
bhaumāpāhṛtakuṇḍalamiti | bhaumenendrādapahṛtaṃ kuṇḍalam aditestanmātre dātum | ghustrīṣu śacyurvaśyādiṣu rūpātiśayena dattā hrīryayā tathā dayitayā samaṃ saha mahitaḥ pūjitaḥ | tat pārijātaharaṇenendrasya paribhavajananaṃ śrīmadadoṣa īdṛśo yathendrasyeti vyākhyātuṃ prakāśayitumevākṛthāḥ kṛtavān || 9 ||
kalpadruṃ satyabhāmābhavanabhuvi sṛjan vghaṣṭasāhasrayoṣāḥ
svīkṛtya pratyagāraṃ vihitabahuvapurlālayan kelibhedaiḥ |
āścaryānnāradālokitavividhagatistatra tatrāpi gehe
bhūyaḥ sarvāsu kurvan daśa daśa tanayān pāhi vātālayeśa ! ||10||
kalpadrumiti | satyabhāmāyā bhavanabhuvi gṛhodyāne sṛjan sthāpayan pratyagāraṃ vihitabahuvapuḥ bhavanaṃ prati bhavanaṃ prati rūpabhedena sthitvā vdhaṣṭasāhasrayoṣāḥ yugapat svīkṛtya pariṇīya āścaryāt tadāścarya śrutvā nāradenālokitā vividhā gatayo gārhasthyabhedā yasya || 10 ||
iti subhadrāharaṇavarṇanaṃ śrīkṛṣṇasya mahiṣyantaraparigrahavarṇanaṃ
narakāsuravadhādivarṇanaṃ ca
ekāśītitamaṃ daśakam |
pradyumno raukmiṇeyaḥ sa khalu tava kalā śambareṇāhṛtastaṃ
hatvā ratyā sahāpto nijapuramaharad rukmikanyāṃ ca dhanyām |
tatputro'thāniruddho guṇanidhiravahad rocanāṃ rukmipautrīṃ
tatrodvāhe gatastvaṃ nyavadhi musalinā rukmyapi ghūtavairāt || 1 ||
praghumna iti | tava kalā viṣṇvaṃśaḥ taṃ śambaraṃ hatvā ratyā bhāryayā saha nijapuramāptaḥ praghumno rukmikanyāṃ rukmavatīm avahad upayeme | tatrāniruddhodvāhe ghūtavairāt chalena balabhadre parājayamāropyādhikṣepād musalinā rukmī nyavadhi hataḥ| apiśabdena kāliṅgasya dantāḥ pāṭitā ityapyuktam || 1 ||
vāṇasya sā valimutasya sahasravāhormāheśvarasya mahitā duhitā kilopā |
tvatpautramenamaniruddhamadṛṣṭapūrva
svapne'nubhūya bhagavan ! virahāturābhūt || 2 ||
bāṇasyeti | māheśvarasya śivabhaktasya | mahitā kṣlāghyā || 2 ||
yoginyatīva kuśalā khalu citralekhā
tasyāḥ sakhī vilikhatī taruṇānaśeṣān |
tatrāniruddhamupayā viditaṃ niśāyāmāneṣṭa yogavalato bhavato niketāt || 3 ||
yogīti | yoginī aṇimādyaṣṭhaiśvaryavatī atīva kuśalā citrakarmādikalākauśalavatī ca | tatra vilikhiteṣu tathā viditam ayameva svapnadṛṣṭa iti, aniruddhaṃ bhavato niketād dvārakāyāḥ āneṣṭa ānītavatī || 3 ||
kanyāpure dayitayā sukhamāramantaṃ
cainaṃ kathañcana babandhupi śarvabandhau |
śrīnāradoktatadudantadurantaroṣaistvaṃ tasya śoṇitapuraṃ yadubhirnyarundhāḥ || 4 ||
kanyāpura iti | dayitayā uṣayā samam āramantaṃ krīḍantam | enam aniruddhaṃ śarvabandhau bāṇe babandhuṣi nāgapāśena baddhaṃ kurvati sati śrīnāradoktāt tadudantād aniruddhabandhanarūpād vṛttāntād duranto'navadhī roṣo yeṣāṃ tairyadubhiḥ saha tvaṃ tasya bāṇasya śoṇitā khyaṃ nagaraṃ nyarundhāḥ āvṛṇoḥ || 4 ||
purīpālaḥ śailapriyaduhitnātho'sya bhagavān
samaṃ bhūtatrātairyadubalamaśaṅkaṃ nirurudhe |
mahāprāṇo vāṇo śaṭiti yuyudhānena yuyudhe
guhaḥ pradhumnena tvamapi purahantrā jaghaṭiṣe || 5 ||
purīpāla iti | asya bāṇasya purīpālaḥ śailasya himavataḥ priyaduhituḥ pārvatyāḥ nātho bhagavān | mahāprāṇaḥ mahābalaḥ | tvamapi purahantrā śrīśaṅkareṇa saha jaghaṭiṣe ayudhyathāḥ || 5 ||
niruddhāśeṣāstre mumuhuṣi tavāstreṇa giriśe
drutā bhūtā bhītāḥ pramathakulavīrāḥ pramathitāḥ |
parāskandat skandaḥ kusumaśaravāṇaiśca sacivaḥ
sa kumbhāṇḍo bhāṇḍaṃ navamiva balenāśu vibhide || 6 ||
niruddheti | niruddhāni pratyastraiḥ śamitāni aśeṣāstrāṇi yasya | tava mohanāstreṇa mumuhuṣi mohaṃ gate ca sati | drutāḥ palāyitāḥ | pramathitāḥ pīḍitāḥ | kusumaśarasya pradyumnasya bāṇaiḥ skandaḥ parāskandat pratinivṛttaḥ | bāṇasya sacivaḥ kumbhāṇḍo navaṃ jalapūrṇa mṛṇmayaṃ bhāṇḍamiva bibhide bhinnaḥ || 6 ||
cāpānāṃ pañcaśatyā prasabhamupagate chinnacāpe'tha vāṇe
vyarthe yāte sameto jvarapatiraśanairajvari tvajjvareṇa |
jñānī stutvātha dattvā tava caritajuṣāṃ vijvaraṃ sa jvaro'gāt
prāyo'ntarjñānavanto'pi ca bahutamasā raudraceṣṭā hi raudrāḥ || 7 ||
cāpānāmiti | bāṇe vyarthe virathe yāte jvarapatirmāheśvarajvaraḥ tvajjvareṇa sameto yudhyan aśanairjhaṭiti ajvari santapto'bhūt | atha māheśvaro jvaraḥ jñānī tvāṃ stutvā tvatprasādāt tava caritajuṣāṃ vijvaraṃ jvarābhavaṃ dattvānugṛhya sa māheśvaro jvaro'gāt | yadvā sajvaraḥ santāpayuktaḥ | yato'ntarhṛdi jñānavanto'pi bahutamasā tamoguṇādhikyena raudraceṣṭāḥ krūrakarmāṇo hi raudrāḥ rudrapārṣadāḥ || 7 ||
bāṇaṃ nānāyudhograṃ punarabhipatitaṃ darpadoṣād vitanvan
nirlūnāśeṣadoṣaṃ sapadi bubudhuṣā śaṅkareṇopagītaḥ |
tadvācā śiṣṭavāhūdvitayamubhayato nirbhayaṃ tatpriyaṃ taṃ
muktvā taddattamāno nijapuramagamaḥ sāniruddhaḥ sahoṣaḥ || 8 ||
bāṇamiti | darpa eka eva doṣaḥ tasmād nirlanāḥ chinnāḥ aśeṣā doṣo bāhavaḥ doṣā garvādayaśca yasya | bubudhuṣā ātmānaṃ viṣṇumapyekamadhijagmuṣā upagītaḥ stutaḥ | tasya śrīśaṅkarasya vācā | ubhayato viṣṇuśaṅkarayorapi nirbhayaṃ tatpriyaṃ śivabhaktam | taddattamānaḥ bāṇena dattapāribarhaḥ | nijapuraṃ dvārakām || 8 ||
muhustāvacchakraṃ varuṇamajayo nandaharaṇe
yamaṃ vālānītau davadahanapāne'nilasakham |
vidhiṃ vatsasteye giriśamiha bāṇasya samare
vibho! viśvotkarṣī tadayamavatāro jayati te || 9 ||
muhuriti | muhuḥ indrayāgapārijātāpaharaṇakhāṇḍavadāhādau śakramajayaḥ | tat tasmāt te tava ayamavatāraḥ viśvebhyo devebhya utkarṣo'syāstīti tathā | ata eva jayati sarvāvatārebhya utkṛṣṭo bhavati || 9 ||
dvijaruṣā kukalāsavapurdharaṃ nṛganṛpaṃ tridivālayamāpayan |
nijajane dvijabhaktimanuttamāmupadiśan pavaneśvara! pāhi mām ||10||
dvijaruṣeti | dvijasya dattāpaharaṇajanitayā ruṣā tatkāryeṇa śāpena nirudakakūpasthaṃ kṛkalāsavapurdharaṃ nṛganṛpaṃ tridivālayaṃ svargam āpayan prāpayan| na vidyateṃ uttamo ya (smādi ? syā i)tyanuttamām | nṛgasya brāhmaṇagavāpaharaṇena kukalāsatvāpattestaddānamāhātmyena svargaprāpteśca nṛgamukhena nivedana purassaramupadiśan || 10 ||
iti uṣāpariṇayavarṇanaṃ bāṇāsurayuddhavarṇanaṃ nṛgasya śāpamokṣavarṇanaṃ ca
vghaśītitamaṃ daśakam |
catvāro'sya brāhavaḥ śiṣṭā iti ubhayata ityasya pūrvatraivānvayo yujyate |
rāme'tha gokulagate mamadāprasakte
hūtānupetayamunādamane madāndhe |
svairaṃ samāramati sevakavādamūḍho
dūtaṃ nyayuṅkta tava pauṇḍakavāsudevaḥ || 1 ||
rāma iti | pramadāsu gopīṣu prasakte lole | hūtām āhūtāṃ tathāpyanupetāmanāgatāṃ yamunāṃ damayatīti tathā | vāruṇīmadirāmadāndhe rāme svairaṃ samāramati sati tvaṃ jagadīśvara iti sevakavādairmūḍhaḥ pauṇḍrakavāsudevaḥ tava dūtaṃ nyayuṅkta niyuktavān || 1 ||
nārāyaṇo'hamavatīrṇa ihāsmi bhūmau
dhatse kila tvamapi māmakalakṣaṇāni |
utsṛjya tāni śaraṇaṃ vraja māmiti tvāṃ
dūto jagāda sakalairhasitaḥ sabhāyām || 2 ||
nārāyaṇa iti | tvamapi māmakalakṣaṇāni śrīvatsakaustubhādīni dhatse kila, tānyutsṛjya māṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajeti sabhāyāṃ jagāda || 2 ||
dūte'tha yātavati yādavasainikastvaṃ
yāto dadarśitha vapuḥ kila pauṇḍrakīyam |
tāpena vakṣasi kṛtāṅkamanalpamūlyaśrīkaustubhaṃ makarakuṇḍalapītacelam || 3 ||
dūta iti | yādavāḥ sainikā yasya sa tvaṃ kāśīṃ yātaḥ pauṇḍrakīyaṃ vapurdadarśitha | tāpena taptalohanyāsena kṛtaḥ śrīvatsāṅko yasya | analpamūlyaḥ analpena mūlyena krītvānīya kaṇṭhe baddhaḥ śrīkaustubho yasya || 3 ||
kālāyasaṃ nijasudarśanamasyato'sya
kālānalotkarakireṇa sudarśanena |
śīrṣaṃ cakartitha mamarditha cāsya senāṃ
tanmitrakāśipaśiro'pi cakartha kāśyām || 4 ||
kālāyasamiti |kālāyasamayaṃ nijasudarśanam asyataḥ prayuñjataḥ asya pauṇḍrakasya | kālānalotkarakireṇa saṃhārakāryaṅgāravarṣiṇā | cakartitha kṛttabān | tanmitrasya kāśipasya śiraḥ kāśyāṃ patitaṃ cakartha akaroḥ || 4 ||
jāḍyena bālakagirāpi kilāhameva
śrīvāsudeva iti rūḍhamatiściraṃ saḥ |
sāyujyameva bhavadaikyadhiyā gato'bhūt
ko nāma kasya sukṛtaṃ kathamityaveyāt || 5 ||
jāḍyeneti | jāḍyena mūḍhatāhetukena parapratyayatvena bālakānāṃ mandadhiyāṃ vācā girā rūḍhā sthirā matiryasya || 5 ||
kāśīśvarasya tanayo'tha sudakṣiṇākhyaḥ
śarvaṃ prapūjya bhavate vihitābhicāraḥ |
kṛtyānalaṃ kamapi bāṇaraṇātibhītairbhūtaiḥ kathañcana vṛtaiḥ samamabhyamuñcat || 6 ||
kāśīśvarasyeti | kathañcana vṛtaiḥ bhūtaiḥ samaṃ saha kṛtyānalam ābhicārakuṇḍotthitaṃ mūrtimantamagnim abhyamuñcad visasarja || 6 ||
tālapramāṇacaraṇāmakhilaṃ dahantīṃ
kṛtyāṃ vilokya cakitaiḥ kathito'pi pauraiḥ |
ghūtotsave kimapi no calito vibho! tvaṃ
pārśvasthamāśu visasarjitha kālacakram || 7 ||
tālapramāṇeti | pauraiḥ kathito vijñāpito'pi ghūtotsave ghatakrīḍākautuke no calitaḥ na vimukho jātaḥ, kintu pārśvasthaṃ cakraṃ hastasaṃjñayā visarjitha ||7||
abhyāpatatyamitadhāmni bhavanmahāstre
hā heti vidrutavatī khalu ghorakṛtyā |
roṣāt mudakṣiṇamadakṣiṇaceṣṭitaṃ taṃ
puploṣa cakramapi kāśipurīmadhākṣīt || 8 ||
abhyāpatatīti | amitam aparicchinnaṃ dhāma tejo yasya, parimitadhāmnāmagnyāditejasāmapi tejaḥpradatvādamitaṃ dhāmaiva vā, 'yadapi ca paramaṃ sarvadhāmnāṃ ca dhāma' iti vacanāt tasmin bhavanmahāstre sudarśanā  khye abhyāpatati kṛtyāmabhidhāvati sati hā hetyākrandantī vidrutavatī | ābhicārasyābrahmaṇye saphalatāyāḥ śarveṇānugṛhītatvāt tadanādareṇa brahmaṇyadeve śrīkṛṣṇe prayogādadakṣiṇaceṣṭitaṃ sudakṣiṇaṃ roṣāt puploṣa | kāśipurīṃ sudakṣiṇarājadhānīmapi adhākṣīd dadāha || 8 ||
sa khalu vivido rakṣoghāte kṛtopakṛtiḥ purā
tava tu kalayā mṛtyuṃ prāptuṃ tadā khalatāṃ gataḥ |
narakasacivo deśakleśaṃ sṛjan nagarāntike
jhaṭiti halinā yudhyannaddhā papāta talāhataḥ || 9||
sa khalviti | rakṣoghāte rāvaṇavadhe kṛtopakṛtiḥ śrīrāmapakṣīyaḥ, tathāpi tava kalayā aṃśabhūtena śrībalabhadreṇa mṛtyuṃ mṛtiṃ prāptuṃ khalatāṃ tava vairitāṃ gataḥ narakasacivaḥ bhaumasya sakhā tvannagarāntike deśānāṃ kleśam upadravaṃ sṛjan utpādayan halinā talena karatalena āhataḥ addhā samyag anāyāsena papāta mṛtaḥ ||9||
sāmbaṃ kauravyaputrīharaṇaniyamitaṃ sāntvanārthī kurūṇāṃ
yātastadvākyaroṣodghṛtakarinagaro mocayāmāsa rāmaḥ |
te ghātyāḥ pāṇḍaveyairiti yadupṛtanāṃ nāmucastvaṃ tadānīṃ
taṃ tvāṃ durbodhalīlaṃ pavanapurapate! tāpaśāntyai niṣeve || 10 ||
sāmbamiti | kauravyaputrīṃ suyodhanasutāṃ lakṣaṇāṃ sāmbaḥ svayaṃvare hṛtavān |
tatra ca kurubhirniyamitaṃ baddhaṃ śrutvā kurubhirvirodho māstviṃti sāntvanārthī yātaḥ teṣāmadhikṣepavākyairjātena roṣeṇoddhṛtaṃ karinagaraṃ hastinapuraṃ yena saḥ rāmaḥ sāmbaṃ mocayāmāsa | tadānīm agrajaparibhave tvaṃ yaduṭatanāṃ nāmucaḥ te pāṇḍaveyairghātyā iti | evaṃ durbodhā līlā yasya || 10 ||
iti pauṇḍrakavadhavarṇanaṃ kāśipurīdāhavarṇanaṃ balabhadrapratāpavarṇanaṃ ca
tryaśītitamaṃ daśakam |
kvacidatha tapanoparāgakāle puri nidadhat kṛtavarmakāmasūnū |
yadukulamahilāvṛtaḥ sutīrthaṃ samupagato'si samantapañcakākhyam || 1 ||
kvaciditi | tapanoparāgakāle sūryagrahaṇakāle puri dvārakāyāṃ kṛtavarmāṇamaniruddhaṃ ca nidadhad rakṣaṇārthe nikṣipya || 1 ||
bahutarajanatāhitāya tatra tvamapi punan vinimajjya tīrthatoye |
dvijagaṇaparimuktavittarāśiḥ samamilathāḥ kurupāṇḍavādimitraiḥ || 2 ||
bahutareti | tatra samantapañcake tīrthatoyaṃ punan tīrthatoyasya pāpaśodhakatvamutpādayan | tadapi bahutarajanatāhitāya tīrthasnāyijanānugrahāya dvijagaṇebhyaḥ parimukto datto vittarāśiryena sa tvaṃ mitrairbandhubhiḥ samamilathāḥ sammilito'bhūḥ ||2||
tava khalu dayitājanaiḥ sametā drupadasutā tvayi gāḍhabhaktibhārā |
taduditabhavadāhṛtitrakārairatimumude samamanvayāminībhiḥ || 3 ||
taveti | drupadasutā tava dayitājanaiḥ rukmiṇyādibhirmilitā taduditaiḥ svaiyitābhiruditaiḥ bhavatkartṛkātmāharaṇaprakāraiḥ saptagovṛṣabandhanādirūpaiḥ anyabhāminībhiḥ subhadrādibhiḥ samamatimumude || 3 ||
tadanu ca bhagavan ! nirīkṣya gopānatikutukādupagamya mānayitvā |
cirataravirahāturāṅgarekhāḥ paśupavadhūḥ sarasaṃ tvamanvayāsīḥ || 4 ||
tadanviti | cirataraviraheṇa āturāḥ aṅgarekhāḥ kṛśaśarīrāṇi yāsāṃ tāḥ | anvayāsīḥ anugataḥ || 4 ||
sapadi ca bhanadīkṣaṇotsavena pramuṣitamānahṛdāṃ nitambinīnām |
atirasaparimuktakañculīke paricayahṛdyatare kuce nyalaiṣīḥ || 5 ||
sapadīti | pramuṣito māno yasmin tādṛśaṃ hṛd yāsām | atirasena atiśayitenānyonyānurāgeṇa parimuktā yathākathañcidapanītā kañculīkā gātrikā yasmāt | paricayahṛdyatare prāganubhūtasya pratyabhijñāyamānatayā kautukaviśeṣajanake kuce nyalaiṣīḥ kṣīranīrākhyaparirambhaṇasukhamanvabhūḥ || 5 ||
ripujanakalahaiḥ punaḥ punarme samupagatairiyatī vilambanābhūt |
iti kṛtaparirambhaṇe tvayi drāgativivaśā khalu rādhikā nililye || 6 ||
ripujanakalahairiti | me mama punaḥ punaḥ samupagatai ripujanakalahaiḥ iyatī atimahatī vilambanā kālakṣepo'bhūditi vacanapurassaraṃ kṛtaparirambhaṇe tvayi drāgativivaśā jhaṭiti tyaktamānā nililye tvadaikyamivānvabhūt || 6 ||
apagatavirahavyathāstadā tā rahasi vidhāya dadātha tattvavodham |
paramasukhacidātmako'hamātmetyudayatu vaḥ sphuṭamevaṃ cetasīti || 7 ||
apagateti | tadā rahasi tāḥ parirambhaṇādibhirapagatavirahavyathā vidhāya ahaṃ paramasukhacidātmakaḥ paramānandabodhasvarūpaḥ sarveṣāmātmāntaryāmī ca ato vo madvirahaḥ śokāvakāśaśca paramārthato nāstyeveti vaścetasi sphuṭamudayatu niścayātmikā buddhirmadanugrahāt syādeveti tattvabodhaṃ dadātha || 7 ||
sukharasaparimiśrito viyogaḥ kimapi purābhavaduddhavopadeśaiḥ |
samabhavadamutaḥ paraṃ tu tāsāṃ paramasukhaikyamayī bhavādvicintā || 8 ||
sukharaseti | tāsāṃ gopastrīṇāṃ purā uddhavopadeśaiḥ udbhavamukhena kṛtairupadeśaiḥ sukharasenālpīyasā parimiśrito viyogo virahaduḥkhaṃ kimapi kiñcidabhavat | amu
to bhavadupadeśāt paraṃ tu bhavadvicintā paramasukhaikyamayī paramānandaikarasarūpā samabhavat || 8 ||
munivaranivahaistavātha pitrā duritaśamāya śubhāni pṛcchyamānaiḥ |
tvayi sati kimidaṃ śubhāntarairityuruhasitairapi yājitastadāsau || 9 ||
munivareti | atha tava pitrā vasudevena duritaśamāya duṣkarmanivṛttaye anuṣṭheyāni śubhakarmāṇi pṛcchayamānairanuyujyamānairmunivaranivahairvyāsanāradādibhistvayi sakalaśubhāspade putre satyapyasya śubhāntaraiḥ karmādibhiḥ kimidaṃ duritaśamanaprārthanamityuruhasitairapi tathāpi tadāsau vasudevo yājitaḥ yajanaṃ kāritaḥ || 9 ||
sumahati yajane vitāyamāne pramuditamitrajane sahaiva gopāḥ |
yadujanamahitāstrimāsamātraṃ bhavadanuṣaṅgarasaṃ pureva bhejuḥ || 10 ||
sumahatīti | sumahati uttamakalpake yajane vitāyamāne kriyamāṇe sati | pramuditā mitrajanā bandhuvargā yasmin tasmin taiḥ sahaiva nandādayo gopā yadujanairmahitāḥ sammānitāḥ adya śva iti trimāsamātraṃ bhavadanuṣaṅgarasaṃ bhavatsaṅgamasukhaṃ purā nandavaja iva bhejuḥ anvabhūvan || 10 ||
vyapagamasamaye sametya rādhāṃ dṛḍhamupagṛhya nirīkṣya vītakhedām |
pramuditahṛdayaḥ puraṃ prayātaḥ pavanapureśvara ! pāhi māṃ gadebhyaḥ || 11 ||
vyapagamasamaya iti | vyapagamasamaye viyogakāle prasthāne rādhāṃ sametya dṛḍhamupagūhya vītakhedāṃ tadanugraharūpopagūhanenaiva tyaktavirahavyathāṃ nirīkṣya pramuditahṛdayaḥ puraṃ dvāravatīṃ prayātaḥ | atastādṛśabhaktavātsalyayuktastvaṃ māṃ gadebhyaḥ pāhi || 11 ||
iti samantapañcakayātrāvarṇanaṃ caturaśītitamaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
tato magadhabhūbhṛtā ciranirodhasaṃkleśitaṃ
śatāṣṭakayutāyutadvitayamīśa! bhūmībhṛtām |
anāthaśaraṇāya te kamapi pūruṣa prāhiṇodayācata sa māgadhakṣapaṇameva kiṃ bhūyasā || 1 ||
tata iti | tataḥ anantaraṃ magadhabhūbhṛtā jarāsandhena giriguhāyāṃ ciranirodhena saṃkleśitaṃ bhūmībhṛtāṃ śatāṣṭakayutāyutadvitayam aṣṭaśatottarāyutadvayaṃ | te tubhyam | saḥ dūtaḥ | kiṃ bhūyasā vijñāpanena | māgadhakṣapaṇaṃ jarāsandhavadhamevāyācata || 1 ||
yiyāsurabhimāgadhaṃ tadanu nāradodīritād
yudhiṣṭhiramakhodyamādubhayakāryaparyākulaḥ |
viruddhajayino'dhvarādubhayasiddhirityuddhave
śaśaṃsuṣi nijaiḥ samaṃ puramiyetha yaudhiṣṭhirīm || 2 ||
yiyāsuriti | abhimāgadhaṃ māgadhamabhimukhīkṛtya yiyāsuḥ jigamiṣuḥ | ubhayeti | māgadhayudhiṣṭhiranigrahānugraharūpobhaya kāryaparyākulaḥ viruddhajayinaḥ śatrunigrahapūrvakaṃ kartavyād adhvarād rājasūyākhyād ubhayasiddhiḥ māgadhanigrahayudhiṣṭhireṣṭasiddhirityuddhave śaśaṃsuṣi uktavati sati yaudhiṣṭhirīṃ puram indraprastham iyetha gatavān || 2 ||
aśeṣadayitāyute tvayi samāgate dharmajo
vijitya sahajairmahīṃ bhavadapāṅgasaṃvardhitaiḥ |
śriyaṃ nirupamāṃ vahannahaha bhaktadāsāyitaṃ
bhavantamayi ! māgadhe prahitavān sabhīmārjunam || 3 ||
aśeṣeti | tvāyi aśeṣadayitāyute ṣoḍaśasahasapatnīyute samāgate sati dharmajaḥ bhavadapāṅgena tvatkaṭākṣeṇa saṃvardhitaiḥ vardhitabalaiḥ mahīṃ kṛtsnāṃ vijitya nirupamāṃ śriyaṃ digvijaye labdhaṃ dhanasañcayaṃ vahan ayi ! bhagavan ! ahaha āścaryametat bhaktadāsāyitaṃ bhaktadāsavadācarantaṃ māgadhe jarāsandhe tajjayārthaṃ prahitavān || 3 ||
girivrajapuraṃ gatāstadanu deva! yūyaṃ trayo
yayāca samarotsavaṃ dvijamiṣeṇa taṃ māgadham |
apūrṇasukṛtaṃ tvamuṃ pavanajena saṃgrāmayan
nirīkṣya saha jiṣṇunā tvamapi rājayudhvā sthitaḥ || 4 ||
girivrajeti | girivrajapuraṃ jarāsandhanagarī gatāḥ samarotsavaṃ draṣṭṭajanakautukajanakaṃ dvandvayuddhaṃ yayāca prārthitavantaḥ | apūrṇasukkṛtamiti | yadyapi mṛtisamaye tvadarśanāvakāśaḥ sukṛtena, tathāpi sadyomuktipradatvatkartṛkavadhasukṛtahīnamityarthaḥ | ataḥ pavanajena saṃgrāmayan yuddhaṃ kārayan rājānau bhīmamāgadhau, athavā pāṇḍavaduryodhanatatpakṣīyān rājño'nyonyaṃ yodhitavāniti rājayudhvā tvam || 4 ||
aśāntasamaroddhataṃ viṭapapāṭanāsaṃjñayā
nipātya jarasaḥ sutaṃ pavanajena niṣpāṭitam |
vimucya nṛpatīn mudā samanugṛhya bhakti parāṃ
dideśitha gataspṛhānapi ca dharmaguptyai bhuvaḥ || 5 ||
aśānteti | aśānte adṛṣṭānyatarajayaparājaye samare uddhatam ahaṅkṛtaṃ jarasaḥ sutaṃ jarānāmnyā piśācyā jātamātra eva dhānyā vyaktaṃ dehaśakaladvayaṃ saṃyojya sañjātacetanatayā putratvena parigṛhītam | evañca dehaśakalayodvaidhībhāva evāsya mṛtiḥ | etad vidvāṃstvaṃ viṭapapāṭanārūpayā saṃjñayā jñāpakena jñātatattvena pavanajena niṣpāṭitaṃ vidāritaśarīraṃ kṛtvā nipātya ghātayitvā tena digjaye gṛhītvā giridaryā niruddhān nṛpatīn vimucya tebhyo mudā parāṃ premalakṣaṇāṃ bhaktiṃ samanugṛhya nijarājyabhoge gataspṛhānapi bhuvo dharmeṇa guptyai dideśitha preṣayāmāsitha | athavā rājñāṃ prajāpālanarūpasya svadharmasya guptyai bhuvaḥ svasvaviṣayān dideśitha dattavān || 5 ||
pracakruṣi yudhiṣṭhire tadanu rājasūyādhvaraṃ
prasannabhṛtakībhavatsakalarājakavyākulam |
tvamapyāyi jagatpate! dvijapadāvanejādikaṃ
cakartha kimu kathyate nṛpavarasya bhāgyonnatiḥ || 6 ||
pracakruṣīti | pracakruṣi prārabdhavati | abhṛtakā bhṛtakā dāsā bhavantīti bhṛtakībhavantaḥ, prasannaiḥ anasūyakaiḥ bhṛtakībhavadbhiḥ sakalai rājakairvyākulam || 6 ||
tataḥ savanakarmaṇi pravaramagryapūjāvidhiṃ
vicārya sahadevavāganugataḥ sa dharmātmajaḥ |
vyadhatta bhavate mudā sadasi viśvabhūtātmane
tadā sasuramānuṣaṃ bhuvanameva tṛptiṃ dadhau || 7 ||
tata iti | bhagavān śrīkṛṣṇo'grapūjāmarhatīti sahadevasya vācamanugataḥ anusṛtaḥ viśvabhūtātmane sarvabhūtānāmātmabhūtāya agryapūjāvidhiṃ vyadhatta | ataḥ sasuramānuṣaṃ carācarātmakaṃ bhuvanameva tṛptiṃ dadhau || 7 ||
tataḥ sapadi cedipo muninṛpeṣu tiṣṭhatsvaho
sabhājayati ko jaḍaḥ paśupadurdurūṭaṃ vaṭum |
iti tvayi sa durvacovitatimudramannāsanādudāpatadudāyudhaḥ samapatannamuṃ pāṇḍavāḥ || 8 ||
tata iti | cedipaḥ śiśupālaḥ | sabhājayati pūjayati | paśupadurdurūṭaṃ paśupāpaśadam | tvayi tvadviṣaye durvacovitatiṃ yathā kaścinmumūrṣurbhuktaṃ sarvamudramati, tathodvaman jalpan udāyudhaḥ uddhṛtāyudhaḥ āsanādudāpatad utthāyābhyayāt | tadā tvatparibhavena pāṇḍavāḥ amuṃ samapatan hantumudyatāḥ || 8 ||
nivārya nijapakṣagānabhimukhasya vidveṣiṇastvameva "jahiṣe śiro danujadāriṇā svāriṇā |
janustritayalabdhayā satatacintayā śuddhadhīstvayā sa paramekatāmadhṛta yogināṃ durlabhām || 9 ||
hakārarephasaṃyoge pākṣikaṃ laghutvaṃ hrasvasya 'prahe vā' iti | nivāryeti | nijapakṣagān pāṇḍavān nivārya tvaṃ danujadāriṇā asuraśarīrabhedanaśīlena svasyāriṇā sudarśanākhyena cakreṇa vidveṣiṇaḥ śiśupālasya śiro jaddiṣe acchinaḥ | janustritaye hiraṇyakaśipurāvaṇaśiśupālarūpe labdhayā || 9 ||
tataḥ sumahite tvayā kratuvare nirūḍhe jano
yayau jayati dharmajo jayati kṛṣṇa ityālapan |
khalaḥ sa tu suyodhano dhutamanāḥ sapatnaśriyā
mayārpitasabhāmukhe sthalajalabhramādabhramīt || 10 ||
tata iti | tataḥ śiśupālavadhānantaraṃ sutarāṃ mahite pūjite | tvayā nirūḍhe parisamāpite śrīkṛṣṇo jayati sarvasmādutkṛṣṭo bhavati, sa īśvaro yasya vaśe sa dharmajo jayatītyālapan jano mahājanaḥ svaṃ svaṃ bhavanaṃ yayau | sa kalyaṃśaḥ suyodhanastu sapatnānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ pāṇḍavānāṃ śriyā samṛdhghā dhutam asūyādibhicalitaṃ mano yasya | khāṇḍavadāhe mayenārpitāyā dattāyāḥ sabhāyā mukhe sthalajalabhramāt sthaladhiyā jale patanād jaladhiyā sthale vastrāntagrahaṇācca abhramīt sambhrānto'bhūt ||10||
tadā hasitamutthitaṃ drupadanandanābhīmayorapāṅgakalayā vibho ! kimapi tāvadujjṛmbhayan |
dharābharanirākṛtau sapadi nāma bījaṃ vapan
janārdana! marutpurīnilaya! pāhi māmāmayāt || 11 ||
tadeti | tadā suyodhanasya jāḍaye sati drupadanandanābhīmayormukha utthitaṃ hasitaṃ apāṅgakalayā kaṭākṣaleśena kimapyujjṛmbhayan sātiśayaṃ kurvan tade va dharābharanirākṛtau bījaṃ vapan kāraṇatvena sthāpayan || 11 || iti jarāsandhavadhavarṇanaṃ yudhiṣṭhirarājasūyavarṇanaṃ ca pañcāśītitamaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
sālvo bhaiṣmīvivāhe yadubalavijitakṣcandracūḍād vimānaṃ
vindan saubhaṃ sa māyī tvayi vasati kurūṃstvatpurīmabhyabhāṅkṣīt |
praghumnastaṃ nirundhannakhilayadubhaṭairnyagrahīdugravīrye
tasyāmātyaṃ ghumantaṃ vyajani ca samaraḥ saptaviṃśatyahāntam || 1 ||
sālva iti | bhaiṣmīvivāhe rukmiṇīsvayaṃvare yadubalena yadusenayā vijitaḥ parājitaḥ yādavocchedapratijñāpūrvakaṃ prasāditāt candracūḍāt saubhākhyaṃ vimānaṃ vindan tvayi kurūn vasati sati tvatpurī dvārakām abhyabhāṅkṣīt babhañja | praghumnastu taṃ sālvaṃ nirundhan ghumantaṃ nyagrahīd jaghāna || 1||
tāvat tvaṃ rāmaśālī tvaritamupagataḥ khaṇḍitaprāya sainyaṃ
saubheśaṃ taṃ nyarundhāḥ sa ca kila gadayā śārṅgamabhraṃśayat te |
māyātātaṃ vyahiṃsīdapi tava puratastat tvayāpi kṣaṇārdhaṃ
nājñāyītyāhureke tadidamavamataṃ vyāsa eva nyaṣedhīt || 2 ||
tāvāditi | saubheśaṃ sālvam | te śārṅga dhanuḥ abhraṃśayat tyājayāmāsa, tava purato māyānirmitaṃ tātaṃ vasudevaṃ vyahiṃsīdapi | tadidaṃ sālvamāyāmohanamavamatamiti vyāsa eva
"kva śokamohau sneho vā bhayaṃ vā ye'jñasambhavāḥ |
kva cākhaṇḍitavijñānajñānaiśvaryeśvaro hariḥ ||"
iti nyaṣedhīt || 2 ||
kṣiptvā saubhaṃ gadācūrṇitamudakanidhau maṅkṣu sālve'pi cakreṇotkṛtte dantavaktraḥ prasabhamabhipatannabhyamuñcad gadāṃ te |
kaumodakyā hato'sāvapi sukṛtanidhiścaidyavat prāpadaivayaṃ
sarveṣāmeṣa pūrvaṃ tvayi dhṛtamanasāṃ mokṣaṇārtho'vatāraḥ || 3 ||
kṣiptveti | te tubhyaṃ tvāṃ hantuṃ gadāmabhyamuñcat | eṣo'vatārastvayi ghṛtamanasāṃ mokṣaṇāya ||3||
tvayyāyāte'tha jāte kila kurusadasi ghūtake saṃyatāyāḥ
ṛndantyā yājñasenyāḥ sakaruṇamakṛthākṣcelamālāmanantām |
annāntamātaśarvāṃśajamunicakitadraupadīcintito'tha
prāptaḥ śākānnamaśnan muni gaṇamakṛthāstṛptimantaṃ vanānte || 4 ||
tvayīti | atha tvayi indraprasthād dvārakāmāyāte sati kurusadasi jāte ghūtake savyāje ghūte saṃyatāyāḥ duśśāsanena gṛhītāyāḥ celamālāṃ vastraparamparām | annānte annāvasāne prāptāt śarvośajād durvāsaso muneścakitayā śāpaśaṅkitayā draupadyā cintitastatra prāptaḥ pātralagnaṃ śākānnam aśvan munigaṇaṃ śiṣyavargasahitaṃ durvāsasaṃ tṛptimantamakṛthāḥ || 4 ||
yuddhodyoge'tha mantre milati sati vṛtaḥ phalgunena tvamekaḥ
kauravye dattasainyaḥ karipuramagamo dūtyakṛt pāṇḍavārtham |
bhīṣmadroṇādimānye tava khalu vacane dhikkṛte kauraveṇa
vyāvṛṇvan viśvarūpaṃ munisadasi purīṃ kṣobhayitvāgato'bhūḥ || 5 ||
yuddhodyoga iti | atha yuddhodyoge milati sati phalgunena tvameko mantre kāryanirūpaṇe vṛtaḥ samakālamāgate kauravye dattasainyaḥ duryodhanāya dattayadusainyaḥ pāṇḍavārtha pāṇḍavānāmardharājyalabdhaye dūtyaṃ dūtakarma karotīti tathā | bhīṣmadroṇādimirmānye pūjanīye kauraveṇa duryodhanena dhikṛte sati munisadasi viśvātmakaṃ svaṃ rūpaṃ vyāvṛṇvan prakāśayan || 5 ||
jiṣṇostvaṃ kṛṣṇa ! sūtaḥ khalu samaramukhe bandhughāte dayāluṃ
khinnaṃ taṃ vīkṣya vīraṃ kimidamayi sakhe ! nitya eko'yamātmā |
ko vadhyaḥ ko'tra hantā tadiha vadhabhiyaṃ projjhya mayyarpitātmā
dharmyaṃ yuddhaṃ careti prakṛtimanayathā darśayan viśvarūpam || 6 ||
jiṣṇoriti | khinnaṃ yuddhāṅka vimukhaṃ tamarjunaṃ vakṣya | nitya eka iti | nityatvādavadhyatvam ekatvād vadhyahantṛbhedābhāvaśca | tat tasmād, yasmādavadhya ātmā tasmād vadhabhiyaṃ bhīṣmadroṇādivadhe yādharmabhīstāṃ projjhya tyakttvā dharmyaṃ kṣatriyadharmādanapetaṃ yuddhaṃ caretyupadiśya viśvāsārthaṃ viśvarūpamaikātmyaṃ darśayan prakṛtiṃ gatasammohatām anayathāḥ prāpayāmāsitha || 6||
bhaktottaṃse'tha bhīṣme tava dharaṇibharakṣepakṛtyaikasakte
nityaṃ nityaṃ vibhindatyavānabhṛdayutaṃ prāptasāde ca pārthe |
niśśastratvapratijñāṃ vijahadarivaraṃ dhārayan krodhaśālīvādhāvan prāñjaliṃ taṃ nataśirasamatho vīkṣya modādapāgāḥ||7||
bhaktottaṃsa iti | pārthe bhīṣmasāyakaiḥ prāptasāde sajjātakleśe sati śastraṃ na gṛhṇāmīti pratijñāṃ vijahat tyajan krodhaśālīva arivaraṃ sudarśanam | śastraṃ grāhayāmīti tatpratijñāṃ satyaṃ kartumeva, na tava krodhaśālitvāt || 7 ||
bhagadattaprayuktanārāyaṇāstrād', jayadrathavadhapratijñāyāḥ, karṇanāgāstrāccārjunaṃ rakṣitavānityāha -- 
yuddhe droṇasya hastisthiraraṇabhagadatteritaṃ vaiṣṇavāstraṃ
vakṣasyādhatta caṛsthagitaravimahāḥ prārdayan sindhurājam |
nāgāstre karṇamukte kṣitimavanamayan kevalaṃ kṛttamauliṃ
tatre tatrāpi pārthaṃ kimiva na hi bhavān pāṇḍavānāmakārṣīt || 8||
yuddha iti | cakreṇa sthagitaṃ tiraskṛtaṃ ravimaho yena | sindhurājaṃ jayadratham | kṛttamauliṃ khaṇḍitasakeśakirīṭam | tatre rarakṣa | pāṇḍavānāṃ kiṃmivopakāraṃ bhavān nākārṣīt || 8 ||
yuddhādau tīrthagāmī sa khalu haladharo naimiśakṣetramṛcchannapratyutthāyisūtakṣayakṛdaya sutaṃ tatpade kalpayitvā |
yajñaghnaṃ balvalaṃ parvaṇi paridalayan snātatīrtho raṇānte
samprāpto bhīmaduryodhanaraṇamaśamaṃ vīkṣya yātaḥ purīṃ te || 9 ||
yuddhādāviti | ṛcchan gacchan apratyutthāyinaḥ sūtasya romaharṣaṇasya kṣayakṛd vadhaṃ kurvan atha munivacanāt sutaṃ tatputraṃ tatpade munidatte brahmāsane | parvaṇi parvaṇi yajñaghnaṃ balvalaṃ daityaṃ paridalayana nighnan || 9 ||
sasuptadraupadeyakṣapaṇahatadhiyaṃ drauṇimetya tvaduktyā
tanmuktaṃ brāhmamastraṃ samadṛta vijayo mauliratnaṃ ca jahe |
ucchintyai pāṇḍavānāṃ punarapi ca viśatyuttarāgarbhamastre
rakṣannaṅguṣṭhamātraḥ kila jaṭharamagāścakrapāṇirvibho ! tvam || 10 ||
saṃsupteti | saṃsuptapañcadraupadeyakṣapaṇena  hatadhiyam aviditetikartavyatākaṃ drauṇim aśvatthāmānametya tena aśvatthāmnā muktaṃ brāhmamastraṃ tvaduktyā vijayaḥ samahṛta saṃhṛtavān, tasya mauliratnaṃ ca jahe | pāṇḍavānāmucchittyai mūlacchedāya punastatprayukte astre brahmāstre uttarāgarbhaṃ viśati sati aṅguṣṭhamātravapuścakrapāṇiḥ san tvamuttarāyā jaṭharamagāḥ kila || 10 ||
atha dharmajabhīṣmayormuktimuktī pradāya kṛtakṛtyo bhagavānityāha -- 
dharmoghaṃ dharmasūnorabhidadhadakhilaṃ chandamṛtyuḥ sa bhīṣmastvāṃ paśyan bhaktibhūmnaiva hi sapadi yayau niṣkalabrahmabhūyam |
saṃyājyāthāśvamedhaistribhiratimahitairdharmajaṃ pūrṇakāmaṃ
sammāpto dvārakāṃ tvaṃ pavanapurapate ! pāhi māṃ sarvarogāt || 11 ||
dharmaughamiti | saḥ bhūbhāraharaṇe paramasuhṛd bhīṣmaḥ tvāṃ paśyan tvadbhakterbhūmnaivānapāyinyā bhaktyā niṣkalabrahmabhūyaṃ niṣkalabrahmatvaṃ mokṣaṃ sapadi yayau | athāśvamedhaiḥ saṃyājya yājayitvā || 11 ||
iti sālvādivadhavarṇanaṃ bhāratayuddhopakramavarṇanaṃ bhāratayuddhavarṇanaṃ ca
ṣaḍaśītitamaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
kucelanāmā bhavataḥ satīrthyatāṃ gataḥ sa sāndīpanimandire dvijaḥ |
tvadekarāgeṇa dhanādiniḥspṛho dināni ninye praśamī gṛhāśramī || 1 ||
'kucelanāmeti | satīrthyatāṃ sabrahmacāritām | tvadekarāgeṇa tvayi premalakṣaṇayā bhaktyā | praśamī jitendriyaḥ || 1 ||
samānaśīlāpi tadīyavallabhā tathaiva no cittajayaṃ sameyuṣī |
kadācidūce vata vṛttilabdhaye ramāpatiḥ kiṃ na sakhā niṣevyate || 2 ||
samānaśīleti | yadyapi samānaśīlā, tathāpi tathaiva kucelavaccittajayaṃ dhanādiniḥspṛhatvaṃ no sameyuṣī prāptavatī | vṛtterjīvanopāyasya labdhaye ramāpatiḥ | sakheti niṣevaucityam ||2||
itīrito'yaṃ priyayā kṣudhārtayā jugupsamāno'pi dhane madāvahe |
tadā tvadālokanakautukād yayau vahan paṭānte pṛthukānupāyanam || 3 ||
itīrita iti | paṭānte upāyanam upahāraṃ vahan dvārakāṃ yayau || 3 ||
gato'yamāścaryamayīṃ bhavatpurīṃ gṛheṣu śaibyābhavanaṃ sameyivān |
praviśya vaikuṇṭhamivāpa nirvṛtiṃ tavātisambhāvanayā tu kiṃ punaḥ || 4 ||
gata iti | āścaryamayīm analpaśilpāṃ bhavatpurīṃ dvārakāṃ gato'yaṃ kucelaḥ tatra gṛheṣu vghaṣṭasahasreṣu madhye śaivyāyā bhavanaṃ sameyivān prāptaḥ | tavātisambhāvanayā atisammānena nirvṛtiṃ sukhamavāpeti kiṃ punarucyate ||4||
prapūjitaṃ taṃ priyayā ca vījitaṃ kare gṛhītvākathayaḥ purā kṛtam |
yadindhanārthaṃ gurudāracoditairapartuvarṣe tadamarṣi kānane || 5||
prapūjitamiti| apartau varṣarttuvyatiriktakāle tat sumahad varṣamamarṣi soḍhamiti yat, tat purā kṛtaṃ gurukule kṛtaṃ karmākathayaḥ || 5||
trapājuṣo'smāt pṛthukaṃ balādatha pragṛhya muṣṭau sakṛdāśite tvayā |
kṛtaṃ kṛtaṃ nanviyateti sambhramādaramā kilopetya karaṃ rurodha te || 6 ||
trapājupa iti | trapājuṣaḥ śrīpataye pṛthukaprasṛtipradānalajjayāvāṅmukhād balātkāreṇa pṛthukaṃ pragṛhya tvayā muṣṭau sakṛdāśite sati ramā śrīḥ sambhramādupetyeyatā prasādena kṛtaṃ kṛtam alamalaṃ nanu ito'dhikatvatprasādocita sampatsampādane'hamasamartheti te karaṃ rurodha kila || 6 || bhakteṣu bhaktena sa mānitastvayā purīṃ vasannekaniśāṃ mahāsukham | batāpareviṇaṃ vinā yayau vicitrarūpastava khalvanugrahaḥ || 7 ||
bhakteṣviti | bhakteṣu uddhavādiṣu bhaktena prītiyuktena tvayā saḥ kucelaḥ mānitaḥ pūjitaḥ, bateti khede, aparedhuḥ śvobhūte draviṇaṃ dhanaṃ vinā yayau | vicitrarūpaḥ samakṣāsamakṣādibhedana bahuprakāraḥ || 7 || yadi hyayāciṣyamadāsyadacyuto vadāmi bhāryo kimiti vrajannasau | tvaduktilīlāsmitamanadhīḥ punaḥ kramādapaśyanmaṇidīpramālayam || 8 ||
yadīti | yadyahamayāciṣyaṃ, tarhyacyuto'dāsyat | nāhaṃ yācitavān acyutaśca na dattavānityarthaḥ | ato bhārthī kiṃ vadāmīti cintayanneva vrajan tavoktau līlāyāṃ smite ca manā dhīryasya | maṇibhī ratnaiḥ dīpaṃ dīpanaśīlam ālayaṃ nijabhavanaṃ kramādapaśyat || 8 ||
kiṃ mārgavibhraṃśa iti bhraman kṣaṇaṃ gṛhaṃ praviṣṭaḥ sa dadarśa bahubhām |
sakhīparītāṃ maṇihemabhūṣitāṃ buvodha ca tvatkaruṇāṃ mahādbhutām || 9 ||
kimiti | kiṃ mārgavibhraṃśaḥ punarapi dvārakāmāgatavānahamiti kṣaṇakālaṃ bhraman gṛhaṃ praviṣṭaḥ vallabhāṃ dṛṣṭvā mahādbhutāṃ tvatkaruṇāmātmasampadaṃ taddhetukāṃ bubodha ca ||9||
sa ratnaśālāsu vasannapi svayaṃ sasutmannadbhaktibharo'mṛtaṃ yayau |
tvamevamāpūritabhaktavāñchito mahatpurādhīśa ! harasva me gān || 10 ||
sa iti | saḥ svayaṃ ratnaśālāsu kāmoddīpanavibhā veṣu vasannapi samunnamadbhaktibharaḥ kramāt pravṛddhabhaktyatiśayaḥ amṛtaṃ mokṣaṃ yayau || 10 ||
iti kucelopākhyānaṃ saptāśītitamaṃ daśakam |
prāgevācāryaputrātiniśamanayā svīyaṣaṭmūnuvīkṣāṃ
kāṅkṣakṣantyā māturuktyā sutalabhuvi valiṃ prāpya tenārcitastvam |
dhātuḥ śāpāddhiraṇyānvitakaśiṣubhavān śaurijān kaṃsabhagnānānīyainān pradarśya svapadamanayathāḥ pūrvaputrān marīceḥ || 1 ||
prāgeveti | mṛtaṃ putramānīyācāryadakṣiṇāṃ kṛtavāniti niśamanayā śravaṇena kaṃsahatasvīyaṣaṭsūnuvīkṣāṃ prāgeva aticiraṃ kāṅkṣantyā māturdevakyā uktyā mahābaliṃ prāpya pūrve marīceḥ putrān smarodgīthapariṣvaṅgapataṅgakṣudrabhugvṛṇyākhyān punaśca dhāturbrahmaṇaḥ śāpāḍ hiraṇyakaśipubhavān śaurijān vasudevaputrān mātre pradarśya svapadaṃ svargam anayathāḥ prāpayāmāsitha || 1 ||
śrutadeva iti śrutaṃ dvijendraṃ bahulāśvaṃ nṛpatiṃ ca bhaktipūrṇam |
yugapat tvamanugrahītukāmo mithilāṃ prāpitha tāpasaiḥ sametaḥ || 2 ||
śrutadeva iti| śrutaṃ viśrutam ||2||
gacchan dvimūrtirubhayoryugapanniketamekena bhūrivibhavairvihitopacāraḥ |
anyena tadinabhṛtaiśca phalaudanādyaistulyaṃ praseditha dadātha ca muktimābhyām || 3 ||
gacchanniti | dvimūrtiḥ aṅgīkṛtaśarīradvayaḥ | ekena bahulāśvena bhūribhirvibhavaiḥ upakaraṇaiḥ vihitaḥ kṛtaḥ upacāraḥ pūjā yasya || 3 ||
atha dvijasya mṛtaputranayanenārjunasya garvānayanapūrvakamātmani tattvabuddhyāpādanaprakāramāha -- 
bhūyo'tha dvāravayāṃ dvāravatyāṃ dvijatanayamṛtiṃ tatmalāpānapi tvaṃ
ko vā daivaṃ nirandhyāditi kala kathayan viśvavoḍhāpyasoḍhāḥ |
jiṣṇorgarvaṃ vinetuṃ tvayi manujadhiyā kuṇṭhitāṃ cāsya buddhiṃ
tattvārūḍhāṃ vidhātuṃ paramatamapadapreṇeneti manye || 4||
bhūya iti | daivaṃ svakarmaphalaṃ ko nirundhyād anyathākartuṃ kaḥ samartha iti kathayan tvaṃ tasya dvijasya pralāpānapi viśvavoḍhā jagadīśvaro'pi asoḍhāḥ marṣitavān | paramatamasya padasya vaikuṇṭhapadasya prekṣaṇena asya jiṣṇoḥ garvaṃ vinetum apanetum | manujadhiyā tvayi kuṇṭhitāṃ niścayarahitāṃ tattvārūḍhāṃ vidhātum idamiti manye || 4 ||
naṣṭā aṣṭasya putrāḥ punarapi taba tūpekṣayā kaṣṭavādaḥ
spaṣṭo jāto janānāmatha tadavasare dvārakāmāra pārthaḥ |
maitryā tatroṣito'sau navamasutamṛtau vipravaryaprarodaṃ
śrutvā cakre pratijñāmanupahṛtasutaḥ sanmivekṣye kṛśānum || 5 ||
naṣṭā iti | asya dvijavarasya aṣṭa putrā naṣṭāḥ | tadā tu tavopekṣayā dvijasutarakṣaṇaudāsīnyād janānāṃ kaṣṭavādo lokāpavādo jātaḥ | tadavasare pārtho dvārakām āra prāpa | maitryā sakhyena tatroṣitaḥ uvāsa | anupahṛtasutaḥ adattajaniṣyamāṇakumāraśced ahaṃ kṛśānum agniṃ sannivekṣye praviśāmi || 5 ||
mānī sa tvāmāpṛṣṭvā dvijanilayagato vāṇajālairmahāstrairundhānaḥ sūtigehaṃ punarapi sahasā dṛṣṭanaṣṭe kumāre |
yāmyāmaindrī tathānyāḥ suravaranagarīrvidyayāsādya sadyo
moghodyogaḥ patiṣyan hutabhuji bhavatā sasmitaṃ vārito'bhūt || 6 ||
mānīti | sa pārthaḥ mānī āśritarakṣaṇe'dvitīyo'hamityabhimānī | ata eva tvāṃ paramavandhumapyapṛṣṭvā mahāstraiḥ āgneyādibhiḥ sūtigehaṃ rundhānaḥ kṛtaśarapañjaraḥ dṛṣṭanaṣṭe jātamātra evaṃ saśarīramadarśanaṃ gate yāmyāṃ yamanagarīm aindrīm indrasya nagarīm anyāḥ varuṇakuberādisuravaranagarīḥ vidyayā yogaiśvaryeṇa āsādya prāpya hutabhuji agnau patipyan patinumārabdho bhavatā sasmitam | pauruṣasya daivasāpekṣatvam idānīṃ kimavasitamityarjunasya bhāvāvedanaṃ tava smitahetuḥ || 6 ||
sārdhaṃ tena pratīcī diśamatijavinā syandanenābhiyāto
lokālokaṃ vyatītastimirabharamatho cakradhāmnā nirundhan |
cakrāṃśukliṣṭadṛṣṭiṃ sthitamatha vijayaṃ paśya paśyeti vārāṃ
pāre tvaṃ prādadarśaḥ kimapi hi tapasāṃ dūradūraṃ padaṃ te ||7||
sārdhamiti| atijavinā vegātiśayavatā | vārāṃ pāre kāraṇajalamadhyavartini kimapi anirdeśyaṃ tamasāṃ dūradūram atyantadūrasthitam || 7||
tatrāsīnaṃ bhujaṅgādhipaśayanatale divyabhūṣāyudhādyairāvītaṃ pītacelaṃ pratinavajaladaśyāmalaṃ śrīmadaṅgam |
mūrtīnāmīśitāraṃ paramiha tisṛṇāmekamarthe śrutīnāṃ
tyāmeva tvaṃ parātman ! priyasakhasahito nemitha kṣemarūpam || 8 ||
tatreti | tisṛṇāṃ mūrtīnāmīśitāra niyantāram ata eva paraṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ śrutīnāmekamarthe pradhānapratipādyaṃ kṣemarūpaṃ puruṣārthabhūtaṃ tvāmeva tvaṃ nemitha namaścakṛṣe || 8 ||
yuvāṃ gāmeva dvāvadhikavivṛtarhitatayā
vibhinnau sandraṣṭuṃ svayamahamahārṣe dvijasutān |
nayetaṃ drāgenāniti khalu vitīrṇān punarabhūn
dvijāyādāyādāḥ praṇutamahimā pāṇḍujanuṣā ||9||
yuvāmiti | yuvāṃ kṛṣṇārjunau māmeva madaṃśabhūtāveva dvau adhikavivṛtāntarhitatayā vibhinnau| ekaḥ śuddhasattvopādhikatayādhikavivṛtaḥ prakaṭitanijaiśvaryaḥ | anya upādhyantarhitanijaiśvaryaḥ | ato jīdheśvararūpeṇa vibhinnau dvidhābhūtau samdraṣṭuṃ samīpe draṣṭumahaṃ svayaṃ dvijasutānahārṣam ānītavān | enān drāg jhaṭiti nayetaṃ dvijavaranikaṭaṃ prāpayetam iti vitīrṇān dattān amūn dvijasutān ādāya brāhmaṇāyādāḥ dattavān | pāṇḍujanuṣā pārthena praṇutaḥ stuto mahimā yasya || 9 ||
evaṃ nānāvihārairjagadabhiramayan dṛṣṇivaṃśaṃ prapuṣṇannījāno yajñabhaidairatulavihṛtibhiḥ prīṇayanneṇanetrāḥ |
bhūbhārakṣepadambhāt padakamalajuṣāṃ mokṣaṇāyāvatīrṇaḥ
pūrṇa brahmaiva sākṣād yaduṣu manujatārūṣitastvaṃ vyalāsīḥ || 10 ||
evamiti| bhūbhārakṣepamiṣeṇa manujatayā rūṣito bahirliptaḥ || 10 ||
prāyeṇa dvāravatyāmavṛtadayi tadā nāradastvadrasārdrastasmāllebhe kadācit khalu sukṛtanidhistvatpitā tattvabodham |
bhaktānāmagrayāyī sa ca khalu matimānuddhavastvatta eva
prāpto vijñānasāraṃ sa kila janahitāyādhunāste badaryām || 11 ||
prāyeṇeti | śrīnāradaḥ prāyeṇa dakṣaśāpācciramekatra sthātumaśakyatayā gatāgatapūrvakaṃ dvāravatyāmavṛtad vartate sma | tadā tasmānnāradāt | janahitāya bhaktānāṃ jñānopadeśāya || 11 ||
so'yaṃ kṛṣṇāvatāro jayati tava vibho ! yatra sauhārdabhītisnehadveṣānurāgaprabhṛtibhiratulairaśramairyogabhedaiḥ |
ārti tīrtvā samastāmṛtapadamaguḥ sarvataḥ sarvalokāḥ
sa tvaṃ viśvārtiśāntyai pavanapurapate ! bhaktipūrtyai ca bhūyāḥ || 12 ||
so'yamiti | yatra śrīkṛṣṇe sauhārdādibhiḥ yogabhedaiḥ śrīkṛṣṇe manoniveśanopāyaviśeṣaiḥ| tatra sauhārdāt pāṇḍavādayaḥ, bhītyā kaṃsaḥ, snehād yadavaḥ, dveṣāt śiśupālādayaḥ, anurāgād gopyaḥ | sarvataḥ sarvatra | sarve lokāḥ samastāmādhyātmikādirūpām ārti pīḍāṃ tīrtvā atikramya amṛtapadaṃ mokṣam aguḥ jagmuḥ | viśvārtiśāntyai sakalapīḍāpahatyai bhakteḥ prārabdhāyāḥ pūrtyai pravṛddhaye ca bhūyāḥ || 12 ||
ityarjuna gavapinayanayavarṇatam aṣṭāśītitamaṃ daśakaṃ sadikam |
ramājāne ! jāne yadiha tava bhakteṣu vibhavo
na sampaghaḥ sadyastadiha madakṛttvādaśaminām |
praśānti kṛtvaiva pradiśasi tataḥ kāmamakhilaṃ
praśānteṣu kṣipraṃ na khalu bhavadīye cyutikathā ||1||
ramājāna iti | he ramājāne ! lakṣmīpate ! tava bhakteṣu vibhavaḥ phalaṃ na sadyaḥ kiñcid bhajanamātra eva sampadyaḥ pariṇato na bhavati yat, tad madakṛttvād vibhavasya madajanakatvāditi jāne | ataḥ aśaminām aśāntānāṃ praśānti kṛtvaiva kāmam abhīṣṭamakhilaṃ pradiśasi | prāgeva praśānteṣu tu kṣipraṃ pradiśasi | atastvadīye tvadbhakte na khalu cyutikathā bhraṃśasya kathā śabdo'pi nāstyeva || 1 ||
vidhiśaṅkarādayastu na tathetyāha
sadyaḥprasādaruṣitān vidhiśaṅkarādīna
kecid vibho ! nijaguṇānuguṇaṃ bhajantaḥ |
sampadyata iti sampadyaḥ| 'kṛbhvastiyoge sampayakartari' ityatra kartṛśapratyayāntatayā nipātanāt sādhutā |
bhraṣṭā bhavanti bata kaṣṭamadīrghadṛṣṭyā
spaṣṭaṃ ṭakāsura udāharaṇaṃ kilāsmin || 2 ||
sadya iti | sadyaḥ alpābhyāṃ guṇadoṣābhyāṃ, sadyaḥ prasādāḥ sadyoruṣitāśca vidhiśaṅkarādayaḥ | kecid nijaguṇānuguṇaṃ svasvavāsanānusāreṇa tān bhajantaḥ anantaramaśāntatayā tadroṣeṇaiva bhraṣṭā bhavanti | kaṣṭaṃ bateti khede | adīrghadṛṣṭyā aticiraviṣṇubhajanamāndyādevānyabhajanamiti bhāvaḥ | taduktaṃ -- 
"rāgī sa puruṣo'vaśyaṃ phalaṃ prepsati śaṅkarāt |
tataḥ phalaṃ sa labhate tatastaṃ vismariṣyati ||
iti | asminnarthe vṛkāsuraḥ spaṣṭam anurūpam udāharaṇam || 2 ||
śakunijaḥ sa hi nāradamekadā tvaritatoṣamapṛcchadadhīśvaram |
saca dideśa girīśamupāsituṃ na tu bhavantayayandhumasādhuṣu || 3 ||
śakunija iti | sa hi vṛkāsuraḥ tvaritatoṣaṃ sadyaḥprasādamadhīśvaramapṛcchat ||
tapastaptvā ghoraṃ sa khalu kupitaḥ saptamadine
śiraśchittvā sadyaḥ puraharasupasthāpya purataḥ |
atikṣudraṃ raudraṃ śirasi karadānena nidhanaṃ
jagannāthād vatre bhavati vimukhānāṃ ka śubhadhīḥ || 4 ||
tapa iti| upasthāpya pratyakṣīkṛtya | atikṣudram asāram | raudraṃ krūram | bhavati viṣṇau || 4 ||
moktāraṃ vandhamukto hariṇapatiriva prādravat so'tha rudraṃ
daityād bhītyā sma devo diśi diśi valate pṛṣṭhato dattadṛṣṭiḥ |
tūṣṇīke sarvaloke tava padamadhirokṣyantamudrīkṣya śarve
dūrādevāgratastvaṃ paṭuvaṭuvapuṣā tasthiṣe dānavāya || 5 ||
moktāramiti | sa daityaḥ rudrameva prādravat | bandhamukto hariṇapatirmṛgendraḥ moktāramiva | tava padaṃ vaikuṇṭham | tasthiṣe dānavāya vṛkāsurāyātmānaṃ prakāśya sthitavān ||5||
bhadraṃ te śākuneya ! bhramasi kimadhunā tvaṃ piśācasya vācā
sandehakṣcenmaduktau tava kimu na karoṣyagulīmaṅga ! maulau |
itthaṃ tvadvākyamūḍhaḥ śirasi kṛtakaraḥ so'patacchinnapātaṃ
bhraṃśo hyevaṃ paropāsiturapi ca gatiḥ śūlino'pi tvameva || 6 ||
bhadramiti | chinnapātamapatat chinnamūlataruvat papāta | paropāsiturapyevaṃ tvatkṛto bhraṃśo bhavati | śūlino'pi gatiḥ śaraṇaṃ ca tvaṃ viṣṇureva bhavati ||6||
bhṛguṃ kila sarasvatīnikaṭavāsinastāpasāstrimūrtiṣu samādiśannadhikasattvatāṃ veditum |
ayaṃ punaranādarāduditaruddharoṣe vidhau
hare'pi ca jihiṃsiṣau girijayā dhṛte tvāmagāt || 7 ||
bhṛgumiti| adhikasattvatāṃ mahattvam | ayaṃ bhṛguḥ brahmaputraḥ akṛtapraṇāmastadanādarāduditaḥ punaśca ruddho roṣo yena tathāvidhe sati vidhau hare rudre'pi ca bhrātṛsnehena parirambhaṇārambhe tadanādarajanitaruṣā jihiṃsiṣau hantumicchati sati girijayā dhṛte vārite ca sati vaikuṇṭhamagāt || 7 ||
saptaṃ ramāṅkabhuvi paṅkajalocanaṃ tvāṃ
vipre vinighnati padena mudotthitastvam |
sarvaṃ kṣamasva munivarya ! bhavet sadā me
tvatpādacihnamiha bhūṣaṇamityavādīḥ ||8||
suptamiti | tvatpādacihnaṃ śrīvatsākhyam || 8||
niścitya te ca sudṛḍhaṃ tvayi baddhabhāvāḥ
sārasvatā munivarā dadhire vimokṣam |
tvāmevamacyuta ! punaścyutidoṣahīnaṃ
sattvoccayaikatanumeva vayaṃ bhajāmaḥ || 9 ||
niścityeti | baddho bhāvo bhaktiryeṣām | sārasvatāḥ sarasvatītīravāsinaḥ || 9||
jagatsṛṣṭyādau tvāṃ nigamanivahairvandibhiriva
stutaṃ viṣṇo ! saccitparamarasanirdvaitavapuṣam |
parātmānaṃ bhūman ! paśupavanitābhāgyanivahaṃ
parītāpaśrāntyai pavanapuravāsin ! paribhaje || 10 ||
jagaditi | nigamanivahaiḥ mūrtimatībhirupaniṣadbhiḥ jagatsṛṣṭyādau saguṇatvena stutam | sañciditi | saccidānandādvayaparamātmarūpeṇa ca stutamiti bhāvaḥ | paśupavanitānāṃ bhāgyaṃ tatphalaṃ nitarāṃ vahati prāpayatīti tathā | yadvā bhāgyānāṃ nivahaṃ śrīkṛ ṣṇarūpeṇa pariṇatamiti | evañca trimūrtiṣu viṣṇoreva mahattvamityasminnarthe sārasvatā munivarā nigamanivahāśca pramāṇamityapyuktaṃ veditavyam || 10 ||
iti vṛkāsuravadhavarṇanaṃ mūrtitritaye bhagavataḥ śreṣṭhacavarṇanaṃ ca
ekonanavatitamaṃ daśakam |
nanu trimūrtiṣu mahattvaṃ śrīśaṅkarasyaiveti skāndādau bahuśaḥ śrūyata ityāśaṅkaya pariharati -- 
vṛkabhṛgumunimohinyambarīṣādivṛtteṣvayi tava hi mahattvaṃ sarvaśarvādijaitram |
sthitamiha paramātman ! niṣkalārvāgabhinnaṃ
kimapi yadavabhātaṃ taddhi rūpaṃ tavaiva || 1 ||
vṛketi | mohinī viṣṇumāyā | tava viṣṇormahattvaṃ sarveṣāṃ śarvādīnāṃ devānāṃ jaitraṃ nyūnatāpādakam | tatra vṛkādicaturṇāṃ cariteṣu śivasya jaitraṃ tvanmahattvam, indrayāganandaharaṇabālānayana -- analapānavatsasteyādiṣu indravaruṇayamānalabrahmādijaitraṃ tvanmahattvamiti sthitam atraiva dṛḍhīkṛtam | atra hetuḥ -- paramātmanniti | nirupādhikaparamātmatvamevopapādayati -- niṣkaleti | niṣkale paramātmani tato'rvāk sakaleṣu brahmaviṣṇugirīśādiṣu ca yat kimapyabhinnaṃ rūpaṃ paramātmatattvam avabhātaṃ, tadeva taba viṣṇo rūpaṃ tadrūpaṃ ca tavaiva, na teṣām | hi yasmādevaṃ viṣṇoravasthābhedā giriśādayaḥ, tasmāt trimūrtiṣu viṣṇoreva mahattvamuktamityarthaḥ | taduktaṃ śrībhāgavate -- 
" sattvaṃ rajastama iti prakṛterguṇāstairyuktaḥ paraḥ puruṣa eka ihāsya dhatte |
sthityādaye hariviriñcaharerota saṃjñāḥ śreyāṃsi tatra khalu sattvatanornṛṇāṃ syuḥ||"
iti || 1 || 
nanu
"evameko mahādevo māyayā guṇarūpayā |
nāmarūpakriyābhedairbhinnavat pratibhāsate || "
iti vacanāt śivasyaivāvasthābhedo mūrtitrayaprādhānyaṃ cetyāśaṅkaya saṃjñābheda evāyaṃ,
na saṃjñini vastusvarūpa iti pariharati -- 
mūrtitrayeśvarasadāśivapañcakaṃ yat
prāhuḥ parātmavapureva sadāśivo'smin |
tatreśvarastu sa vikuṇṭhapadastvameva
tritvaṃ punarbhajasi satyapade tribhāge || 2 ||
mūrtitrayeti| brahmaviṣṇugiriśeśvarasadāśivākhyamūrtibhedena pañcātmakaḥ śiva iti yat prāhuḥ śaivāḥ, tatra śaivānāṃ pakṣe yaḥ sadāśiva  śabdavācyaḥ, so'smin
pakṣe " viṣṇuṃ pañcātmakaṃ vande " ityukte śrībhāgavatapakṣe parātmavapuḥ paramātmasvarūpastvaṃ viṣṇurevetyarthaḥ | evaṃ vikuṇṭhapaḍho vaikuṇṭhavāsī tvameveśvaraśabdavācyaḥ | trayo bhāgā brahmaviṣṇuśivalokā yasmiṃstat tribhāgaṃ tasmin satyapade satyaloke trayo brahmaviṣṇugiriśāḥ teṣāṃ bhāvastritvaṃ, satyalokasthabrahmaviṣṇuśivalokeṣu trimūrtitvamapi tvameva bhajasītyarthaḥ | yathā harivaṃśe -- 
" ahaṃ tvaṃ sarvago deva! tvamevāhaṃ janārdana ! |
āvayorantara nāsti śabdairarthairjagatpate ! ||"
iti || 2 ||
nanu yadi viṣṇuśivayorabhedaḥ, tarhi kathaṃ viṣṇoḥ prādhānyamucyate | satyam | yadyapi tattvadṛṣṭyā bhedo nāsti, tathāpi gauṇo bhedaḥ utkarṣo nikarṣaścānayorbhavatyevetyāha -- 
tatrāpi sāttvikatanuṃ tava viṣṇumāhurdhātā tu satcaviralo rajasaiva pūrṇaḥ |
sattvotkaṭatvamapi cāsti tamovikāraceṣṭādikaṃ ca tava śaṅkaranāmni mūrtau || 3 ||
tatrāpīti | tatra trimūrtiṣu tava sāttvikī śuddhasattvamayīṃ tanuṃ viṣṇuṃ kevalaviṣṇuśabdavācyāmāhurityarthaḥ| taduktaṃ -- 
"rājaso bhagavān brahmā sāttviko viṣṇurucyate |
īṣattamoguṇo rudraḥ sṛjatyavati hantyajaḥ ||"
iti || 3 ||
nanu "tatreśvarastu sa vikuṇṭhapadastvameve"tyuktam | tadayuktam,
"ambikāpatirīśāna upāsyo guṇamūrtibhiḥ |
īśvaraḥ paramātmaiko māyayā sa tridhā sthitaḥ ||
iti vacanāditi cedū, netyāha -- 
taṃ ca trimūrtyatigataṃ parapūruṣaṃ tvāṃ
śarvātmanāpi khalu sarvamayatvahetoḥ |
śaṃsantyupāsanavidhau tadapi svatastu
tvadrūpamityatidṛḍhaṃ bahu naḥ pramāṇam || 4 ||
taṃ ceti | trimūrtyatigataṃ mūrtitrayāt paraṃ taṃ śaivānāmīśvaraśabdavācyaṃ paraṃ brahmāṇḍād bahiṣṭhaṃ puruṣam ekāṃśena puraṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ praviśya ca sthitam antargatānekabrahmāṇḍaṃ tvāṃ khalūpāsanavidhau śaivān prati śarvātmanā śivasvarūpeṇāpi śaṃsanti paurāṇikāḥ| sarvamayatvahetoriti | tava trimūrtyadhiṣṭhānatvāt tadapi nātyantamayuktamityarthaḥ | tadapi tathāpi svataḥ paramārthatastvadrūpameva tad, na brahmaśivayorityatra bahu naḥ pramāṇamiti | śrutipurāṇavacanapratyakṣādipramāṇāni bahūni santītyarthaḥ | śrutistāvat puruṣasūktādau | 'kkehagvidhāvigaṇite tyādīni purāṇabacanāni | evaṃrūpasyārjunādibhirhaṣṭatvāt pratyakṣamapi pramāṇamityalam || 4 ||
śrīśaṅkaro'pi  bhagavān sakaleṣu tāvat
tvāmeva mānayāti yo na hi pakṣapātī |
tvanniṣṭhameva sa hi nāmasahasrakādi
vyākhyad bhavatstutiparaśca gatiṃ gato'nte ||5||
śrīśaṅkara iti | kiñca śrīśaṅkaraḥ bhagava tpādācāryaḥ | tvānniṣṭhaṃ viṣṇuparam| nāmasahasrakādīti | ādiśabdena śrīgītādi gṛhyate | so'pi tadubhayamapi śivaparatayā vyākhyātuṃ śakyamapi viṣṇuparatayaiva vyākhyātavān | ante ca bhavatstutiparaḥ śrīmatpādādikeśastutiṃ kurvan gatiṃ mokṣaṃ gataḥ, na tu śivastutiparaḥ || 5||
sakaleṣu tvāmeva mānayatīti yaduktaṃ, tadevāha -- 
mūrtitrayātigamuvāca ca mantraśāstrasyādau kalāyasuṣamaṃ sakaleśvaraṃ tvām |
dhyānaṃ ca niṣkalamasau praṇave khalūktvā
tvāmeva tatra sakalaṃ nijagāda nānyam || 6 ||
mūrtitrayeti | mūrtitrayātigaṃ caturthaṃ parameśvaraṃ tvāṃ kalāyasuṣamaṃ śrīkṛṣṇamuvāca | praṇave praṇavārthatvena tatra niṣkaladhyānāṅgatvena sakalaṃ tvāmeva nijagāda, nānyaṃ śivamityarthaḥ || 6||
purāṇānāṃ viṣṇumahimaparatvaṃ purāṇasaṃgrahe spaṣṭamityāha -- 
samastasāre ca purāṇasaṃgrahe visaṃśayaṃ tvanmahimaiva varṇyate |
trimūrtiyuksatyapadatribhāgataḥ paraṃ padaṃ te kathitaṃ na śūlinaḥ || 7 ||
samasteti | nanu vaikuṇṭhalokaḥ satyalokāntargataḥ brahmāṇḍād bahiṣṭhastu nahi loka iti ced naivamityāha -- trimūrtīti | mūrtitrayayuktebhyaḥ satyalokasthalokatrayebhyaḥ paraṃ brahmāṇḍād bahiṣṭhaṃ yat padaṃ lokaḥ, tat te tava vaikuṇṭhaloka eveti purāṇasaṃgrahe kathitam || 7||
yad brāhmakalpa iha bhāgavatadvitīyaskandhoditaṃ vapuranāvṛtamīśa! dhātre |
tasyaiva nāma hariśarvamukhaṃ jagāda
śrīmādhavaḥ śivaparo'pi purāṇasāre ||8||
yaditi | yasmin brahmābhūt sa brāhmaḥ kalpaḥ, tasmin bhagavatā dhātre pratyakṣīkṛtaṃ yad vapuḥ, tasya vapuṣa eva hariśarvamukhaṃ nāma jagāda śrīmādhavācāryaḥ purāṇasāre | yadyapyasau śivaparastathāpi purāṇasāre dhātre darśitasya vapuṣo na śivamūrtitvamuktavān, sarvamayastu tvameveti ca tvāmevoktavānityarthaḥ || 8 ||
nanu
"goptā viṣṇustamomūrtirvyāpāreṇa tu sāttvikaḥ | "
"tadadhīno hariḥ sākṣād"
"viṣṇurviriñcaśca yatpādapadmaṃ śirasā bibharti " ityādivaca nairharernikṛṣṭatā  vyāsenaikokke pāśaṅkaya tasyārthavādatvenātatparatvamāha 'ye svaprakṛtyanuguṇam' iti dvābhyāṃ -- 
ye svaprakṛtyanuguṇā giriśaṃ bhajante
teṣāṃ phalaṃ hi ddaḍhayaiva tadīyabhaktyā |
vyāso hi tena kṛtavānadhikārihetoḥ
skāndādikeṣu taba hānivaco'rthavādai || 9 ||
ya iti| "yasyecchā jāyate yasmin taṃ devaṃ sa samāśrayed" iti nyāyena svaprakṛtyanu guṇaṃ prāgjanmavāsanānusāreṇa | teṣāṃ dṛḍhayā tadīyabhaktyaiva phalaṃ bhaviṣyatīti yat tena hetunā vyāso hyadhikārihetoḥ girīśabhajanādhikāriṇaṃ prati arthavādaiḥ tava hānivaco nyūnatāvacanāni kṛtavān | arthavādāśca giriśabhajanarucyutpādanārthāḥ || 9 ||
nanu rucyutpādanārthaṃ ced, giriśaṃ stūyādeva, nānyahānivacaḥ kuryādityatrāha -- 
bhūtārthakīrtiranuvādaviruddhavādau tredhārthavādagatayaḥ khalu rocanārthāḥ |
skāndādikeṣu bahavo'tra viruddhavādāstvattāmasatvaparibhūtyupaśikṣaṇādyāḥ || 10 ||
bhūtārtheti | bhṛtārthakīrtiḥ yathāvasthitārthakīrtanam anuvādo'viruddhaguṇāropaḥ, pramāṇāntaraviruddhārthakathanaṃ viruddhavāda iti tredhārthavādasya gatayo mārgāḥ | trayo'pi rocanārthā eva | tatra teṣāṃ madhye tvattāmasatvādayo viruddhavādāḥ pramāṇāntaraviruddhavacanāni te ca rocanārthāḥ | viṣṇurapi yatprasādamapekṣate, aho giri -- 
śasya prādhānyamiti giriśabhajanarucyutpāda kā evaite, na tu viṣṇostāmasatvaparājayādijñāpanādau pramāṇabhūtāḥ || 10 ||
ukte'rthe svakapolakalpitatvaśaṅkāṃ vārayan kaviḥ śrotṛśraddhāṃ sampādayati -- 
yatkiñcidapyaviduṣāpi vibho! mayoktaṃ
tanmantraśāstravacanādyabhiddaṣṭameva |
vyāsoktisāramayabhāgavatopagīta !
kleśāna vidhūya kuru bhaktibharaṃ parātman ! || 11 ||
yatkiñciditi | vyāsoktayaḥ purāṇāni teṣāṃ sāramayaṃ śrībhāgavataṃ, śrīvedavyāsasya kṛtakṛtyatāpādakatvāt tadupagīta ! tatpratipādya ! || 11 || 559 ||
iti āgamādīnāṃ bhagavatyeva paramatātparyanirūpaṇaṃ navatitamaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyākhyāyāṃ
daśamaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
pravṛttilakṣaṇā līlā prācyaiḥ padyaiḥ prakīrtitā |
nivṛttilakṣaṇā sayaṃ harerlīṃlā nirūpyate ||
samprati bhaktānāṃ muktimārgapradarśanāya bhagavadguṇān varṇayitumārabhamāṇaḥ prathamaṃ mokṣopāyamaulibhūtasya bhagavadupāsanasya māhātmyaṃ darśayati -- 
śrīkṛṣṇa! tvatpadopāsanamabhayatamaṃ baddhamithyārthadṛṣṭermartyasyārtasya manye vyapasarati bhayaṃ yena sarvātmanaiva |
yattāvat tvatpraṇītāniha bhajanavidhīnāsthito mohamārge
dhāvannapyāvṛtākṣaḥ svalati na kuhacid devadevākhilātman ! || 1 ||
śrīkṛṣṇeti | baddhā rūḍhapadā mithyārthe śarīrādau dṛṣṭiḥ ahammamābhimāno yasya, ata eva martyasya jananamaraṇadharmiṇaḥ, tasmādārtasya ādhyātmikādiduḥkhapīḍitasya tvatpadopāsanamabhayatamaṃ na vidyate bhayaṃ yasmāt tadabhayam atiśayenābhayam abhayatamaṃ bhavabhayanivartakaṃ manye | abhayatamatvamāha -- yeneti | yena tvatpadopāsanena bhayaṃ sarvātmanaiva niravaśeṣameva vyapasarati apagacchati | upāsanasādhaneṣu ca bhagavaddharmo'kutobhaya ityāha -- yaditi | iha bhagavadbhajanamārgeṣu madhye tvatpraṇītān bhaktānugrahāya bhagavataiva svamukhenaiva proktān ata eva bhāgavatākhyān bhajanavidhīn dharmān tāvat satsaṅgadvārā kramādāsthita āśritaḥ san mohamārge ajñānadvārabhūtarathyāpaṇagaṇikālayagoṣṭhayādipradeśeṣvāsthito dhāvan kiñcit kiñcidatikramyātikramya atiśīghramanutiṣṭhan apica āvṛtākṣaḥ nimīlitaśśrutismṛtyākhyanayanadvandvaḥ | ajñātvāpītyarthaḥ | yadāhuḥ -- 
"śrutismṛtī ubhe netre viprāṇāṃ parikīrtite |
ekena rahitaḥ kāṇo dvābhyāmandhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||"
iti | kuhacit kutrāpi na svalati anuṣṭhānavaikalyādinā na pratyavāyī syāt | nāpi phalasyānityatayā punarapi saṃsārī syādityarthaḥ | iti yat, tadabhayatamaṃ manya iti pūrveṇānvayaḥ || 1 ||
bhagavaddharmānāha -- 
bhūman ! kāyena vācā muhurapi manasā tvaddhalapreritātmā
yadyat kurve samastaṃ tadiha paratare tvayyasāvarpayāmi |
jātyāpīha śvapākastvayi nihitamanaḥ karmavāgindriyārthaprāṇo viśvaṃ punīte na tu vimukhamanāstvatpadād vipravaryaḥ ||2||
bhūmanniti | kāyena śiraḥpāṇyādinā vācā vāgindriyeṇa manasā antarindriyeṇa | apiśabdāt sarvendriyairityarthaḥ | tava antaryāmiṇaḥ balena vāsanārūpeṇa prerita ātmā mano yasya sa tathā muhuḥ yadyat kurve iti | ayamarthaḥ -- na kevalaṃ vihitaṃ karmaiveti niyamaḥ, kintu svabhāvānusāri laukikamapi karma śrīkṛṣṇe samarpitaṃ ced bhagavaddharmo bhavatyeveti | asāvahaṃ tat samastaṃ paratare paramātmani tvayi śrīkṛṣṇe arpayāmi śrīkṛṣṇāyedamiti samarpayāmi | ayaṃ cāśayaḥ -- yathā rājñā preritasya rājapuruṣasya karma rājñaḥ prītaye bhavati, tathā sarvātmaneśvareṇa preritasya mama sakalamapi karma śrīkṛṣṇaprītaye syāditi | athavā tatsamarpaṇamantrapūrvakaṃ samarpayāmīti | evaṃ bhagavaddharmānuṣṭhāturmāhātmyamāha -- jātyeti | manaḥ saṅkalpavikalpātmakaṃ mānasaṃ karma | karma kāyikam | vāg vācikam | indriyāṇi jñānakarmalakṣaṇāni | arthāstadviṣayāḥ | prāṇāḥ pañca | vidhipratiṣedhalakṣaṇaṃ sarve karma śarīravāṅmanaḥpradhānaṃ, tadaṅgatayā cetarendriyakarmeti trayāṇāṃ pṛthagupādānam | tvayi īśvare nihitāḥ samarpitāḥ manaḥkarmavāgindriyārthaprāṇā yena tathābhūtaḥ san jātyā śvapāko'pi viśvaṃ punīte | kimucyate svātmānaṃ punīta iti | tvatpadādū vimukhaṃ smaraṇādirahitaṃ mano yasya sa tathā | evambhūtastu vipravaryaḥ sannapi nāsau paraṃ cātmanaṃ vā  śodhayituṃ samartha ityarthaḥ || 2 ||
nanu viṣayavikṣiptacetasāṃ kuto manaḥkarmavāgādīnāmīśvare samarpaṇaṃ saṃbhavatītyāśaṅkaya na tāvad viṣayo nāma vāstavo'sti kintu manovilāsamātram | ato manonirodhapūrvakaṃ sarve samarpayedityabhiprāyeṇāha -- 
bhītirnāma dvitīyād bhavati nanu manaḥkalpitaṃ ca dvitīyaṃ
tenaikyābhyāsaśīlo hṛdayamiha yathāśakti buddhyā nirundhyām |
māyāviddhe tu tasmin punarapi na tathā bhāti māyādhināthaṃ
tat tvāṃ bhaktyā mahatyā satatamanubhajannīśa! bhītiṃ vijahyām || 3 ||
bhītiriti | dvitīyād ātmātmīyatvenābhimanyamānāccharīrādeḥ | nāma prasiddhau | dehātmavādināmanyeṣāmapi dehasyaiva janmādi, nātmanaḥ, tasya nityatvāditi prasiddham | manaḥkalpitaṃ manasā saṅkalpavikalpātmikayāntaḥkaraṇavṛttyā kalpitam āropitam | aikyābhyāsaśīlaḥ yathā mṛdāderutpannasya ghaṭādermṛdādyātmakatvaṃ, tathā brahmaṇa utpannatvād brahmaivedaṃ sarve na tataḥ pṛthak kiñcidastīti cintanaśīlaḥ san hṛdayaṃ budhghā niścayātmikayāntaḥkaraṇavṛttyā yathāśakti yāvahuddhibalam iha cintite'rthe nirundhyāṃ niyaccheyam | tasmin hṛdaye māyāviddhe māyākāryakāmakrodhādirañjite sati punarapi nigṛhīte'pi hṛdaye tathā na bhāti naikyaṃjñānamavatiṣṭhate | tat tasmād māyādhināthaṃ māyākhāminaṃ tvāṃ mahatyā anapāyinyā bhaktyā premalakṣaṇayā satatamanubhajan yathāvasaraṃ sevamānaḥ bhītiṃ saṃsārabhayaṃ vijayāṃ viśeṣeṇa sarvātmanā tyajeyam || 3 ||
bhaktyānubhajannityukte bhakterutpattihetvapekṣāyāmāha -- 
bhaktterutpattivṛddhī tava caraṇajuṣāṃ saṅgamenaiva puṃsāmāsādye puṇyabhājāṃ śriya iva jagati śrīmatāṃ saṅgamena |
tatsaṅgo deva! bhūyānmama khalu satataṃ tanmukhādunmiṣadbhistvanmāhātmyaprakārairbhavati ca sudṛḍhā bhaktiruddhūtapāpā || 4 ||
bhakteriti | utpattiśca vṛddhiśca utpattivṛddhī taveśvarasya caraṇau juṣante sevante iti caraṇajuṣo bhaktāḥ, teṣāṃ saṅgamena saṃsargeṇaiva puṃsāṃ śarīriṇām āsādye labdhaṃ śakye, nānyo'tropāya ityarthaḥ | 'satsevayā vinā kasya viṣṇubhaktiḥ prajāyata' iti vacanāt | tarhi satsaṅgamaḥ kathaṃ syādityata āha -- puṇyabhājāmiti | anekajanmārjitapuṇyasañcayānāmeva satsaṅgamaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | atrānurūpaṃ dṛṣṭāntamāha -- śriya iveti | yathā śriyo dhanadhānyāderutpattivṛddhī śrī  matāṃ saṅgamenaivotpādye, tathetyarthaḥ | he deva! śrīkṛṣṇa! mama khalu satataṃ tatsaṅgaḥ teṣāṃ bhaktānāṃ saṅgaḥ saṃsargaḥ bhūyāt | tanmukhāt teṣāṃ tava caraṇajuṣāṃ mukhād unmiṣadbhiḥ mithastvaccaritaprastāva prādurbhūtaiḥ na tu tairupadiṣṭaiḥ | anena ca teṣāṃ bhagavaccaritavarṇanaśīlatvaṃ vyajyate | tvanmāhātmyaprakāraiḥ tava viṣṇormāhātmyasya prakāraiḥ śravaṇakīrtanasmaraṇādyāśritatayā mārgabhedaiḥ bhavati śrīkṛṣṇe sudṛḍhā anapāyinī udbhūtapāpā yathā dūrato'pasāritatimiranikara eva taraṇirudeti, tadvad dūrotsāritaduritanicayā premalakṣaṇā bhaktiśca mama satataṃ bhūyādityanuṣajyate || 4 ||
atha satsaṅgamātreṇa svasya sādhanabhaktidvārā sādhyabhaktyutpattiprakāra mutpaśyan prathamaṃ svasya bhaktimārge'dhikāra prāptimāha
śreyomārgeṣu bhaktāvadhikabahumatirjanmakarmāṇi bhūyo
gāyan kṣemāṇi nāmānyapi tadubhayataḥ pradrutaṃ pradrutātmā |
udyaddhāsaḥ kadācit kuhacidapi rudan kāpi garjan pragāyannunmādīva pranṛtyannayi kuru karuṇāṃ lokabāhyaśvareyam || 5 ||
śreyomārgeṣviti | śreyasaḥ puruṣārthasya mārgeṣu prāptisādhaneṣu karmajñānatapodāmatratādiṣu madhye bhaktau bhaktimārge aghikā bahumatiḥ śraddhā yasya so'ghikabahumatiḥ| so'yaṃ bhaktimārge'ghikriyata iti bhāvaḥ | yaduktaṃ -- 
"yadṛcchayā matkathādau jātaśraddhastu yaḥ pumān |
na nirviṇṇo nātisakto bhaktiyogo'sya siddhidaḥ ||"
iti | janmakarmāṇi avatārataccaritāni tannibandhanāni nāmāni ca kṣemāṇi puruṣārtharūpāṇi tadupāyabhūtānītyarthaḥ | etacca janmāditrayaviśeṣaṇam | bhūyaḥ punaḥ punargāyan kīrtayan | etacca smaraṇādīnāmapyupalakṣaṇam | evaṃ sādhanabhaktyanuṣṭhānena sañjātapremalakṣaṇabhakteḥ saṃsāradharmātītāṃ gatimāha -- tadubhayata iti | janmakarmakīrtanānnāmakīrtanāñca hetorityarthaḥ | pradrutamatiśīghraṃ pradrutaḥ dravībhūta ātmā mano yasya sa pradrutātmā kadācid udyaddhāsaḥ bhaktaparājitaṃ bhagavantamākalayya sahastatāḍanaṃ hasatītyarthaḥ | kuhacid kvacid rudannapi etāvantaṃ kālamupekṣito'smīti roditi, athavā bhagavati cittasyānekāgratākhedāt | yadvā tadrūpasākṣātkārāddharṣāśrūṇi muñcanniti | kvāpi kadācid garjan atyautsukyāduccaiśaśabdaṃ kurvan atiharṣeṇa pragāyan kkaciñca jitaṃ jitamiti pranṛtyan prakarṣeṇa sahastatālaṃ saśiraḥkampaṃ nartanaṃ kurvan | kiṃ tarhi bhakto'yamiti parān prati prakāśayitumetaditi ced, netyāha -- unmādīveti | grahagṛhīta ivetyarthaḥ | ayi bhagavan! mayi karuṇāṃ kuru, yenāhamevaṃ lokabāhyaścareyam alaukiko bhūtvā careyam || 5||
bhūtānyetāni bhūtātmakamapi sakalaṃ pakṣimatsyān mṛgādīn
martyān mitrāṇi śatrūnapi yamitamatistvanmayānyānamāni |
tvatsevāyāṃ hi sidhyenmama tava kṛpayā bhaktidāḍharyaṃ virāgastvattattvasyāvabodho'pi ca bhuvanapate ! yatnabhedaṃ vinaiva || 6 ||
bhūtānīti| kiñca bhūtāni pṛthivyādīni pañcaitāni | bhūtātmakaṃ pāñcabhautikaṃ sakalaṃ carācarātmakamapi pakṣimatsyān mṛgādīn jalasthalādyāśritān martyān mitrāṇi śatrūnapi bandhuśatrūdāsīnāṃśca tvanmayāni tvadrūpāṇīti yamitamatiḥ etāni sarvāṇi śrīhare rūpāṇīti anusandadhānaḥ san ānamāni praṇāmaṃ karavāṇi| nanūktalakṣaṇā bhaktiryogināmapi bahujanmasādhyā kathaṃ bhajanamātreṇaikasmin janmani bhavedityāśaṅkayāha -- tvatsevāyāṃ hi sidhyediti | sarvakarmaṇāṃ samarpaṇaṃ,
janmakarmaṇāṃ nāmnāṃ ca kīrtanaṃ, sarvatreśvaradṛṣṭyā praṇāmaśceha tvatsevocyate | tasyāṃ satyāṃ tava kṛpāvaśyaṃbhāvinī | tayā mama bhakterīśvarapremṇo dārḍhyaṃ sthairya, tato virāgo viṣayaviraktiḥ, tatastvattattvasyeśvarayāthā tathyasyāvabodhaḥ samyaṃganubhavaḥ, etat tritayamapi ca sidhyet | yatnabhedaṃ vinaiveti | tvatkṛpāyāmeva mayā yatnaḥ kāryaḥ | tasyāṃ jātāyām etat trayaṃ yugapadeva sidhyedityarthaḥ || 6 ||
kiñcaivamīśvarakṛpayā bhaktiviraktijñāneṣu jāteṣu bhaktakulottaṃsatāṃ prāpnuyāmityāha -- 
no muhyan kṣuttṛḍhādyairbhavasaraṇibhavaistvannilīnāśayatvāccintāsātatyaśālī nimiṣalavamapi tvatpadādaprakampaḥ |
iṣṭāniṣṭeṣu tuṣṭivyasanavirahito māyikatvāvabodhājjyotsnābhistvannakhendoradhikaśiśiritenātmanā sañcareyam ||7||
no muhyanniti | no muhyan paravaśatāmagacchan | kṣuttṛḍādyaiḥ bhayatarṣakṛcchrādibhiḥ bhavasaraṇibhavaiḥ anādisaṃsāramārgotthaiḥ | tvannilīnāśayatvāt tvayi īśvare samāhitacittavṛttitvāt| kiñca, cintāyā upāsanāyāḥ sātatyaṃ satatamanuṣṭhānaṃ tadasyāstīti tathā| nimiṣalavamapi nimeṣakālasyārdhamapi kālaṃ tvatpadād bhagavatpādāravindabhajanād aprakampaḥ pramādarahitaḥ iṣṭāniṣṭeṣu satsu tuṣṭivyasanavirahitaḥ iṣṭeṣu putrajanmādiṣu tuṣṭivirahitaḥ aniṣṭeṣu putramaraṇādiṣu vyasanavirahitaḥ| māyikatvāvabodhād śarīrāderbhogyasya māyānirmitatvānusandhānāt | jyotsnābhiḥ śītalābhirdīptibhiḥ | tvannakhendoḥ tvatpādanakhacandrasya | adhikaśiśiritena santāparāhityādatiśayena sañjātānandena ātmanā manasā upalakṣitaḥ san sañcareyaṃ varteya || 7||
bhūteṣveṣu tvadaikyasmṛtisamadhigatau nādhikāro'dhunā cet
tvatprema tvatkamaitrī jaḍamatiṣu kṛpā dviṭsu bhūyādupekṣā |
arcāyāṃ vā samarcākutukamurutaraśraddhayā vardhatāṃ me
tvatsaṃsevī tathāpi drutamupalabhate bhaktalokottamatvam || 8 ||
bhūteṣviti | eṣu bhūteṣūtkṛṣṭeṣu nikṛṣṭeṣu maśakādiṣvapi jagadāśrayasyāpracyutaiśvaryasya taveśvarasyaiva jīvarūpeṇāvasthānād yadaikyaṃ tasya smṛtiranusandhānaṃ tasya samaghigatiḥ prāptiḥ sarvabhūteṣvantarbahiśca paripūrṇatayā bhagavaddṛṣṭirbhāgavatottamadharmaḥ tatprāptau mamādhunādhikāro na cet tvayi īśvare prema, tvatkeṣu tvadīya puruṣeṣu bhakteṣviti yāvad, maitrī sakhyaṃ, jaḍamatiṣu mūḍheṣu kṛpā, dviṭsu śatruṣūpekṣā ca bhūyāt | atreśvarādiṣu bhedadṛṣṭerapi sadbhāvādete madhyamabhāgavatadharmāḥ | tatrāpyadhikāro na ced arcāyāṃ pratimāyāṃ yā samarcā pūjā tasyāṃ kutukam āgrahaḥ urutarayā īśvaraguruśāstraviṣayayā śraddhayā viśvāsena| tathā tvatsaṃsevī adhamabhaktaḥ | so'pi drutam asmin janmanyeva | upalabhate prāpnoti | adhamo'pi kramāduttamabhakto bhaviṣyatītyato'dhamatvamapi prārthyata iti bhāvaḥ || 8 ||
‘māyāviddhe tu' ityuktermāyāsvarūpajijñāsāyāmāha -- 
āvṛtya tvatsvarūpaṃ kṣitijalamarudādyātmanā vikṣipantī
jīvān bhūyiṣṭhakarmāvalivivaśagatīn duḥkhajāle kṣipantī |
tvanmāyā mābhibhūnmāmayi bhuvanapate! kalpate tatpraśāntyai
tvatpāde bhaktirevetyavadadayi vibho! siddhayogī prabuddhaḥ|| 9 ||
āvṛtyeti | tvatsvarūpaṃ tava svīyaṃ rūpaṃ brahma āvṛtya āvaraṇaśaktyācchādya| kṣitijalamarudādītyādiśabdena sthūlasūkṣmātmakaṃ bhūtapañcakaṃ caturvidhasthūlaśarīrāṇi brahmāṇḍaṃ ca gṛhyate | tattadātmanā svarūpeṇa vikṣipantī vikṣepaśaktyāvirbhāvayantī jīvān śarīriṇaḥ bhūyiṣṭhakarmāvalivivaśagatīn bhūyiṣṭhāni kāmyaniṣiddhabhedena bahuprakārāṇi karmāṇi teṣām āvaliḥ samūhaḥ tayā tajjanyādṛṣṭavāsanā -- 
"iti bhāgavatān dharmān śikṣan bhaktyā tadutthayā |
nārāyaṇaparo māyāmañjastarati dustarām ||"
iti prabuddhīktiḥ |
bhyāmiti bhāvaḥ | vivaśā asvatantrā gatiḥ uccāvacaśarīraprāptiryeṣāṃ te tathā | duḥkhajāle saṃsārārṇave kṣipantī pātayantī tvanmāyā tvadadhīnā māyā māṃ tvatsevakamapi mābhibhūd mahuddhimākramya tvatsmṛtiṃ māpanaiṣīt | ayi bhuvanapate! śrīkṛṣṇa! tāṃ vārayeti bhāvaḥ | tvatpāde bhaktireva tatpraśāntyai tasyā māyāyāḥ prahāṇāya kalpate upāyo bhavati, nānyo'styupāya ityavadat | siddhayogī siddho jīvanmuktaḥ yogī ceti siddhayogī navayogiṣvanyatamaḥ | yogiṣu madhye jñātṛjñeyajñānarahitatvāt prabuddha iti saṃjñā | sa hi videhāya rājñe "tanmāyayāto budha ābhajet taṃ bhaktyaikayeśam" iti māyātārakatvena bhaktimevāvocat || 9 ||
atha vairāgyavivekadvārā gurūpasattilabdhabhaktijñānasamuccayena māyājayaprakāramāha -- 
duḥkhānyālokya jantuṣvalamuditaviveko'hamācāryavaryāllabdhvā tvadrūpatattvaṃ guṇacaritakathāghudbhavaktibhūmā |
māyāmenāṃ taritvā paramasukhamaye tvatpade moditāhe
tasyāyaṃ pūrvaraṅgaḥ pavanapurapate! nāśayāśeṣarogān || 10 ||
duḥkhānīti | jantuṣu sadaiva duḥkhaparihārāya sukhaprāptaye ca prayatamāneṣu śarīriṣu tata eva jātāni duḥkhānyālokya alam atiśayena uditavivekaḥ yatheha putramitradhanādayaḥ sukhasādhanatvenābhimanyamānāḥ duḥkhameva janayanti karmanirmitatvādū, evaṃ svarge'pītyutpannaviviktajñānaḥ san ahamācāryeṣu jñānavijñānasampanneṣu baryaḥ paropadeśakuśalaḥ tasmāt tvadrūpasya īśvarasvarūpasya tattvaṃ samyagjñānaṃ labdhvā avāpya guṇacaritānāṃ guṇānāṃ bhaktavātsalyādibhagavadguṇānāṃ taccaritānāṃ ca kathā kathanam | ārdaśabdena kīrtanādi gṛhyate | guṇacaritakathādibhiḥ udbhavan bhakterbhūmā bāhulyam anapāyitvaṃ yasya sa tathā | enāmityuktirmāyākāryasya rāgāderanubhūya -- 
iyaṃ tu kavināmnaḥ siddhayogina uktiḥ |
mānatvād | taritvā atikramya paramasukhamaye niratiśayānandasvarūpe tvatpade padyata iti padaṃ brahma tvatsvarūpe brahmaṇītyarthaḥ | athavā paramasukhamaye niratiśayānandajanake tvatpadābje moditāhe prasanno bhavitāsmi bhaktajanaiḥ saha bhāgavatadharmābhyāsena seviṣya iti bhāvaḥ | tasya modasya ayaṃ māyājayaḥ pūrvaraṅgaḥ kāraṇatvena prathamamanuṣṭheyaḥ | yadāha boppadevaḥ --  'māyājayo'parā bhaktirjñānakarmasamuccayād' iti | yadvā tasya māyātaraṇādermadanuṣṭheyasyārthasya ayaṃ mayāsakṛt prārthyamāno madroganāśaḥ pūrvaraṅgaḥ nāṭakaprayoge nāndyādivat prathamamavaśya nirvartyaḥ | sa ca tvatkaruṇaikāyatta iti tvāmeva prārthayāmītyāśayena prārthayate -- pavanapurapate ! iti || 10 ||
iti bhaktereva niḥśreyasāvāptihetutvavarṇanaṃ, bhaktisvarūpatatkāryavarṇanaṃ ca
ekanavatitamaṃ daśakam |
nanu sarvakarmaṇāṃ bhagavaddharmatvāpādanāya vinā phalānusandhānamīśvarasamarpaṇenānuṣṭhānamuktam | tadanupapannaṃ, svargadyarthameva jyotiṣṭomādīnāṃ vihitatvāt phalakāmasyaiva ca tatrādhikārādityāśaṅkaca vedasya parokṣavādatvāt tātparyai durjñeyamityabhiprāyeṇa pariharati
vedaiḥ sarvāṇi karmāṇyaphalaparatayā varṇitānīti buddhvā
tāni tvayyarpitānyeva hi samanucaran yāni naiṣkarmyamīśa ! |
mā bhūd vedairniṣiddhe kuhacidapi manaḥkarmavācāṃ pravṛttirdurvarje cedavāptaṃ tadapi khalu bhavatyarpaye citprakāśe || 1 ||
vedairiti | vedaiḥ vidhivākyaiḥ sarvāṇi jyotiṣṭomādīni karmāṇi aphalaparatayā varṇitāni na phalasādhanatvena vihitāni, kintu naiṣkarmyārthaṃ vihitāni | phalaśrutistu rocanārthā | yathā mātā bālamauṣadhaṃ pāyayantī guḍaśarkarādibhiḥ pralobhayantī pāyayati, dadāti ca guḍaśarkarādi | naitāvatauṣadhapānasya tallābhaḥ prayojanam, api tu roganivṛttiḥ, tathā vedo'pyabāntaraphalaiḥ pralobhayan karmamokṣāyaiva karma vidadhātīti buddhvā tāni phalaparatvena śrutānyapi | evakāreṇa phalābhisandhirvyāvartyate | nanu yadi naiṣkarmyaṃ karmaṇāṃ phalaṃ, tarhi prathamameva karma tyajyatāmityāśa ṅkāyām evaṃ sati vikarmalakṣaṇenādharmeṇa patatītyabhiprāyeṇāha -- samanucaranniti | naiṣkarmyaṃ karmanivṛttisādhyaṃ jñānaṃ yāni prāpnuyāmityarthaḥ | vedairiti smṛtyāderapyupalakṣaṇam | niṣiddhe abhakṣyabhakṣaṇādau kuhacidapi  alpaduṣkṛteṣvapi karmasu manaḥkarmavācāṃ pravṛttiḥ mā bhūt | durvarje varjayitumaśakyaṃ yad niṣiddhakarma, taccedavāptam anuṣṭhitaṃ tarhi tadapi bhavati  ciraprakāśe brahmaṇi arpaye mantrapurassaraṃ samarpayāmītyarthaḥ || 1 ||
vaidikaṃ karmayogamuktvā tāntrikamāha -- 
yastvanyaḥ karmayogastava bhajanamayastatra cābhāṣṭamūrti
hudyāṃ savaikarūpāṃ dṛṣadi hudi mṛdi kāpi vā bhāvayitvā |
puṣpairmandharnivedyairapi ca viracitaiḥ śaktito bhaktipūternityaṃ varyā saparyā vidadhadayi ghibho! tvalpasādaṃ bhajeyam || 2 ||
yastviti | anyaḥ vaidikā vanyastāntrikaḥ tava bhajanamayaḥ tvatpūjārūpaḥ āśu tvatprasādajanakaḥ | abhīṣṭāṃ śrīnārāyaṇavāsudevādirūpāṃ mūrti hṛdyām atimanoharagheṣaviśiṣṭāṃ sattvaikarūpāṃ śuddhasattvamayīṃ dṛṣadi śilāmayyāṃ mṛdi lepyāyāṃ hṛdi manomayyāṃ kvāpi kāṃsyarajatasuvarṇādipratimāyāṃ vā | yathoktaṃ -- 
"śailī dārumayī lauhī lepyā lekhyā ca saikatī |
manomayī maṇimayī pratimāṣṭavidhā smṛtaḥ || "
iti | bhāvayitvā tava mūrti saṅkalpya viracitaiḥ sampāditaiḥ śaktitaḥ yathāśakti "patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalam" ityādivacanāt | bhaktipūtaiḥ puṣpādibhiḥ nityaṃ niyamena trikālaṃ vā varyo 'puṃsi jāyālayaḥ pūje'tyuktarītyā īśvare svaśarīrārambhakāryakāraṇalakṣaṇaprakṛtilayātmakatayā śreṣṭhāṃ saparyā pūjāṃ vidadhat kurvan || 2 ||
samprati bhaktidārḍhyāyābhaktānāmaniṣṭāṃ gatiṃ varṇayati 'strīśūdrā' iti dvābhyāṃ -- 
strīśūdrāstvatkathādiśravaṇavirahitā āsatāṃ te dayārhāstvatpādāsannayātān dvijakulajalupo hanta śocāmyaśāntān |
vṛttyarthaṃ te yajanto bahukathitamapi tvāmanākarṇayanto haptā vidyābhijātyaiḥ kimu na vidadhate tādṛśaṃ mā kṛthā mām || 3 ||
