strīśūdrā iti | ādiśabdena nāmādi gṛhyate | śravaṇaṃ kīrtanāderapyupalakṣaṇam | tvatkathāśravaṇatvannāmakīrtanatvatsmaraṇādivirahitā ityarthaḥ | etaccājñānād, nānadhikārāt | ‘bhaktiścecchūdrayoṣitāmiti bacanāt | ata eva te dayārhāḥ sadbhirdayayā sukhaṃ bodhanīyāḥ | te tāvat tiṣṭhantyiti bhāvaḥ | tvatpādāsannayātān upanayanādisaṃskārairarthāvabodhaparyantenādhyayanena ca tvatpādabhajanādhikāraṃ prāptān dvijānāṃ traivarṇikānāṃ kule janurjanma yeṣāṃ tān aśāntān viṣayaparān hanta śocāmi| jñānalavadurvidagdhatayā bodhayitumaśakyā  ete iti mama kheda iti bhāvaḥ | vṛttyartham udarabharaṇārthaṃ yajantaḥ paśuhiṃsāṃ kurvantaḥ bahukathitamapi śrutyādiṣu saguṇanirguṇabhedena pratipāditamapi tvām īśvaram anākarṇayantaḥ śṛṇvanto'pyaśraddhānatayā śravaṇaphalamanāsādayantaḥ| atra hetumāha -- haptā vidyābhijātyaiḥ | 'vidyāmado dhanamadastṛtīyo'bhijano mada' ityuktamadatrayayuktāḥ kimu na vidadhate kiṃ na kurvanti | ābhicārasajjananindādyapi kurvantyevetyarthaḥ | māṃ tādṛśam ātmahanaṃ mā kṛthāḥ || 3||
sannindāmeva prapañcayati -- 
pāpo'yaṃ kṛṣṇa! rāmetyabhilapati nijaṃ gṛhituṃ duścaritraṃ
nirlajjasyāsya vācā bahutakanīyāni me vidhritāni |
bhrātā me vandhyaśīlo bhajati kila sadā viṣṇumitthaṃ budhāṃste
nindantyuccairhasanti tvayi nihitaratīṃstādṛśaṃ mā kṛthā mām || 4||
pāpa iti | ayaṃ pāpaḥ paradravyāpahārī | tat kathamavagamyate, kṛṣṇa! rāmetyabhilapati| tacca nijaṃ svenānuṣṭitaṃ duścaritraṃ mopaṇādi gṛhituṃ sādhurayaṃ tādṛśaṃ na karotītyasmān pratibodhyāpahotum | asya nirlajjasya haribhakteranyat sāraṃ nāstītyādyasmatsannidhau lajjāṃ nirjitya jalpato'sya yācā hetunā me bahutarāṇi kathanīyāni bandhubhirnirūpaṇīyāni kāryajātāni vighnitāni | nanu tasmin karmāṇi bhrātā kiṃ na niyujyata iti cet tatrāha -- bhrātā me vandhyaśīla iti | vandhyaṃ niṣphalaṃ śīlaṃ yasya sa tathā | tena mamopakāro nāstīti bhāvaḥ  | sadā viṣṇuṃ bhajati kila puṣpāñjalinamaskāranāmakīrtanādi karoti | tat kila viṣṇubhajanam | tena tasya mama vā kiṃ syāt | itthaṃ te aśāntāḥ budhān bhaktān nindanti | tvayi īśvare nihitā sthirīkṛtā ratiḥ premalakṣaṇāṃ bhaktiryaistāna uccairhasanti tvaddāsyādiprārthanāmanyaiḥ saha parihasanti | eteṣāṃ kadācidapi saṃsāranivṛttyasaṃbhavād māṃ tādṛśaṃ mā kṛthāḥ || 4 ||
yugānurūpaṃ sevāprakāramāha -- 
śvetacchāyaṃ kṛte tvāṃ munivaravapuṣaṃ prīṇayante tapobhistretāyāṃ strukastruvādyaṅkitamararuṇatuṃ yajñarūpaṃ yajante |
sevante tantramārgai visadarigadaṃ dvāpare śyāmalā
nīlaṃ saṅkīrtanācairiha kalisamaye mānuṣāstvāṃ bhajante || 5 ||
śvetacchāyamiti | śvetacchāyaṃ śuklavarṇaṃ kṛte kṛtayuge munivaravapuṣam | atra muniśabdena brahmacārī vivakṣyate, jaṭāvalkalakṛṣṇājinopavītākṣamālādaṇḍakamaṇḍalvādīn bibhratamityarthaḥ| tvāṃ caturbāhuṃ tapobhiḥ dhyānamārgaiḥ prīṇayante prasādayanti | mānuṣā iti sarvatrānuṣajyate | tretāyāṃ tretāyuge strakkhuvādyaṅkitaṃ srug juhūḥ suvaśca tadādibhiḥ karāgrasthitaiḥ aṅkitam upalakṣitaṃ yajante | vaidikaiḥ karmabhiriti śeṣaḥ | dvāpare tantramārgaiḥ pūjāprakāraiḥ sevante pūjayanti | vilasadarigadaṃ vilasantyau
arigade yasya tam | etañcanyāyudhānāmapyupalakṣaṇam | śyāmalāṅgam atasīkusumacchāyam| iha kalisamaye nīlaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇam | saṅkīrtanaṃ nāmoccāraṇam ādyaṃ pradhānaṃ yeṣāṃ taiḥ bhajante sevante || 5 ||
tatra kalī sevāsaukaryamāha pañcabhiḥ
so'yaṃ kāleyakālo jayati muraripo! yatra saṅkīrtanādyairniryanatnaireṣa mārgerakhilada ! nacirāta tvatprasādaṃ bhajante|
jātāratretākṛtādāvapi hi kila kalau sambhavaṃ kāmayante
daivāt tatraiva jātān viṣayaviṣarasairmā vibho! vañcayāsmān || 6 ||
so'yamitti | sa pūrvoktaḥ ayaṃ vartamānaḥ kalerayaṃ kāleyaḥ kālo jayati itarayugebhya utkṛṣṭo bhavati | yatra kaliyuge niryantai yatnavirahitaiḥ saṅkīrtanādyairmagairupāyaireva nacirāt śīghraṃ tvatprasādaṃ bhajante labhante | nanu kṛtādiṣu jātā apyevaṃvādinaḥ, netyāha -- jātā iti | hiśabdasahitaḥ kilaśabdo'syārthasya purāṇādiprasiddhiṃ dyotayati | daivāt sukṛtaparipākena tatra kalāveva jātānasmān viṣayaviṣarasaiḥ viṣayā eva viṣarasā viṣadravāḥ, yasmāt tadupayoge mohaṃ gaccheyuḥ | tairmā vañcaya || 6 ||
bhaktāstāvat kalau syurdramilabhuvi tato bhūriśastatra coccaiḥ
kāverī tāmraparṇīmanu kila kṛtamālāṃ ca puṇyāṃ pratīcīm |
hā māmapyetadantarbhavamapi ca vibho ! kiñcidaścadrasaṃ tvayyāśāpāśairnibadhya bhramaya na magavan! pūraya tvanniṣevām || 7 ||
bhaktā iti | tāvat prathamameva kalauṃ bhaktāḥ bhūriśaḥ tatra tatra bahavaḥ syuḥ  , tatastatra kalāvapi dramilabhuvi bhūriśaḥ | tatra ca dramilāviṣaye'pi purṇyā kāveryādinadīmanu tattīreṣu uñcaiḥ tato'pyadhikāḥsyuḥ | kāveryādīnāṃ jalaṃ ye pibanti, te'tīva
bhaktā bhavantīti bhāvaḥ | etadantarbhavaṃ dramilaviṣayāntaḥpātinam apica tvāyi kiñcidañcadrasam īṣadārabdhabhakti māṃ na bhramaya || 7 ||
dṛṣṭvā dharmaguhaṃ taṃ kālimapakaruṇaṃ prāṅ mahīkṣit parīkṣiddhantuṃ vyākṛṣṭakhaḍgo'pi na vinihatavān sāravedī guṇāṃśāt |
tvatsevādyāśu sidhyedasadiha na tathā tvatpare caiṣa bhīruryattu prāgeva rogādibhirapaharate tatra hā śikṣayainam || 8 ||
dṛṣṭveti | prāk purā parīkṣinnāma mahīkṣid rājā dharmadruhaṃ vṛṣarūpiṇaṃ dharme daṇḍena praharantaṃ taṃ kaliṃ dṛṣṭvā hantuṃ vyākṛṣṭakhopi guṇāṃśād na vinihatavān | nanu guṇāṃśe satyapi dharmagṛhaṃ kiṃ na nihatānityatrāha -- sāravedīti| alpatve'pi guṇasya sārāṃśatvaṃ jānannityarthaḥ | tadevāha -- vatsevādīti | arcananāmakīrtanādi āśu iha janmanyeva sidhyed bhaktidvārā muktimutpādayet | asad duṣkarma tu iha kalau na tathā nāśu sidhyet | kiñca, tvatpare tvadbhaktaviṣaye caiṣa kalirbhīruḥ īśvaravimukheṣveva kaliḥ prabhavatīti bhāvaḥ | kiñca, prāgeva tvatsevādyārambhāt pūrvameva rogādibhirapaharate tvadbhajanānnivartayatīti tu yat hā mama tu tadevāpatitam | tanna apaharaṇe enaṃ kaliṃ śikṣaya | yathā svatsevāyāmughuktāt kalirdūrato'pasarati, tathainaṃ kurviti bhāvaḥ ||8||
gaṅgā gītā ca gāyatryapi ca tulasikā gopikācandanaṃ tat
sālagrāmāmipūjā parapuruṣa ! tathaikādaśī nāmavarṇāḥ |
etānyaṣṭāpyayatnānyayi kalisamaye tvatprasādapravṛddhyā
kṣipraṃ muktipradānītyābhidadhurṝṣayasteṣu māṃ sajjayethāḥ || 9||
gaṅgeti | tvatprasādapravṛddhyā taddvāreṇa muktipradānyetānītyatra smṛtipramāṇatvamāha -- abhidadhurṛṣaya iti || 9 ||
nanu tribhiḥ ṛṇairjātasya tadanapākaraṇe kathaṃ muktiḥ syādityata āha -- 
devarṣīṇāṃ pitṛṇāmapi na punarṛṇī kiṅkaro vā sa bhūman !
yo'sau sarvātmanā tvāṃ śaraṇamupagataḥ sarvakṛtyāni hitvā |
tasyotpanaṃ vikarmāpyakhilamapanudasyeva cittasthitastvaṃ
tanme pā potthatāpān pavanapurapate ! rundhi bhakti praṇīyāḥ || 10 ||
devarṣīṇāmiti | devānām ṛṣīṇāṃ pitṝṇāmapi, yathā abhakta ṛṇī adhamarṇo bhavati | ata eva tadapākaraṇāya yajñādhyayanaputrotpādanādi tena kartavyamiti teṣāṃ kiṅkaraśca bhavati | tathāca smṛtiḥ "hīnajāti parikṣīṇamṛṇārthaṃ karma kārayet " iti | sa bhaktaḥ punarṛṇī kiṅkaro vā na bhavati | tatra hetumāha -- yo'sāviti | yaḥ puruṣaḥ sarvātmanā sarvabhāvena tvāṃ śaraṇamupagataḥ sarvakṛtyāni vihitasakalakarmāṇi hitvā | nanu  tarhi sarvātmanā niṣiddhakarmaṇo'parihāryatvāt prāyaścittādikarma kartavyaṃ, netyāha -- tasyeti | vikarma niṣiddhakurmaṇaḥ phalaṃ tasya tvāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatasya tāvanna saṃbhavati | yadi pramādādinā bhavet, tarhi tadapītyapiśabdārthaḥ | akhilaṃ niravaśeṣam apanudāsa nāśayasi | nanu nāsya pāpakṣayārthaṃ bhagavadupāsanam, ata āha -- cittasthitastvamiti | na hi vastuśaktirthitāmapekṣate kāryotpādanāya, yathā hyauṣadhaṃ phalārthināmaphalārthināmapyaviśeṣeṇārogyādi janayati tatheti bhāvaḥ | tat tasmād yasmāñcittasthaḥ pāpamapanudasi, pāpotthān pāpakarmajanitān tāpān duritāni rundhi vāraya | bhakti praṇīyāḥ anapāyinīṃ kṛṣīṣṭhāḥ || 10 ||
iti karmamiśrabhaktisvarūpavarṇanaṃ dvinavatitamaṃ daśakam |
evaṃ viṃśatyā kṣlokaiḥ karmamiśrāṃ bhakti nirūpya anantaraṃ jñānamiśrāṃ nirūpayiṣyan prathamaṃ tadadhikāraprāṃptimāha -- 
bandhusnehaṃ vijahyāṃ tava hi karuṇayā tvayyupāveśitātmā
sarva tyaktvā careyaṃ sakalamapi jagad vīkṣya māyāvilāsam |
nānātvād bhrāntijanyāt sati khalu guṇadoṣāvavodhe vidhirvā
vyāsedho vā kathaṃ tau tvayi nihitamatervītavaiṣamyabuddheḥ || 1 ||
bandhusnehamiti | bandhuṣu putramitrakalatrādiṣu snehaṃ yatastvanmāyākṛtam atastava kṛpayā vijahyāṃ tyajeyam | hiśabdastaddhāne sādhanāntararāhityaṃ darśayati | tatastvatkṛpayaiva tvayi upāveśitātmā samāhitacitaḥ san jagad māyāvilāsaṃ tvanmāyākalpitaṃ vīkṣya avagamya tvadupāsanāto'nyat sarvaṃ karma vidhiniṣedhabhedamapi tyaktvā careyamiti gṛhādyanapekṣā dyotyate | nanu vihitasya karmaṇo'nanuṣṭhānaṃ na yuktaṃ, pāpotpattau prāyaścittamapi kāryamevetyāśaṅkaya karmaṇāmavidvadadhikāritvena viduṣastadakaraṇe na doṣa ityāha nānātvāditi | manuṣyo'hamiti dehe ahamabhimāno bhrāntiḥ | tajjanyaṃ nānātvaṃ varṇāśramādibhedaḥ | tatsatyatāṅgīkāra eva guṇadoṣau nityanaimittikaṃ karma sattvaśodhakatvād guṇaḥ, hiṃsādi doṣaḥ, pāpakṣayasādhanatvāta kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādi guṇaḥ ityādiguṇadoṣāvabodhe satyeva vidhirvyāsedho niṣedho vā bhavet | tvayi nihitamateḥ brahmaivedaṃ sarvamiti niścitabuddheḥ ata eva vītavaiṣamyabuddheḥ tyaktanānātvabhramasya mama tu kathaṃ tau vidhiniṣaidhau syātām | ato vidhiniṣedhānadhikārāt sarvaṃ tyaktvā careyamityarthaḥ | evañca vidhiniṣedhātīto jñānamiśrāyāṃ bhaktāvadhikārītyapi darśitam | taduktaṃ muktāphale "jñānamiśraikā bhikṣūṇām" iti || 1 ||
etat sarva puruṣaśarīreṇaiva sādhyamiti vaktuṃ tiryagādidehebhyaḥ puruṣatanurutkṛṣṭeti puruṣatvaṃ stauti -- 
kṣuttṛṣṇālopamātre satatakṛtadhiyo jantaghaḥ santyanantāstebhyo vijñānavattvāt puruṣa iha varastajjanirdurlabhaiva |
tatrāpyātmātmanaḥ syāt suhṛdapi ca ripuryastvayi nyastacetāsvāpocchinterupācaṃ smarati sa hi suhṛt svātmavairī tato'nyaḥ || 2 ||
kṣuttṛṣṇeti | kṣuttṛṣṇayoḥ aśanāyāpipāsayoḥ lopaḥ tṛṇakavalanajalapānādi etāvanmātre satataṃ kṛtadhiyo niścitabuddhayaḥ jantavaḥ paśvādayaḥ anantāḥ bahavaḥ santi tebhyaḥ vijñānavattvād ātmadarśanasāmarthyena iha bhūmau puruṣo manuṣyaḥ varaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ | tajjanirmanuṣyajanma | tatrāpi manuṣyeṣvapi ātmaivātmanaḥ suhṛd upakārī ripuḥ apakārī ca | vivekamāha -- yastvayi nyastacetāḥ ata eva tāpocchitteḥ mokṣasya upāyaṃ sādhanaṃ smarati jānāti, tathāvidhaśvedātmā svayameva ātmanaḥ svasya suhṛd bandhuḥ syāt | tato'nyaḥ naivaṃvidhaḥ sa ātmavairī ātmahā ||2||
nanbātmaivātmano gururityuktaṃ tadasaṅgatamityāśaṅkaya yad yasyājñānanāśahetuḥ sa tasya gururitīha vivakṣitatvāt sthiracarāṇāmapi taddhetutvād gurutvamūrīkaraṇīyaṃ, kiṃ punarātmana iti pariharan pṛthivyādibhyo gurubhyaḥ śikṣaṇīyāni kṣamādīnyātmanaḥ prārthayate -- 
tvatkāruṇye pravṛtte ka iva na hi gururlokavṛtte'pi bhūman !
sarvākrāntāpi bhūmirna hi calati tataḥ satkṣamāṃ śikṣayeyam |
gṛhṇīyāmīśa! tattadviṣayaparicaye'pyaprasaktiṃ samīrād
vyāptatvaṃ cātmano me gaganagurubaśād bhātu nirlepatā ca || 3 ||
tvatkāruṇya iti | ka iva nahi guruḥ, na kevalamātmaiva, pṛthivyādipadārthā api gurava eveti bhāvaḥ | kimebhyaḥ śikṣaṇīyaṃ, tadāha -- lokavṛtta iti | lokāḥ sthiracarādayaḥ teṣāṃ vṛttaṃ kṣamānissaṅgatvālepakatvādisvabhāvaḥ | tatraite gurava ityarthaḥ | he vibhūman ! viṣṇo! bhūmiḥ sarvaiḥ khalairākrāntāpi na calati hi | tataḥ bhūmeḥ satkṣamāṃ svamārgādacalanarūpāṃ śikṣayeyaṃ kartavyatayāvagaccheyam | tattadviṣayairguṇavadbhirdoṣavadbhiśca paricaye samparke satyapyaprasaktim asaṅgatvaṃ samīrād gṛhṇīyāṃ śikṣayeyam | ātmanaḥ śarīraparicchinnatayā pratīyamānasya vyāptatvaṃ sarvaśarīreṣvantarbahiśca vyāptatvam | me gaganaguruvaśāditi | gaganaṃ hi ghaṭādiparicchinnatayā pratīyamānamapi rbāherantaśca vyāptam | kiñca, nirlepatā śarīrasthatve'pi tatsparśarahito nirlepaḥ, tasya bhāvo nirlepatā | sā ca gaganaguruvaśādeva me bhātu | yathā gaganaṃ meghādibhirna spṛśyate tatheti || 3 ||
jalānalaśaśira vibhyaḥ śikṣaṇīyamāha -- 
svacchaḥ syāṃ pāvano'haṃ madhura udakavad vahnivanmā sma gṛhṇāṃ
sarvānnīno'pi doṣaṃ taruṣu tamiva māṃ sarvabhūteṣvaveyām |
puṣṭirnaṣṭiḥ kalānāṃ śaśina iva tanornātmano'stīti vidyāṃ
toyādivyastamārtaṇḍavadapi ca tanuṣvekatāṃ tvatprasādāt || 4 ||
svaccha iti | ahamudakavat svaccho nirmalaḥ pāvanaḥ darśanamātreṇa pāpaśodhakaḥ madhuro madhurālāpī ca syām | sarvānnīnaḥ sarvabhakṣo'pi vahnivad doṣaṃ mā sma gṛhṇāṃ pāpaṃ nādadyām | agnergrāhyāntaramāha -- taruṣviti | taruṣu kāṣṭheṣu tamagnimiva yathā kāṣṭheṣu praviṣṭo'gniḥ kāṣṭhasamānarūpo hasvadīrghasthūlasūkṣmādirūpo'vagamyate, tathā mām ātmānamapi sarvabhūteṣu suranaratiryagādiśarīreṣu tadupādhivaśāt tattatsamānarūpo'vagamyata iti aveyām avagaccheyam | śaśinaḥ śikṣaṇīyamāha -- 
§ āṃdadyāmityasya sthāne 'ādadīya' iti sādhu |
puṣṭiriti | pūrvapaṃkṣapratipadamārabhya kramād dūra  sthitasyādityamaṇḍalasya pañcadaśaḥ pañcadaśo bhāgo jalamaye candramaṇḍale pratibimbito dṛśyate | te pratibimbāścandrasya kalā ityucyante | yathā tāsāmeva puṣṭiṃ naṣṭiṃ ca vidyāṃ, na śaśinaḥ, tadvat tanoreva janmāstitva vipariṇāmavivṛddhyapakṣayavināśarūpāḥ ṣaḍ bhāvavikārāḥ, na tvātmana iti vidyāmityarthaḥ | ādityadṛṣṭāntenāvagantavyamāha -- toyādīti | yathaiko  mārtaṇḍo'nekeṣu ghaṭodakādiṣu vyasto'neko dṛśyate, tadvat tanuṣu sarvaśarīreṣu ātmana upādhibhedena bhedapratītāvapyekatāmapi ca vidyām | tvatprasādāditi sarva tra yojyam||
snehād vyādhāstaputrapraṇayamṛtakapotāyito mā sma bhūvaṃ
prāptaṃ prāśnan saheya kṣudhamapi śayuvat sindhuvat syāmagādhaḥ |
mā pataṃ yoṣidādau śikhini śalabhavad bhṛṅgavat sārabhāgī
bhūyāsaṃ kintu tadvad dhanacayanavaśānmāhamīśa! praṇeśam || 5||
snehāditi | snehaḥ santāpaheturiti kapotād grāhyamityāha -- snehāditi | vyādhena lubdhakena asteṣu nigṛhīteṣu putreṣu praṇayena mṛto yaḥ kapotaḥ sa ivācaratīti tathā | kaścid vanecaro nīḍāntike sañcarataḥ pakṣiṇo jālamātatya pratipālayāmāsa | tatra prathamamajānantaḥ kapotaśiśavo jālenābṛtāḥ| tān dṛṣṭvā kapotī ca duḥkhenāpatat | kapotaśca putrabhāryāvilapanaviceṣṭanādidarśanajanitaduḥkhapraṇayakaruṇāndhadhīḥ svayamapi vilapannapāyamagamat | evaṃ mā bhūvamiti bhāvaḥ | ajagarādupadeṣṭavyamāha -- prāptamiti | śayuḥ ajagaraḥ tadvat prāptaṃ prāśnan yathālabdhena śarīranirvāhamātraṃ kurvan kṣudhaṃ saheya soḍhuṃ śakto naśarluṅi puṣāditvādaṅ | 'naśimanyoraliṭyetvamiti bhāṣyavārtikādata etvam | chandograhaṇaṃ tu vākyabhedenāmipacoreva sambadhyate, 'aneśad menakām' ityādilakṣyānurodhāditi vṛttyanusāreṇedam |
bhaveyam | anena ca prārabdhakarmaphalasyāvaśyabhogyatvāt sukhaduḥkhaprāptiparihārārthaḥ prayāso'pi vṛthākālakṣepaikaphala ityapi darśitam | agādho gambhīraḥ evambhūta iti parikalayitumaśakyaḥ | rūpavilāsamohito naśyatīti śalabhācchikṣaṇīyamityāha -- mā paptamiti| yoṣiti pramadāyāṃ hāvabhāvādibhiḥ, ādiśabdāt kaṭakakaṭisūtrahāradukūlādāvatimanohare vastuni paramārthato duḥkhahetāvupabhogyadhiyā mā paptaṃ yathāgnau tadva rṇalobhitaḥ pataṅgaḥ patati tadvat | kiñca, yathā bhṛṅgaḥ puṣpeṣu sārabhūtaṃ madhu bhajati, evaṃ sarvaśāstreṣu sāraṃ śrīnārāyaṇasmaraṇarūpamarthameva bhajeyam | śikṣaṇīyāntaramāha -- kintviti | kintu viśeṣo'sti | tadvad bhṛṅgavat | yathā bhṛṅgo viśiṣṭagandhalobhenaikasminneva padme vasannastamaye mukulite tasmin badhyate, tadvadahamapyarthārjanādyābhiniveśenaikatra sakto na nāśaṃ gaccheyamityarthaḥ || 5 ||
mā badhyāsaṃ taruṇyā gaja iva vaśayā nārjayeyaṃ dhanaughaṃ
hartānyastaṃ hi mādhvīhara iva mṛgavanmā muhaṃ grāmyagītaiḥ |
nātyāsajjeya bhojye jhapa iva baḍiśe piṅgalāvannirāśaḥ
supyāṃ bhartavyayogāt kurara iva vibho! sāmiṣo'nyairna hanyai ||6||
meti | strīṇāmaṅgasaṅgo bandhaheturiti gajācchikṣaṇīyamityāha -- mā badhyāsamiti| vaśayā kariṇyā| gajo hi kariṇīṃ pradarśya nikhātapihitagarte nipātya badhyate | kariṇījaṭhare baddhacarmāntarbhūtapuruṣeṇeti vā | tyāgabhogahīnaṃ dhanaṃ na sañcinuyāditi mādhvīharācchikṣaṇīyamityāha -- nārjayeyamiti | taṃ dhanaugham anyaścorādiḥ hartā hariṣyati | mādhvīharo hi tarukoṭarādiṣu makṣikābhiḥ sañcitaṃ madhu harati tadvat | gītādiśabdasaktirnāśa heturiti mṛgācchikṣaṇīyamityāha -- mṛgavaditi | mṛgo hi mṛgayorgītena mohitastena nigṛhyate, tadvat | grāmyagītairiti | bhagavadgīteṣvevāsaktirmavediti bhāvaḥ | rasāsaktirnāśaheturiti mīnācchikṣaṇīyamityāha -- nātīti | jhaṣo matsyaḥ baḍiśe āmiṣāvṛtalohakaṇṭake | āśā duḥkhahetuḥ nairāśyaṃ sukhaheturiti piṅgalāyāḥ śikṣaṇīyamityāha -- piṅgalāvaditi | piṅgalā nāma svairiṇī arthāśayā
prathamamāgatān svalpārthadān puruṣānanādṛtya apināmānyo bhūriprado māmupeṣyatīti durāśayā dūyamānamanāḥ niśīthe'pi jāgratī punaśca nirvedāsinā dhanādau durāśāṃpāśāṃśchittvā sukhaṃ suṣvāpa | evaṃ nirāśo bhūtvā | supyāmiti sukhaṃ labheyetyeva vivakṣyate | kasyacidapi mamatvena svīkāro duḥkhaheturityatra kuraro nāma pakṣī gururityāha -- bhartavyeti | āmiṣaṃ gṛhītvāpatantaṃ kuraramanye pakṣiṇo jaghnuḥ | tadvad bhartavyayogād mamatvena rakṣaṇīyadravyasambandhād anyaiḥ balibhiḥ na hanyai na hato bhaveyam || 6 ||
varteya tyaktamānaḥ sukhamatiśiśuvannissahāyaścareyaṃ
kanyāyā ekaśeṣo valaya iva vibho! varjitānyonyaghoṣaḥ |
tvaccitto nāvabudhyai paramiṣukṛdiva kṣmābhṛdāyānaghoṣaṃ
geheṣvanyapraṇīteṣvahiriva nivasānyundurormandireṣu || 7 ||
varteyeti | mānāvamānaparityāgaḥ sukhaheturityarbhakācchikṣayeyamityāha -- varteyeti|bahubhiḥ saha sañcāre goṣṭhīkalahādinā śrīharismaraṇakīrtanādau pramādo bhavediti kanyāyāḥ śikṣayeyamityāha -- nissahāya iti | kācit kanyakātmavaraṇāyāgatān puruṣān bhojayituṃ svayaṃ śālyavahananaṃ kurvatī prakoṣṭhasthaśaṅkhavalayasvanajugupsayā śaṅkhavalayamekāvaśiṣṭamakarot | sa iva varjitānyonyaghoṣaḥ tyaktetaretaravyarthapralāpādiḥ | īśvare samādhāvitikartavyatāṃ śarakārācchikṣayeyamityāha -- tvaccitta iti | yatheṣukṛd iṣvṛjūkaraṇe dattacittatayā kṣmābhṛdāyāne bheryādighoṣaṃ na bubudhe, tadvat tvañcitto'hamapi param anyaṃ nāvabudhyai | viṣayavāsanayā mano na vikṣiped, nāpi suṣuptāviva sarvathā līyeteti bhāvaḥ | gṛhārambho'narthāyeti sarpācchikṣayeyāmityāha -- geheṣviti | ahiryathondurormūṣikasya mandireṣu kuhareṣu vasati, sa iva nivasāni || 7 ||
tvayyeva tvatkṛtaṃ tvaṃ kṣapayasi jagadityūrṇanābhāt pratīyāṃ
tvaccintā tvatsvarūpaṃ kuruta iti dṛḍhaṃ śikṣaye peśakārāt |
viḍbhasmātmā ca deho bhavati guruvaro yo vivekaṃ viraktiṃ
dhatte sañcintyamāno mama tu bahurujāpīḍito'yaṃ viśeṣāt ||8||
tvayīti | īśvarasya jagatkāraṇatvamūrṇanābhācchikṣaṇīyamityāha svayīti | tvatkṛtaṃ tvayā nimittopādānakāraṇabhūtena sṛṣṭaṃ tvayyeva kṣapayasi saṃharasi | ūrṇanābhaḥ lūtā| sa hi nimittopādānabhūtena svātmanā sṛṣṭaṃ tantuvitānaṃ svasminneva svayaṃ saṃharati | bhagavaddhyānaṃ tatsārūpyamāpādayatīti peśakārācchikṣayeyamityāha -- tvañcinteti | tvatsvarūpaṃ tvatsārūpyam | dṛḍhaṃ niścitam | peśaḥ kṣudrakuṭī, tāṃ karotīti peśakāro bhramaraviśeṣaḥ | tena kṣudrakuṭīṃ praveśito niruddhaḥ kīṭo bhayena tameva dhyāyan tatsārūpyaṃ gacchati | svaśarīrācchikṣaṇīyamāha -- viḍiti | deho mama guruvaro bhavati| kutaḥ, sañcintyamāno viraktiṃ vivekaṃ ca dhatte karoti | durlabhatvopakāritve cintyamāne ātmānātmavivekahetuḥ anityatvapārakyatvaduḥkhodarkatveṣu cintyamāneṣu viraktihetuśca bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | viraktivivekahetutvamāha -- viḍbhasmātmeti | yadi śvādibhirbhakṣitaḥ, tarhi viṣṭhātmā bhavati | yadyagninā dagdhaḥ, tarhi bhasmātmā bhavati | ataḥ parakīyo'nityaścāyam | ātmā tu sañcidānandasvarūpastadvyatirikta iti bhāvaḥ | kiñca, mama tu ayaṃ dehaḥ bahurujāpīḍitaḥ vividhābhirvedanābhiḥ paravaśīkṛtaḥ san viśeṣād vivekaṃ viraktiṃ ca dhatta iti pūrveṇānvayaḥ || 8 ||
nanu vivekahetutvenopakāriṇi dehe mohaḥ kathaṃ na sambhavedityāśaṅkaya dehamohe doṣamāviṣkurvan bhagavantaṃ prārthayate -- 
hī hī me dehamohaṃ tyaja pavanapurādhīśa! yatpremahetorgehe vitte kalatrādiṣu ca vivaśitāstvatpadaṃ vismaranti |
so'yaṃ vahneḥ śuno vā paramiha parataḥ sāmprataṃ cākṣikarṇatvagjihvādyā vikarṣantyavaśamataitaḥ ko'pi na tvatpadābje || 9 ||
hī hīti | hī hī evambhūte'pi dehe mama mahān moha ityetadāścaryam | taṃ dehamohaṃ dehaviṣayasukhasādhanatvabhrāntiṃ tyaja | tatra doṣamāha -- yatpremahetoriti yasmin dehe premṇā hetunā gehādiṣu vivaśitāḥ tadyogakṣemaikacittāḥ | kiñca, so 'yaṃ dehaḥ iha sakalajantuṣvapi parataḥ avasāne paraṃ kevalaṃ vahnerdahanāya śuno bhakṣaṇāya vā bhavati | sāmprataṃ ca jīvatyapi sati | akṣikarṇatvagjihvādyā iti tadadhiṣṭhātāro'rkādidevā gṛhyante, akṣyādīnāṃ tadadhīnapravṛttitvāt | ādiśabdena vākūpāṇyādi gṛhyate | enam ataitaḥ svasvaviṣayeṣu rūpādiṣu karṣanti | eṣu ko'pi tvatpadābje bhagavatpādāravindaviṣaye na karṣati || 9 ||
nanu prārabdhakarmaphalasyāvaśyaṃ bhogyatvāt tadbhogasya ca dehagehāsaktyāyattatvāt tattyāgo'śakya iti cet tarhi prakārāntareṇa mama janmasāphalyaṃ kurviti prārthayate -- 
durvāro dehamoho yadi punaradhunā tarhi niśśeṣarogān
haṭtvā bhaktiṃ draḍhiṣṭhāṃ kuru tava padapaṅkeruhe paṅkajākṣa ! |
nūnaṃ nānābhavānte samadhigatamimaṃ muktidaṃ vipradehaṃ
kṣudre hā hanta mā mā kṣipa viṣayarase pāhi māṃ māruteśa ! || 10 ||
durvāra iti | yadi punardehamoho durvāraḥ, tarhi adhunā asmin janmanyeva niśśeṣān bāhyān vātādirogān āntarān rāgādirogān | draḍhaṣṭhim anapāyinīm | atidurlabhasyā sya vipradehasya saphalīkaraṇena māṃ pāhītyāha -- nūnamiti || 10 ||
iti pañcaviṃśatergurubhyaḥ śikṣaṇīyasya varṇanaṃ trinavatitamaṃ daśakam |
evaṃ pañcaviṃśatyā gurubhirutpannavivekajñānasya tattvajñānotpattiprakāraṃ darśayati -- 
śuddhā niṣkāmadharmaiḥ pravaragurugirā tat svarūpaṃ paraṃ te
śuddhaṃ dehendriyādivyapagatamakhilaṃ vyāptamāvedayante |
nānātvasthaulyakārśyādi tu guṇajavapussaṅgato'dhyāsitaṃ te
vahnerdāruprabhedeṣviva mahadaṇutādīptatāśāntatādi ||1||
śuddhā iti | niṣkāmadharmaiḥ phalakāmanāmantareṇānuṣṭhitairvarṇāśramadharmaiḥ śuddhāḥ rāgādidoṣarahitāḥ, śamadamādisādhanacatuṣṭayasampannā iti yāvat | pravarasya vedatadarthaniṣṇātatayā śreṣṭhasya guroḥ ajñānanirodhanacaturasya girā pravacanātmikayā te taveśvarasya tad niṣkalasvarūpam āvedayante avagacchanti | kīdṛśaṃ, dehendriyādibhyo vyapagataṃ vyatiriktam ata eva śuddhaṃ māyātatkāryoparāgarahitaṃ paraṃ parabrahmākhyam akhilaṃ vyāptaṃ sarvāśrayaṃ sarvānusyūtaṃ ca | nanvakhilavyāptatvaṃ kṛśo'haṃ sthūlo'hamityādipratīteḥ pratītiviruddhamityāśaṅkacāha -- nānātveti | tavātmano nānātvādi tu guṇajavapussaṅgataḥ māyāmayaśarīropādhitvena adhyāsitaṃ kalpitam | ata aupādhikatvena nānātvādayo nātmadharmā ityarthaḥ | atrānurūpaṃ dṛṣṭāntamāha -- vahneriti| dāruprabhedeṣu kāṣṭheṣu sthitasya vaheryathā mahadaṇutādi | mahattvamaṇutvaṃ dīptatvamutpannatvaṃ śāntatvaṃ naṣṭatvam | ādiśabdena nānātvādi gṛhyate | ete dārudharmā api yathāgnerdāruṇo'viviktatvādagnidharmatayā pratīyante, tathātmanaḥ śarīrasyāpi vivicya jñānābhāvānnānātvādayo jarāmaraṇādayo'pyātmadharmā iva pratīyante na paramārthata iti bhāvaḥ || 1 ||
etacca jñānamācāryāllubdhamevājñānatatkāryanirasanakṣamamiti sphuṭīkartuṃ vidyotpattiṃ rūpakeṇa nirūpayati -- 
ācāryākhyādharasthāraṇisamanumilacchiṣyarūpottarāraṇyāvedhodbhāsitena sphuṭataraparibodhāgninā dahyamāne |
karmālīvāsanātatkṛtatanubhuvanabhrāntikāntārapūre
dāhyābhāvena vidyāśikhini ca virate tvanmayī khalvavasthā || 2 ||
ācāryākhyeti | ācāryākhyo yo'varasthārāṇaḥ adhogatamathanakāṣṭhaviśeṣaḥ, samanumilan upanayanadvārā ācāryasannidhiṃ prāpto yaḥ śiṣyaḥ, tadrūpo ya uttarāraṇiḥ, tayorāvedhaḥ pravacanarūpeṇa mathanadaṇḍena saṃghaṭṭanaṃ, tenodbhāsitena prakāśitena sphuṭataraparibodhaḥ samyagjñānaṃ tadrūpeṇāgninā karmālīvāsanāḥ sajātīyakarmotpādakāḥ saṃskārāḥ, tatkṛtā tanubhuvanabhrāntiḥ śarīrādiprapañcaviṣayamajñānaṃ, tadrūpe kāntārapūre vanasamūhe dahyamāne sati dāhyābhāvena indhananāśena vidyā brahmajñānaṃ, taccājñānatatkāryanāśe sati svayamapi tadantaḥpātitayā naśyatītyagnisāmyāt, tadrūpe śikhini agnau ca virate kāryakāraṇāvyavadhānāt tvanmayī khalvavasthā tvadrūpeṇaivāvasthānam| saccidānandarūpo bhavatīti bhāvaḥ || 2||
evaṃ kṣlokadvayena sarvato viraktasya tattvajñānena saṃsāraduḥkhocchedaḥ paramānandāvāptiścetyuktam | tadeva dṛḍhīkartu matāntaraṃ nirasyati
evaṃ tvatprāptito'nyo nahi khalu nikhilakleśahānerupāyo
naikāntātyantikāste kṛṣivadagadaṣāḍguṇyaṣaṭkarmayogāḥ |
durvaikalyairakalyā api nigamapathāstatphalānyapyavāptā
matāstvāṃ vismarantaḥ prasajati patane yāntyanantān viṣādān || 3 ||
evamiti | nanu nānāvidhairmaṇimantrauṣadharasāyanaprayogaiḥ ṣaṇṇayaiścaturupāyaiḥ svargādīṣṭasādhanairjyotiṣṭomādibhiradhyāpanādibhiśca karmabhireva dehināṃ nikhilakleśahāneḥ sambhavāt kimarthamatiduṣkaraṃ nivṛttimārgāśrayaṇamityāśaṅkāyāmāha -- naikānteti | agadāḥ auṣadhānyāyurvedoktā ārogyāghupāyāḥ, ṣaḍguṇā eva ṣāḍguṇyaṃ daṇḍanītyuktāḥ sandhivigrahādayo'rthārjanopāyāḥ, ṣaṭkarmāṇi adhyayanādhyāpanādīni, vedoktā yogā upāyāḥ ye, te naikāntāḥ na kṛtsnaduḥkhanivartakāḥ duḥkhamiśrasukhahetutvād, na cātyantikāḥ na duḥkhapunarāvṛttinivartakāḥ anityaphalatvāt, kṛṣivat| nanviha putramitradhanadhānyārogyādiparipanthinirāsaphalakāni paratra svargādisādhanānyapi vaidikakarmāṇi vidyante, tatrāha -- durvaikalyairiti | nigamapathāḥ vedamārgāḥ durvaikalyaiḥ apariharaṇīyairdravyakarmakālakartṛdoṣarūpairvaiguṇyaiḥ akalyā aśakyānuṣṭhānā api kevalaṃ naikāntātyantikā evetyapiśabdārthaḥ | kiñca kathañcinnirvaikalyāstatphalāni
svargādiphalāni prāptāḥ mattāḥ vimāne strībhiḥ saha nandanādiṣu krīḍantaḥ pramattāḥ karmakṣayeṇa patane adhaḥpatane prasajati sati anantān viṣādān duḥkhāni yānti || 3 ||
nanu vedoktopāsanādikarmabhiḥ satyalokaṃ prāptāḥ kleśarahitā brahmaṇā saha modanta ityāśaṅkāyāmāha -- 
tvallokādanyalokaḥ ka nu bhayarahito yat parārdhadvayānte
tvadbhītaḥ satyaloke'pi na sukhavasatiḥ padmabhūḥ padmanābha ! |
evambhāve tvadharmārjitabahutamasāṃ kā kathā nārakāṇāṃ
tanme tvaṃ chindhi bandhaṃ varada ! kṛpaṇabandho ! kṛpāpūrasindho ! || 4 ||
tvallokāditi | tvallokād baikuṇṭhād anyalokaḥ satyalokādiḥ bhayarahito vināśabhayarahitaḥ kva nu nāstītyarthaḥ | atra hetuḥ -- parārdhadvayam anto'vadhiryasya sa tathā tādṛśe satyaloke sa padmabhūrapi tvat kālasvarūpādīśvarād bhī taḥ ato na sukhavasatiḥ sukharūpā vināśabhayarahitā vasatirvāso yasya sa tathā | padmabhūriti brahmaṇa utpattivināśau dyotayati | evambhāve evaṃ sati vidyākarmabhyāṃ prāśvaryasya brahmaṇo'pi duḥkhātyantikanivṛttyabhāve sati adharmairārjita bahutamo duritaṃ yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ nārakāṇāṃ narakapatitānāṃ saṃsāriṇāṃ kleśahāne kā kathā | tat tasmād bandhaṃ śarīrādyahammamābhimānaṃ chindhi, mokṣaṃ prayacchetyarthaḥ || 4 ||
nanu jīvo nāma neśvarādanyaḥ | kutastasya bandhamokṣāvityāśaṅkaya pariharati -- 
yāthārthyāt tvanmayasyaiva hi mama na vibho ! vastuto bandhamokṣau
māyāvidyātanubhyāṃ tava tu viracitau svapnabodhopamau tau |
baddhe jīvadvimuktiṃ gatavati ca bhidā tāvatī tāvadeko
bhuṅke dehadrumastho viṣayaphalarasān nāparo nirvyathātmā ||5||
yāthārthyāditi| paramārthatastvanmayasya īśvarādabhinnasyaiva mama jīvasya na vastuto bandhamokṣau, tathāpi tava īśvarasya māthāvidyātanubhyāṃ vidyāvidyārūpaśaktidvayena viracitau tau bandhamokṣau | avidyayā bandho vidyayā ca mokṣa iti bhāvaḥ | tau ca svapnabodhopamau yathā svapnadraṣṭuḥ svapnadṛṣṭena dehena bandhaḥ prabudhya ca tanmokṣaḥ, tadvad aviduṣo dehabandhaḥ viduṣo mokṣaśca | tathāca svapnavanmāyāmayatve'pi bandhasya duḥkhahetutvād jñānena bandhacchedaḥ prārthanīya iti bhāvaḥ | tadeva baddhamuktayorvailakṣaṇyamāha -- baddha iti | baddhe saṃsāriṇi jīvadvimuktiṃ gatavati jīvanmukte ca tāvat tāvatī vakṣyamāṇarūpā | eko baddhaḥ deha eva drumaḥ, drumavadanityatvādidharmatvāt, tatsthaḥ tasmādapṛthagbhūtaḥ san viṣayarasaphalaṃ viṣayānubhavarūpaṃ karmaphalaṃ bhuṅkte | aparaḥ jīvanmuktaḥ na viṣayarasaphalaṃ bhuṅktte | tathāpi jñānaśaktayā nirvyathātmā pīḍārahitaḥ || 5 ||
nanu tarhi jīvanmuktatvameva prārthyatāmityāśaṅkaca tadanadhikāramākalayya bhakti prārthayate -- 
jīvanmukttatvāmiti vacasā kiṃ phalaṃ, na dūradūre
tannāmāśuddhabuddherna ca laghu manasaḥ śodhanaṃ bhaktito'nyat |
tanme viṣṇo ! kṛṣīṣṭhāstvayi kṛtasaṃkalaprārpaṇaṃ bhaktibhāraṃ
yena syāṃ maṅkṣu kiñcidguruvacana milatattvatprabodhastvadātmā || 6 ||
jīvanmuktatvamiti | vacasā kiṃ phalaṃ, na tatprāptiḥ syāt | aśuddhabuddheḥ rāgādiduṣṭāntaḥkaraṇasya tad jīvanmuktitvaṃ dūre nāma | tarhyantaḥkaraṇaśudhghapāyā anveṣṭavyā ityāśaṅkaya kimupāyāntareṇa bhaktireva laghurupāya ityāha -- na ceti | na ca bhaktito'nyad manasaḥ śodhanaṃ śuddhayupāyaḥ tīrthasnānādi laghu atiśīghram aprayatnaṃ ca bhavati | tat tasmāt tvayi bhaktibhāram anapāyinīṃ bhakti kṛṣīṣṭhāḥ | kṛtam anuṣṭhitaṃ sakalānāṃ śarīratatsambandhināṃ prārpaṇaṃ mantrapurassara samarpaṇaṃ yasmin bhaktibhāre | kiñcidguruvacaneti bhaktau satyāṃ svayameva jñānotpattiriti darśayati| maṅkṣu śīghraṃ guruvacanamātranimittena milatā sañjātena tvatprabodhena brahmajñānena tvadātmā mukto bhavāmītyarthaḥ || 6 ||
abhaktānāṃ śāstraśramo'pi pāṇḍityamātraphala ityāha -- 
śabdabrahmaṇyapīha prayatitamanasastvāṃ na jānanti kecit
kaṣṭaṃ vandhyaśramāste cirataramiha gāṃ vibhrate niṣpratim |
yasyāṃ viśvābhirāmāḥ sakalamalaharā divyalīlāvatārāḥ
saccitsāndraṃ ca rūpaṃ tava na nigaditaṃ tāṃ na vācaṃ bhriyāsam || 7 ||
śabdabrahmaṇīti | mīmāṃsādhupāṅgopabṛṃhite sājhe vede prayatitamanasaḥ kṛtaśramā api iha kecit tatpradhānapratipādyaṃ tvāṃ paraṃ brahma na jānanti nopāsanādi kurvanti | kaṣṭam | te vandhyaśramāḥ vyarthapariśramāḥ | te cirataraṃ dīrghakālaṃ niṣprasūtiṃ bhaktijñānaphalotpādanarahitāṃ gāṃ vācaṃ jalpavitaṇḍākhyāṃ bibhrate| athavā prasavahīnatayā nirdohāṃ gāṃ dhenuṃ bibhrate puṣṇanti | ahaṃ tu yasyāṃ vāci viśvān sarvajanān abhiramayantīti viśvābhirāmāḥ sakalānāṃ malānāṃ duritānāṃ rāgādīnāṃ ca nivartakāḥ divyāḥ śuddhasattvamayāḥ śrīrāmakṛṣṇādyavatārā na nigaditāḥ, saccitsāndraṃ rūpaṃ paraṃ brahma na nigaditaṃ na pratipāditaṃ, tāṃ saguṇanirguṇeśvarapratipādanavidhurāṃ vācaṃ na bhriyāsaṃ na vyavahareyam || 7 ||
atha satsaṅgamadvārā bhakti prārthayate tribhiḥ -- 
yo yāvān yādṛśo vā tvamiti kimapi naivāvagacchāmi bhūmannevañcānanyabhāvastvadanubhajanamevādriye caidyavairin ! |
tvalliṅgānāṃ tvadaṅghripriyajanasadasāṃ darśanasparśanādirbhūyānme tvatprapūjānatinutiguṇakarmānukīrtyādaro'pi || 8 ||
ya iti | yaḥ yatsvarūpaḥ yāvān yāhaṅmahimā yādṛśaḥ yaddharmo vā tvamiti kimapi naivāvagacchāmi | he bhūman ! īśvara ! evañca sati ananyabhāvaḥ tvadekacittaḥ san tvadanubhajanaṃ tvatsevāmevādriye | kathamajñātvā madbhajanamityata āha -- caidyavairinniti | śiśupālavairin ! gopālabālakatayā śrutatvādetāvadavagacchāmīti bhāvaḥ | tvalliṅgānāṃ tvatpratimānāṃ tvadaṅghripriyā bhaktajanāḥ, teṣāṃ sadasāṃ sabhānāma | darśanasparśanādiriti namaskārādi gṛhyate | yadvā satsaṃṅga viśeṣaṇatvāt pum̐lliṅgatvam | prakṛṣṭā samagropacārayuktā pūjā prapūjā, natirnamaskāraḥ, nutiḥ stavanaṃ, guṇaiḥ sattvādibhiḥ kṛtānāṃ sṛṣṭyādikarmaṇāmanukīrtiḥ, āsu ādaro'pi me bhūyāt ||8||
yadyallabhyeta tattat tava samupahṛtaṃ deva ! dāso'smi te'haṃ
tvadnehonmārjanādyaṃ bhavatu mama muhuḥ karma nirmāyameva |
sūryāgnibrāhmaṇātmādiṣu lasitacaturbāhumārādhaye tvāṃ
tvatpremārdratvarūpo mama satatamabhiṣyandatāṃ bhaktiyogaḥ || 9||
yadyaditi | samupahṛtaṃ samarpitam | tvadveheṣu kṣetrādiṣu unmārjanaṃ rajaso'pākaraṇam ādyaṃ yasya secanapuṣpopahārādeḥ nirmāyaṃ dāmbhikatārahitaṃ karma mama muhurbhavatu | pūjādhiṣṭhānānyāha -- sūryeti | ādiśabdena gavādi gṛhyate | eṣvadhiṣṭhāneṣu lasitacaturbāhumārādhaye saurasūktahavirātithyaviṣayabhogayavasādibhiḥ pūjayāmi | tvatpremṇā ārdrā drutahṛdayaḥ, tasya bhāvastattvaṃ, tadrūpo bhaktiyogo mama satatamabhiṣyandatāṃ tvadviṣaye pravāharūpeṇa pravartatāmityarthaḥ || 9 ||
aikyaṃ te dānahomavrataniyamatapassāṅkhyayogairdurāpaṃ
tvatsaṅgenaiva gopyaḥ kila sukṛtitamāḥ prāpurānandasāndram |
bhakteṣvanyeṣu bhūyassvapi bahumanuṣe bhaktimeva tvamāsāṃ
tanme tvadbhaktimeva draḍhaya hara gadān kṛṣṇa! vātālayeśa! ||10||
aikyamiti | dānādibhiryogināmapi durāpaṃ te tava aikyaṃ mokṣaṃ gopyastvatsaṅgenaiva prāpuḥ kila | atastāḥ sukṛtitamāḥ | ānandasāndram ānandaikarasam | anyeṣu bhūyassu bahuṣvapi bhakteṣu uddhavādiṣu satsvapi tvam āsāṃ gopikānāṃ bhaktimeva bahumanuṣe | tat tasmād me tvayi premalakṣaṇāṃ bhaktibheva draḍhaya anapāyinīṃ kuru || 10 ||
iti tattvajñānotpattiprakāravarṇanaṃ, bandhamokṣasvarūpavarṇanam, abhaktanindāpūrvakaṃ bhaktiprārthanāvarṇanaṃ ca
caturnavatitamaṃ daśakam |
nanu bhaktyā jīveśvaraikye sati saṃsāranivṛttirityuktam | tadanupapannaṃ, tayorbhinnatve satyaikyāyogād, abhinnatve tayārjīvasya saṃsārānupapatterityāśaṅkaca īśvarasyaiva māyāmayaśarīrasambandhāt saṃsāritvaṃ tadapāye svasvarūpeṇāvasthānaṃ ca bhavatīti pariharati -- 
ādī hairaṇyagarbhī tanumavikalajīvātmikāmāsthitastvaṃ
jīvatvaṃ prāpya māyāguṇagaṇakhacito vartase viśvayone! |
tatrodvṛddhena sattvena tu guṇayugalaṃ bhaktibhāvaṃ gatenacchitvā sattvaṃ ca hitvā punaranupahito vartitāhe tvameva || 1 ||
ādāviti | ādau brahmapralayāvasāne tava sisṛkṣāyāṃ satyāṃ tvaṃ hiraṇyagarbhasyeyaṃ hairaṇyagarbhī, tāṃ tanumāsthitaḥ tadabhimānyabhūḥ | kīdṛśīm, avikalajīvātmikāṃ | atra jīvaśabdena sūkṣmaśarīramucyate | sūkṣmaśarīrasamaṣṭirūpāmityarthaḥ | kimarthamityapekṣāyāṃ taddhetutvena sambodhayati | viśvayone! iti | viśvasya virāṭccharīrasya prapañcasya yoniḥ kāraṇam | sūkṣmaśarīrāddhi sthūlaśarīrotpattiḥ| evañca jagatsṛṣṭyarthaṃ hiraṇyagarbho brahmābhūriti bhāvaḥ | evambhūtastvameva tathyaṣṭirūpaṃ jīvatvaṃ prāpya māyāguṇānāṃ māyākāryāṇāṃ mahadahaṅkārabhūtendriyaprāṇapañcakarūpāṇāṃ tattvānāṃ gaṇena, tatsaṅghātātmakena śarīreṇetyarthaḥ, khacitaḥ sambaddho vartase tvameva śarīrābhimānitayā saṃsārī bhavasi | ato mama tvadrūpatvāt tvadaikyam yujyata iti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ saṃsāriṇo jīvasya rajastamovṛddhau saṃsārānivṛttiḥ | sattvavṛddhau viśeṣamāha -- tatra tviti | tatra māyāguṇeṣu tu sattvavṛddhau viśeṣo'stītyarthaḥ| sāttvikapadārthasevayā uddddhena ata eva bhaktibhāvaṃ gatena bhaktirūpaṇe pariṇatenetyarthaḥ | guṇayugalaṃ rajastamaśca tatkāryabhūtaṃ śarīrādikaṃ ca chitvā māyāmayatvena jñānamutpādyāpākṛtya kṛtakāryatayā sādhanarūpaṃ sattvaṃ ca hitvā punaḥ kāryakāraṇāvyavadhānād anupahitaḥ paripūrṇastvamevāhaṃ vartitāhe tvadekībhāvamanubhavitāsmīti bhāvaḥ || 1 || nanu tarhi sattvavṛddhireva prārthyatāṃ, kiṃ bhaktyetyāśaṅkāyāmāha -- 
sattvonmeṣāt kadācit khalu viṣayarase doṣabodhe'pi bhūman !
bhūyo'pyeṣu pravṛttiḥ satamasi rajasi proddhate durnivārā |
cittaṃ tāvad guṇākṣca grathitamiha mithastāni sarvāṇi roddhuṃ
turye tvayyekabhaktiḥ śaraṇamiti bhavān haṃsarūpī nyagādīt || 2 ||
sattvonmeṣāditi | sattvasya unmeṣo vṛddhiḥ, tataḥ khalu viṣayarase viṣayāsvāde doṣabodhe satyapi bhūyo'pyeṣu viṣayeṣu pravṛttirdurnivārā bhavati | tatra hetuḥ -- satamasi rajasi proddhata iti | rajastamobhyāṃ sattvasyābhibhavāditi bhāvaḥ | yāvadevaṃ, tāvad guṇā viṣayāścittaṃ ca mitho grathitaṃ bhavati | cittaṃ hi rāgādivaśād viṣayeṣu praviśati | te cānubhūtā viṣayā vāsanārūpeṇa citte ca praviśanti | tato mitho grathitaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | tāni sarvāṇi guṇacittarajastamāṃsi roddhuṃ tyaktuṃ turye avasthātrayātīte tatsākṣiṇi tvāye īśvare bhaktireva śaraṇam | haṃsarūpīti | kṣīranīravivekacaturo haṃsaḥ, tadvadātmānātmavivekacaturatayā paramahaṃsaḥ parivrāṭ, tadrūpī bhūtvā bhavān brahmaṇaḥ purataḥ sanakādibhyo nyagādīd upadideśa ||
nanu kiṃ bhaktitulyaṃ śreyassādhanamasti, netyāha -- 
santi śreyāṃsi bhūyāṃsyapi rucibhidayā karmiṇāṃ nirmitāni
kṣudrānandākṣca sāntā bahuvidhagatayaḥ kṛṣṇa! tebhyo bhaveyuḥ |
tvaṃ cācakhyātha sakhye nanu mahitatamāṃ śreyasāṃ bhaktimekāṃ
tvadbhaktyānandatulyaḥ khalu viṣayajuṣāṃ sammadaḥ kena vā syāt || 3 ||
santīti | śreyāṃsi śreyassādhanāni karmiṇāṃ rāgādinā karmādhikāriṇāṃ rucibhidayā adhikārabhedena nirmitāni bṛhaspativyāsapatañjaliprabhṛtibhirācāryaiḥ proktāni | tebhyaḥ śreyassādhanebhyaḥ bahuvidhāḥ svargādirūpāḥ gatayaḥ phalāni bhaveyuḥ| kintu tāḥ kṣudrānandāḥ duḥkhamiśratayā svalpasukhāḥ sāntāḥ
anityāśca | tvaṃ tu śreyasāṃ madhye bhaktim ekāṃ kevalām avyabhicariṇīṃ vā mahitatamām atiśayena pūjanīyāṃ sakhye uddhavāya ācakhyātha ākhyātavān | viṣayajuṣām indriyāṇi prīṇayatāṃ tvadbhaktyā ya ānandamtattulyaḥ sammadaḥ sukhaṃ kena vā syād, na kenāpītyarthaḥ || 3 ||
tadevāha -- 
tvadbhaktyā tuṣṭabuddheḥ sukhamiha carato vicyutāśasya cāśāḥ
sarvāḥ syuḥ saukhyamayyaḥ salilakuharagasyeva toyaikamayyaḥ |
so'yaṃ khalvindralokaṃ kamalajabhavanaṃ yogasiddhīkṣca hṛdyā
nākāṅkṣatyetadāstāṃ svayamanupatite mokṣasaukhye'pyanīhaḥ || 4 ||
tvadbhaktyeti | tvadbhaktyā premalakṣaṇayā tuṣṭabuddheḥ bhaktyaivālaṃ kimanyairiti jātālaṃbuddheḥ ata eva vicyutāśasya viṣayatṛṣṇārahitasya ata eva iha sarvatra sukhaṃ carataḥ āśāḥ diśaḥ sarvāḥ saukhyamayyaḥ syuḥ | anubhūyamānaṃ sarva sukhātmakameveti bhāvaḥ | yathā salilakuharaṃ mahāhūdaḥ, tadgasya matsyādeḥ sarvā diśastoyaikamayyo bhavanti, evaṃ tvadbhaktyānandanimagnasya bhaktasyāpi sarvā diśaḥ saukhyamayya evetyarthaḥ | kiñca, so'yaṃ bhaktaḥ khalu indrādilokaṃ hṛdyā manoharāḥ yogasiddhīḥ upāsanaphalabhūtāḥ parakāyapraveśāṣṭaiśvaryadūraśravaṇadarśanādyāḥ nākāṅkṣatītyetadāstām, asau mokṣasaukhye'pyanīho nirāgrahaḥ | kiṃ tarhi duṣkaratādṛṣṭeḥ, na | svayamanupatite bhaktimahimnā svayameva prādurbhūte || 4 ||
āstāntāvaduttamabhaktakathā yataḥ prākṛtā api bhaktāḥ kṛtārthā ityāha -- 
tvadbhakto bādhyamāno'pi ca viṣayarasairindriyāśāntihetorbhaktyaivākramyamāṇaiḥ punarapi khalu tairdurbalairnābhijayyaḥ |
saptārcidardīpitārcirdahati kila yathā bhūridārumapañcaṃ
tvadbhaktyoghe tathaiva pradahati duritaṃ durmadaḥ kendriyāṇām || 5 ||
tvadbhakta iti | indriyāśānteḥ avaśīkṛtendriyatvāddhetoḥ viṣayarasairvādhyamānaḥ ākṛṣyamāṇo'pi tairnābhijayyaḥ na vaśīkartuṃ śakyaḥ | atra hetuḥ -- durbalairiti |
tatra hetuḥ -- bhaktyā balavatyā ākramyamāṇaiḥ abhibhūyamānairiti | punarapi bhaktau jātāyāmityarthaḥ | yathā saptārciḥ agniḥ bhūridārūprapañcaṃ mahāntaṃ kāṣṭhasañcayaṃ dahati, tathaiva tvadbhakteroghe pravāhe duritaṃ dahati sati bhakteḥ purataḥ indriyāṇāṃ kka durmado garvaḥ | anenendriyāṇāṃ bhaktiprātikūlyāśakterduritasya mūlacchedo darśitaḥ || 5||
bhaktyabhāve'nyat sādhanaṃ vyarthamityāha -- 
cittādrīṃbhāvamuccairvapuṣi ca pulakaṃ harṣavāṣpaṃ ca hitvā
cittaṃ śudhyet kathaṃ vā kimu bahutapasā vidyayā vītabhakteḥ |
tvadgāthāsvādasiddhāñjanasatatamarīsṛjyamāno'yamātmā
cakṣurvat tattvasūkṣmaṃ bhajati na tu tathābhyastayā tarkakoṭyā || 6 ||
cittārdrībhāvamiti | cittārdrībhāvādi hitvā sphuṭapratīyamānaiścidairgamyamānayā bhaktyā vinā kathaṃ cittaṃ śudhyedityarthaḥ | nanu brahmajñānādevājñānakalpitasaṃsāranivṛttistatsvarūpāvāptiśca, kimatra bhaktiḥ karotītyāśaṅkāyāmāha -- tvadgātheti | tvatpuṇyagāthānāmāsvādaḥ śravaṇaṃ kathanaṃ vā, tadeva siddhāñjanaṃ, tena satatamarībhṛjyamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ śodhyamānaḥ ayamātmā ciṃttaṃ tattvam anāropitaṃ yat sūkṣmaṃ vastu paraṃ tattvaṃ, tad bhajati viṣayīkaroti | abhyastayā āvartitayā tarkakoṭyā yuktisamūhena tathā na bhajati | bhakteravāntaravyāpāra eva jñānam | atastatprayāso vyartha eveti bhāvaḥ || 6 ||
evaṃ śuddhacittasya dhyānayogyatāmāha -- 
dhyānaṃ te śīlayeyaṃ samatanusukhabaddhāsano nāsikāgranyastākṣaḥ pūrakādyairjitapavanapathaścittapadmaṃ tvavāñcam |
ūrdhvāgraṃ āvayitvā ravividhuśikhinaḥ saṃvicintyopariṣṭāt
tatrasthaṃ bhāvaye tvāṃ sajalajaladharaśyāmalaṃ komalāṅgam || 7 ||
dhyānamiti | samatanuḥ samakāyaśirogrīvaḥ sukhabaddhāsanaḥ | nāsti svastikādiniyamaḥ | akṣgornimīlane manolayaḥ, unmīlane manovikṣepaḥ, tadubhayābhāvāya mukulīkaraṇe'kṣṇornāsikāgranyastatā svayameva syāt | pūrakarecakakumbhakabhedaiḥ prāṇāyāmaiḥ jitaḥ kaphādidoṣanirasanena śodhitaḥ pavanapathaḥ vāyusañcaraṇasthānaṃ nāḍīvivaraṃ yena sa tathā cittapadmaṃ hṛdayāmbujam avāñcam adhomukham ūrdhvāgraṃ bhāvayitvā tatkarṇikāyāṃ raviśaśiśikhinaḥ upariṣṭād uttarottaraṃ saṃvicintya tatrasthaṃ vahnimadhyastham || 7 ||
ānīlakṣlakṣṇakeśaṃ jvalitamakarasatkuṇḍalaṃ mandahāsasyandāīṃ kaustubhaśrīparigatavanamāloruhārābhirāmam |
śrīvatsā subāhuṃ mṛdulasadudaraṃ kāñcanacchāyacelaṃ
cārusnigdhorumambhoruhalalitapaḍhabhāvayeyaṃ bhavantam || 8 ||
ānīleti | atiśayena nīlāḥ kṣlakṣṇāḥ kṣliṣṭāḥ keśā yasya, jvalite makarākāre satī kuṇḍale yasya, mandahāsasya sya do dravaḥ tena ārdraṃ tnigdhaṃ, kaustubhaśrībhiḥ parigatayā vyāptathā vanamālayā uruhāraiḥ hārapaṭalaiḥ abhirāmaṃ, vakṣodakṣiṇabhāge śrīvatsākhyo'ṅko yasya, śobhanā dīrghapīvarā bāhavo yasya, mṛdu lasat śobhamānamudaraṃ yasya, kāñcanacchāyacelaṃ parihitapītāmbaraṃ, cārū snigdhau māṃsalāvūrū yasya, taṃ bhavantaṃ bhāvaye cintayāmi || 8 ||
atha kramāt samādhibhūmikārohaṇaprakāramāha -- 
sarvāṅgeṣvaṅga ! raṅgatkutuka matimuhurdhārayannīśa ! cittaṃ
tatrāpyekatra yuñje vadanasarasije sundare mandahāse |
tatrālīnaṃ tu cetaḥ paramasukhacidadvaitarūpe vitantrannanyanno cintayeyaṃ muhuriti samupārūḍhayogo bhaveyam || 9 ||
sarvāṅgeṣviti | raṅgatkutukaṃ prāptakautukaṃ cittaṃ sarvāṅgeṣu atimuhuḥ punaḥ punaḥ dhārayan tatra avayaveṣvapi madhye ekatra ekasminnavayave yuñje | tat kutra, vadanasarasije | mandahāsa iti vadanasarasijaviśeṣaṇam | mandaḥ hāso yasmin tatra śrīmukhe ālīnaṃ susthiraṃ tu cittaṃ paramasukhacidadvaitarūpe saccidānandasvarūpe brahmaṇi vitanvan brahmākāraṃ kurvan anyad dhyātṛdhyeyavibhāgādi no cintayeyam | muhuḥ yāvajjīvaṃ brahmaikyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ iti anena prakāreṇa samupārūḍhayogaḥ bhagavadupāsanaśīlaḥ bhaveyam || 9||
itthaṃ tvaddhyānayoge sati punaraṇimādyaṣṭasaṃsiddhayastā
dūraśrutyādayo'pi hyahamahamikayā sampateyurmurāre ! |
tvatsamprāptau vilambāvahamakhilamidaṃ nādriye kāmaye'haṃ
tvāmevānandapūrṇa pavanapurapate ! pāhi māṃ sarvatāpāt || 10 ||
itthamiti | ittham uktaprakāreṇa tvaddhyānameva yogaḥ puruṣārthasyopāyaḥ tasmin sati tāḥ sattvotkarṣajāḥ aṣṭau saṃsiddhayaḥ aṭaiśvaryāṇi dūraśravaṇadarśanatrikālajñatvādikṣudrasiddhyo'pi ahamahamikayā sampateyuḥ | idamakhilaṃ tvatsamprāptau vilambāvahamityahaṃ nādriye | kiṃ tu aham ānandapūrṇa mokṣarūpaṃ tvāmeva kāmaye | māṃ sarvatāpāt saṃsārāt pāhi | punarapyaṣṭaiśvaryādibhiḥ saṃsārārṇave nikṣipya mā māṃ bhrāmayeti bhāvaḥ || 10 ||
iti bhaktyā viśuddhacittasyaiva bhagavatsvarūpadhyānayogyatāvarṇanaṃ
paścanavatitamaṃ daśakam |
athoktadhyānayogyatāsiddhyai viśvamūrtīśopāsanāya bhagavadvibhūti nirūpayati -- 
tvaṃ hi brahmaiva sākṣāt paramurumahimannakṣarāṇāmakārastāro mantreṣu rājñāṃ manurasi muniṣu tvaṃ bhṛgurnārado'pi |
prahlādo dānavānāṃ paśuṣu ca surabhiḥ pakṣiṇāṃ vainateyo
nāgānāmasyanantaḥ surasaridapi ca srotasāṃ viśvamūrte ! || 1 ||
tvaṃ hīti | he urumahiman ! viśvamūrte ! tvaṃ hi sākṣāt paraṃ brahmaiva | sarvatra ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyau nirdhāraṇe | akṣarāṇāṃ madhye'kāraḥ, mantreṣu tāraḥ praṇavaḥ, tvamiti yojanīyam | muniṣviti | brahmarṣiṣu bhṛguḥ devarṣiṣu nāradaḥ || 1 ||
brahmaṇyānāṃ balistvaṃ ṛtuṣu ca japayajño'si vīreṣu pārtho
bhaktānāmuddhavastvaṃ balamasi balināṃ dhāma tejasvināṃ tvam |
nāstyantastvadvibhūtervikasadatiśayaṃ vastu sarvaṃ tvameva
tvaṃ jīvastvaṃ pradhānaṃ yadihaṃ bhavahate tanna kiñcit prapañce || 2 ||
brahmaṇyānāmiti | brahmaṇyānāṃ brāhmaṇabhaktānām | balināṃ tejasvināmiti sambandhe ṣaṣṭhī | yatastvadvibhūteranto nāsti ata etadavadhārayāmi | vikasadatiśayaṃ sphuṭapratīyamānātiśayayuktaṃ yad vastu tat sarvaṃ tvameva | kiñca, tvaṃ jīvaḥ, tvaṃ pradhānaṃ, prakṛtipuruṣau tvameva | ataḥ prakṛtipuruṣātmake iha asmin prapañce tat tādṛśaṃ kiñcidapi nāsti, yad bhavaddate tvaghghatirekeṇa pratīyate | tvamevedaṃ sarvamityarthaḥ ||2||
uktopāsanādāvanadhikāriṇāmīśvarārpaṇena svadharmānuṣṭhānād vairāgyadvārā muktirityāha -- 
dharma varṇāśramāṇāṃ śrutipathavihitaṃ tvatparatvena bhaktyā
kurvanto'ntarvirāge vikasati śanakaiḥ santyajanto labhante |
sattāsphūrtibhiyatvātmakamakhilapadārtheṣu bhinneṣvabhinnaṃ
nirmūlaṃ viśvamūlaṃ paramamahamiti tvadvivodhaṃ viśuddham || 3 ||
dharmamiti | varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak śrutipathavihitaṃ nityanaimittikādirūpaṃ bhaktyā tvatparatvena īśvarārpitatayā kurvantaḥ śanairantarvirāge vikasati prādurbhūte sati santyajantaḥ vihitāni karmāṇi vidhinā tyajantaḥ paramahaṃsā bhūtvā viśuddhaṃ viṣayākāraśūnyaṃ tvadvibodhaṃ tvatsvarūpabhūtaṃ vibodhaṃ paraṃ brahma paramamahaṃ paraṃ brahmaivāham iti
anena prakāreṇa labhante prāpnuvanti | brahmaṇastaṭasthalakṣaṇamāha -- viśvamūlamiti | jaga jjanmādikāraṇamityarthaḥ | tadupapādayati -- akhilapadārthepviti | bhinneṣu svabhāvataḥ pṛthagbhūteṣu akhilapadārtheṣu svasṛṣṭheṣu kāryeṣu abhinnaṃ kāraṇarūpeṇaikatvena sthitaṃ, kaṭakakuṇḍalādau suvarṇavat | nanu tadvatsaccidānandaṃ brahma svasṛṣṭeṣu kāryeṣu na dṛśyata iti ced dṛśyata evetyāha -- satteti | yeyaṃ bhinneṣu ghaṭapaṭādiṣu ghaṭaḥ san paṭaḥ sannityekarūpā sattā pratīyate, yā ca bhinneṣvabhinnā ghaṭo'yaṃ paṭo'yamiti sphūrtijñānamekarūpaṃ pratīyate, yacca bhinneṣu srakkandanādiṣu abhinnaṃ priyatvam ānukūlyaṃ sukhamiti yāvat, tāni sattāsphūrtipriyatvāni ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya tadeva brahmetyarthaḥ | atra ca bhinneṣvabhinnamityanena kāryakāraṇānanyatvokteradvitīyatvamapyuktam | ataḥ sattāsphūrtipriyatvātmakamiti ca saccidānandādvayaṃ brahmeti svarūpalakṣaṇamapi darśitam | nirmūlaṃ niṣkāraṇaṃ nityam | atastat prāptānāṃ na punarāvṛttirityapi sūcitam || 3 ||
nanu bhaktyaiva kṛtārthā ityuktaṃ prāk 'santi śreyāṃsi' ityādinā | kimidānīṃ karmajñānayogopādānamityāśaṅkayādhikāribhedādityāha -- 
jñānaṃ karmāpi bhaktistritayamiha bhavatprāpakaṃ tatra tāvad
nirviṇṇānāmaśeṣe viṣaya iha bhaveda jñānayoge'dhikāraḥ |
saktānāṃ karmayogastvayi hi vinihito ye tu nātyantasaktā
nāpyatyantaṃ viraktāstvayi ca dhṛtarasā bhaktiyogo hyamīṣām || 4 ||
jñānāmati | bhavatprāpakaṃ mokṣasādhanam | tatra teṣu triṣu madhye | aśeṣe viṣaye ihāmutra srakkandanādau amṛtādau iha śarīre ca nirviṇṇānāṃ viraktānāṃ jñānayoge'dhikāro bhavet | saktānāmanirviṇṇānāṃ hi tvayi vinihitaḥ īśvare samarpitaḥ karmayogaḥ | ye tu madhyasthāḥ tvayi īśvare dhṛtarasā yadṛcchayā tvatkathādau jātaśraddhāścetyarthaḥ | teṣāmamīṣāṃ bhaktiyogaḥ || 4 ||
etacca tritayaṃ manuṣyaśarīrasādhyāmiti tatprāptau na kālakṣepaḥ kartavya ityabhiprāyeṇāha -- 
jñānaṃ khadbhaktatāṃ vā laghu sukṛtavaśānmartyaloke labhante
tasmāt tatraiva janma spṛhayati bhagavan! nākago nārako vā |
āviṣṭaṃ māṃ tu daivād bhavajalanidhipotāyite martyadehe
tvaṃ kṛtvā karṇadhāraṃ gurumanuguṇavātāyitastārayethāḥ || 5 ||
jñānamiti | tvadbhaktatāṃ vā laghu anāyāsena | daivāt sukṛtena bhavajalanidhau potavadācarite āviṣṭaṃ praviṣṭaṃ tu taraṇopāyavidhuraṃ māṃ gurumupadeṣṭāraṃ karṇadhāraṃ nāvikaṃ kṛtvā tvaṃ vātālayeśaḥ svayamanuguṇavātavadācaran tārayethāḥ pāraṃ naya || 5 ||
tatra sādhaneṣu bhaktireva sugamamārga ityāha -- 
avyaktaṃ mārgayantaḥ śrutibhirapi nayaiḥ kevalajñānalubdhāḥ
lkiśyante'tīva siddhiṃ bahutarajanuṣāmanta evāpnuvanti |
dūrasthaḥ karmayogo'pi ca paramaphale nanvayaṃ bhaktiyogastvāmṛlādeva hṛyastvaritamāya! bhavatprāpako vardhatāṃ me || 6||
avyaktamiti | kevalajñāne lubdhāḥ kevalaṃ jñānalubdhā vā śrutibhirūpaniṣadbhiḥ nayaiḥ mīmāṃsoktayuktibhirapi avyaktaṃ brahma mārgayantaḥ vicārayantaḥ atīva kliśyante | bahutarajanuṣāmanta eva siddhiṃ phalamāpnuvanti | karmayogo'pi ca paramaphale paramapuruṣārthe mokṣe dūrasthaḥ kālāntara eva phalapradaḥ kleśarūpaśca | pitṛlokasatyalokāvāptyādirūpeṇa tvavāntaraphalena dūrasthaḥ | bhaktiyogastvāmūlāt śravaṇakīrtanādikrameṇārambhāt prabhṛtyeva hṛdyaḥ tvaritaṃ bhavatprāpakaśca | ayi bhagavan | mama sa eva vardhatām || 6 ||
uktamarthe sadācārapramāṇena draḍhayati -- 
jñānāyaivātiyatnaṃ munirapavadate brahmatattvaṃ tu śṛṇvan
gāḍhaṃ tvatpādabhaktiṃ śaraṇamayati yastasya muktiḥ karāgre |
tvaddhayāne'pīha tulyā punarasukaratā cittacāñcalyahetorabhyāsādāśu śakyaṃ tadapi vaśayituṃ tvatkṛpācārutābhyām || 7 ||
jñānāyaiveti | jñānāyaiva kevalaṃ jñānameva prāptum atiyatnaṃ bahugranthābhyāsaṃ munirvedavyāsaḥ apavadate purāṇeṣu tatra tatra pratiṣedhati | kiṃ tu brahmatattvaṃ siddhāntasāramātraṃ śṛṇvan yuktyānusandadhānaḥ gāḍham atiśayena tvatpāde bhaktiṃ yaḥ śaraṇamayati tasya muktiḥ karāgre | jīvanneva mukta iti bhāvaḥ | iha yamādisahite tvaddhyāne'pi cittasya cāñcalyāddhetorasukaratā duranuṣṭheyatā tulyā | tadapi duṣkaramapi dhyānaṃ tvatkṛpayā tvañcarutayā śrīmūrteḥ saundaryeṇa cābhyāsādāśu vaśayituṃ vaśīkartu śakyamityarthaḥ || 7 ||
nirviṇṇaḥ karmamārge khalu viṣamatame tvatkathādau ca gāḍhaṃ
jātaśraddho'pi kāmānayi bhuvanapate ! naiva śakromi hātum |
tad bhūyo niścayena tvayi nihitamanā doṣabuddhayā bhajaṃstān
puṣṇīyāṃ bhaktimeva tvayi hṛdayagate maṅkṣu nakṣyanti saṅgāḥ || 8 ||
nirviṇṇa iti | kiñca, viṣamatame duḥkhabhūyiṣṭhe karmamārge svargādau nirviṇṇaḥ viraktaḥ tvatkathādau jātaśraddhaśca yadyapi bhavati, tathāpi kāmān kāmyamānān putramitravittādīn naiva hātuṃ śaknomi | tat tasmād bhūyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ niścayena tvayi nihitamanā bhūtvā doṣabuddhayā tān kāmān bhajan sevamānaḥ bhaktimeva puṣṇīyām | evaṃ bhaktyā bhajanena tvayi hṛdayagate saṅgā viṣayaśraddhāḥ mañju jhaṭiti naṅkṣyanti nāśaṃ gamiṣyanti || 8 ||
bhagavadbhajane manonigrahasyāvaśyakatvād bhikṣupurūravasornidarśanena cittaśānti prārthayate dvābhyāṃ -- 
kaścit kleśārjitārthakṣayavimalamatirnudyamāno janaudhaiḥ
prāgevaṃ prāha vipro na khalu mama janaḥ kālakarmagrahā vā |
ceto me duḥkhahetustadiha guṇagaṇaṃ bhāvayat sarvakārītyuktvā śānto gatastvāṃ mama ca kuru vibho! tādṛśīṃ cittaśāntim ||
kaściditi | prāk purā kaścid vipraḥ mahatā kleśenārjitasyārthasya kṣayeṇa corādibhyo jātena nāśena santapyamāno nirviṇṇaḥ san vimalamatiḥ pravrajan punarapi nudyamānaḥ pīḍyamānaḥ sannevaṃ prāha -- ahaṃ tāvad janaiḥ pīḍita iva pratīye | na khalu mama jano duḥkhahetuḥ, asya mamātmanaścaikatvāt | na ca kālaḥ, tasyeśvarātmakatvād, mamātmanaśceśvarāṃśatvāt, svāṃśasya sataḥ pīḍāyogācca | na ca karma duḥkhahetuḥ, acetanasya dehasya citsvarūpasyātmanaśca karmāyogāt | na grahā duḥkhanimittaṃ, yato janmalagnāpekṣayā dvādaśāṣṭamādirāśisthairgrahaiḥ janimato dehasyaiva pīḍā na mamātmanaḥ | kintvātmatvenābhimanyamānaṃ ceta eva me duḥkhahetuḥ | taccittam iha ātmani svagataṃ kartṛtvabhoktṛtvādirūpaṃ guṇagaṇaṃ bhāvayad āropayat sat sarvakāri suranaranārakīyādiśarīrabhogyabhogasthānādi sarve karotīti sarvakārītyuktvā śāntaḥ san tvāṃ gataḥ mukto babhūva | mama ca mamāpi tādṛśīṃ cittaśānti kurū |
"parasya saṃsṛtiścittvādacittvād manaso'pi na |
manobhimāninaścittvādacittvāñca parasya sā ||"
iti siddhāntaḥ || 9 ||
ailaḥ prāgurvaśīṃ pratyativivaśamanāḥ sevamānakṣciraṃ tāṃ
gāḍhaṃ nirvidya bhūyo yuvatisukhamidaṃ kṣudrameveti gāyan |
tvadbhaktiṃ prāpya pūrṇaḥ sukhataramacarat tadvaduddhūya saṅgaṃ
bhaktottaṃsaṃ kriyā māṃ pavanapurapate ! hanta me rundhi rogān || 10 ||
aila iti | ailaḥ prāgurvaśīmityādi spaṣṭam | prāk purūravāḥ svayamāgatāmurvaśīṃ sevamānaḥ punaḥ samayabhaṅgena svarlokaṃ gatāṃ tāṃ yajñaiḥ prāpya ciraṃ sevamāno nirvidya śāntacitto'bhūditi vedapurāṇaprasiddhiḥ || 10 ||
iti bhagavadvibhūtivarṇanaṃ karmajñānabhaktimārgādhikārivarṇanaṃ cittopaśamaprārthanāvarṇanaṃ ca
ṣaṇṇavatitamaṃ daśakam |
atha guṇatrayavṛttijayena dvandvoparamāya traiguṇyavibhāgamāha -- 
traiguṇyād bhinnarūpaṃ bhavati hi bhuvane hīnamadhyottamaṃ yajjñānaṃ śraddhā ca kartā vasatirapi sukhaṃ karma cāhārabhedāḥ |
tvatkṣetratvanniṣevādi tu yadiha punastvatparaṃ tattu sarvaṃ prāhurnairguṇyaniṣṭhaṃ tadanubhajanato maṅkṣu siddho bhaveyam || 1 ||
traiguṇyāditi | iha bhuvane jñānādi yad vastu tat sarvaṃ triguṇamayatvād bhinnarūpam ata eva hīnamadhyottamaṃ ca bhavati | tatra sarvabhūteṣvātmana ekatvajñānaṃ sāttvikaṃ, pṛthaktvena jñānaṃ rājasaṃ, bālāditulyaṃ jñānaṃ tāmasam | śraddhā cādhyātmikadharmādharmaviṣayatayā tridhā | kartā ca vairāgyarāgājñānabhedena trividhaḥ | vanagrāmaghūtasadanabhedena vasatirapi tridhā | ātmānubhavasukhaṃ viṣayasukhaṃ nidrādyutthasukhamiti tacca trividham | īśvarārpitakāmyaniṣiddhabhedena karmāpi trividham | śuddhamindriyapreṣṭhamaśuṃci cetyāhāradravyamapi trividham | etat sarvamīśvaraviṣayatayā nirguṇaṃ bhavatītyāha -- tvatkṣetreti | iha punastvatkṣetratvānniṣevādi tu yat tat tvatparaṃ na phalaparaṃ ced bhavati, tarhi tat sarvaṃ nairguṇyaniṣṭhaṃ nirguṇaṃ prāhuḥ | tadanubhajanato maṅkṣu jhaṭiti mukto bhaveyam || 1 ||
samprati ' śrīkṛṣṇa! tvatpadopāsanam' iti prastutaṃ bhāgavataṃ dharmamupasaṃharati dvābhyāṃ -- 
tvayyeva nyastacittaḥ sukhamayi vicaran sarvaceṣṭāstvadarthaṃ tvadbhaktaiḥ sevyamānānapi caritacarānāśrayan puṃṇyadeśān |
dasyau viṣre mṛgādiṣvapi ca samamatirmucyamānāvamānaspardhātryādidoṣaḥ satatamakhilabhūteṣu sampūjaye tvām || 2 ||
tvayyeveti | sarvā vihitā avihitāśca ceṣṭāḥ karmāṇi tvadarthaṃ kurvanniti śeṣaḥ | tvadbhaktaiḥ samprati sevyamānān caritacarān taireva pūrvaṃ sevitān | athavā tvadbhaktaiḥ sevyamānān deśān, taiścaritacarān puṇyān puṇyakarmāṇīti vibhajya yojanā | dasyau nīce vipre uttame mṛgādiṣvapi | ādiśabdena caṇḍā
lādiṣvapi samamatiḥ san | atropāyamāha -- mucyamāneti | aikātmyajñānabalena mucyamānā avamānaspardhāsūyādayo dehābhimānakṛtā doṣā yasyaivambhūtaḥ san tvām akhilabhūteṣu satataṃ saṃpūjaye | athavā samamatitvasyāvāntaraphalamāha mucyamāneti | avamānādidoṣanāśa evāvāntaraphalam || 2 ||
evaṃ samadṛṣṭayopāsanasyāvadhimāha
tvadbhāvo yāvadeṣu sphurati na viśadaṃ tāvadevaṃ hyupāstiṃ
kurvannaikātmyabodhe jhaṭiti vikasati tvanmayo'haṃ careyam |
tvaddharmasyāsya tāvat kimapi na bhagavan ! prastutasya praṇāśastasmāt sarvātmanaiva maṃdiśa mama vibho! bhaktimārgaṃ manojñam || 3 ||
tvadbhāva iti | eṣu dasyuviprādiṣu sarvajantuṣvapi tvadbhāvaḥ tavāntaryāmirūpeṇāvasthānaṃ yāvatkālaparyantaṃ na viśadaṃ sphurati na samyaganubhavapadavīmavatarati, tāvatkālaparyantam evaṃ hi pūrvoktaprakāreṇaiva upāsti kurvan tyaktalajjaśca caṇḍālādibhyo'pyantaryāmirūpeṇeśvaro'tra vartata iti dṛṣṭyā namaskārādi kurvannityarthaḥ | tataśca jhaṭityaikātmyabodhe vikasati sarvabhūteṣveka evātmeti jñāne'nubhavarūpe sati ahaṃ tvanmayaścareyam | he bhagavan ! tvaddharmasya bhāgavatadharmasyāsya prastutasyārabdhasya tāvad ādita ārabhya yāvatsamāpti kimapi kiñcidapi praṇāśo vaikalyaṃ na bhavati yathā yāgādeḥ | tasmād anuṣṭhānādisaukaryād mama manojñaṃ samīcīnaṃ bhaktimārga premalakṣaṇāyā bhakteḥ sādhanarūpaṃ bhagavaddhamai sarvātmanā tadapekṣitādhikārāṅgādisahitaṃ pradiśa, māṃ tatrādhikāriṇaṃ kurvityarthaḥ || 3 ||
ārogyahīno na kutrāpi karmaṇyadhikārīti tadeve prārthayate -- 
taṃ cainaṃ bhaktiyogaṃ draḍhayitumayi! me sādhyamārogyamāyurdiṣṭayā tatrāpi sevyaṃ tava caraṇamaho bheṣajāyeva dugdham |
mārkaṇḍeyo hi pūrvaṃ gaṇakanigaditadvādaśābdāyuruccaiḥ
sevitvā vatsaraṃ tvāṃ tava bhaṭanivardrāvayāmāsa mṛtyum || 4 ||
taṃ cainamiti | taṃ pūrvoktam enam upasaṃhṛtaṃ bhaktiyogaṃ sādhyasādhanarūpaṃ draḍhayituṃ kiñcidārabdhaṃ sthirīkartum ayi ! bhagavan ! me mama ārogyaṃ bāhyāntararogarāhityam āyuśca sādhyam | tatra āyurārogyanimittamapi tava caraṇaṃ mayā sevyam | aho diṣṭyā sādhanasyāpi sukharūpatvādahaṃ kṛtārtho jātaḥ | bheṣajāya dugdhamiva yathā sannipātajvarādinivṛttathe payaḥ pātavyaṃ bhavati, tathetyarthaḥ | nanvāyuṣaḥ prārabdhakarmaniyamitatvāt tadadhikasya lābhaḥ prārthanayāpi na sambhāvyate ityāśaṅkaya mārkaṇḍeyadṛṣṭāntena pariharati -- mārkaṇḍeya iti | nanu tasya cirāyuṣṭvaṃ prāk kalpitaṃ na tu bhagavatsevayā labdhamiti ced, netyāha -- gaṇaketi | gaṇakena jātakakovidena nigaditam uktaṃ dvādaśābdāyuḥ yasya sa punaḥ 'atyutkaṭaiḥ puṇyapāpairihaiva phalamaśnuta' iti nyāyena saṃvatsaraṃ tvām uccaistītreṇa samādhiyogena sevitvā dvādaśābdānte ātmānaṃ netumāgataṃ pāśadaṇḍahastaṃ mṛtyum antakaṃ tatkālopanataiḥ tava bhaṭanivahaiḥ viṣṇudūtaiḥ drāvayāmāsa palāyanamakārayat || 4 || 64 ||
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktapriyākhyāyām
ekādaśaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
muktānāṃ saccidānandabrahmarūpatayā sthitiḥ |
dvādaśasthā harerlīlā samprati pratipādyate ||
idānīṃ mārkaṇḍeyakathāprasaṅgena tasya tapasā viṣṇumāyāśivadarśanādicaritrakathanamiṣeṇādhikāriṇo yogatrayeṇa brahma prāptasya brahmamātratayāvasthānaṃ nirūpyate -- 
mārkaṇḍeyaścirāyuḥ sa khalu punarapi tvatparaḥ puṣpabhadrātīre ninye tapasyannatulasukharatiḥ ṣaṭ tu manvantarāṇi |
devendraḥ saptamastaṃ surayuvatimarunmanmathairmohayiṣyan
yogoṣmanuṣyamāṇairna tu punaraśakat tvajjanaṃ nirjayet kaḥ || 5 ||
mārkaṇḍeya iti | sa khalu punarapi cirāyuṣṭve labdhe'pi tvatparo bhagavadbhajanaparo'bhūt | atra hetuḥ -- atulamukharatiriti | niratiśaya sukharūpa tvadbhajane ratiḥ śraddhā yasya saḥ puṣpabhadrātīre himavaduttarapārśve yasyāstīre citraśilā nāma śilā bhadravaṭo nāma vaṭaścāsti, tasyāḥ puṣpabhedrānāmanadyāstīre tapasyanneva ṣaḍ manvantarāṇi ekasaptaticaturyugalakṣaṇāni ninye | devendraḥ saptama iti manvantareṣvindrādīnāṃ vyatyāsaṃ darśayati | saptame manvantare yaḥ saptamo devendraḥ so'yaṃ tapasā svadhāma jighṛkṣatīti bhiyā taṃ mārkaṇḍeyaṃ surasuvatibhiḥ apsarobhiḥ marutā vāsantena mārutena manmathena ca mohayiṣyan yogasya tapasa ūṣmaṇā pṭhuṣyamāṇaiḥ dahyamānaistaiḥ surayuvatyādibhiḥ na tu punaraśakat taṃ mohayitumaśakto'bhūt | tvajanaṃ tvadbhaktaṃ ko nirjayed na ko'pi | aho moho devendrasyāpīti bhāvaḥ || 5 ||
prītyā nārāyaṇākhyastvamatha narasakhaḥ prāptavānasya pārśva
tuṣṭayā toṭryamānaḥ sa tu vividhavarairlobhito nānumene |
draṣṭuṃ māyāṃ tvadīyāṃ kila punaravṛṇod bhaktitṛptāntarātmā
māyāduḥkhānabhijñastadapi mṛgayate nūnamācaryahetoḥ || 6 ||
prītyeti | tattapasā jātena prasādena narasakho nārāyaṇākhyastvamasya pārśva prāptavān | atha anantaraṃ sa tu tuṣṭyā toṣṭūyamānaḥ punaḥ punaratiśayena vā stutiṃ kurvan tvayā vividhairvaraiḥ pralobhito nānumene na tān varānavṛṇot | atra hetuḥ -- bhaktitṛptāntarātmeti | bhaktyā tava bhajanenaiva tṛptaḥ pūrṇaḥ antarātmā mano yasya | kiñca, tvadīyāṃ māyāṃ draṣṭumavṛṇocca | ko hi nāma duḥkhadātrīṃ māyāṃ vṛṇuyāt | ato'sminnasambhāvanīyametaditi kilaśabdārthaḥ | athavā māyayā yad duḥkhaṃ, tadanabhijño janmaprabhṛti | ata āścaryahetoḥ ananubhūtasya darśane hyāścaryaṃ bhavati, ataḥ tadapi māyāduḥkhamapi mṛgayate anububhūṣati nūnam iti tarkayāmi || 6 ||
yāte tvayyāśu vātākulajaladagalattoyapūrṇātighūrṇatsaptārṇorāśimagne jagati sa tu jale sambhraman varṣakoṭīḥ |
dīnaḥ maikṣiṣṭa dūre vaṭadalaśayanaṃ kañcidāścaryabālaṃ tvāmeva śyāmalāṅgaṃ badanasarasijanyastapādāṅgulīkam || 7 ||
yāta iti | tvayi naranārāyaṇākhye āśu tasya māyādarśanaprārthanānantarameva yāte badarikāśramaṃ prati gate sati | vātena ākulebhyaḥ itastataścālitebhyo jaladebhyo galatā toyena pūrṇaiḥ ata eva atighūrṇadbhiḥ uparyupari prasarpadbhiḥ saptārṇorāśibhiḥ jagati magne sa ti sa tu mārkaṇḍeyaḥ varṣakoṭīḥ jale sambhraman dīno vaṭadalaśayanaṃ kañcidāścaryabālaṃ dūre praikṣiṣṭa || 7 ||
dṛṣṭvā tvāṃ hṛṣṭaromā tvaritamabhigataḥ spraṣṭukāmo munīndraḥ
śvāsenāntarniviṣṭaḥ punariha sakalaṃ dṛṣṭavān viṣṭapaugham |
bhūyo'pi śvāsavātairvahiranupatito vīkṣitastvatkaṭākṣairmodādāśleṣṭukāmastvayi pihitatanau svāśrame prāgvadāsīt || 8 ||
dṛṣṭveti | śvāsenāntaḥ praviṣṭo viṣṭapaughaṃ caturdaśabhuvanātmakaṃ jagad dṛṣṭavān|
punaśca bahiranupatitaḥ tvāya pihitatanau antarhite sati svāśrame puṣpabhadrātīre prāgvad yathāsthānaṃ sthitaścāsīt | atra ca māyāyāḥ svarūpataḥ pradarśanasyāśakyatvād bhagavatā kāryamukhena darśiteti draṣṭavyam || 8 ||
atha viṣṇubhaktyā santuṣṭasya śivasyānugrahaprakāramāha -- 
gauryā sārdhaṃ tadagre purabhidatha gatastvatpriyaprekṣaṇārthī
siddhānevāsya dattvā svayamaya majarāmṛtyutādīn gato'bhūt |
evaṃ tvatsevayaiva smararipurapi sa prīyate yena tasmānmūrtitrayyātmakastvaṃ nanu sakalaniyanteti suvyaktamāsīt || 9 ||
gauryeti | tasya mārkaṇḍeyasya agre purataḥ | asya svayaṃ siddhān svatapaḥ prabhāveṇaiva prāptān ajarāmṛtyutādīn, ādiśabdena viṣṇubhaktiyaśojñānavijñānapurāṇācāryatādīn varān dattvā ayaṃ śivaḥ gato'bhūt | anena ca viṣṇoḥ sarveśvaratvaṃ siddhamityāha -- evamiti | tvaṃ viṣṇuḥ sakalaniyantā sarveśvara iti suvyaktaṃ niścitamāsīt | atra hetuḥ -- mūrtitrayyātmakastvaṃ nanviti | mūrtitrayī brahmaviṣṇugirīśāḥ tadātmakaḥ tatsvarūpaḥ | atra hetuḥ -- tvatsevayeti | sa smararipurapi tvatsevayaiva prīyate yena tasmād viṣṇoreva kāryanimittamavasthābhedo mūrtitrayamityabhiprāyaḥ || 9 ||
nanu śivasyāvasthābheda iti kiṃ na syādityāśaṅkya sthānabhedena viṣṇostebhyo vyatireka aikye'pi sambhavatītyāha -- 
tryaṃśe'smin satyaloke vidhiharipurabhinmandirāṇyūrdhvamūrdhva
tebhyo'pyūrdhvaṃ tu māyāvikṛtivirahito bhāti vaikuṇṭhalokaḥ |
tatra tvaṃ kāraṇāmbhasyapi paśupakule śuddhasattvaikarūpī
saccidbrahmādvayātma pavanapurapate! pāhi māṃ sarvarogāt || 10 ||
tryaṃśa iti | trayo brahmaviṣṇuśivalokarūpā aṃśā lokāḥ yasmin sa tryaṃśaḥ, tasmin satyaloke ūrdhvamūrdhvamiti prathamaṃ brahmaloke vidhimandiraṃ, tadupari viṣṇuloke viṣṇumandiraṃ, tadupari śivaloke śivamandiramiti | tatrasthāḥ sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārakartārastrimūrtayaḥ | tebhyo'pyūrdhvaṃ tu brahmāṇḍād bahiḥ | ata eva māyāvikṛtayaḥ mahadahaṅkārādayaḥ ṣoḍaśavikārāḥ, tairvirahitaḥ śuddhasattvaguṇamayo vaikuṇṭhaloko bhāti | tatra vaikuṇṭhaloke kāraṇāmbhasi āvaraṇodake paśupakule nandagehe'pi tvaṃ śuddhasattvaikarūpī tatsvacchatayāvaraṇābhāvāt saccidbrahmādvayātmā saccidānandādvayabrahmavapureva tvam | ataḥ sarvarogānmāṃ pāhi mocayeti ||10||
ityuttamabhaktiprārthanāvarṇanaṃ mārkaṇḍeyasya pralayapradarśanavarṇanaṃ, parameśvaraprasādavarṇanaṃ ca
saptanavatitamaṃ daśakam |
evaṃ stutyasya viṣṇorniṣkalatvaṃ prasādhya tadrūpeṇa stauti -- 
yasminnetad vibhātaṃ yata idamabhavad yena cedaṃ ya etad
yo'smāduttīrṇarūpaḥ khalu sakalamidaṃ bhāsitaṃ yasya bhāsā |
yo vācāṃ dūradūre punarapi manasāṃ yasya devā munīndrā
no vidyustattvarūpaṃ kimu punarapare kṛṣṇa ! tasmai namaste || 1 ||
yasminniti | he kṛṣṇa ! tasmai saccidānandasvarūpāya te namaḥ | nanu stūyatāṃ, kiṃ namaskāramātreṇetyatrāha -- yasyeti | devā munīndrā api yasya tattvarūpaṃ, taditi sarvanāma, sarve ca brahma, tasya bhāvastattvaṃ brahmatvaṃ, tadrūpaṃ na viduḥ | ke punaratra vayaṃ jñātuṃ stotuṃ veti bhāvaḥ | durjeyatvamevāha -- yo vācāmiti | laukikānāṃ vaidikānāmapi vacasāṃ dūradūre, vācyavācakasambandhābhāvāt | punarmanasāmapi dūradūre | atra hetuḥ -- yasya bhāsā cicchaktyā sakalamidaṃ jagad bhāsitaṃ prakāśitam| yatprakāśena labdhasattākaṃ manaādi, tena tatprakāśanamaśakyamityarthaḥ | tarhi jñātumaśakyatvādasadevedamityatrāha -- yasminniti | yasminnadhiṣṭhāne etad jagad vibhātaṃ tiṣṭhati | yato nimittopādānakāraṇabhūtādidaṃ jagadabhavat | yena idaṃ jagad, cakārādaikyaṃ yāti līyata ityarthaḥ | ato jagatkāraṇatvād etad jagad yaḥ yadrūpaḥ īśvara eva jagadātmanā bhāti | ato jagadātmanā pratītasya siddhau na pramāṇāntarāpekṣeti bhāvaḥ | nanvīśvarasya jagadātmatve vikāritvaprasaṅga
iti ced, netyāha -- yo'smāditi | asmād ātmanyavidyā kalpitājjagataḥ uttīrṇaṃ vyatiriktaṃ rūpaṃ yasya || 1 ||
evamīśvarasya jagatkāraṇatvamupapādya anantaraṃ jagadanugrāhakatvamāha -- 
janmātho karma nāma sphuṭamiha guṇadoṣādikaṃ vā na yasmin
lokānāmṛtaye yaḥ svayamanubhajate tāni māyānusārī |
vibhracchaktīrarūpo'pi ca bahutararūpo'vabhātyabhduttātmā
tasmai kaivalyadhāmne pararasaparipūrṇāya viṣṇo ! namaste || 2 ||
janmeti | janma śrīkṛṣṇādyavatāraḥ atho anantaraṃ karma kaṃsahananādi nāma janmakarmanimittam | guṇadoṣāḥ nigrahānugrahādayaḥ | ādiśabdena putramitrādayaḥ | etāni yasmin sphuṭaṃ paramārthato na santi, tathāpi lokānāmūtaye jagadanugrahāya māyānusārī tāni janmakarmādīni svayam ananyādhīnasvabhāvaḥ san anubhajate aṅgīkaroti apica śaktīḥ vidyāvidyājñānaiśvaryādilakṣaṇāḥ bibhrat svayamarūpo nirguṇo'pi sthāvarajaṅgamabhedena suranaratiryagādibhedena ca bahutararūpaścāvabhāti, ato'dbhutātmā āścaryasvarūpaḥ, tasmai jagadanugrāhakāya pararasena paramānandena paripūrṇāya ata eva kaivalyadhāmne mokṣapradāya || 2 ||
nanu bahutararūpatve kathamarūpatvamityāśaṅkaya tatsvarūpasya sarvadvaitaniṣedhāvadhitvenopapādayati -- 
no tiryañcaṃ na martya na ca suramasuraṃ na striyaṃ no pumāṃsaṃ
na dravyaṃ karma jātiṃ guṇamapi sadasad vāpi te rūpamāhuḥ |
śiṣṭaṃ yat syānniṣedhe sati nigamaśatairlakṣaṇāvṛttitastat
kṛccheṇāvedyamānaṃ paramasukhamayaṃ bhāti tasmai namaste || 3 ||
no tiryañcamiti | te taveśvasya rūpaṃ parabrahma no tiryañcaṃ na paśupakṣyādikamāhuḥ āgamāstadvido vā | na martyaṃ manujaṃ suraṃ devam asuraṃ na striyamiti | strīpunnapuṃsakabhedena triprakāraṃ śarīramucyate | dravyaṃ viṣayān | karma karmendriyāṇi | jātiṃ brāhmaṇatvādi| guṇaṃ jñānendriyāṇi | sad antaḥkaraṇam | asad avyaktam | devādīnāṃ paricchinnatvena dehādīnāṃ jaḍatvena caitāni na te svarūpamāhuḥ, kintu nigamenaivaṃ niṣedhe sati yattacchiṣṭam avadhibhūtaṃ syāt | ata eva nigamaśataiḥ upaniṣadbhiḥ kṛcchreṇa vācyavācakasambandhaṃ vinā lakṣaṇāvṛttita āvedyamānaṃ pratipādyamānaṃ paramasukhamayaṃ paramānandarūpaṃ bhāti ca | atra niṣedhavākyasya mukhyārthasya niṣedhe bādhitatvāt tadavadhibhūte'rthe yā vṛttiḥ, sā lakṣaṇāvṛttiḥ | tathā pratipādyamānaṃ sad ahaṃ brahmāsmīti niṣkarmatayā tvatsvarūpabhūtaṃ paraṃ brahma prakāśata ityarthaḥ || 3 ||
atha niṣedhaśeṣasyāśeṣatvamaśeṣasākṣitvena pratipādayati -- 
māyāyāṃ vimbitastvaṃ sṛjasi mahadahaṅkāratanmātrabhedairbhūtagrāmendriyādyairapi sakalajagat svapnasaṅkalpakalpam |
bhūyaḥ saṃhṛtya sarva kamaṭha iva padānyātmanā kālaśaktyā gambhīre jāyamāne tamasi vitimiro bhāsi tasmai namaste || 4 ||
māyāyāmiti | svasmāt pṛthagavasthitāyāṃ māyāyāṃ cicchaktyā pratibimvitastvaṃ mahattattvena ahaṅkārabhedaiḥ rājasatāmasasāttvikaiḥ śabdasparśarūparasagandhā iti pañcatanmātrabhedaiḥ bhūtādibhiḥ ekādaśendriyaiḥ ādiśabdena prāṇādibhirapi sakalaṃ jagat sṛjasi | svapnasaṅkalpakalpamiti āgamāpāyitvāt svapnasaṅkalpavadayaṃ jāgratprapañco mithyetyarthaḥ | bhūyaḥ punaśca yathā kūrmaściraṃ vihṛtya bhūyaḥ svapadāni svasmin saṃhṛtya tūṣṇīṃ bhavati, evaṃ kālaśaktyā sarva saṃhṛtya ātmanā svasvarūpeṇava bhāsi | evañcādāvante cāsattvāt prapañcasya madhye'pi mithyātvamuktam | īśvarasyādimadhyānteṣu prapañcaprakāśakatvena sattvāt sākṣitvaṃ nityatvaṃ coktam | nanu
sarvavināśe īśvarasyāpi nāśa iti kiṃ na syādityatrāha -- gambhīra iti | gambhīre apratarkye tamasi suṣuptau jāyamāna iti | ayamabhiprāyaḥ -- sarvasaṃhāre suṣuptau sati sakalajagannidānabhūtamajñānaṃ tamaśśabdavācyaṃ na layaṃ  gacchati, anyathā jagataḥ punarutpattyabhāvāt, sukhamahamasvāpsaṃ na kiñcidavediṣamityutthitasya parāmarśānupapatteśceti | evañca tadāpi vitimiraḥ nityoditānastamitaprakāśarūpatayājñānasākṣitvena bhāsi | draṣṭrabhāve dṛśyāsiddherityabhiprāyaḥ | ya evambhūtaḥ, tasmai namaste ||4||
nanvīśvarasya jagatkāraṇatvamasiddhaṃ vādivipratipatterityāśaṅkāyāṃ saṃjñābheda eva vipratipattiḥ na saṃjñinītyāha -- 
śabdabrahmeti karmetyaṇuriti bhagavan ! kāla ityālapanti
tvāmekaṃ viśvahetuṃ sakalamayatayā sarvathā kalpyamānam |
vedāntairyat tu gītaṃ puruṣaparacidātmābhidhaṃ tat tu tattvaṃ
prekṣāmātreṇa mūlaprakṛtivikṛtikṛt kṛṣṇa ! tasmai namaste ||5||
śabdabrahmeti | tvāmekameva śabdabrahmetyādibhiḥ śabdaiḥ viśvahetuṃ jagatkāraṇamālapanti vādinaḥ | śabdabrahma jagatkāraṇamiti kecidālapanti, kecit karma jīvādṛṣṭamiti, anye paramāṇuriti, apare kāla iti | eṣāṃ śabdānāṃ tvameka evābhidheya iti bhāvaḥ | atra hetuḥ -- sakalamayatayā tava sarvātmakatvena | sarvathā tattaddarśanānusāreṇa śabdabrahmādirūpeṇa tavaiva kalpyamānatvādityarthaḥ | siddhāntamāha -- vedāntairiti | vedāntaistu yat puruṣaparacidātmābhidhaṃ tattvaṃ, tattu prekṣāmātreṇa mūlaprakṛtivikṛtikṛd gītamityanvayaḥ | atra puruṣaḥ 'māyāyāṃ bimbitastvam' iti prāguktaṃḥ, paraḥ māyāsambandharahitaḥ, ciditi śuddhacaitanyam, ātmā sarvānugataḥ | puruṣādiśabdā abhidhā yasya tat tathā | tattu tadeva tattvaṃ paraṃ brahma prekṣāmātreṇa māyāpreraṇamātreṇa mūlaprakṛtirmāyā, tasyā vikṛtiḥ kāryaṃ jagat, tat karotīti tathā | nanu prakṛteḥ kārya kathaṃ brahma karotīti | ucyate | yathā rājā prekṣaṇamātreṇa sannidhiṃ prāptayā pratīhāryā karma karoti, tadvat svasamavetakriyāphalaśālitvalakṣaṇamukhyakartṛtvaṃ vinā prakṛtimukheneśvarasya jagatkartṛtvamiti bhāvaḥ | he kṛṣṇa ! ya evaṃ, tasmai te namo'stu || 5 ||
nanu keyaṃ mūlaprakṛtirityāśaṅkaya sadasattvābhyāmanirvacanīyā vidyāvidyātmikeśvarasya śaktirityāha -- 
satvenāsattayā vā na ca khalu sadasattvena nirvācyarūpā
dhatte yāsāvavidyā guṇaphaṇimativad viśvadṛśyāvabhāsam |
vidyātvaṃ seva yātā śrutivacanalavairyatkṛpāsyandalābhe
saṃsārāraṇya sagrasṭa naparaśutāmeti tasmai namaste || 6 ||
sattveneti | yasyeyamavidyā tajjñāne sati nivartate | ataḥ satīti vaktumaśakyā, sattāsphūrtyanupapatteḥ | nāsatī ca, ekaiva satī asatī ceti nitarāmanupapannā virodhāt | ato'vidyā sattvenāsattayā sadasattvena vā na nirvācyarūpā niṣkṛṣya vaktumaśakyaṃ svarūpaṃ yasyāḥ sā tathā | tarhi nāstīti ced, netyāha -- asāviti | ‘ahamajña' iti sarvānubhavasiddhatayā tadanavakalpanamaśakyamiti bhāvaḥ | yāsāvavidyā guṇaphaṇimativad rajjusarpabhrāntivad viśvadṛśyasya sakalajagataḥ avabhāsaṃ jīvasya saṃsāra duḥkhanivāsabhūtaṃ śarīrādyahammamābhimānādirūpaṃ dhatte karotītyarthaḥ | yasyeśvarasya kṛpāyāḥ syandaḥ pravāhaḥ, tasya lābhe sati saiva avidyā śrutivacanalavaiḥ vedāntavākyaleśaiḥ, śravaṇamātreṇetyarthaḥ, vidyātvaṃ yātā vidyārūpiṇī bhūtvā saṃsārarūpasyāraṇyasya vanasyātiniśitatayā sadyaḥ śīghraṃ truṭane khaṇḍane paraśutāmeti, tasmai namaste || 1 ||
evaṃ prakṛtyadhiṣṭhitasya brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatvamuktam | atha tadananyatvena jagadātma tvamucyate -- 
bhūṣāsu svarṇavad vā jagati ghaṭaśarāvādike mṛttikāvat
tattve sañcintyamāne sphurati tadadhunāpyadvitīyaṃ vapuste |
svapnadraṣṭuḥ prabodhe timiralayavidhau jīrṇarajjokṣca yadvad
vidyālābhe tathaiva sphuṭamapi vikaset kṛṣṇa ! tasmai namaste || 7 ||
bhūṣāsviti| te tad jagatkāraṇabhūtam advitīyaṃ parabrahmākhyaṃ vapuḥ svarūpam (adhunā) adyāpi jagataḥ sthitikāle'pi jagati jagadviṣaye sphurati | nanvidaṃ brahmeti kasyāpi nānubhava ityatrāha -- tattva iti | tattve paramārthe sañcintyamāne vicāryamāṇe | brahma upādānaṃ, jagat tatkāyeṃ, kāryakāraṇayorananyatvaṃ ca śāstre sthitam ityanusandadhato brahmaivedaṃ sarvamiti sphuraṇaṃ sambhavatyeveti bhāvaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntamāha -- bhūṣāsviti | kaṭakakuṇḍalādiṣu yathā svarṇe sphurati, yathā vā ghaṭaśarāvādike mṛtti kā, svarṇādibhiḥ kriyamāṇeṣu kāryeṣu kaṭakādiṣu svarṇamevedamityanubhavarūpā pratītirudeti, tadvadityarthaḥ | kiñca, vidyālābhe brahmajñāne sati avidyāstamayāt sphuṭaṃ vikasedapi prapañcākāraparityāgenākhaṇḍaṃ brahmeti prakāśeta cetyarthaḥ | atra dṛṣṭāntaḥ --  svapneti | svapnedraṣṭuḥ prabodhe sati yadvadajñānakalpitaprapañcalayaḥ, yadvacca dīpādinā timiralayavidhau jīrṇarajorjñānena tadajñānakalpitasarpalayaḥ, tathaivetyarthaḥ | he kṛṣṇa ! yasya te vapuruktaprakāreṇa vikaset, tasmai namaste iti yojanā || 7||
evaṃ saprapañcaniṣprapañcabhedena tatsvarūpaṃ stutvā sarvaniyantṛtvena stauti -- 
yadbhītyodeti sūryo dahati ca dahano vāti vāyustathānye
yadbhītāḥ padmajādyāḥ punarucitavalīnāharante'nukālam |
yenaivāropitāḥ prāṅ nijapadamapi te cyāvitāraśca paścāt
tasmai viśvaṃ niyantre vayamapi bhavate kṛṣṇa! kurmaḥ praṇāmam || 8||
yadbhītyeti | yasmād bhītyā sūrya udeti | ucitabalīn yathādhikāraṃ vihitāḥ pūjā āharante prayacchanti | te padmajādyāḥ yena prāṅ nijapadaṃ satyalokādi āropitāḥ prāpitāḥ, paścād dviparārdhādyavasāne yena cyāvitāraḥ cyutiṃ prāpitāśca bhaviṣyanti, tasmai viśvaṃ niyantre brahmādīnāmapi niyāmakāya sarveśvarāya vayamapi bhavate he kṛṣṇa ! praṇāmaṃ kurmaḥ || 8 ||
kiñca,
trailokyaṃ bhāvayantaṃ triguṇamayamidaṃ tryakṣarasyaikavācyaṃ
trīśānāmaikyarūpaṃ tribhirapi nigamairgīyamānasvarūpam |
tisro'vasthā vidantaṃ triyugajanijuṣaṃ trikramakrāntaviśvaṃ
traikālye bhedahīnaṃ tribhirahamaniśaṃ yogabhedairbhaje tvām || 9 ||
trailokyamiti | idaṃ triguṇamayaṃ sattvarajastamastāratamyena vilakṣaṇaṃ trailokyaṃ bhāvayantaṃ niyamena sṛjantaṃ vyakṣarasya praṇavasya ekaṃ pradhānaṃ vācyam abhidheyaṃ trīśānāṃ trimūrtīnām aikyaṃ svarūpaṃ yasya tam | 'brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca parasyaiva vibhūtayaḥ" iti vacanāt | tribhirnigamaiḥ trayyā gīyamānaṃ pratipādyamānaṃ rūpaṃ yasya | tisro'vasthāḥ jāgratsvapnasuṣuptayaḥ, tā vettīti vidan avasthātrayasākṣī tam | kṛtayuge prajānāṃ kṛtakṛtyatayāvatāraprayojanābhāvāditareṣu triṣu tretādvāparakaliyugeṣu janim avatāraṃ juṣata iti tathā | tribhiḥ kramaiḥ padavikṣepaiḥ krāntam ākrāntaṃ viśvaṃ yena | traikālye bhūtabhaviṣyadvartamāneṣu kāleṣu bhedahīnam advitīyaṃ, jāyate'stītyādiṣaḍbhāvavikārahīnaṃ vā | evambhūtaṃ tvām ahamaniśaṃ tribhiryogabhedaiḥ karmajñānabhaktiyogaiḥ bhaje || 9 ||
kiśca,
satyaṃ śuddhaṃ vibuddhaṃ jayati tava vapurnityamuktaṃ nirīhaṃ nirdvandvaṃ nirvikāraṃ nikhilaguṇagaṇavyañjanādhārabhūtam |
nirmūlaṃ nirmalaṃ tanniravadhimahimollāsi nirlīnamantarnissaṅgānāṃ munīnāṃ nirupamaparamānandasāndraprakāśam || 10 ||
satyamiti | tava tadvapuḥ svarūpaṃ jayati āvirbhūtaniratiśayotkarṣe prakāśate | satyādīni vapurviśeṣaṇāni | satyaṃ paramārthasatyam asataḥ sattāpradatvāt | vibuddhaṃ svaprakāśasiddham | muktāvasthāyā janyatve'satyatvaprasakteḥ nityamuktam | nirīhaṃ
kartṛtvabhoktṛtvādirahitam| nirdvandvam advitīyam | nirvikāra pariṇāmādivikriyāra hitam | nikhilānāṃ ye guṇagaṇāḥ kṣamāsatyadayaiśvaryādayaḥ, teṣāṃ vyañjanam utpattiḥ, tadādhārabhūtam | yadupāsane hi mahāguṇānāmutpattiḥ | nirmūlaṃ niṣkāraṇam | nirmalaṃ rāgādidoṣahīnaṃ, citprakāśatvād nirastājñānam | niravadhimahimollāsi kāladeśāparicchinnavaibhavayuktam | nissaṅgānāṃ munīnāmantaḥ samādhau nirlīnaṃ pratiṣṭhitam | nirupamaḥ paramānandaḥ sāndraḥ prakāśaḥ sāndrāvabodhaśca yasmin || 10 ||
athoktarūpaṃ paraṃ brahma prāptānāṃ na punaḥ saṃsāra ityabhiprāyeṇa kālacaṛparibhramabhayanivṛttiṃ prārthayate -- 
durvāraṃ dvādaśāraṃ triśataparimilatṣaṣṭiparvābhivītaṃ
saṃbhrāmyat krūravegaṃ kṣaṇamanu jagadācchidya sandhāvamānam |
cakraṃ te kālarūpaṃ vyathayatu na tu māṃ tvatpadaikāvalambaṃ
viṣṇo ! kāruṇyasindho ! pavanapurapate ! pāhi sarvāmayaughāt || 11 ||
durvāramiti | he viṣṇo! te kālacakraṃ saṃvatsarātmakaṃ cakraṃ na tu māṃ vyathayatu saṃsāraduḥkhaṃ mā kārṣīt | kīdṛśaṃ, durvāraṃ kenāpyanivāraṇīyam| saṃvatsarasya dvādaśa māsāḥ, tadātmakadvādaśaśikharayuktam | triśataparimilatpaṣṭacā ṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatrayeṇa dinaiḥ parvabhirdhārāsandhibhiḥ abhivītam abhivyāptam | saṃbhrāmyad baṃbhramyamāṇam | trkūravegaṃ kṣaṇamanu pratikṣaṇaṃ jagadācchidya ādāyādarśanaṃ netuṃ sandhāvamānam || 11 ||
iti satyajñānādisvarūpād brahmaṇo jagadutpattyādinirūpaṇam
aṣṭanavatitama daśakaṃ saikam |
atha navabhiḥ kṣlokairbhagavanmahimā varṇyate -- 
viṣṇorvīryāṇi ko vā kathayatu dharaṇeḥ kaśca reṇūna mimīte
yasyaivāṅghritrayeṇa trijagadabhimitaṃ modate pūrṇasampat |
yo'sau viśvāni dhatte priyamiha paramaṃ dhāma tasyābhiyāyāṃ
tadbhaktā yatra mādyantyamṛtarasamarandasya yatra pravāhaḥ || 1 ||
viṣṇoriti | vīryāṇi mahimnaḥ ko vā kathayatu gaṇayed, na ko'pi | kaśca dharaṇeḥ reṇūn mimīte | tadapi na ko'pi | yasya viṣṇoḥ aṅghritrayeṇa tribhiḥ padanyāsaiḥ trijagat trailokyam abhimitaṃ paricchinnam | ato balerādāya svabhujaguptatayā pūrṇasampad modate santuṣṭasakalajanaṃ ca vartate | kiñca, yo'sau viṣṇuḥ viśvāni sarvalokaṃ dhatte yogaiśvaryeṇa dhārayati, tasya priyaṃ dhāma vaikuṇṭham abhiyāyām ahaṃ prāpnuyāṃ, yatra tadbhaktā mādyanti | amṛtaraso mokṣasukhaṃ, tadrūpasya marandasya madhuno yatra vaikuṇṭhe pravāhaḥ sarvato niṣyandaḥ || 1 ||
ādyāyāśeṣakartre pratinimiṣanavīnāya bhartre vibhūterbhaktātmā viṣṇave yaḥ pradiśati havirādīni yajñārcanādau |
kṛṣṇādyaṃ janma yo vā mahadiha mahato varṇayet so'yameva
prītaḥ pūrṇo yaśobhistvaritamabhisaret prāpyamante padaṃ tat || 2 ||
ādyāyeti | ādau bhavāya ata eva aśeṣakartre sarvakāraṇāya pratinimiṣanavīnāya upāsakānāṃ nityanūtanāya vibhūteḥ śriyaḥ bhartre | premalakṣaṇabhaktiyukta ātmā mano yasya saḥ | yastathābhūtaḥ uktarūpāya viṣṇave yajñārcanādau havirādīni pradiśati, yo vā mahato viṣṇoḥ mahat pūjanīyaṃ kṛṣṇādyaṃ janma avatāraṃ varṇayet, so'yaṃ bhakta eveha yatheṣṭasukhānubhavena prītaḥ yaśobhiḥ pūrṇaḥ ante śarīrapāte tvaritaṃ janmāntaravilambamantareṇa tat padaṃ vaikuṇṭhalokam abhisaret prāpnuyād, nānyaḥ ||
yata evam, atastameva stuvīdhvamityāha -- 
he stotāraḥ! kavīndrāstamiha khalu yathā cetayadhve tathaiva
vyaktaṃ vedasya sāraṃ praṇuvata jananopāttalīlākathābhiḥ |
jānantaścāsya nāmānyakhilasukhakarāṇīti saṅkīrtayadhvaṃ
he viṣṇo! kīrtanādyaistava khalu mahatastatvabodhaṃ bhajeyam ||
he stotāra iti | iha ye kavīndrāḥ kāvyakuśalāḥ ata eva stotāro rājādivarṇanaparāḥ, te khalu yūyaṃ yathā cetayadhve avagacchatha, tathaiva jananairavatāraiḥ upāttābhiraṅgīkṛtābhiḥ līlābhiḥ taṃ viṣṇuṃ praṇuvata stutiṃ kuruta | vyaktaṃ pramāṇasiddhaṃ vedasya sāraṃ pradhānapratipādyam | he jānantaḥ ! paramārthajñāḥ! asya viṣṇornāmānyakhilasukhakarāṇi mokṣapradānītyataḥ saṅkīrtayadhvam | he viṣṇo! mahatastava kīrtanādyaiḥ khaluṃ tattvabodhaṃ tattvajñānaṃ bhajeyam || 3 || |
kiñca,
viṣṇoḥ karmāṇi sampaśyata manasi sadā yaiḥ sa dharmānabadhnād
yānīndrasyaiṣa bhṛtyaḥ priyasakha iva ca vyātanot kṣemakārī |
vīkṣante yogasiddhāḥ parapadamaniśaṃ yasya samyakramakāśaṃ
viprendrā jāgarūkāḥ kṛtabahututayo yacca nirbhāsayante || 4 ||
viṣṇoriti | yūyaṃ viṣṇoḥ karmāṇi caritāni manasi sadā saṃpaśyata cintayata | sa viṣṇuḥ yaiḥ duṣṭanigrahavidyāsthāpanalokasaṃgrahaṇarūpaiḥ karmabhiḥ dharmān abhyudayaniḥśreyasārthān abaghnāt tattadadhikāribhiḥ saṃyojayāmāsa, tāni karmāṇi saṃpaśyatetyarthaḥ | eṣa viṣṇurindrasya trailokyarakṣāyāmātmanādhikṛtasya bhṛtya iva, indrakartṛkasya jagadrakṣaṇasya tadaśakteḥ svayaṃ karaṇāt | ata eva mahatopakāreṇāsya priyasakha iva ca bhagavān yāni karmāṇi vyātanot cakāra | kṣemakārī evaṃ jagadrakṣaṇaśīla ityarthaḥ| kiñca, yogasiddhāḥ samādhinirjitasamagraiśvaryādiguṇā munayo yasya parapadaṃ māyāsambandharahitaṃ prāptisthānamaniśaṃ vīkṣante paśyanti | samyak prakāśaṃ yasmāt sarvasyeti parapadaṃ viśeṣyate | yat parapadaṃ vimendrā jāgarūkāstātparyavantaḥ kṛtabahunutayaḥ saguṇanirguṇabhedena stutiṃ kurvanto nirbhāsayante prakāśayanti || 8 ||
no jāto jāyamāno'pi ca samadhigatastvanmahimno'vasānaṃ deva! śreyāṃsi vidvān pratimuhurapi te nāma śaṃsāmi viṣṇo ! |
taṃ tvāṃ saṃstaumi nānāvidhanutivacanairasya lokatrayasyāpyūrdhve vibhrājamāne viracitavasatiṃ tatra vaikuṇṭhaloke ||5||
no jāta iīta | he deva! jāto jāyamānaśca yaḥ puruṣaḥ, sa ko'pi tvanmahimno'vasānaṃ nādhigataḥ na prāptaḥ | ato'haṃ śreyāṃsi vidvān idemava śreya iti niścitya pratimuhuḥ aniśaṃ te nāma śaṃsāmi saṅkīrtayāmi | he viṣṇo ! tamaparicchinnamahimānaṃ tvāṃ nānāvidhastutivacanaiḥ saṃstaumi | nirguṇe manonavagāhāt saguṇaṃ tvāṃ staumītyarthaḥ | asya lokatrayasya brahmāṇḍasyordhve vibhrājamāne tatra vekuṇṭhaloke viracitā vasatirvāso yasya || 5 ||
atha śrīnārāyaṇarūpaṃ stauti -- 
āpaḥ sṛṣṭyādijanyāḥ prathamamayi vibho! garbhadeśe dadhustvāṃ
yatra tvayyeva jīvā jalaśayana ! hare ! saṅgatā aikyamāpan |
tasyājasya prabho ! te vinihitamabhavat padmamekaṃ hi nābhau
dikpatraṃ yat kilāhuḥ kanakadharaṇibhṛtkarṇikaṃ lokarūpam || 6 ||
āpa iti | sṛṣṭyādijanyāḥ 'apa eva sasarjādau' iti vacanād āpaḥ prathamamādau tvāṃ garbhadeśe dadhuḥ | yatra apāṃ madhye'vasthite tvayyeva jīvāḥ saṅgatāḥ prāptāḥ aikyamāpan anuśayitā abhavan | kalpānte sarvajīvā brahmaṇā saha jalaśāyini śrīnārāyaṇe saṅgatā anuśerate | tasya jalaśayanasya ajasya viṣṇoste nābhau vinihitamadhiṣṭhitamekaṃ padmamabhavat | yat padmaṃ lokarūpamāhuḥ | tadevāha -- diśaḥ patrāṇi dalāṃni yasya, kanakadharaṇibhṛd mahāmeruḥ karṇikā tat | śrīviṣṇureva kamalajaḥ san nābhikamalena bhuvanamajanayadityarthaḥ || 6 ||
uktarūpaṃ viṣṇumajānataḥ śocannāha -- 
he lokā viṣṇuretad bhuvanamaṃjanayat tanna jānītha yūyaṃ
yuṣmākaṃ hyantarasthaṃ kimapi tadaparaṃ vidyate viṣṇurūpam |
nīhāraprakhyamāyāparivṛtamanaso mohitā nāmarūpaiḥ
māṇaprītyaikatṛptāśvaratha makhaparā hanta necchā mukunde ||7||
he lokā iti | he lokāḥ ! janāḥ ! yo viṣṇuḥ etat paridṛśyamānaṃ bhuvanamajanayat, taṃ viṣṇuṃ yūyaṃ kiṃ na jānītha | kiñca, yuṣmākamantarasthaṃ śarīrāntarbhūtamapi tadaparaṃ tasmādanyat saṃsāradharmayuktaṃ viṣṇurūpaṃ vidyate | aho tadapi na jānītha | kimiti na jānīmaḥ, tatrāha -- nīhāreti | āvaraṇaśaktyā himatulyayā māyayā parivṛtaṃ vyāptaṃ mano yeṣāṃ tathābhūtāḥ santaḥ nāmarūpairmohitā mithyābhūteṣu śarīrādiṣva haṃmametyabhimanyamānāḥ prāṇānāmindriyāṇāṃ prītirevaikā pradhānabhūtā tayā tṛptāḥ ata eva svargādisukhecchayā makhaparāḥ kāmyakarmaparāḥ caratha | hanta aho kaṣṭaṃ mukunde mokṣaprade viṣṇau necchā || 7 ||
athāmūrtāṃ viśvamūrtermūrtimanusandhatte -- 
mūrdhnāmakṣṇāṃ padānāṃ vahasi khalu sahasrāṇi sampūrṇa viśvaṃ
tat motkramyāpi tiṣṭhan parimitavivare bhāsi cittāntare'ṣi |
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ ca sarva parapuruṣa! bhavān kiñca dehendriyādipvāviṣṭo yudgatatvādamṛtasukharasaṃ cānubhuṅkṣe tvameva || 8 ||
mūrdhnāmiti | tvaṃ virāḍabhimānī mūrdhnāmakṣṇāṃ padānāṃ ca sahasrāṇi vahasi | brahmāṇḍānāmanekatvāt tatsthasatyalokādirūpāṇāṃ mūrdhādīnāmanekatvam | tato viśvaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ sampūrya vyāpya tad brahmāṇḍaṃ protkramya atikramya tiṣṭhannapi parimitavivare atisaṃkucite cittāntare hṛdayākāśe bhāsi | he paramapuruṣa! bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ sarve bhavāṃścaiva | kiñca, dehendriyādiṣvāviṣṭo yastvaṃ viṣayarasān bhuṅkṣe, sa tvameva tasmādudgatatvādamṛtasukhasya paramānandasya rasaṃ cāpyanubhuṅkṣe || 8 ||
yat tu trailokyarūpaṃ dadhadapi ca tatonirgatānantaśuddhajñānātmā vartase tvaṃ taba khalu mahimā so'pi tāvān kimanyat |
stokaste bhāga evākhilabhuvanatayā dṛśyate tryaṃśakalpaṃ
bhūyiṣṭhaṃ sāndramodātmakamupari tato bhāti tasmai namaste || 9 ||
yaviti | he ananta ! trailokyamayaṃ rūpaṃ dadhadapi tato nirgato bahiḥ
śuddhajñānātmā ca vartase tvamiti yat, so'pi tāvat tava khalu mahimā | kimanyad na kiñcidapi| apica, te stoko māgaḥ pādakalpa evākhilabhuvanatayā brahmāṇḍarūpeṇa dṛśyate | tryaṃśakalpaṃ prāyeṇa tryaṃśavyavahārayogyaṃ bhūyiṣṭham adhikataraṃ te rūpaṃ sāndramodātmakaṃ paramānandarūpaṃ tato brahmāṇḍād upari vahirdeśe bhāti | tasmai aparicchinnātmane ||9||
evaṃ ‘yasmin' ityārabhya viṃśatyā kṣlokai saguṇanirguṇabhedena bhagavantaṃ stutimiṣeṇānusandadhānaḥ svasya tatrānadhikāramāśaṅkamāno'bhīṣṭāyāṃ mūrtau cittamavatārayitumārabhate -- 
avyaktaṃ te svarūpaṃ duradhigamatamaṃ tat tu śuddhaikasattvaṃ
vyakta cāpyetadeva sphuṭamamṛtarasāmbhodhikallolatulyama |
sarvotkṛṣṭāmabhīṣṭāṃ tadiha guṇarasenaiva cittaṃ harantīṃ
mūrti te saṃśraye'haṃ pavanapurapate! pāhi māṃ kṛṣṇa! rogāt || 10 ||
