avyaktamiti | te avyaktaṃ kenāpi na vyajyata ityavyaktaṃ brahmasvarūpaṃ duradhigamatamam atiśayena duradhigamaṃ duḥkhena jñātuṃ prāptuṃ vā śakyaṃ, "kleśo'dhikatarasteṣāṃmavyaktāsaktacetasām" iti bhagavadvacanāt | tat śuddhaikasattvaṃ śuddhasattvātmakaṃ tu te svarūpaṃ vyaktaṃ jñātuṃ śakyam | apica etadeva śrīkṛṣṇādirūpam amṛtarasāmbhodhikallolatulyaṃ brahmānandāmṛtarasāmbhodheḥ kallolena tulyaṃ mukhasevyam | yasmādevaṃ tat tasmāt sarvotkṛṣṭāmabhīṣṭām iha bhagavati ye guṇā bhaktavātsalyādayaḥ ta eva rasāḥ āsvādanīyatvāt tenaiva cittaṃ harantīṃ manoharāṃ te mūrtimahaṃ saṃśraye stotuṃ dhyātuṃ vā samārabhe | he pavanapurapate ! śrīkṛṣṇa ! māṃ rogāt saṃsāralakṣaṇāt pāhi ||10||
iti bhagavanmahimānuvarṇanamekonaśatatamaṃ daśakam |
evaṃ bhagavadavatāracaritasmaraṇa kīrtanādimirupabṛṃhitapremalakṣaṇabhaktiparipākavaśena hṛdayākāśamavatīrṇa sphuṭapratīyamānāvayathaveṣaviśepādikaṃ bhagavadapa pura ivāvalokayan yathānubhūtaṃ stauti -- 
agre paśyāmi tejo nivitarakalāyāvalobhanīyaṃ
pīyūṣāplāvito'haṃ tadanu tadusare divyakaśoravepam |
tāruṇyārambharamyaṃ paramasukharasāsvādaromāścitāṅgairāvītaṃ nāradādhairvilasadupaniṣatsundarīmaṇḍalaitha || 1 ||
agra iti | tejaḥ prabhāmaṇḍalam | tadnu taddarśanānandaraṃ divyaḥ sāttvikaḥ prakāśojjvalaḥ ā ṣoḍaśābdāt kaiśoraḥ, tatsambandhī veṣo yasmin | tāruṇyārambharamyaṃ ṣoḍaśavarṣīyaṃ kimapi rūpaṃ ca paśyāmi | ādhīta parivāritam | upaniṣatsundarīmaṇḍalairityanenedamavagamyate -- paraṃ brahma śrīkṛṣṇaḥ gopyasta tpratipādakopaniṣadaḥ, śrīnāradāiyastadupadeṣṭāraḥ draṣṭādhikārī ||1||
divyakaiśoraveṣamiti yaduktaṃ tadevāha -- 
nīlābhaṃ kuñcitāgraṃ dhanabhagalatara saṃgataṃ cārubhaṅgyā
ratnottaṃsābhirāmaṃ balagitamudakaiḥ piñchajālaiḥ |
mandārasanivīnaṃ tava pṛthukabarībhāramālokaye'haṃ
snigdhaśvetordhvapuṇḍrāmapi ca sulalitāṃ phālavālenduvīthīm || 2 ||
nīlābhamiti | ghanaṃ niviḍam | udayaccandrakaiḥ prakāśamānāgradeśasthacitravarṇaiḥ | mandārasrajā surakusumaviracitāmlānamālayā nighītaṃ veṣṭitam || 2 ||
yathānubhṛtaṃ netrāmbujadvandvaṃ stauti -- 
hṛdyaṃ pūrṇānukampārṇavamṛdulaharīcañcalavilāsairānīlasnigdhapakṣmāvaliparilasitaṃ netrayugmaṃ vibho ! te |
sāndracchāyaṃ viśālāruṇakamaladalākāramāmugdhatāraṃ kāruṇyālokalīlāśiśiritabhuvanaṃ kṣipyatāṃ mayyanāthe || 3 ||
hṛdyamiti | anāthe aśaraṇe || 3 ||
uttuṅgollāsināsaṃ harimaṇimukuraprollasadgaṇḍapālīvyālolatkarṇapāśāñcitamakaramaṇīkuṇḍaladvandvadīpram |
unmīladdantapaṅktisphuradaruṇataracchāyabimbādharāntaḥ prītiprasyandimandasmitaśiśirataraṃ vatrtamudbhāsatāṃ me || 4 ||
'uttuṅgeti | harimaṇimukuravad indranīlamaṇimayadarpaṇavat prollasantyāṃ gaṇḍapālyāṃ kapoladeśe vyālolatā bimbapratibimbadvairūpyeṇa dolāyamānena makarākārayormaṇikuṇḍalayordvandvena dīpraṃ dīpanaśīlam || 4 ||
bāhuindrena ratnojjalavalayabhṛtā śoṇapāṇipravālenopāttāṃ veṇunālīṃ prasṛtanakhamayūkhāṅagulīsaṅgaśārām |
kṛtvā vakrāravinde sumadhuravikasadrāgamudbhāvyamānaiḥ śabdabrahmāmṛtastvaṃ śiśiritabhuvanaiḥ siñca me karṇavīthīm || 5 ||
bāhudvandveneti | prasṛtanakhamayūkhāṅgulīsaṅgaśārāṃ prasṛtanakhamayūkhānām aṅgulīnāṃ saṅgena śārāṃ śabalavarṇām | sumadhuro vikasan prakāśamāno rāgo gītaviśeṣo yasminniti kriyāviśeṣaṇam | udbhāvyamānaiḥ prakāśyamānaiḥ śabdabrahmāmṛtaiḥ nādāmṛtaiḥ || 5 ||
utsarpatkaustubhaśrītātībhiraruṇitaṃ komalaṃ kaṇṭhadeśaṃ vakṣaḥ śrīvatsaramyaṃ taralatarasamuhīprahārapratānam |
nānāvarṇaprasūnāvalikisalayinīṃ vanyamālāṃ vilolallolambāṃ lambamānāmurasi tava tathā bhāvaye ratnamālām || 6 ||
utsarpaditi | hāspratānaṃ mauktikamālājālam | vilolallolambāmurāsi tathā ratnamālāṃ ca bhāvaye || 6 ||
aṅge pañcāṅgarāgairatiśayavikasatsaurabhākṛṣṭalokaṃ
līnānekatrilokīvitatimapi kṛśāṃ bibhrataṃ madhyavallīm |
śakrāśmanyastataptojjvalakanakanibhaṃ pītacelaṃ dadhānaṃ
dhyāyāmo dīptarakṣsphuṭamaṇiraśanākiṅkiṇīmaṇḍitaṃ tvām || 7 ||
aṅga iti | pañcāṅgarāgaiḥ haricandanagorocanādibhiḥ atiśayena vikasatā vyāptena saurabheṇākṛṣṭā lokā yena sa tathā | līnānekatrilokīm antargatānekabrahmāṇḍāmapi kṛśāmiti | ayamarthaḥ -- yadyapīdamapi brahmāṇḍaṃ śrīkṛṣṇodarasthaṃ, tathāpi etadbrahmāṇḍāntasthānāṃ tadānīntanānāṃ svamūrteridantayā pradarśanaṃ bhagavato yogaśvaryavaibhavena | evañca madhyavallyāḥ kṛśatvāpādanaṃ nāsya kiñcid bhāra iti | śakrāśmeti | bhagavanmūrterindranīlaśyāmatvāt pītāmbarasya kanakanibhatvāñca sañjātaparabhāgamiti bhāvaḥ | dīptābhiḥ raśmibhiḥ maṇidīptibhiḥ sphuṭayā prakāśamānayā || 7 ||
ūrū cārū tavorū ghanamasṛṇarucau cittacorau ramāyā viśvakṣobhaṃ viśaṅkaya dhruvamaniśamubhau pītacelāhavṛtāṅgau |
ānamrāṇāṃ purastānnyasanadhṛtasamastārthapālīsamudracchāyaṃ jānudvayaṃ ca kramapṛthulamanojñe ca jaṅge niṣeve || 8 ||
'ūrū iti| viśvakṣobhaṃ lokottararāmaṇīyakatvena draṣṭṛjanapāravaśyaṃ viśaṅkaya| samastārthapālīnāṃ sakalapuruṣārthasārthānāṃ yaḥ samudgaḥ sampuṭakaḥ, tasyacchāyā śobheva śobhā yasya || 8 ||
mañjīraṃ mañjunādairiva padabhajanaṃ śreya ityālapantaṃ
pādāgraṃ bhrāntimajjatpraṇatajanamanomandaroddhārakūrmam |
uttuṅgatāmrarājannakharahimakarajyotstrayā cāśritānāṃ
santāpadhvāntahantrīṃ tatimanukalaye maṅgalāmaṅgulīnām || 9 ||
mañjīramiti | bhrāntirūpe'bdhau majjatāṃ praṇatajanānāmamṛtārthināṃ manorūpasya mandarasya uddhāre kūrmam | santāpasya dhvāntasya ajñānasya ca hantrīm||9||
yogandriāṇāṃ tvadaṅgeṣvadhikasumadhuraṃ muktibhājāṃ nivāso
bhaktānāṃ kāmavarṣayutarukisalayaṃ nātha! te pādamūlam |
nityaṃ cittasthitaṃ me pavanapurapate! kṛṣṇa! kāruṇyasindho !
hṭatvā niḥśeṣatāpān pradiśatu paramānandasandohalakṣmīm || 10 ||
yogīndrāṇāmiti | yogīndrāṇāṃ dhyātṝṇāṃ tvadaṅgeṣu madhye'dhikasumadhuram | muktibhājāṃ tu nivāsa āśrayaḥ | paramānandasandohalakṣmīṃ mokṣam || 10 ||
evaṃ stutimupasaṃhṛtya tatra vaiguṇyādisambhavamāśaṅkatha stotrasya sādguṇyakaraṇāyeśvare samarpayan vaktṛśrotṝṇāṃ paṭhanaśravaṇaphalamāśāste -- 
ajñātvā te mahattvaṃ yadiha nigaditaṃ viśvanātha ! kṣamethāḥ
stotraṃ caitat sahasrottaramadhikataraṃ tvatprasādāya bhūyāt |
dvedhā nārāyaṇīyaṃ śrutiṣu ca januṣā stutyatāvarṇanena
sphītaṃ līlāvatārairidamiha kurutāmāyurārogyasaukhyam || 11 ||
ajñātveti | dvedhā nārāyaṇīyaṃ nārāyaṇamadhikṛtya kṛtatvād nārāyaṇena proktatvāñca|nanu vihitamavihitaṃ vedaṃ stotraṃ tvatprasādāya bhūyāditi samarpyata iti cet, śruātivihitamevetyāha -- śrutiṣviti | januṣā avatārairbhagavataḥ stutyatayā varṇanaṃ vidhirvidyate, tena sphītaṃ vyāptam, athavā śrutiṣu yā januṣā stutyatā, tasyāḥ 'he stotāraḥ' ityādiśabdāntareṇa varṇanena līlāvatāraiśca sphītam idaṃ stotram iha vaktṛśrotṛṇām āyurārogyaṃ cānte saukhyaṃ mokṣaṃ va kurutām || 11 || 38 ||
iti keśādipādavarṇanaṃ śatatamaṃ daśakaṃ saikam |
śrībhāgavatatavghākhyādṛṣṭānarthāḥ padānyapi |
stotravyākhyānarūpeṇa racitāni paraṃ mayā ||
yannāmnā sammataṃ stotraṃ janānāmantarāntarā |
tābhyāmeva hṛdisthābhyāṃ mayā neyaṃ kṛtā kṛtiḥ ||
iti nārāyaṇīyastotravyākhyāyāṃ
bhaktamiyākhyāyāṃ
dvādaśaskandhaparicchedaḥ |
samāptaścārya granthaḥ |
